namespace HYSYSHelper;

public class HYSYSActivityPropertyConverter
{
	private List<string> _StringsForAcitivity;

	private string _TempfolerPath;

	private string _StrOutputFile;

	private bool _HasActivityProperty;

	private string _SimplifiedFileName;

	private Process procAPlusConverter;

	private BasisManager _Manager;

	private bool _HasAspenPlusActivityProperty;

	private System.Windows.Forms.Timer timer = new System.Windows.Forms.Timer();

	public bool ImportSuccesse { get; set; }

	public HYSYSActivityPropertyConverter(BasisManager manager, HYSYSFluidPackages packages)
	{
		_Manager = manager;
		if (packages != null)
		{
			_StringsForAcitivity = packages.StringsForAspenPlus;
			_HasActivityProperty = packages.HasAspenPlusActivityProperty;
			_HasAspenPlusActivityProperty = packages.IsEOSUsingAspenPlus;
		}
		_TempfolerPath = Environment.GetFolderPath(Environment.SpecialFolder.Personal) + "\\AspenTech\\Pro2Converter\\Temp\\";
		DirectoryInfo directoryInfo = new DirectoryInfo(_TempfolerPath);
		if (!directoryInfo.Exists)
		{
			directoryInfo.Create();
		}
		if (_HasActivityProperty || _HasAspenPlusActivityProperty)
		{
			try
			{
				DeleteTempFiles();
				CreateSimplePro2InputFile();
				CreateAspenPlusBkp();
				ImportPlusPropToHYSYS();
			}
			catch
			{
			}
		}
	}

	private void ImportPlusPropToHYSYS()
	{
		BackDoor backDoor = _Manager as BackDoor;
		string text = _TempfolerPath + "Simplified_plus.bkp";
		if (File.Exists(text))
		{
			backDoor.SendBackDoorMessage(":RecallFluidPkg2 \"" + text + "\"");
			if (_Manager.FluidPackages.Count == 0)
			{
				ImportSuccesse = false;
				DebugPrint.OutputDebugString(DebugLevel.BuildHYSYSCase, WarningLevel.Warning, P2Globalization.GetString("ConvertPropFailed"));
			}
			else
			{
				ImportSuccesse = true;
			}
		}
		else
		{
			ImportSuccesse = false;
			DebugPrint.OutputDebugString(DebugLevel.BuildHYSYSCase, WarningLevel.Warning, P2Globalization.GetString("ConvertPropFailed"));
		}
	}

	private string GetPlusConverterPath()
	{
		RegistryKey registryKey = Registry.LocalMachine.OpenSubKey("Software\\Aspentech\\Aspen Plus\\CurVer");
		if (registryKey != null)
		{
			string text = registryKey.GetValue(string.Empty).ToString();
			registryKey = Registry.LocalMachine.OpenSubKey("Software\\Aspentech\\Aspen Plus\\" + text + "\\aplus");
			string text2 = registryKey.GetValue("asptop").ToString();
			return text2 + "\\Xeq\\atp3.exe";
		}
		return string.Empty;
	}

	private void CreateSimplePro2InputFile()
	{
		_SimplifiedFileName = _TempfolerPath + "simple.inp";
		if (File.Exists(_SimplifiedFileName))
		{
			File.Delete(_SimplifiedFileName);
		}
		StreamWriter streamWriter = new StreamWriter(_SimplifiedFileName, append: false, Encoding.ASCII);
		if (_StringsForAcitivity != null && (_HasActivityProperty || _HasAspenPlusActivityProperty))
		{
			foreach (string item in _StringsForAcitivity)
			{
				string text = item;
				if (item.Trim().StartsWith("MWAVG"))
				{
					text = item.Replace("MWAVG", "MW");
				}
				if (!text.TrimStart().StartsWith("ENTH", StringComparison.InvariantCultureIgnoreCase))
				{
					streamWriter.WriteLine(text.Trim());
				}
			}
		}
		streamWriter.Close();
		StreamReader streamReader = new StreamReader(_SimplifiedFileName);
		List<string> list = new List<string>();
		while (!streamReader.EndOfStream)
		{
			string text2 = streamReader.ReadLine();
			if (text2.StartsWith("MWAVG"))
			{
				text2 = text2.Replace("MWAVG", "MW");
			}
			list.Add(text2);
		}
		streamReader.Close();
	}

	private void CreateAspenPlusBkp()
	{
		procAPlusConverter = new Process();
		_StrOutputFile = _TempfolerPath + "Simplified_plus.inp";
		string arg = _TempfolerPath + "error.txt";
		string arguments = $"\"{_SimplifiedFileName}\" \"{_StrOutputFile}\" \"{arg}\"";
		string plusConverterPath = GetPlusConverterPath();
		if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(plusConverterPath))
		{
			return;
		}
		procAPlusConverter.StartInfo.FileName = "\"" + plusConverterPath + "\"";
		procAPlusConverter.StartInfo.Arguments = arguments;
		procAPlusConverter.StartInfo.WorkingDirectory = _TempfolerPath;
		procAPlusConverter.StartInfo.CreateNoWindow = false;
		procAPlusConverter.Start();
		while (!procAPlusConverter.HasExited)
		{
			Thread.Sleep(50);
		}
		StreamReader streamReader = null;
		try
		{
			streamReader = new StreamReader(_StrOutputFile);
			string text = streamReader.ReadToEnd();
			if (text.Contains("DEXTROSE XYLOSE/"))
			{
				streamReader.Close();
				string[] array = text.Split('\n');
				string text2 = string.Empty;
				string[] array2 = array;
				foreach (string text3 in array2)
				{
					if (text3.Contains("DEXTROSE XYLOSE/"))
					{
						text2 = text3;
						break;
					}
				}
				if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(text2))
				{
					int num = text2.IndexOf("DEX");
					int num2 = text2.IndexOf("DEXTROSE XYLOSE/");
					if (num2 > num)
					{
						string oldValue = text2.Substring(num, num2 - num + 15);
						text = text.Replace(oldValue, "D-XYLOSE D-XYLOSE XYLOSE");
						StreamWriter streamWriter = new StreamWriter(_StrOutputFile, append: false, Encoding.ASCII);
						streamWriter.Write(text);
						streamWriter.Flush();
						streamWriter.Close();
					}
				}
			}
		}
		catch (Exception)
		{
			streamReader?.Close();
		}
		plusConverterPath = Path.GetDirectoryName(plusConverterPath);
		plusConverterPath += "\\aspen.exe";
		Process process = new Process();
		process.StartInfo.FileName = "\"" + plusConverterPath + "\"";
		string arguments2 = $"\"{_StrOutputFile}\" \"/mmbackup\"";
		process.StartInfo.Arguments = arguments2;
		process.StartInfo.WorkingDirectory = Path.GetDirectoryName(_StrOutputFile);
		process.StartInfo.CreateNoWindow = false;
		process.Start();
		while (!process.HasExited)
		{
			Thread.Sleep(50);
		}
	}

	private void DeleteTempFiles()
	{
		if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(_TempfolerPath) || !Directory.Exists(_TempfolerPath))
		{
			return;
		}
		string[] files = Directory.GetFiles(_TempfolerPath);
		foreach (string path in files)
		{
			try
			{
				File.Delete(path);
			}
			catch
			{
			}
		}
	}
}
internal class HYSYSAirCoolerConverter : HYSYSObjectConverterBase
{
	private AirCoolerOp _aircooler;

	private HYSYSAirCooler _data;

	private double _airFlow;

	public HYSYSAirCoolerConverter(SimulationCase simulationCase, HYSYSAirCooler data, bool bEDR)
		: base(simulationCase)
	{
		try
		{
			_data = data;
			_aircooler = _Flowsheet.get_Operations((object)"AirCoolerOpObject").Add(data.UID, "AirCoolerOpObject") as AirCoolerOp;
			CreateAirCooler(bEDR);
		}
		catch (Exception)
		{
		}
	}

	private void CreateAirCooler(bool bUsingEDR)
	{
		ProcessStream processStream = null;
		ProcessStream processStream2 = null;
		ProcessStream processStream3 = null;
		BackDoor backDoor = null;
		BackDoor backDoor2 = null;
		BackDoor backDoor3 = null;
		try
		{
			backDoor2 = _aircooler as BackDoor;
			if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(_data.FluidPackage))
			{
				string methodName = HYSYSFluidPackages.GetMethodName(_data.FluidPackage);
				if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(methodName))
				{
					backDoor3 = _SimCase.BasisManager.FluidPackages[methodName] as BackDoor;
					backDoor2.SendAttachObject("ExchSide.500.0:CompList.300", backDoor3);
				}
			}
			processStream = GetProcessStream(_data.TubeSideInletStreams[0]);
			backDoor = processStream as BackDoor;
			backDoor2.SendAttachObject(":MaterialStream.400", backDoor);
			processStream2 = GetProcessStream(_data.TubeSideOutletStreams[0]);
			backDoor = processStream2 as BackDoor;
			backDoor2.SendAttachObject(":MaterialStream.401", backDoor);
			if (_data.AirSideInletStreams.Count > 0)
			{
				processStream3 = GetProcessStream(_data.AirSideInletStreams[0]);
				InternalRealVariable obj = backDoor2.get_BackDoorVariable((object)":Temperature.500").Variable as InternalRealVariable;
				obj.SetValue(processStream3.Temperature.Value, "");
				CleanResource.ReleaseComObjectHelper(obj, bFinalRelease: false);
				InternalRealVariable obj2 = backDoor2.get_BackDoorVariable((object)":Pressure.500").Variable as InternalRealVariable;
				obj2.SetValue(processStream3.Pressure.Value, "");
				CleanResource.ReleaseComObjectHelper(obj2, bFinalRelease: false);
				foreach (string airSideInletStream in _data.AirSideInletStreams)
				{
					ProcessStream processStream4 = GetProcessStream(airSideInletStream);
					_airFlow += processStream4.ActualGasFlow.Value;
					CleanResource.ReleaseComObjectHelper(processStream4, bFinalRelease: false);
				}
			}
			InternalRealVariable internalRealVariable = backDoor2.get_BackDoorVariable((object)":Enum.500").Variable as InternalRealVariable;
			if (bUsingEDR)
			{
				internalRealVariable.Value = 2.0;
				if (processStream2.Temperature.CanModify)
				{
					processStream2.Temperature.Value = -32767.0;
				}
				if (processStream2.Pressure.CanModify)
				{
					processStream2.Pressure.Value = -32767.0;
				}
				if (processStream3 != null)
				{
					InternalRealVariable obj3 = backDoor2.get_BackDoorVariable((object)":MassFlow.501").Variable as InternalRealVariable;
					obj3.SetValue(processStream3.MassFlowValue, "");
					CleanResource.ReleaseComObjectHelper(obj3, bFinalRelease: false);
				}
				SetEDRModelParameters(backDoor2);
			}
			else
			{
				internalRealVariable.Value = 0.0;
				SetSimpleModelParameters(backDoor2, processStream, processStream3, processStream2);
			}
			foreach (string tobeDeletedStream in _data.TobeDeletedStreams)
			{
				RemoveStream(tobeDeletedStream);
			}
			InternalTextVariable internalTextVariable = backDoor2.get_BackDoorTextVariable((object)":Notes.400").Variable as InternalTextVariable;
			foreach (string convertNote in _data.ConvertNotes)
			{
				internalTextVariable.Value = internalTextVariable.Value + convertNote + "\n";
			}
		}
		catch (Exception)
		{
			DebugPrint.OutputDebugString(DebugLevel.BuildHYSYSCase, WarningLevel.Error, string.Format(P2Globalization.GetString("UOConverter_Failed"), "HYSYS Aircooler unit operation block", _data.UID));
		}
		finally
		{
			CleanResource.ReleaseComObjectHelper(processStream3, bFinalRelease: false);
			CleanResource.ReleaseComObjectHelper(processStream, bFinalRelease: false);
			CleanResource.ReleaseComObjectHelper(processStream2, bFinalRelease: false);
			CleanResource.ReleaseComObjectHelper(backDoor2, bFinalRelease: false);
			CleanResource.ReleaseComObjectHelper(backDoor, bFinalRelease: false);
			CleanResource.ReleaseComObjectHelper(backDoor3, bFinalRelease: false);
		}
	}

	private void SetSimpleModelParameters(BackDoor aircoolerBD, ProcessStream tubeIn, ProcessStream airIn, ProcessStream tubeOut)
	{
		BackDoor backDoor = null;
		bool flag = false;
		bool flag2 = false;
		bool flag3 = false;
		if (_data.ATempSpec != null || _data.HICOSpec != null || (_data.COCISpec != null && airIn != null))
		{
			flag = true;
		}
		try
		{
			backDoor = tubeOut as BackDoor;
			InternalRealVariable internalRealVariable = null;
			if (_data.FanNumber == 0)
			{
				(aircoolerBD.get_BackDoorVariable((object)":Boolean.520.0").Variable as InternalRealVariable).Value = 1.0;
				flag = true;
			}
			else if (_data.FanNumber > 1)
			{
				InternalRealVariable obj = aircoolerBD.get_BackDoorVariable((object)":Index.501").Variable as InternalRealVariable;
				obj.Value = Convert.ToDouble(_data.FanNumber);
				CleanResource.ReleaseComObjectHelper(obj, bFinalRelease: false);
				if (flag)
				{
					for (int i = 0; i < _data.FanNumber; i++)
					{
						internalRealVariable = aircoolerBD.get_BackDoorVariable((object)(":ActGasFlow.501." + i)).Variable as InternalRealVariable;
						internalRealVariable.SetValue(-32767.0, "");
					}
				}
				else if (airIn != null && _airFlow != 0.0)
				{
					double val = _airFlow / Convert.ToDouble(_data.FanNumber);
					for (int j = 0; j < _data.FanNumber; j++)
					{
						internalRealVariable = aircoolerBD.get_BackDoorVariable((object)(":ActGasFlow.501." + j)).Variable as InternalRealVariable;
						internalRealVariable.SetValue(val, "");
					}
				}
			}
			else
			{
				internalRealVariable = aircoolerBD.get_BackDoorVariable((object)":ActGasFlow.501.0").Variable as InternalRealVariable;
				if (flag)
				{
					internalRealVariable.Value = -32767.0;
				}
				else if (airIn != null && _airFlow != 0.0)
				{
					internalRealVariable.SetValue(_airFlow, "");
				}
			}
			CleanResource.ReleaseComObjectHelper(internalRealVariable, bFinalRelease: false);
			bool flag4 = false;
			string text = _data.TubeRowNumber + "-" + _data.TubePassNumber;
			if (_data.TubeConfiguration.Contains(text))
			{
				flag4 = true;
				int value = _data.TubeConfiguration.IndexOf(text);
				InternalRealVariable obj2 = aircoolerBD.get_BackDoorVariable((object)":Index.500").Variable as InternalRealVariable;
				obj2.Value = Convert.ToDouble(value);
				CleanResource.ReleaseComObjectHelper(obj2, bFinalRelease: false);
			}
			InternalRealVariable internalRealVariable2 = aircoolerBD.get_BackDoorVariable((object)":PressureDrop.500.0").Variable as InternalRealVariable;
			if (internalRealVariable2.Value == -32767.0)
			{
				if (_data.DP != null)
				{
					tubeOut.Pressure.Value = -32767.0;
					internalRealVariable2.SetValue(_data.DP.Value, _data.DP.Unit);
					flag2 = true;
				}
				else
				{
					InternalRealVariable obj3 = backDoor.get_BackDoorVariable((object)":Pressure.502.0").Variable as InternalRealVariable;
					if (obj3.Value == -32767.0)
					{
						if (_data.MINDP != null && _data.MAXDP != null)
						{
							tubeOut.Pressure.Value = -32767.0;
							internalRealVariable2.SetValue((_data.MINDP.Value + _data.MAXDP.Value) / 2.0, _data.MAXDP.Unit);
							flag2 = true;
						}
						else if (_data.MINDP != null)
						{
							tubeOut.Pressure.Value = -32767.0;
							internalRealVariable2.SetValue(_data.MINDP.Value, _data.MINDP.Unit);
							flag2 = true;
						}
						else if (_data.MAXDP != null)
						{
							tubeOut.Pressure.Value = -32767.0;
							internalRealVariable2.SetValue(_data.MAXDP.Value, _data.MAXDP.Unit);
							flag2 = true;
						}
					}
					CleanResource.ReleaseComObjectHelper(obj3, bFinalRelease: false);
				}
			}
			InternalRealVariable internalRealVariable3 = aircoolerBD.get_BackDoorVariable((object)":Temperature.501").Variable as InternalRealVariable;
			if (internalRealVariable3.Value == -32767.0)
			{
				if (_data.ATempSpec != null)
				{
					internalRealVariable3.SetValue(_data.ATempSpec.Value, _data.ATempSpec.Unit);
				}
				if (_data.COCISpec != null && airIn != null)
				{
					internalRealVariable3.SetValue(airIn.TemperatureValue + _data.COCISpec.Value, _data.COCISpec.Unit);
				}
				if (_data.HICOSpec != null)
				{
					internalRealVariable3.SetValue(tubeIn.TemperatureValue - _data.HICOSpec.Value, _data.HICOSpec.Unit);
				}
				CleanResource.ReleaseComObjectHelper(internalRealVariable3, bFinalRelease: false);
			}
			InternalRealVariable obj4 = backDoor.get_BackDoorVariable((object)":Temperature.502.0").Variable as InternalRealVariable;
			if (obj4.Value == -32767.0)
			{
				RealVariable temperature = tubeOut.Temperature;
				if (_data.TTempSpec != null)
				{
					tubeOut.Temperature.Value = -32767.0;
					temperature.SetValue(_data.TTempSpec.Value, _data.TTempSpec.Unit);
					flag3 = true;
				}
				if (_data.HOCISpec != null && airIn != null)
				{
					tubeOut.Temperature.Value = -32767.0;
					temperature.SetValue(airIn.Temperature.Value + _data.HOCISpec.Value, _data.HOCISpec.Unit);
					flag3 = true;
				}
				if (_data.HIHOSpec != null)
				{
					tubeOut.Temperature.Value = -32767.0;
					temperature.SetValue(tubeIn.Temperature.Value - _data.HIHOSpec.Value, _data.HIHOSpec.Unit);
					flag3 = true;
				}
				CleanResource.ReleaseComObjectHelper(temperature, bFinalRelease: false);
				if (_data.TLFracSpec != null && (!flag2 || !flag3))
				{
					tubeOut.VapourFraction.SetValue(1.0 - _data.TLFracSpec.Value, _data.TLFracSpec.Unit);
				}
			}
			CleanResource.ReleaseComObjectHelper(obj4, bFinalRelease: false);
			_data.ConvertNotes.Add(string.Format(P2Globalization.GetString("KeyWordWasNotConverted"), "IfCoCurrent"));
			_data.ConvertNotes.Add(string.Format(P2Globalization.GetString("KeyWordWasNotConverted"), "nTubes"));
			if (!flag4)
			{
				_data.ConvertNotes.Add(string.Format(P2Globalization.GetString("KeyWordWasNotConverted"), "nTubePass"));
			}
			_data.ConvertNotes.Add(string.Format(P2Globalization.GetString("KeyWordWasNotConverted"), "iPattern"));
			if (!flag4)
			{
				_data.ConvertNotes.Add(string.Format(P2Globalization.GetString("KeyWordWasNotConverted"), "nTubeRow"));
			}
			_data.ConvertNotes.Add(string.Format(P2Globalization.GetString("KeyWordWasNotConverted"), "NBunParallel"));
			_data.ConvertNotes.Add(string.Format(P2Globalization.GetString("KeyWordWasNotConverted"), "nBayParallel"));
			_data.ConvertNotes.Add(string.Format(P2Globalization.GetString("KeyWordWasNotConverted"), "iDraft"));
			if (_data.TubeLength != null)
			{
				_data.ConvertNotes.Add(string.Format(P2Globalization.GetString("KeyWordWasNotConverted"), "TubeLength"));
			}
			if (_data.TubeID != null)
			{
				_data.ConvertNotes.Add(string.Format(P2Globalization.GetString("KeyWordWasNotConverted"), "TubeID"));
			}
			if (_data.TubeOD != null)
			{
				_data.ConvertNotes.Add(string.Format(P2Globalization.GetString("KeyWordWasNotConverted"), "TubeOD"));
			}
			if (_data.TubeThickness != null)
			{
				_data.ConvertNotes.Add(string.Format(P2Globalization.GetString("KeyWordWasNotConverted"), "TubeThick"));
			}
			if (_data.TransPitch != null)
			{
				_data.ConvertNotes.Add(string.Format(P2Globalization.GetString("KeyWordWasNotConverted"), "TransPitch"));
			}
			if (_data.LongPitch != null)
			{
				_data.ConvertNotes.Add(string.Format(P2Globalization.GetString("KeyWordWasNotConverted"), "LongPitch"));
			}
			if (_data.TubeTCond != null)
			{
				_data.ConvertNotes.Add(string.Format(P2Globalization.GetString("KeyWordWasNotConverted"), "TubeTCond"));
			}
			if (_data.TubeFoul != null)
			{
				_data.ConvertNotes.Add(string.Format(P2Globalization.GetString("KeyWordWasNotConverted"), "TubeFoul"));
			}
			if (_data.FinThickness != null)
			{
				_data.ConvertNotes.Add(string.Format(P2Globalization.GetString("KeyWordWasNotConverted"), "FinThick"));
			}
			if (_data.FinTCond != null)
			{
				_data.ConvertNotes.Add(string.Format(P2Globalization.GetString("KeyWordWasNotConverted"), "FinTCond"));
			}
			if (_data.AirFouling != null)
			{
				_data.ConvertNotes.Add(string.Format(P2Globalization.GetString("KeyWordWasNotConverted"), "AFoulResist"));
			}
			if (_data.FanDiameter != null)
			{
				_data.ConvertNotes.Add(string.Format(P2Globalization.GetString("KeyWordWasNotConverted"), "FanDiam"));
			}
			_data.ConvertNotes.Add(string.Format(P2Globalization.GetString("KeyWordWasNotConverted"), "FanEff"));
			if (_data.NozInID != null)
			{
				_data.ConvertNotes.Add(string.Format(P2Globalization.GetString("KeyWordWasNotConverted"), "NozInID"));
			}
			if (_data.NozOutID != null)
			{
				_data.ConvertNotes.Add(string.Format(P2Globalization.GetString("KeyWordWasNotConverted"), "NozOutID"));
			}
		}
		catch (Exception)
		{
			DebugPrint.OutputDebugString(DebugLevel.BuildHYSYSCase, WarningLevel.Error, string.Format(P2Globalization.GetString("SetAIRCSimpleParasFailed"), _data.UID));
		}
		finally
		{
			CleanResource.ReleaseComObjectHelper(backDoor, bFinalRelease: false);
		}
	}

	private void SetEDRModelParameters(BackDoor aircoolerBD)
	{
		InternalRealVariable internalRealVariable = null;
		try
		{
			if (_data.TubeNumber != 0)
			{
				internalRealVariable = aircoolerBD.get_BackDoorVariable((object)"AirCoolerDesign.300/ACOLPlusCalculation.300:ExtraData.800(TubePerBund)").Variable as InternalRealVariable;
				internalRealVariable.Value = Convert.ToDouble(_data.TubeNumber);
			}
			if (_data.TubePassNumber != 0)
			{
				internalRealVariable = aircoolerBD.get_BackDoorVariable((object)"AirCoolerDesign.300/ACOLPlusCalculation.300:ExtraData.800(TubePassPerBund)").Variable as InternalRealVariable;
				internalRealVariable.Value = Convert.ToDouble(_data.TubePassNumber);
			}
			internalRealVariable = aircoolerBD.get_BackDoorVariable((object)"AirCoolerDesign.300/ACOLPlusCalculation.300:ExtraData.800(FlowOrientation)").Variable as InternalRealVariable;
			internalRealVariable.Value = Convert.ToDouble((!_data.IsCoCurrent) ? 1 : 2);
			internalRealVariable = aircoolerBD.get_BackDoorVariable((object)"AirCoolerDesign.300/ACOLPlusCalculation.300:ExtraData.800(TypeBundle)").Variable as InternalRealVariable;
			internalRealVariable.Value = Convert.ToDouble(_data.IsInlineBundle ? 1 : 0);
			if (_data.TubeRowNumber != 0)
			{
				internalRealVariable = aircoolerBD.get_BackDoorVariable((object)"AirCoolerDesign.300/ACOLPlusCalculation.300:ExtraData.800(TubeRowDeep)").Variable as InternalRealVariable;
				internalRealVariable.Value = Convert.ToDouble(_data.TubeRowNumber);
			}
			if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(_data.TubeMaterial))
			{
				_data.ConvertNotes.Add(string.Format(P2Globalization.GetString("KeyWordWasNotConverted"), "TubeMaterial"));
			}
			internalRealVariable = aircoolerBD.get_BackDoorVariable((object)"AirCoolerDesign.300/ACOLPlusCalculation.300:ExtraData.800(BundPar)").Variable as InternalRealVariable;
			internalRealVariable.Value = Convert.ToDouble(_data.BundleNumberPerBay);
			if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(_data.FinMaterial))
			{
				_data.ConvertNotes.Add(string.Format(P2Globalization.GetString("KeyWordWasNotConverted"), "FinMaterial"));
			}
			else
			{
				internalRealVariable = aircoolerBD.get_BackDoorVariable((object)"AirCoolerDesign.300/ACOLPlusCalculation.300:ExtraData.857.0(arTubeFinMtl)").Variable as InternalRealVariable;
				internalRealVariable.Value = 1.0;
			}
			if (_data.BayNumberPerUnit != 0)
			{
				internalRealVariable = aircoolerBD.get_BackDoorVariable((object)"AirCoolerDesign.300/ACOLPlusCalculation.300:ExtraData.800(BayPar)").Variable as InternalRealVariable;
				internalRealVariable.Value = Convert.ToDouble(_data.BayNumberPerUnit);
			}
			internalRealVariable = aircoolerBD.get_BackDoorVariable((object)"AirCoolerDesign.300/ACOLPlusCalculation.300:ExtraData.800(DraftType)").Variable as InternalRealVariable;
			if (_data.FanNumber == 0)
			{
				internalRealVariable.Value = 3.0;
			}
			else
			{
				internalRealVariable.Value = Convert.ToDouble(_data.IsFanForced ? 1 : 2);
			}
			if (_data.TubeLength != null)
			{
				internalRealVariable = aircoolerBD.get_BackDoorVariable((object)"AirCoolerDesign.300/ACOLPlusCalculation.300:ExtraData.800(TubeLng)").Variable as InternalRealVariable;
				internalRealVariable.SetValue(_data.TubeLength.Value, _data.TubeLength.Unit);
			}
			if (_data.TubeID != null)
			{
				internalRealVariable = aircoolerBD.get_BackDoorVariable((object)"AirCoolerDesign.300/ACOLPlusCalculation.300:ExtraData.857.0(arTubeID)").Variable as InternalRealVariable;
				internalRealVariable.SetValue(_data.TubeID.Value, _data.TubeID.Unit);
			}
			if (_data.TubeOD != null)
			{
				internalRealVariable = aircoolerBD.get_BackDoorVariable((object)"AirCoolerDesign.300/ACOLPlusCalculation.300:ExtraData.857.0(arTubeOD)").Variable as InternalRealVariable;
				internalRealVariable.SetValue(_data.TubeOD.Value, _data.TubeOD.Unit);
			}
			if (_data.TubeThickness != null)
			{
				internalRealVariable = aircoolerBD.get_BackDoorVariable((object)"AirCoolerDesign.300/ACOLPlusCalculation.300:ExtraData.857.0(arTubeWallThk)").Variable as InternalRealVariable;
				internalRealVariable.SetValue(_data.TubeThickness.Value, _data.TubeThickness.Unit);
			}
			if (_data.TransPitch != null)
			{
				internalRealVariable = aircoolerBD.get_BackDoorVariable((object)"AirCoolerDesign.300/ACOLPlusCalculation.300:ExtraData.800(TubePitFR)").Variable as InternalRealVariable;
				internalRealVariable.SetValue(_data.TransPitch.Value, _data.TransPitch.Unit);
			}
			if (_data.LongPitch != null)
			{
				internalRealVariable = aircoolerBD.get_BackDoorVariable((object)"AirCoolerDesign.300/ACOLPlusCalculation.300:ExtraData.800(TubePitRD)").Variable as InternalRealVariable;
				internalRealVariable.SetValue(_data.LongPitch.Value, _data.LongPitch.Unit);
			}
			if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(_data.TubeMaterial))
			{
				internalRealVariable = aircoolerBD.get_BackDoorVariable((object)"AirCoolerDesign.300/ACOLPlusCalculation.300:ExtraData.800(TubeThCo)").Variable as InternalRealVariable;
				internalRealVariable.SetValue(51.9, "W/m-K");
			}
			else if (_data.TubeTCond != null)
			{
				_data.ConvertNotes.Add("TubeTCond(Value=" + _data.TubeTCond.Value + "was not converted");
			}
			if (_data.TubeFoul != null)
			{
				internalRealVariable = aircoolerBD.get_BackDoorVariable((object)"AirCoolerDesign.300/ACOLPlusCalculation.300:ExtraData.803(FoulResTS)").Variable as InternalRealVariable;
				internalRealVariable.SetValue(_data.TubeFoul.Value, _data.TubeFoul.Unit);
			}
			if (_data.FinThickness != null)
			{
				internalRealVariable = aircoolerBD.get_BackDoorVariable((object)"AirCoolerDesign.300/ACOLPlusCalculation.300:ExtraData.857.0(arFinthk)").Variable as InternalRealVariable;
				internalRealVariable.SetValue(_data.FinThickness.Value, _data.FinThickness.Unit);
			}
			if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(_data.FinMaterial))
			{
				internalRealVariable = aircoolerBD.get_BackDoorVariable((object)"AirCoolerDesign.300/ACOLPlusCalculation.300:ExtraData.857.0(arTubeFinThCo)").Variable as InternalRealVariable;
				internalRealVariable.SetValue(51.9, "W/m-K");
			}
			else if (_data.FinTCond != null)
			{
				_data.ConvertNotes.Add(string.Format(P2Globalization.GetString("KeyWordWasNotConverted"), "FinTCond"));
			}
			if (_data.AirFouling != null)
			{
				internalRealVariable = aircoolerBD.get_BackDoorVariable((object)"AirCoolerDesign.300/ACOLPlusCalculation.300:ExtraData.803(FoulResOT)").Variable as InternalRealVariable;
				internalRealVariable.SetValue(_data.AirFouling.Value, _data.AirFouling.Unit);
			}
			if (_data.FanDiameter != null)
			{
				internalRealVariable = aircoolerBD.get_BackDoorVariable((object)"AirCoolerDesign.300/ACOLPlusCalculation.300:ExtraData.800(FanOD)").Variable as InternalRealVariable;
				internalRealVariable.SetValue(_data.FanDiameter.Value, _data.FanDiameter.Unit);
			}
			internalRealVariable = aircoolerBD.get_BackDoorVariable((object)"AirCoolerDesign.300/ACOLPlusCalculation.300:ExtraData.800(FanDvEffy)").Variable as InternalRealVariable;
			internalRealVariable.Value = _data.FanEfficiency;
			if (_data.NozInID != null)
			{
				internalRealVariable = aircoolerBD.get_BackDoorVariable((object)"AirCoolerDesign.300/ACOLPlusCalculation.300:ExtraData.856(NozActualIDTS)").Variable as InternalRealVariable;
				internalRealVariable.SetValue(_data.NozInID.Value, _data.NozInID.Unit);
			}
			if (_data.NozOutID != null)
			{
				internalRealVariable = aircoolerBD.get_BackDoorVariable((object)"AirCoolerDesign.300/ACOLPlusCalculation.300:ExtraData.856(NozActualODTS)").Variable as InternalRealVariable;
				internalRealVariable.SetValue(_data.NozOutID.Value, _data.NozOutID.Unit);
			}
			if (_data.FanNumber != 0)
			{
				internalRealVariable = aircoolerBD.get_BackDoorVariable((object)"AirCoolerDesign.300/ACOLPlusCalculation.300:ExtraData.800(FanPerBay)").Variable as InternalRealVariable;
				internalRealVariable.Value = Convert.ToDouble(_data.FanNumber);
			}
			if (_data.DP != null)
			{
				_data.ConvertNotes.Add(string.Format(P2Globalization.GetString("KeyWordWasNotConverted"), "dPUnit"));
			}
			if (_data.TTempSpec != null)
			{
				_data.ConvertNotes.Add(string.Format(P2Globalization.GetString("KeyWordWasNotConverted"), "TTempSpec"));
			}
			if (_data.ATempSpec != null)
			{
				_data.ConvertNotes.Add(string.Format(P2Globalization.GetString("KeyWordWasNotConverted"), "ATempSpec"));
			}
			if (_data.HOCISpec != null)
			{
				_data.ConvertNotes.Add(string.Format(P2Globalization.GetString("KeyWordWasNotConverted"), "HOCISpec"));
			}
			if (_data.COCISpec != null)
			{
				_data.ConvertNotes.Add(string.Format(P2Globalization.GetString("KeyWordWasNotConverted"), "COCISpec"));
			}
			if (_data.HIHOSpec != null)
			{
				_data.ConvertNotes.Add(string.Format(P2Globalization.GetString("KeyWordWasNotConverted"), "HIHOSpec"));
			}
			if (_data.HICOSpec != null)
			{
				_data.ConvertNotes.Add(string.Format(P2Globalization.GetString("KeyWordWasNotConverted"), "HICOSpec"));
			}
			if (_data.TLFracSpec != null)
			{
				_data.ConvertNotes.Add(string.Format(P2Globalization.GetString("KeyWordWasNotConverted"), "TLFracSpec"));
			}
		}
		catch (Exception)
		{
			DebugPrint.OutputDebugString(DebugLevel.BuildHYSYSCase, WarningLevel.Error, string.Format(P2Globalization.GetString("SetAIRCEDRParasFailed"), _data.UID));
		}
		finally
		{
			CleanResource.ReleaseComObjectHelper(internalRealVariable, bFinalRelease: false);
		}
	}
}
public class HYSYSDistillationColumnConverter : HYSYSObjectConverterBase, IDisposable
{
	private HYSYSColumn _Data;

	private ColumnFlowsheet _ColumnFlowsheet;

	private ColumnOp _Column;

	private TraySection _TraySectionMainTS;

	private BackDoor _ColumnBD;

	private BackDoor _MainTSBD;

	private BackDoor _StreamBD;

	private int _InternalTrayNumberDiff;

	private int _AttachNumber;

	private int _internalFeedsNumber;

	private int _internalProductsNumber;

	private string method;

	private Dictionary<string, BackDoor> SubObjColl = new Dictionary<string, BackDoor>();

	private Dictionary<string, HYSYSColumn> SideColumnData = new Dictionary<string, HYSYSColumn>();

	private Dictionary<string, TraySection> SideColumnTraySection = new Dictionary<string, TraySection>();

	private Dictionary<string, int> SideColumnTrayDiff = new Dictionary<string, int>();

	private Dictionary<string, List<string>> overheadFeeds = new Dictionary<string, List<string>>();

	private Dictionary<string, List<string>> bottomFeeds = new Dictionary<string, List<string>>();

	private Dictionary<string, List<string>> additionalCondenserFeeds = new Dictionary<string, List<string>>();

	private Dictionary<string, List<string>> additionalReboilerFeeds = new Dictionary<string, List<string>>();

	private bool bCondVDrawExist;

	private bool bCondLDrawExist;

	private bool bCondDrawAdded;

	private bool bRROrRefluxSpecActive;

	private ColumnSpecification RROrRefluxSpec;

	private bool bReboilerQExist;

	private TraySizingUtility _tsUtility;

	private BackDoor _tsUtlBD;

	private Dictionary<string, BackDoor> sideTraySecBDColl = new Dictionary<string, BackDoor>();

	private List<ColumnSpecification> colStrmSpecsBeVaried = new List<ColumnSpecification>();

	private Dictionary<string, int> HysysCondType = new Dictionary<string, int>();

	private Dictionary<string, List<string>> HysysCondInletStreams = new Dictionary<string, List<string>>();

	private Dictionary<string, string> HysysCondVapOutStm = new Dictionary<string, string>();

	private Dictionary<string, string> HysysCondWaterOutStm = new Dictionary<string, string>();

	private Dictionary<string, string> HysysCondHCOutStm = new Dictionary<string, string>();

	private Dictionary<string, List<string>> HysysRebInletStreams = new Dictionary<string, List<string>>();

	private Dictionary<string, string> HysysRebLiqProdStm = new Dictionary<string, string>();

	private Dictionary<string, string> HysysRebVapProdStm = new Dictionary<string, string>();

	private Dictionary<string, List<IntValue>> HysysSideExchangerType = new Dictionary<string, List<IntValue>>();

	private Dictionary<string, List<IntValue>> HysysSideExchangerDrawStm = new Dictionary<string, List<IntValue>>();

	private Dictionary<string, List<IntValue>> HysysSideExchangerEnergyStm = new Dictionary<string, List<IntValue>>();

	private Dictionary<string, string> ExportStmAndPANamePair = new Dictionary<string, string>();

	private Dictionary<string, string> ExportStmAndPADrawNamePair = new Dictionary<string, string>();

	private Dictionary<int, PumpAround> paDutyID = new Dictionary<int, PumpAround>();

	private Dictionary<int, PumpAround> paDic = new Dictionary<int, PumpAround>();

	private int _specNumber;

	public HYSYSDistillationColumnConverter(SimulationCase simulationCase, HYSYSColumn data, ref Dictionary<string, BackDoor> columnColl)
		: base(simulationCase)
	{
		_Data = data;
		if (_Data.PhaseType == ColumnPhaseEnum.LL)
		{
			CreateExtractor(ref columnColl);
		}
		else
		{
			CreateColumn(ref columnColl);
		}
		SetIgnoreCheckBox();
	}

	private void SetIgnoreCheckBox()
	{
		if (_ColumnBD == null)
		{
			return;
		}
		try
		{
			if (Options.IgnoreSolvingColumn)
			{
				(_ColumnBD.get_BackDoorVariable((object)":Ignored.300").Variable as InternalRealVariable).Value = 1.0;
			}
		}
		catch (Exception)
		{
			DebugPrint.OutputDebugString(DebugLevel.BuildHYSYSCase, WarningLevel.Error, string.Format(string.Format(P2Globalization.GetString("SetIgnoreCheckboxErr"), _Data.UID)));
		}
	}

	private void CreateExtractor(ref Dictionary<string, BackDoor> columnColl)
	{
		try
		{
			_Column = _Flowsheet.get_Operations((object)"LiqLiqExtractorObject").Add(_Data.UID, "LiqLiqExtractorObject") as ColumnOp;
			_ColumnBD = _Column as BackDoor;
			_ColumnBD.SendBackDoorMessage("ReadPaletteTemplate liqliqex.COL");
			_ColumnFlowsheet = _Column.ColumnFlowsheet;
			_TraySectionMainTS = _ColumnFlowsheet.get_Operations((object)"traysection")[0] as TraySection;
			_MainTSBD = _TraySectionMainTS as BackDoor;
			SetLLExtractor(ref columnColl);
		}
		catch (Exception)
		{
			DebugPrint.OutputDebugString(DebugLevel.BuildHYSYSCase, WarningLevel.Error, string.Format(P2Globalization.GetString("CreateLLExtractorFailed"), _Data.UID));
		}
	}

	private void SetLLExtractor(ref Dictionary<string, BackDoor> columnColl)
	{
		SetTrayNumber(_Data.UID, bMainTray: true);
		SetColumnMainFlowsheetFeeds();
		SetColumnMainFlowsheetProducts();
		SetColumnData();
		InternalRealVariable obj = (_Flowsheet as BackDoor).get_BackDoorVariable((object)"SolverObject.400:Attribute.300").Variable as InternalRealVariable;
		obj.Value = 1.0;
		AddColumnSpecification();
		AddTraySizingUtility();
		AddUnconvertedNotes();
		obj.Value = 0.0;
		BackDoor value = _ColumnFlowsheet as BackDoor;
		columnColl.Add(_Data.UID, value);
	}

	private void CreateColumn(ref Dictionary<string, BackDoor> columnColl)
	{
		_Column = _Flowsheet.get_Operations((object)"ColumnOp").Add(_Data.UID, "DistillationObject") as ColumnOp;
		GetVarysInSidecolumns();
		InternalRealVariable obj = (_Flowsheet as BackDoor).get_BackDoorVariable((object)"SolverObject.400:Attribute.300").Variable as InternalRealVariable;
		obj.Value = 0.0;
		CustomizeColumn();
		obj.Value = 1.0;
		SetAdditionalEnergyStream();
		SetColumnData();
		obj.Value = 1.0;
		AddColumnSpecification();
		AddTraySizingUtility();
		AddUnconvertedNotes();
		obj.Value = 0.0;
		BackDoor value = _ColumnFlowsheet as BackDoor;
		columnColl.Add(_Data.UID, value);
	}

	private void CustomizeColumn()
	{
		(_Column as BackDoor).SendBackDoorMessage("StartNewTemplate");
		_ColumnFlowsheet = _Column.ColumnFlowsheet;
		_TraySectionMainTS = _ColumnFlowsheet.get_Operations((object)"traysection").Add("TS-1", "TraySectionObject") as TraySection;
		ConfigureMainFlowsheet();
		ConfigureSubFlowsheet();
	}

	private void GetVarysInSidecolumns()
	{
		foreach (HYSYSColumn sIDESTRIPPER in _Data.SIDESTRIPPERS)
		{
			foreach (string varyDutyID in sIDESTRIPPER.VaryDutyIDs)
			{
				_Data.VaryDutyIDs.Add(varyDutyID);
			}
			foreach (string varyDutyName in sIDESTRIPPER.VaryDutyNames)
			{
				_Data.VaryDutyNames.Add(varyDutyName);
			}
			foreach (string varyDrawStreamID in sIDESTRIPPER.VaryDrawStreamIDs)
			{
				_Data.VaryDrawStreamIDs.Add(varyDrawStreamID);
			}
			foreach (string varyFeedStreamID in sIDESTRIPPER.VaryFeedStreamIDs)
			{
				_Data.VaryFeedStreamIDs.Add(varyFeedStreamID);
			}
		}
		foreach (HYSYSColumn sIDERECTIFIER in _Data.SIDERECTIFIERS)
		{
			foreach (string varyDutyID2 in sIDERECTIFIER.VaryDutyIDs)
			{
				_Data.VaryDutyIDs.Add(varyDutyID2);
			}
			foreach (string varyDutyName2 in sIDERECTIFIER.VaryDutyNames)
			{
				_Data.VaryDutyNames.Add(varyDutyName2);
			}
			foreach (string varyDrawStreamID2 in sIDERECTIFIER.VaryDrawStreamIDs)
			{
				_Data.VaryDrawStreamIDs.Add(varyDrawStreamID2);
			}
			foreach (string varyFeedStreamID2 in sIDERECTIFIER.VaryFeedStreamIDs)
			{
				_Data.VaryFeedStreamIDs.Add(varyFeedStreamID2);
			}
		}
	}

	private void ConfigureMainFlowsheet()
	{
		SetSolvingMethod();
		SetTrayNumber(_Data.UID, bMainTray: true);
		SetColumnMainFlowsheetFeeds();
		SetColumnMainFlowsheetProducts();
	}

	private void SetTrayNumber(string columnID, bool bMainTray)
	{
		HYSYSColumn hYSYSColumn;
		TraySection traySection;
		if (bMainTray)
		{
			hYSYSColumn = _Data;
			traySection = _TraySectionMainTS;
		}
		else
		{
			hYSYSColumn = SideColumnData[columnID];
			traySection = SideColumnTraySection[columnID];
		}
		int num = hYSYSColumn.stageNumber;
		int num2 = 0;
		if (hYSYSColumn.bCondenserExist)
		{
			num2 = 1;
			num--;
		}
		if (hYSYSColumn.bReboilerExist)
		{
			num--;
		}
		traySection.NumberOfTrays = num;
		if (bMainTray)
		{
			_InternalTrayNumberDiff = num2;
		}
		else
		{
			SideColumnTrayDiff.Add(columnID, num2);
		}
	}

	private void SetSolvingMethod()
	{
		_ColumnBD = _Column as BackDoor;
		try
		{
			InternalRealVariable internalRealVariable = _ColumnBD.get_BackDoorVariable((object)"FlowSht.600:Enum.502").Variable as InternalRealVariable;
			if (_Data.SolvingMethod == SolveMethodTypeEnum.SPARSECONTINUATIONSOLVER && _Data.bVLLECheckOn)
			{
				internalRealVariable.Value = 5.0;
			}
			else
			{
				internalRealVariable.Value = 1.0;
			}
		}
		catch (Exception)
		{
			DebugPrint.OutputDebugString(DebugLevel.BuildHYSYSCase, WarningLevel.Warning, string.Format(string.Format(P2Globalization.GetString("SetColumnMethodFailed"), _Data.UID)));
		}
	}

	private void SetColumnMainFlowsheetFeeds()
	{
		_ColumnBD = _Column as BackDoor;
		_MainTSBD = _TraySectionMainTS as BackDoor;
		try
		{
			if (_Data.HYSYSMainFlowsheetFeeds.Count == 0)
			{
				return;
			}
			List<string> list = new List<string>();
			List<string> list2 = new List<string>();
			List<string> list3 = new List<string>();
			List<string> list4 = new List<string>();
			foreach (string hYSYSMainFlowsheetFeed in _Data.HYSYSMainFlowsheetFeeds)
			{
				if (!_Data.ColumnFeeds.Keys.Contains(hYSYSMainFlowsheetFeed))
				{
					continue;
				}
				int num = _Data.ColumnFeeds[hYSYSMainFlowsheetFeed];
				if (num == 1 && _Data.bCondenserExist)
				{
					list.Add(hYSYSMainFlowsheetFeed);
					continue;
				}
				if (num == _Data.stageNumber && _Data.bReboilerExist)
				{
					list2.Add(hYSYSMainFlowsheetFeed);
					continue;
				}
				if (num == 2 && _Data.bCondenserExist)
				{
					list3.Add(hYSYSMainFlowsheetFeed);
				}
				if (num == _Data.stageNumber - 1 && _Data.bReboilerExist)
				{
					list4.Add(hYSYSMainFlowsheetFeed);
				}
				num -= _InternalTrayNumberDiff;
				_AttachNumber = GetAttachedFeedNumber(_Data.UID, _TraySectionMainTS, bSideFeed: false);
				_StreamBD = _Flowsheet.MaterialStreams[hYSYSMainFlowsheetFeed] as BackDoor;
				_ColumnBD.SendAttachObject(":Stream.500." + _AttachNumber, _StreamBD);
				BackDoor backDoor = _TraySectionMainTS.SeparationStages[num - 1] as BackDoor;
				_ColumnBD.SendAttachObject(":SepStage.500." + _AttachNumber, backDoor);
			}
			if (list.Count > 0)
			{
				additionalCondenserFeeds.Add(_Data.UID, list);
			}
			if (list2.Count > 0)
			{
				additionalReboilerFeeds.Add(_Data.UID, list2);
			}
			if (list3.Count > 0)
			{
				overheadFeeds.Add(_Data.UID, list3);
			}
			if (list4.Count > 0)
			{
				bottomFeeds.Add(_Data.UID, list4);
			}
		}
		catch (Exception)
		{
			DebugPrint.OutputDebugString(DebugLevel.BuildHYSYSCase, WarningLevel.Error, string.Format(P2Globalization.GetString("SetFlowSheetFeedsFailed"), _Data.UID));
		}
	}

	private void SetColumnMainFlowsheetProducts()
	{
		_ColumnBD = _Column as BackDoor;
		_MainTSBD = _TraySectionMainTS as BackDoor;
		try
		{
			if (_Data.HYSYSMainFlowsheetProducts.Count == 0)
			{
				return;
			}
			foreach (string hYSYSMainFlowsheetProduct in _Data.HYSYSMainFlowsheetProducts)
			{
				if (!_Data.ColumnProducts.Keys.Contains(hYSYSMainFlowsheetProduct) || !DrawForSideColumn(hYSYSMainFlowsheetProduct))
				{
					continue;
				}
				Dictionary<int, TypeUnitValue> dictionary = _Data.ColumnProducts[hYSYSMainFlowsheetProduct];
				foreach (int key in dictionary.Keys)
				{
					string text = string.Empty;
					string text2 = string.Empty;
					string empty = string.Empty;
					if ((key != 1 || !_Data.bCondenserExist) && (key != _Data.stageNumber || !_Data.bReboilerExist))
					{
						TypeUnitValue typeUnitValue = dictionary[key];
						if (typeUnitValue.Name == "V")
						{
							text = "MaterialStream.506";
							text2 = "SepStage.502";
						}
						else if (typeUnitValue.Name == "L")
						{
							text = "MaterialStream.505";
							text2 = "SepStage.501";
						}
						else if (typeUnitValue.Name == "W")
						{
							text = "MaterialStream.507";
							text2 = "SepStage.503";
						}
						if (key == 1 && typeUnitValue.Name == "V")
						{
							_Column.TopVapourProduct = _Flowsheet.MaterialStreams[hYSYSMainFlowsheetProduct] as ProcessStream;
							continue;
						}
						if (key == _Data.stageNumber && typeUnitValue.Name == "L")
						{
							_Column.BtmLiquidProduct = _Flowsheet.MaterialStreams[hYSYSMainFlowsheetProduct] as ProcessStream;
							continue;
						}
						int num = key - _InternalTrayNumberDiff;
						_MainTSBD = _TraySectionMainTS as BackDoor;
						_AttachNumber = GetAttachedProductNumber(_Data.UID, _TraySectionMainTS, bSideDraw: true, typeUnitValue.Name);
						empty = ":" + text + "." + _AttachNumber;
						ProcessStream processStream = _ColumnFlowsheet.MaterialStreams.Add(hYSYSMainFlowsheetProduct, "") as ProcessStream;
						_MainTSBD.SendAttachObject(empty, processStream);
						BackDoor backDoor = _TraySectionMainTS.SeparationStages[num - 1] as BackDoor;
						empty = ":" + text2 + "." + _AttachNumber;
						_MainTSBD.SendAttachObject(empty, backDoor);
						_AttachNumber = GetAttachedProductNumber(_Data.UID, _TraySectionMainTS, bSideDraw: false, "");
						processStream = _Flowsheet.MaterialStreams[hYSYSMainFlowsheetProduct] as ProcessStream;
						_ColumnBD = _Column as BackDoor;
						_ColumnBD.SendAttachObject(":Stream.511." + (_AttachNumber - 1), processStream);
					}
				}
			}
		}
		catch (Exception)
		{
			DebugPrint.OutputDebugString(DebugLevel.BuildHYSYSCase, WarningLevel.Error, string.Format(P2Globalization.GetString("SetFlowSheetProductsFailed"), _Data.UID));
		}
	}

	private bool DrawForSideColumn(string sName)
	{
		foreach (HYSYSColumn sIDESTRIPPER in _Data.SIDESTRIPPERS)
		{
			if (sIDESTRIPPER.OutletStreams.Contains(sName))
			{
				return false;
			}
		}
		foreach (HYSYSColumn sIDERECTIFIER in _Data.SIDERECTIFIERS)
		{
			if (sIDERECTIFIER.OutletStreams.Contains(sName))
			{
				return false;
			}
		}
		return true;
	}

	private void ConfigureSubFlowsheet()
	{
		if (_Data.bCondenserExist)
		{
			SetCondenser(_Data.UID, bMainTS: true);
		}
		if (_Data.bReboilerExist)
		{
			SetReboiler();
		}
		if (_Data.PA.Count > 0)
		{
			SetPumparound(_Data.UID, bMainTS: true);
		}
		if (_Data.SIDESTRIPPERS.Count > 0)
		{
			SetSideStripper();
		}
		if (_Data.SIDERECTIFIERS.Count > 0)
		{
			SetSideRectifier();
		}
	}

	private void SetCondenser(string columnID, bool bMainTS)
	{
		Condenser condenser = null;
		HYSYSColumn hYSYSColumn = ((!bMainTS) ? SideColumnData[columnID] : _Data);
		try
		{
			if (hYSYSColumn.CondenserType == HYSYSCondenserTypeEnum.FULLREFLUX)
			{
				if (hYSYSColumn.bDrawWaterFromCondenser)
				{
					condenser = _ColumnFlowsheet.get_Operations((object)"Condenser3Op").Add(columnID + "_Condenser", "Condenser3OpObject") as Condenser;
					HysysCondType.Add(columnID, 2);
				}
				else
				{
					condenser = _ColumnFlowsheet.get_Operations((object)"PartialCondenser").Add(columnID + "_Condenser", "PartialCondenserObject") as Condenser;
					HysysCondType.Add(columnID, 1);
				}
			}
			else if (hYSYSColumn.CondenserType == HYSYSCondenserTypeEnum.PARTIAL)
			{
				if (hYSYSColumn.bDrawWaterFromCondenser)
				{
					condenser = _ColumnFlowsheet.get_Operations((object)"Condenser3Op").Add(columnID + "_Condenser", "Condenser3OpObject") as Condenser;
					HysysCondType.Add(columnID, 2);
				}
				else
				{
					condenser = _ColumnFlowsheet.get_Operations((object)"PartialCondenser").Add(columnID + "_Condenser", "PartialCondenserObject") as Condenser;
					HysysCondType.Add(columnID, 1);
				}
			}
			else if (hYSYSColumn.CondenserType == HYSYSCondenserTypeEnum.TOTAL)
			{
				if (hYSYSColumn.bDrawWaterFromCondenser)
				{
					condenser = _ColumnFlowsheet.get_Operations((object)"Condenser3Op").Add(columnID + "_Condenser", "Condenser3OpObject") as Condenser;
					HysysCondType.Add(columnID, 2);
				}
				else
				{
					condenser = _ColumnFlowsheet.get_Operations((object)"TotalCondenser").Add(columnID + "_Condenser", "TotalCondenserObject") as Condenser;
					HysysCondType.Add(columnID, 0);
				}
			}
			List<string> list = new List<string>();
			BackDoor backDoor = condenser as BackDoor;
			SubObjColl.Add(columnID + "_Condenser", backDoor);
			TraySection traySection;
			if (bMainTS)
			{
				traySection = _TraySectionMainTS;
				_Data.conderser = condenser;
			}
			else
			{
				traySection = SideColumnTraySection[columnID];
			}
			BackDoor backDoor2 = traySection as BackDoor;
			ProcessStream processStream = _ColumnFlowsheet.MaterialStreams.Add(columnID + "_ToCon", "") as ProcessStream;
			list.Add(columnID + "_ToCon");
			backDoor2.SendAttachObject(":MaterialStream.503", processStream);
			backDoor.SendAttachObject(":MaterialStream.502.0", processStream);
			processStream = _ColumnFlowsheet.MaterialStreams.Add(columnID + "Reflux", "") as ProcessStream;
			backDoor.SendAttachObject(":MaterialStream.600", processStream);
			backDoor2.SendAttachObject(":MaterialStream.500", processStream);
			foreach (string key in hYSYSColumn.ColumnProducts.Keys)
			{
				Dictionary<int, TypeUnitValue> dictionary = hYSYSColumn.ColumnProducts[key];
				foreach (int key2 in dictionary.Keys)
				{
					if (key2 != 1)
					{
						continue;
					}
					_AttachNumber = GetAttachedProductNumber(columnID, traySection, bSideDraw: false, "");
					processStream = _ColumnFlowsheet.MaterialStreams.Add(key, "") as ProcessStream;
					TypeUnitValue typeUnitValue = dictionary[key2];
					if (typeUnitValue.Name == "V")
					{
						HysysCondVapOutStm.Add(columnID, key);
						backDoor.SendAttachObject(":MaterialStream.501", processStream);
						if (bMainTS)
						{
							ProcessStream topVapourProduct = _Flowsheet.MaterialStreams[key] as ProcessStream;
							_Column.TopVapourProduct = topVapourProduct;
							bCondVDrawExist = true;
						}
					}
					else if (typeUnitValue.Name == "L")
					{
						HysysCondHCOutStm.Add(columnID, key);
						backDoor.SendAttachObject(":MaterialStream.500", processStream);
						if (bMainTS)
						{
							ProcessStream topLiquidProduct = _Flowsheet.MaterialStreams[key] as ProcessStream;
							_Column.TopLiquidProduct = topLiquidProduct;
							bCondLDrawExist = true;
						}
					}
					else if (typeUnitValue.Name == "L1")
					{
						HysysCondHCOutStm.Add(columnID, key);
						backDoor.SendAttachObject(":MaterialStream.500", processStream);
						if (bMainTS)
						{
							ProcessStream topLiquidProduct2 = _Flowsheet.MaterialStreams[key] as ProcessStream;
							_Column.TopLiquidProduct = topLiquidProduct2;
							bCondLDrawExist = true;
						}
					}
					else if (typeUnitValue.Name == "L2" || typeUnitValue.Name == "W")
					{
						HysysCondWaterOutStm.Add(columnID, key);
						backDoor.SendAttachObject(":MaterialStream.710", processStream);
						if (bMainTS)
						{
							ProcessStream topWaterProduct = _Flowsheet.MaterialStreams[key] as ProcessStream;
							_Column.TopWaterProduct = topWaterProduct;
						}
					}
					if (!bMainTS)
					{
						_ColumnBD = _Column as BackDoor;
						_ColumnBD.SendAttachObject(":Stream.511." + _AttachNumber, processStream);
					}
				}
			}
			if (additionalCondenserFeeds.Keys.Contains(columnID))
			{
				foreach (string item in additionalCondenserFeeds[columnID])
				{
					list.Add(item);
					int count = condenser.AttachedFeeds.Count;
					_AttachNumber = GetAttachedFeedNumber(columnID, traySection, bSideFeed: false);
					processStream = _ColumnFlowsheet.MaterialStreams.Add(item, "") as ProcessStream;
					backDoor.SendAttachObject(":MaterialStream.502." + count, processStream);
					processStream = _Flowsheet.MaterialStreams[item] as ProcessStream;
					_ColumnBD = _Column as BackDoor;
					_ColumnBD.SendAttachObject(":Stream.500." + _AttachNumber, processStream);
				}
			}
			HysysCondInletStreams.Add(columnID, list);
			if (overheadFeeds.Keys.Contains(columnID))
			{
				foreach (string item2 in overheadFeeds[columnID])
				{
					_AttachNumber = GetAttachedFeedNumber(columnID, traySection, bSideFeed: true);
					processStream = _ColumnFlowsheet.MaterialStreams[item2] as ProcessStream;
					backDoor2.SendAttachObject(":Stream.504." + _AttachNumber, processStream);
					BackDoor backDoor3 = traySection.SeparationStages[0] as BackDoor;
					backDoor2.SendAttachObject(":SepStage.500." + _AttachNumber, backDoor3);
				}
			}
			_AttachNumber = GetAttachedProductNumber(columnID, traySection, bSideDraw: false, "");
			processStream = _ColumnFlowsheet.EnergyStreams.Add("Q-Condenser_" + columnID, "") as ProcessStream;
			backDoor.SendAttachObject(":EnergyStream.504", processStream);
			if (bMainTS)
			{
				processStream = _Flowsheet.EnergyStreams.Add("Q-Condenser_" + columnID, "") as ProcessStream;
				_ColumnBD = _Column as BackDoor;
				_ColumnBD.SendAttachObject(":Stream.511." + _AttachNumber, processStream);
			}
			else
			{
				_internalProductsNumber++;
			}
			InternalRealVariable internalRealVariable = backDoor.get_BackDoorVariable((object)":PressureDrop.500").Variable as InternalRealVariable;
			if (hYSYSColumn.CondenserPressureDrop != null)
			{
				internalRealVariable.SetValue(hYSYSColumn.CondenserPressureDrop.Value, hYSYSColumn.CondenserPressureDrop.Unit);
			}
			else
			{
				internalRealVariable.Value = -32767.0;
			}
			if (hYSYSColumn.CondenserTemperature != null)
			{
				condenser.SeparationStage.SubCoolTemperature.SetValue(hYSYSColumn.CondenserTemperature.Value, hYSYSColumn.CondenserTemperature.Unit);
			}
			if (hYSYSColumn.CondenserSubcoolDT != null)
			{
				condenser.SeparationStage.SubCoolDeltaTemperature.SetValue(hYSYSColumn.CondenserSubcoolDT.Value, hYSYSColumn.CondenserSubcoolDT.Unit);
			}
			if (hYSYSColumn.CondenserEstimateTemp != null)
			{
				(backDoor.get_BackDoorVariable((object)"SepStage.500:Temperature.300").Variable as InternalRealVariable).SetValue(hYSYSColumn.CondenserEstimateTemp.Value, hYSYSColumn.CondenserEstimateTemp.Unit);
			}
			else if (hYSYSColumn.TempProfileEstimate.Keys.Contains(1))
			{
				DoubleValue doubleValue = hYSYSColumn.TempProfileEstimate[1];
				(backDoor.get_BackDoorVariable((object)"SepStage.500:Temperature.300").Variable as InternalRealVariable).SetValue(doubleValue.Value, doubleValue.Unit);
			}
			if (hYSYSColumn.LiquidFlowProfileEstimate.Keys.Contains(1))
			{
				TypeUnitValue typeUnitValue2 = hYSYSColumn.LiquidFlowProfileEstimate[1];
				(backDoor.get_BackDoorVariable((object)"SepStage.500:ExtraData.303").Variable as InternalRealVariable).SetValue(typeUnitValue2.Value, typeUnitValue2.Unit);
			}
			if (hYSYSColumn.VaporFlowProfileEstimate.Keys.Contains(1))
			{
				TypeUnitValue typeUnitValue3 = hYSYSColumn.VaporFlowProfileEstimate[1];
				(backDoor.get_BackDoorVariable((object)"SepStage.500:ExtraData.300").Variable as InternalRealVariable).SetValue(typeUnitValue3.Value, typeUnitValue3.Unit);
			}
			if (hYSYSColumn.LiqPhaseCompositionProfileEstimate.Keys.Contains(1))
			{
				Dictionary<int, double> dictionary2 = hYSYSColumn.LiqPhaseCompositionProfileEstimate[1];
				foreach (int key3 in dictionary2.Keys)
				{
					string moniker = "SepStage.500:MoleFraction.301." + (key3 - 1);
					double value = dictionary2[key3];
					(backDoor.get_BackDoorVariable((object)moniker).Variable as InternalRealVariable).Value = value;
				}
			}
			if (hYSYSColumn.VapPhaseCompositionProfileEstimate.Keys.Contains(1))
			{
				(_ColumnBD.get_BackDoorVariable((object)"FlowSht.600:Index.500.4").Variable as InternalRealVariable).Value = 0.0;
				Dictionary<int, double> dictionary3 = hYSYSColumn.VapPhaseCompositionProfileEstimate[1];
				foreach (int key4 in dictionary3.Keys)
				{
					string moniker2 = "SepStage.500:MoleFraction.301." + (key4 - 1);
					double value2 = dictionary3[key4];
					(backDoor.get_BackDoorVariable((object)moniker2).Variable as InternalRealVariable).Value = value2;
				}
			}
			int plotPoints = 11;
			int dutyNOByStage = GetDutyNOByStage(hYSYSColumn, 1);
			if (hYSYSColumn.DutyHCurvePlotPoints.Keys.Contains(dutyNOByStage))
			{
				plotPoints = hYSYSColumn.DutyHCurvePlotPoints[dutyNOByStage];
			}
			AddSupportForHCurve(backDoor, bPumpAround: false, plotPoints);
		}
		catch (Exception)
		{
			DebugPrint.OutputDebugString(DebugLevel.BuildHYSYSCase, WarningLevel.Error, string.Format(P2Globalization.GetString("SetCondenserFailed"), columnID));
		}
	}

	private void SetPumparound(string columnID, bool bMainTS)
	{
		int num = 0;
		HYSYSColumn hYSYSColumn;
		TraySection traySection;
		if (bMainTS)
		{
			hYSYSColumn = _Data;
			traySection = _TraySectionMainTS;
		}
		else
		{
			hYSYSColumn = SideColumnData[columnID];
			traySection = SideColumnTraySection[columnID];
		}
		try
		{
			BackDoor backDoor = traySection as BackDoor;
			List<IntValue> list = new List<IntValue>();
			List<IntValue> list2 = new List<IntValue>();
			List<IntValue> list3 = new List<IntValue>();
			List<int> paDutyIDFound = new List<int>();
			foreach (PumpAround item in hYSYSColumn.PA)
			{
				bool flag = false;
				int sourceTrayNo = item.SourceTrayNo;
				int targetTrayNo = item.TargetTrayNo;
				string drawPhase = item.DrawPhase;
				TypeUnitValue dutySpecValueByStage = GetDutySpecValueByStage(hYSYSColumn, item.TargetTrayNo, ref paDutyIDFound);
				double num2 = 0.0;
				if (dutySpecValueByStage != null)
				{
					num2 = dutySpecValueByStage.Value;
				}
				else if (item.PumparoundRate == null && item.ReturnStreamLiqFrac == null && item.ReturnStreamTemp == null && item.ReturnStreamTempChange == null)
				{
					flag = true;
				}
				sourceTrayNo -= _InternalTrayNumberDiff;
				targetTrayNo -= _InternalTrayNumberDiff;
				string empty = string.Empty;
				empty = ((!string.IsNullOrEmpty(item.Name)) ? (columnID + "_PA_" + item.Name) : (columnID + "_PA_" + sourceTrayNo + "_TO_" + targetTrayNo));
				string text = empty + "_Draw_" + item.DrawPhase.ToUpper().Trim();
				string name = empty + "_Return_" + item.DrawPhase.ToUpper().Trim();
				string name2 = empty + "_" + item.DrawPhase.ToUpper().Trim() + "_Q";
				string empty2 = string.Empty;
				string empty3 = string.Empty;
				string empty4 = string.Empty;
				if (drawPhase == "V")
				{
					empty3 = "MaterialStream.506";
					empty4 = "SepStage.502";
				}
				else
				{
					empty3 = "MaterialStream.505";
					empty4 = "SepStage.501";
				}
				int num3 = paDutyIDFound.Last();
				IntValue intValue = new IntValue();
				IntValue intValue2 = new IntValue();
				if (flag)
				{
					_AttachNumber = GetAttachedProductNumber(columnID, traySection, bSideDraw: true, drawPhase);
					ProcessStream processStream = _ColumnFlowsheet.MaterialStreams.Add(text, "") as ProcessStream;
					empty2 = ":" + empty3 + "." + _AttachNumber;
					backDoor.SendAttachObject(empty2, processStream);
					BackDoor backDoor2 = _TraySectionMainTS.SeparationStages[sourceTrayNo - 1] as BackDoor;
					empty2 = ":" + empty4 + "." + _AttachNumber;
					backDoor.SendAttachObject(empty2, backDoor2);
					_AttachNumber = GetAttachedFeedNumber(columnID, traySection, bSideFeed: true);
					empty2 = ":Stream.504." + _AttachNumber;
					backDoor.SendAttachObject(empty2, processStream);
					backDoor2 = _TraySectionMainTS.SeparationStages[targetTrayNo - 1] as BackDoor;
					empty2 = ":SepStage.500." + _AttachNumber;
					backDoor.SendAttachObject(empty2, backDoor2);
				}
				else if (num2 > 0.0 || item.TargetTrayNo > item.SourceTrayNo || (item.TargetTrayNo == item.SourceTrayNo && item.DrawPhase.Contains("L")))
				{
					HeaterOp obj = _ColumnFlowsheet.get_Operations((object)"HeaterOpObject").Add(empty, "HeaterOpObject") as HeaterOp;
					BackDoor backDoor3 = obj as BackDoor;
					obj.PressureDropValue = 0.0;
					_AttachNumber = GetAttachedProductNumber(columnID, traySection, bSideDraw: true, drawPhase);
					ProcessStream processStream = _ColumnFlowsheet.MaterialStreams.Add(text, "") as ProcessStream;
					backDoor3.SendAttachObject(":MaterialStream.400", processStream);
					empty2 = ":" + empty3 + "." + _AttachNumber;
					backDoor.SendAttachObject(empty2, processStream);
					BackDoor backDoor2 = _TraySectionMainTS.SeparationStages[sourceTrayNo - 1] as BackDoor;
					empty2 = ":" + empty4 + "." + _AttachNumber;
					backDoor.SendAttachObject(empty2, backDoor2);
					_AttachNumber = GetAttachedFeedNumber(columnID, traySection, bSideFeed: true);
					processStream = _ColumnFlowsheet.MaterialStreams.Add(name, "") as ProcessStream;
					backDoor3.SendAttachObject(":MaterialStream.401", processStream);
					empty2 = ":Stream.504." + _AttachNumber;
					backDoor.SendAttachObject(empty2, processStream);
					backDoor2 = _TraySectionMainTS.SeparationStages[targetTrayNo - 1] as BackDoor;
					empty2 = ":SepStage.500." + _AttachNumber;
					backDoor.SendAttachObject(empty2, backDoor2);
					processStream = _ColumnFlowsheet.EnergyStreams.Add(name2, "") as ProcessStream;
					backDoor3.SendAttachObject(":EnergyStream.300", processStream);
					_internalFeedsNumber++;
					int plotPoints = 11;
					if (hYSYSColumn.DutyHCurvePlotPoints.Keys.Contains(num3))
					{
						plotPoints = hYSYSColumn.DutyHCurvePlotPoints[num3];
					}
					AddSupportForHCurve(backDoor3, bPumpAround: true, plotPoints);
					IntValue intValue3 = new IntValue();
					intValue3.Name = "HEATER";
					intValue3.Value = num3;
					list.Add(intValue3);
					intValue.Name = text;
					intValue.Value = num3;
					list2.Add(intValue);
					intValue2.Value = num3;
					intValue2.Name = name2;
					list3.Add(intValue2);
				}
				else if (num2 < 0.0 || item.TargetTrayNo < item.SourceTrayNo || (item.TargetTrayNo == item.SourceTrayNo && item.DrawPhase.Contains("V")))
				{
					CoolerOp obj2 = _ColumnFlowsheet.get_Operations((object)"CoolerOpObject").Add(empty, "CoolerOpObject") as CoolerOp;
					BackDoor backDoor4 = obj2 as BackDoor;
					obj2.PressureDropValue = 0.0;
					_AttachNumber = GetAttachedProductNumber(columnID, traySection, bSideDraw: true, drawPhase);
					ProcessStream processStream = _ColumnFlowsheet.MaterialStreams.Add(text, "") as ProcessStream;
					backDoor4.SendAttachObject(":MaterialStream.400", processStream);
					empty2 = ":" + empty3 + "." + _AttachNumber;
					backDoor.SendAttachObject(empty2, processStream);
					BackDoor backDoor2 = _TraySectionMainTS.SeparationStages[sourceTrayNo - 1] as BackDoor;
					empty2 = ":" + empty4 + "." + _AttachNumber;
					backDoor.SendAttachObject(empty2, backDoor2);
					_AttachNumber = GetAttachedFeedNumber(columnID, traySection, bSideFeed: true);
					processStream = _ColumnFlowsheet.MaterialStreams.Add(name, "") as ProcessStream;
					backDoor4.SendAttachObject(":MaterialStream.401", processStream);
					empty2 = ":Stream.504." + _AttachNumber;
					backDoor.SendAttachObject(empty2, processStream);
					backDoor2 = _TraySectionMainTS.SeparationStages[targetTrayNo - 1] as BackDoor;
					empty2 = ":SepStage.500." + _AttachNumber;
					backDoor.SendAttachObject(empty2, backDoor2);
					processStream = _ColumnFlowsheet.EnergyStreams.Add(name2, "") as ProcessStream;
					backDoor4.SendAttachObject(":EnergyStream.300", processStream);
					_internalProductsNumber++;
					int plotPoints2 = 11;
					if (hYSYSColumn.DutyHCurvePlotPoints.Keys.Contains(num3))
					{
						plotPoints2 = hYSYSColumn.DutyHCurvePlotPoints[num3];
					}
					AddSupportForHCurve(backDoor4, bPumpAround: true, plotPoints2);
					IntValue intValue4 = new IntValue();
					intValue4.Name = "COOLER";
					intValue4.Value = num3;
					list.Add(intValue4);
					intValue.Name = text;
					intValue.Value = num3;
					list2.Add(intValue);
					intValue2.Value = num3;
					intValue2.Name = name2;
					list3.Add(intValue2);
				}
				ColumnUserPumpAround obj3 = _ColumnFlowsheet.LiquidPumpArounds[num] as ColumnUserPumpAround;
				obj3.name = empty;
				BackDoor value = obj3 as BackDoor;
				SubObjColl.Add(empty, value);
				if (!_Data.paColl.Keys.Contains(num3.ToString()))
				{
					_Data.paColl.Add(num3.ToString(), value);
				}
				string exportStmName = string.Empty;
				if (PANeedsToExport(hYSYSColumn, item.SourceTrayNo, item.TargetTrayNo, drawPhase, ref exportStmName) && !ExportStmAndPANamePair.Keys.Contains(exportStmName))
				{
					ExportStmAndPANamePair.Add(exportStmName, empty);
					ExportStmAndPADrawNamePair.Add(exportStmName, text);
				}
				num++;
				paDic.Add(num, item);
			}
			if (list.Count > 0)
			{
				HysysSideExchangerType.Add(columnID, list);
			}
			if (list2.Count > 0)
			{
				HysysSideExchangerDrawStm.Add(columnID, list2);
			}
			if (list3.Count > 0)
			{
				HysysSideExchangerEnergyStm.Add(columnID, list3);
			}
		}
		catch (Exception)
		{
			DebugPrint.OutputDebugString(DebugLevel.BuildHYSYSCase, WarningLevel.Error, string.Format(P2Globalization.GetString("SetPumparoundFailed"), _Data.UID));
		}
	}

	private void SetSideStripper()
	{
		int num = 0;
		foreach (HYSYSColumn sIDESTRIPPER in _Data.SIDESTRIPPERS)
		{
			string uID = sIDESTRIPPER.UID;
			try
			{
				TraySection traySection = _ColumnFlowsheet.get_Operations((object)"TraySection").Add(uID, "TraySectionObject") as TraySection;
				BackDoor backDoor = traySection as BackDoor;
				sideTraySecBDColl.Add(uID, backDoor);
				(backDoor.get_BackDoorVariable((object)":ExtraData.500.1").Variable as InternalRealVariable).Value = 1.0;
				SideColumnData.Add(uID, sIDESTRIPPER);
				SideColumnTraySection.Add(uID, traySection);
				AddStripperOrRectifier(uID, bStripper: true);
				if (_ColumnFlowsheet.SideStrippers.Count - num == 1)
				{
					(_ColumnFlowsheet.SideStrippers[num] as ColumnUserSideStripper).name = uID;
					num++;
				}
			}
			catch (Exception)
			{
				DebugPrint.OutputDebugString(DebugLevel.BuildHYSYSCase, WarningLevel.Error, string.Format(P2Globalization.GetString("SetSideStripperFailed"), uID, _Data.UID));
			}
		}
	}

	private void SetSideRectifier()
	{
		int num = 0;
		foreach (HYSYSColumn sIDERECTIFIER in _Data.SIDERECTIFIERS)
		{
			string uID = sIDERECTIFIER.UID;
			try
			{
				TraySection traySection = _ColumnFlowsheet.get_Operations((object)"TraySection").Add(uID, "TraySectionObject") as TraySection;
				BackDoor backDoor = traySection as BackDoor;
				sideTraySecBDColl.Add(uID, backDoor);
				(backDoor.get_BackDoorVariable((object)":ExtraData.500.1").Variable as InternalRealVariable).Value = 2.0;
				SideColumnData.Add(uID, sIDERECTIFIER);
				SideColumnTraySection.Add(uID, traySection);
				AddStripperOrRectifier(uID, bStripper: false);
				if (_ColumnFlowsheet.SideRectifiers.Count - num == 1)
				{
					(_ColumnFlowsheet.SideRectifiers[num] as ColumnUserSideRectifier).name = uID;
					num++;
				}
			}
			catch (Exception)
			{
				DebugPrint.OutputDebugString(DebugLevel.BuildHYSYSCase, WarningLevel.Error, string.Format(P2Globalization.GetString("SetSideRectifierFailed"), uID, _Data.UID));
			}
		}
	}

	private void AddStripperOrRectifier(string columnID, bool bStripper)
	{
		SetTrayNumber(columnID, bMainTray: false);
		if (bStripper && SideColumnData[columnID].bReboilerExist)
		{
			SetKettleReboiler(columnID, bMainTS: false);
		}
		else if (SideColumnData[columnID].bCondenserExist)
		{
			SetCondenser(columnID, bMainTS: false);
		}
		SetSideColumnFeeds(columnID);
		SetSideColumnProducts(columnID);
	}

	private void SetReboiler()
	{
		try
		{
			if (_Data.ReboilerType != HYSYSReboilerTypeEnum.KETTLE)
			{
				System.Windows.MessageBox.Show(string.Format(P2Globalization.GetString("ThermosyphonRebConvertMsg"), _Data.UID), P2Globalization.GetString("P2Convert"), MessageBoxButton.OK, MessageBoxImage.Exclamation, MessageBoxResult.OK, System.Windows.MessageBoxOptions.DefaultDesktopOnly);
			}
			DebugPrint.OutputDebugString(DebugLevel.BuildHYSYSCase, WarningLevel.Notes, string.Format(P2Globalization.GetString("ThermosyphonRebConvertMsg"), _Data.UID));
			SetKettleReboiler(_Data.UID, bMainTS: true);
		}
		catch (Exception)
		{
			DebugPrint.OutputDebugString(DebugLevel.BuildHYSYSCase, WarningLevel.Error, string.Format(P2Globalization.GetString("SetReboilerFailed"), _Data.UID));
		}
	}

	private void SetKettleReboiler(string columnID, bool bMainTS)
	{
		Reboiler reboiler = _ColumnFlowsheet.get_Operations((object)"BPReboiler").Add(columnID + "_Reboiler", "BPReboilerObject") as Reboiler;
		BackDoor backDoor = reboiler as BackDoor;
		SubObjColl.Add(columnID + "_Reboiler", backDoor);
		HYSYSColumn hYSYSColumn;
		TraySection traySection;
		int num;
		if (bMainTS)
		{
			hYSYSColumn = _Data;
			traySection = _TraySectionMainTS;
			num = _InternalTrayNumberDiff;
			_Data.reboiler = reboiler;
		}
		else
		{
			hYSYSColumn = SideColumnData[columnID];
			traySection = SideColumnTraySection[columnID];
			num = SideColumnTrayDiff[columnID];
		}
		BackDoor backDoor2 = traySection as BackDoor;
		List<string> list = new List<string>();
		ProcessStream processStream = _ColumnFlowsheet.MaterialStreams.Add(columnID + "_ToReb", "") as ProcessStream;
		list.Add(columnID + "_ToReb");
		BackDoor backDoor3 = processStream as BackDoor;
		backDoor2.SendAttachObject(":MaterialStream.502", backDoor3);
		backDoor.SendAttachObject(":MaterialStream.502.0", backDoor3);
		processStream = _ColumnFlowsheet.MaterialStreams.Add(columnID + "_Boilup", "") as ProcessStream;
		backDoor3 = processStream as BackDoor;
		backDoor2.SendAttachObject(":MaterialStream.501", backDoor3);
		backDoor.SendAttachObject(":MaterialStream.501", backDoor3);
		foreach (string key in hYSYSColumn.ColumnProducts.Keys)
		{
			if (!bMainTS && _Data.ColumnFeeds.Keys.Contains(key))
			{
				continue;
			}
			Dictionary<int, TypeUnitValue> dictionary = hYSYSColumn.ColumnProducts[key];
			foreach (int key2 in dictionary.Keys)
			{
				if (key2 != hYSYSColumn.stageNumber)
				{
					continue;
				}
				string text = dictionary[key2].Name.Trim();
				_AttachNumber = GetAttachedProductNumber(_Data.UID, _TraySectionMainTS, bSideDraw: false, text);
				processStream = _ColumnFlowsheet.MaterialStreams.Add(key, "") as ProcessStream;
				if (text == "L")
				{
					backDoor.SendAttachObject(":MaterialStream.500", processStream);
					HysysRebLiqProdStm.Add(columnID, key);
				}
				else if (text == "V")
				{
					backDoor.SendAttachObject(":MaterialStream.600", processStream);
					HysysRebVapProdStm.Add(columnID, key);
				}
				if (!(_Flowsheet.MaterialStreams[key] is ProcessStream processStream2))
				{
					continue;
				}
				_ColumnBD = _Column as BackDoor;
				if (bMainTS)
				{
					if (text == "L")
					{
						_Column.BtmLiquidProduct = processStream2;
					}
					else if (text == "V")
					{
						_ColumnBD.SendAttachObject(":Stream.511." + _AttachNumber, processStream2);
					}
				}
				else
				{
					_ColumnBD.SendAttachObject(":Stream.511." + _AttachNumber, processStream2);
				}
			}
		}
		if (additionalReboilerFeeds.Keys.Contains(columnID))
		{
			foreach (string item in additionalReboilerFeeds[columnID])
			{
				list.Add(item);
				int count = reboiler.AttachedFeeds.Count;
				_AttachNumber = GetAttachedFeedNumber(columnID, traySection, bSideFeed: false);
				processStream = _ColumnFlowsheet.MaterialStreams.Add(item, "") as ProcessStream;
				backDoor.SendAttachObject(":MaterialStream.502." + count, processStream);
				processStream = _Flowsheet.MaterialStreams[item] as ProcessStream;
				_ColumnBD = _Column as BackDoor;
				_ColumnBD.SendAttachObject(":Stream.500." + _AttachNumber, processStream);
			}
		}
		HysysRebInletStreams.Add(columnID, list);
		if (bottomFeeds.Keys.Contains(columnID))
		{
			foreach (string item2 in bottomFeeds[columnID])
			{
				_AttachNumber = GetAttachedFeedNumber(columnID, traySection, bSideFeed: true);
				processStream = _ColumnFlowsheet.MaterialStreams[item2] as ProcessStream;
				backDoor2.SendAttachObject(":Stream.504." + _AttachNumber, processStream);
				BackDoor backDoor4 = traySection.SeparationStages[hYSYSColumn.stageNumber - num - 2] as BackDoor;
				backDoor2.SendAttachObject(":SepStage.500." + _AttachNumber, backDoor4);
			}
		}
		_AttachNumber = GetAttachedFeedNumber(columnID, traySection, bSideFeed: false);
		processStream = _ColumnFlowsheet.EnergyStreams.Add(columnID + "_Reb_Q", "") as ProcessStream;
		backDoor3 = processStream as BackDoor;
		backDoor.SendAttachObject(":EnergyStream.503", backDoor3);
		bReboilerQExist = true;
		if (bMainTS)
		{
			processStream = _Flowsheet.EnergyStreams.Add(columnID + "_Reb_Q", "") as ProcessStream;
			_ColumnBD = _Column as BackDoor;
			_ColumnBD.SendAttachObject(":Stream.500." + _AttachNumber, processStream);
		}
		else
		{
			_internalFeedsNumber++;
		}
		if (hYSYSColumn.ReboilerPressureDrop != null)
		{
			(backDoor.get_BackDoorVariable((object)":PressureDrop.500").Variable as InternalRealVariable).SetValue(hYSYSColumn.ReboilerPressureDrop.Value, hYSYSColumn.ReboilerPressureDrop.Unit);
		}
		if (hYSYSColumn.TempProfileEstimate.Keys.Contains(hYSYSColumn.stageNumber))
		{
			DoubleValue doubleValue = hYSYSColumn.TempProfileEstimate[hYSYSColumn.stageNumber];
			(backDoor.get_BackDoorVariable((object)"SepStage.500:Temperature.300").Variable as InternalRealVariable).SetValue(doubleValue.Value, doubleValue.Unit);
		}
		if (hYSYSColumn.LiquidFlowProfileEstimate.Keys.Contains(hYSYSColumn.stageNumber))
		{
			TypeUnitValue typeUnitValue = hYSYSColumn.LiquidFlowProfileEstimate[hYSYSColumn.stageNumber];
			(backDoor.get_BackDoorVariable((object)"SepStage.500:ExtraData.303").Variable as InternalRealVariable).SetValue(typeUnitValue.Value, typeUnitValue.Unit);
		}
		if (hYSYSColumn.VaporFlowProfileEstimate.Keys.Contains(hYSYSColumn.stageNumber))
		{
			TypeUnitValue typeUnitValue2 = hYSYSColumn.VaporFlowProfileEstimate[hYSYSColumn.stageNumber];
			(backDoor.get_BackDoorVariable((object)"SepStage.500:ExtraData.300").Variable as InternalRealVariable).SetValue(typeUnitValue2.Value, typeUnitValue2.Unit);
		}
		if (hYSYSColumn.LiqPhaseCompositionProfileEstimate.Keys.Contains(hYSYSColumn.stageNumber))
		{
			Dictionary<int, double> dictionary2 = hYSYSColumn.LiqPhaseCompositionProfileEstimate[hYSYSColumn.stageNumber];
			foreach (int key3 in dictionary2.Keys)
			{
				string moniker = "SepStage.500:MoleFraction.301." + (key3 - 1);
				double value = dictionary2[key3];
				(backDoor.get_BackDoorVariable((object)moniker).Variable as InternalRealVariable).Value = value;
			}
		}
		if (hYSYSColumn.VapPhaseCompositionProfileEstimate.Keys.Contains(hYSYSColumn.stageNumber))
		{
			(_ColumnBD.get_BackDoorVariable((object)"FlowSht.600:Index.500.4").Variable as InternalRealVariable).Value = 0.0;
			Dictionary<int, double> dictionary3 = hYSYSColumn.VapPhaseCompositionProfileEstimate[hYSYSColumn.stageNumber];
			foreach (int key4 in dictionary3.Keys)
			{
				string moniker2 = "SepStage.500:MoleFraction.301." + (key4 - 1);
				double value2 = dictionary3[key4];
				(backDoor.get_BackDoorVariable((object)moniker2).Variable as InternalRealVariable).Value = value2;
			}
		}
		int plotPoints = 11;
		int dutyNOByStage = GetDutyNOByStage(hYSYSColumn, hYSYSColumn.stageNumber);
		if (hYSYSColumn.DutyHCurvePlotPoints.Keys.Contains(dutyNOByStage))
		{
			plotPoints = hYSYSColumn.DutyHCurvePlotPoints[dutyNOByStage];
		}
		AddSupportForHCurve(backDoor, bPumpAround: false, plotPoints);
	}

	private void SetThermosReboilerWithBaffle()
	{
		BackDoor backDoor = (_ColumnFlowsheet.get_Operations((object)"HeaterOpObject").Add(_Data.UID + "_Reboiler", "HeaterOpObject") as HeaterOp) as BackDoor;
		SubObjColl.Add(_Data.UID + "_Reboiler", backDoor);
		_MainTSBD = _TraySectionMainTS as BackDoor;
		BackDoor backDoor2 = (_ColumnFlowsheet.get_Operations((object)"FlashTankObject").Add("Flsh", "FlashTankObject") as Separator) as BackDoor;
		SubObjColl.Add("Flsh", backDoor2);
		Separator separator = _ColumnFlowsheet.get_Operations((object)"FlashTankObject").Add("Sump", "FlashTankObject") as Separator;
		BackDoor backDoor3 = separator as BackDoor;
		SubObjColl.Add("Sump", backDoor3);
		BackDoor backDoor4 = (_ColumnFlowsheet.get_Operations((object)"MixerOpObject").Add("MixBoilup", "MixerOpObject") as MixerOp) as BackDoor;
		SubObjColl.Add("MixBoilup", backDoor4);
		BackDoor backDoor5 = (_ColumnFlowsheet.get_Operations((object)"TeeOpObject").Add("BtmSplit", "TeeOpObject") as TeeOp) as BackDoor;
		SubObjColl.Add("BtmSplit", backDoor5);
		ProcessStream processStream = _ColumnFlowsheet.MaterialStreams.Add(_Data.UID + "_ToReb", "") as ProcessStream;
		BackDoor backDoor6 = processStream as BackDoor;
		_MainTSBD.SendAttachObject(":MaterialStream.502", backDoor6);
		backDoor3.SendAttachObject(":MaterialStream.502.0", backDoor6);
		processStream = _ColumnFlowsheet.MaterialStreams.Add("ToSump", "") as ProcessStream;
		backDoor6 = processStream as BackDoor;
		backDoor5.SendAttachObject(":MaterialStream.501.0", backDoor6);
		backDoor3.SendAttachObject(":MaterialStream.502.1", backDoor6);
		processStream = _ColumnFlowsheet.MaterialStreams.Add("ToTee", "") as ProcessStream;
		backDoor6 = processStream as BackDoor;
		backDoor5.SendAttachObject(":MaterialStream.500", backDoor6);
		backDoor2.SendAttachObject(":MaterialStream.500", backDoor6);
		processStream = _ColumnFlowsheet.MaterialStreams.Add("ToReb", "") as ProcessStream;
		backDoor6 = processStream as BackDoor;
		backDoor3.SendAttachObject(":MaterialStream.500", backDoor6);
		backDoor.SendAttachObject(":MaterialStream.400", backDoor6);
		processStream = _ColumnFlowsheet.MaterialStreams.Add("ToFlsh", "") as ProcessStream;
		backDoor6 = processStream as BackDoor;
		backDoor2.SendAttachObject(":MaterialStream.502.0", backDoor6);
		backDoor.SendAttachObject(":MaterialStream.401", backDoor6);
		processStream = _ColumnFlowsheet.MaterialStreams.Add("FlshV", "") as ProcessStream;
		backDoor6 = processStream as BackDoor;
		backDoor2.SendAttachObject(":MaterialStream.501", backDoor6);
		backDoor4.SendAttachObject(":Stream.501.0", backDoor6);
		processStream = _ColumnFlowsheet.MaterialStreams.Add("SumpV", "") as ProcessStream;
		backDoor6 = processStream as BackDoor;
		backDoor3.SendAttachObject(":MaterialStream.501", backDoor6);
		backDoor4.SendAttachObject(":Stream.501.1", backDoor6);
		processStream = _ColumnFlowsheet.MaterialStreams.Add(_Data.UID + "_Boilup", "") as ProcessStream;
		backDoor6 = processStream as BackDoor;
		backDoor4.SendAttachObject(":Stream.500", backDoor6);
		_MainTSBD.SendAttachObject(":MaterialStream.501", backDoor6);
		_AttachNumber = GetAttachedFeedNumber(_Data.UID, _TraySectionMainTS, bSideFeed: false);
		processStream = _ColumnFlowsheet.EnergyStreams.Add(_Data.UID + "_Reb_Q", "") as ProcessStream;
		backDoor6 = processStream as BackDoor;
		backDoor.SendAttachObject(":EnergyStream.300", backDoor6);
		processStream = _Flowsheet.EnergyStreams.Add(_Data.UID + "_Reb_Q", "") as ProcessStream;
		_ColumnBD = _Column as BackDoor;
		_ColumnBD.SendAttachObject(":Stream.500." + _AttachNumber, processStream);
		foreach (string key in _Data.ColumnProducts.Keys)
		{
			foreach (int key2 in _Data.ColumnProducts[key].Keys)
			{
				if (key2 == _Data.stageNumber)
				{
					_AttachNumber = GetAttachedProductNumber(_Data.UID, _TraySectionMainTS, bSideDraw: false, "");
					processStream = _ColumnFlowsheet.MaterialStreams.Add(key, "") as ProcessStream;
					backDoor5.SendAttachObject(":MaterialStream.501.1", processStream);
					processStream = _Flowsheet.MaterialStreams[key] as ProcessStream;
					_ColumnBD = _Column as BackDoor;
					_ColumnBD.SendAttachObject(":Stream.511." + _AttachNumber, processStream);
				}
			}
		}
		if (additionalReboilerFeeds.Keys.Contains(_Data.UID))
		{
			foreach (string item in additionalReboilerFeeds[_Data.UID])
			{
				int count = separator.AttachedFeeds.Count;
				_AttachNumber = GetAttachedFeedNumber(_Data.UID, _TraySectionMainTS, bSideFeed: false);
				processStream = _ColumnFlowsheet.MaterialStreams.Add(item, "") as ProcessStream;
				backDoor3.SendAttachObject(":MaterialStream.502." + count, processStream);
				processStream = _Flowsheet.MaterialStreams[item] as ProcessStream;
				_ColumnBD = _Column as BackDoor;
				_ColumnBD.SendAttachObject(":Stream.500." + _AttachNumber, processStream);
			}
		}
		if (bottomFeeds.Keys.Contains(_Data.UID))
		{
			foreach (string item2 in bottomFeeds[_Data.UID])
			{
				_AttachNumber = GetAttachedFeedNumber(_Data.UID, _TraySectionMainTS, bSideFeed: true);
				processStream = _ColumnFlowsheet.MaterialStreams[item2] as ProcessStream;
				_MainTSBD.SendAttachObject(":Stream.504." + _AttachNumber, processStream);
				BackDoor backDoor7 = _TraySectionMainTS.SeparationStages[_Data.stageNumber - _InternalTrayNumberDiff - 1] as BackDoor;
				_MainTSBD.SendAttachObject(":SepStage.500." + _AttachNumber, backDoor7);
			}
		}
		if (_Data.TempProfileEstimate.Keys.Contains(_Data.stageNumber))
		{
			DoubleValue doubleValue = _Data.TempProfileEstimate[_Data.stageNumber];
			(backDoor2.get_BackDoorVariable((object)"SepStage.500:Temperature.300").Variable as InternalRealVariable).SetValue(doubleValue.Value, doubleValue.Unit);
		}
		if (_Data.LiquidFlowProfileEstimate.Keys.Contains(_Data.stageNumber))
		{
			TypeUnitValue typeUnitValue = _Data.LiquidFlowProfileEstimate[_Data.stageNumber];
