			InternalRealVariable internalRealVariable = _ColumnBD.get_BackDoorVariable((object)"FlowSht.600:ExtraData.500.20").Variable as InternalRealVariable;
			if (typeUnitValue.Type == "M")
			{
				internalRealVariable.Value = 3.0;
			}
			else if (typeUnitValue.Type == "WT")
			{
				internalRealVariable.Value = 4.0;
			}
			else if (typeUnitValue.Type == "LV")
			{
				internalRealVariable.Value = 5.0;
			}
			else if (typeUnitValue.Type == "GV")
			{
				internalRealVariable.Value = 1023.0;
			}
			(backDoor2.get_BackDoorVariable((object)"SepStage.500:ExtraData.303").Variable as InternalRealVariable).SetValue(typeUnitValue.Value, typeUnitValue.Unit);
		}
		if (_Data.VaporFlowProfileEstimate.Keys.Contains(_Data.stageNumber))
		{
			TypeUnitValue typeUnitValue2 = _Data.VaporFlowProfileEstimate[_Data.stageNumber];
			InternalRealVariable internalRealVariable2 = _ColumnBD.get_BackDoorVariable((object)"FlowSht.600:ExtraData.500.20").Variable as InternalRealVariable;
			if (typeUnitValue2.Type == "M")
			{
				internalRealVariable2.Value = 3.0;
			}
			else if (typeUnitValue2.Type == "WT")
			{
				internalRealVariable2.Value = 4.0;
			}
			else if (typeUnitValue2.Type == "LV")
			{
				internalRealVariable2.Value = 5.0;
			}
			else if (typeUnitValue2.Type == "GV")
			{
				internalRealVariable2.Value = 1023.0;
			}
			(backDoor2.get_BackDoorVariable((object)"SepStage.500:ExtraData.300").Variable as InternalRealVariable).SetValue(typeUnitValue2.Value, typeUnitValue2.Unit);
		}
		if (_Data.LiqPhaseCompositionProfileEstimate.Keys.Contains(_Data.stageNumber))
		{
			Dictionary<int, double> dictionary = _Data.LiqPhaseCompositionProfileEstimate[_Data.stageNumber];
			foreach (int key3 in dictionary.Keys)
			{
				string moniker = "SepStage.500:MoleFraction.301." + (key3 - 1);
				double value = dictionary[key3];
				(backDoor2.get_BackDoorVariable((object)moniker).Variable as InternalRealVariable).Value = value;
			}
		}
		if (!_Data.VapPhaseCompositionProfileEstimate.Keys.Contains(_Data.stageNumber))
		{
			return;
		}
		(_ColumnBD.get_BackDoorVariable((object)"FlowSht.600:Index.500.4").Variable as InternalRealVariable).Value = 0.0;
		Dictionary<int, double> dictionary2 = _Data.VapPhaseCompositionProfileEstimate[_Data.stageNumber];
		foreach (int key4 in dictionary2.Keys)
		{
			string moniker2 = "SepStage.500:MoleFraction.301." + (key4 - 1);
			double value2 = dictionary2[key4];
			(backDoor2.get_BackDoorVariable((object)moniker2).Variable as InternalRealVariable).Value = value2;
		}
	}

	private void SetThermosReboilerWithoutBaffle()
	{
		BackDoor backDoor = (_ColumnFlowsheet.get_Operations((object)"HeaterOpObject").Add(_Data.UID + "_Reboiler", "HeaterOpObject") as HeaterOp) as BackDoor;
		SubObjColl.Add(_Data.UID + "_Reboiler", backDoor);
		_MainTSBD = _TraySectionMainTS as BackDoor;
		BackDoor backDoor2 = (_ColumnFlowsheet.get_Operations((object)"FlashTankObject").Add("Flsh", "FlashTankObject") as Separator) as BackDoor;
		SubObjColl.Add("Flsh", backDoor2);
		Separator separator = _ColumnFlowsheet.get_Operations((object)"FlashTankObject").Add("Sump", "FlashTankObject") as Separator;
		BackDoor backDoor3 = separator as BackDoor;
		SubObjColl.Add("Sump", backDoor3);
		BackDoor backDoor4 = (_ColumnFlowsheet.get_Operations((object)"MixerOpObject").Add("MixBoilup", "MixerOpObject") as MixerOp) as BackDoor;
		SubObjColl.Add("MixBoilup", backDoor4);
		BackDoor backDoor5 = (_ColumnFlowsheet.get_Operations((object)"TeeOpObject").Add("BtmSplit", "TeeOpObject") as TeeOp) as BackDoor;
		SubObjColl.Add("BtmSplit", backDoor5);
		ProcessStream processStream = _ColumnFlowsheet.MaterialStreams.Add(_Data.UID + "_ToReb", "") as ProcessStream;
		_StreamBD = processStream as BackDoor;
		_MainTSBD.SendAttachObject(":MaterialStream.502", _StreamBD);
		backDoor3.SendAttachObject(":MaterialStream.502.0", _StreamBD);
		processStream = _ColumnFlowsheet.MaterialStreams.Add("ToTee", "") as ProcessStream;
		_StreamBD = processStream as BackDoor;
		backDoor5.SendAttachObject(":MaterialStream.500", _StreamBD);
		backDoor3.SendAttachObject(":MaterialStream.500", _StreamBD);
		processStream = _ColumnFlowsheet.MaterialStreams.Add("ToReb", "") as ProcessStream;
		_StreamBD = processStream as BackDoor;
		backDoor5.SendAttachObject(":MaterialStream.501.0", _StreamBD);
		backDoor.SendAttachObject(":MaterialStream.400", _StreamBD);
		processStream = _ColumnFlowsheet.MaterialStreams.Add("ToFlsh", "") as ProcessStream;
		_StreamBD = processStream as BackDoor;
		backDoor2.SendAttachObject(":MaterialStream.502.0", _StreamBD);
		backDoor.SendAttachObject(":MaterialStream.401", _StreamBD);
		processStream = _ColumnFlowsheet.MaterialStreams.Add("FlshV", "") as ProcessStream;
		_StreamBD = processStream as BackDoor;
		backDoor2.SendAttachObject(":MaterialStream.501", _StreamBD);
		backDoor4.SendAttachObject(":Stream.501.0", _StreamBD);
		processStream = _ColumnFlowsheet.MaterialStreams.Add("SumpV", "") as ProcessStream;
		_StreamBD = processStream as BackDoor;
		backDoor3.SendAttachObject(":MaterialStream.501", _StreamBD);
		backDoor4.SendAttachObject(":Stream.501.1", _StreamBD);
		processStream = _ColumnFlowsheet.MaterialStreams.Add("ToSump", "") as ProcessStream;
		_StreamBD = processStream as BackDoor;
		backDoor2.SendAttachObject(":MaterialStream.500", _StreamBD);
		backDoor3.SendAttachObject(":MaterialStream.502.1", _StreamBD);
		processStream = _ColumnFlowsheet.MaterialStreams.Add(_Data.UID + "_Boilup", "") as ProcessStream;
		_StreamBD = processStream as BackDoor;
		backDoor4.SendAttachObject(":Stream.500", _StreamBD);
		_MainTSBD.SendAttachObject(":MaterialStream.501", _StreamBD);
		_AttachNumber = GetAttachedFeedNumber(_Data.UID, _TraySectionMainTS, bSideFeed: false);
		processStream = _ColumnFlowsheet.EnergyStreams.Add(_Data.UID + "_Reb_Q", "") as ProcessStream;
		_StreamBD = processStream as BackDoor;
		backDoor.SendAttachObject(":EnergyStream.300", _StreamBD);
		processStream = _Flowsheet.EnergyStreams.Add(_Data.UID + "_Reb_Q", "") as ProcessStream;
		_ColumnBD = _Column as BackDoor;
		_ColumnBD.SendAttachObject(":Stream.500." + _AttachNumber, processStream);
		foreach (string key in _Data.ColumnProducts.Keys)
		{
			foreach (int key2 in _Data.ColumnProducts[key].Keys)
			{
				if (key2 == _Data.stageNumber)
				{
					_AttachNumber = GetAttachedProductNumber(_Data.UID, _TraySectionMainTS, bSideDraw: false, "");
					processStream = _ColumnFlowsheet.MaterialStreams.Add(key, "") as ProcessStream;
					backDoor5.SendAttachObject(":MaterialStream.501.1", processStream);
					processStream = _Flowsheet.MaterialStreams[key] as ProcessStream;
					_ColumnBD = _Column as BackDoor;
					_ColumnBD.SendAttachObject(":Stream.511." + _AttachNumber, processStream);
				}
			}
		}
		if (additionalReboilerFeeds.Keys.Contains(_Data.UID))
		{
			foreach (string item in additionalReboilerFeeds[_Data.UID])
			{
				int count = separator.AttachedFeeds.Count;
				_AttachNumber = GetAttachedFeedNumber(_Data.UID, _TraySectionMainTS, bSideFeed: false);
				processStream = _ColumnFlowsheet.MaterialStreams.Add(item, "") as ProcessStream;
				backDoor3.SendAttachObject(":MaterialStream.502." + count, processStream);
				processStream = _Flowsheet.MaterialStreams[item] as ProcessStream;
				_ColumnBD = _Column as BackDoor;
				_ColumnBD.SendAttachObject(":Stream.500." + _AttachNumber, processStream);
			}
		}
		if (bottomFeeds.Keys.Contains(_Data.UID))
		{
			foreach (string item2 in bottomFeeds[_Data.UID])
			{
				_AttachNumber = GetAttachedFeedNumber(_Data.UID, _TraySectionMainTS, bSideFeed: true);
				processStream = _ColumnFlowsheet.MaterialStreams[item2] as ProcessStream;
				_MainTSBD.SendAttachObject(":Stream.504." + _AttachNumber, processStream);
				BackDoor backDoor6 = _TraySectionMainTS.SeparationStages[_Data.stageNumber - _InternalTrayNumberDiff - 1] as BackDoor;
				_MainTSBD.SendAttachObject(":SepStage.500." + _AttachNumber, backDoor6);
			}
		}
		if (_Data.TempProfileEstimate.Keys.Contains(_Data.stageNumber))
		{
			DoubleValue doubleValue = _Data.TempProfileEstimate[_Data.stageNumber];
			(backDoor2.get_BackDoorVariable((object)"SepStage.500:Temperature.300").Variable as InternalRealVariable).SetValue(doubleValue.Value, doubleValue.Unit);
		}
		if (_Data.LiquidFlowProfileEstimate.Keys.Contains(_Data.stageNumber))
		{
			TypeUnitValue typeUnitValue = _Data.LiquidFlowProfileEstimate[_Data.stageNumber];
			InternalRealVariable internalRealVariable = _ColumnBD.get_BackDoorVariable((object)"FlowSht.600:ExtraData.500.20").Variable as InternalRealVariable;
			if (typeUnitValue.Type == "M")
			{
				internalRealVariable.Value = 3.0;
			}
			else if (typeUnitValue.Type == "WT")
			{
				internalRealVariable.Value = 4.0;
			}
			else if (typeUnitValue.Type == "LV")
			{
				internalRealVariable.Value = 5.0;
			}
			else if (typeUnitValue.Type == "GV")
			{
				internalRealVariable.Value = 1023.0;
			}
			(backDoor2.get_BackDoorVariable((object)"SepStage.500:ExtraData.303").Variable as InternalRealVariable).SetValue(typeUnitValue.Value, typeUnitValue.Unit);
		}
		if (_Data.VaporFlowProfileEstimate.Keys.Contains(_Data.stageNumber))
		{
			TypeUnitValue typeUnitValue2 = _Data.VaporFlowProfileEstimate[_Data.stageNumber];
			InternalRealVariable internalRealVariable2 = _ColumnBD.get_BackDoorVariable((object)"FlowSht.600:ExtraData.500.20").Variable as InternalRealVariable;
			if (typeUnitValue2.Type == "M")
			{
				internalRealVariable2.Value = 3.0;
			}
			else if (typeUnitValue2.Type == "WT")
			{
				internalRealVariable2.Value = 4.0;
			}
			else if (typeUnitValue2.Type == "LV")
			{
				internalRealVariable2.Value = 5.0;
			}
			else if (typeUnitValue2.Type == "GV")
			{
				internalRealVariable2.Value = 1023.0;
			}
			(backDoor2.get_BackDoorVariable((object)"SepStage.500:ExtraData.300").Variable as InternalRealVariable).SetValue(typeUnitValue2.Value, typeUnitValue2.Unit);
		}
		if (_Data.LiqPhaseCompositionProfileEstimate.Keys.Contains(_Data.stageNumber))
		{
			Dictionary<int, double> dictionary = _Data.LiqPhaseCompositionProfileEstimate[_Data.stageNumber];
			foreach (int key3 in dictionary.Keys)
			{
				string moniker = "SepStage.500:MoleFraction.301." + (key3 - 1);
				double value = dictionary[key3];
				(backDoor2.get_BackDoorVariable((object)moniker).Variable as InternalRealVariable).Value = value;
			}
		}
		if (!_Data.VapPhaseCompositionProfileEstimate.Keys.Contains(_Data.stageNumber))
		{
			return;
		}
		(_ColumnBD.get_BackDoorVariable((object)"FlowSht.600:Index.500.4").Variable as InternalRealVariable).Value = 0.0;
		Dictionary<int, double> dictionary2 = _Data.VapPhaseCompositionProfileEstimate[_Data.stageNumber];
		foreach (int key4 in dictionary2.Keys)
		{
			string moniker2 = "SepStage.500:MoleFraction.301." + (key4 - 1);
			double value2 = dictionary2[key4];
			(backDoor2.get_BackDoorVariable((object)moniker2).Variable as InternalRealVariable).Value = value2;
		}
	}

	private void SetSideColumnFeeds(string columnID)
	{
		try
		{
			if (SideColumnData[columnID].InletStreams.Count == 0)
			{
				return;
			}
			HYSYSColumn hYSYSColumn = SideColumnData[columnID];
			string empty = string.Empty;
			string empty2 = string.Empty;
			string empty3 = string.Empty;
			int num = 0;
			List<string> list = new List<string>();
			List<string> list2 = new List<string>();
			List<string> list3 = new List<string>();
			List<string> list4 = new List<string>();
			foreach (string inletStream in hYSYSColumn.InletStreams)
			{
				num = hYSYSColumn.ColumnFeeds[inletStream];
				if (num == 1 && hYSYSColumn.bCondenserExist)
				{
					list.Add(inletStream);
					continue;
				}
				if (num == hYSYSColumn.stageNumber && hYSYSColumn.bReboilerExist)
				{
					list2.Add(inletStream);
					continue;
				}
				if (num == 2 && hYSYSColumn.bCondenserExist)
				{
					list3.Add(inletStream);
				}
				if (num == hYSYSColumn.stageNumber - 1 && hYSYSColumn.bReboilerExist)
				{
					list4.Add(inletStream);
				}
				ProcessStream processStream = _ColumnFlowsheet.MaterialStreams.Add(inletStream, "") as ProcessStream;
				num = hYSYSColumn.ColumnFeeds[inletStream] - SideColumnTrayDiff[columnID];
				TraySection traySection = SideColumnTraySection[columnID];
				if (num == hYSYSColumn.stageNumber && !hYSYSColumn.bReboilerExist)
				{
					traySection.VapourFeedStream = processStream;
				}
				else if (num == 1 && !hYSYSColumn.bCondenserExist)
				{
					traySection.LiquidFeedStream = processStream;
				}
				else
				{
					_AttachNumber = GetAttachedFeedNumber(columnID, traySection, bSideFeed: true);
					BackDoor obj = traySection as BackDoor;
					empty = ":Stream.504." + _AttachNumber;
					obj.SendAttachObject(empty, processStream);
					BackDoor backDoor = traySection.SeparationStages[num - 1] as BackDoor;
					empty = ":SepStage.500." + _AttachNumber;
					obj.SendAttachObject(empty, backDoor);
				}
				if (_Data.ColumnProducts.Keys.Contains(inletStream))
				{
					Dictionary<int, TypeUnitValue> dictionary = _Data.ColumnProducts[inletStream];
					num = dictionary.Keys.ToList()[0];
					string name = dictionary[num].Name;
					num -= _InternalTrayNumberDiff;
					traySection = _TraySectionMainTS;
					if (name == "V")
					{
						empty2 = "MaterialStream.506";
						empty3 = "SepStage.502";
					}
					else
					{
						empty2 = "MaterialStream.505";
						empty3 = "SepStage.501";
					}
					BackDoor obj2 = traySection as BackDoor;
					_AttachNumber = GetAttachedProductNumber(_Data.UID, traySection, bSideDraw: true, name);
					empty = ":" + empty2 + "." + _AttachNumber;
					obj2.SendAttachObject(empty, processStream);
					BackDoor backDoor = traySection.SeparationStages[num - 1] as BackDoor;
					empty = ":" + empty3 + "." + _AttachNumber;
					obj2.SendAttachObject(empty, backDoor);
				}
				else
				{
					_AttachNumber = GetAttachedFeedNumber(_Data.UID, traySection, bSideFeed: false);
					if (bReboilerQExist)
					{
						_AttachNumber--;
						bReboilerQExist = false;
					}
					processStream = _Flowsheet.MaterialStreams[inletStream] as ProcessStream;
					_ColumnBD = _Column as BackDoor;
					_ColumnBD.SendAttachObject(":Stream.500." + _AttachNumber, processStream);
				}
			}
			if (list.Count > 0)
			{
				additionalCondenserFeeds.Add(columnID, list);
			}
			if (list2.Count > 0)
			{
				additionalReboilerFeeds.Add(columnID, list2);
			}
			if (list3.Count > 0)
			{
				overheadFeeds.Add(columnID, list3);
			}
			if (list4.Count > 0)
			{
				bottomFeeds.Add(columnID, list4);
			}
		}
		catch (Exception)
		{
			DebugPrint.OutputDebugString(DebugLevel.BuildHYSYSCase, WarningLevel.Error, string.Format(P2Globalization.GetString("SetSideStripperFailed"), columnID, _Data.UID));
		}
	}

	private void SetSideColumnProducts(string columnID)
	{
		try
		{
			if (SideColumnData[columnID].OutletStreams.Count == 0)
			{
				return;
			}
			HYSYSColumn hYSYSColumn = SideColumnData[columnID];
			string empty = string.Empty;
			string empty2 = string.Empty;
			string empty3 = string.Empty;
			int num = 0;
			foreach (string outletStream in hYSYSColumn.OutletStreams)
			{
				TraySection traySection = SideColumnTraySection[columnID];
				Dictionary<int, TypeUnitValue> dictionary = hYSYSColumn.ColumnProducts[outletStream];
				num = dictionary.Keys.ToList()[0];
				if ((num == 1 && hYSYSColumn.bCondenserExist) || (num == hYSYSColumn.stageNumber && hYSYSColumn.bReboilerExist))
				{
					continue;
				}
				string name = dictionary[num].Name;
				if (name == "V")
				{
					empty2 = "MaterialStream.506";
					empty3 = "SepStage.502";
				}
				else
				{
					empty2 = "MaterialStream.505";
					empty3 = "SepStage.501";
				}
				ProcessStream processStream = _ColumnFlowsheet.MaterialStreams.Add(outletStream, "") as ProcessStream;
				if (num == 1 && !hYSYSColumn.bCondenserExist)
				{
					traySection.VapourProductStream = processStream;
				}
				else if (num == hYSYSColumn.stageNumber && !hYSYSColumn.bReboilerExist)
				{
					traySection.LiquidProductStream = processStream;
				}
				else
				{
					num -= SideColumnTrayDiff[columnID];
					BackDoor obj = traySection as BackDoor;
					_AttachNumber = GetAttachedProductNumber(columnID, traySection, bSideDraw: true, name);
					empty = ":" + empty2 + "." + _AttachNumber;
					obj.SendAttachObject(empty, processStream);
					BackDoor backDoor = traySection.SeparationStages[num - 1] as BackDoor;
					empty = ":" + empty3 + "." + _AttachNumber;
					obj.SendAttachObject(empty, backDoor);
				}
				if (_Data.ColumnFeeds.Keys.Contains(outletStream))
				{
					num = _Data.ColumnFeeds[outletStream];
					traySection = _TraySectionMainTS;
					if (num == _Data.stageNumber)
					{
						traySection.VapourFeedStream = processStream;
						continue;
					}
					if (num == 1)
					{
						traySection.LiquidFeedStream = processStream;
						continue;
					}
					_AttachNumber = GetAttachedFeedNumber(_Data.UID, traySection, bSideFeed: true);
					BackDoor obj2 = traySection as BackDoor;
					empty = ":Stream.504." + _AttachNumber;
					obj2.SendAttachObject(empty, processStream);
					num -= _InternalTrayNumberDiff;
					BackDoor backDoor = _TraySectionMainTS.SeparationStages[num - 1] as BackDoor;
					empty = ":SepStage.500." + _AttachNumber;
					obj2.SendAttachObject(empty, backDoor);
				}
				else
				{
					_AttachNumber = GetAttachedProductNumber(_Data.UID, traySection, bSideDraw: false, "");
					_AttachNumber--;
					processStream = _Flowsheet.MaterialStreams[outletStream] as ProcessStream;
					_ColumnBD = _Column as BackDoor;
					_ColumnBD.SendAttachObject(":Stream.511." + _AttachNumber, processStream);
				}
			}
		}
		catch (Exception)
		{
			DebugPrint.OutputDebugString(DebugLevel.BuildHYSYSCase, WarningLevel.Error, string.Format(P2Globalization.GetString("SetSideStripperFailed"), columnID, _Data.UID));
		}
	}

	private int GetAttachedProductNumber(string columnID, TraySection traySec, bool bSideDraw, string phase)
	{
		int result = 0;
		if (!bSideDraw)
		{
			result = _Column.AttachedProducts.Count + _internalProductsNumber;
		}
		else
		{
			switch (phase)
			{
			case "L":
				result = traySec.LiquidDrawStreams.Count;
				break;
			case "V":
				result = traySec.VapourDrawStreams.Count;
				break;
			case "W":
				result = traySec.WaterDrawStreams.Count;
				break;
			}
		}
		return result;
	}

	private int GetAttachedFeedNumber(string columnID, TraySection traySec, bool bSideFeed)
	{
		if (!bSideFeed)
		{
			return _Column.AttachedFeeds.Count;
		}
		return traySec.TraySectionFeeds.Count;
	}

	private void SetAdditionalEnergyStream()
	{
		SetQStreamForDutySpec(_Data.UID, bMainTS: true);
		SetQStreamForHeatLossGain(_Data.UID, bMainTS: true);
		foreach (string key in SideColumnData.Keys)
		{
			SetQStreamForDutySpec(key, bMainTS: false);
			SetQStreamForHeatLossGain(key, bMainTS: false);
		}
	}

	private void SetQStreamForDutySpec(string columnID, bool bMainTS)
	{
		try
		{
			HYSYSColumn hYSYSColumn;
			TraySection traySection;
			int num;
			if (bMainTS)
			{
				hYSYSColumn = _Data;
				traySection = _TraySectionMainTS;
				num = _InternalTrayNumberDiff;
			}
			else
			{
				hYSYSColumn = SideColumnData[columnID];
				traySection = SideColumnTraySection[columnID];
				num = SideColumnTrayDiff[columnID];
			}
			if (hYSYSColumn.DutySpec.Count == 0)
			{
				return;
			}
			foreach (int key in hYSYSColumn.DutySpec.Keys)
			{
				if (NeedsQStream(hYSYSColumn, key))
				{
					int num2 = hYSYSColumn.DutySpec[key].Keys.First();
					_AttachNumber = GetAttachedFeedNumber(columnID, traySection, bSideFeed: true);
					ProcessStream processStream = _ColumnFlowsheet.EnergyStreams.Add(hYSYSColumn.UID + "_Duty_" + key, "") as ProcessStream;
					string text = columnID + "_" + key;
					if (!_Data.QStreamForDuty.Keys.Contains(text))
					{
						_Data.QStreamForDuty.Add(text, processStream);
					}
					BackDoor obj = traySection as BackDoor;
					obj.SendAttachObject(":Stream.504." + _AttachNumber, processStream);
					num2 -= num;
					obj.SendAttachObject(Object: traySection.SeparationStages[num2 - 1] as BackDoor, Moniker: ":SepStage.500." + _AttachNumber);
					_AttachNumber = GetAttachedFeedNumber(columnID, traySection, bSideFeed: false);
					_ColumnBD = _Column as BackDoor;
					processStream = _Flowsheet.EnergyStreams.Add(hYSYSColumn.UID + "_Duty_" + key, "") as ProcessStream;
					_ColumnBD.SendAttachObject(":Stream.500." + (_AttachNumber - 1), processStream);
				}
			}
		}
		catch (Exception)
		{
			DebugPrint.OutputDebugString(DebugLevel.BuildHYSYSCase, WarningLevel.Error, string.Format(P2Globalization.GetString("SetQStreamFailed"), columnID));
		}
	}

	private void SetQStreamForHeatLossGain(string columnID, bool bMainTS)
	{
		try
		{
			HYSYSColumn hYSYSColumn;
			TraySection traySection;
			int num;
			if (bMainTS)
			{
				hYSYSColumn = _Data;
				traySection = _TraySectionMainTS;
				num = _InternalTrayNumberDiff;
			}
			else
			{
				hYSYSColumn = SideColumnData[columnID];
				traySection = SideColumnTraySection[columnID];
				num = SideColumnTrayDiff[columnID];
			}
			if (hYSYSColumn.HeatLossGainSpec.Count == 0)
			{
				return;
			}
			foreach (int key in hYSYSColumn.HeatLossGainSpec.Keys)
			{
				Dictionary<int, TypeUnitValue> dictionary = hYSYSColumn.HeatLossGainSpec[key];
				int num2 = dictionary.Keys.First();
				if (dictionary[num2].Value != 0.0 && (num2 != 1 || !hYSYSColumn.bCondenserExist) && (num2 != hYSYSColumn.stageNumber || !hYSYSColumn.bReboilerExist))
				{
					_AttachNumber = GetAttachedFeedNumber(columnID, traySection, bSideFeed: true);
					ProcessStream processStream = _Flowsheet.EnergyStreams.Add(hYSYSColumn.UID + "_HLG_" + key, "") as ProcessStream;
					_ColumnBD = _Column as BackDoor;
					_ColumnBD.SendAttachObject(":Stream.607." + _AttachNumber, processStream);
					num2 -= num;
					BackDoor backDoor = traySection.SeparationStages[num2 - 1] as BackDoor;
					_ColumnBD.SendAttachObject(":SepStage.607." + _AttachNumber, backDoor);
				}
			}
		}
		catch (Exception)
		{
			DebugPrint.OutputDebugString(DebugLevel.BuildHYSYSCase, WarningLevel.Error, string.Format(P2Globalization.GetString("SetQStreamFailed1"), columnID));
		}
	}

	private bool NeedsQStream(HYSYSColumn columnData, int dutyNO)
	{
		Dictionary<int, TypeUnitValue> dictionary = columnData.DutySpec[dutyNO];
		bool result = true;
		int num = dictionary.Keys.First();
		if ((num == 1 && columnData.bCondenserExist) || (num == columnData.stageNumber && columnData.bReboilerExist))
		{
			return false;
		}
		foreach (PumpAround item in columnData.PA)
		{
			if (item.TargetTrayNo == num)
			{
				result = false;
				break;
			}
		}
		return result;
	}

	private void SetColumnData()
	{
		SetThermodynamicMethod();
		SetVLLECheck();
		SetCovergenceData();
		SetPressureProfile();
		SetTempProfileEstimate();
		SetVaporFlowProfileEstimate();
		SetLiquidFlowProfileEstimate();
		SetLiqPhaseCompositionProfileEstimate();
		SetVapPhaseCompositionProfileEstimate();
		SetExportPseudoStreamData();
		AddHCurve();
	}

	private void SetThermodynamicMethod()
	{
		try
		{
			if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(_Data.FluidPackage) || !string.IsNullOrEmpty(_Data.VLLECheckSet))
			{
				if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(_Data.VLLECheckSet))
				{
					method = _Data.VLLECheckSet;
				}
				else
				{
					method = _Data.FluidPackage;
				}
				string methodName = HYSYSFluidPackages.GetMethodName(method);
				if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(methodName))
				{
					_ColumnBD = _Column as BackDoor;
					BackDoor backDoor = _SimCase.BasisManager.FluidPackages[methodName] as BackDoor;
					_ColumnBD.SendAttachObject(":CompList.400", backDoor);
					SetOutletStreamFluidPkg(backDoor);
				}
			}
		}
		catch (Exception)
		{
			DebugPrint.OutputDebugString(DebugLevel.BuildHYSYSCase, WarningLevel.Error, string.Format(P2Globalization.GetString("SetthermodynamicFailed"), _Data.UID));
		}
	}

	private void SetOutletStreamFluidPkg(BackDoor fluidpackageBD)
	{
		foreach (string hYSYSMainFlowsheetProduct in _Data.HYSYSMainFlowsheetProducts)
		{
			ProcessStream processStream = GetProcessStream(hYSYSMainFlowsheetProduct);
			if (processStream != null)
			{
				(processStream as BackDoor).SendAttachObject(":CompList.300", fluidpackageBD);
			}
		}
		foreach (HYSYSColumn value in SideColumnData.Values)
		{
			foreach (string outletStream in value.OutletStreams)
			{
				if (_Data.HYSYSMainFlowsheetProducts.Contains(outletStream))
				{
					ProcessStream processStream2 = GetProcessStream(outletStream);
					if (processStream2 != null)
					{
						(processStream2 as BackDoor).SendAttachObject(":CompList.300", fluidpackageBD);
					}
				}
			}
		}
	}

	private void SetVLLECheck()
	{
		try
		{
			if (_Data.bVLLECheckOn)
			{
				_ColumnBD = _Column as BackDoor;
				(_ColumnBD.get_BackDoorVariable((object)"FlowSht.600:Enum.527").Variable as InternalRealVariable).Value = 1.0;
				string text = "FlowSht.600/ColumnStage.530.";
				for (int i = 1; i <= _Data.VLLECheckTrays; i++)
				{
					text = text + i + ":Boolean.301";
					(_ColumnBD.get_BackDoorVariable((object)text).Variable as InternalRealVariable).Value = 1.0;
				}
			}
		}
		catch (Exception)
		{
			DebugPrint.OutputDebugString(DebugLevel.BuildHYSYSCase, WarningLevel.Error, string.Format(P2Globalization.GetString("SetVLLECheckFailed"), _Data.UID));
		}
	}

	private void SetCovergenceData()
	{
		try
		{
			_ColumnBD = _Column as BackDoor;
			(_ColumnBD.get_BackDoorVariable((object)"FlowSht.600:Count.500.1").Variable as InternalRealVariable).Value = 250.0;
			if (_Data.EquilTolerance > 0.0)
			{
				(_ColumnBD.get_BackDoorVariable((object)"FlowSht.600:ExtraData.500.4").Variable as InternalRealVariable).Value = _Data.EquilTolerance;
			}
			if (_Data.HeatSpecTolerance > 0.0)
			{
				(_ColumnBD.get_BackDoorVariable((object)"FlowSht.600:ExtraData.500.3").Variable as InternalRealVariable).Value = _Data.HeatSpecTolerance;
			}
			if (_Data.fixingDampFactor != null)
			{
				(_ColumnBD.get_BackDoorVariable((object)"FlowSht.600:Count.500.3").Variable as InternalRealVariable).Value = _Data.fixingDampFactor.Value;
			}
			if (_Data.bFlashFeed)
			{
				(_ColumnBD.get_BackDoorVariable((object)"FlowSht.600:Boolean.500.55").Variable as InternalRealVariable).Value = 1.0;
			}
			(_ColumnBD.get_BackDoorVariable((object)"FlowSht.600:Boolean.500.121").Variable as InternalRealVariable).Value = 1.0;
		}
		catch (Exception)
		{
			DebugPrint.OutputDebugString(DebugLevel.BuildHYSYSCase, WarningLevel.Error, string.Format(P2Globalization.GetString("SetConvergenceToleranceFailed"), _Data.UID));
		}
	}

	private void SetPressureProfile()
	{
		try
		{
			SetPressureData(_Data.UID, bMainColumn: true);
			foreach (string key in SideColumnData.Keys)
			{
				SetPressureData(key, bMainColumn: false);
			}
		}
		catch (Exception)
		{
			DebugPrint.OutputDebugString(DebugLevel.BuildHYSYSCase, WarningLevel.Error, string.Format(P2Globalization.GetString("SetPressureProfileFailed"), _Data.UID));
		}
	}

	private void SetPressureData(string columnID, bool bMainColumn)
	{
		string empty = string.Empty;
		int num = 0;
		HYSYSColumn hYSYSColumn;
		TraySection traySection;
		if (bMainColumn)
		{
			hYSYSColumn = _Data;
			traySection = _TraySectionMainTS;
			num = _InternalTrayNumberDiff;
		}
		else
		{
			hYSYSColumn = SideColumnData[columnID];
			traySection = SideColumnTraySection[columnID];
			num = SideColumnTrayDiff[columnID];
		}
		BackDoor backDoor = traySection as BackDoor;
		if (hYSYSColumn.PressureProfile.Count > 0)
		{
			foreach (int key in hYSYSColumn.PressureProfile.Keys)
			{
				if ((key != 1 || !hYSYSColumn.bCondenserExist) && (key != hYSYSColumn.stageNumber || !hYSYSColumn.bReboilerExist))
				{
					int num2 = key - num;
					empty = ":Pressure.500." + (num2 - 1);
					(backDoor.get_BackDoorVariable((object)empty).Variable as InternalRealVariable).SetValue(hYSYSColumn.PressureProfile[key].Value, hYSYSColumn.PressureProfile[key].Unit);
				}
			}
		}
		if (hYSYSColumn.ColumnTopPressure != null)
		{
			empty = ":Pressure.500.0";
			(backDoor.get_BackDoorVariable((object)empty).Variable as InternalRealVariable).SetValue(hYSYSColumn.ColumnTopPressure.Value, hYSYSColumn.ColumnTopPressure.Unit);
		}
		else if (bMainColumn && _Data.CondenserPressure != null)
		{
			(_ColumnBD.get_BackDoorVariable((object)"FlowSht.600/ColumnStage.530.0:Pressure.300").Variable as InternalRealVariable).SetValue(_Data.CondenserPressure.Value, _Data.CondenserPressure.Unit);
		}
		if (hYSYSColumn.ColumnBottomPressure != null)
		{
			int num3 = hYSYSColumn.stageNumber - num - 1;
			if (hYSYSColumn.bReboilerExist)
			{
				num3--;
			}
			empty = ":Pressure.500." + num3;
			(backDoor.get_BackDoorVariable((object)empty).Variable as InternalRealVariable).SetValue(hYSYSColumn.ColumnBottomPressure.Value, hYSYSColumn.ColumnBottomPressure.Unit);
		}
	}

	private void SetTemperatureData(HYSYSColumn columnData, bool bMainTS)
	{
		string empty = string.Empty;
		TraySection traySection;
		int num;
		if (bMainTS)
		{
			traySection = _TraySectionMainTS;
			num = _InternalTrayNumberDiff;
		}
		else
		{
			traySection = SideColumnTraySection[columnData.UID];
			num = SideColumnTrayDiff[columnData.UID];
		}
		try
		{
			if (columnData.TempProfileEstimate.Count == 0)
			{
				if (bMainTS)
				{
					DebugPrint.OutputDebugString(DebugLevel.BuildHYSYSCase, WarningLevel.Notes, string.Format(P2Globalization.GetString("NoTemperatureEstimate"), _Data.UID));
				}
				else
				{
					DebugPrint.OutputDebugString(DebugLevel.BuildHYSYSCase, WarningLevel.Notes, string.Format(P2Globalization.GetString("NoTemperatureEstimate1"), columnData.IDENTIFICATION, columnData.UID, _Data.UID));
				}
				return;
			}
			BackDoor backDoor = traySection as BackDoor;
			foreach (int key in columnData.TempProfileEstimate.Keys)
			{
				if ((key != 1 || !columnData.bCondenserExist) && (key != columnData.stageNumber || !columnData.bReboilerExist))
				{
					empty = "SepStage.508." + (key - num - 1) + ":Temperature.300";
					DoubleValue doubleValue = columnData.TempProfileEstimate[key];
					(backDoor.get_BackDoorVariable((object)empty).Variable as InternalRealVariable).SetValue(doubleValue.Value, doubleValue.Unit);
				}
			}
		}
		catch (Exception)
		{
			DebugPrint.OutputDebugString(DebugLevel.BuildHYSYSCase, WarningLevel.Error, string.Format(P2Globalization.GetString("SetTemperatureProfileFailed"), columnData.UID));
		}
	}

	private void SetVaporFlowData(HYSYSColumn columnData, bool bMainTS)
	{
		string empty = string.Empty;
		TraySection traySection;
		int num;
		if (bMainTS)
		{
			traySection = _TraySectionMainTS;
			num = _InternalTrayNumberDiff;
		}
		else
		{
			traySection = SideColumnTraySection[columnData.UID];
			num = SideColumnTrayDiff[columnData.UID];
		}
		try
		{
			BackDoor backDoor = traySection as BackDoor;
			bool flag = false;
			foreach (int key in columnData.VaporFlowProfileEstimate.Keys)
			{
				if ((key == 1 && columnData.bCondenserExist) || (key == columnData.stageNumber && columnData.bReboilerExist))
				{
					continue;
				}
				empty = "SepStage.508." + (key - num - 1) + ":ExtraData.300";
				TypeUnitValue typeUnitValue = columnData.VaporFlowProfileEstimate[key];
				if (!flag)
				{
					_ColumnBD = _Column as BackDoor;
					InternalRealVariable internalRealVariable = _ColumnBD.get_BackDoorVariable((object)"FlowSht.600:ExtraData.500.20").Variable as InternalRealVariable;
					if (typeUnitValue.Type == "M")
					{
						internalRealVariable.Value = 3.0;
					}
					else if (typeUnitValue.Type == "WT")
					{
						internalRealVariable.Value = 4.0;
					}
					else if (typeUnitValue.Type == "LV")
					{
						internalRealVariable.Value = 5.0;
					}
					else if (typeUnitValue.Type == "GV")
					{
						internalRealVariable.Value = 1023.0;
					}
					flag = true;
				}
				(backDoor.get_BackDoorVariable((object)empty).Variable as InternalRealVariable).SetValue(typeUnitValue.Value, typeUnitValue.Unit);
			}
		}
		catch (Exception)
		{
			DebugPrint.OutputDebugString(DebugLevel.BuildHYSYSCase, WarningLevel.Error, string.Format(P2Globalization.GetString("SetVaporFlowProfileFailed"), columnData.UID));
		}
	}

	private void SetLiqPhaseCompositionData(HYSYSColumn columnData, bool bMainTS)
	{
		string empty = string.Empty;
		TraySection traySection;
		int num;
		if (bMainTS)
		{
			traySection = _TraySectionMainTS;
			num = _InternalTrayNumberDiff;
		}
		else
		{
			traySection = SideColumnTraySection[columnData.UID];
			num = SideColumnTrayDiff[columnData.UID];
		}
		try
		{
			BackDoor backDoor = traySection as BackDoor;
			(_ColumnBD.get_BackDoorVariable((object)"FlowSht.600:Index.500.4").Variable as InternalRealVariable).Value = 1.0;
			foreach (int key in columnData.LiqPhaseCompositionProfileEstimate.Keys)
			{
				if ((key == 1 && columnData.bCondenserExist) || (key == columnData.stageNumber && columnData.bReboilerExist))
				{
					continue;
				}
				empty = "SepStage.508." + (key - num - 1);
				Dictionary<int, double> dictionary = columnData.LiqPhaseCompositionProfileEstimate[key];
				foreach (int key2 in dictionary.Keys)
				{
					string empty2 = string.Empty;
					empty2 = empty + ":MoleFraction.301." + (key2 - 1);
					double value = dictionary[key2];
					(backDoor.get_BackDoorVariable((object)empty2).Variable as InternalRealVariable).Value = value;

		}
		catch (Exception)
		{
			DebugPrint.OutputDebugString(DebugLevel.BuildHYSYSCase, WarningLevel.Error, string.Format(P2Globalization.GetString("SetLiquidPhaseCompositionProfiledFailed"), columnData.UID));

	private void SetExportPseudoStreamData()
	{
		int iExportCount = 0;
		ExportPseudoStream(_Data.UID, bMainTS: true, ref iExportCount);
		foreach (string key in SideColumnData.Keys)
		{
			ExportPseudoStream(key, bMainTS: false, ref iExportCount);
		}
	}

	private void ExportPseudoStream(string columnID, bool bMainTS, ref int iExportCount)
	{
		int num = 0;
		_ColumnBD = _Column as BackDoor;
		_ = string.Empty;
		int num2 = 0;
		string empty = string.Empty;
		double num3 = -1.0;
		double num4 = -1.0;
		HYSYSColumn hYSYSColumn;
		TraySection traySec;
		if (bMainTS)
		{
			hYSYSColumn = _Data;
			traySec = _TraySectionMainTS;
			num2 = _InternalTrayNumberDiff;
		}
		else
		{
			hYSYSColumn = SideColumnData[columnID];
			traySec = SideColumnTraySection[columnID];
			num2 = SideColumnTrayDiff[columnID];
		}
		try
		{
			_ColumnBD = _Column as BackDoor;
			string[] source = (string[])_Flowsheet.Streams.Names;
			int totalStage = hYSYSColumn.stageNumber - num2;
			if (hYSYSColumn.ExportNetLiquidFlow.Count > 0)
			{
				foreach (string key in hYSYSColumn.ExportNetLiquidFlow.Keys)
				{
					empty = (source.Contains(key) ? ("Export_" + columnID + "_NET_L_" + key) : key);
					num = hYSYSColumn.ExportNetLiquidFlow[key] - num2;
					num3 = 2.0;
					num4 = 0.0;
					ExportStreamHelper(columnID, traySec, empty, ref iExportCount, num, bReboiler: false, num3, num4, totalStage, hYSYSColumn.bCondenserExist, hYSYSColumn.bReboilerExist);
					if (source.Contains(key))
					{
						AddValveForExportStream(empty, key);
					}
				}
			}
			if (hYSYSColumn.ExportNetVaporFlow.Count > 0)
			{
				foreach (string key2 in hYSYSColumn.ExportNetVaporFlow.Keys)
				{
					empty = (source.Contains(key2) ? ("Export_" + columnID + "_NET_V_" + key2) : key2);
					num = hYSYSColumn.ExportNetVaporFlow[key2] - num2;
					num3 = 1.0;
					num4 = 0.0;
					ExportStreamHelper(columnID, traySec, empty, ref iExportCount, num, bReboiler: false, num3, num4, totalStage, hYSYSColumn.bCondenserExist, hYSYSColumn.bReboilerExist);
					if (source.Contains(key2))
					{
						AddValveForExportStream(empty, key2);
					}
				}
			}
			if (hYSYSColumn.ExportTotalLiquidFlow.Count > 0)
			{
				foreach (string key3 in hYSYSColumn.ExportTotalLiquidFlow.Keys)
				{
					empty = (source.Contains(key3) ? ("Export_" + columnID + "_TOTAL_L_" + key3) : key3);
					num = hYSYSColumn.ExportTotalLiquidFlow[key3] - num2;
					num3 = 2.0;
					num4 = 1.0;
					ExportStreamHelper(columnID, traySec, empty, ref iExportCount, num, bReboiler: false, num3, num4, totalStage, hYSYSColumn.bCondenserExist, hYSYSColumn.bReboilerExist);
					if (source.Contains(key3))
					{
						AddValveForExportStream(empty, key3);
					}
				}
			}
			if (hYSYSColumn.ExportTotalVaporFlow.Count > 0)
			{
				foreach (string key4 in hYSYSColumn.ExportTotalVaporFlow.Keys)
				{
					empty = (source.Contains(key4) ? ("Export_" + columnID + "_TOTAL_V_" + key4) : key4);
					num = hYSYSColumn.ExportTotalVaporFlow[key4] - num2;
					num3 = 1.0;
					num4 = 1.0;
					ExportStreamHelper(columnID, traySec, empty, ref iExportCount, num, bReboiler: false, num3, num4, totalStage, hYSYSColumn.bCondenserExist, hYSYSColumn.bReboilerExist);
					if (source.Contains(key4))
					{
						AddValveForExportStream(empty, key4);
					}
				}
			}
			if (hYSYSColumn.ExportPumpAroundLiquidFlow.Count > 0)
			{
				foreach (string key5 in hYSYSColumn.ExportPumpAroundLiquidFlow.Keys)
				{
					(SubObjColl[ExportStmAndPANamePair[key5]].get_BackDoorVariable((object)":Boolean.400").Variable as InternalRealVariable).Value = 1.0;
					empty = ExportStmAndPADrawNamePair[key5];
					if (!source.Contains(key5))
					{
						_Flowsheet.MaterialStreams.Add(key5, "");
					}
					AddValveForExportStream(empty, key5);
				}
			}
			if (hYSYSColumn.ExportPumpAroundVaporFlow.Count <= 0)
			{
				return;
			}
			foreach (string key6 in hYSYSColumn.ExportPumpAroundVaporFlow.Keys)
			{
				(SubObjColl[ExportStmAndPANamePair[key6]].get_BackDoorVariable((object)":Boolean.400").Variable as InternalRealVariable).Value = 1.0;
				empty = ExportStmAndPADrawNamePair[key6];
				if (!source.Contains(key6))
				{
					_Flowsheet.MaterialStreams.Add(key6, "");
				}
				AddValveForExportStream(empty, key6);
			}
		}
		catch (Exception)
		{
			DebugPrint.OutputDebugString(DebugLevel.BuildHYSYSCase, WarningLevel.Error, string.Format(P2Globalization.GetString("SetExportPseudoStreamFailed"), columnID));
		}
	}

	private void AddColumnSpecification()
	{
		_ColumnFlowsheet.Specifications.RemoveAll();
		AddReboilerSpec();
		AddPumparoundspec();
		AddDutySpec();
		AddHeatLossGainSpec();
		AddLocalSpec();
		AddLocalDefine();
		ReAnalyzeActiveOfVarySpec();
		SetProductFlowrateSpec();
		AddGlobalSpec();
		AddGlobalDefine();
		if (_Data.bRROrRefluxEstExist && RROrRefluxSpec == null)
		{
			AddRefluxOrRRSpec();
		}
	}

	private void SetProductFlowrateSpec()
	{
		AddMainColumnDrawToSideColumnProdSpec();
		AddProductFlowrateSpec(_Data, bSideCol: false);
		foreach (string key in SideColumnData.Keys)
		{
			AddProductFlowrateSpec(SideColumnData[key], bSideCol: true);
		}
	}

	private void AddMainColumnDrawToSideColumnProdSpec()
	{
		try
		{
			if (SideColumnData.Count == 0)
			{
				return;
			}
			foreach (string key in _Data.ColumnProducts.Keys)
			{
				if (!_Data.HYSYSMainFlowsheetProducts.Contains(key) && !IsDuplicateOfSpec(key))
				{
					DrawFlowrateSpecGeneral(_Data, key, bSideDraw: true);
				}
			}
		}
		catch (Exception)
		{
			DebugPrint.OutputDebugString(DebugLevel.BuildHYSYSCase, WarningLevel.Error, string.Format(P2Globalization.GetString("AddProduct2SideColumnFlowSpecificationFailed"), _Data.UID));
		}
	}

	private void AddProductFlowrateSpec(HYSYSColumn columnData, bool bSideCol)
	{
		try
		{
			if (columnData.ColumnProducts.Count <= 0)
			{
				return;
			}
			foreach (string key in columnData.ColumnProducts.Keys)
			{
				if ((!bSideCol || !_Data.ColumnFeeds.Keys.Contains(key)) && (bSideCol || _Data.HYSYSMainFlowsheetProducts.Contains(key)) && !IsDuplicateOfSpec(key))
				{
					DrawFlowrateSpecGeneral(columnData, key, bSideDraw: false);
				}
			}
		}
		catch (Exception)
		{
			DebugPrint.OutputDebugString(DebugLevel.BuildHYSYSCase, WarningLevel.Error, string.Format(P2Globalization.GetString("AddProduct2SideColumnFlowSpecificationFailed"), columnData.UID));
		}
	}

	private void DrawFlowrateSpecGeneral(HYSYSColumn columnData, string streamID, bool bSideDraw)
	{
		Dictionary<int, TypeUnitValue> dictionary = columnData.ColumnProducts[streamID];
		bool flag = false;
		foreach (int key in dictionary.Keys)
		{
			if (key == 1 && bCondLDrawExist && bCondVDrawExist)
			{
				foreach (string key2 in columnData.ColumnProducts.Keys)
				{
					Dictionary<int, TypeUnitValue> dictionary2 = columnData.ColumnProducts[key2];
					if (dictionary2.Keys.First() == 1)
					{
						TypeUnitValue typeUnitValue = dictionary2[key];
						if (!(typeUnitValue.Name == "V") && !(typeUnitValue.Name == "W") && !(typeUnitValue.Name == "L2") && typeUnitValue.Value > 0.0)
						{
							flag = true;
							break;
						}
					}
				}
			}
			TypeUnitValue typeUnitValue2 = dictionary[key];
			if (typeUnitValue2 == null || typeUnitValue2.Value <= 0.0 || typeUnitValue2.Name == "W")
			{
				continue;
			}
			bool bActive = true;
			if (_ColumnFlowsheet.DegreesOfFreedom != 0)
			{
				bActive = !_Data.VaryDrawStreamIDs.Contains(streamID) && !_Data.VaryFeedStreamIDs.Contains(streamID) && ((key == 1 && typeUnitValue2.Name == "L") || typeUnitValue2.Name == "L1" || (!(key == 1 && typeUnitValue2.Name == "V" && flag) && IsProductDrawSpecActive(columnData, streamID, key)));
			}
			else
			{
				InternalRealVariable obj = (_Flowsheet as BackDoor).get_BackDoorVariable((object)"SolverObject.400:Attribute.300").Variable as InternalRealVariable;
				obj.Value = 0.0;
				if (_ColumnFlowsheet.DegreesOfFreedom == 0)
				{
					bActive = false;
				}
				obj.Value = 1.0;
			}
			DoubleValue originValue = new DoubleValue(typeUnitValue2.Unit, typeUnitValue2.Value);
			if (ConvertedGVValueToMoleValue(typeUnitValue2.Name, typeUnitValue2.Type, ref originValue))
			{
				typeUnitValue2.Type = "M";
			}
			ColumnDrawRateSpec(columnData.UID, streamID, typeUnitValue2.Type, originValue, bActive, bNeedStore: false);
		}
	}

	public void AddReboilerSpec()
	{
		SetReboilerSpec(_Data);
		foreach (string key in SideColumnData.Keys)
		{
			SetReboilerSpec(SideColumnData[key]);
		}
	}

	private void SetReboilerSpec(HYSYSColumn columnData)
	{
		try
		{
			if (columnData.bReboilerExist)
			{
				bool bActive = false;
				if (columnData.ReboilerType == HYSYSReboilerTypeEnum.KETTLE)
				{
					bActive = true;
				}
				if (columnData.ReboilerTemperature != null)
				{
					ColumnTemperatureSpec(columnData.UID, columnData.stageNumber, columnData.ReboilerTemperature, bActive);
				}
			}
		}
		catch (Exception)
		{
			DebugPrint.OutputDebugString(DebugLevel.BuildHYSYSCase, WarningLevel.Error, string.Format(P2Globalization.GetString("AddReboilerSpecificationFailed"), columnData.UID));
		}
	}

	private void AddPumparoundspec()
	{
		SetPumparoundSpec(_Data);
		foreach (string key in SideColumnData.Keys)
		{
			SetPumparoundSpec(SideColumnData[key]);
		}
	}

	private void SetPumparoundSpec(HYSYSColumn columnData)
	{
		if (columnData.PA.Count == 0)
		{
			return;
		}
		try
		{
			List<int> paDutyIDFound = new List<int>();
			foreach (PumpAround item in columnData.PA)
			{
				int num = 0;
				bool bActive = true;
				int sourceTrayNo = item.SourceTrayNo;
				int targetTrayNo = item.TargetTrayNo;
				_ = item.DrawPhase;
				sourceTrayNo -= _InternalTrayNumberDiff;
				targetTrayNo -= _InternalTrayNumberDiff;
				string empty = string.Empty;
				string uID = columnData.UID;
				if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(item.Name))
				{
					uID = uID + "_PA_" + targetTrayNo;
					empty = columnData.UID + "_PA_" + sourceTrayNo + "_TO_" + targetTrayNo;
				}
				else
				{
					uID = uID + "_" + item.Name;
					empty = columnData.UID + "_PA_" + item.Name;
				}
				if (item.PumparoundRate != null)
				{
					DoubleValue originValue = new DoubleValue(item.PumparoundRate.Unit, item.PumparoundRate.Value);
					if (ConvertedGVValueToMoleValue(item.DrawPhase, item.PumparoundRate.Type, ref originValue))
					{
						item.PumparoundRate.Type = "M";
					}
					ColumnPumpAroundSpec(columnData.UID, uID, empty, "Rate", item.PumparoundRate.Type, originValue, bActive);
					num++;
				}
				if (item.ReturnStreamLiqFrac != null)
				{
					ColumnPumpAroundSpec(columnData.UID, uID, empty, "Return Vf", "", new DoubleValue("", 1.0 - item.ReturnStreamLiqFrac.Value), bActive);
					num++;
				}
				if (item.ReturnStreamTempChange != null)
				{
					if (num == 2)
					{
						bActive = false;
					}
					else
					{
						num++;
					}
					ColumnPumpAroundSpec(columnData.UID, uID, empty, "DT", "", item.ReturnStreamTempChange, bActive);
				}
				if (item.ReturnStreamTemp != null)
				{
					if (num == 2)
					{
						bActive = false;
					}
					else
					{
						num++;
					}
					ColumnPumpAroundSpec(columnData.UID, uID, empty, "ReturnT", "", item.ReturnStreamTemp, bActive);
				}
				TypeUnitValue dutySpecValueByStage = GetDutySpecValueByStage(columnData, item.TargetTrayNo, ref paDutyIDFound);
				if (dutySpecValueByStage != null && paDutyIDFound.Last() > -1)
				{
					paDutyID.Add(paDutyIDFound.Last(), item);
				}
				if (dutySpecValueByStage != null && dutySpecValueByStage.Value != 0.0)
				{
					int num2 = paDutyIDFound.Last();
					if (num == 2)
					{
						bActive = false;
					}
					else
					{
						if (num2 <= -1)
						{
							continue;
						}
						bActive = IsActiveDutyspec(num2.ToString(), dutySpecValueByStage.Name);
					}
					DoubleValue specValue = new DoubleValue(dutySpecValueByStage.Unit, dutySpecValueByStage.Value);
					ColumnPumpAroundSpec(columnData.UID, uID, empty, "Duty", "", specValue, bActive);
				}
				if (num < 2 && dutySpecValueByStage == null)
				{
					ColumnPumpAroundSpec(columnData.UID, uID, empty, "DT", "", new DoubleValue("C", 0.0), bActive: true);
				}
			}
		}
		catch (Exception)
		{
			DebugPrint.OutputDebugString(DebugLevel.BuildHYSYSCase, WarningLevel.Error, string.Format(P2Globalization.GetString("AddPumparoundSpecificationFailed"), columnData.UID));
		}
	}

	private void AddDutySpec()
	{
		SetDutySpec(_Data);
		foreach (string key in SideColumnData.Keys)
		{
			SetDutySpec(SideColumnData[key]);
		}
	}

	private void SetDutySpec(HYSYSColumn columnData)
	{
		try
		{
			if (columnData.DutySpec.Count <= 0)
			{
				return;
			}
			foreach (int key in columnData.DutySpec.Keys)
			{
				if (paDutyID.Keys.Contains(key))
				{
					continue;
				}
				Dictionary<int, TypeUnitValue> dictionary = columnData.DutySpec[key];
				foreach (int key2 in dictionary.Keys)
				{
					TypeUnitValue typeUnitValue = dictionary[key2];
					if (typeUnitValue.Value != 0.0)
					{
						bool bActive = IsActiveDutyspec(key.ToString(), typeUnitValue.Name);
						string empty = string.Empty;
						empty = ((key2 == columnData.stageNumber && columnData.bReboilerExist) ? (columnData.UID + "_Reb_Q") : ((key2 != 1 || !columnData.bCondenserExist) ? (columnData.UID + "_Duty_" + key) : ("Q-Condenser_" + columnData.UID)));
						DoubleValue specVal = new DoubleValue(typeUnitValue.Unit, typeUnitValue.Value);
						ColumnDutySpec(columnData.UID, empty, specVal, bActive);
					}
				}
			}
		}
		catch (Exception)
		{
			DebugPrint.OutputDebugString(DebugLevel.BuildHYSYSCase, WarningLevel.Error, string.Format(P2Globalization.GetString("AddDutySpecificationFailed"), columnData.UID));
		}
	}

	private void AddHeatLossGainSpec()
	{
		SetHeatLossGainSpec(_Data);
		foreach (string key in SideColumnData.Keys)
		{
			SetHeatLossGainSpec(SideColumnData[key]);
		}
	}

	private void SetHeatLossGainSpec(HYSYSColumn columnData)
	{
		try
		{
			if (columnData.HeatLossGainSpec.Count <= 0)
			{
				return;
			}
			foreach (int key in columnData.HeatLossGainSpec.Keys)
			{
				Dictionary<int, TypeUnitValue> dictionary = columnData.HeatLossGainSpec[key];
				foreach (int key2 in dictionary.Keys)
				{
					if ((key2 != 1 || !columnData.bCondenserExist) && (key2 != columnData.stageNumber || !columnData.bReboilerExist))
					{
						TypeUnitValue typeUnitValue = dictionary[key2];
						if (typeUnitValue.Value != 0.0)
						{
							string streamName = columnData.UID + "_HLG_" + key;
							DoubleValue specVal = new DoubleValue(typeUnitValue.Unit, typeUnitValue.Value);
							ColumnDutySpec(columnData.UID, streamName, specVal, bActive: true);
						}
					}
				}
			}
		}
		catch (Exception)
		{
			DebugPrint.OutputDebugString(DebugLevel.BuildHYSYSCase, WarningLevel.Error, string.Format(P2Globalization.GetString("AddHeatLossGainSpecificationFailed"), columnData.UID));
		}
	}

	private void AddLocalSpec()
	{
		SetLocalSpec(_Data);
		foreach (string key in SideColumnData.Keys)
		{
			SetLocalSpec(SideColumnData[key]);
		}
	}

	private void SetLocalSpec(HYSYSColumn columnData)
	{
		try
		{
			if (columnData.LocalSpec.Count == 0)
			{
				return;
			}
			foreach (Specification item in columnData.LocalSpec)
			{
				if (item.SpecType == SpecificationType.UNITOPERATION)
				{
					SetUnitOperationTypeSpec(item, columnData);
				}
				else if (item.SpecType == SpecificationType.STREAM)
				{
					SetStreamTypeSpec(item, columnData);
				}
				else if (item.SpecType == SpecificationType.COLUMNINTERNALSTREAM)
				{
					SetInternalStreamTypeSpec(item, columnData);
				}
			}
		}
		catch (Exception)
		{
			DebugPrint.OutputDebugString(DebugLevel.BuildHYSYSCase, WarningLevel.Error, string.Format(P2Globalization.GetString("AddLocalSpecificationFailed"), _Data.UID));
		}
	}

	private void SetUnitOperationTypeSpec(Specification spec, HYSYSColumn columnData)
	{
		DoubleValue doubleValue = null;
		BackDoor colSpecBD = null;
		if (spec.PrimaryPropertyName.ToUpper().Trim().StartsWith(CUnitOperation.REFLUX))
		{
			string flowbasis = "M";
			string unit = string.Empty;
			double refValue = -1.0;
			ParseUtilty.GetFlowbasisAndUnit(spec.PrimaryPropertyName, ref flowbasis, ref unit, ref refValue);
			if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(spec.OperatorName))
			{
				TypeUnitValue hysysValue = UnitConverter.GetHysysValue(UnitClassEnum.Rate, spec.Value.Value, unit, flowbasis);
				doubleValue = new DoubleValue(hysysValue.Unit, hysysValue.Value);
				colSpecBD = ColumnLiquidFlowSpec(columnData.UID, 0, flowbasis, doubleValue, !spec.bInactiveSpec);
			}
			else if (spec.Operator == Specification.OperatorType.divide && _Data.HYSYSMainFlowsheetFeeds.Contains(spec.ReferenceID))
			{
				bool bLight = true;
				bool bTotalLiq = false;
				GetRRSpecproperty(spec.PrimaryPropertyName, ref flowbasis, ref bLight, ref bTotalLiq);
				List<string> list = new List<string>();
				list.Add(spec.ReferenceID);
				colSpecBD = ColumnRefluxFeedRatioSpec(columnData.UID, list, flowbasis, bLight, new DoubleValue("", spec.Value.Value), !spec.bInactiveSpec);
			}
			else
			{
				_Data.ConvertNotes.Add(string.Format(P2Globalization.GetString("LSNotConvert"), spec.P2SpecExpression, _Data.UID));
			}
		}
		else if (spec.PrimaryPropertyName.ToUpper().Trim().StartsWith("RATI") || spec.PrimaryPropertyName.ToUpper().Trim().StartsWith("RRAT"))
		{
			string flowbasis2 = "M";
			bool bLight2 = true;
			bool bTotalLiq2 = false;
			GetRRSpecproperty(spec.PrimaryPropertyName, ref flowbasis2, ref bLight2, ref bTotalLiq2);
			doubleValue = new DoubleValue("", spec.Value.Value);
			if (!_Data.HYSYSMainFlowsheetFeeds.Contains(spec.ReferenceID))
			{
				bool bIncludeV = true;
				colSpecBD = ColumnRefluxRatioSpec(columnData.UID, flowbasis2, bIncludeV, bTotalLiq2, doubleValue, !spec.bInactiveSpec);
			}
			else if (columnData.UID == _Data.UID && _Data.ColumnFeeds.ContainsKey(spec.ReferenceID))
			{
				List<string> list2 = new List<string>();
				list2.Add(spec.ReferenceID);
				colSpecBD = ColumnRefluxFeedRatioSpec(columnData.UID, list2, flowbasis2, bLight2, doubleValue, !spec.bInactiveSpec);
			}
			else
			{
				_Data.ConvertNotes.Add(string.Format(P2Globalization.GetString("LSNotConvert"), spec.P2SpecExpression, _Data.UID));
			}
		}
		SetSpecTolerance(spec, colSpecBD, doubleValue);
	}

	private void SetStreamTypeSpec(Specification spec, HYSYSColumn columnData)
	{
		string flowbasis = "M";
		string unit = string.Empty;
		DoubleValue originValue = null;
		double refValue = -1.0;
		BackDoor colSpecBD = null;
		string ColUID = string.Empty;
		ParseUtilty.GetFlowbasisAndUnit(spec.PrimaryPropertyName, ref flowbasis, ref unit, ref refValue);
		if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(unit) && spec.Value != null)
		{
			spec.Value.Unit = unit;
		}
		if (refValue < -1.0)
		{
			GetAssayIPEPValue(ref refValue);
		}
		if (spec.PrimaryPHASE.ToUpper().Trim().StartsWith("T") && columnData.ColumnProducts.Keys.Contains(spec.PrimaryID) && columnData.ColumnProducts[spec.PrimaryID].Values.First().Name == "V")
		{
			spec.PrimaryPHASE = "V";
		}
		if (spec.PrimaryPropertyName.ToUpper().Trim().StartsWith(CStream.RATE))
		{
			switch (spec.Operator)
			{
			case Specification.OperatorType.plus:
				_Data.ConvertNotes.Add(string.Format(P2Globalization.GetString("LSNotConvert"), spec.P2SpecExpression, _Data.UID));
				break;
			case Specification.OperatorType.minus:
				_Data.ConvertNotes.Add(string.Format(P2Globalization.GetString("LSNotConvert"), spec.P2SpecExpression, _Data.UID));
				break;
			case Specification.OperatorType.times:
				_Data.ConvertNotes.Add(string.Format(P2Globalization.GetString("LSNotConvert"), spec.P2SpecExpression, _Data.UID));
				break;
			case Specification.OperatorType.divide:
				if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(spec.ReferenceName))
				{
					_Data.ConvertNotes.Add(string.Format(P2Globalization.GetString("LSNotConvert"), spec.P2SpecExpression, _Data.UID));
					break;
				}
				originValue = spec.Value;
				if (ConvertedGVValueToMoleValue(spec.PrimaryPHASE, flowbasis, ref originValue))
				{
					flowbasis = "M";
				}
				if (IsRecoverySpec(columnData, spec))
				{
					if (spec.PrimaryStartComponent > 0)
					{
						List<int> list2 = new List<int>();
						for (int j = spec.PrimaryStartComponent; j <= spec.PrimaryEndComponent; j++)
						{
							list2.Add(j);
						}
						colSpecBD = ColumnComponentRecoverySpec(columnData.UID, bStreamType: true, spec.PrimaryID, -1, spec.PrimaryPHASE, list2, spec.Value, !spec.bInactiveSpec);
					}
					else
					{
						colSpecBD = ColumnRecoverySpec(columnData.UID, spec.PrimaryID, flowbasis, spec.Value, !spec.bInactiveSpec);
					}
				}
				else if (spec.PrimaryID == spec.ReferenceID && spec.PrimaryStartComponent > 0)
				{
					List<int> list3 = new List<int>();
					List<int> list4 = new List<int>();
					for (int k = spec.PrimaryStartComponent; k <= spec.PrimaryEndComponent; k++)
					{
						list3.Add(k);
					}
					if (spec.ReferenceStartComponent > 0)
					{
						for (int l = spec.ReferenceStartComponent; l <= spec.ReferenceEndComponent; l++)
						{
							list4.Add(l);
						}
						colSpecBD = ColumnComponentRatioSpec(columnData.UID, bStreamType: true, spec.PrimaryID, 0, spec.PrimaryPHASE, flowbasis, list3, list4, spec.Value, !spec.bInactiveSpec);
					}
					else if (spec.ReferencePHASE.ToUpper().StartsWith("T"))
					{
						colSpecBD = ColumnComponentFractionSpec(columnData.UID, bStreamType: true, spec.PrimaryID, -1, spec.PrimaryPHASE, flowbasis, list3, spec.Value, !spec.bInactiveSpec);
					}
				}
				else if (columnData.ColumnFeeds.Keys.Contains(spec.ReferenceID) && spec.PrimaryStartComponent == 0)
				{
					int iFeedStage = columnData.ColumnFeeds[spec.ReferenceID];
					colSpecBD = ColumnFeedRatiospec(columnData.UID, "Draw", spec.PrimaryID, 0, iFeedStage, flowbasis, spec.Value, !spec.bInactiveSpec);
				}
				else
				{
					_Data.ConvertNotes.Add(string.Format(P2Globalization.GetString("LSNotConvert"), spec.P2SpecExpression, _Data.UID));
				}
				break;
			case Specification.OperatorType.none:
			{
				TypeUnitValue typeUnitValue = null;
				typeUnitValue = (string.IsNullOrEmpty(spec.Value.Unit) ? UnitConverter.GetHysysValue(UnitClassEnum.Rate, spec.Value.Value, spec.Value.Unit, flowbasis) : UnitConverter.GetHysysValue(UnitClassEnum.Rate, spec.Value.Value, spec.Value.Unit, ""));
				flowbasis = typeUnitValue.Type;
				originValue = new DoubleValue(typeUnitValue.Unit, typeUnitValue.Value);
				if (ConvertedGVValueToMoleValue(spec.PrimaryPHASE, flowbasis, ref originValue))
				{
					flowbasis = "M";
				}
				if (spec.PrimaryStartComponent > 0)
				{
					List<int> list = new List<int>();
					for (int i = spec.PrimaryStartComponent; i <= spec.PrimaryEndComponent; i++)
					{
						list.Add(i);
					}
					colSpecBD = ColumnComponentFlowSpec(columnData.UID, bStreamType: true, spec.PrimaryID, -1, "", flowbasis, list, originValue, !spec.bInactiveSpec);
					break;
				}
				bool bActive = false;
				bool bNeedStore = false;
				if (!spec.bInactiveSpec)
				{
					if (!columnData.VaryDrawStreamIDs.Contains(spec.PrimaryID) && !_Data.VaryDrawStreamIDs.Contains(spec.PrimaryID))
					{
						bActive = true;
					}
					else
					{
						bNeedStore = true;
					}
				}
				colSpecBD = ColumnDrawRateSpec(columnData.UID, spec.PrimaryID, flowbasis, originValue, bActive, bNeedStore);
				break;
			}
			default:
				_Data.ConvertNotes.Add(string.Format(P2Globalization.GetString("LSNotConvert"), spec.P2SpecExpression, _Data.UID));
				break;
			}
		}
		else if (spec.PrimaryPropertyName.ToUpper().Trim().StartsWith(CUnitOperation.TEMPERATURE))
		{
			if (spec.Operator == Specification.OperatorType.minus)
			{
				originValue = UnitConverter.GetHysysValue(UnitClassEnum.DropTemperature, spec.Value);
				colSpecBD = ColumnDTSpec(columnData.UID, spec.PrimaryID, spec.ReferenceID, originValue, !spec.bInactiveSpec);
			}
			else
			{
				int productDrawNO = GetProductDrawNO(columnData, spec.PrimaryID, ref ColUID);
				if (productDrawNO == -1)
				{
					_Data.ConvertNotes.Add(string.Format(P2Globalization.GetString("LSNotConvert"), spec.P2SpecExpression, _Data.UID));
				}
				else
				{
					originValue = UnitConverter.GetHysysValue(UnitClassEnum.Temperature, spec.Value);
					colSpecBD = ColumnTemperatureSpec(columnData.UID, productDrawNO, originValue, !spec.bInactiveSpec);
				}
			}
		}
		else if (spec.PrimaryPropertyName.ToUpper().Trim().StartsWith(CUnitOperation.FRACTION) || spec.PrimaryPropertyName.ToUpper().Trim().StartsWith("PPM") || spec.PrimaryPropertyName.ToUpper().Trim().StartsWith("PCT"))
		{
			originValue = new DoubleValue(spec.Value.Unit, spec.Value.Value);
			if (spec.Operator == Specification.OperatorType.none)
			{
				if (spec.PrimaryStartComponent > 0)
				{
					List<int> list5 = new List<int>();
					for (int m = spec.PrimaryStartComponent; m <= spec.PrimaryEndComponent; m++)
					{
						list5.Add(m);
					}
					if (spec.PrimaryPropertyName.ToUpper().Trim().StartsWith("PPM"))
					{
						originValue.Value = spec.Value.Value / 1000000.0;
					}
					else if (spec.PrimaryPropertyName.ToUpper().Trim().StartsWith("PCT"))
					{
						originValue.Value = spec.Value.Value / 100.0;
					}
					if (ConvertedGVValueToMoleValue(spec.PrimaryPHASE, flowbasis, ref originValue))
					{
						flowbasis = "M";
					}
					colSpecBD = ColumnComponentFractionSpec(columnData.UID, bStreamType: true, spec.PrimaryID, -1, spec.PrimaryPHASE, flowbasis, list5, originValue, !spec.bInactiveSpec);
				}
				else
				{
					_Data.ConvertNotes.Add(string.Format(P2Globalization.GetString("LSNotConvert"), spec.P2SpecExpression, _Data.UID));
				}
			}
			else if (spec.Operator == Specification.OperatorType.divide)
			{
				List<int> list6 = new List<int>();
				List<int> list7 = new List<int>();
				for (int n = spec.PrimaryStartComponent; n <= spec.PrimaryEndComponent; n++)
				{
					list6.Add(n);
				}
				for (int num = spec.ReferenceStartComponent; num <= spec.ReferenceEndComponent; num++)
				{
					list7.Add(num);
				}
				colSpecBD = ColumnComponentRatioSpec(columnData.UID, bStreamType: true, spec.PrimaryID, 0, spec.PrimaryPHASE, flowbasis, list6, list7, originValue, !spec.bInactiveSpec);
			}
			else
			{
				_Data.ConvertNotes.Add(string.Format(P2Globalization.GetString("LSNotConvert"), spec.P2SpecExpression, _Data.UID));
			}
		}
		else if (spec.Operator != Specification.OperatorType.none)
		{
			_Data.ConvertNotes.Add(string.Format(P2Globalization.GetString("LSNotConvert"), spec.P2SpecExpression, _Data.UID));
		}
		else if (spec.PrimaryPropertyName.ToUpper().Trim().StartsWith("VISC"))
		{
			DoubleValue doubleValue = HYSYSUOMMap.GetDefault(UnitClassEnum.Viscosity);
			DoubleValue hysysValue = UnitConverter.GetHysysValue(UnitClassEnum.Viscosity, spec.Value);
			originValue = HYSYSUOMMap.GetConvertedValue(UnitClassEnum.Viscosity, hysysValue, doubleValue.Unit);
			originValue.Unit = "";
			string productPhase = GetProductPhase(columnData, spec.PrimaryID);
			int productDrawNO2 = GetProductDrawNO(columnData, spec.PrimaryID, ref ColUID);
			colSpecBD = ColumnTransportPropertySpec(ColUID, productDrawNO2, "Viscosity", productPhase, null, originValue, !spec.bInactiveSpec);
		}
		else if (spec.PrimaryPropertyName.ToUpper().Trim().StartsWith("COND"))
		{
			DoubleValue doubleValue2 = HYSYSUOMMap.GetDefault(UnitClassEnum.ThermalConductivity);
			DoubleValue hysysValue2 = UnitConverter.GetHysysValue(UnitClassEnum.ThermalConductivity, spec.Value);
			originValue = HYSYSUOMMap.GetConvertedValue(UnitClassEnum.ThermalConductivity, hysysValue2, doubleValue2.Unit);
			originValue.Unit = "";
			string productPhase2 = GetProductPhase(columnData, spec.PrimaryID);
			int productDrawNO3 = GetProductDrawNO(columnData, spec.PrimaryID, ref ColUID);
			colSpecBD = ColumnTransportPropertySpec(ColUID, productDrawNO3, "Thermo Conductivity", productPhase2, null, originValue, !spec.bInactiveSpec);
		}
		else if (spec.PrimaryPropertyName.ToUpper().Trim().StartsWith("SURF"))
		{
			DoubleValue doubleValue3 = HYSYSUOMMap.GetDefault(UnitClassEnum.SurfaceTension);
			DoubleValue hysysValue3 = UnitConverter.GetHysysValue(UnitClassEnum.SurfaceTension, spec.Value);
			originValue = HYSYSUOMMap.GetConvertedValue(UnitClassEnum.SurfaceTension, hysysValue3, doubleValue3.Unit);
			originValue.Unit = "";
			string productPhase3 = GetProductPhase(columnData, spec.PrimaryID);
			int productDrawNO4 = GetProductDrawNO(columnData, spec.PrimaryID, ref ColUID);
			colSpecBD = ColumnTransportPropertySpec(ColUID, productDrawNO4, "Surface Tension", productPhase3, null, originValue, !spec.bInactiveSpec);
		}
		else if (spec.PrimaryPropertyName.ToUpper().Trim().StartsWith("ENTR"))
		{
			originValue = UnitConverter.GetHysysValue(UnitClassEnum.HeatCapacity, spec.Value);
			colSpecBD = ColumnStreamPropertySpec(columnData.UID, spec.PrimaryID, "Standard", "Mass Entropy", originValue, !spec.bInactiveSpec);
		}
		else if (spec.PrimaryPropertyName.ToUpper().Trim().StartsWith("CP"))
		{
			originValue = UnitConverter.GetHysysValue(UnitClassEnum.HeatCapacity, spec.Value);
			colSpecBD = ColumnStreamPropertySpec(columnData.UID, spec.PrimaryID, "Standard", "Mass Heat Capacity", originValue, !spec.bInactiveSpec);
		}
		else if (spec.PrimaryPropertyName.ToUpper().Trim().StartsWith("CV"))
		{
			originValue = UnitConverter.GetHysysValue(UnitClassEnum.HeatCapacity, spec.Value);
			colSpecBD = ColumnStreamPropertySpec(columnData.UID, spec.PrimaryID, "Standard", "Mass Cv", originValue, !spec.bInactiveSpec);
		}
		else if (spec.PrimaryPropertyName.ToUpper().Trim().StartsWith("MW"))
		{
			originValue = new DoubleValue("", spec.Value.Value);
			colSpecBD = ColumnStreamPropertySpec(columnData.UID, spec.PrimaryID, "Standard", "Molecular Weight", originValue, !spec.bInactiveSpec);
		}
		else if (spec.PrimaryPropertyName.ToUpper().Trim().StartsWith("DENS"))
		{
			DoubleValue doubleValue4 = HYSYSUOMMap.GetDefault(UnitClassEnum.LiquidDensity);
			DoubleValue hysysValue4 = UnitConverter.GetHysysValue(UnitClassEnum.LiquidDensity, spec.Value);
			originValue = HYSYSUOMMap.GetConvertedValue(UnitClassEnum.LiquidDensity, hysysValue4, doubleValue4.Unit);
			originValue.Unit = "";
			colSpecBD = ColumnStreamPropertySpec(columnData.UID, spec.PrimaryID, "Standard", "Liq. Mass Density (Std. Cond)", originValue, !spec.bInactiveSpec);
		}
		else if (spec.PrimaryPropertyName.ToUpper().Trim().StartsWith("MOLV"))
		{
			DoubleValue doubleValue5 = HYSYSUOMMap.GetDefault(UnitClassEnum.LiquidDensity);
			int num2 = spec.Value.Unit.IndexOf("/");
			string unit2 = spec.Value.Unit.Substring(num2 + 1) + "/" + spec.Value.Unit.Substring(0, num2);
			DoubleValue hysysValue5 = UnitConverter.GetHysysValue(UnitClassEnum.LiquidDensity, new DoubleValue(unit2, 1.0 / spec.Value.Value));
			originValue = HYSYSUOMMap.GetConvertedValue(UnitClassEnum.MoleLiquidVolume, hysysValue5, doubleValue5.Unit);
			originValue.Unit = "";
			colSpecBD = ColumnStreamPropertySpec(columnData.UID, spec.PrimaryID, "Standard", "Liq. Mass Density (Std. Cond)", originValue, !spec.bInactiveSpec);
		}
		else if (spec.PrimaryPropertyName.ToUpper().Trim().StartsWith("HOTV"))
		{
			TypeUnitValue hysysValue6 = UnitConverter.GetHysysValue(UnitClassEnum.Rate, spec.Value.Value, spec.Value.Unit, "V");
			originValue = new DoubleValue(hysysValue6.Unit, hysysValue6.Value);
			colSpecBD = ColumnStreamPropertySpec(columnData.UID, spec.PrimaryID, "Standard", "Act. Volume Flow", originValue, !spec.bInactiveSpec);
		}
		else if (spec.PrimaryPropertyName.ToUpper().Trim().StartsWith("ZVAL"))
		{
			originValue = spec.Value;
			colSpecBD = ColumnStreamPropertySpec(columnData.UID, spec.PrimaryID, "Standard", "Z Factor", spec.Value, !spec.bInactiveSpec);
		}
		else if (spec.PrimaryPropertyName.ToUpper().Trim().StartsWith("LHV"))
		{
			originValue = UnitConverter.GetHysysValue(UnitClassEnum.VolumEnthalpy, spec.Value);
			colSpecBD = ColumnStreamPropertySpec(columnData.UID, spec.PrimaryID, "Gas", "LHV Vol. Basis", originValue, !spec.bInactiveSpec);
		}
		else if (spec.PrimaryPropertyName.ToUpper().Trim().StartsWith("WOBB"))
		{
			originValue = UnitConverter.GetHysysValue(UnitClassEnum.VolumEnthalpy, spec.Value);
			colSpecBD = ColumnStreamPropertySpec(columnData.UID, spec.PrimaryID, "Gas", "Wobbe Index", originValue, !spec.bInactiveSpec);
		}
		else if (spec.PrimaryPropertyName.ToUpper().Trim().StartsWith("TBP("))
		{
			string productPhase4 = GetProductPhase(columnData, spec.PrimaryID);
			int productDrawNO5 = GetProductDrawNO(columnData, spec.PrimaryID, ref ColUID);
			originValue = UnitConverter.GetHysysValue(UnitClassEnum.Temperature, spec.Value.Value, unit);
			if (flowbasis == "M")
			{
				flowbasis = "V";
			}
			colSpecBD = ColumnCutPointSpec(ColUID, "TBP", productDrawNO5, productPhase4, flowbasis, refValue, originValue, !spec.bInactiveSpec);
		}
		else if (spec.PrimaryPropertyName.ToUpper().Trim().StartsWith("D86"))
		{
			string productPhase5 = GetProductPhase(columnData, spec.PrimaryID);
			int productDrawNO6 = GetProductDrawNO(columnData, spec.PrimaryID, ref ColUID);
			originValue = UnitConverter.GetHysysValue(UnitClassEnum.Temperature, spec.Value.Value, unit);
			flowbasis = "V";
			colSpecBD = ColumnCutPointSpec(ColUID, "ASTMD86", productDrawNO6, productPhase5, flowbasis, refValue, originValue, !spec.bInactiveSpec);
		}
		else if (spec.PrimaryPropertyName.ToUpper().Trim().StartsWith("D2887"))
		{
			string productPhase6 = GetProductPhase(columnData, spec.PrimaryID);
			int productDrawNO7 = GetProductDrawNO(columnData, spec.PrimaryID, ref ColUID);
			originValue = UnitConverter.GetHysysValue(UnitClassEnum.Temperature, spec.Value.Value, unit);
			flowbasis = "WT";
			colSpecBD = ColumnCutPointSpec(ColUID, "ASTMD2887", productDrawNO7, productPhase6, flowbasis, refValue, originValue, !spec.bInactiveSpec);
		}
		else if (spec.PrimaryPropertyName.ToUpper().Trim().StartsWith("D1160"))
		{
			string sType = "D1160Vac";
			if (spec.PrimaryPropertyName.ToUpper().Trim().Contains("P760"))
			{
				sType = "D1160ATM";
			}
			string productPhase7 = GetProductPhase(columnData, spec.PrimaryID);
			int productDrawNO8 = GetProductDrawNO(columnData, spec.PrimaryID, ref ColUID);
			originValue = UnitConverter.GetHysysValue(UnitClassEnum.Temperature, spec.Value.Value, unit);
			flowbasis = "V";
			colSpecBD = ColumnCutPointSpec(ColUID, sType, productDrawNO8, productPhase7, flowbasis, refValue, originValue, !spec.bInactiveSpec);
		}
		else if (spec.PrimaryPropertyName.ToUpper().Trim().StartsWith("ANIL"))
		{
			originValue = UnitConverter.GetHysysValue(UnitClassEnum.Temperature, spec.Value.Value, unit);
			colSpecBD = ColumnStreamPropertySpec(columnData.UID, spec.PrimaryID, "Petroleum", "Aniline Point", originValue, !spec.bInactiveSpec);
		}
		else if (spec.PrimaryPropertyName.ToUpper().Trim().StartsWith("CHRA"))
		{
			originValue = spec.Value;
			colSpecBD = ColumnStreamPropertySpec(columnData.UID, spec.PrimaryID, "Petroleum", "C to H Ratio", spec.Value, !spec.bInactiveSpec);
		}
		else if (spec.PrimaryPropertyName.ToUpper().Trim().StartsWith("CLOU"))
		{

			colSpecBD = ColumnStreamPropertySpec(columnData.UID, spec.PrimaryID, "Petroleum", "Cloud Point", originValue, !spec.bInactiveSpec);
		}
		else if (spec.PrimaryPropertyName.ToUpper().Trim().StartsWith("CCR"))
		{
			originValue = spec.Value;
			colSpecBD = ColumnStreamPropertySpec(columnData.UID, spec.PrimaryID, "Petroleum", "Conradson Carbon Content", spec.Value, !spec.bInactiveSpec);
		}
		else if (spec.PrimaryPropertyName.ToUpper().Trim().StartsWith("FLPO") && !spec.PrimaryPropertyName.ToUpper().Contains("OC"))
		{
			originValue = UnitConverter.GetHysysValue(UnitClassEnum.Temperature, spec.Value.Value, unit);
			colSpecBD = ColumnStreamPropertySpec(columnData.UID, spec.PrimaryID, "Petroleum", "Flash Point", originValue, !spec.bInactiveSpec);
		}
		else if (spec.PrimaryPropertyName.ToUpper().Trim().StartsWith("FRZP"))
		{
