			colSpecBD = ColumnStreamPropertySpec(columnData.UID, spec.PrimaryID, "Petroleum", "Freeze Point", originValue, !spec.bInactiveSpec);
		}
		else if (spec.PrimaryPropertyName.ToUpper().Trim().StartsWith("H2"))
		{
			originValue = UnitConverter.GetHysysValue(UnitClassEnum.Fraction, spec.Value.Value, unit);
			colSpecBD = ColumnStreamPropertySpec(columnData.UID, spec.PrimaryID, "Petroleum", "Hydrogen Content", originValue, !spec.bInactiveSpec);
		}
		else if (spec.PrimaryPropertyName.ToUpper().Trim().StartsWith("IRON"))
		{
			originValue = UnitConverter.GetHysysValue(UnitClassEnum.Fraction, spec.Value.Value, unit);
			colSpecBD = ColumnStreamPropertySpec(columnData.UID, spec.PrimaryID, "Petroleum", "Iron Content", originValue, !spec.bInactiveSpec);
		}
		else if (spec.PrimaryPropertyName.ToUpper().Trim().StartsWith("KVIS"))
		{
			_ = (_SimCase.CorrelationManager as BackDoor).get_BackDoorVariable((object)":Selection.503").Variable;
			DoubleValue hysysValue7 = UnitConverter.GetHysysValue(UnitClassEnum.Temperature, Convert.ToDouble(spec.PrimPropRefTemp), "");
			originValue = UnitConverter.GetHysysValue(UnitClassEnum.KinematicViscosity, spec.Value.Value, unit);
			string productPhase8 = GetProductPhase(columnData, spec.PrimaryID);
			int productDrawNO9 = GetProductDrawNO(columnData, spec.PrimaryID, ref ColUID);
			colSpecBD = ColumnTransportPropertySpec(ColUID, productDrawNO9, "Kinematic Viscosity", productPhase8, hysysValue7, originValue, !spec.bInactiveSpec);
		}
		else if (spec.PrimaryPropertyName.ToUpper().Trim().StartsWith("LUMI"))
		{
			originValue = spec.Value;
			colSpecBD = ColumnStreamPropertySpec(columnData.UID, spec.PrimaryID, "Petroleum", "Luminometer Number", spec.Value, !spec.bInactiveSpec);
		}
		else if (spec.PrimaryPropertyName.ToUpper().Trim().StartsWith("MEAB"))
		{
			originValue = UnitConverter.GetHysysValue(UnitClassEnum.Temperature, spec.Value.Value, unit);
			colSpecBD = ColumnStreamPropertySpec(columnData.UID, spec.PrimaryID, "Petroleum", "Mean Average Boiling Point", originValue, !spec.bInactiveSpec);
		}
		else if (spec.PrimaryPropertyName.ToUpper().Trim().StartsWith("NAPH"))
		{
			originValue = GetContentValueByPercent(spec.Value, unit);
			colSpecBD = ColumnStreamPropertySpec(columnData.UID, spec.PrimaryID, "Petroleum", "Naphthenes by Weight", originValue, !spec.bInactiveSpec);
		}
		else if (spec.PrimaryPropertyName.ToUpper().Trim().StartsWith("NICK"))
		{
			originValue = GetContentValueByPercent(spec.Value, unit);
			originValue.Unit = "wt %";
			colSpecBD = ColumnStreamPropertySpec(columnData.UID, spec.PrimaryID, "Petroleum", "Nickel Content", originValue, !spec.bInactiveSpec);
		}
		else if (spec.PrimaryPropertyName.ToUpper().Trim().StartsWith("NITR(TOT"))
		{
			originValue = GetContentValueByPercent(spec.Value, unit);
			originValue.Unit = "wt %";
			colSpecBD = ColumnStreamPropertySpec(columnData.UID, spec.PrimaryID, "Petroleum", "Nitrogen Content", originValue, !spec.bInactiveSpec);
		}
		else if (spec.PrimaryPropertyName.ToUpper().Trim().StartsWith("OLEF"))
		{
			originValue = GetContentValueByPercent(spec.Value, unit);
			originValue.Unit = "%";
			colSpecBD = ColumnStreamPropertySpec(columnData.UID, spec.PrimaryID, "Petroleum", "Olefins by Weight", originValue, !spec.bInactiveSpec);
		}
		else if (spec.PrimaryPropertyName.ToUpper().Trim().StartsWith("PARA(TOT"))
		{
			originValue = GetContentValueByPercent(spec.Value, unit);
			originValue.Unit = "%";
			colSpecBD = ColumnStreamPropertySpec(columnData.UID, spec.PrimaryID, "Petroleum", "Paraffins by Wt", originValue, !spec.bInactiveSpec);
		}
		else if (spec.PrimaryPropertyName.ToUpper().Trim().StartsWith("POUR"))
		{
			originValue = UnitConverter.GetHysysValue(UnitClassEnum.Temperature, spec.Value.Value, unit);
			colSpecBD = ColumnStreamPropertySpec(columnData.UID, spec.PrimaryID, "Petroleum", "Pour Point", originValue, !spec.bInactiveSpec);
		}
		else if (spec.PrimaryPropertyName.ToUpper().Trim().StartsWith("SULF"))
		{
			originValue = GetContentValueByPercent(spec.Value, unit);
			originValue.Unit = "%";
			colSpecBD = ColumnStreamPropertySpec(columnData.UID, spec.PrimaryID, "Petroleum", "Sulfur Wt Pct", originValue, !spec.bInactiveSpec);
		}
		else if (spec.PrimaryPropertyName.ToUpper().Trim().StartsWith("VANA"))
		{
			originValue = GetContentValueByPercent(spec.Value, unit);
			originValue.Unit = "wt %";
			colSpecBD = ColumnStreamPropertySpec(columnData.UID, spec.PrimaryID, "Petroleum", "Vanadium Content", originValue, !spec.bInactiveSpec);
		}
		else if (spec.PrimaryPropertyName.ToUpper().Trim().StartsWith("WAX"))
		{
			originValue = GetContentValueByPercent(spec.Value, unit);
			originValue.Unit = "%";
			colSpecBD = ColumnStreamPropertySpec(columnData.UID, spec.PrimaryID, "Petroleum", "Wax Content", originValue, !spec.bInactiveSpec);
		}
		else if (spec.PrimaryPropertyName.ToUpper().Trim().StartsWith("RVP"))
		{
			string propName = "API 5B1.1";
			spec.Value.Unit = "psi";
			P2Title p2Title = P2BaseObject.g_Map[Categories.Title] as P2Title;
			if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(p2Title.RVPBasis))
			{
				string text = p2Title.RVPBasis.ToUpper();
				if (text.StartsWith("APIC"))
				{
					propName = "API 5B1.2";
				}
				else if (text.StartsWith("P323"))
				{
					propName = "ASTM_D323-73/79";
				}
				else if (text.StartsWith("D323"))
				{
					propName = "ASTM D323-82";
				}
				else if (text.StartsWith("D4953"))
				{
					propName = "ASTM D4953-91";
				}
				else if (text.StartsWith("D5191"))
				{
					propName = "ASTM D5191-91";
				}
			}
			originValue = UnitConverter.GetHysysValue(UnitClassEnum.Pressure, spec.Value);
			colSpecBD = ColumnStreamPropertySpec(columnData.UID, spec.PrimaryID, "RVP", propName, originValue, !spec.bInactiveSpec);
		}
		else if (spec.PrimaryPropertyName.ToUpper().Trim().StartsWith("TVP"))
		{
			P2Title p2Title2 = P2BaseObject.g_Map[Categories.Title] as P2Title;
			if (p2Title2.TVPBasis == null)
			{
				_Data.ConvertNotes.Add(string.Format(P2Globalization.GetString("LSNotConvert"), spec.P2SpecExpression, _Data.UID));
			}
			else
			{
				DoubleValue hysysValue8 = UnitConverter.GetHysysValue(UnitClassEnum.Temperature, p2Title2.TVPBasis);
				if (HYSYSUOMMap.GetConvertedValue(UnitClassEnum.Temperature, hysysValue8, "C").Value - 37.8 < 0.1)
				{
					originValue = UnitConverter.GetHysysValue(UnitClassEnum.Pressure, spec.Value);
					colSpecBD = ColumnStreamPropertySpec(columnData.UID, spec.PrimaryID, "Standard", "True VP at 37.8C", originValue, !spec.bInactiveSpec);
				}
				else
				{
					_Data.ConvertNotes.Add(string.Format(P2Globalization.GetString("LSNotConvert"), spec.P2SpecExpression, _Data.UID));
				}
			}
		}
		else
		{
			_Data.ConvertNotes.Add(string.Format(P2Globalization.GetString("LSNotConvert"), spec.P2SpecExpression, _Data.UID));
		}
		SetSpecTolerance(spec, colSpecBD, originValue);
	}

	private void SetInternalStreamTypeSpec(Specification spec, HYSYSColumn columnData)
	{
		string flowbasis = "M";
		string unit = string.Empty;
		DoubleValue originValue = null;
		double refValue = -1.0;
		ParseUtilty.GetFlowbasisAndUnit(spec.PrimaryPropertyName, ref flowbasis, ref unit, ref refValue);
		if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(unit))
		{
			spec.Value.Unit = unit;
		}
		if (refValue < -1.0)
		{
			GetAssayIPEPValue(ref refValue);
		}
		BackDoor colSpecBD = null;
		if (spec.PrimaryPropertyName.ToUpper().Trim().StartsWith(CStream.RATE))
		{
			switch (spec.Operator)
			{
			case Specification.OperatorType.plus:
				_Data.ConvertNotes.Add(string.Format(P2Globalization.GetString("LSNotConvert"), spec.P2SpecExpression, _Data.UID));
				break;
			case Specification.OperatorType.minus:
				_Data.ConvertNotes.Add(string.Format(P2Globalization.GetString("LSNotConvert"), spec.P2SpecExpression, _Data.UID));
				break;
			case Specification.OperatorType.times:
				_Data.ConvertNotes.Add(string.Format(P2Globalization.GetString("LSNotConvert"), spec.P2SpecExpression, _Data.UID));
				break;
			case Specification.OperatorType.divide:
				if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(spec.ReferenceName))
				{
					_Data.ConvertNotes.Add(string.Format(P2Globalization.GetString("LSNotConvert"), spec.P2SpecExpression, _Data.UID));
					break;
				}
				originValue = spec.Value;
				if (ConvertedGVValueToMoleValue(spec.PrimaryPHASE, flowbasis, ref originValue))
				{
					flowbasis = "M";
				}
				if (IsRecoverySpec(columnData, spec))
				{
					if (spec.PrimaryStartComponent > 0)
					{
						List<int> list2 = new List<int>();
						for (int j = spec.PrimaryStartComponent; j <= spec.PrimaryEndComponent; j++)
						{
							list2.Add(j);
						}
						colSpecBD = ColumnComponentRecoverySpec(columnData.UID, bStreamType: false, spec.PrimaryID, spec.PrimaryTrayNumber, spec.PrimaryPHASE, list2, spec.Value, !spec.bInactiveSpec);
						break;
					}
					string flowType = string.Empty;
					if (spec.PrimaryPHASE == "L")
					{
						flowType = "TrayL";
					}
					else if (spec.PrimaryPHASE == "V")
					{
						flowType = "TrayV";
					}
					int iFeedStage = columnData.ColumnFeeds[spec.ReferenceID];
					colSpecBD = ColumnFeedRatiospec(columnData.UID, flowType, "", spec.PrimaryTrayNumber, iFeedStage, flowbasis, spec.Value, !spec.bInactiveSpec);
				}
				else if (IsRefluxFeedRatioSpec(columnData, spec))
				{
					colSpecBD = ColumnRefluxFeedRatioSpec(columnData.UID, columnData.ColumnFeeds.Keys.ToList(), flowbasis, bLight: true, originValue, !spec.bInactiveSpec);
				}
				else if (spec.PrimaryTrayNumber == spec.ReferenceTrayNumber && !spec.ReferenceName.ToUpper().Trim().StartsWith("REFF") && spec.PrimaryStartComponent > 0)
				{
					List<int> list3 = new List<int>();
					List<int> list4 = new List<int>();
					for (int k = spec.PrimaryStartComponent; k <= spec.PrimaryEndComponent; k++)
					{
						list3.Add(k);
					}
					for (int l = spec.ReferenceStartComponent; l <= spec.ReferenceEndComponent; l++)
					{
						list4.Add(l);
					}
					colSpecBD = ColumnComponentRatioSpec(columnData.UID, bStreamType: false, spec.PrimaryID, spec.PrimaryTrayNumber, spec.PrimaryPHASE, flowbasis, list3, list4, spec.Value, !spec.bInactiveSpec);
				}
				else if (columnData.ColumnFeeds.Keys.Contains(spec.ReferenceID))
				{
					int iFeedStage2 = columnData.ColumnFeeds[spec.ReferenceID];
					if (spec.PrimaryPHASE == "L")
					{
						colSpecBD = ColumnFeedRatiospec(columnData.UID, "TrayL", "", spec.PrimaryTrayNumber, iFeedStage2, flowbasis, spec.Value, !spec.bInactiveSpec);
					}
					else if (spec.PrimaryPHASE == "V")
					{
						colSpecBD = ColumnFeedRatiospec(columnData.UID, "TrayV", "", spec.PrimaryTrayNumber, iFeedStage2, flowbasis, spec.Value, !spec.bInactiveSpec);
					}
				}
				else if (columnData.ColumnProducts.ContainsKey(spec.ReferenceID) && IsInternalStageVFSpec(columnData, spec.PrimaryTrayNumber, spec.PrimaryPHASE))
				{
					double value = spec.Value.Value;
					value /= 1.0 + value;
					colSpecBD = ColumnVaporFractionSpec(columnData.UID, spec.PrimaryTrayNumber, new DoubleValue("", value), !spec.bInactiveSpec);
				}
				else
				{
					_Data.ConvertNotes.Add(string.Format(P2Globalization.GetString("LSNotConvert"), spec.P2SpecExpression, _Data.UID));
				}
				break;
			case Specification.OperatorType.none:
			{
				TypeUnitValue hysysValue = UnitConverter.GetHysysValue(UnitClassEnum.Rate, spec.Value.Value, spec.Value.Unit, flowbasis);
				originValue = new DoubleValue(hysysValue.Unit, hysysValue.Value);
				if (ConvertedGVValueToMoleValue(spec.PrimaryPHASE, flowbasis, ref originValue))
				{
					flowbasis = "M";
				}
				if (spec.PrimaryStartComponent > 0)
				{
					List<int> list = new List<int>();
					for (int i = spec.PrimaryStartComponent; i <= spec.PrimaryEndComponent; i++)
					{
						list.Add(i);
					}
					colSpecBD = ColumnComponentFlowSpec(columnData.UID, bStreamType: false, spec.PrimaryID, spec.PrimaryTrayNumber, spec.PrimaryPHASE, flowbasis, list, originValue, !spec.bInactiveSpec);
				}
				else if (spec.PrimaryPHASE == "L")
				{
					colSpecBD = ColumnLiquidFlowSpec(columnData.UID, spec.PrimaryTrayNumber, flowbasis, originValue, !spec.bInactiveSpec);
				}
				else if (spec.PrimaryPHASE == "V")
				{
					colSpecBD = ColumnVaporFlowSpec(columnData.UID, spec.PrimaryTrayNumber, flowbasis, originValue, !spec.bInactiveSpec);
				}
				break;
			}
			default:
				_Data.ConvertNotes.Add(string.Format(P2Globalization.GetString("LSNotConvert"), spec.P2SpecExpression, _Data.UID));
				break;
			}
		}
		else if (spec.PrimaryPropertyName.ToUpper().Trim().StartsWith("TBP"))
		{
			originValue = UnitConverter.GetHysysValue(UnitClassEnum.Temperature, spec.Value.Value, unit);
			if (spec.Operator == Specification.OperatorType.minus)
			{
				flowbasis = "V";
				string flowbasis2 = "V";
				string unit2 = string.Empty;
				double refValue2 = -1.0;
				ParseUtilty.GetFlowbasisAndUnit(spec.ReferencePropertyName, ref flowbasis2, ref unit2, ref refValue2);
				if (refValue2 < -1.0)
				{
					GetAssayIPEPValue(ref refValue2);
				}
				colSpecBD = ColumnGapCutPointSpec(columnData.UID, "TBP", spec.PrimaryTrayNumber, spec.ReferenceTrayNumber, spec.PrimaryPHASE, flowbasis, refValue, refValue2, originValue, !spec.bInactiveSpec);
			}
			else if (spec.Operator == Specification.OperatorType.none)
			{
				if (flowbasis == "M")
				{
					flowbasis = "V";
				}
				colSpecBD = ColumnCutPointSpec(columnData.UID, "TBP", spec.PrimaryTrayNumber, spec.PrimaryPHASE, flowbasis, refValue, originValue, !spec.bInactiveSpec);
			}
			else
			{
				_Data.ConvertNotes.Add(string.Format(P2Globalization.GetString("LSNotConvert"), spec.P2SpecExpression, _Data.UID));
			}
		}
		else if (spec.PrimaryPropertyName.ToUpper().Trim().StartsWith("D86"))
		{
			originValue = UnitConverter.GetHysysValue(UnitClassEnum.Temperature, spec.Value.Value, unit);
			if (spec.Operator == Specification.OperatorType.minus)
			{
				flowbasis = "V";
				string flowbasis3 = "V";
				string unit3 = string.Empty;
				double refValue3 = -1.0;
				ParseUtilty.GetFlowbasisAndUnit(spec.ReferencePropertyName, ref flowbasis3, ref unit3, ref refValue3);
				if (refValue3 < -1.0)
				{
					GetAssayIPEPValue(ref refValue3);
				}
				colSpecBD = ColumnGapCutPointSpec(columnData.UID, "ASTMD86", spec.PrimaryTrayNumber, spec.ReferenceTrayNumber, spec.PrimaryPHASE, flowbasis, refValue, refValue3, originValue, !spec.bInactiveSpec);
			}
			else if (spec.Operator == Specification.OperatorType.none)
			{
				flowbasis = "V";
				colSpecBD = ColumnCutPointSpec(columnData.UID, "ASTMD86", spec.PrimaryTrayNumber, spec.PrimaryPHASE, flowbasis, refValue, originValue, !spec.bInactiveSpec);
			}
			else
			{
				_Data.ConvertNotes.Add(string.Format(P2Globalization.GetString("LSNotConvert"), spec.P2SpecExpression, _Data.UID));
			}
		}
		else if (spec.PrimaryPropertyName.ToUpper().Trim().StartsWith("D1160"))
		{
			int startIndex = spec.PrimaryPropertyName.LastIndexOf(",");
			string text = spec.PrimaryPropertyName.Substring(startIndex);
			if (text.ToUpper().Trim() == "P10")
			{
				text = "D1160Vac";
			}
			else if (text.ToUpper().Trim() == "P760")
			{
				text = "D1160ATM";
			}
			originValue = UnitConverter.GetHysysValue(UnitClassEnum.Temperature, spec.Value.Value, unit);
			if (spec.Operator == Specification.OperatorType.minus)
			{
				flowbasis = "V";
				string flowbasis4 = "V";
				string unit4 = string.Empty;
				double refValue4 = -1.0;
				ParseUtilty.GetFlowbasisAndUnit(spec.ReferencePropertyName, ref flowbasis4, ref unit4, ref refValue4);
				if (refValue4 < -1.0)
				{
					GetAssayIPEPValue(ref refValue4);
				}
				colSpecBD = ColumnGapCutPointSpec(columnData.UID, text, spec.PrimaryTrayNumber, spec.ReferenceTrayNumber, spec.PrimaryPHASE, flowbasis, refValue, refValue4, originValue, !spec.bInactiveSpec);
			}
			else if (spec.Operator == Specification.OperatorType.none)
			{
				flowbasis = "V";
				colSpecBD = ColumnCutPointSpec(columnData.UID, text, spec.PrimaryTrayNumber, spec.PrimaryPHASE, flowbasis, refValue, originValue, !spec.bInactiveSpec);
			}
			else
			{
				_Data.ConvertNotes.Add(string.Format(P2Globalization.GetString("LSNotConvert"), spec.P2SpecExpression, _Data.UID));
			}
		}
		else if (spec.PrimaryPropertyName.ToUpper().Trim().StartsWith("D2887"))
		{
			originValue = UnitConverter.GetHysysValue(UnitClassEnum.Temperature, spec.Value.Value, unit);
			if (spec.Operator == Specification.OperatorType.minus)
			{
				flowbasis = "WT";
				string flowbasis5 = "WT";
				string unit5 = string.Empty;
				double refValue5 = -1.0;
				ParseUtilty.GetFlowbasisAndUnit(spec.ReferencePropertyName, ref flowbasis5, ref unit5, ref refValue5);
				if (refValue5 < -1.0)
				{
					GetAssayIPEPValue(ref refValue5);
				}
				colSpecBD = ColumnGapCutPointSpec(columnData.UID, "ASTMD2887", spec.PrimaryTrayNumber, spec.ReferenceTrayNumber, spec.PrimaryPHASE, flowbasis, refValue, refValue5, originValue, !spec.bInactiveSpec);
			}
			else if (spec.Operator == Specification.OperatorType.none)
			{
				flowbasis = "WT";
				colSpecBD = ColumnCutPointSpec(columnData.UID, "ASTMD2887", spec.PrimaryTrayNumber, spec.PrimaryPHASE, flowbasis, refValue, originValue, !spec.bInactiveSpec);
			}
			else
			{
				_Data.ConvertNotes.Add(string.Format(P2Globalization.GetString("LSNotConvert"), spec.P2SpecExpression, _Data.UID));
			}
		}
		else if (spec.PrimaryPropertyName.ToUpper().Trim().StartsWith(CUnitOperation.FRACTION) || spec.PrimaryPropertyName.ToUpper().Trim().StartsWith("PPM") || spec.PrimaryPropertyName.ToUpper().Trim().StartsWith("PCT"))
		{
			originValue = new DoubleValue(spec.Value.Unit, spec.Value.Value);
			if (spec.Operator == Specification.OperatorType.none)
			{
				if (spec.PrimaryStartComponent > 0)
				{
					List<int> list5 = new List<int>();
					for (int m = spec.PrimaryStartComponent; m <= spec.PrimaryEndComponent; m++)
					{
						list5.Add(m);
					}
					if (spec.PrimaryPropertyName.ToUpper().Trim().StartsWith("PPM"))
					{
						originValue.Value = spec.Value.Value / 1000000.0;
					}
					else if (spec.PrimaryPropertyName.ToUpper().Trim().StartsWith("PCT"))
					{
						originValue.Value = spec.Value.Value / 100.0;
					}
					if (ConvertedGVValueToMoleValue(spec.PrimaryPHASE, flowbasis, ref originValue))
					{
						flowbasis = "M";
					}
					colSpecBD = ColumnComponentFractionSpec(columnData.UID, bStreamType: false, spec.PrimaryID, spec.PrimaryTrayNumber, spec.PrimaryPHASE, flowbasis, list5, originValue, !spec.bInactiveSpec);
				}
				else
				{
					_Data.ConvertNotes.Add(string.Format(P2Globalization.GetString("LSNotConvert"), spec.P2SpecExpression, _Data.UID));
				}
			}
			else if (spec.Operator == Specification.OperatorType.divide)
			{
				List<int> list6 = new List<int>();
				List<int> list7 = new List<int>();
				for (int n = spec.PrimaryStartComponent; n <= spec.PrimaryEndComponent; n++)
				{
					list6.Add(n);
				}
				for (int num = spec.ReferenceStartComponent; num <= spec.ReferenceEndComponent; num++)
				{
					list7.Add(num);
				}
				colSpecBD = ColumnComponentRatioSpec(columnData.UID, bStreamType: false, spec.PrimaryID, spec.PrimaryTrayNumber, spec.PrimaryPHASE, flowbasis, list6, list7, originValue, !spec.bInactiveSpec);
			}
			else
			{
				_Data.ConvertNotes.Add(string.Format(P2Globalization.GetString("LSNotConvert"), spec.P2SpecExpression, _Data.UID));
			}
		}
		else
		{
			if (spec.Operator != Specification.OperatorType.none)
			{
				_Data.ConvertNotes.Add(string.Format(P2Globalization.GetString("LSNotConvert"), spec.P2SpecExpression, _Data.UID));
				return;
			}
			if (spec.PrimaryPropertyName.ToUpper().Trim().StartsWith(CUnitOperation.TEMPERATURE))
			{
				originValue = UnitConverter.GetHysysValue(UnitClassEnum.Temperature, spec.Value);
				colSpecBD = ColumnTemperatureSpec(columnData.UID, spec.PrimaryTrayNumber, originValue, !spec.bInactiveSpec);
			}
			else if (spec.PrimaryPropertyName.ToUpper().Trim().StartsWith("VISC"))
			{
				originValue = UnitConverter.GetHysysValue(UnitClassEnum.Viscosity, spec.Value);
				colSpecBD = ColumnTransportPropertySpec(columnData.UID, spec.PrimaryTrayNumber, "Viscosity", spec.PrimaryPHASE, null, originValue, !spec.bInactiveSpec);
			}
			else if (spec.PrimaryPropertyName.ToUpper().Trim().StartsWith("COND"))
			{
				originValue = UnitConverter.GetHysysValue(UnitClassEnum.ThermalConductivity, spec.Value);
				colSpecBD = ColumnTransportPropertySpec(columnData.UID, spec.PrimaryTrayNumber, "Thermo Conductivity", spec.PrimaryPHASE, null, originValue, !spec.bInactiveSpec);
			}
			else if (spec.PrimaryPropertyName.ToUpper().Trim().StartsWith("SURF"))
			{
				originValue = UnitConverter.GetHysysValue(UnitClassEnum.SurfaceTension, spec.Value);
				colSpecBD = ColumnTransportPropertySpec(columnData.UID, spec.PrimaryTrayNumber, "Surface Tension", spec.PrimaryPHASE, null, originValue, !spec.bInactiveSpec);
			}
			else if (spec.PrimaryPropertyName.ToUpper().Trim().StartsWith("KVIS"))
			{
				string[] array = ParseUtilty.CutByEqual(spec.PrimPropRefTemp);
				unit = string.Empty;
				double num2 = -1000.0;
				if (array.Length != 0)
				{
					if (array[0].Contains("("))
					{
						unit = ParseUtilty.GetParentheses(array[0]);
					}
					num2 = double.Parse(array[1]);
				}
				DoubleValue refTemp = null;
				if (num2 > -1000.0)
				{
					refTemp = UnitConverter.GetHysysValue(UnitClassEnum.Temperature, num2, unit);
				}
				originValue = UnitConverter.GetHysysValue(UnitClassEnum.KinematicViscosity, spec.Value);
				colSpecBD = ColumnTransportPropertySpec(columnData.UID, spec.PrimaryTrayNumber, "Kinematic Viscosity", spec.PrimaryPHASE, refTemp, originValue, !spec.bInactiveSpec);
			}
			else if (spec.PrimaryPropertyName.ToUpper().Trim().StartsWith("MW"))
			{
				originValue = UnitConverter.GetHysysValue(UnitClassEnum.Mass, spec.Value);
				colSpecBD = ColumnPhysicalPropertySpec(columnData.UID, "Molecular WT", spec.PrimaryTrayNumber, flowbasis, spec.PrimaryPHASE, originValue, !spec.bInactiveSpec);
			}
			else if (spec.PrimaryPropertyName.ToUpper().Trim().StartsWith("API"))
			{
				originValue = UnitConverter.GetHysysValue(UnitClassEnum.PetroDensity, spec.Value.Value, "API");
				colSpecBD = ColumnPhysicalPropertySpec(columnData.UID, "Avg. Mass Liq Den", spec.PrimaryTrayNumber, flowbasis, spec.PrimaryPHASE, originValue, !spec.bInactiveSpec);
			}
			else if (spec.PrimaryPropertyName.ToUpper().Trim().StartsWith("SPGR"))
			{
				spec.Value.Unit = "KG/M3";
				DoubleValue doubleValue = new DoubleValue();
				doubleValue.Value = spec.Value.Value * 1000.0;
				doubleValue.Unit = spec.Value.Unit;
				colSpecBD = ColumnPhysicalPropertySpec(specVal: (!(spec.PrimaryPHASE == "V")) ? UnitConverter.GetHysysValue(UnitClassEnum.LiquidDensity, doubleValue) : UnitConverter.GetHysysValue(UnitClassEnum.VaporDensity, doubleValue), columnID: columnData.UID, propName: "Avg. Mass Liq Den", iStage: spec.PrimaryTrayNumber, flowbasis: flowbasis, phase: spec.PrimaryPHASE, bActive: !spec.bInactiveSpec);
			}
			else if (spec.PrimaryPropertyName.ToUpper().Trim().StartsWith("DENS"))
			{
				originValue = ((!(spec.PrimaryPHASE == "V")) ? UnitConverter.GetHysysValue(UnitClassEnum.LiquidDensity, spec.Value) : UnitConverter.GetHysysValue(UnitClassEnum.VaporDensity, spec.Value));
				colSpecBD = ColumnPhysicalPropertySpec(columnData.UID, "Avg. Mass Liq Den", spec.PrimaryTrayNumber, flowbasis, spec.PrimaryPHASE, originValue, !spec.bInactiveSpec);
			}
			else if (spec.PrimaryPropertyName.ToUpper().Trim().StartsWith("MOLV"))
			{
				DoubleValue doubleValue2 = new DoubleValue();
				if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(spec.Value.Unit))
				{
					if (spec.PrimaryPHASE == "V")
					{
						doubleValue2.Unit = UnitConverter.GetP2GlobalUnit(UnitClassEnum.VaporDensity);
					}
					else
					{
						doubleValue2.Unit = UnitConverter.GetP2GlobalUnit(UnitClassEnum.LiquidDensity);
					}
				}
				else
				{
					unit = spec.Value.Unit;
					string[] array2 = unit.Split('/');
					doubleValue2.Unit = array2[1].Trim() + "/" + array2[0].Trim();
				}
				doubleValue2.Value = 1.0 / spec.Value.Value;
				colSpecBD = ColumnPhysicalPropertySpec(specVal: (!(spec.PrimaryPHASE == "V")) ? UnitConverter.GetHysysValue(UnitClassEnum.LiquidDensity, doubleValue2) : UnitConverter.GetHysysValue(UnitClassEnum.VaporDensity, doubleValue2), columnID: columnData.UID, propName: "Avg. Mass Liq Den", iStage: spec.PrimaryTrayNumber, flowbasis: flowbasis, phase: spec.PrimaryPHASE, bActive: !spec.bInactiveSpec);
			}
			else if (spec.PrimaryPropertyName.ToUpper().Trim().StartsWith("HOTV"))
			{
				TypeUnitValue typeUnitValue = null;
				if (spec.PrimaryPHASE == "V")
				{
					typeUnitValue = UnitConverter.GetHysysValue(UnitClassEnum.Rate, spec.Value.Value, spec.Value.Unit, "GV");
					originValue = new DoubleValue(typeUnitValue.Unit, typeUnitValue.Value);
					colSpecBD = ColumnVaporFlowSpec(columnData.UID, spec.PrimaryTrayNumber, "GV", originValue, !spec.bInactiveSpec);
				}
				else
				{
					typeUnitValue = UnitConverter.GetHysysValue(UnitClassEnum.Rate, spec.Value.Value, spec.Value.Unit, "LV");
					originValue = new DoubleValue(typeUnitValue.Unit, typeUnitValue.Value);
					colSpecBD = ColumnLiquidFlowSpec(columnData.UID, spec.PrimaryTrayNumber, "LV", originValue, !spec.bInactiveSpec);
				}
			}
			else if (spec.PrimaryPropertyName.ToUpper().Trim() == "RVP")
			{
				spec.Value.Unit = "psi";
				originValue = UnitConverter.GetHysysValue(UnitClassEnum.Pressure, spec.Value);
				colSpecBD = ColumnVaporPressureSpec(columnData.UID, spec.PrimaryTrayNumber, "Reid VP", spec.PrimaryPHASE, originValue, !spec.bInactiveSpec);
			}
			else if (spec.PrimaryPropertyName.ToUpper().Trim() == "TVP")
			{
				originValue = UnitConverter.GetHysysValue(UnitClassEnum.Pressure, spec.Value);
				colSpecBD = ColumnVaporPressureSpec(columnData.UID, spec.PrimaryTrayNumber, "TVP", spec.PrimaryPHASE, originValue, !spec.bInactiveSpec);
			}
			else
			{
				_Data.ConvertNotes.Add(string.Format(P2Globalization.GetString("LSNotConvert"), spec.P2SpecExpression, _Data.UID));
			}
		}
		SetSpecTolerance(spec, colSpecBD, originValue);
	}

	private void AddLocalDefine()
	{
		SetLocalDefine(_Data);
		foreach (string key in SideColumnData.Keys)
		{
			SetLocalDefine(SideColumnData[key]);
		}
	}

	private void SetLocalDefine(HYSYSColumn columnData)
	{
		try
		{
			if (columnData.LocalDefine.Count == 0)
			{
				return;
			}
			List<DEFINE> list = new List<DEFINE>();
			foreach (DEFINE item in columnData.LocalDefine)
			{
				if (item.MainProperty.ToUpper().Trim().StartsWith("PROD") && string.IsNullOrEmpty(item.OperatorName) && item.FirstRefProperty.ToUpper().StartsWith("RATE") && item.FirstRefCStartComp > 0 && string.IsNullOrEmpty(item.Value))
				{
					HYSYSStream streamByName = HYSYSStream.GetStreamByName(item.FirstRefID);
					if (streamByName != null && streamByName.Source == null && streamByName.Composition.Data.Count > 0)
					{
						ProcessStream processStream = GetProcessStream(item.FirstRefID);
						if (processStream != null)
						{
							DoubleValue doubleValue = new DoubleValue();
							for (int i = item.FirstRefCStartComp; i <= item.FirstRefCEndComp; i++)
							{
								doubleValue.Value += ((double[])processStream.ComponentMolarFlow.Values)[i - 1];
							}
							doubleValue.Unit = HYSYSUOMMap.GetDefault(UnitClassEnum.Rate, "M").Unit;
							ColumnDrawRateSpec(columnData.UID, ParseUtilty.GetStringValue(item.MainProperty), "M", doubleValue, bActive: true, bNeedStore: false);
							item.bConverted = true;
						}
					}
				}
				if (!item.bConverted)
				{
					list.Add(item);
				}
			}
			foreach (DEFINE item2 in list)
			{
				columnData.LocalDefine.Remove(item2);
				item2.bGlobalDefine = true;
			}
		}
		catch (Exception)
		{
			DebugPrint.OutputDebugString(DebugLevel.BuildHYSYSCase, WarningLevel.Error, string.Format(P2Globalization.GetString("AddLocalDefineFailed"), _Data.UID));
		}
	}

	private void AddGlobalSpec()
	{
		try
		{
			foreach (Specification item in _Data.GlobalSpec)
			{
				if (item.bGlobalSpec)
				{
					DebugPrint.OutputDebugString(DebugLevel.BuildHYSYSCase, WarningLevel.Warning, string.Format(P2Globalization.GetString("GSNotConvert"), item.P2SpecExpression, _Data.UID));
					_Data.ConvertNotes.Add(string.Format(P2Globalization.GetString("GSNotConvert"), item.P2SpecExpression, _Data.UID));
				}
			}
			if (SideColumnData.Count <= 0)
			{
				return;
			}
			foreach (string key in SideColumnData.Keys)
			{
				HYSYSColumn hYSYSColumn = SideColumnData[key];
				foreach (Specification item2 in hYSYSColumn.GlobalSpec)
				{
					if (item2.bGlobalSpec)
					{
						DebugPrint.OutputDebugString(DebugLevel.BuildHYSYSCase, WarningLevel.Warning, string.Format(P2Globalization.GetString("GSNotConvert1"), item2.P2SpecExpression, _Data.UID, key));
						hYSYSColumn.ConvertNotes.Add(string.Format(P2Globalization.GetString("GSNotConvert1"), item2.P2SpecExpression, _Data.UID, key));
					}
				}
			}
		}
		catch (Exception)
		{
			DebugPrint.OutputDebugString(DebugLevel.BuildHYSYSCase, WarningLevel.Error, string.Format(P2Globalization.GetString("AddGlobalSpecificationFailed"), _Data.UID));
		}
	}

	private void AddGlobalDefine()
	{
		SetGlobalDefine(_Data, bMain: true);
		if (SideColumnData.Count <= 0)
		{
			return;
		}
		foreach (string key in SideColumnData.Keys)
		{
			HYSYSColumn columnData = SideColumnData[key];
			SetGlobalDefine(columnData, bMain: false);
		}
	}

	private void SetGlobalDefine(HYSYSColumn columnData, bool bMain)
	{
		try
		{
			foreach (DEFINE item in columnData.GlobalDefine)
			{
				if (!item.bGlobalDefine || item.bConverted)
				{
					continue;
				}
				if (!item.bLinkToCalculator)
				{
					if (item.MainProperty.ToUpper().Trim().StartsWith("PTOP"))
					{
						continue;
					}
					if (item.MainProperty.ToUpper().Trim().StartsWith("DUTY") || item.MainProperty.ToUpper().Trim().StartsWith("HEAT"))
					{
						string parentheses = ParseUtilty.GetParentheses(item.MainProperty);
						parentheses = ParseUtilty.CutByComma(parentheses)[0];
						int num = int.Parse(parentheses);
						if (paDutyID.Keys.Contains(num))
						{
							string specName = columnData.UID;
							string paName = columnData.UID;
							GetPumparoundInfo(parentheses, ref specName, ref paName, bPumpAround: false);
							ColumnPumpAroundSpec(columnData.UID, specName, paName, "Duty", "", null, bActive: true);
							item.sColumnSpecName = "Flowsht.600/ColumnSpec.500." + (_specNumber - 1) + "(" + specName + "_Duty):ExtraData.200";
						}
						else
						{
							string heatStreamNameFromDutyID = GetHeatStreamNameFromDutyID(columnData, num);
							ColumnDutySpec(columnData.UID, heatStreamNameFromDutyID, null, bActive: true);
							item.sColumnSpecName = "Flowsht.600/ColumnSpec.500." + (_specNumber - 1) + "(" + columnData.UID + "_" + heatStreamNameFromDutyID + "_Duty):ExtraData.200";
						}
						item.bConverted = true;
					}
					else if (item.MainProperty.ToUpper().Trim().StartsWith("DNAM"))
					{
						string dutyIDFromDutyName = GetDutyIDFromDutyName(columnData, item.MainProperty);
						int num2 = int.Parse(dutyIDFromDutyName);
						if (paDutyID.Keys.Contains(num2))
						{
							string specName2 = columnData.UID;
							string paName2 = columnData.UID;
							GetPumparoundInfo(dutyIDFromDutyName, ref specName2, ref paName2, bPumpAround: false);
							ColumnPumpAroundSpec(columnData.UID, specName2, paName2, "Duty", "", null, bActive: true);
							item.sColumnSpecName = "Flowsht.600/ColumnSpec.500." + (_specNumber - 1) + "(" + specName2 + "_Duty):ExtraData.200";
						}
						else
						{
							string heatStreamNameFromDutyID2 = GetHeatStreamNameFromDutyID(columnData, num2);
							ColumnDutySpec(columnData.UID, heatStreamNameFromDutyID2, null, bActive: true);
							item.sColumnSpecName = "Flowsht.600/ColumnSpec.500." + (_specNumber - 1) + "(" + columnData.UID + "_" + heatStreamNameFromDutyID2 + "_Duty):ExtraData.200";
						}
						item.bConverted = true;
					}
					else if (!item.MainProperty.ToUpper().Trim().StartsWith("DPCO"))
					{
						if (item.MainProperty.ToUpper().Trim().StartsWith("PARA"))
						{
							string specName3 = columnData.UID;
							string paName3 = columnData.UID;
							GetPumparoundInfo(item.MainProperty, ref specName3, ref paName3, bPumpAround: true);
							ColumnPumpAroundSpec(columnData.UID, specName3, paName3, "Rate", "M", null, bActive: true);
							item.sColumnSpecName = "Flowsht.600/ColumnSpec.500." + (_specNumber - 1) + "(" + specName3 + "_Rate):ExtraData.200";
							item.bConverted = true;
						}
						else if (item.MainProperty.ToUpper().Trim().StartsWith("PATE"))
						{
							string specName4 = columnData.UID;
							string paName4 = columnData.UID;
							GetPumparoundInfo(item.MainProperty, ref specName4, ref paName4, bPumpAround: true);
							ColumnPumpAroundSpec(columnData.UID, specName4, paName4, "ReturnT", "", null, bActive: true);
							item.sColumnSpecName = "Flowsht.600/ColumnSpec.500." + (_specNumber - 1) + "(" + specName4 + "_ReturnT):ExtraData.200";
							item.bConverted = true;
						}
						else if (item.MainProperty.ToUpper().Trim().StartsWith("PROD"))
						{
							string stringValue = ParseUtilty.GetStringValue(item.MainProperty);
							ColumnDrawRateSpec(columnData.UID, stringValue, "M", null, bActive: true, bNeedStore: false);
							item.sColumnSpecName = "Flowsht.600/ColumnSpec.500." + (_specNumber - 1) + "(" + columnData.UID + "_DrawFlowRate_" + stringValue + "):ExtraData.200";
							item.bConverted = true;
						}
						else if (!item.MainProperty.ToUpper().Trim().StartsWith("TSVF") && !item.MainProperty.ToUpper().Trim().StartsWith("TSTE") && !item.MainProperty.ToUpper().Trim().StartsWith("TSDT"))
						{
						}
					}
					continue;
				}
				if (bMain)
				{
					if (item.MainProperty.ToUpper().Trim().StartsWith("PTOP"))
					{
						DebugPrint.OutputDebugString(DebugLevel.BuildHYSYSCase, WarningLevel.Warning, string.Format(P2Globalization.GetString("PressureDataInComplete"), item.P2DefineExpression, _Data.UID));
					}
					else
					{
						DebugPrint.OutputDebugString(DebugLevel.BuildHYSYSCase, WarningLevel.Warning, string.Format(P2Globalization.GetString("ColumnDefineNotConvert"), _Data.UID, item.P2DefineExpression));
					}
					_Data.ConvertNotes.Add(string.Format(P2Globalization.GetString("ColumnDefineNotConvert"), _Data.UID, item.P2DefineExpression));
				}
				else
				{
					DebugPrint.OutputDebugString(DebugLevel.BuildHYSYSCase, WarningLevel.Warning, string.Format(P2Globalization.GetString("GDNotConvert1"), _Data.UID, columnData.UID, item.P2DefineExpression));
					columnData.ConvertNotes.Add(string.Format(P2Globalization.GetString("GDNotConvert1"), _Data.UID, columnData.UID, item.P2DefineExpression));
				}
				item.bConverted = true;
			}
		}
		catch (Exception)
		{
			DebugPrint.OutputDebugString(DebugLevel.BuildHYSYSCase, WarningLevel.Error, string.Format(P2Globalization.GetString("AddGlobalDefineFailed"), _Data.UID));
		}
	}

	private void GetPumparoundInfo(string sProp, ref string specName, ref string paName, bool bPumpAround)
	{
		PumpAround pumpAround = null;
		if (bPumpAround)
		{
			string text = string.Empty;
			if (sProp.Contains("("))
			{
				text = ParseUtilty.GetParentheses(sProp);
			}
			if (text.Contains(','))
			{
				text = ParseUtilty.CutByComma(text)[0];
			}
			pumpAround = paDic[int.Parse(text)];
		}
		else
		{
			pumpAround = paDutyID[int.Parse(sProp)];
		}
		int sourceTrayNo = pumpAround.SourceTrayNo;
		int targetTrayNo = pumpAround.TargetTrayNo;
		sourceTrayNo -= _InternalTrayNumberDiff;
		targetTrayNo -= _InternalTrayNumberDiff;
		if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(pumpAround.Name))
		{
			specName = specName + "_PA_" + targetTrayNo;
			paName = paName + "_PA_" + sourceTrayNo + "_TO_" + targetTrayNo;
		}
		else
		{
			specName = specName + "_" + pumpAround.Name;
			paName = paName + "_PA_" + pumpAround.Name;
		}
	}

	private string GetHeatStreamNameFromDutyID(HYSYSColumn columnData, int dutyID)
	{
		string result = string.Empty;
		foreach (int key in columnData.DutySpec[dutyID].Keys)
		{
			result = ((key == columnData.stageNumber && columnData.bReboilerExist) ? (columnData.UID + "_Reb_Q") : ((key != 1 || !columnData.bCondenserExist) ? (columnData.UID + "_Duty_" + dutyID) : ("Q-Condenser_" + columnData.UID)));
		}
		return result;
	}

	private string GetDutyIDFromDutyName(HYSYSColumn columnData, string sProp)
	{
		string result = string.Empty;
		string stringValue = ParseUtilty.GetStringValue(sProp);
		foreach (int key in columnData.DutySpec.Keys)
		{
			if (columnData.DutySpec[key].Values.First().Name == stringValue)
			{
				result = key.ToString();
				break;
			}
		}
		return result;
	}

	private void AddRefluxOrRRSpec()
	{
		DoubleValue doubleValue = null;
		bool bActive = false;
		if (_ColumnFlowsheet.DegreesOfFreedom > 0)
		{
			bActive = true;
		}
		if (_Data.EstRRatio != null)
		{
			bool bLight = true;
			bool bTotalLiq = false;
			string flowbasis = _Data.EstRRatio.Type;
			bool bIncludeV = true;
			GetRRSpecproperty(_Data.EstRRatio.Name, ref flowbasis, ref bLight, ref bTotalLiq);
			doubleValue = UnitConverter.GetHysysValue(UnitClassEnum.Fraction, _Data.EstRRatio.Value, _Data.EstRRatio.Unit);
			if (_Data.EstRRatio.Name == "LF")
			{
				ColumnRefluxFeedRatioSpec(_Data.UID, _Data.HYSYSMainFlowsheetFeeds, flowbasis, bLight, doubleValue, bActive);
			}
			else
			{
				ColumnRefluxRatioSpec(_Data.UID, flowbasis, bIncludeV, bTotalLiq, doubleValue, bActive);
			}
		}
		else if (_Data.EstReflux != null)
		{
			doubleValue = new DoubleValue(_Data.EstReflux.Unit, _Data.EstReflux.Value);
			ColumnLiquidFlowSpec(_Data.UID, 0, _Data.EstReflux.Type, doubleValue, bActive);
		}
	}

	private bool IsDuplicateOfSpec(string streamID)
	{
		bool flag = false;
		flag = FoundStreamSpec(_Data, streamID);
		if (!flag)
		{
			foreach (HYSYSColumn value in SideColumnData.Values)
			{
				flag = FoundStreamSpec(value, streamID);
				if (flag)
				{
					break;
				}
			}
		}
		return flag;
	}

	private bool FoundStreamSpec(HYSYSColumn columnData, string streamID)
	{
		bool flag = false;
		foreach (Specification item in columnData.LocalSpec)
		{
			if (item.SpecType == SpecificationType.STREAM && item.PrimaryID == streamID && item.PrimaryPropertyName.StartsWith(CUnitOperation.RATE) && item.PrimaryStartComponent == 0 && item.Operator == Specification.OperatorType.none)
			{
				flag = true;
				break;
			}
		}
		if (!flag)
		{
			foreach (Specification item2 in columnData.GlobalSpec)
			{
				if (item2.SpecType == SpecificationType.STREAM && item2.PrimaryID == streamID && item2.PrimaryPropertyName.StartsWith(CUnitOperation.RATE) && item2.Operator == Specification.OperatorType.none)
				{
					flag = true;
					break;
				}
			}
		}
		return flag;
	}

	private bool IsActiveDutyspec(string dutyNO, string dutyName)
	{
		if (_Data.VaryDutyIDs.Contains(dutyNO))
		{
			return false;
		}
		if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(dutyName) && _Data.VaryDutyNames.Contains(dutyName.Trim()))
		{
			return false;
		}
		return true;
	}

	private bool IsActiveDrawSpec(string streamID)
	{
		bool result = false;
		if (!_Data.VaryDrawStreamIDs.Contains(streamID))
		{
			result = true;
		}
		return result;
	}

	private void ReAnalyzeActiveOfVarySpec()
	{
		if (colStrmSpecsBeVaried.Count == 0 || _ColumnFlowsheet.DegreesOfFreedom <= 0)
		{
			return;
		}
		foreach (ColumnSpecification item in colStrmSpecsBeVaried)
		{
			if (_ColumnFlowsheet.DegreesOfFreedom > 0)
			{
				item.IsActive = true;
				item.IsUsedAsEstimate = true;
				continue;
			}
			break;
		}
	}

	private BackDoor ColumnColdPropertiesSpec(string columnID, string sName, int iStage, string sType, string sPhase, DoubleValue specValue, bool bActive)
	{
		try
		{
			HYSYSColumn columnData = null;
			TraySection traySec = null;
			int trayDiff = 0;
			int num = iStage;
			InitColumnGeneralData(columnID, ref trayDiff, ref columnData, ref traySec);
			num -= trayDiff;
			ColumnSpecification obj = _ColumnFlowsheet.Specifications.Add(columnID + "_" + sName, "ClmColdPropEnvObject") as ColumnSpecification;
			_specNumber++;
			if (_ColumnFlowsheet.DegreesOfFreedom == 0)
			{
				bActive = false;
			}
			obj.IsActive = bActive;
			obj.IsUsedAsEstimate = bActive;
			BackDoor backDoor = obj as BackDoor;
			SetSpecStage(iStage, num, sName, backDoor, columnData, traySec, ":SepStage.200");
			InternalRealVariable internalRealVariable = backDoor.get_BackDoorVariable((object)":Selection.301").Variable as InternalRealVariable;
			switch (sType)
			{
			case "Pour Point":
				internalRealVariable.Value = 41.0;
				break;
			case "RON":
				internalRealVariable.Value = 42.0;
				break;
			case "Flash Point":
				internalRealVariable.Value = 40.0;
				break;
			}
			(backDoor.get_BackDoorVariable((object)":Selection.300").Variable as InternalRealVariable).Value = GetHYSYSPhaseValue(sPhase);
			if (specValue != null)
			{
				(backDoor.get_BackDoorVariable((object)":ExtraData.200").Variable as InternalRealVariable).SetValue(specValue.Value, specValue.Unit);
			}
			return backDoor;
		}
		catch (Exception)
		{
			DebugPrint.OutputDebugString(DebugLevel.BuildHYSYSCase, WarningLevel.Error, string.Format(P2Globalization.GetString("AddColdPropertySpecificationFailed"), _Data.UID));
			return null;
		}
	}

	private BackDoor ColumnComponentFlowSpec(string columnID, bool bStreamType, string streamName, int iStage, string phase, string flowbasis, List<int> components, DoubleValue specVal, bool bActive)
	{
		try
		{
			HYSYSColumn columnData = null;
			TraySection traySec = null;
			int trayDiff = 0;
			int num = iStage;
			InitColumnGeneralData(columnID, ref trayDiff, ref columnData, ref traySec);
			num -= trayDiff;
			string text = columnID + "_CompFlowSpec_";
			text = (string.IsNullOrEmpty(streamName) ? (text + num) : (text + streamName));
			ColumnSpecification obj = _ColumnFlowsheet.Specifications.Add(text, "ClmCompFlowSpecObject") as ColumnSpecification;
			_specNumber++;
			if (_ColumnFlowsheet.DegreesOfFreedom == 0)
			{
				bActive = false;
			}
			obj.IsActive = bActive;
			obj.IsUsedAsEstimate = bActive;
			BackDoor backDoor = obj as BackDoor;
			InternalRealVariable internalRealVariable = backDoor.get_BackDoorVariable((object)":Selection.310").Variable as InternalRealVariable;
			if (!bStreamType)
			{
				internalRealVariable.Value = 1.0;
			}
			else
			{
				internalRealVariable.Value = 0.0;
			}
			if (bStreamType)
			{
				ProcessStream processStream = _ColumnFlowsheet.MaterialStreams[streamName] as ProcessStream;
				backDoor.SendAttachObject(":Stream.400", processStream);
			}
			else
			{
				SetSpecStage(iStage, num, text, backDoor, columnData, traySec, ":SepStage.200");
				(backDoor.get_BackDoorVariable((object)":Enum.311").Variable as InternalRealVariable).Value = GetHYSYSPhaseValue(phase);
			}
			(backDoor.get_BackDoorVariable((object)":Selection.300").Variable as InternalRealVariable).Value = GetHYSYSFlowBasisValue(flowbasis);
			if (specVal != null && flowbasis != "GV")
			{
				(backDoor.get_BackDoorVariable((object)":ExtraData.200").Variable as InternalRealVariable).SetValue(specVal.Value, specVal.Unit);
			}
			int num2 = 0;
			foreach (int component in components)
			{
				string hYSYSCompName = P2Objects.P2Component.GetHYSYSCompName(component);
				if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(hYSYSCompName))
				{
					Component hYSYSComponet = GetHYSYSComponet(hYSYSCompName);
					if (hYSYSComponet != null)
					{
						backDoor.SendAttachObject(":AbstractComp.500." + num2, hYSYSComponet);
						num2++;
					}
					else
					{
						_Data.ConvertNotes.Add(string.Format(P2Globalization.GetString("NoCompsFound"), hYSYSCompName, text));
					}
				}
			}
			return backDoor;
		}
		catch (Exception)
		{
			DebugPrint.OutputDebugString(DebugLevel.BuildHYSYSCase, WarningLevel.Error, string.Format(P2Globalization.GetString("AddComponentFlowSpecificationFailed"), _Data.UID));
			return null;
		}
	}

	private BackDoor ColumnComponentFractionSpec(string columnID, bool bStreamType, string streamName, int iStage, string phase, string flowbasis, List<int> components, DoubleValue specVal, bool bActive)
	{
		try
		{
			HYSYSColumn columnData = null;
			TraySection traySec = null;
			int trayDiff = 0;
			int num = iStage;
			InitColumnGeneralData(columnID, ref trayDiff, ref columnData, ref traySec);
			num -= trayDiff;
			string text = columnID + "_CompFracSpec_";
			text = (string.IsNullOrEmpty(streamName) ? (text + num) : (text + streamName));
			ColumnSpecification obj = _ColumnFlowsheet.Specifications.Add(text, "ClmCompFracSpecObject") as ColumnSpecification;
			_specNumber++;
			if (_ColumnFlowsheet.DegreesOfFreedom == 0)
			{
				bActive = false;
			}
			obj.IsActive = bActive;
			obj.IsUsedAsEstimate = bActive;
			BackDoor backDoor = obj as BackDoor;
			InternalRealVariable internalRealVariable = backDoor.get_BackDoorVariable((object)":Selection.310").Variable as InternalRealVariable;
			if (!bStreamType)
			{
				internalRealVariable.Value = 1.0;
			}
			else
			{
				internalRealVariable.Value = 0.0;
			}
			if (bStreamType)
			{
				ProcessStream processStream = _ColumnFlowsheet.MaterialStreams[streamName] as ProcessStream;
				backDoor.SendAttachObject(":Stream.300", processStream);
			}
			else
			{
				SetSpecStage(iStage, num, text, backDoor, columnData, traySec, ":SepStage.200");
				(backDoor.get_BackDoorVariable((object)":Selection.301").Variable as InternalRealVariable).Value = GetHYSYSPhaseValue(phase);
			}
			(backDoor.get_BackDoorVariable((object)":Selection.300").Variable as InternalRealVariable).Value = GetHYSYSFracBasisValue(flowbasis);
			if (specVal != null)
			{
				(backDoor.get_BackDoorVariable((object)":ExtraData.200").Variable as InternalRealVariable).SetValue(specVal.Value, "");
			}
			int num2 = 0;
			foreach (int component in components)
			{
				string hYSYSCompName = P2Objects.P2Component.GetHYSYSCompName(component);
				if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(hYSYSCompName))
				{
					Component hYSYSComponet = GetHYSYSComponet(hYSYSCompName);
					if (hYSYSComponet != null)
					{
						backDoor.SendAttachObject(":AbstractComp.300." + num2, hYSYSComponet);
						num2++;
					}
					else
					{
						_Data.ConvertNotes.Add(string.Format(P2Globalization.GetString("NoCompsFound"), hYSYSCompName, text));
					}
				}
			}
			return backDoor;
		}
		catch (Exception)
		{
			DebugPrint.OutputDebugString(DebugLevel.BuildHYSYSCase, WarningLevel.Error, string.Format(P2Globalization.GetString("AddComponentFractionSpecificationFailed"), _Data.UID));
			return null;
		}
	}

	private BackDoor ColumnComponentRatioSpec(string columnID, bool bStreamType, string streamName, int iStage, string phase, string flowbasis, List<int> NumeratorComps, List<int> DenominatorComps, DoubleValue specVal, bool bActive)
	{
		try
		{
			HYSYSColumn columnData = null;
			TraySection traySec = null;
			int trayDiff = 0;
			int num = iStage;
			InitColumnGeneralData(columnID, ref trayDiff, ref columnData, ref traySec);
			num -= trayDiff;
			string text = columnID + "_CompRatioSpec_";
			text = (string.IsNullOrEmpty(streamName) ? (text + num) : (text + streamName));
			ColumnSpecification obj = _ColumnFlowsheet.Specifications.Add(text, "ClmCompRatioSpecObject") as ColumnSpecification;
			_specNumber++;
			if (_ColumnFlowsheet.DegreesOfFreedom == 0)
			{
				bActive = false;
			}
			obj.IsActive = bActive;
			obj.IsUsedAsEstimate = bActive;
			BackDoor backDoor = obj as BackDoor;
			InternalRealVariable internalRealVariable = backDoor.get_BackDoorVariable((object)":Selection.310").Variable as InternalRealVariable;
			if (!bStreamType)
			{
				internalRealVariable.Value = 1.0;
			}
			else
			{
				internalRealVariable.Value = 0.0;
			}
			if (bStreamType)
			{
				ProcessStream processStream = _ColumnFlowsheet.MaterialStreams[streamName] as ProcessStream;
				backDoor.SendAttachObject(":Stream.300", processStream);
			}
			else
			{
				SetSpecStage(iStage, num, text, backDoor, columnData, traySec, ":SepStage.200");
				(backDoor.get_BackDoorVariable((object)":Selection.301").Variable as InternalRealVariable).Value = GetHYSYSPhaseValue(phase);
			}
			(backDoor.get_BackDoorVariable((object)":Selection.300").Variable as InternalRealVariable).Value = GetHYSYSFracBasisValue(flowbasis);
			if (specVal != null)
			{
				(backDoor.get_BackDoorVariable((object)":ExtraData.200").Variable as InternalRealVariable).SetValue(specVal.Value, "");
			}
			int num2 = 0;
			foreach (int NumeratorComp in NumeratorComps)
			{
				string hYSYSCompName = P2Objects.P2Component.GetHYSYSCompName(NumeratorComp);
				if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(hYSYSCompName))
				{
					Component hYSYSComponet = GetHYSYSComponet(hYSYSCompName);
					if (hYSYSComponet != null)
					{
						backDoor.SendAttachObject(":Comp.300." + num2, hYSYSComponet);
						num2++;
					}
					else
					{
						_Data.ConvertNotes.Add(string.Format(P2Globalization.GetString("NoCompsFound"), hYSYSCompName, text));
					}
				}
			}
			foreach (int DenominatorComp in DenominatorComps)
			{
				string hYSYSCompName2 = P2Objects.P2Component.GetHYSYSCompName(DenominatorComp);
				if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(hYSYSCompName2))
				{
					Component hYSYSComponet2 = GetHYSYSComponet(hYSYSCompName2);
					if (hYSYSComponet2 != null)
					{
						backDoor.SendAttachObject(":Comp.400." + num2, hYSYSComponet2);
						num2++;
					}
					else
					{
						_Data.ConvertNotes.Add(string.Format(P2Globalization.GetString("NoCompsFound"), hYSYSCompName2, text));
					}
				}
			}
			return backDoor;
		}
		catch (Exception)
		{
			DebugPrint.OutputDebugString(DebugLevel.BuildHYSYSCase, WarningLevel.Error, string.Format(P2Globalization.GetString("AddComponentRatioSpecificationFailed"), _Data.UID));
			return null;
		}
	}

	private BackDoor ColumnCutPointSpec(string columnID, string sType, int iStage, string phase, string flowbasis, double cutPoint, DoubleValue specVal, bool bActive)
	{
		try
		{
			HYSYSColumn columnData = null;
			TraySection traySec = null;
			int trayDiff = 0;
			int num = iStage;
			InitColumnGeneralData(columnID, ref trayDiff, ref columnData, ref traySec);
			num -= trayDiff;
			string text = columnID + "_CutPointSpec_" + sType;
			ColumnSpecification obj = _ColumnFlowsheet.Specifications.Add(text, "ClmCutPointSpecObject") as ColumnSpecification;
			_specNumber++;
			if (_ColumnFlowsheet.DegreesOfFreedom == 0)
			{
				bActive = false;
			}
			obj.IsActive = bActive;
			obj.IsUsedAsEstimate = bActive;
			BackDoor backDoor = obj as BackDoor;
			(backDoor.get_BackDoorVariable((object)":Selection.301").Variable as InternalRealVariable).Value = GetHYSYSCutPointTypeValue(sType);
			SetSpecStage(iStage, num, text, backDoor, columnData, traySec, ":SepStage.200");
			(backDoor.get_BackDoorVariable((object)":Selection.302").Variable as InternalRealVariable).Value = GetHYSYSFracBasisValue(flowbasis);
			(backDoor.get_BackDoorVariable((object)":Selection.300").Variable as InternalRealVariable).Value = GetHYSYSPhaseValue(phase);
			(backDoor.get_BackDoorVariable((object)":User.300").Variable as InternalRealVariable).Value = cutPoint;
			if (specVal != null)
			{
				(backDoor.get_BackDoorVariable((object)":ExtraData.200").Variable as InternalRealVariable).SetValue(specVal.Value, specVal.Unit);
			}
			SetAssayCorrelationType(backDoor, sType);
			return backDoor;
		}
		catch (Exception)
		{
			DebugPrint.OutputDebugString(DebugLevel.BuildHYSYSCase, WarningLevel.Error, string.Format(P2Globalization.GetString("AddCutPointSpecificationFailed"), _Data.UID));
			return null;
		}
	}

	private BackDoor ColumnDrawRateSpec(string columnID, string streamName, string flowbasis, DoubleValue specVal, bool bActive, bool bNeedStore)
	{
		try
		{
			ColumnSpecification columnSpecification = null;
			columnSpecification = _ColumnFlowsheet.Specifications.Add(columnID + "_DrawFlowRate_" + streamName, "ClmDrawSpecObject") as ColumnSpecification;
			_specNumber++;
			if (_ColumnFlowsheet.DegreesOfFreedom == 0)
			{
				bActive = false;
			}
			columnSpecification.IsActive = bActive;
			columnSpecification.IsUsedAsEstimate = bActive;
			BackDoor backDoor = columnSpecification as BackDoor;
			ProcessStream processStream = _ColumnFlowsheet.MaterialStreams[streamName] as ProcessStream;
			backDoor.SendAttachObject(":Stream.400", processStream);
			(backDoor.get_BackDoorVariable((object)":Selection.300").Variable as InternalRealVariable).Value = GetHYSYSFlowBasisValue(flowbasis);
			if (specVal != null && flowbasis != "GV")
			{
				(backDoor.get_BackDoorVariable((object)":ExtraData.200").Variable as InternalRealVariable).SetValue(specVal.Value, specVal.Unit);
			}
			if (bNeedStore)
			{
				colStrmSpecsBeVaried.Add(columnSpecification);
			}
			return backDoor;
		}
		catch (Exception)
		{
			DebugPrint.OutputDebugString(DebugLevel.BuildHYSYSCase, WarningLevel.Error, string.Format(P2Globalization.GetString("AddDrawRateSpecificationFailed"), _Data.UID));
			return null;
		}
	}

	private BackDoor ColumnDTSpec(string columnID, string firstStreamName, string secondStreamName, DoubleValue specVal, bool bActive)

	private BackDoor ColumnFeedRatiospec(string columnID, string flowType, string streamName, int iStage, int iFeedStage, string flowBasis, DoubleValue specVal, bool bActive)
	{
		try
		{
			HYSYSColumn columnData = null;
			TraySection traySec = null;
			int trayDiff = 0;
			InitColumnGeneralData(columnID, ref trayDiff, ref columnData, ref traySec);
			int stageNO = iStage - trayDiff;
			int stageNO2 = iFeedStage - trayDiff;
			string text = string.Empty;
			double value = -1.0;
			switch (flowType)
			{
			case "TrayL":
				value = 1.0;
				text = columnID + "_" + iStage + "_L_FeedRatio";
				break;
			case "TrayV":
				value = 0.0;
				text = columnID + "_" + iStage + "_L_FeedRatio";
				break;
			case "Draw":
				value = 2.0;
				text = columnID + "_" + streamName + "_FeedRatio";
				break;
			}
			ColumnSpecification obj = _ColumnFlowsheet.Specifications.Add(text, "ClmFeedRatioSpecObject") as ColumnSpecification;
			_specNumber++;
			if (_ColumnFlowsheet.DegreesOfFreedom == 0)
			{
				bActive = false;
			}
			obj.IsActive = bActive;
			obj.IsUsedAsEstimate = bActive;
			BackDoor backDoor = obj as BackDoor;
			(backDoor.get_BackDoorVariable((object)":Selection.301").Variable as InternalRealVariable).Value = value;
			if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(streamName))
			{
				SetSpecStage(iStage, stageNO, text, backDoor, columnData, traySec, ":SepStage.200");
			}
			else
			{
				ProcessStream processStream = _ColumnFlowsheet.MaterialStreams[streamName] as ProcessStream;
				backDoor.SendAttachObject(":Stream.300", processStream);
			}
			SetSpecStage(iFeedStage, stageNO2, text, backDoor, columnData, traySec, ":SepStage.300");
			InternalRealVariable obj2 = backDoor.get_BackDoorVariable((object)":Selection.300").Variable as InternalRealVariable;
			if (flowBasis.Contains("V"))
			{
				flowBasis = "V";
			}
			obj2.Value = GetHYSYSFlowBasisValue(flowBasis);
			if (specVal != null && flowBasis != "GV")
			{
				(backDoor.get_BackDoorVariable((object)":ExtraData.200").Variable as InternalRealVariable).SetValue(specVal.Value, "");
			}
			return backDoor;
		}
		catch (Exception)
		{
			DebugPrint.OutputDebugString(DebugLevel.BuildHYSYSCase, WarningLevel.Error, string.Format(P2Globalization.GetString("AddFeedRatioSpecificationFailed"), _Data.UID));
			return null;
		}
	}

	private BackDoor ColumnGapCutPointSpec(string columnID, string sType, int iStageA, int iStageB, string phase, string flowbasis, double cutPointA, double cutPointB, DoubleValue specVal, bool bActive)
	{
		try
		{
			HYSYSColumn columnData = null;
			TraySection traySec = null;
			int trayDiff = 0;
			InitColumnGeneralData(columnID, ref trayDiff, ref columnData, ref traySec);
			int stageNO = iStageA - trayDiff;
			int stageNO2 = iStageB - trayDiff;
			string text = columnID + "_GapCutPointSpec_" + sType;
			ColumnSpecification obj = _ColumnFlowsheet.Specifications.Add(text, "ClmGapSpecObject") as ColumnSpecification;
			_specNumber++;
			if (_ColumnFlowsheet.DegreesOfFreedom == 0)
			{
				bActive = false;
			}
			obj.IsActive = bActive;
			obj.IsUsedAsEstimate = bActive;
			BackDoor backDoor = obj as BackDoor;
			(backDoor.get_BackDoorVariable((object)":Selection.301").Variable as InternalRealVariable).Value = GetHYSYSCutPointTypeValue(sType);
			(backDoor.get_BackDoorVariable((object)":Selection.302").Variable as InternalRealVariable).Value = GetHYSYSFracBasisValue(flowbasis);
			(backDoor.get_BackDoorVariable((object)":Selection.300").Variable as InternalRealVariable).Value = GetHYSYSPhaseValue(phase);
			SetSpecStage(iStageA, stageNO, text, backDoor, columnData, traySec, ":SepStage.200");
			(backDoor.get_BackDoorVariable((object)":User.300").Variable as InternalRealVariable).Value = cutPointA;
			SetSpecStage(iStageB, stageNO2, text, backDoor, columnData, traySec, ":SepStage.400");
			(backDoor.get_BackDoorVariable((object)":User.400").Variable as InternalRealVariable).Value = cutPointB;
			if (specVal != null)
			{
				(backDoor.get_BackDoorVariable((object)":ExtraData.200").Variable as InternalRealVariable).SetValue(specVal.Value, specVal.Unit);
			}
			return backDoor;
		}
		catch (Exception)
		{
			DebugPrint.OutputDebugString(DebugLevel.BuildHYSYSCase, WarningLevel.Error, string.Format(P2Globalization.GetString("AddGapCutPointSpecificationFailed"), _Data.UID));
			return null;
		}
	}

	private BackDoor ColumnLiquidFlowSpec(string columnID, int iStage, string flowbasis, DoubleValue specVal, bool bActive)
	{
		try
		{
			ColumnSpecification columnSpecification = null;
			HYSYSColumn columnData = null;
			TraySection traySec = null;
			int trayDiff = 0;
			int num = iStage;
			InitColumnGeneralData(columnID, ref trayDiff, ref columnData, ref traySec);
			num -= trayDiff;
			string empty = string.Empty;
			empty = ((iStage != 0) ? (columnID + "_" + iStage + "_LFlow") : (columnID + "_RefluxRate"));
			columnSpecification = _ColumnFlowsheet.Specifications.Add(empty, "ClmLiquidFlowSpecObject") as ColumnSpecification;
			_specNumber++;
			if (_ColumnFlowsheet.DegreesOfFreedom == 0)
			{
				bActive = false;
			}
			columnSpecification.IsActive = bActive;
			columnSpecification.IsUsedAsEstimate = bActive;
			BackDoor backDoor = columnSpecification as BackDoor;
			if (iStage > 0)
			{
				SetSpecStage(iStage, num, empty, backDoor, columnData, traySec, ":SepStage.200");
			}
			else if (columnID == _Data.UID)
			{
				RROrRefluxSpec = columnSpecification;
				bRROrRefluxSpecActive = bActive;
			}
			(backDoor.get_BackDoorVariable((object)":Selection.300").Variable as InternalRealVariable).Value = GetHYSYSFlowBasisValue(flowbasis);
			if (specVal != null && flowbasis != "GV")
			{
				(backDoor.get_BackDoorVariable((object)":ExtraData.200").Variable as InternalRealVariable).SetValue(specVal.Value, specVal.Unit);
			}
			return backDoor;
		}
		catch (Exception)
		{
			DebugPrint.OutputDebugString(DebugLevel.BuildHYSYSCase, WarningLevel.Error, string.Format(P2Globalization.GetString("AddLiquidFlowSpecificationFailed"), _Data.UID));
			return null;
		}
	}

	private BackDoor ColumnPhysicalPropertySpec(string columnID, string propName, int iStage, string flowbasis, string phase, DoubleValue specVal, bool bActive)
	{
		try
		{
			HYSYSColumn columnData = null;
			TraySection traySec = null;
			int trayDiff = 0;
			int num = iStage;
			InitColumnGeneralData(columnID, ref trayDiff, ref columnData, ref traySec);
			num -= trayDiff;
			string text = columnID + "_" + iStage + "_" + propName;
			ColumnSpecification obj = _ColumnFlowsheet.Specifications.Add(text, "ClmPhysPropEnvObject") as ColumnSpecification;
			_specNumber++;
			if (_ColumnFlowsheet.DegreesOfFreedom == 0)
			{
				bActive = false;
			}
			obj.IsActive = bActive;
			obj.IsUsedAsEstimate = bActive;
			BackDoor backDoor = obj as BackDoor;
			SetSpecStage(iStage, num, text, backDoor, columnData, traySec, ":SepStage.200");
			InternalRealVariable internalRealVariable = backDoor.get_BackDoorVariable((object)":Selection.301").Variable as InternalRealVariable;
			switch (propName)
			{
			case "Mole Den":
				internalRealVariable.Value = 31.0;
				break;
			case "Molecular WT":
				internalRealVariable.Value = 32.0;
				break;
			case "Actual Mass Den":
				internalRealVariable.Value = 33.0;
				break;
			case "Avg. Mass Liq Den":
				internalRealVariable.Value = 30.0;
				break;
			}
			(backDoor.get_BackDoorVariable((object)":Selection.300").Variable as InternalRealVariable).Value = GetHYSYSPhaseValue(phase);
			if (specVal != null)
			{
				(backDoor.get_BackDoorVariable((object)":ExtraData.200").Variable as InternalRealVariable).SetValue(specVal.Value, specVal.Unit);
			}
			return backDoor;
		}
		catch (Exception)
		{
			DebugPrint.OutputDebugString(DebugLevel.BuildHYSYSCase, WarningLevel.Error, string.Format(P2Globalization.GetString("AddPhysicalPropertySpecificationFailed"), _Data.UID));
			return null;
		}
	}

	private void ColumnPumpAroundSpec(string columnID, string sName, string paName, string sType, string sFlowBasis, DoubleValue specValue, bool bActive)
	{
		try
		{
			ColumnSpecification obj = _ColumnFlowsheet.Specifications.Add(sName + "_" + sType, "ClmPumpSpecObject") as ColumnSpecification;
			_specNumber++;
			if (_ColumnFlowsheet.DegreesOfFreedom == 0)
			{
				bActive = false;
			}
			obj.IsActive = bActive;
			obj.IsUsedAsEstimate = bActive;
			BackDoor backDoor = obj as BackDoor;
			InternalRealVariable internalRealVariable = backDoor.get_BackDoorVariable((object)":Selection.300").Variable as InternalRealVariable;
			switch (sType)
			{
			case "DT":
				internalRealVariable.Value = 1.0;
				break;
			case "ReturnT":
				internalRealVariable.Value = 2.0;
				break;
			case "Duty":
				internalRealVariable.Value = 3.0;
				break;
			case "Return Vf":
				internalRealVariable.Value = 4.0;
				break;
			case "Rate":
			{
				internalRealVariable.Value = 0.0;
				double hYSYSFlowBasisValue = GetHYSYSFlowBasisValue(sFlowBasis);
				if (hYSYSFlowBasisValue != -1.0)
				{
					(backDoor.get_BackDoorVariable((object)":Selection.301").Variable as InternalRealVariable).Value = hYSYSFlowBasisValue;
				}
				break;
			}
			}
			obj.name = sName + "_" + sType;
			BackDoor backDoor2 = SubObjColl[paName];
			backDoor.SendAttachObject(":ColumnUserPumpAround.300", backDoor2);
			if (specValue != null && sFlowBasis != "GV")
			{
				(backDoor.get_BackDoorVariable((object)":ExtraData.200").Variable as InternalRealVariable).SetValue(specValue.Value, specValue.Unit);
			}
		}
		catch (Exception)
		{
			DebugPrint.OutputDebugString(DebugLevel.BuildHYSYSCase, WarningLevel.Error, string.Format(P2Globalization.GetString("AddPumparoundSpecificationFailed"), _Data.UID));
		}
	}

	private BackDoor ColumnRefluxFeedRatioSpec(string columnID, List<string> feedName, string flowbasis, bool bLight, DoubleValue specVal, bool bActive)
	{
		try
		{
			ColumnSpecification columnSpecification = null;
			HYSYSColumn columnData = null;
			TraySection traySec = null;
			int trayDiff = 0;
			InitColumnGeneralData(columnID, ref trayDiff, ref columnData, ref traySec);
			string text = columnID + "_RefluxFeedRatio";
			columnSpecification = _ColumnFlowsheet.Specifications.Add(text, "ClmReflux2FeedSpecObject") as ColumnSpecification;
			_specNumber++;
			if (_ColumnFlowsheet.DegreesOfFreedom == 0)
			{
				bActive = false;
			}
			columnSpecification.IsActive = bActive;
			columnSpecification.IsUsedAsEstimate = bActive;
			BackDoor backDoor = columnSpecification as BackDoor;
			if (columnID == _Data.UID)
			{
				RROrRefluxSpec = columnSpecification;
				bRROrRefluxSpecActive = bActive;
			}
			SetSpecStage(1, 1, text, backDoor, columnData, traySec, ":SepStage.200");
			(backDoor.get_BackDoorVariable((object)":Selection.300").Variable as InternalRealVariable).Value = GetHYSYSFlowBasisValue(flowbasis);
			if (specVal != null && flowbasis != "GV")
			{
				(backDoor.get_BackDoorVariable((object)":ExtraData.200").Variable as InternalRealVariable).SetValue(specVal.Value, "");
			}
			InternalRealVariable internalRealVariable = backDoor.get_BackDoorVariable((object)":Selection.301").Variable as InternalRealVariable;
			if (bLight)
			{
				internalRealVariable.Value = 1.0;
			}
			else
			{
				internalRealVariable.Value = 2.0;
			}
			int num = 0;
			foreach (string item in feedName)
			{
				ProcessStream processStream = _ColumnFlowsheet.MaterialStreams[item] as ProcessStream;
				backDoor.SendAttachObject(":Stream.300." + num, processStream);
				num++;
			}
			return backDoor;
		}
		catch (Exception)
		{
			DebugPrint.OutputDebugString(DebugLevel.BuildHYSYSCase, WarningLevel.Error, string.Format(P2Globalization.GetString("AddRefluxFeedRatioSpecificationFailed"), _Data.UID));
			return null;
		}
	}

	private BackDoor ColumnRefluxRatioSpec(string columnID, string flowbasis, bool bIncludeV, bool bIncludeBothL, DoubleValue specVal, bool bActive)
	{
		try
		{
			ColumnSpecification columnSpecification = null;
			HYSYSColumn columnData = null;
			TraySection traySec = null;
			int trayDiff = 0;
			InitColumnGeneralData(columnID, ref trayDiff, ref columnData, ref traySec);
			string text = columnID + "_RefluxRatio";

			}

	private BackDoor ColumnTemperatureSpec(string columnID, int iStage, DoubleValue specValue, bool bActive)
	{
		try
		{
			int num = 0;
			HYSYSColumn hYSYSColumn;
			TraySection traySection;
			if (columnID == _Data.UID)
			{
				hYSYSColumn = _Data;
				traySection = _TraySectionMainTS;
				num = iStage - _InternalTrayNumberDiff;
			}
			else
			{
				hYSYSColumn = SideColumnData[columnID];
				traySection = SideColumnTraySection[columnID];
				num = iStage - SideColumnTrayDiff[columnID];
			}
			string text = columnID + "_Tray" + num + "_Temp";
			ColumnSpecification obj = _ColumnFlowsheet.Specifications.Add(text, "ClmTempSpecObject") as ColumnSpecification;
			_specNumber++;
			if (_ColumnFlowsheet.DegreesOfFreedom == 0)
			{
				bActive = false;
			}
			obj.IsActive = bActive;
			obj.IsUsedAsEstimate = bActive;
			BackDoor backDoor = obj as BackDoor;
			if (iStage == 1 && hYSYSColumn.bCondenserExist)
			{
				(_ColumnFlowsheet as BackDoor).SendAttachObject2("ColumnSpec.500(" + text + "):SepStage.200", "UnitOpObject.400(" + columnID + "_Condenser)/SepStage.500");
			}
			else if (iStage == hYSYSColumn.stageNumber && hYSYSColumn.bReboilerExist)
			{
				(_ColumnFlowsheet as BackDoor).SendAttachObject2("ColumnSpec.500(" + text + "):SepStage.200", "UnitOpObject.400(" + columnID + "_Reboiler)/SepStage.500");
			}
			else
			{
				BackDoor backDoor2 = traySection.SeparationStages[num - 1] as BackDoor;
				backDoor.SendAttachObject(":SepStage.200", backDoor2);
			}
			if (specValue != null)
			{
				(backDoor.get_BackDoorVariable((object)":ExtraData.200").Variable as InternalRealVariable).SetValue(specValue.Value, specValue.Unit);
			}
			return backDoor;
		}
		catch (Exception)
		{
			DebugPrint.OutputDebugString(DebugLevel.BuildHYSYSCase, WarningLevel.Error, string.Format(P2Globalization.GetString("AddTemperatureSpecificationFailed"), _Data.UID));
			return null;
		}
	}

	private BackDoor ColumnTransportPropertySpec(string columnID, int iStage, string sType, string phase, DoubleValue refTemp, DoubleValue specVal, bool bActive)
	{
		try
		{
			HYSYSColumn columnData = null;
			TraySection traySec = null;
			int trayDiff = 0;
			int num = iStage;
			InitColumnGeneralData(columnID, ref trayDiff, ref columnData, ref traySec);
			num -= trayDiff;
			string text = columnID + "_" + iStage + sType;
			ColumnSpecification obj = _ColumnFlowsheet.Specifications.Add(text, "ClmTransPropEnvObject") as ColumnSpecification;
			_specNumber++;
			if (_ColumnFlowsheet.DegreesOfFreedom == 0)
			{
				bActive = false;
			}
			obj.IsActive = bActive;
			obj.IsUsedAsEstimate = bActive;
			BackDoor backDoor = obj as BackDoor;
			SetSpecStage(iStage, num, text, backDoor, columnData, traySec, ":SepStage.200");
			InternalRealVariable internalRealVariable = backDoor.get_BackDoorVariable((object)":Selection.301").Variable as InternalRealVariable;
			switch (sType)
			{
			case "Surface Tension":
				internalRealVariable.Value = 20.0;
				break;
			case "Thermo Conductivity":
				internalRealVariable.Value = 21.0;
				break;
			case "Viscosity":
				internalRealVariable.Value = 22.0;
				break;
			case "Kinematic Viscosity":
				internalRealVariable.Value = 84.0;
				break;
			}
			(backDoor.get_BackDoorVariable((object)":Selection.300").Variable as InternalRealVariable).Value = GetHYSYSPhaseValue(phase);
			if (refTemp != null)
			{
				(backDoor.get_BackDoorVariable((object)":Temperature.300").Variable as InternalRealVariable).SetValue(refTemp.Value, refTemp.Unit);
			}
			if (specVal != null)
			{
				(backDoor.get_BackDoorVariable((object)":ExtraData.200").Variable as InternalRealVariable).SetValue(specVal.Value, specVal.Unit);
			}
			return backDoor;
		}
		catch (Exception)
		{
			DebugPrint.OutputDebugString(DebugLevel.BuildHYSYSCase, WarningLevel.Error, string.Format(P2Globalization.GetString("AddTransportPropertySpecificationFailed"), _Data.UID));
			return null;
		}
	}

	private void ColumnUserPropertySpec()
	{
	}

	private BackDoor ColumnVaporFlowSpec(string columnID, int iStage, string flowbasis, DoubleValue specVal, bool bActive)
	{
		try
		{
			HYSYSColumn columnData = null;
			TraySection traySec = null;
			int trayDiff = 0;
			int num = iStage;
			InitColumnGeneralData(columnID, ref trayDiff, ref columnData, ref traySec);
			num -= trayDiff;
			string text = columnID + "_" + iStage + "_VFlow";
			ColumnSpecification obj = _ColumnFlowsheet.Specifications.Add(text, "ClmVapourFlowSpecObject") as ColumnSpecification;
			_specNumber++;
			if (_ColumnFlowsheet.DegreesOfFreedom == 0)
			{
				bActive = false;
			}
			obj.IsActive = bActive;
			obj.IsUsedAsEstimate = bActive;
			BackDoor backDoor = obj as BackDoor;
			SetSpecStage(iStage, num, text, backDoor, columnData, traySec, ":SepStage.200");
			(backDoor.get_BackDoorVariable((object)":Selection.300").Variable as InternalRealVariable).Value = GetHYSYSFlowBasisValue(flowbasis);

				(backDoor.get_BackDoorVariable((object)":ExtraData.200").Variable as InternalRealVariable).SetValue(specVal.Value, specVal.Unit);
			}
			return backDoor;
		}
		catch (Exception)

			DebugPrint.OutputDebugString(DebugLevel.BuildHYSYSCase, WarningLevel.Error, string.Format(P2Globalization.GetString("AddVaporFlowSpecificationFailed"), _Data.UID));
			return null;
		}
	}

	private BackDoor ColumnEndPointBasedColumnCutPointSpec(string columnID, string sType, int iStage, string phase, string flowbasis, double cutPoint, DoubleValue specVal, bool bActive)
