	private BackDoor ColumnEndPointBasedColumnGapSpec(string columnID, string sType, int iStageA, int iStageB, string phase, string flowbasis, double cutPointA, double cutPointB, DoubleValue specVal, bool bActive)
	{
		try
		{
			HYSYSColumn columnData = null;
			TraySection traySec = null;
			int trayDiff = 0;
			InitColumnGeneralData(columnID, ref trayDiff, ref columnData, ref traySec);
			int stageNO = iStageA - trayDiff;
			int stageNO2 = iStageB - trayDiff;
			string text = columnID + "_EndPointBasedGapCutPointSpec_" + sType;
			ColumnSpecification obj = _ColumnFlowsheet.Specifications.Add(text, "RefsysClmGapSpecObject") as ColumnSpecification;
			_specNumber++;
			if (_ColumnFlowsheet.DegreesOfFreedom == 0)
			{
				bActive = false;
			}
			obj.IsActive = bActive;
			obj.IsUsedAsEstimate = bActive;
			BackDoor backDoor = obj as BackDoor;
			(backDoor.get_BackDoorVariable((object)":Selection.301").Variable as InternalRealVariable).Value = GetHYSYSCutPointTypeValue(sType);
			(backDoor.get_BackDoorVariable((object)":Selection.302").Variable as InternalRealVariable).Value = GetHYSYSFracBasisValue(flowbasis);
			(backDoor.get_BackDoorVariable((object)":Selection.300").Variable as InternalRealVariable).Value = GetHYSYSPhaseValue(phase);
			SetSpecStage(iStageA, stageNO, text, backDoor, columnData, traySec, ":SepStage.200");
			(backDoor.get_BackDoorVariable((object)":User.300").Variable as InternalRealVariable).Value = cutPointA;
			SetSpecStage(iStageB, stageNO2, text, backDoor, columnData, traySec, ":SepStage.400");
			(backDoor.get_BackDoorVariable((object)":User.400").Variable as InternalRealVariable).Value = cutPointB;
			if (specVal != null)
			{
				(backDoor.get_BackDoorVariable((object)":ExtraData.200").Variable as InternalRealVariable).SetValue(specVal.Value, specVal.Unit);
			}
			return backDoor;
		}
		catch (Exception)
		{
			DebugPrint.OutputDebugString(DebugLevel.BuildHYSYSCase, WarningLevel.Error, string.Format(P2Globalization.GetString("AddEndPointBasedColumnGapSpecificationFailed"), _Data.UID));
			return null;
		}
	}

	private void ColumnStreamSpecification()
	{
	}

	private void SetSpecTolerance(Specification spec, BackDoor colSpecBD, DoubleValue specValue)
	{
		if (colSpecBD != null && (spec.ATOL != 0.0 || spec.RTOL != 0.0))
		{
			DebugPrint.OutputDebugString(DebugLevel.BuildHYSYSCase, WarningLevel.Warning, string.Format(P2Globalization.GetString("ToleranceNotConverted"), spec.P2SpecExpression, _Data.UID));
		}
	}

	private void SetAssayCorrelationType(BackDoor columnSpecBD, string assayType)
	{
		if (assayType == "ASTMD2887")
		{
			(columnSpecBD.get_BackDoorVariable((object)":Enum.302").Variable as InternalRealVariable).Value = 16.0;
		}
		else if (assayType == "ASTMD86")
		{
			(columnSpecBD.get_BackDoorVariable((object)":Enum.301").Variable as InternalRealVariable).Value = 13.0;
		}
	}

	private void AddHCurve()
	{
		SetHCurve(_Data);
		foreach (string key in SideColumnData.Keys)
		{
			SetHCurve(SideColumnData[key]);
		}
	}

	private void SetHCurve(HYSYSColumn columnData)
	{
		try
		{
			if (columnData.HCurvePressureColl.Count == 0)
			{
				return;
			}
			foreach (string key in columnData.HCurvePressureColl.Keys)
			{
				List<DoubleValue> list = columnData.HCurvePressureColl[key];
				DoubleValue dP = new DoubleValue("", 0.0);
				DoubleValue doubleValue = null;
				if (list.Count == 2)
				{
					doubleValue = HYSYSUOMMap.GetConvertedValue(UnitClassEnum.Pressure, list[0], "bar");
					DoubleValue convertedValue = HYSYSUOMMap.GetConvertedValue(UnitClassEnum.Pressure, list[1], "bar");
					dP = new DoubleValue(doubleValue.Unit, doubleValue.Value - convertedValue.Value);
				}
				int num = columnData.HCurveDutyNO[key];
				if (columnData.DutySpec.Keys.Contains(num))
				{
					int num2 = columnData.DutySpec[num].Keys.First();
					if (num2 == 1 && columnData.bCondenserExist)
					{
						SetCondenserHCurve(columnData, doubleValue, dP, columnData.HCurvePoints[key], key);
					}
					else if (num2 == columnData.stageNumber && columnData.bReboilerExist)
					{
						SetReboilerHCurve(columnData, doubleValue, dP, columnData.HCurvePoints[key], key);
					}
					else
					{
						SetSideHeaterCoolerHCurve(columnData, num, doubleValue, dP, columnData.HCurvePoints[key], key);
					}
				}
			}
		}
		catch (Exception)
		{
		}
	}

	private void SetCondenserHCurve(HYSYSColumn columnData, DoubleValue startP, DoubleValue dP, int point, string hcurID)
	{
		Condenser condenser = null;
		if (HysysCondType[columnData.UID] == 0)
		{
			condenser = _ColumnFlowsheet.get_Operations((object)"TotalCondenser").Add(hcurID + "_Condenser", "TotalCondenserObject") as Condenser;
		}
		else if (HysysCondType[columnData.UID] == 1)
		{
			condenser = _ColumnFlowsheet.get_Operations((object)"PartialCondenser").Add(hcurID + "_Condenser", "PartialCondenserObject") as Condenser;
		}
		else if (HysysCondType[columnData.UID] == 2)
		{
			condenser = _ColumnFlowsheet.get_Operations((object)"Condenser3Op").Add(hcurID + "_Condenser", "Condenser3OpObject") as Condenser;
		}
		BackDoor backDoor = condenser as BackDoor;
		if (dP.Value != 0.0)
		{
			(backDoor.get_BackDoorVariable((object)":PressureDrop.500").Variable as InternalRealVariable).SetValue(dP.Value, dP.Unit);
		}
		BackDoor obj = (_ColumnFlowsheet.get_Operations((object)"BalanceOpObject").Add(hcurID + "_ConB", "BalanceOpObject") as BalanceOp) as BackDoor;
		ProcessStream processStream = _ColumnFlowsheet.MaterialStreams[columnData.UID + "_ToCon"] as ProcessStream;
		obj.SendAttachObject(":Stream.500.0", processStream);
		processStream = _ColumnFlowsheet.MaterialStreams.Add(hcurID + "_ToCon", "") as ProcessStream;
		backDoor.SendAttachObject(":MaterialStream.502.0", processStream);
		obj.SendAttachObject(":Stream.501.0", processStream);
		(obj.get_BackDoorVariable((object)":Selection.500").Variable as InternalRealVariable).Value = 4.0;
		((_ColumnFlowsheet as BackDoor).get_BackDoorVariable((object)("StreamObject.400(" + hcurID + "_ToCon):Pressure.501.0")).Variable as InternalRealVariable).SetValue(startP.Value, startP.Unit);
		BackDoor obj2 = (_ColumnFlowsheet.get_Operations((object)"BalanceOpObject").Add(hcurID + "_RefluxB", "BalanceOpObject") as BalanceOp) as BackDoor;
		processStream = _ColumnFlowsheet.MaterialStreams[columnData.UID + "Reflux"] as ProcessStream;
		obj2.SendAttachObject(":Stream.500.0", processStream);
		processStream = _ColumnFlowsheet.MaterialStreams.Add(hcurID + "_Reflux", "") as ProcessStream;
		backDoor.SendAttachObject(":MaterialStream.600", processStream);
		obj2.SendAttachObject(":Stream.501.0", processStream);
		(obj2.get_BackDoorVariable((object)":Selection.500").Variable as InternalRealVariable).Value = 1.0;
		BackDoor obj3 = (_ColumnFlowsheet.get_Operations((object)"BalanceOpObject").Add(hcurID + "_QConB", "BalanceOpObject") as BalanceOp) as BackDoor;
		processStream = _ColumnFlowsheet.EnergyStreams["Q-Condenser_" + columnData.UID] as ProcessStream;
		obj3.SendAttachObject(":Stream.500.0", processStream);
		processStream = _ColumnFlowsheet.EnergyStreams.Add(hcurID + "_Q-Condenser", "") as ProcessStream;
		backDoor.SendAttachObject(":EnergyStream.504", processStream);
		obj3.SendAttachObject(":Stream.501.0", processStream);
		(obj3.get_BackDoorVariable((object)":Selection.500").Variable as InternalRealVariable).Value = 3.0;
		if (HysysCondVapOutStm.Keys.Contains(columnData.UID))
		{
			processStream = _ColumnFlowsheet.MaterialStreams.Add(hcurID + "_" + HysysCondVapOutStm[columnData.UID], "") as ProcessStream;
			backDoor.SendAttachObject(":MaterialStream.501", processStream);
		}
		if (HysysCondHCOutStm.Keys.Contains(columnData.UID))
		{
			processStream = _ColumnFlowsheet.MaterialStreams.Add(hcurID + "_" + HysysCondHCOutStm[columnData.UID], "") as ProcessStream;
			backDoor.SendAttachObject(":MaterialStream.500", processStream);
		}
		if (HysysCondWaterOutStm.Keys.Contains(columnData.UID))
		{
			processStream = _ColumnFlowsheet.MaterialStreams.Add(hcurID + "_" + HysysCondWaterOutStm[columnData.UID], "") as ProcessStream;
			backDoor.SendAttachObject(":MaterialStream.710", processStream);
		}
		if (HysysCondInletStreams.Keys.Contains(columnData.UID))
		{
			int num = 1;
			foreach (string item in HysysCondInletStreams[columnData.UID])
			{
				if (!(item == columnData.UID + "_ToCon"))
				{
					BackDoor obj4 = (_ColumnFlowsheet.get_Operations((object)"BalanceOpObject").Add(hcurID + "_CondIn_" + num, "BalanceOpObject") as BalanceOp) as BackDoor;
					processStream = _ColumnFlowsheet.MaterialStreams[item] as ProcessStream;
					obj4.SendAttachObject(":Stream.500.0", processStream);
					processStream = _ColumnFlowsheet.MaterialStreams.Add(hcurID + "_" + item, "") as ProcessStream;
					backDoor.SendAttachObject(":MaterialStream.502." + num, processStream);
					obj4.SendAttachObject(":Stream.501.0", processStream);
					(obj4.get_BackDoorVariable((object)":Selection.500").Variable as InternalRealVariable).Value = 4.0;
					(obj4.get_BackDoorVariable((object)":Pressure.501.0").Variable as InternalRealVariable).SetValue(startP.Value, startP.Unit);
					num++;
				}
			}
		}
		AddSupportForHCurve(backDoor, bPumpAround: false, point);
	}

	private void SetReboilerHCurve(HYSYSColumn columnData, DoubleValue startP, DoubleValue dP, int point, string hcurID)
	{
		BackDoor backDoor = (_ColumnFlowsheet.get_Operations((object)"BPReboiler").Add(hcurID + "_Reboiler", "BPReboilerObject") as Reboiler) as BackDoor;
		if (dP.Value != 0.0)
		{
			(backDoor.get_BackDoorVariable((object)":PressureDrop.500").Variable as InternalRealVariable).SetValue(dP.Value, dP.Unit);
		}
		BackDoor obj = (_ColumnFlowsheet.get_Operations((object)"BalanceOpObject").Add(hcurID + "_RebB", "BalanceOpObject") as BalanceOp) as BackDoor;
		ProcessStream processStream = _ColumnFlowsheet.MaterialStreams[columnData.UID + "_ToReb"] as ProcessStream;
		obj.SendAttachObject(":Stream.500.0", processStream);
		processStream = _ColumnFlowsheet.MaterialStreams.Add(hcurID + "_ToReb", "") as ProcessStream;
		backDoor.SendAttachObject(":MaterialStream.502.0", processStream);
		obj.SendAttachObject(":Stream.501.0", processStream);
		(obj.get_BackDoorVariable((object)":Selection.500").Variable as InternalRealVariable).Value = 4.0;
		((_ColumnFlowsheet as BackDoor).get_BackDoorVariable((object)("StreamObject.400(" + hcurID + "_ToReb):Pressure.501.0")).Variable as InternalRealVariable).SetValue(startP.Value, startP.Unit);
		BackDoor obj2 = (_ColumnFlowsheet.get_Operations((object)"BalanceOpObject").Add(hcurID + "_BoilupB", "BalanceOpObject") as BalanceOp) as BackDoor;
		processStream = _ColumnFlowsheet.MaterialStreams[columnData.UID + "Reflux"] as ProcessStream;
		obj2.SendAttachObject(":Stream.500.0", processStream);
		processStream = _ColumnFlowsheet.MaterialStreams.Add(hcurID + "_Boilup", "") as ProcessStream;
		backDoor.SendAttachObject(":MaterialStream.501", processStream);
		obj2.SendAttachObject(":Stream.501.0", processStream);
		(obj2.get_BackDoorVariable((object)":Selection.500").Variable as InternalRealVariable).Value = 1.0;
		BackDoor obj3 = (_ColumnFlowsheet.get_Operations((object)"BalanceOpObject").Add(hcurID + "_QRebB", "BalanceOpObject") as BalanceOp) as BackDoor;
		processStream = _ColumnFlowsheet.EnergyStreams[columnData.UID + "_Reb_Q"] as ProcessStream;
		obj3.SendAttachObject(":Stream.500.0", processStream);
		processStream = _ColumnFlowsheet.EnergyStreams.Add(hcurID + "_Q-Reb", "") as ProcessStream;
		backDoor.SendAttachObject(":EnergyStream.503", processStream);
		obj3.SendAttachObject(":Stream.501.0", processStream);
		(obj3.get_BackDoorVariable((object)":Selection.500").Variable as InternalRealVariable).Value = 3.0;
		if (HysysRebLiqProdStm.Keys.Contains(columnData.UID))
		{
			processStream = _ColumnFlowsheet.MaterialStreams.Add(hcurID + "_" + HysysRebLiqProdStm[columnData.UID], "") as ProcessStream;
			backDoor.SendAttachObject(":MaterialStream.500", processStream);
		}
		if (HysysRebVapProdStm.Keys.Contains(columnData.UID))
		{
			processStream = _ColumnFlowsheet.MaterialStreams.Add(hcurID + "_" + HysysRebVapProdStm[columnData.UID], "") as ProcessStream;
			backDoor.SendAttachObject(":MaterialStream.600", processStream);
		}
		if (HysysRebInletStreams.Keys.Contains(columnData.UID))
		{
			int num = 1;
			foreach (string item in HysysRebInletStreams[columnData.UID])
			{
				if (!(item == columnData.UID + "_ToReb"))
				{
					BackDoor obj4 = (_ColumnFlowsheet.get_Operations((object)"BalanceOpObject").Add(hcurID + "_RebIn_" + num, "BalanceOpObject") as BalanceOp) as BackDoor;
					processStream = _ColumnFlowsheet.MaterialStreams[item] as ProcessStream;
					obj4.SendAttachObject(":Stream.500.0", processStream);
					processStream = _ColumnFlowsheet.MaterialStreams.Add(hcurID + "_" + item, "") as ProcessStream;
					backDoor.SendAttachObject(":MaterialStream.502." + num, processStream);
					obj4.SendAttachObject(":Stream.501.0", processStream);
					(obj4.get_BackDoorVariable((object)":Selection.500").Variable as InternalRealVariable).Value = 4.0;
					(obj4.get_BackDoorVariable((object)":Pressure.501.0").Variable as InternalRealVariable).SetValue(startP.Value, startP.Unit);
					num++;
				}
			}
		}
		AddSupportForHCurve(backDoor, bPumpAround: false, point);
	}

	private void SetSideHeaterCoolerHCurve(HYSYSColumn columnData, int dutyID, DoubleValue startP, DoubleValue dP, int point, string hcurID)
	{
		if (HysysSideExchangerType.Keys.Contains(columnData.UID))
		{
			BackDoor backDoor = null;
			string sBlockName = string.Empty;
			string sDrawName = string.Empty;
			string sEnergyName = string.Empty;
			GetSideHeaterCoolerInfo(columnData.UID, dutyID, ref sBlockName, ref sDrawName, ref sEnergyName);
			if (sBlockName == "HEATER")
			{
				backDoor = (_ColumnFlowsheet.get_Operations((object)"HeaterOpObject").Add(hcurID + "_" + sBlockName, "HeaterOpObject") as HeaterOp) as BackDoor;
			}
			else if (sBlockName == "COOLER")
			{
				backDoor = (_ColumnFlowsheet.get_Operations((object)"CoolerOpObject").Add(hcurID + "_" + sBlockName, "CoolerOpObject") as CoolerOp) as BackDoor;
			}
			(backDoor.get_BackDoorVariable((object)":PressureDrop.511").Variable as InternalRealVariable).SetValue(dP.Value, dP.Unit);
			BackDoor obj = (_ColumnFlowsheet.get_Operations((object)"BalanceOpObject").Add(hcurID + "_Draw", "BalanceOpObject") as BalanceOp) as BackDoor;
			ProcessStream processStream = _ColumnFlowsheet.MaterialStreams[sDrawName] as ProcessStream;
			obj.SendAttachObject(":Stream.500.0", processStream);
			processStream = _ColumnFlowsheet.MaterialStreams.Add(hcurID + "_draw", "") as ProcessStream;
			backDoor.SendAttachObject(":MaterialStream.400", processStream);
			obj.SendAttachObject(":Stream.501.0", processStream);
			(obj.get_BackDoorVariable((object)":Selection.500").Variable as InternalRealVariable).Value = 4.0;
			((_ColumnFlowsheet as BackDoor).get_BackDoorVariable((object)("StreamObject.400(" + hcurID + "_draw):Pressure.501.0")).Variable as InternalRealVariable).SetValue(startP.Value, startP.Unit);
			processStream = _ColumnFlowsheet.MaterialStreams.Add(hcurID + "_Return", "") as ProcessStream;
			backDoor.SendAttachObject(":MaterialStream.401", processStream);
			BackDoor obj2 = (_ColumnFlowsheet.get_Operations((object)"BalanceOpObject").Add(hcurID + "_Q", "BalanceOpObject") as BalanceOp) as BackDoor;
			processStream = _ColumnFlowsheet.EnergyStreams[sEnergyName] as ProcessStream;
			obj2.SendAttachObject(":Stream.500.0", processStream);
			processStream = _ColumnFlowsheet.EnergyStreams.Add(hcurID + "_Out", "") as ProcessStream;
			backDoor.SendAttachObject(":EnergyStream.300", processStream);
			obj2.SendAttachObject(":Stream.501.0", processStream);
			(obj2.get_BackDoorVariable((object)":Selection.500").Variable as InternalRealVariable).Value = 3.0;
			AddSupportForHCurve(backDoor, bPumpAround: true, point);
		}
	}

	private void GetSideHeaterCoolerInfo(string columnID, int dutyID, ref string sBlockName, ref string sDrawName, ref string sEnergyName)
	{
		foreach (IntValue item in HysysSideExchangerType[columnID])
		{
			if (item.Value == dutyID)
			{
				sBlockName = item.Name;
				break;
			}
		}
		foreach (IntValue item2 in HysysSideExchangerDrawStm[columnID])
		{
			if (item2.Value == dutyID)
			{
				sDrawName = item2.Name;
				break;
			}
		}
		foreach (IntValue item3 in HysysSideExchangerEnergyStm[columnID])
		{
			if (item3.Value == dutyID)
			{
				sEnergyName = item3.Name;
				break;
			}
		}
	}

	private void AddTraySizingUtility()
	{
		int iTotalNumber = 0;
		int tsSectionNumber = 0;
		SetTraySizingUtility(_Data, bMain: true, ref tsSectionNumber, ref iTotalNumber);
		if (_Data.PhaseType == ColumnPhaseEnum.LL || SideColumnData.Count <= 0)
		{
			return;
		}
		foreach (string key in SideColumnData.Keys)
		{
			int tsSectionNumber2 = 0;
			SetTraySizingUtility(SideColumnData[key], bMain: false, ref tsSectionNumber2, ref iTotalNumber);
		}
	}

	private void SetTraySizingUtility(HYSYSColumn columnData, bool bMain, ref int tsSectionNumber, ref int iTotalNumber)
	{
		if (columnData.colTrays.Count > 0)
		{
			foreach (ColumnTray colTray in columnData.colTrays)
			{
				AddColumnTray(columnData.UID, colTray, bMain, ref tsSectionNumber, ref iTotalNumber);
			}
		}
		if (columnData.colPacks.Count <= 0)
		{
			return;
		}
		foreach (columnPack colPack in columnData.colPacks)
		{
			AddColumnPack(columnData.UID, colPack, bMain, ref tsSectionNumber, ref iTotalNumber);
		}
	}

	private void AddColumnTray(string columnID, ColumnTray colTray, bool bMain, ref int tsSectionNumber, ref int iTotalNumber)
	{
		try
		{
			if (tsSectionNumber == 0)
			{
				iTotalNumber++;
				string name = _Data.UID + "_TraySzg_" + iTotalNumber;
				_tsUtility = _SimCase.UtilityObjects.Add(name, "TraySizingUtilityObject") as TraySizingUtility;
			}
			_tsUtlBD = _tsUtility as BackDoor;
			BackDoor backDoor = _MainTSBD;
			if (!bMain)
			{
				backDoor = sideTraySecBDColl[columnID];
			}
			if (tsSectionNumber == 0)
			{
				_tsUtlBD.SendAttachObject(":TraySection.500", backDoor);
			}
			_tsUtlBD.SendBackDoorMessage("AddSection");
			TraySection traySection = _TraySectionMainTS;
			int num = _InternalTrayNumberDiff;
			if (!bMain)
			{
				traySection = SideColumnTraySection[columnID];
				num = SideColumnTrayDiff[columnID];
			}
			if (colTray.StartStage > 0)
			{
				int num2 = 0;
				if (colTray.StartStage - num - 1 > 0)
				{
					num2 = colTray.StartStage - num - 1;
				}
				BackDoor backDoor2 = traySection.SeparationStages[num2] as BackDoor;
				_tsUtlBD.SendAttachObject(":SepStage.500." + tsSectionNumber, backDoor2);
			}
			if (colTray.EndStage > 0)
			{
				BackDoor backDoor3 = traySection.SeparationStages[colTray.EndStage - num - 1] as BackDoor;
				_tsUtlBD.SendAttachObject(":SepStage.501." + tsSectionNumber, backDoor3);
			}
			InternalRealVariable internalRealVariable = _tsUtlBD.get_BackDoorVariable((object)(":Selection.501." + tsSectionNumber)).Variable as InternalRealVariable;
			if (colTray.TrayType == TrayTypeEnum.CAP)
			{
				internalRealVariable.Value = 3.0;
			}
			else if (colTray.TrayType == TrayTypeEnum.SIEVE)
			{
				internalRealVariable.Value = 0.0;
			}
			InternalRealVariable internalRealVariable2 = _tsUtlBD.get_BackDoorVariable((object)(":Selection.502." + tsSectionNumber)).Variable as InternalRealVariable;
			if (colTray.CalculationMode == HydraulicsCalulationModeEnum.RATING)
			{
				internalRealVariable2.Value = 1.0;
			}
			if (colTray.NumberOfFlowPaths > 0)
			{
				(_tsUtlBD.get_BackDoorVariable((object)(":Index.520." + tsSectionNumber)).Variable as InternalRealVariable).Value = Convert.ToDouble(colTray.NumberOfFlowPaths);
			}
			if (colTray.TraySpacing != null)
			{
				(_tsUtlBD.get_BackDoorVariable((object)(":Length.540." + tsSectionNumber)).Variable as InternalRealVariable).SetValue(colTray.TraySpacing.Value, colTray.TraySpacing.Unit);
			}
			if (colTray.CalculationMode == HydraulicsCalulationModeEnum.SIZING)
			{
				(_tsUtlBD.get_BackDoorVariable((object)(":Percent.520." + tsSectionNumber)).Variable as InternalRealVariable).SetValue(colTray.MaxFlooding, "%");
				tsSectionNumber++;
				return;
			}
			if (colTray.SectionDiameter != null)
			{
				(_tsUtlBD.get_BackDoorVariable((object)(":Length.520." + tsSectionNumber)).Variable as InternalRealVariable).SetValue(colTray.SectionDiameter.Value, colTray.SectionDiameter.Unit);
			}
			if (colTray.TrayType == TrayTypeEnum.SIEVE && colTray.HoleArea != null)
			{
				_Data.ConvertNotes.Add(string.Format(P2Globalization.GetString("SieveHoleNotConvert"), colTray.HoleArea.Value.ToString()));
			}
			if (colTray.TrayType == TrayTypeEnum.SIEVE && colTray.SieveHoleDiameter != null)
			{
				(_tsUtlBD.get_BackDoorVariable((object)(":SmallLength.561." + tsSectionNumber)).Variable as InternalRealVariable).SetValue(colTray.SieveHoleDiameter.Value, colTray.SieveHoleDiameter.Unit);
			}
			if (colTray.TrayType == TrayTypeEnum.VALVE && colTray.ValveThickness != null)
			{
				(_tsUtlBD.get_BackDoorVariable((object)(":SmallLength.563." + tsSectionNumber)).Variable as InternalRealVariable).SetValue(colTray.ValveThickness.Value, colTray.ValveThickness.Unit);
			}
			if (colTray.DowncomerClearaness != null)
			{
				(_tsUtlBD.get_BackDoorVariable((object)(":SmallLength.541." + tsSectionNumber)).Variable as InternalRealVariable).SetValue(colTray.DowncomerClearaness.Value, colTray.DowncomerClearaness.Unit);
			}
			if (colTray.WeirHeight != null)
			{
				(_tsUtlBD.get_BackDoorVariable((object)(":SmallLength.542." + tsSectionNumber)).Variable as InternalRealVariable).SetValue(colTray.WeirHeight.Value, colTray.WeirHeight.Unit);
			}
			if (colTray.SideDCTopWidth != null)
			{
				(_tsUtlBD.get_BackDoorVariable((object)(":SmallLength.565." + tsSectionNumber)).Variable as InternalRealVariable).SetValue(colTray.SideDCTopWidth.Value, colTray.SideDCTopWidth.Unit);
			}
			if (colTray.CenterDCTopWidth != null)
			{
				(_tsUtlBD.get_BackDoorVariable((object)(":SmallLength.566." + tsSectionNumber)).Variable as InternalRealVariable).SetValue(colTray.CenterDCTopWidth.Value, colTray.CenterDCTopWidth.Unit);
			}
			if (colTray.OffSideDCTopWidth != null)
			{
				(_tsUtlBD.get_BackDoorVariable((object)(":SmallLength.568." + tsSectionNumber)).Variable as InternalRealVariable).SetValue(colTray.OffSideDCTopWidth.Value, colTray.OffSideDCTopWidth.Unit);
			}
			if (colTray.OffCenterDCTopWidth != null)
			{
				(_tsUtlBD.get_BackDoorVariable((object)(":SmallLength.567." + tsSectionNumber)).Variable as InternalRealVariable).SetValue(colTray.OffCenterDCTopWidth.Value, colTray.OffCenterDCTopWidth.Unit);
			}
			tsSectionNumber++;
		}
		catch (Exception)
		{
			DebugPrint.OutputDebugString(DebugLevel.BuildHYSYSCase, WarningLevel.Error, string.Format(P2Globalization.GetString("AddTraySizingUtilityFailed"), _Data.UID));
		}
	}

	private void AddColumnPack(string columnID, columnPack colPack, bool bMain, ref int tsSectionNumber, ref int iTotalNumber)
	{
		string packTypeMoniker = string.Empty;
		string packTypeName = string.Empty;
		try
		{
			if (!IsValidPackType(colPack, ref packTypeMoniker, ref packTypeName))
			{
				_Data.ConvertNotes.Add(string.Format(P2Globalization.GetString("PackSecNotConvert"), packTypeName));
				return;
			}
			if (tsSectionNumber == 0)
			{
				iTotalNumber++;
				string name = _Data.UID + "_TraySzg_" + iTotalNumber;
				_tsUtility = _SimCase.UtilityObjects.Add(name, "TraySizingUtilityObject") as TraySizingUtility;
			}
			_tsUtlBD = _tsUtility as BackDoor;
			BackDoor backDoor = _MainTSBD;
			if (!bMain)
			{
				backDoor = sideTraySecBDColl[columnID];
			}
			_tsUtlBD.SendAttachObject(":TraySection.500", backDoor);
			_tsUtlBD.SendBackDoorMessage("AddSection");
			TraySection traySection = _TraySectionMainTS;
			int num = _InternalTrayNumberDiff;
			if (!bMain)
			{
				traySection = SideColumnTraySection[columnID];
				num = SideColumnTrayDiff[columnID];
			}
			if (colPack.StartStage > 0)
			{
				BackDoor backDoor2 = traySection.SeparationStages[colPack.StartStage - num - 1] as BackDoor;
				_tsUtlBD.SendAttachObject(":SepStage.500." + tsSectionNumber, backDoor2);
			}
			if (colPack.EndStage > 0)
			{
				BackDoor backDoor3 = traySection.SeparationStages[colPack.EndStage - num - 1] as BackDoor;
				_tsUtlBD.SendAttachObject(":SepStage.501." + tsSectionNumber, backDoor3);
			}
			(_tsUtlBD.get_BackDoorVariable((object)(":Selection.501." + tsSectionNumber)).Variable as InternalRealVariable).Value = 2.0;
			InternalRealVariable internalRealVariable = _tsUtlBD.get_BackDoorVariable((object)(":Selection.502." + tsSectionNumber)).Variable as InternalRealVariable;
			if (colPack.CalculationMode == HydraulicsCalulationModeEnum.RATING)
			{
				internalRealVariable.Value = 1.0;
				if (colPack.SectionDiameter != null)
				{
					(_tsUtlBD.get_BackDoorVariable((object)(":Length.520." + tsSectionNumber)).Variable as InternalRealVariable).SetValue(colPack.SectionDiameter.Value, colPack.SectionDiameter.Unit);
				}
			}
			_tsUtlBD.SendAttachObject2(":PackingDBRecord.110." + tsSectionNumber, packTypeMoniker);
			tsSectionNumber++;
		}
		catch (Exception)
		{
			DebugPrint.OutputDebugString(DebugLevel.BuildHYSYSCase, WarningLevel.Error, string.Format(P2Globalization.GetString("AddTraySizingUtilityFailed"), _Data.UID));
		}
	}

	private bool IsValidPackType(columnPack colPack, ref string packTypeMoniker, ref string packTypeName)
	{
		bool result = false;
		if (colPack.IsRandomPack)
		{
			DoubleValue convertedValue = HYSYSUOMMap.GetConvertedValue(UnitClassEnum.FineLength, colPack.RandomPackSize, "IN");
			switch (colPack.RandomPackType.ToString())
			{
			case "1":
				packTypeName = "IMTP (Metal) " + convertedValue.Value + " IN";
				break;
			case "2":
				if (convertedValue.Value == 1.0)
				{
					packTypeMoniker = "/PackingDBRecord.100.72";
					result = true;
				}
				else if (convertedValue.Value == 1.5)
				{
					packTypeMoniker = "/PackingDBRecord.100.73";
					result = true;
				}
				else if (convertedValue.Value == 2.0)
				{
					packTypeMoniker = "/PackingDBRecord.100.74";
					result = true;
				}
				else
				{
					packTypeName = "Hy-Pak (Metal) " + convertedValue.Value + " IN";
				}
				break;
			case "3":
				if (convertedValue.Value == 1.0)
				{
					packTypeMoniker = "/PackingDBRecord.100.253";
					result = true;
				}
				else if (convertedValue.Value == 2.0)
				{
					packTypeMoniker = "/PackingDBRecord.100.254";
					result = true;
				}
				break;
			case "4":
				packTypeName = "Super Intalox Saddles (Ceram) " + convertedValue.Value + " IN";
				break;
			case "5":
				if (convertedValue.Value == 0.625)
				{
					packTypeMoniker = "/PackingDBRecord.100.171";
					result = true;
				}
				else if (convertedValue.Value == 1.0)
				{
					packTypeMoniker = "/PackingDBRecord.100.172";
					result = true;
				}
				else if (convertedValue.Value == 1.5)
				{
					packTypeMoniker = "/PackingDBRecord.100.173";
					result = true;
				}
				else if (convertedValue.Value == 2.0)

					result = true;

					packTypeMoniker = "/PackingDBRecord.100.93";
					result = true;
				}
				else if (convertedValue.Value == 0.375)
				{
					packTypeMoniker = "/PackingDBRecord.100.94";
					result = true;

				break;
			case "23":
				if (convertedValue.Value == 1.0)
				{
					packTypeMoniker = "/PackingDBRecord.100.72";
					result = true;
				}
				else if (convertedValue.Value == 1.5)
				{
					packTypeMoniker = "/PackingDBRecord.100.73";
					result = true;
				}
				else if (convertedValue.Value == 2.0)
				{
					packTypeMoniker = "/PackingDBRecord.100.74";
					result = true;
				}
				else
				{
					packTypeName = "Hy-Pak (Metal) " + convertedValue.Value + " IN";
				}
				break;
			case "24":
				packTypeName = "IMTP (Metal) #60";
				break;
			case "25":
				packTypeName = "Beta Rings (Plastic) " + convertedValue.Value + " IN";
				break;
			case "26":
				packTypeName = "Flexi Saddles LPD (Ceramic) " + convertedValue.Value + " IN";
				break;
			}
		}
		else
		{
			string structurePackDetail = colPack.StructurePackDetail;
			if (colPack.StructurePackType == StructuredPackTypeEnum.SULZER)
			{
				packTypeName = "SULZER " + structurePackDetail;
			}
			else if (colPack.StructurePackType == StructuredPackTypeEnum.FLEXE)
			{
				packTypeName = "FLEXE " + structurePackDetail;
			}
			else if (colPack.StructurePackType == StructuredPackTypeEnum.FLEXI)
			{
				packTypeName = "FLEXI " + structurePackDetail;
			}
			else if (colPack.StructurePackType == StructuredPackTypeEnum.INTALOX)
			{
				packTypeName = "INTALOX " + structurePackDetail;
			}
			else if (colPack.StructurePackType == StructuredPackTypeEnum.KGWIGEGAUZE)
			{
				packTypeMoniker = "/PackingDBRecord.100.118";
				result = true;
			}
		}
		return result;
	}

	private void AddUnconvertedNotes()
	{
		_ColumnBD = _Column as BackDoor;
		InternalTextVariable internalTextVariable = _ColumnBD.get_BackDoorTextVariable((object)":Notes.400").Variable as InternalTextVariable;
		internalTextVariable.Value = "============ Column " + _Data.UID + " ============\n";
		foreach (string convertNote in _Data.ConvertNotes)
		{
			internalTextVariable.Value = internalTextVariable.Value + convertNote + "\n";
		}
		foreach (string key in SideColumnData.Keys)
		{
			HYSYSColumn hYSYSColumn = SideColumnData[key];
			internalTextVariable.Value = "============ Column " + hYSYSColumn.UID + " ============\n";
			foreach (string convertNote2 in hYSYSColumn.ConvertNotes)
			{
				internalTextVariable.Value = internalTextVariable.Value + convertNote2 + "\n";
			}
		}
	}

	private void InitColumnGeneralData(string columnID, ref int trayDiff, ref HYSYSColumn columnData, ref TraySection traySec)
	{
		if (columnID == _Data.UID)
		{
			columnData = _Data;
			traySec = _TraySectionMainTS;
			trayDiff = _InternalTrayNumberDiff;
		}
		else
		{
			columnData = SideColumnData[columnID];
			traySec = SideColumnTraySection[columnID];
			trayDiff = SideColumnTrayDiff[columnID];
		}
	}

	private double GetHYSYSFlowBasisValue(string sBasis)
	{
		if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(sBasis))
		{
			return 3.0;
		}
		double result = -1.0;
		switch (sBasis)
		{
		case "M":
			result = 3.0;
			break;
		case "WT":
			result = 4.0;
			break;
		case "W":
			result = 4.0;
			break;
		case "LV":
			result = 1023.0;
			break;
		case "GV":
			result = 3.0;
			break;
		case "V":
			result = 5.0;
			break;
		}
		return result;
	}

	private double GetHYSYSFracBasisValue(string sBasis)
	{
		if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(sBasis))
		{
			return 9.0;
		}
		double result = -1.0;
		switch (sBasis)
		{
		case "M":
			result = 9.0;
			break;
		case "WT":
			result = 10.0;
			break;
		case "LV":
			result = 11.0;
			break;
		case "GV":
			result = 9.0;
			break;
		case "V":
			result = 11.0;
			break;
		}
		return result;
	}

	private double GetHYSYSPhaseValue(string phase)
	{
		double result = 0.0;
		if (phase == "V")
		{
			result = 0.0;
		}
		else if (phase == "L")
		{
			result = 1.0;
		}
		return result;
	}

	private double GetHYSYSCutPointTypeValue(string sType)
	{
		double result = -1.0;
		switch (sType)
		{
		case "TBP":
			result = 50.0;
			break;
		case "ASTMD86":
			result = 51.0;
			break;
		case "D1160Vac":
			result = 52.0;
			break;
		case "D1160ATM":
			result = 54.0;
			break;
		case "ASTMD2887":
			result = 55.0;
			break;
		}
		return result;
	}

	private void SetSpecStage(int iStage, int stageNO, string sName, BackDoor columnSpecBD, HYSYSColumn columnData, TraySection traySec, string sMoniker)
	{
		BackDoor backDoor = _ColumnFlowsheet as BackDoor;
		if (iStage == 1 && columnData.bCondenserExist)
		{
			backDoor.SendAttachObject2("ColumnSpec.500(" + sName + ")" + sMoniker, "UnitOpObject.400(" + columnData.UID + "_Condenser)/SepStage.500");
			return;
		}
		if (iStage == columnData.stageNumber && columnData.bReboilerExist)
		{
			backDoor.SendAttachObject2("ColumnSpec.500(" + sName + ")" + sMoniker, "UnitOpObject.400(" + columnData.UID + "_Reboiler)/SepStage.500");
			return;
		}
		BackDoor backDoor2 = traySec.SeparationStages[stageNO - 1] as BackDoor;
		columnSpecBD.SendAttachObject(sMoniker, backDoor2);
	}

	private Component GetHYSYSComponet(string compName)
	{
		ComponentList componentList = null;
		if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(_Data.FluidPackage) && ((string[])_SimCase.BasisManager.FluidPackages.Names).Contains(_Data.FluidPackage))
		{
			componentList = (_SimCase.BasisManager.FluidPackages[_Data.FluidPackage] as FluidPackage).ComponentList;
		}
		if (componentList == null)
		{
			componentList = _SimCase.Flowsheet.FluidPackage.ComponentList;
		}
		return (Component)componentList.Components[GetHysysCompIndex(compName)];
	}

	private TypeUnitValue GetDutySpecValueByStage(HYSYSColumn columnData, int StageNO, ref List<int> paDutyIDFound)
	{
		if (columnData.DutySpec.Count == 0)
		{
			return null;
		}
		TypeUnitValue result = null;
		foreach (int key in columnData.DutySpec.Keys)
		{
			Dictionary<int, TypeUnitValue> dictionary = columnData.DutySpec[key];
			if (dictionary.Keys.Contains(StageNO) && !paDutyIDFound.Contains(key))
			{
				result = dictionary[StageNO];
				paDutyIDFound.Add(key);
				break;
			}
		}
		return result;
	}

	private int GetDutyNOByStage(HYSYSColumn columnData, int StageNO)
	{
		if (columnData.DutySpec.Count == 0)
		{
			return -1;
		}
		int result = -1;
		foreach (int key in columnData.DutySpec.Keys)
		{
			if (columnData.DutySpec[key].Keys.Contains(StageNO))
			{
				result = key;
				break;
			}
		}
		return result;
	}

	private bool PANeedsToExport(HYSYSColumn columnData, int drawStage, int retStage, string sPhase, ref string exportStmName)
	{
		if (sPhase == "L" && columnData.ExportPumpAroundLiquidFlow.Count == 0)
		{
			return false;
		}
		if (sPhase == "V" && columnData.ExportPumpAroundVaporFlow.Count == 0)
		{
			return false;
		}
		if (sPhase == "L")
		{
			foreach (string key in columnData.ExportPumpAroundLiquidFlow.Keys)
			{
				short num = Convert.ToInt16(columnData.ExportPumpAroundLiquidFlow[key].StartValue);
				int num2 = Convert.ToInt16(columnData.ExportPumpAroundLiquidFlow[key].EndValue);
				if (num == drawStage && num2 == retStage)
				{
					exportStmName = key;
					return true;
				}
			}
		}
		else
		{
			foreach (string key2 in columnData.ExportPumpAroundVaporFlow.Keys)
			{
				short num3 = Convert.ToInt16(columnData.ExportPumpAroundVaporFlow[key2].StartValue);
				int num4 = Convert.ToInt16(columnData.ExportPumpAroundVaporFlow[key2].EndValue);
				if (num3 == drawStage && num4 == retStage)
				{
					exportStmName = key2;
					return true;
				}
			}
		}
		return false;
	}

	private void ExportStreamHelper(string columnID, TraySection traySec, string streamName, ref int iExportCount, int stageNO, bool bReboiler, double phaseValue, double ntValue, int totalStage, bool bCondExist, bool bRebExist)
	{
		_ColumnBD = _Column as BackDoor;
		_ColumnBD.SendBackDoorMessage("FlowSht.600:CreateInternalStream");
		string moniker = "FlowSht.600/ColumnIntrnlStream.500." + iExportCount + ":ColumnIntStream.300";
		BackDoor backDoor = _ColumnFlowsheet.Streams.Add(streamName, "ColumnIntStream") as BackDoor;
		_ColumnBD.SendAttachObject(moniker, backDoor);
		if (bReboiler || (stageNO == totalStage && bRebExist))
		{
			_ColumnBD.SendAttachObject2("FlowSht.600/ColumnIntrnlStream.500." + iExportCount + ":SepStage.300", "FlowSht.600/UnitOpObject.400(" + columnID + "_Reboiler)/SepStage.500");
		}
		else if (stageNO == 0 && bCondExist)
		{
			_ColumnBD.SendAttachObject2("FlowSht.600/ColumnIntrnlStream.500." + iExportCount + ":SepStage.300", "FlowSht.600/UnitOpObject.400(" + columnID + "_Condenser)/SepStage.500");
		}
		else
		{
			BackDoor backDoor2 = traySec.SeparationStages[stageNO - 1] as BackDoor;
			moniker = "FlowSht.600/ColumnIntrnlStream.500." + iExportCount + ":SepStage.300";
			_ColumnBD.SendAttachObject(moniker, backDoor2);
		}
		moniker = "FlowSht.600/ColumnIntrnlStream.500." + iExportCount + ":ExtraData.302";
		(_ColumnBD.get_BackDoorVariable((object)moniker).Variable as InternalRealVariable).Value = phaseValue;
		moniker = "FlowSht.600/ColumnIntrnlStream.500." + iExportCount + ":ExtraData.301";
		(_ColumnBD.get_BackDoorVariable((object)moniker).Variable as InternalRealVariable).Value = ntValue;
		moniker = "FlowSht.600/ColumnIntrnlStream.500." + iExportCount + ":Boolean.301";
		(_ColumnBD.get_BackDoorVariable((object)moniker).Variable as InternalRealVariable).Value = 1.0;
		iExportCount++;
	}

	private void AddValveForExportStream(string inletStream, string outletStream)
	{
		string name = NameHelper.CreateName(bIsStream: false);
		BackDoor backDoor = (_Flowsheet.get_Operations((object)"ValveOpObject").Add(name, "ValveOpObject") as Valve) as BackDoor;
		string methodName = HYSYSFluidPackages.GetMethodName(method);
		if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(methodName))
		{
			BackDoor backDoor2 = _SimCase.BasisManager.FluidPackages[methodName] as BackDoor;
			backDoor.SendAttachObject(":CompList.300", backDoor2);
		}
		BackDoor backDoor3 = GetProcessStream(inletStream) as BackDoor;
		backDoor.SendAttachObject(":MaterialStream.500", backDoor3);
		backDoor3 = GetProcessStream(outletStream) as BackDoor;
		backDoor.SendAttachObject(":MaterialStream.501", backDoor3);
		(backDoor.get_BackDoorVariable((object)":PressureDrop.500").Variable as InternalRealVariable).SetValue(0.0, "");
	}

	private int GetPASerialNumber(HYSYSColumn columnData, int iStage)
	{
		int num = 0;
		foreach (string key in columnData.ColumnProducts.Keys)
		{
			foreach (int key2 in columnData.ColumnProducts[key].Keys)
			{
				if (key2 == iStage && _Data.HYSYSMainFlowsheetProducts.Contains(key))
				{
					num++;
				}
			}
		}
		return num;
	}

	private bool IsRecoverySpec(HYSYSColumn columnData, Specification spec)
	{
		bool result = false;
		if (spec.ReferenceName.ToUpper().Trim().StartsWith("REFF") && string.IsNullOrEmpty(spec.ReferenceID))
		{
			if (columnData.ColumnProducts.Keys.Contains(spec.PrimaryID))
			{
				result = true;
			}
		}
		else if (columnData.UID == _Data.UID)
		{
			if (_Data.HYSYSMainFlowsheetFeeds.Count == 1 && _Data.HYSYSMainFlowsheetFeeds.Contains(spec.ReferenceID))
			{
				result = true;
			}
		}
		else if (columnData.ColumnFeeds.Count == 1 && columnData.ColumnFeeds.Keys.Contains(spec.ReferenceID))
		{
			result = true;
		}
		return result;
	}

	private bool IsProductDrawSpecActive(HYSYSColumn columnData, string streamID, int trayNO)
	{
		if (columnData.UID != _Data.UID)
		{
			return true;
		}
		bool result = true;
		if (trayNO == 1)
		{
			if (bCondLDrawExist && bCondVDrawExist && !bCondDrawAdded)
			{
				bCondDrawAdded = true;
			}
			else
			{
				result = false;
			}
		}
		if (trayNO == _Data.stageNumber)
		{
			result = false;
		}
		return result;
	}

	private void GetRRSpecproperty(string strExpress, ref string flowbasis, ref bool bLight, ref bool bTotalLiq)
	{
		if (!strExpress.Contains("("))
		{
			return;
		}
		string[] array = ParseUtilty.CutBySpaceOrComma(ParseUtilty.GetParentheses(strExpress));
		for (int i = 0; i < array.Length; i++)
		{
			string text = array[i].ToUpper();
			switch (text)
			{
			case "L":
				bTotalLiq = true;
				break;
			case "LD":
				bTotalLiq = true;
				break;
			case "L2":
				bLight = false;
				break;
			case "LF":
				bTotalLiq = true;
				break;
			case "M":
				flowbasis = text;
				break;
			case "WT":
				flowbasis = text;
				break;
			case "LV":
				flowbasis = text;
				break;
			case "GV":
				flowbasis = text;
				break;
			}
		}
	}

	private bool IsInternalStageVFSpec(HYSYSColumn columnData, int stageNO, string phase)
	{
		if (columnData.ColumnFeeds.ContainsValue(stageNO))
		{
			return false;
		}
		if (phase == "V" && stageNO != columnData.stageNumber)
		{
			return false;
		}
		if (phase == "L" && stageNO != 1)
		{
			return false;
		}
		if (stageNO == 1 && columnData.bCondenserExist)
		{
			return false;
		}
		if (stageNO == columnData.stageNumber && columnData.bReboilerExist)
		{
			return false;
		}
		bool flag = false;
		foreach (string key in columnData.ColumnProducts.Keys)
		{
			if (columnData.ColumnProducts[key].ContainsKey(stageNO))
			{
				if (flag)
				{
					return false;
				}
				flag = true;
			}
		}
		return true;
	}

	private string GetStreamPropBackDoorMsg(string propType, string propName)
	{
		string text = "SelectNewProperty ";
		if (propName.Contains(" "))
		{
			return text + "\"" + propName + "\" " + propType;
		}
		return text + propName + " " + propType;
	}

	private string GetDistCurveProp(string DCType, double primaryRefVal)
	{
		string result = string.Empty;
		switch (Convert.ToInt16(primaryRefVal))
		{
		case 0:
			result = ((!(DCType == "TBP ")) ? (DCType + "IBP") : (DCType + "0%"));
			break;
		case 5:
			result = DCType + "5%";
			break;
		case 10:
			result = DCType + "10%";
			break;
		case 30:
			result = DCType + "30%";
			break;
		case 50:
			result = DCType + "50%";
			break;
		case 70:
			result = DCType + "70%";
			break;
		case 90:
			result = DCType + "90%";
			break;
		case 95:
			result = DCType + "95%";
			break;
		case 100:
			result = ((!(DCType == "D86 ")) ? (DCType + "100%") : (DCType + "FBP"));
			break;
		}
		return result;
	}

	private DoubleValue GetContentValueByPercent(DoubleValue val, string sUnit)
	{
		DoubleValue doubleValue = null;
		if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(sUnit) || sUnit == "FRAC")
		{
			return new DoubleValue("", val.Value * 100.0);
		}
		if (sUnit.ToUpper() == "PPM")
		{
			return new DoubleValue("", val.Value * 1000000.0);
		}
		return new DoubleValue("", val.Value);
	}

	private bool IsRefluxFeedRatioSpec(HYSYSColumn columnData, Specification spec)
	{
		bool result = false;
		if (!columnData.bCondenserExist || spec.PrimaryTrayNumber != 1)
		{
			return false;
		}
		if (columnData.CondenserType == HYSYSCondenserTypeEnum.FULLREFLUX && !columnData.bDrawWaterFromCondenser)
		{
			result = true;
		}
		return result;
	}

	private bool ConvertedGVValueToMoleValue(string sPhase, string flowbasis, ref DoubleValue originValue)
	{
		bool result = false;
		if (sPhase.ToUpper().Trim() == "V" && (flowbasis.ToUpper().Trim() == "GV" || flowbasis.ToUpper().Trim() == "V"))
		{
			result = true;
			DoubleValue doubleValue = UnitConverter.CovertGVToMV(originValue);
			originValue.Value = doubleValue.Value;
			originValue.Unit = doubleValue.Unit;
		}
		return result;
	}

	private void AddSupportForHCurve(BackDoor exchangerBD, bool bPumpAround, int plotPoints)
	{
	}

	private void SetHCurvePlotData(BackDoor heatExchangerBD, string typeName)
	{
		string moniker = ":Count.502";
		if (typeName != "PA")
		{
			moniker = ":Count.603";
		}
		InternalRealVariable obj = heatExchangerBD.get_BackDoorVariable((object)moniker).Variable as InternalRealVariable;
		obj.Value = 11.0;
		CleanResource.ReleaseComObjectHelper(obj, bFinalRelease: false);
	}

	private int GetProductDrawNO(HYSYSColumn columnData, string stmName, ref string ColUID)
	{
		int result = -1;
		if (_Data.ColumnProducts.Keys.Contains(stmName))
		{
			result = _Data.ColumnProducts[stmName].Keys.First();
			ColUID = _Data.UID;
		}
		else
		{
			foreach (HYSYSColumn value in SideColumnData.Values)
			{
				if (value.ColumnProducts.Keys.Contains(stmName))
				{
					result = value.ColumnProducts[stmName].Keys.First();
					ColUID = value.UID;
					break;
				}
			}
		}
		return result;
	}

	private string GetProductPhase(HYSYSColumn columnData, string stmName)
	{
		string result = string.Empty;
		if (_Data.ColumnProducts.Keys.Contains(stmName))
		{
			TypeUnitValue typeUnitValue = _Data.ColumnProducts[stmName].Values.First();
			if (typeUnitValue != null)
			{
				result = typeUnitValue.Name;
			}
		}
		else
		{
			foreach (HYSYSColumn value in SideColumnData.Values)
			{
				if (value.ColumnProducts.Keys.Contains(stmName))
				{
					TypeUnitValue typeUnitValue2 = value.ColumnProducts[stmName].Values.First();
					if (typeUnitValue2 != null)
					{
						result = typeUnitValue2.Name;
						break;
					}
				}
			}
		}
		return result;
	}

	private void GetAssayIPEPValue(ref double primaryRefVal)
	{
		P2Objects.P2Component p2Component = P2BaseObject.g_Map[Categories.Component] as P2Objects.P2Component;
		if (primaryRefVal == -10.0)
		{
			primaryRefVal = p2Component.ASSAY.TBPIP;
		}
		else if (primaryRefVal == -100.0)
		{
			primaryRefVal = p2Component.ASSAY.TBPEP;
		}
	}

	{
		throw new NotImplementedException();
	}
