public enum TraySectionType
{
	MAIN,
	STRIPPER,
	RECTIFIER
}
public class HYSYSComponentConverter
{
	private SimulationCase m_SimCase;

	private FluidPackage m_FluidPacket;

	private HYSYSObjects.HYSYSComponent m_Comps;

	private List<FluidPackage> _FluidPackages;

	private HYSYSFluidPackages _HYSYSFluidPackages;

	private bool _IsAspenPropertyImported;

	private bool _NonLibAdded = true;

	private readonly string AcidGasPackage = "AcidGasPkg";

	private int _CompNum;

	private readonly string AssayCorrSet = "AssayCorrSet";

	public HYSYSComponentConverter(SimulationCase simCase, List<FluidPackage> fluidPackets, HYSYSObjects.HYSYSComponent components, HYSYSFluidPackages logicHyFlowPackages, bool isAspenPropertyImported)
	{
		try
		{
			_IsAspenPropertyImported = isAspenPropertyImported;
			m_SimCase = simCase;
			if ((components == null || fluidPackets == null || fluidPackets.Count == 0) && simCase != null && logicHyFlowPackages != null)
			{
				SetDefaulAndMainFlowSheetFluidPackage(logicHyFlowPackages);
			}
			if (components == null || simCase == null)
			{
				return;
			}
			_HYSYSFluidPackages = logicHyFlowPackages;
			_FluidPackages = fluidPackets;
			m_Comps = components;
			if (fluidPackets == null || fluidPackets.Count == 0 || logicHyFlowPackages == null)
			{
				if (simCase.BasisManager.FluidPackages.Count > 0)
				{
					AddAssayAndBlend(components);
				}
			}
			else
			{
				m_FluidPacket = fluidPackets[0];
				AddHYSYSComponent(components);
			}
		}
		catch (Exception)
		{
		}
	}

	private void AddHYSYSComponent(HYSYSObjects.HYSYSComponent components)
	{
		AddLIBIDComponents(components.TraditionalComponents);
		AddLibCloneComp(components.DuplicateNamedLibComp);
		string thermoKeyByPackageName = HYSYSFluidPackages.GetThermoKeyByPackageName(m_FluidPacket.name);
		if (!_IsAspenPropertyImported)
		{
			if (thermoKeyByPackageName != AcidGasPackage)
			{
				SetNonLibraryComp();
				AddPetroleums(components.Petroleums);
			}
			else
			{
				_NonLibAdded = false;
			}
		}
		else
		{
			foreach (HypoGroup hypoGroup in m_SimCase.BasisManager.HypoGroups)
			{
				for (int i = 0; i < hypoGroup.HypoComponents.Count; i++)
				{
					Component component = hypoGroup.HypoComponents[i] as Component;
					try
					{
						if (!((string[])m_FluidPacket.Components.Names).Contains(component.name))
						{
							m_FluidPacket.Components.Add(component.name, "");
						}
					}
					catch
					{
						DebugPrint.OutputDebugString(DebugLevel.BuildHYSYSCase, WarningLevel.Error, string.Format(P2Globalization.GetString("AddComponents"), component.name));
					}
				}
			}
		}
		CopyComponentList();
		SetDefaulAndMainFlowSheetFluidPackage(_HYSYSFluidPackages);
		if (components.Assays != null && components.Assays.Count != 0 && components.Blends != null && components.Blends.Count != 0)
		{
			AddAssayAndBlend(components);
			CopyAssayComp();
		}
	}

	private void AddAssayAndBlend(HYSYSObjects.HYSYSComponent components)
	{
		m_SimCase.BasisManager.StartOilChange();
		SetAssayCorrelation();
		foreach (HYSYSObjects.HYSYSComponent.HYSYSAssay assay in components.Assays)
		{
			if (assay.AssayBoillingPercent != null && assay.AssayBoillingPercent.Length >= 2)
			{
				new HYSYSAssayConverter(m_SimCase, assay);
			}
		}
		foreach (HYSYSObjects.HYSYSComponent.HYSYSBlend blend in components.Blends)
		{
			AddBlend(blend);
		}
		m_SimCase.BasisManager.EndOilChange();
	}

	private void AddLIBIDComponents(List<string> traditionalComponents)
	{
		if (m_FluidPacket == null)
		{
			return;
		}
		foreach (string traditionalComponent in traditionalComponents)
		{
			try
			{
				m_FluidPacket.Components.Add(traditionalComponent, "");
			}
			catch
			{
				DebugPrint.OutputDebugString(DebugLevel.BuildHYSYSCase, WarningLevel.Error, string.Format(P2Globalization.GetString("AddComponents"), traditionalComponent));
			}
		}
	}

	private void AddPetroleums(List<HYSYSObjects.HYSYSComponent.HYSYSPetrol> items)
	{
		if (items == null)
		{
			return;
		}
		HypoGroup hypoGroup = m_SimCase.BasisManager.HypoGroups.Add("hypo") as HypoGroup;
		foreach (HYSYSObjects.HYSYSComponent.HYSYSPetrol item in items)
		{
			try
			{
				Component component = hypoGroup.HypoComponents.Add(item.Name) as Component;
				if (item.MW != 0.0)
				{
					component.MolecularWeight.SetValue(item.MW);
				}
				if (item.StdLiqDensity != null && item.StdLiqDensity.Value != 0.0)
				{
					component.StdLiquidDensity.SetValue(item.StdLiqDensity.Value, item.StdLiqDensity.Unit);
				}
				if (item.NBP != null && item.NBP.Value != 0.0)
				{
					DoubleValue hysysValue = UnitConverter.GetHysysValue(UnitClassEnum.Temperature, item.NBP.Value, item.NBP.Unit);
					component.NormalBoilingPoint.SetValue(hysysValue.Value, hysysValue.Unit);
					if (HYSYSUOMMap.GetConvertedValue(UnitClassEnum.Temperature, hysysValue, "C").Value >= 370.0 && (item.StdLiqDensity == null || item.StdLiqDensity.Value == 0.0))
					{
						component.StdLiquidDensity.SetValue(1000.0, "kg/m3");
					}
				}
				if (item.AddedProperties.Contains("ACENTRIC"))
				{
					component.Acentricity.SetValue(item.ACENTRIC);
				}
				if (item.AddedProperties.Contains("VC"))
				{
					component.CriticalVolume.SetValue(item.VC.Value, item.VC.Unit);
				}
				if (item.AddedProperties.Contains("TC"))
				{
					component.CriticalTemperature.SetValue(item.TC.Value, item.TC.Unit);
				}
				if (item.AddedProperties.Contains("PC"))
				{
					component.CriticalPressure.SetValue(item.PC.Value, item.PC.Unit);
				}
				if (item.AddedProperties.Contains("RACKETT"))
				{
					component.RackettZra.SetValue(item.RACKETT);
				}
				if (item.AddedProperties.Contains("DIPOLE"))
				{
					component.DipoleMoment.SetValue(item.DIPOLE.Value, item.DIPOLE.Unit);
				}
				if (item.AddedProperties.Contains("RADIUS"))
				{
					component.RadiusOfGyration.SetValue(item.RADIUS.Value, item.RADIUS.Unit);
				}
				if (item.AddedProperties.Contains("SOLUPARA"))
				{
					component.CNSolubility.SetValue(item.SOLUPARA);
				}
				if (item.AddedProperties.Contains("MOLVOL"))
				{
					component.MolarVolume.SetValue(item.MOLVOL.Value, item.MOLVOL.Unit);
				}
				if (item.AddedProperties.Contains("HCOMBUST"))
				{
					component.HeatOfCombustion.SetValue(item.HCOMBUST.Value, item.HCOMBUST.Unit);
				}
			}
			catch
			{
				DebugPrint.OutputDebugString(DebugLevel.BuildHYSYSCase, WarningLevel.Error, string.Format(P2Globalization.GetString("AddPetroleum"), item.Name));
			}
		}
		(hypoGroup as BackDoor).SendBackDoorMessage(":EstimateHypos");
		for (int i = 0; i < hypoGroup.HypoComponents.Count; i++)
		{
			Component component2 = hypoGroup.HypoComponents[i] as Component;
			try
			{
				m_FluidPacket.Components.Add(component2.name, "");
			}
			catch
			{
				DebugPrint.OutputDebugString(DebugLevel.BuildHYSYSCase, WarningLevel.Error, string.Format(P2Globalization.GetString("AddComponents"), component2.name));
			}
		}
	}

	private void AddLibCloneComp(Dictionary<int, ReNameLibComponent> items)
	{
		if (items == null)
		{
			return;
		}
		HypoGroup hypoGroup = m_SimCase.BasisManager.HypoGroups.Add("hypo") as HypoGroup;
		foreach (KeyValuePair<int, ReNameLibComponent> item in items)
		{
			try
			{
				string strMessage = string.Format(P2Globalization.GetString("AddSameNameComponent"), item.Value.ThisIndex, item.Value.P2Key);
				DebugPrint.OutputDebugString(DebugLevel.BuildP2Object, WarningLevel.Warning, strMessage);
				BackDoor obj = m_SimCase.BasisManager as BackDoor;
				BackDoor backDoor = m_SimCase.BasisManager.ComponentLists[0] as BackDoor;
				(obj.get_BackDoorVariable((object)":Boolean.301").Variable as InternalRealVariable).Value = 0.0;
				obj.SendAttachObject(":ComponentList.210", backDoor);
				string message = $"ComponentList.200(Master Component List)/AbstractComp.300({P2Objects.P2Component.GetHYSYSCompName(item.Value.RefIndex)}) :MakeMeAHypoViaCL";
				obj.SendBackDoorMessage(message);
			}
			catch
			{
			}
		}
		foreach (HypoGroup hypoGroup2 in m_SimCase.BasisManager.HypoGroups)
		{
			for (int i = 0; i < hypoGroup2.HypoComponents.Count; i++)
			{
				Component component = hypoGroup2.HypoComponents[i] as Component;
				try
				{
					m_FluidPacket.Components.Add(component.name, "");
				}
				catch
				{
					DebugPrint.OutputDebugString(DebugLevel.BuildHYSYSCase, WarningLevel.Error, string.Format(P2Globalization.GetString("AddComponents"), component.name));
				}
			}
		}
	}

	private void AddBlend(HYSYSObjects.HYSYSComponent.HYSYSBlend blenMapData)
	{
		if (blenMapData == null)
		{
			return;
		}
		HYSYSStream streamByName = HYSYSStream.GetStreamByName(blenMapData.StreamName);
		if (streamByName != null && streamByName.Source == null && m_SimCase.BasisManager.OilManager.Blends.Add("", "") is Blend blend)
		{
			blend.name = blenMapData.BlendName;
			blend.AddAssay(blenMapData.AssaysName);
			if (blenMapData.IsUserRanges)
			{
				blend.CutOptionType = BlendCutOptionType_enum.bco_UserRanges;
				blend.InitialCutPoint.SetValue(blenMapData.InitialCutPointValue, blenMapData.TempUnit);
				blend.EndRangeTemperature.SetValues(blenMapData.EndRangeTemperatureValue, blenMapData.TempUnit);
				blend.NumberOfCutsInRangeValue = blenMapData.NumberOfCutsInRangeValue;
				blend.SubmitUserRanges();
			}
			if (blend.IsReadyToInstall)
			{
				blend.InstallIntoStream(blenMapData.StreamName);
			}
		}
	}

	private void SetAssayCorrelation()
	{
		if (m_Comps.Assays == null || m_Comps.Assays.Count == 0)
		{
			return;
		}
		HYSYSObjects.HYSYSComponent.AssaySetting settings = m_Comps.Assays[0].Settings;
		if (settings != null && (!(settings.Characterize != "CAVETT") || !(settings.Characterize != "LK") || !(settings.MW != "LK")))
		{
			CorrelationSet obj = m_SimCase.BasisManager.OilManager.CorrelationSets.Add("", "CorrelationSet") as CorrelationSet;
			obj.name = AssayCorrSet;
			BackDoor backDoor = obj as BackDoor;
			if (settings.Characterize == "CAVETT")
			{
				InternalRealVariable obj2 = backDoor.get_BackDoorVariable((object)":Selection.200.0").Variable as InternalRealVariable;
				obj2.Value = 2.0;
				CleanResource.ReleaseComObjectHelper(obj2, bFinalRelease: false);
				InternalRealVariable obj3 = backDoor.get_BackDoorVariable((object)":Selection.201.0").Variable as InternalRealVariable;
				obj3.Value = 2.0;
				CleanResource.ReleaseComObjectHelper(obj3, bFinalRelease: false);
				InternalRealVariable obj4 = backDoor.get_BackDoorVariable((object)":Selection.203.0").Variable as InternalRealVariable;
				obj4.Value = 2.0;
				CleanResource.ReleaseComObjectHelper(obj4, bFinalRelease: false);
			}
			else if (settings.Characterize == "LK")
			{
				InternalRealVariable obj5 = backDoor.get_BackDoorVariable((object)":Selection.200.0").Variable as InternalRealVariable;
				obj5.Value = 1.0;
				CleanResource.ReleaseComObjectHelper(obj5, bFinalRelease: false);
				InternalRealVariable obj6 = backDoor.get_BackDoorVariable((object)":Selection.201.0").Variable as InternalRealVariable;
				obj6.Value = 1.0;
				CleanResource.ReleaseComObjectHelper(obj6, bFinalRelease: false);
				InternalRealVariable obj7 = backDoor.get_BackDoorVariable((object)":Selection.203.0").Variable as InternalRealVariable;
				obj7.Value = 1.0;
				CleanResource.ReleaseComObjectHelper(obj7, bFinalRelease: false);
			}
			if (settings.MW == "LK")
			{
				InternalRealVariable obj8 = backDoor.get_BackDoorVariable((object)":Selection.204.0").Variable as InternalRealVariable;
				obj8.Value = 1.0;
				CleanResource.ReleaseComObjectHelper(obj8, bFinalRelease: false);
			}
		}
	}

	private void SetNonLibraryComp()
	{
		if (m_Comps.NoLibraries == null || m_Comps.NoLibraries.Count == 0)
		{
			return;
		}
		HypoGroup hypoGroup = m_SimCase.BasisManager.HypoGroups.Add("NoLibrary") as HypoGroup;
		foreach (HYSYSObjects.HYSYSComponent.NoLibrary noLibrary in m_Comps.NoLibraries)
		{
			if (!noLibrary.AddedProperties.Contains("NBP") && (!noLibrary.AddedProperties.Contains("SPGR") || !noLibrary.AddedProperties.Contains("MW")) && (!noLibrary.AddedProperties.Contains("API") || !noLibrary.AddedProperties.Contains("MW")) && (!noLibrary.AddedProperties.Contains("STDDENSITY") || !noLibrary.AddedProperties.Contains("MW")))
			{
				continue;
			}
			try
			{
				Component component = hypoGroup.HypoComponents.Add(noLibrary.Name) as Component;
				if (noLibrary.AddedProperties.Contains("MW"))
				{
					component.MolecularWeight.SetValue(noLibrary.MW);
				}
				if (noLibrary.AddedProperties.Contains("SPGR"))
				{
					component.StdLiquidDensity.SetValue(noLibrary.SPGR.Value, noLibrary.SPGR.Unit);
				}
				if (noLibrary.AddedProperties.Contains("API"))
				{
					component.StdLiquidDensity.SetValue(noLibrary.API.Value, noLibrary.API.Unit);
				}
				if (noLibrary.AddedProperties.Contains("NBP"))
				{
					component.NormalBoilingPoint.SetValue(noLibrary.NBP.Value, noLibrary.NBP.Unit);
				}
				if (noLibrary.AddedProperties.Contains("ACENTRIC"))
				{
					component.Acentricity.SetValue(noLibrary.ACENTRIC);
				}
				if (noLibrary.AddedProperties.Contains("VC"))
				{
					component.CriticalVolume.SetValue(noLibrary.VC.Value, noLibrary.VC.Unit);
				}
				if (noLibrary.AddedProperties.Contains("TC"))
				{
					component.CriticalTemperature.SetValue(noLibrary.TC.Value, noLibrary.TC.Unit);
				}
				if (noLibrary.AddedProperties.Contains("PC"))
				{
					component.CriticalPressure.SetValue(noLibrary.PC.Value, noLibrary.PC.Unit);
				}
				if (noLibrary.AddedProperties.Contains("RACKETT"))
				{
					component.RackettZra.SetValue(noLibrary.RACKETT);
				}
				if (noLibrary.AddedProperties.Contains("DIPOLE"))
				{
					component.DipoleMoment.SetValue(noLibrary.DIPOLE.Value, noLibrary.DIPOLE.Unit);
				}
				if (noLibrary.AddedProperties.Contains("RADIUS"))
				{
					component.RadiusOfGyration.SetValue(noLibrary.RADIUS.Value, noLibrary.RADIUS.Unit);
				}
				if (noLibrary.AddedProperties.Contains("SOLUPARA"))
				{
					component.CNSolubility.SetValue(noLibrary.SOLUPARA);
				}
				if (noLibrary.AddedProperties.Contains("MOLVOL"))
				{
					component.MolarVolume.SetValue(noLibrary.MOLVOL.Value, noLibrary.MOLVOL.Unit);
				}
				if (noLibrary.AddedProperties.Contains("STDDENSITY"))
				{
					component.StdLiquidDensity.SetValue(noLibrary.STDDENSITY.Value, noLibrary.STDDENSITY.Unit);
				}
				if (noLibrary.AddedProperties.Contains("HCOMBUST"))
				{
					component.HeatOfCombustion.SetValue(noLibrary.HCOMBUST.Value, noLibrary.HCOMBUST.Unit);
				}
			}
			catch
			{
				DebugPrint.OutputDebugString(DebugLevel.BuildHYSYSCase, WarningLevel.Error, string.Format(P2Globalization.GetString("AddNoLibComponent"), noLibrary.Name));
			}
			(hypoGroup as BackDoor).SendBackDoorMessage(":EstimateHypos");
			for (int i = 0; i < hypoGroup.HypoComponents.Count; i++)
			{
				Component component2 = hypoGroup.HypoComponents[i] as Component;
				try
				{
					m_FluidPacket.Components.Add(component2.name, "");
				}
				catch
				{
					DebugPrint.OutputDebugString(DebugLevel.BuildHYSYSCase, WarningLevel.Error, string.Format(P2Globalization.GetString("AddComponents"), component2.name));
				}
			}
		}
	}

	private void CopyComponentList()
	{
		if (_FluidPackages.Count == 0)
		{
			return;
		}
		int num = 0;
		for (int i = 0; i < _FluidPackages.Count; i++)
		{
			if (_FluidPackages[i].Components.Count > _FluidPackages[num].Components.Count)
			{
				num = i;
			}
		}
		_CompNum = _FluidPackages[0].Components.Count;
		for (int j = 0; j < _FluidPackages.Count; j++)
		{
			if (j == num)
			{
				continue;
			}
			string thermoKeyByPackageName = HYSYSFluidPackages.GetThermoKeyByPackageName(_FluidPackages[j].name);
			foreach (Component component in _FluidPackages[0].Components)
			{
				try
				{
					string item = component.name.Replace("*", "");
					if (thermoKeyByPackageName == AcidGasPackage && !m_Comps.GetNoLibAndPetrolNames().Contains(item) && component.name != "Ammonia")
					{
						_FluidPackages[j].Components.Add(component.name);
					}
					else if (thermoKeyByPackageName != AcidGasPackage)
					{
						_FluidPackages[j].Components.Add(component.name);
					}
				}
				catch
				{
					DebugPrint.OutputDebugString(DebugLevel.BuildHYSYSCase, WarningLevel.Error, string.Format(P2Globalization.GetString("AddComponents"), component.name));
				}
			}
			if (!_NonLibAdded && thermoKeyByPackageName != AcidGasPackage)
			{
				try
				{
					m_FluidPacket = _FluidPackages[j];
					SetNonLibraryComp();
					AddPetroleums(m_Comps.Petroleums);
				}
				catch
				{
				}
			}
		}
	}

	private void CopyAssayComp()
	{
		if (_FluidPackages.Count == 0)
		{
			return;
		}
		int num = 0;
		for (int i = 0; i < _FluidPackages.Count; i++)
		{
			if (_FluidPackages[i].Components.Count > _FluidPackages[num].Components.Count)
			{
				num = i;
			}
		}
		for (int j = 0; j < _FluidPackages.Count; j++)
		{
			if (j == num)
			{
				continue;
			}
			string thermoKeyByPackageName = HYSYSFluidPackages.GetThermoKeyByPackageName(_FluidPackages[j].name);
			for (int k = _CompNum; k < _FluidPackages[num].Components.Count; k++)
			{
				Component component = _FluidPackages[num].Components[k] as Component;
				try
				{
					if (component != null)
					{
						string item = component.name.Replace("*", "");
						if (thermoKeyByPackageName == AcidGasPackage && !m_Comps.GetNoLibAndPetrolNames().Contains(item) && component.name != "Ammonia")
						{
							_FluidPackages[j].Components.Add(component.name);
						}
						else if (thermoKeyByPackageName != AcidGasPackage)
						{
							_FluidPackages[j].Components.Add(component.name);
						}
					}
				}
				catch
				{
					DebugPrint.OutputDebugString(DebugLevel.BuildHYSYSCase, WarningLevel.Error, string.Format(P2Globalization.GetString("AddComponents"), component.name));
				}
			}
		}
	}

	private void SetDefaulAndMainFlowSheetFluidPackage(HYSYSFluidPackages packages)
	{
		if (m_SimCase == null || packages == null)
		{
			return;
		}
		string[] source = (string[])m_SimCase.BasisManager.FluidPackages.Names;
		string text = packages.DefaultMehodeName;
		if (!source.Contains(text))
		{
			text = GetNewDefaultWhenDefautMethodNotConverted(packages);
			if (source.Contains(text))
			{
				DebugPrint.OutputDebugString(DebugLevel.BuildHYSYSCase, WarningLevel.Notes, string.Format(P2Globalization.GetString("DefaultMethodNotConverted"), packages.DefaultMehodeName, text));
			}
		}
		if (m_SimCase.BasisManager.FluidPackages.Count > 0 && !string.IsNullOrEmpty(packages.DefaultMehodeName) && source.Contains(text))
		{
			BackDoor backDoor = m_SimCase.BasisManager.FluidPackages[text] as BackDoor;
			(m_SimCase.BasisManager as BackDoor).SendAttachObject(":CompList.301", backDoor);
			m_SimCase.Flowsheet.FluidPackage = (FluidPackage)m_SimCase.BasisManager.FluidPackages[text];
		}
	}

	private string GetNewDefaultWhenDefautMethodNotConverted(HYSYSFluidPackages packages)
	{
		if (packages == null || string.IsNullOrEmpty(packages.DefaultMehodeName))
		{
			return string.Empty;
		}
		string result = string.Empty;
		bool flag = false;
		foreach (HYSYSFluidPackage eOSFluidPackage in packages.EOSFluidPackages)
		{
			if (eOSFluidPackage.FluidPackageName == packages.DefaultMehodeName)
			{
				flag = true;
				break;
			}
		}
		if (!flag)
		{
			foreach (HYSYSFluidPackage activityFluidPackage in packages.ActivityFluidPackages)
			{
				if (activityFluidPackage.FluidPackageName == packages.DefaultMehodeName)
				{
					flag = true;
					break;
				}
			}
		}
		if (!flag)
		{
			for (int i = 0; i < packages.EOSFluidPackages.Count; i++)
			{
				if (packages.EOSFluidPackages[i].FluidPackageName != "AMINE")
				{
					result = packages.EOSFluidPackages[i].FluidPackageName;
					flag = true;
					break;
				}
			}
		}
		if (!flag)
		{
			for (int j = 0; j < packages.ActivityFluidPackages.Count; j++)
			{
				if (packages.EOSFluidPackages[j].FluidPackageName != "AMINE")
				{
					result = packages.EOSFluidPackages[j].FluidPackageName;
					break;
				}
			}
		}
		return result;
	}
}
public class HYSYSAssayConverter
{
	private readonly string AssayCorrSet = "AssayCorrSet";

	private SimulationCase m_SimCase;

	private OilManager m_OilManager;

	private HYSYSObjects.HYSYSComponent.HYSYSAssay m_AssayData;

	private BackDoor m_BdOilManager;

	private BackDoor m_BdAssay;

	private InternalRealVariable m_InterRealVal;

	private InternalTextVariable m_InterTextVal;

	private RealFlexVariable m_UserPropCurve;

	public HYSYSAssayConverter(SimulationCase simuCase, HYSYSObjects.HYSYSComponent.HYSYSAssay assayData)
	{
		m_SimCase = simuCase;
		m_OilManager = simuCase.BasisManager.OilManager;
		m_AssayData = assayData;
		m_BdOilManager = m_OilManager as BackDoor;
		CreateAssay();
	}

	private void CreateAssay()
	{
		if (m_AssayData.DistillationType == HYSYSObjects.HYSYSComponent.DistillationTypeEnum.TBP)
		{
			AssayTBP assayTBP = m_OilManager.Assays.Add(m_AssayData.AssayName, "TBP") as AssayTBP;
			m_UserPropCurve = assayTBP.UserPropertyCurve;
		}
		else if (m_AssayData.DistillationType == HYSYSObjects.HYSYSComponent.DistillationTypeEnum.D86)
		{
			m_OilManager.Assays.Add(m_AssayData.AssayName, "D86");
		}
		else if (m_AssayData.DistillationType == HYSYSObjects.HYSYSComponent.DistillationTypeEnum.D1160)
		{
			m_OilManager.Assays.Add(m_AssayData.AssayName, "D1160");
		}
		else if (m_AssayData.DistillationType == HYSYSObjects.HYSYSComponent.DistillationTypeEnum.D2887)
		{
			m_OilManager.Assays.Add(m_AssayData.AssayName, "ASTMD2887");
		}
		int num = m_OilManager.Assays.Count - 1;
		if (num >= 0)
		{
			m_BdAssay = m_OilManager.Assays[num] as BackDoor;
		}
		if (m_BdAssay != null)
		{
			m_InterTextVal = m_BdAssay.get_BackDoorVariable((object)":Name.200").Variable as InternalTextVariable;
			m_InterTextVal.Value = m_AssayData.AssayName;
			CleanResource.ReleaseComObjectHelper(m_InterTextVal, bFinalRelease: false);
			m_InterRealVal = m_BdAssay.get_BackDoorVariable((object)":Boolean.210").Variable as InternalRealVariable;
			m_InterRealVal.Value = 1.0;
			CleanResource.ReleaseComObjectHelper(m_InterRealVal, bFinalRelease: false);
			SetDistillationData();
			SetDensityCurveData();
			SetMWData();
			SetKivs();
			SetUserProperties();
			SetAssaySettings();
			m_BdAssay.SendBackDoorMessage("ExecuteAssay");
			SetLightendsData();
			m_BdAssay.SendBackDoorMessage("ExecuteAssay");
		}
	}

	private void SetDistillationData()
	{
		m_InterRealVal = m_BdAssay.get_BackDoorVariable((object)":Selection.207").Variable as InternalRealVariable;
		m_InterRealVal.Value = 0.0;
		CleanResource.ReleaseComObjectHelper(m_InterRealVal, bFinalRelease: false);
		if (m_AssayData.AssayBasis == "LV")
		{
			m_InterRealVal = m_BdAssay.get_BackDoorVariable((object)":Selection.201").Variable as InternalRealVariable;
			m_InterRealVal.Value = -1.0;
			CleanResource.ReleaseComObjectHelper(m_InterRealVal, bFinalRelease: false);
		}
		else if (m_AssayData.AssayBasis == "WT")
		{
			m_InterRealVal = m_BdAssay.get_BackDoorVariable((object)":Selection.201").Variable as InternalRealVariable;
			m_InterRealVal.Value = -3.0;
			CleanResource.ReleaseComObjectHelper(m_InterRealVal, bFinalRelease: false);
		}
		if (m_AssayData.AssayBoillingPercent == null || m_AssayData.AssayBoillingPercent.Length < 2)
		{
			return;
		}
		m_BdAssay.SendBackDoorMessage("InitModalAssayInput");
		if (m_AssayData.AssayBoillingPercent != null && m_AssayData.AssayBoillingPercent.Length >= 2)
		{
			for (int i = 0; i < m_AssayData.AssayBoillingPercent.Length; i++)
			{
				m_InterRealVal = m_BdAssay.get_BackDoorVariable((object)(":ExtraData.230." + i)).Variable as InternalRealVariable;
				m_InterRealVal.Value = m_AssayData.AssayBoillingPercent[i];
				CleanResource.ReleaseComObjectHelper(m_InterRealVal, bFinalRelease: false);
			}
		}
		if (m_AssayData.BoilingTempValues != null && m_AssayData.BoilingTempValues.Length >= 2)
		{
			for (int j = 0; j < m_AssayData.BoilingTempValues.Length; j++)
			{
				m_InterRealVal = m_BdAssay.get_BackDoorVariable((object)(":ExtraData.231." + j)).Variable as InternalRealVariable;
				m_InterRealVal.SetValue(m_AssayData.BoilingTempValues[j], m_AssayData.DistillationTempUnit);
				CleanResource.ReleaseComObjectHelper(m_InterRealVal, bFinalRelease: false);
			}
		}
		m_BdAssay.SendBackDoorMessage("SubmitModalAssayInput");
		if (m_AssayData.DistillationPressure != null)
		{
			m_InterRealVal = m_BdAssay.get_BackDoorVariable((object)":Boolean.202").Variable as InternalRealVariable;
			m_InterRealVal.Value = 1.0;
			CleanResource.ReleaseComObjectHelper(m_InterRealVal, bFinalRelease: false);
			m_InterRealVal = m_BdAssay.get_BackDoorVariable((object)":Pressure.201").Variable as InternalRealVariable;
			m_InterRealVal.SetValue(m_AssayData.DistillationPressure.Value, m_AssayData.DistillationPressure.Unit);
			CleanResource.ReleaseComObjectHelper(m_InterRealVal, bFinalRelease: false);
		}
	}

	private void SetLightendsData()
	{
		if (m_AssayData.LightEndData == null)
		{
			return;
		}
		try
		{
			if (m_AssayData.LightEndData.RATE == null && m_AssayData.LightEndData.PERCENT == null && m_AssayData.LightEndData.FRACTION == null)
			{
				double num = 0.0;
				foreach (KeyValuePair<int, double> item in m_AssayData.LightEndData.COMPOSITION)
				{
					num += item.Value;
				}
				m_AssayData.LightEndData.RATE = new QualifierValue(m_AssayData.LightEndData.COMPOSITIONBasis, num.ToString());
			}
			PreHandleLightEndsData();
			if (m_AssayData.LightEndData.HyssyComposition != null && m_AssayData.LightEndData.LightEndsIsConverted)
			{
				m_InterRealVal = m_BdAssay.get_BackDoorVariable((object)":Selection.210").Variable as InternalRealVariable;
				m_InterRealVal.Value = -1.0;
				CleanResource.ReleaseComObjectHelper(m_InterRealVal, bFinalRelease: false);
				m_InterRealVal = m_BdAssay.get_BackDoorVariable((object)":Selection.207").Variable as InternalRealVariable;
				m_InterRealVal.Value = 2.0;
				CleanResource.ReleaseComObjectHelper(m_InterRealVal, bFinalRelease: false);
				if (m_AssayData.LightEndData.HysysBasis == HYSYSObjects.HYSYSComponent.LightEndsBasis.MolarRate)
				{
					m_InterRealVal = m_BdAssay.get_BackDoorVariable((object)":Selection.211").Variable as InternalRealVariable;
					m_InterRealVal.Value = -6.0;
					CleanResource.ReleaseComObjectHelper(m_InterRealVal, bFinalRelease: false);
				}
				else if (m_AssayData.LightEndData.HysysBasis == HYSYSObjects.HYSYSComponent.LightEndsBasis.VolumRate)
				{
					m_InterRealVal = m_BdAssay.get_BackDoorVariable((object)":Selection.211").Variable as InternalRealVariable;
					m_InterRealVal.Value = -5.0;
					CleanResource.ReleaseComObjectHelper(m_InterRealVal, bFinalRelease: false);
				}
				else if (m_AssayData.LightEndData.HysysBasis == HYSYSObjects.HYSYSComponent.LightEndsBasis.WeightRate)
				{
					m_InterRealVal = m_BdAssay.get_BackDoorVariable((object)":Selection.211").Variable as InternalRealVariable;
					m_InterRealVal.Value = -7.0;
					CleanResource.ReleaseComObjectHelper(m_InterRealVal, bFinalRelease: false);
				}
				else if (m_AssayData.LightEndData.HysysBasis == HYSYSObjects.HYSYSComponent.LightEndsBasis.VolumPercent)
				{
					m_InterRealVal = m_BdAssay.get_BackDoorVariable((object)":Selection.211").Variable as InternalRealVariable;
					m_InterRealVal.Value = -1.0;
					CleanResource.ReleaseComObjectHelper(m_InterRealVal, bFinalRelease: false);
				}
				else if (m_AssayData.LightEndData.HysysBasis == HYSYSObjects.HYSYSComponent.LightEndsBasis.WeightPercent)
				{
					m_InterRealVal = m_BdAssay.get_BackDoorVariable((object)":Selection.211").Variable as InternalRealVariable;
					m_InterRealVal.Value = -3.0;
					CleanResource.ReleaseComObjectHelper(m_InterRealVal, bFinalRelease: false);
				}
				else if (m_AssayData.LightEndData.HysysBasis == HYSYSObjects.HYSYSComponent.LightEndsBasis.MolarPercent)
				{
					m_InterRealVal = m_BdAssay.get_BackDoorVariable((object)":Selection.211").Variable as InternalRealVariable;
					m_InterRealVal.Value = -2.0;
					CleanResource.ReleaseComObjectHelper(m_InterRealVal, bFinalRelease: false);
				}
				if (m_AssayData.LightEndData.HyssyComposition.Count >= 0)
				{
					foreach (KeyValuePair<int, double> item2 in m_AssayData.LightEndData.HyssyComposition)
					{
						int hysysCompIndex = GetHysysCompIndex(item2.Key);
						if (hysysCompIndex >= 0)
						{
							m_InterRealVal = m_BdAssay.get_BackDoorVariable((object)(":ExtraData.200." + hysysCompIndex)).Variable as InternalRealVariable;
							if (m_AssayData.LightEndData.RATE != null)
							{
								m_InterRealVal.Value = item2.Value;
							}
							else
							{
								m_InterRealVal.SetValue(item2.Value * m_AssayData.LightEndData.HysysPercent, "");
							}
							CleanResource.ReleaseComObjectHelper(m_InterRealVal, bFinalRelease: false);
						}
					}
				}
				if (m_AssayData.LightEndData.HysysPercent > 0.0)
				{
					m_InterRealVal = m_BdAssay.get_BackDoorVariable((object)":Index.207").Variable as InternalRealVariable;
					m_InterRealVal.Value = m_AssayData.LightEndData.HysysPercent;
					CleanResource.ReleaseComObjectHelper(m_InterRealVal, bFinalRelease: false);
				}
				m_InterRealVal = m_BdAssay.get_BackDoorVariable((object)":Boolean.243").Variable as InternalRealVariable;
				m_InterRealVal.Value = 1.0;
			}
			else if (m_AssayData.LightEndData.HyssyComposition != null && !m_AssayData.LightEndData.LightEndsIsConverted)
			{
				DebugPrint.OutputDebugString(DebugLevel.BuildHYSYSObject, WarningLevel.Warning, string.Format("PROPERTY STREAM \"{0}\"  LIGHTEND {1}", m_AssayData.LightEndData.StreamName, P2Globalization.GetString("WasNotConverted")));
			}
		}
		catch
		{
		}
	}

	private void PreHandleLightEndsData()
	{
		ConvertLitghtEndsRate();
		if (m_AssayData.LightEndData.PERCENT != null)
		{
			ConvertLitghtEndsPERCENTorFRACTION(m_AssayData.LightEndData.PERCENT, isFraction: false);
		}
		else if (m_AssayData.LightEndData.FRACTION != null)
		{
			ConvertLitghtEndsPERCENTorFRACTION(m_AssayData.LightEndData.FRACTION, isFraction: true);
		}
	}

	private void ConvertLitghtEndsRate()
	{
		if (m_AssayData.LightEndData.RATE == null || m_AssayData.LightEndData.COMPOSITION == null)
		{
			return;
		}
		m_AssayData.LightEndData.COMPOSITIONUnit = UnitConverter.GetHysysUnit(UnitClassEnum.Rate, string.Empty, m_AssayData.LightEndData.COMPOSITIONBasis);
		for (int i = 0; i < m_AssayData.LightEndData.COMPOSITION.Count; i++)
		{
			int key = m_AssayData.LightEndData.COMPOSITION.Keys.ToList()[i];
			DoubleValue val = new DoubleValue(m_AssayData.LightEndData.COMPOSITIONUnit, m_AssayData.LightEndData.COMPOSITION[key]);
			DoubleValue convertedValue = HYSYSUOMMap.GetConvertedValue(UnitClassEnum.Rate, val, "", m_AssayData.LightEndData.COMPOSITIONBasis);
			m_AssayData.LightEndData.COMPOSITION[key] = convertedValue.Value;
		}
		Dictionary<int, double> dictionary = new Dictionary<int, double>();
		if ((m_AssayData.LightEndData.RATE.Qualifier.StartsWith("L") && m_AssayData.LightEndData.COMPOSITIONBasis.StartsWith("L")) || (m_AssayData.LightEndData.RATE.Qualifier.StartsWith("V") && m_AssayData.LightEndData.COMPOSITIONBasis.StartsWith("V")) || (m_AssayData.LightEndData.RATE.Qualifier.StartsWith("G") && m_AssayData.LightEndData.COMPOSITIONBasis.StartsWith("G")) || (m_AssayData.LightEndData.RATE.Qualifier.StartsWith("M") && m_AssayData.LightEndData.COMPOSITIONBasis.StartsWith("M")) || (m_AssayData.LightEndData.RATE.Qualifier.StartsWith("W") && m_AssayData.LightEndData.COMPOSITIONBasis.StartsWith("W")))
		{
			foreach (KeyValuePair<int, double> item in m_AssayData.LightEndData.COMPOSITION)
			{
				DoubleValue val2 = new DoubleValue(m_AssayData.LightEndData.COMPOSITIONUnit, item.Value);
				string unit = HYSYSUOMMap.GetDefault(UnitClassEnum.Rate, m_AssayData.LightEndData.COMPOSITIONBasis).Unit;
				val2 = HYSYSUOMMap.GetConvertedValue(UnitClassEnum.Rate, val2, unit, m_AssayData.LightEndData.COMPOSITIONBasis);
				dictionary.Add(item.Key, val2.Value);
			}
			if (m_AssayData.LightEndData.RATE.Qualifier.StartsWith("M"))
			{
				m_AssayData.LightEndData.HysysBasis = HYSYSObjects.HYSYSComponent.LightEndsBasis.MolarRate;
			}
			else if (m_AssayData.LightEndData.RATE.Qualifier.StartsWith("L") || m_AssayData.LightEndData.RATE.Qualifier.StartsWith("G") || m_AssayData.LightEndData.RATE.Qualifier.StartsWith("V"))
			{
				m_AssayData.LightEndData.HysysBasis = HYSYSObjects.HYSYSComponent.LightEndsBasis.VolumRate;
			}
			else if (m_AssayData.LightEndData.RATE.Qualifier.StartsWith("W"))
			{
				m_AssayData.LightEndData.HysysBasis = HYSYSObjects.HYSYSComponent.LightEndsBasis.WeightRate;
			}
		}
		else if (m_AssayData.LightEndData.RATE.Qualifier.StartsWith("L") && m_AssayData.LightEndData.COMPOSITIONBasis.StartsWith("M"))
		{
			m_AssayData.LightEndData.HysysBasis = HYSYSObjects.HYSYSComponent.LightEndsBasis.VolumRate;
			foreach (KeyValuePair<int, double> item2 in m_AssayData.LightEndData.COMPOSITION)
			{
				Component hysysComponent = GetHysysComponent(item2.Key);
				if (hysysComponent != null)
				{
					DoubleValue val3 = new DoubleValue(m_AssayData.LightEndData.COMPOSITIONUnit, item2.Value);
					string unit2 = HYSYSUOMMap.GetDefault(UnitClassEnum.Rate, m_AssayData.LightEndData.COMPOSITIONBasis).Unit;
					val3 = HYSYSUOMMap.GetConvertedValue(UnitClassEnum.Rate, val3, unit2, m_AssayData.LightEndData.COMPOSITIONBasis);
					double value = val3.Value * hysysComponent.MolecularWeightValue / hysysComponent.StdLiquidDensityValue;
					dictionary.Add(item2.Key, value);
				}
			}
		}
		else if (m_AssayData.LightEndData.RATE.Qualifier.StartsWith("L") && m_AssayData.LightEndData.COMPOSITIONBasis.StartsWith("W"))
		{
			m_AssayData.LightEndData.HysysBasis = HYSYSObjects.HYSYSComponent.LightEndsBasis.VolumRate;
			foreach (KeyValuePair<int, double> item3 in m_AssayData.LightEndData.COMPOSITION)
			{
				Component hysysComponent2 = GetHysysComponent(item3.Key);
				if (hysysComponent2 != null)
				{
					DoubleValue val4 = new DoubleValue(m_AssayData.LightEndData.COMPOSITIONUnit, item3.Value);
					string unit3 = HYSYSUOMMap.GetDefault(UnitClassEnum.Rate, m_AssayData.LightEndData.COMPOSITIONBasis).Unit;
					val4 = HYSYSUOMMap.GetConvertedValue(UnitClassEnum.Rate, val4, unit3, m_AssayData.LightEndData.COMPOSITIONBasis);
					double value2 = val4.Value / hysysComponent2.StdLiquidDensityValue;
					dictionary.Add(item3.Key, value2);
				}
			}
		}
		else if (m_AssayData.LightEndData.RATE.Qualifier.StartsWith("W") && m_AssayData.LightEndData.COMPOSITIONBasis.StartsWith("M"))
		{
			m_AssayData.LightEndData.HysysBasis = HYSYSObjects.HYSYSComponent.LightEndsBasis.WeightRate;
			foreach (KeyValuePair<int, double> item4 in m_AssayData.LightEndData.COMPOSITION)
			{
				Component hysysComponent3 = GetHysysComponent(item4.Key);
				if (hysysComponent3 != null)
				{
					DoubleValue val5 = new DoubleValue(m_AssayData.LightEndData.COMPOSITIONUnit, item4.Value);
					string unit4 = HYSYSUOMMap.GetDefault(UnitClassEnum.Rate, m_AssayData.LightEndData.COMPOSITIONBasis).Unit;
					val5 = HYSYSUOMMap.GetConvertedValue(UnitClassEnum.Rate, val5, unit4, m_AssayData.LightEndData.COMPOSITIONBasis);
					double value3 = val5.Value * hysysComponent3.MolecularWeightValue;
					dictionary.Add(item4.Key, value3);
				}
			}
		}
		else if (m_AssayData.LightEndData.RATE.Qualifier.StartsWith("W") && (m_AssayData.LightEndData.COMPOSITIONBasis.StartsWith("L") || m_AssayData.LightEndData.COMPOSITIONBasis.StartsWith("G") || m_AssayData.LightEndData.COMPOSITIONBasis.StartsWith("V")))
		{
			m_AssayData.LightEndData.HysysBasis = HYSYSObjects.HYSYSComponent.LightEndsBasis.WeightRate;
			foreach (KeyValuePair<int, double> item5 in m_AssayData.LightEndData.COMPOSITION)
			{
				Component hysysComponent4 = GetHysysComponent(item5.Key);
				if (hysysComponent4 != null)
				{
					DoubleValue val6 = new DoubleValue(m_AssayData.LightEndData.COMPOSITIONUnit, item5.Value);
					string unit5 = HYSYSUOMMap.GetDefault(UnitClassEnum.Rate, m_AssayData.LightEndData.COMPOSITIONBasis).Unit;
					val6 = HYSYSUOMMap.GetConvertedValue(UnitClassEnum.Rate, val6, unit5, m_AssayData.LightEndData.COMPOSITIONBasis);
					double value4 = val6.Value * hysysComponent4.StdLiquidDensityValue;
					dictionary.Add(item5.Key, value4);
				}
			}
		}
		else if (m_AssayData.LightEndData.RATE.Qualifier.StartsWith("M") && m_AssayData.LightEndData.COMPOSITIONBasis.StartsWith("W"))
		{
			m_AssayData.LightEndData.HysysBasis = HYSYSObjects.HYSYSComponent.LightEndsBasis.MolarRate;
			foreach (KeyValuePair<int, double> item6 in m_AssayData.LightEndData.COMPOSITION)
			{
				Component hysysComponent5 = GetHysysComponent(item6.Key);
				if (hysysComponent5 != null)
				{
					DoubleValue val7 = new DoubleValue(m_AssayData.LightEndData.COMPOSITIONUnit, item6.Value);
					string unit6 = HYSYSUOMMap.GetDefault(UnitClassEnum.Rate, m_AssayData.LightEndData.COMPOSITIONBasis).Unit;
					val7 = HYSYSUOMMap.GetConvertedValue(UnitClassEnum.Rate, val7, unit6, m_AssayData.LightEndData.COMPOSITIONBasis);
					double value5 = val7.Value / hysysComponent5.MolecularWeightValue;
					dictionary.Add(item6.Key, value5);
				}
			}
		}
		else if (m_AssayData.LightEndData.RATE.Qualifier.StartsWith("M") && (m_AssayData.LightEndData.COMPOSITIONBasis.StartsWith("L") || m_AssayData.LightEndData.COMPOSITIONBasis.StartsWith("V")))
		{
			m_AssayData.LightEndData.HysysBasis = HYSYSObjects.HYSYSComponent.LightEndsBasis.MolarRate;
			foreach (KeyValuePair<int, double> item7 in m_AssayData.LightEndData.COMPOSITION)
			{
				Component hysysComponent6 = GetHysysComponent(item7.Key);
				if (hysysComponent6 != null)
				{
					DoubleValue val8 = new DoubleValue(m_AssayData.LightEndData.COMPOSITIONUnit, item7.Value);
					string unit7 = HYSYSUOMMap.GetDefault(UnitClassEnum.Rate, m_AssayData.LightEndData.COMPOSITIONBasis).Unit;
					val8 = HYSYSUOMMap.GetConvertedValue(UnitClassEnum.Rate, val8, unit7, m_AssayData.LightEndData.COMPOSITIONBasis);
					double value6 = val8.Value * hysysComponent6.StdLiquidDensityValue / hysysComponent6.MolecularWeightValue;
					dictionary.Add(item7.Key, value6);
				}
			}
		}
		m_AssayData.LightEndData.HyssyComposition = dictionary;
		double num = CacuLightEndsPercentWithRate();
		if (num > 0.0)
		{
			m_AssayData.LightEndData.LightEndsIsConverted = true;
			m_AssayData.LightEndData.HysysPercent = num;
		}
		else
		{
			m_AssayData.LightEndData.LightEndsIsConverted = false;
			m_AssayData.LightEndData.HysysPercent = -1.0;
		}
	}

	private double CacuLightEndsPercentWithRate()
	{
		double result = -1.0;
		if (m_AssayData.LightEndData.RATE == null || m_AssayData.LightEndData.COMPOSITION == null)
		{
			return -1.0;
		}
		P2Stream obj = P2BaseObject.g_Map[Categories.Stream] as P2Stream;
		P2Stream.AssayStream assayStream = null;
		foreach (P2Stream.BaseStream stream in obj.Streams)
		{
			if (stream.Stream == m_AssayData.LightEndData.StreamName)
			{
				assayStream = stream as P2Stream.AssayStream;
				break;
			}
		}
		if (assayStream == null)
		{
			return -1.0;
		}
		double num = 0.0;
		double num2 = 0.0;
		if (m_AssayData.DistillationType == HYSYSObjects.HYSYSComponent.DistillationTypeEnum.TBP)
		{
			AssayTBP assayTBP = m_OilManager.Assays[m_AssayData.AssayName] as AssayTBP;
			num2 = assayTBP.BulkMassDensity.Value;
			num = assayTBP.BulkMolecularWeight.Value;
			if (num2 <= 0.0)
			{
				double[] array = (double[])assayTBP.WorkingCurveMassDensity.Values;
				double[] array2 = (double[])assayTBP.WorkingCurveMolesValue;
				if (array != null && array.Length != 0)
				{
					double num3 = 0.0;
					for (int i = 0; i < array.Length; i++)
					{
						num3 += array[i] * array2[i];
					}
					num2 = num3;
				}
			}
			if (num <= 0.0)
			{
				double[] array3 = (double[])assayTBP.WorkingCurveMW.Values;
				double[] array4 = (double[])assayTBP.WorkingCurveMolesValue;
				if (array3 != null && array3.Length != 0)
				{
					double num4 = 0.0;
					for (int j = 0; j < array3.Length; j++)
					{
						num4 += array3[j] * array4[j];
					}
					num = num4;
				}
			}
		}
		else if (m_AssayData.DistillationType == HYSYSObjects.HYSYSComponent.DistillationTypeEnum.D86)
		{
			AssayD86 assayD = m_OilManager.Assays[m_AssayData.AssayName] as AssayD86;
			num2 = assayD.BulkMassDensity.Value;
			num = assayD.BulkMolecularWeight.Value;
			if (num2 <= 0.0)
			{
				double[] array5 = (double[])assayD.WorkingCurveMassDensity.Values;
				double[] array6 = (double[])assayD.WorkingCurveMolesValue;
				if (array5 != null && array5.Length != 0)
				{
					double num5 = 0.0;
					for (int k = 0; k < array5.Length; k++)
					{
						num5 += array5[k] * array6[k];
					}
					num2 = num5;
				}
			}
			if (num <= 0.0)
			{
				double[] array7 = (double[])assayD.WorkingCurveMW.Values;
				double[] array8 = (double[])assayD.WorkingCurveMolesValue;
				if (array7 != null && array7.Length != 0)
				{
					double num6 = 0.0;
					for (int l = 0; l < array7.Length; l++)
					{
						num6 += array7[l] * array8[l];
					}
					num = num6;
				}
			}
		}
		else if (m_AssayData.DistillationType == HYSYSObjects.HYSYSComponent.DistillationTypeEnum.D1160)
		{
			AssayD86_D1160 assayD86_D = m_OilManager.Assays[m_AssayData.AssayName] as AssayD86_D1160;
			num2 = assayD86_D.BulkMassDensity.Value;
			num = assayD86_D.BulkMolecularWeight.Value;
			if (num2 <= 0.0)
			{
				double[] array9 = (double[])assayD86_D.WorkingCurveMassDensity.Values;
				double[] array10 = (double[])assayD86_D.WorkingCurveMolesValue;
				if (array9 != null && array9.Length != 0)
				{
					double num7 = 0.0;
					for (int m = 0; m < array9.Length; m++)
					{
						num7 += array9[m] * array10[m];
					}
					num2 = num7;
				}
			}
			if (num <= 0.0)
			{
				double[] array11 = (double[])assayD86_D.WorkingCurveMW.Values;
				double[] array12 = (double[])assayD86_D.WorkingCurveMolesValue;
				if (array11 != null && array11.Length != 0)
				{
					double num8 = 0.0;
					for (int n = 0; n < array11.Length; n++)
					{
						num8 += array11[n] * array12[n];
					}
					num = num8;
				}
			}
		}
		else if (m_AssayData.DistillationType == HYSYSObjects.HYSYSComponent.DistillationTypeEnum.D2887)
		{
			AssayASTM_D2887 assayASTM_D = m_OilManager.Assays[m_AssayData.AssayName] as AssayASTM_D2887;
			num2 = assayASTM_D.BulkMassDensity.Value;
			num = assayASTM_D.BulkMolecularWeight.Value;
			if (num2 <= 0.0)
			{
				double[] array13 = (double[])assayASTM_D.WorkingCurveMassDensity.Values;
				double[] array14 = (double[])assayASTM_D.WorkingCurveMolesValue;
				if (array13 != null && array13.Length != 0)
				{
					double num9 = 0.0;
					for (int num10 = 0; num10 < array13.Length; num10++)
					{
						num9 += array13[num10] * array14[num10];
					}
					num2 = num9;
				}
			}
			if (num <= 0.0)
			{
				double[] array15 = (double[])assayASTM_D.WorkingCurveMW.Values;
				double[] array16 = (double[])assayASTM_D.WorkingCurveMolesValue;
				if (array15 != null && array15.Length != 0)
				{
					double num11 = 0.0;
					for (int num12 = 0; num12 < array15.Length; num12++)
					{
						num11 += array15[num12] * array16[num12];
					}
					num = num11;
				}
			}
		}
		if (num2 <= 0.0)
		{
			return -1.0;
		}
		QualifierValue rATE = m_AssayData.LightEndData.RATE;
		TypeUnitValue hysysValue = UnitConverter.GetHysysValue(UnitClassEnum.Rate, rATE.GetValueAsDouble(), "", m_AssayData.LightEndData.RATE.Qualifier);
		TypeUnitValue hysysValue2 = UnitConverter.GetHysysValue(UnitClassEnum.Rate, assayStream.Rate.Value, assayStream.Rate.Unit, assayStream.Rate.Type);
		DoubleValue val = new DoubleValue(hysysValue.Unit, hysysValue.Value);
		DoubleValue val2 = new DoubleValue(hysysValue2.Unit, hysysValue2.Value);
		string unit = HYSYSUOMMap.GetDefault(UnitClassEnum.Rate, m_AssayData.LightEndData.RATE.Qualifier).Unit;
		val = HYSYSUOMMap.GetConvertedValue(UnitClassEnum.Rate, val, unit, m_AssayData.LightEndData.RATE.Qualifier);
		unit = HYSYSUOMMap.GetDefault(UnitClassEnum.Rate, assayStream.Rate.Type).Unit;
		val2 = HYSYSUOMMap.GetConvertedValue(UnitClassEnum.Rate, val2, unit, assayStream.Rate.Type);
		if (val != null && val2 != null && val2.Value > 0.0)
		{
			if ((rATE.Qualifier.StartsWith("W") && assayStream.Rate.Type.StartsWith("W")) || (rATE.Qualifier.StartsWith("L") && assayStream.Rate.Type.StartsWith("L")) || (rATE.Qualifier.StartsWith("V") && assayStream.Rate.Type.StartsWith("V")) || (rATE.Qualifier.StartsWith("G") && assayStream.Rate.Type.StartsWith("G")) || (rATE.Qualifier.StartsWith("M") && assayStream.Rate.Type.StartsWith("M")))
			{
				result = val.Value / val2.Value * 100.0;
			}
			else if (m_AssayData.LightEndData.RATE.Qualifier.StartsWith("L") && assayStream.Rate.Type.StartsWith("M"))
			{
				if (num > 0.0)
				{
					result = val.Value / val2.Value * num2 / num * 100.0;
				}
			}
			else if (m_AssayData.LightEndData.RATE.Qualifier.StartsWith("L") && assayStream.Rate.Type.StartsWith("W"))
			{
				result = val.Value / val2.Value * num2 * 100.0;
			}
			else if (m_AssayData.LightEndData.RATE.Qualifier.StartsWith("W") && assayStream.Rate.Type.StartsWith("M"))
			{
				if (num > 0.0)
				{
					result = val.Value / val2.Value / num * 100.0;
				}
			}
			else if (m_AssayData.LightEndData.RATE.Qualifier.StartsWith("W") && (assayStream.Rate.Type.StartsWith("L") || assayStream.Rate.Type.StartsWith("L") || assayStream.Rate.Type.StartsWith("G") || assayStream.Rate.Type.StartsWith("V")))
			{
				result = val.Value / val2.Value / num2 * 100.0;
			}
			else if (m_AssayData.LightEndData.RATE.Qualifier.StartsWith("M") && assayStream.Rate.Type.StartsWith("W"))
			{
				if (num > 0.0)
				{
					result = val.Value / val2.Value * num * 100.0;
				}
			}
			else if (m_AssayData.LightEndData.RATE.Qualifier.StartsWith("M") && (assayStream.Rate.Type.StartsWith("L") || assayStream.Rate.Type.StartsWith("L") || assayStream.Rate.Type.StartsWith("V")) && num > 0.0)
			{
				result = val.Value / val2.Value * num / num2 * 100.0;
			}
		}
		return result;
	}

	private void ConvertLitghtEndsPERCENTorFRACTION(QualifierValue qulifiedInput, bool isFraction)
	{
		if (qulifiedInput == null || m_AssayData.LightEndData.COMPOSITION == null)
		{
			return;
		}
		m_AssayData.LightEndData.LightEndsIsConverted = true;
		double num = 0.0;
		foreach (KeyValuePair<int, double> item in m_AssayData.LightEndData.COMPOSITION)
		{
			num += item.Value;
		}
		Dictionary<int, double> dictionary = new Dictionary<int, double>();
		if ((qulifiedInput.Qualifier.StartsWith("W") && m_AssayData.LightEndData.COMPOSITIONBasis.StartsWith("W")) || (qulifiedInput.Qualifier.StartsWith("L") && m_AssayData.LightEndData.COMPOSITIONBasis.StartsWith("L")) || (qulifiedInput.Qualifier.StartsWith("V") && m_AssayData.LightEndData.COMPOSITIONBasis.StartsWith("V")))
		{
			foreach (KeyValuePair<int, double> item2 in m_AssayData.LightEndData.COMPOSITION)
			{
				dictionary.Add(item2.Key, item2.Value / num);
			}
			if (qulifiedInput.Qualifier.StartsWith("W"))
			{
				m_AssayData.LightEndData.HysysBasis = HYSYSObjects.HYSYSComponent.LightEndsBasis.WeightPercent;
			}
			else if (qulifiedInput.Qualifier.StartsWith("L") || qulifiedInput.Qualifier.StartsWith("V"))
			{
				m_AssayData.LightEndData.HysysBasis = HYSYSObjects.HYSYSComponent.LightEndsBasis.VolumPercent;
			}
		}
		else if (qulifiedInput.Qualifier.StartsWith("L") && m_AssayData.LightEndData.COMPOSITIONBasis.StartsWith("M"))
		{
			double num2 = 0.0;
			m_AssayData.LightEndData.HysysBasis = HYSYSObjects.HYSYSComponent.LightEndsBasis.VolumPercent;
			foreach (KeyValuePair<int, double> item3 in m_AssayData.LightEndData.COMPOSITION)
			{
				Component hysysComponent = GetHysysComponent(item3.Key);
				if (hysysComponent != null)
				{
					double num3 = item3.Value * hysysComponent.MolecularWeightValue / hysysComponent.StdLiquidDensityValue;
					num2 += num3;
					dictionary.Add(item3.Key, num3);
				}
			}
			for (int i = 0; i < dictionary.Count; i++)
			{
				int key = dictionary.Keys.ToList()[i];
				if (num2 > 0.0)
				{
					dictionary[key] /= num2;
				}
			}
		}
		else if (qulifiedInput.Qualifier.StartsWith("L") && m_AssayData.LightEndData.COMPOSITIONBasis.StartsWith("W"))
		{
			m_AssayData.LightEndData.HysysBasis = HYSYSObjects.HYSYSComponent.LightEndsBasis.VolumPercent;
			double num4 = 0.0;
			foreach (KeyValuePair<int, double> item4 in m_AssayData.LightEndData.COMPOSITION)
			{
				Component hysysComponent2 = GetHysysComponent(item4.Key);
				if (hysysComponent2 != null)
				{
					double num5 = item4.Value / hysysComponent2.StdLiquidDensityValue;
					num4 += num5;
					dictionary.Add(item4.Key, num5);
				}
			}
			for (int j = 0; j < dictionary.Count; j++)
			{
				int key2 = dictionary.Keys.ToList()[j];
				if (num4 > 0.0)
				{
					dictionary[key2] /= num4;
				}
			}
		}
		else if (qulifiedInput.Qualifier.StartsWith("W") && m_AssayData.LightEndData.COMPOSITIONBasis.StartsWith("M"))
		{
			m_AssayData.LightEndData.HysysBasis = HYSYSObjects.HYSYSComponent.LightEndsBasis.WeightPercent;
			double num6 = 0.0;
			foreach (KeyValuePair<int, double> item5 in m_AssayData.LightEndData.COMPOSITION)
			{
				Component hysysComponent3 = GetHysysComponent(item5.Key);
				if (hysysComponent3 != null)
				{
					double num7 = item5.Value * hysysComponent3.MolecularWeightValue;
					num6 += num7;
					dictionary.Add(item5.Key, num7);
				}
			}
			for (int k = 0; k < dictionary.Count; k++)
			{
				int key3 = dictionary.Keys.ToList()[k];
				if (num6 > 0.0)
				{
					dictionary[key3] /= num6;
				}
			}
		}
		else if (qulifiedInput.Qualifier.StartsWith("W") && (m_AssayData.LightEndData.COMPOSITIONBasis.StartsWith("L") || m_AssayData.LightEndData.COMPOSITIONBasis.StartsWith("L") || m_AssayData.LightEndData.COMPOSITIONBasis.StartsWith("V")))
		{
			m_AssayData.LightEndData.HysysBasis = HYSYSObjects.HYSYSComponent.LightEndsBasis.WeightPercent;
			double num8 = 0.0;
			foreach (KeyValuePair<int, double> item6 in m_AssayData.LightEndData.COMPOSITION)
			{
				Component hysysComponent4 = GetHysysComponent(item6.Key);
				if (hysysComponent4 != null)
				{
					double num9 = item6.Value * hysysComponent4.StdLiquidDensityValue;
					num8 += num9;
					dictionary.Add(item6.Key, num9);
				}
			}
			for (int l = 0; l < dictionary.Count; l++)
			{
				int key4 = dictionary.Keys.ToList()[l];
				if (num8 > 0.0)
				{
					dictionary[key4] /= num8;
				}
			}
		}
		else
		{
			m_AssayData.LightEndData.LightEndsIsConverted = false;
		}
		m_AssayData.LightEndData.HyssyComposition = dictionary;
		if (isFraction)
		{
			m_AssayData.LightEndData.HysysPercent = qulifiedInput.GetValueAsDouble() * 100.0;
		}
		else
		{
			m_AssayData.LightEndData.HysysPercent = qulifiedInput.GetValueAsDouble();
		}
	}

	private Component GetHysysComponent(int p2CompIndex)
	{
		string hYSYSCompName = P2Objects.P2Component.GetHYSYSCompName(p2CompIndex);
		if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(hYSYSCompName))
		{
			return null;
		}
		if (((string[])m_SimCase.Flowsheet.FluidPackage.ComponentList.Components.Names).Contains(hYSYSCompName))
		{
			return m_SimCase.Flowsheet.FluidPackage.ComponentList.Components[hYSYSCompName] as Component;
		}
		return null;
	}

	private int GetHysysCompIndex(int p2index)
	{
		string hYSYSCompName = P2Objects.P2Component.GetHYSYSCompName(p2index);
		int result = -1;
		string[] source = (string[])m_SimCase.Flowsheet.FluidPackage.ComponentList.Components.Names;
		if (source.Contains(hYSYSCompName))
		{
			result = m_SimCase.Flowsheet.FluidPackage.ComponentList.Components.get_index((object)hYSYSCompName);
		}
		else if (source.Contains(hYSYSCompName + "*"))
		{
			result = m_SimCase.Flowsheet.FluidPackage.ComponentList.Components.get_index((object)(hYSYSCompName + "*"));
		}
		else
		{
			string plusCompNameFromHYSYS = P2Objects.P2Component.GetPlusCompNameFromHYSYS(hYSYSCompName);
			if (source.Contains(plusCompNameFromHYSYS))
			{
				result = m_SimCase.Flowsheet.FluidPackage.ComponentList.Components.get_index((object)plusCompNameFromHYSYS);
			}
		}
		return result;
	}

	private void SetDensityCurveData()
	{
		if (m_AssayData.IsWATSONK && m_AssayData.BulkMassDensity != null)
		{
			m_InterRealVal = m_BdAssay.get_BackDoorVariable((object)":WatsonK.200").Variable as InternalRealVariable;
			m_InterRealVal.Value = m_AssayData.BulkMassDensity.Value;
			CleanResource.ReleaseComObjectHelper(m_InterRealVal, bFinalRelease: false);
		}
		else if (m_AssayData.BulkMassDensity != null)
		{
			m_InterRealVal = null;
			m_InterRealVal = m_BdAssay.get_BackDoorVariable((object)":StdLiqMassDen.200").Variable as InternalRealVariable;
			m_InterRealVal.SetValue(m_AssayData.BulkMassDensity.Value, m_AssayData.BulkMassDensity.Unit);
			CleanResource.ReleaseComObjectHelper(m_InterRealVal, bFinalRelease: false);
		}
		if (m_AssayData.DensityPercent == null || m_AssayData.DensityPercent.Length < 2 || m_AssayData.IsWATSONK)
		{
			return;
		}
		m_InterRealVal = m_BdAssay.get_BackDoorVariable((object)":Selection.205").Variable as InternalRealVariable;
		m_InterRealVal.Value = 2.0;
		CleanResource.ReleaseComObjectHelper(m_InterRealVal, bFinalRelease: false);
		m_InterRealVal = m_BdAssay.get_BackDoorVariable((object)":Selection.207").Variable as InternalRealVariable;
		m_InterRealVal.Value = 2.0;
		CleanResource.ReleaseComObjectHelper(m_InterRealVal, bFinalRelease: false);
		m_BdAssay.SendBackDoorMessage("InitModalAssayInput");
		if (m_AssayData.DensityPercent != null && m_AssayData.DensityPercent.Length >= 2)
		{
			for (int i = 0; i < m_AssayData.DensityPercent.Length; i++)
			{
				m_InterRealVal = m_BdAssay.get_BackDoorVariable((object)(":ExtraData.230." + i)).Variable as InternalRealVariable;
				m_InterRealVal.Value = m_AssayData.DensityPercent[i];
				CleanResource.ReleaseComObjectHelper(m_InterRealVal, bFinalRelease: false);
			}
		}
		if (m_AssayData.DensityValues != null && m_AssayData.DensityValues.Length >= 2)
		{
			try
			{
				for (int j = 0; j < m_AssayData.DensityValues.Length; j++)
				{
					m_InterRealVal = m_BdAssay.get_BackDoorVariable((object)(":ExtraData.231." + j)).Variable as InternalRealVariable;
					if (m_AssayData.BulkMassDensity.Unit.StartsWith("API"))
					{
						m_InterRealVal.SetValue(m_AssayData.DensityValues[j], "API");
					}
					else
					{
						m_InterRealVal.SetValue(m_AssayData.DensityValues[j], m_AssayData.BulkMassDensity.Unit);
					}
					CleanResource.ReleaseComObjectHelper(m_InterRealVal, bFinalRelease: false);
				}
			}
			catch (Exception ex)
			{
				DebugPrint.OutputDebugString(DebugLevel.BuildHYSYSCase, WarningLevel.Error, string.Format(P2Globalization.GetString("SetAssayDataOfDensityFailed"), ex.StackTrace));
			}
		}
		m_BdAssay.SendBackDoorMessage("SubmitModalAssayInput");
	}

	private void SetMWData()
	{
		if (m_AssayData.MWAverage != 0.0)
		{
			m_InterRealVal = m_BdAssay.get_BackDoorVariable((object)":MoleWeight.200").Variable as InternalRealVariable;
			m_InterRealVal.Value = m_AssayData.MWAverage;
			CleanResource.ReleaseComObjectHelper(m_InterRealVal, bFinalRelease: false);
		}
		if (m_AssayData.MWData == null || m_AssayData.MWData.Count <= 2)
		{
			return;
		}
		m_InterRealVal = m_BdAssay.get_BackDoorVariable((object)":Selection.204").Variable as InternalRealVariable;
		m_InterRealVal.Value = 2.0;
		CleanResource.ReleaseComObjectHelper(m_InterRealVal, bFinalRelease: false);
		m_InterRealVal = m_BdAssay.get_BackDoorVariable((object)":Selection.207").Variable as InternalRealVariable;
		m_InterRealVal.Value = 1.0;
		CleanResource.ReleaseComObjectHelper(m_InterRealVal, bFinalRelease: false);
		m_BdAssay.SendBackDoorMessage("InitModalAssayInput");
		int num = -1;
		foreach (KeyValuePair<double, double> mWDatum in m_AssayData.MWData)
		{
			num++;
			m_InterRealVal = m_BdAssay.get_BackDoorVariable((object)(":ExtraData.230." + num)).Variable as InternalRealVariable;
			m_InterRealVal.Value = mWDatum.Key;
			CleanResource.ReleaseComObjectHelper(m_InterRealVal, bFinalRelease: false);
			m_InterRealVal = m_BdAssay.get_BackDoorVariable((object)(":ExtraData.231." + num)).Variable as InternalRealVariable;
			m_InterRealVal.SetValue(mWDatum.Value);
			CleanResource.ReleaseComObjectHelper(m_InterRealVal, bFinalRelease: false);
		}
		m_BdAssay.SendBackDoorMessage("SubmitModalAssayInput");
	}

			CleanResource.ReleaseComObjectHelper(m_InterRealVal, bFinalRelease: false);

		else if (m_AssayData.KVIS.DataTemp1 != null && m_AssayData.KVIS.DataTemp2 != null)

				CleanResource.ReleaseComObjectHelper(m_InterRealVal, bFinalRelease: false);

				m_InterRealVal.SetValue(m_AssayData.KVIS.DataTemp1.Values.ToArray()[i], m_AssayData.KVIS.KinematicViscosityUnit);
				CleanResource.ReleaseComObjectHelper(m_InterRealVal, bFinalRelease: false);

			m_InterRealVal.Value = 4.0;

				m_InterRealVal.Value = m_AssayData.KVIS.DataTemp2.Keys.ToArray()[j];
				CleanResource.ReleaseComObjectHelper(m_InterRealVal, bFinalRelease: false);
				m_InterRealVal = m_BdAssay.get_BackDoorVariable((object)(":ExtraData.231." + j)).Variable as InternalRealVariable;
				m_InterRealVal.SetValue(m_AssayData.KVIS.DataTemp2.Values.ToArray()[j], m_AssayData.KVIS.KinematicViscosityUnit);
				CleanResource.ReleaseComObjectHelper(m_InterRealVal, bFinalRelease: false);
			}
			m_BdAssay.SendBackDoorMessage("SubmitModalAssayInput");

			CleanResource.ReleaseComObjectHelper(m_InterRealVal, bFinalRelease: false);
		}
	}

	private void SetUserProperties()
	{
		if (m_AssayData.UserProperties == null || m_AssayData.UserProperties.Count == 0)
		{
			return;
		}
		string[] source = (string[])m_OilManager.UserProperties.Names;
		foreach (HYSYSObjects.HYSYSComponent.HYSYSUserProperty userProperty2 in m_AssayData.UserProperties)
		{
			if (!source.Contains(userProperty2.Name))
			{
				UserProperty userProperty = m_OilManager.UserProperties.Add("UP-" + userProperty2.Name, "UserProperty") as UserProperty;
				userProperty.name = userProperty2.Name;
				if (userProperty2.VariableType == UnitClassEnum.Temperature.ToString())
				{
					userProperty.UserPropertyVariableType = evtVariableType_enum.evtTemperature;
				}
				else if (userProperty2.VariableType == UnitClassEnum.Fraction.ToString())
				{
					userProperty.UserPropertyVariableType = evtVariableType_enum.evtPercent;
				}
				else if (userProperty2.VariableType == UnitClassEnum.Enthalpy.ToString())
				{
					userProperty.UserPropertyVariableType = evtVariableType_enum.evtMassEnthalpy;
				}
			}
		}
		source = (string[])m_OilManager.UserProperties.Names;
		foreach (HYSYSObjects.HYSYSComponent.HYSYSUserProperty userProperty3 in m_AssayData.UserProperties)
		{
			try
			{
				string text = string.Empty;
				if (source.Contains(userProperty3.Name))
				{
					text = "AddUSerCurve \"" + userProperty3.Name + "\"";
				}
				else if (source.Contains("UP-" + userProperty3.Name))
				{
					text = "AddUSerCurve \"UP-" + userProperty3.Name + "\"";
				}
				if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(text))
				{
					DebugPrint.OutputDebugString(DebugLevel.BuildHYSYSCase, WarningLevel.Notes, "user property " + userProperty3.Name + "is not converted");
					continue;
				}
				m_BdAssay.SendBackDoorMessage(text);
				m_InterRealVal = m_BdAssay.get_BackDoorVariable((object)":ExtraData.205").Variable as InternalRealVariable;
				m_InterRealVal.SetValue(userProperty3.Average.Value, userProperty3.Average.Unit);
				CleanResource.ReleaseComObjectHelper(m_InterRealVal, bFinalRelease: false);
				if (userProperty3.Data != null && userProperty3.Data.Count > 2)
				{
					m_BdAssay.SendBackDoorMessage("InitModalUserCurveAssayInput");
					for (int i = 0; i < userProperty3.Data.Count; i++)
					{
						m_InterRealVal = m_BdAssay.get_BackDoorVariable((object)(":ExtraData.233." + i)).Variable as InternalRealVariable;
						m_InterRealVal.Value = userProperty3.Data.Keys.ToList()[i];
						CleanResource.ReleaseComObjectHelper(m_InterRealVal, bFinalRelease: false);
						m_InterRealVal = m_BdAssay.get_BackDoorVariable((object)(":ExtraData.234." + i)).Variable as InternalRealVariable;
						m_InterRealVal.SetValue(userProperty3.Data.Values.ToArray()[i], userProperty3.DataUnit);
						CleanResource.ReleaseComObjectHelper(m_InterRealVal, bFinalRelease: false);
					}
					m_BdAssay.SendBackDoorMessage("SubmitModalUserCurveAssayInput");
				}
			}
			catch (Exception ex)
			{
				DebugPrint.OutputDebugString(DebugLevel.BuildHYSYSCase, WarningLevel.Error, string.Format(P2Globalization.GetString("SetComponentUserPropertyFailed"), ex.StackTrace));
			}
		}
	}

	private void SetAssaySettings()
	{
		if (m_AssayData.Settings != null)
		{
			if (m_AssayData.Settings.Conversion == "API87")
			{
				m_InterRealVal = m_BdAssay.get_BackDoorVariable((object)":Selection.203").Variable as InternalRealVariable;
				m_InterRealVal.Value = 1.0;
				CleanResource.ReleaseComObjectHelper(m_InterRealVal, bFinalRelease: false);
				m_InterRealVal = m_BdAssay.get_BackDoorVariable((object)":Selection.212").Variable as InternalRealVariable;
				m_InterRealVal.Value = 0.0;
				CleanResource.ReleaseComObjectHelper(m_InterRealVal, bFinalRelease: false);
			}
			else if (m_AssayData.Settings.Conversion == "EDOK")
			{
				m_InterRealVal = m_BdAssay.get_BackDoorVariable((object)":Selection.203").Variable as InternalRealVariable;
				m_InterRealVal.Value = 4.0;
				CleanResource.ReleaseComObjectHelper(m_InterRealVal, bFinalRelease: false);
			}
			else if (m_AssayData.Settings.Conversion == "API94")
			{
				m_InterRealVal = m_BdAssay.get_BackDoorVariable((object)":Selection.203").Variable as InternalRealVariable;
				m_InterRealVal.Value = 3.0;
				CleanResource.ReleaseComObjectHelper(m_InterRealVal, bFinalRelease: false);
				m_InterRealVal = m_BdAssay.get_BackDoorVariable((object)":Selection.212").Variable as InternalRealVariable;
				m_InterRealVal.Value = 1.0;
				CleanResource.ReleaseComObjectHelper(m_InterRealVal, bFinalRelease: false);
			}
			if (m_AssayData.Settings.Curvefit == "CURRENT" || m_AssayData.Settings.Curvefit == "VER6")
			{
				m_InterRealVal = m_BdAssay.get_BackDoorVariable((object)":Selection.214.0").Variable as InternalRealVariable;
				m_InterRealVal.Value = 1.0;
				CleanResource.ReleaseComObjectHelper(m_InterRealVal, bFinalRelease: false);
				m_InterRealVal = m_BdAssay.get_BackDoorVariable((object)":Selection.216").Variable as InternalRealVariable;
				m_InterRealVal.Value = 1.0;
				CleanResource.ReleaseComObjectHelper(m_InterRealVal, bFinalRelease: false);
				m_InterRealVal = m_BdAssay.get_BackDoorVariable((object)":Selection.217").Variable as InternalRealVariable;
				m_InterRealVal.Value = 1.0;
				CleanResource.ReleaseComObjectHelper(m_InterRealVal, bFinalRelease: false);
				m_InterRealVal = m_BdAssay.get_BackDoorVariable((object)":Selection.218").Variable as InternalRealVariable;
				m_InterRealVal.Value = 1.0;
				CleanResource.ReleaseComObjectHelper(m_InterRealVal, bFinalRelease: false);
				m_InterRealVal = m_BdAssay.get_BackDoorVariable((object)":Selection.219").Variable as InternalRealVariable;
				m_InterRealVal.Value = 1.0;
				CleanResource.ReleaseComObjectHelper(m_InterRealVal, bFinalRelease: false);
			}
			else if (m_AssayData.Settings.Curvefit == "VER8")
			{
				m_InterRealVal = m_BdAssay.get_BackDoorVariable((object)":Selection.214.0").Variable as InternalRealVariable;
				m_InterRealVal.Value = 2.0;
				CleanResource.ReleaseComObjectHelper(m_InterRealVal, bFinalRelease: false);
				m_InterRealVal = m_BdAssay.get_BackDoorVariable((object)":Selection.216").Variable as InternalRealVariable;
				m_InterRealVal.Value = 1.0;
				CleanResource.ReleaseComObjectHelper(m_InterRealVal, bFinalRelease: false);
				m_InterRealVal = m_BdAssay.get_BackDoorVariable((object)":Selection.217").Variable as InternalRealVariable;
				m_InterRealVal.Value = 1.0;
				CleanResource.ReleaseComObjectHelper(m_InterRealVal, bFinalRelease: false);
				m_InterRealVal = m_BdAssay.get_BackDoorVariable((object)":Selection.218").Variable as InternalRealVariable;
				m_InterRealVal.Value = 1.0;
				CleanResource.ReleaseComObjectHelper(m_InterRealVal, bFinalRelease: false);
				m_InterRealVal = m_BdAssay.get_BackDoorVariable((object)":Selection.219").Variable as InternalRealVariable;
				m_InterRealVal.Value = 1.0;
				CleanResource.ReleaseComObjectHelper(m_InterRealVal, bFinalRelease: false);
			}
			if (((string[])m_OilManager.CorrelationSets.Names).Contains(AssayCorrSet))
			{
				BackDoor backDoor = (m_OilManager.CorrelationSets[AssayCorrSet] as CorrelationSet) as BackDoor;
				m_BdAssay.SendAttachObject(":CorrelationSet.201", backDoor);
			}
		}
	}
}
internal class HYSYSComponentSplitterConverter : HYSYSObjectConverterBase
{
	private FractOp _componentSplitter;

	private HYSYSComponentSplitter _data;

	private double FeedTemperature;

	private double FeedPressure;

	public HYSYSComponentSplitterConverter(SimulationCase simulationCase, HYSYSComponentSplitter data)
		: base(simulationCase)
	{
		try
		{
			_data = data;
			_componentSplitter = _Flowsheet.get_Operations((object)"FractOpObject").Add(data.UID, "FractOpObject") as FractOp;
			CreateComponentSplitter();
		}
		catch (Exception)
		{
		}
	}

	private void CreateComponentSplitter()
	{
		BackDoor backDoor = null;
		ProcessStream processStream = null;
		ProcessStream processStream2 = null;
		ProcessStream processStream3 = null;
		BackDoor backDoor2 = null;
		ProcessStream processStream4 = null;
		BackDoor backDoor3 = null;
		bool flag = false;
		bool flag2 = false;
		BackDoor backDoor4 = null;
		try
		{
			backDoor = _componentSplitter as BackDoor;
			if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(_data.FluidPackage))
			{
				string methodName = HYSYSFluidPackages.GetMethodName(_data.FluidPackage);
				if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(methodName))
				{
					backDoor4 = _SimCase.BasisManager.FluidPackages[methodName] as BackDoor;
					backDoor.SendAttachObject(":CompList.300", backDoor4);
				}
			}
			for (int i = 0; i < _data.InletStreams.Count; i++)
			{
				processStream = GetProcessStream(_data.InletStreams[i]);
				backDoor2 = processStream as BackDoor;
				backDoor.SendAttachObject(":MaterialStream.500." + i, backDoor2);
				if (_data.InletStreams.Count == 1)
				{
					GetFeedInfo(backDoor2);
				}
				CleanResource.ReleaseComObjectHelper(processStream, bFinalRelease: false);
				CleanResource.ReleaseComObjectHelper(backDoor2, bFinalRelease: false);
			}
			processStream2 = GetProcessStream(_data.OVHDStreams[0]);
			backDoor2 = processStream2 as BackDoor;
			backDoor.SendAttachObject(":MaterialStream.503.0", backDoor2);
			CleanProductInfo(backDoor2);
			if (_data.OVHDPressure != null)
			{
				InternalRealVariable obj = backDoor2.get_BackDoorVariable((object)":Pressure.501.0").Variable as InternalRealVariable;
				obj.SetValue(_data.OVHDPressure.Value, _data.OVHDPressure.Unit);
				CleanResource.ReleaseComObjectHelper(obj, bFinalRelease: false);
			}
			if (_data.OVHDDP != null && FeedPressure != 0.0)
			{
				InternalRealVariable internalRealVariable = backDoor2.get_BackDoorVariable((object)":Pressure.501.0").Variable as InternalRealVariable;
				DoubleValue convertedValue = HYSYSUOMMap.GetConvertedValue(UnitClassEnum.DropPressure, _data.OVHDDP, "kpa");
				if (FeedPressure - convertedValue.Value > 0.0)
				{
					internalRealVariable.SetValue(FeedPressure - convertedValue.Value, "");
				}
				else
				{
					_data.ConvertNotes.Add(string.Format(P2Globalization.GetString("KeyWordWasNotConverted"), "OVHDDP"));
				}
				CleanResource.ReleaseComObjectHelper(internalRealVariable, bFinalRelease: false);
			}
			else if (_data.OVHDDP != null && FeedPressure == 0.0)
			{
				_data.ConvertNotes.Add(string.Format(P2Globalization.GetString("KeyWordWasNotConverted"), "OVHDDP"));
			}
			if (_data.OVHDTemperature != null)
			{
				flag2 = true;
				InternalRealVariable obj2 = backDoor2.get_BackDoorVariable((object)":Temperature.501.0").Variable as InternalRealVariable;
				obj2.SetValue(_data.OVHDTemperature.Value, _data.OVHDTemperature.Unit);
				CleanResource.ReleaseComObjectHelper(obj2, bFinalRelease: false);
			}
			if (_data.OVHDDT != null && FeedTemperature != 0.0)
			{
				flag2 = true;
				InternalRealVariable obj3 = backDoor2.get_BackDoorVariable((object)":Temperature.501.0").Variable as InternalRealVariable;
				DoubleValue convertedValue2 = HYSYSUOMMap.GetConvertedValue(UnitClassEnum.DropTemperature, _data.OVHDDT, "c");
				obj3.SetValue(FeedTemperature + convertedValue2.Value, "");
				CleanResource.ReleaseComObjectHelper(obj3, bFinalRelease: false);
			}
			else if (_data.OVHDDT != null && FeedTemperature == 0.0)
			{
				_data.ConvertNotes.Add(string.Format(P2Globalization.GetString("KeyWordWasNotConverted"), "OVHDDT"));
			}
			processStream3 = GetProcessStream(_data.BTMStreams[0]);
			backDoor2 = processStream3 as BackDoor;
			backDoor.SendAttachObject(":MaterialStream.502", backDoor2);
			CleanProductInfo(backDoor2);
			if (_data.BTMSPressure != null)
			{
				InternalRealVariable obj4 = backDoor2.get_BackDoorVariable((object)":Pressure.501.0").Variable as InternalRealVariable;
				obj4.SetValue(_data.BTMSPressure.Value, _data.BTMSPressure.Unit);
				CleanResource.ReleaseComObjectHelper(obj4, bFinalRelease: false);
			}
			if (_data.BTMSDP != null && FeedPressure != 0.0)
			{
				InternalRealVariable obj5 = backDoor2.get_BackDoorVariable((object)":Pressure.501.0").Variable as InternalRealVariable;
				DoubleValue convertedValue3 = HYSYSUOMMap.GetConvertedValue(UnitClassEnum.DropPressure, _data.BTMSDP, "kpa");
				obj5.SetValue(FeedPressure - convertedValue3.Value, "");
				CleanResource.ReleaseComObjectHelper(obj5, bFinalRelease: false);
			}
			else if (_data.BTMSDP != null && FeedPressure == 0.0)
			{
				_data.ConvertNotes.Add(string.Format(P2Globalization.GetString("KeyWordWasNotConverted"), "BTMSDP"));
			}
			if (_data.BTMSTemperature != null)
			{
				flag = true;
				InternalRealVariable obj6 = backDoor2.get_BackDoorVariable((object)":Temperature.501.0").Variable as InternalRealVariable;
				obj6.SetValue(_data.BTMSTemperature.Value, _data.BTMSTemperature.Unit);
				CleanResource.ReleaseComObjectHelper(obj6, bFinalRelease: false);
			}
			if (_data.BTMSDT != null && FeedTemperature != 0.0)
			{
				flag = true;
				InternalRealVariable obj7 = backDoor2.get_BackDoorVariable((object)":Temperature.501.0").Variable as InternalRealVariable;
				DoubleValue convertedValue4 = HYSYSUOMMap.GetConvertedValue(UnitClassEnum.DropTemperature, _data.BTMSDT, "c");
				obj7.SetValue(FeedTemperature + convertedValue4.Value, "");
				CleanResource.ReleaseComObjectHelper(obj7, bFinalRelease: false);
			}
			else if (_data.BTMSDT != null && FeedTemperature == 0.0)
			{
				_data.ConvertNotes.Add(string.Format(P2Globalization.GetString("KeyWordWasNotConverted"), "BTMSDT"));
			}
			if (!flag2 && !flag)
			{
				InternalRealVariable obj8 = backDoor.get_BackDoorVariable((object)":Selection.503").Variable as InternalRealVariable;
				obj8.Value = 1.0;
				CleanResource.ReleaseComObjectHelper(obj8, bFinalRelease: false);
			}
			string name = NameHelper.CreateEnergyStreamName();
			processStream4 = _Flowsheet.EnergyStreams.Add(name, "") as ProcessStream;
			backDoor3 = processStream4 as BackDoor;
			backDoor.SendAttachObject(":EnergyStream.503.0", backDoor3);
			if (_data.DUTY != null && (_data.BTMSTemperature == null || _data.OVHDTemperature == null))
			{
				InternalRealVariable obj9 = backDoor3.get_BackDoorVariable((object)":HeatFlow.501.0").Variable as InternalRealVariable;
				obj9.SetValue(_data.DUTY.Value, _data.DUTY.Unit);
				CleanResource.ReleaseComObjectHelper(obj9, bFinalRelease: false);
			}
			foreach (Specification oVHDSpec in _data.OVHDSpecs)
			{
				SetSplits(backDoor, oVHDSpec, bBottom: false);
			}
			foreach (Specification bTMSSpec in _data.BTMSSpecs)
			{
				SetSplits(backDoor, bTMSSpec, bBottom: true);
			}
			InternalTextVariable internalTextVariable = backDoor.get_BackDoorTextVariable((object)":Notes.400").Variable as InternalTextVariable;
			foreach (string convertNote in _data.ConvertNotes)
			{
				internalTextVariable.Value = internalTextVariable.Value + convertNote + "\n";
			}
		}
		catch (Exception)
		{
			DebugPrint.OutputDebugString(DebugLevel.BuildHYSYSCase, WarningLevel.Error, string.Format(P2Globalization.GetString("UOConverter_Failed"), "HYSYS Component Splitter unit operation block", _data.UID));
		}
		finally
		{
			CleanResource.ReleaseComObjectHelper(backDoor, bFinalRelease: false);
			CleanResource.ReleaseComObjectHelper(processStream, bFinalRelease: false);
			CleanResource.ReleaseComObjectHelper(processStream2, bFinalRelease: false);
			CleanResource.ReleaseComObjectHelper(processStream3, bFinalRelease: false);
			CleanResource.ReleaseComObjectHelper(backDoor2, bFinalRelease: false);
			CleanResource.ReleaseComObjectHelper(processStream4, bFinalRelease: false);
			CleanResource.ReleaseComObjectHelper(backDoor3, bFinalRelease: false);
		}
	}

	private void GetFeedInfo(BackDoor feedBD)
	{
		InternalRealVariable internalRealVariable = null;
		try
		{
			internalRealVariable = feedBD.get_BackDoorVariable((object)":Temperature.501.0").Variable as InternalRealVariable;
			if (internalRealVariable.Value != -32767.0)
			{
				FeedTemperature = internalRealVariable.Value;
			}
			internalRealVariable = feedBD.get_BackDoorVariable((object)":Pressure.501.0").Variable as InternalRealVariable;
			if (internalRealVariable.Value != -32767.0)
			{
				FeedPressure = internalRealVariable.Value;
			}
		}
		catch (Exception)
		{
			DebugPrint.OutputDebugString(DebugLevel.BuildHYSYSCase, WarningLevel.Error, string.Format(P2Globalization.GetString("GetUOFeedInfoFailed"), "Component Splitter", _data.UID));
		}
		finally
		{
			CleanResource.ReleaseComObjectHelper(internalRealVariable, bFinalRelease: false);
		}
	}

	private void SetSplits(BackDoor target, Specification spec, bool bBottom)
	{
		InternalRealVariable internalRealVariable = null;
		try
		{
			string sTCASPECTYPE = spec.STCASPECTYPE;
			int num = -1;
			int num2 = -1;
			num = Convert.ToInt32(spec.STCASPECFIRSTCOMP);
			num2 = (string.IsNullOrEmpty(spec.STCASPECLASTCOMP) ? num : Convert.ToInt32(spec.STCASPECLASTCOMP));
			string sTCASPECVALUE = spec.STCASPECVALUE;
			for (int i = num - 1; i < num2; i++)
			{
				if (sTCASPECTYPE.StartsWith("F") || sTCASPECTYPE.StartsWith("X"))
				{
					int hysysindex = -1;
					if (!IsComponentConverted(i + 1, out hysysindex))
					{
						continue;
					}
					internalRealVariable = target.get_BackDoorVariable((object)(":MoleFraction.503.0." + hysysindex)).Variable as InternalRealVariable;
					if (internalRealVariable != null && internalRealVariable.CanModify)
					{
						if (bBottom)
						{
							internalRealVariable.Value = 1.0 - Convert.ToDouble(sTCASPECVALUE);
						}
						else
						{
							internalRealVariable.Value = Convert.ToDouble(sTCASPECVALUE);
						}
					}
				}
				else
				{
					_data.ConvertNotes.Add(P2Globalization.GetString("STCALRateNotConverted"));
				}
			}
		}
		catch (Exception)
		{
			DebugPrint.OutputDebugString(DebugLevel.BuildHYSYSCase, WarningLevel.Error, string.Format(P2Globalization.GetString("SetUOSplitsFailed"), "Component Splitter", _data.UID));
		}
		finally
		{
			CleanResource.ReleaseComObjectHelper(internalRealVariable, bFinalRelease: false);
		}
	}

	private bool IsComponentConverted(int p2index, out int hysysindex)
	{
		bool result = false;
		hysysindex = -1;
		string hYSYSCompName = P2Objects.P2Component.GetHYSYSCompName(p2index);
		if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(hYSYSCompName))
		{
			hysysindex = GetHysysCompIndex(hYSYSCompName);
			if (!hysysindex.Equals(-1))
			{
				result = true;
			}
		}
		return result;
	}

	private void CleanProductInfo(BackDoor productBD)
	{
		InternalRealVariable internalRealVariable = null;
		InternalRealVariable internalRealVariable2 = null;
		try
		{
			internalRealVariable = productBD.get_BackDoorVariable((object)":MolarFlow.502.0").Variable as InternalRealVariable;
			if (internalRealVariable.Value != -32767.0)
			{
				if (internalRealVariable.CanModify)
				{
					internalRealVariable.Value = -32767.0;
				}
				else
				{
					internalRealVariable = productBD.get_BackDoorVariable((object)":MassFlow.502.0").Variable as InternalRealVariable;
					if (internalRealVariable.CanModify)
					{
						internalRealVariable.Value = -32767.0;
					}
					else
					{
						internalRealVariable = productBD.get_BackDoorVariable((object)":LiqVolFlow.502.0").Variable as InternalRealVariable;
						if (internalRealVariable.CanModify)
						{
							internalRealVariable.Value = -32767.0;
						}
					}
				}
			}
			internalRealVariable2 = productBD.get_BackDoorVariable((object)":MoleFraction.530.0.0").Variable as InternalRealVariable;
			if (internalRealVariable2.Value != -32767.0)
			{
				internalRealVariable2.Value = -32767.0;
				productBD.SendBackDoorMessage("EraseComp");
				productBD.SendBackDoorMessage("AcceptComp");
			}
		}
		catch (Exception)
		{
			DebugPrint.OutputDebugString(DebugLevel.BuildHYSYSCase, WarningLevel.Error, string.Format(P2Globalization.GetString("CleanCompSplitterProductInfoFailed"), _data.UID));
		}
		finally
		{
			CleanResource.ReleaseComObjectHelper(internalRealVariable, bFinalRelease: false);
			CleanResource.ReleaseComObjectHelper(internalRealVariable2, bFinalRelease: false);
		}
	}
}
internal class HYSYSConversionReactorConverter : HYSYSObjectConverterBase, IDisposable
{
	private ConversionReactor _ConversionReactor;

	private HYSYSConversionReactor _Data;

	private SimulationCase _SimulationCase;

	public void Dispose()
	{
	}

	public HYSYSConversionReactorConverter(SimulationCase simulationCase, HYSYSConversionReactor data)
		: base(simulationCase)
	{
		_Data = data;
		_SimulationCase = simulationCase;
		try
		{
			_ConversionReactor = _Flowsheet.get_Operations((object)"ConversionReactorOp").Add(data.UID, "ConversionReactorOp") as ConversionReactor;
			CreateConversionReactor();
		}
		catch (Exception)
		{
		}
	}

	private void CreateConversionReactor()
	{
		BackDoor backDoor = null;
		ProcessStream processStream = null;
		ProcessStream processStream2 = null;
		ProcessStream[] array = null;
		ProcessStream processStream3 = null;
		BackDoor backDoor2 = null;
