		InternalRealVariable internalRealVariable = null;
		BackDoor backDoor3 = null;
		BackDoor backDoor4 = null;
		try
		{
			backDoor = _ConversionReactor as BackDoor;
			try
			{
				if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(_Data.FluidPackage))
				{
					string methodName = HYSYSFluidPackages.GetMethodName(_Data.FluidPackage);
					if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(methodName))
					{
						backDoor4 = _SimCase.BasisManager.FluidPackages[methodName] as BackDoor;
						backDoor.SendAttachObject(":CompList.300", backDoor4);
					}
				}
			}
			catch (Exception)
			{
				DebugPrint.OutputDebugString(DebugLevel.BuildHYSYSCase, WarningLevel.Error, string.Format(P2Globalization.GetString("ConvRConverter_SetFPFailed"), _ConversionReactor.name));
			}
			array = new ProcessStream[_Data.InletStreams.Count];
			try
			{
				for (int i = 0; i < _Data.InletStreams.Count; i++)
				{
					array[i] = GetProcessStream(_Data.InletStreams[i].Trim());
					backDoor3 = array[i] as BackDoor;
					backDoor.SendAttachObject(":MaterialStream.502." + i, backDoor3);
				}
			}
			catch (Exception)
			{
				DebugPrint.OutputDebugString(DebugLevel.BuildHYSYSCase, WarningLevel.Error, string.Format(P2Globalization.GetString("ConvRConverter_SetFeedStreamsFailed"), _ConversionReactor.name));
			}
			try
			{
				processStream = GetProcessStream(_Data.V.Trim());
				backDoor3 = processStream as BackDoor;
				backDoor.SendAttachObject(":MaterialStream.501", backDoor3);
			}
			catch (Exception)
			{
				DebugPrint.OutputDebugString(DebugLevel.BuildHYSYSCase, WarningLevel.Error, string.Format(P2Globalization.GetString("ConvRConverter_SetVFailed"), _ConversionReactor.name));
			}
			try
			{
				processStream2 = GetProcessStream(_Data.L.Trim());
				backDoor3 = processStream2 as BackDoor;
				backDoor.SendAttachObject(":MaterialStream.500", backDoor3);
			}
			catch (Exception)
			{
				DebugPrint.OutputDebugString(DebugLevel.BuildHYSYSCase, WarningLevel.Error, string.Format(P2Globalization.GetString("ConvRConverter_SetLFailed"), _ConversionReactor.name));
			}
			try
			{
				string name = NameHelper.CreateEnergyStreamName();
				processStream3 = _Flowsheet.EnergyStreams.Add(name, "") as ProcessStream;
				backDoor2 = processStream3 as BackDoor;
				backDoor.SendAttachObject(":EnergyStream.503", backDoor2);
			}
			catch (Exception)
			{
				DebugPrint.OutputDebugString(DebugLevel.BuildHYSYSCase, WarningLevel.Error, string.Format(P2Globalization.GetString("ConvRConverter_SetEnergyStreamFailed"), _ConversionReactor.name));
			}
			try
			{
				if (_Data.AttachedDTFEED != null && array[0].Temperature != null)
				{
					if (array[0].Temperature.Value == -32767.0)
					{
						DebugPrint.OutputDebugString(DebugLevel.BuildHYSYSCase, WarningLevel.Error, string.Format(P2Globalization.GetString("DTFEEDNotSet"), _ConversionReactor.name));
					}
					else
					{
						DoubleValue doubleValue = new DoubleValue();
						doubleValue.Value = array[0].Temperature.Value + HYSYSUOMMap.GetConvertedValue(UnitClassEnum.DropTemperature, _Data.AttachedDTFEED, "C").Value;
						doubleValue.Unit = "C";
						processStream.Temperature.SetValue(doubleValue.Value, doubleValue.Unit);
						processStream2.Temperature.SetValue(doubleValue.Value, doubleValue.Unit);
					}
				}
			}
			catch (Exception)
			{
				DebugPrint.OutputDebugString(DebugLevel.BuildHYSYSCase, WarningLevel.Error, string.Format(P2Globalization.GetString("ConvRConverter_SetDTFEEDFailed"), _ConversionReactor.name));
			}
			try
			{
				if (_Data.AttachedTemperature != null)
				{
					processStream.Temperature.SetValue(_Data.AttachedTemperature.Value, _Data.AttachedTemperature.Unit);
					processStream2.Temperature.SetValue(_Data.AttachedTemperature.Value, _Data.AttachedTemperature.Unit);
				}
			}
			catch (Exception)
			{
				DebugPrint.OutputDebugString(DebugLevel.BuildHYSYSCase, WarningLevel.Error, string.Format(P2Globalization.GetString("ConvRConverter_SetTemperatureFailed"), _ConversionReactor.name));
			}
			try
			{
				if (_Data.AttachedPressure != null)
				{
					processStream.Pressure.SetValue(_Data.AttachedPressure.Value, _Data.AttachedPressure.Unit);
					processStream2.Pressure.SetValue(_Data.AttachedPressure.Value, _Data.AttachedPressure.Unit);
					internalRealVariable = backDoor.get_BackDoorVariable((object)":PressureDrop.500").Variable as InternalRealVariable;
					internalRealVariable.SetValue(-32767.0, "");
				}
			}
			catch (Exception)
			{
				DebugPrint.OutputDebugString(DebugLevel.BuildHYSYSCase, WarningLevel.Error, string.Format(P2Globalization.GetString("ConvRConverter_SetPressureFailed"), _ConversionReactor.name));
			}
			try
			{
				if (_Data.DP != null)
				{
					internalRealVariable = backDoor.get_BackDoorVariable((object)":PressureDrop.500").Variable as InternalRealVariable;
					internalRealVariable.SetValue(_Data.DP.Value, _Data.DP.Unit);
				}
			}
			catch (Exception)
			{
				DebugPrint.OutputDebugString(DebugLevel.BuildHYSYSCase, WarningLevel.Error, string.Format(P2Globalization.GetString("ConvRConverter_SetDPFailed"), _ConversionReactor.name));
			}
			try
			{
				if (_Data.Duty != null)
				{
					internalRealVariable = backDoor2.get_BackDoorVariable((object)":HeatFlow.501.0").Variable as InternalRealVariable;
					internalRealVariable.SetValue(_Data.Duty.Value, _Data.Duty.Unit);
				}
			}
			catch (Exception)
			{
				DebugPrint.OutputDebugString(DebugLevel.BuildHYSYSCase, WarningLevel.Error, string.Format(P2Globalization.GetString("ConvRConverter_SetDutyFailed"), _ConversionReactor.name));
			}
			try
			{
				if (_Data.ReactionSet != null)
				{
					backDoor.SendAttachObject2(":RxnSet.400", "SimCase.200/FluidPkgMgr.300/RxnPackageManager.300/RxnSet.300(" + _Data.ReactionSet.ID + ")");
				}
			}
			catch (Exception)
			{
				DebugPrint.OutputDebugString(DebugLevel.BuildHYSYSCase, WarningLevel.Error, string.Format(P2Globalization.GetString("ConvRConverter_SetRXSetFailed"), _ConversionReactor.name));
			}
			try
			{
				InternalTextVariable internalTextVariable = backDoor.get_BackDoorTextVariable((object)":Notes.400").Variable as InternalTextVariable;
				foreach (string convertNote in _Data.ConvertNotes)
				{
					internalTextVariable.Value = internalTextVariable.Value + convertNote + "\n";
				}
			}
			catch (Exception)
			{
				DebugPrint.OutputDebugString(DebugLevel.BuildHYSYSCase, WarningLevel.Error, string.Format(P2Globalization.GetString("ConvRConverter_SetNotesFailed"), _ConversionReactor.name));
			}
		}
		catch (COMException)
		{
		}
		finally
		{
			CleanResource.ReleaseComObjectHelper(backDoor, bFinalRelease: false);
			CleanResource.ReleaseComObjectHelper(processStream, bFinalRelease: false);
			CleanResource.ReleaseComObjectHelper(processStream2, bFinalRelease: false);
			for (int j = 0; j < array.Length; j++)
			{
				CleanResource.ReleaseComObjectHelper(array[j], bFinalRelease: false);
			}
			CleanResource.ReleaseComObjectHelper(processStream3, bFinalRelease: false);
			CleanResource.ReleaseComObjectHelper(backDoor2, bFinalRelease: false);
			CleanResource.ReleaseComObjectHelper(backDoor3, bFinalRelease: false);
			CleanResource.ReleaseComObjectHelper(internalRealVariable, bFinalRelease: false);
			CleanResource.ReleaseComObjectHelper(backDoor4, bFinalRelease: false);
		}
	}
}
public class HYSYSCSTReactorConverter : HYSYSObjectConverterBase, IDisposable
{
	private KineticReactor _CSTReactor;

	private HYSYSCSTReactor _Data;

	private BackDoor _BdCSTReactor;

	private InternalRealVariable _InterReal;

	public HYSYSCSTReactorConverter(SimulationCase simulationCase, HYSYSCSTReactor data)
		: base(simulationCase)
	{
		_Data = data;
		try
		{
			_CSTReactor = _Flowsheet.get_Operations((object)"KineticReactorOp").Add(data.UID, "KineticReactorOp") as KineticReactor;
			_BdCSTReactor = _CSTReactor as BackDoor;
			(_BdCSTReactor.get_BackDoorVariable((object)":Name.0").Variable as InternalTextVariable).Value = data.UID;
			CreateCSTReactor();
		}
		catch (Exception)
		{
			DebugPrint.OutputDebugString(DebugLevel.BuildHYSYSCase, WarningLevel.Error, string.Format(P2Globalization.GetString("UOConverter_Failed"), "CSTReactor", _Data.UID));
		}
	}

	private void CreateCSTReactor()
	{
		if (_Data == null)
		{
			return;
		}
		if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(_Data.FluidPackage))
		{
			string methodName = HYSYSFluidPackages.GetMethodName(_Data.FluidPackage);
			if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(methodName) && ((string[])_SimCase.BasisManager.FluidPackages.Names).Contains(methodName))
			{
				BackDoor backDoor = (_SimCase.BasisManager.FluidPackages[methodName] as FluidPackage) as BackDoor;
				_BdCSTReactor.SendAttachObject(":CompList.300", backDoor);
			}
		}
		SetStreams();
		SetSize();
		if (_Data.ReactionSet != null)
		{
			_BdCSTReactor.SendAttachObject2(":RxnSet.400", "SimCase.200/FluidPkgMgr.300/RxnPackageManager.300/RxnSet.300(" + _Data.ReactionSet.ID + ")");
		}
		if (_Data.IsSinglePhase)
		{
			_InterReal = _BdCSTReactor.get_BackDoorVariable((object)":Boolean.600").Variable as InternalRealVariable;
			_InterReal.Value = 1.0;
		}
		if (_Data.DeltaPresure != null && (_Data.ProductPressure == null || _Data.ProductPressure.Value == 0.0))
		{
			_InterReal = _BdCSTReactor.get_BackDoorVariable((object)":PressureDrop.500").Variable as InternalRealVariable;
			_InterReal.SetValue(_Data.DeltaPresure.Value, _Data.DeltaPresure.Unit);
		}
		InternalTextVariable internalTextVariable = _BdCSTReactor.get_BackDoorTextVariable((object)":Notes.400").Variable as InternalTextVariable;
		foreach (string convertNote in _Data.ConvertNotes)
		{
			internalTextVariable.Value = internalTextVariable.Value + convertNote + "\n";
		}
	}

	private void SetStreams()
	{
		BackDoor backDoor = null;
		ProcessStream processStream = null;
		try
		{
			for (int i = 0; i < _Data.InletStreams.Count; i++)
			{
				processStream = GetProcessStream(_Data.InletStreams[i].Trim());
				backDoor = processStream as BackDoor;
				_BdCSTReactor.SendAttachObject(":MaterialStream.502." + i, backDoor);
			}
			if (_Data.VapourOutlet != null)
			{
				processStream = GetProcessStream(_Data.VapourOutlet.Trim());
				backDoor = processStream as BackDoor;
				_BdCSTReactor.SendAttachObject(":MaterialStream.501", backDoor);
			}
			if (_Data.LquidOutlet != null)
			{
				processStream = GetProcessStream(_Data.LquidOutlet.Trim());
				backDoor = processStream as BackDoor;
				_BdCSTReactor.SendAttachObject(":MaterialStream.500", backDoor);
			}
			if (_Data.ProductPressure != null)
			{
				_InterReal = _BdCSTReactor.get_BackDoorVariable((object)":PressureDrop.500").Variable as InternalRealVariable;
				_InterReal.Value = -32767.0;
				processStream.Pressure.SetValue(_Data.ProductPressure.Value, _Data.ProductPressure.Unit);
			}
			if (_Data.OutletStreamTemperature != null)
			{
				processStream.Temperature.SetValue(_Data.OutletStreamTemperature.Value, _Data.OutletStreamTemperature.Unit);
			}
			processStream = GetEnergyStream(_Data.EnergyName.Trim());
			backDoor = processStream as BackDoor;
			_BdCSTReactor.SendAttachObject(":EnergyStream.503", backDoor);
			if (_Data.Duty != null)
			{
				_InterReal = backDoor.get_BackDoorVariable((object)":HeatFlow.501.0").Variable as InternalRealVariable;
				_InterReal.SetValue(_Data.Duty.Value, _Data.Duty.Unit);
			}
		}
		catch (COMException)
		{
			DebugPrint.OutputDebugString(DebugLevel.BuildHYSYSCase, WarningLevel.Error, string.Format(P2Globalization.GetString("SetCSTRPropFailed"), "Streams", _Data.UID));
		}
	}

	private void SetSize()
	{
		try
		{
			if (_Data.Volumn != null)
			{
				_InterReal = _BdCSTReactor.get_BackDoorVariable((object)":Volume.500").Variable as InternalRealVariable;
				_InterReal.SetValue(_Data.Volumn.Value, _Data.Volumn.Unit);
			}
			if (_Data.LiquidVolumnPercent1 >= 0.0)
			{
				_InterReal = _BdCSTReactor.get_BackDoorVariable((object)":Percent.501").Variable as InternalRealVariable;
				_InterReal.Value = _Data.LiquidVolumnPercent1 * 100.0;
			}
		}
		catch (COMException)
		{
			DebugPrint.OutputDebugString(DebugLevel.BuildHYSYSCase, WarningLevel.Error, string.Format(P2Globalization.GetString("SetCSTRPropFailed"), "Size", _Data.UID));
		}
	}

	public void Dispose()
	{
	}
}
internal class HYSYSCutterConverter : HYSYSObjectConverterBase, IDisposable
{
	private HYSYSCutter _Data;

	private StreamCutterOp _Cutter;

	private BackDoor _BdCutter;

	public void Dispose()
	{
	}

	public HYSYSCutterConverter(SimulationCase simulationCase, HYSYSCutter data)
		: base(simulationCase)
	{
		_Data = data;
		try
		{
			_Cutter = _Flowsheet.get_Operations((object)"StreamCutterOp").Add(data.UID, "StreamCutterOp") as StreamCutterOp;
			if (_Cutter != null)
			{
				_BdCutter = _Cutter as BackDoor;
				CreateCutter();
			}
		}
		catch (Exception)
		{
		}
	}

	private void CreateCutter()
	{
		if (_Data.InletStreams.Count > 0 && !string.IsNullOrEmpty(_Data.InletStreams[0]))
		{
			BackDoor backDoor = GetProcessStream(_Data.InletStreams[0]) as BackDoor;
			_BdCutter.SendAttachObject(":Stream.500", backDoor);
		}
		if (_Data.OutletStreams.Count > 0 && !string.IsNullOrEmpty(_Data.OutletStreams[0]))
		{
			ProcessStream processStream = GetProcessStream(_Data.OutletStreams[0]);
			BackDoor backDoor2 = processStream as BackDoor;
			_BdCutter.SendAttachObject(":Stream.501", backDoor2);
			if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(_Data.FluidPackage))
			{
				string methodName = HYSYSFluidPackages.GetMethodName(_Data.FluidPackage);
				if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(methodName))
				{
					processStream.FluidPackage.name = (_SimCase.BasisManager.FluidPackages[methodName] as FluidPackage).name;
				}
			}
		}
		InternalRealVariable internalRealVariable = _BdCutter.get_BackDoorVariable((object)"Transition.500(FluidPkgTransitionObject):Enum.300").Variable as InternalRealVariable;
		if (_Data.TransferBasis == TransferBasisEnum.TP)
		{
			internalRealVariable.Value = 0.0;
		}
		else if (_Data.TransferBasis == TransferBasisEnum.PH)
		{
			internalRealVariable.Value = 5.0;
		}
		else if (_Data.TransferBasis == TransferBasisEnum.VFP)
		{
			internalRealVariable.Value = 2.0;
		}
		else if (_Data.TransferBasis == TransferBasisEnum.DEWP)
		{
			internalRealVariable.Value = 3.0;
			if (_Data.OutletStreams.Count > 0 && !string.IsNullOrEmpty(_Data.OutletStreams[0]))
			{
				GetProcessStream(_Data.OutletStreams[0]).VapourFraction.Value = 1.0;
			}
			AddSetToSetPressure();
		}
		else if (_Data.TransferBasis == TransferBasisEnum.BUBBLEP)
		{
			internalRealVariable.Value = 3.0;
			if (_Data.OutletStreams.Count > 0 && !string.IsNullOrEmpty(_Data.OutletStreams[0]))
			{
				GetProcessStream(_Data.OutletStreams[0]).VapourFraction.Value = 0.0;
			}
			AddSetToSetPressure();
		}
		InternalTextVariable internalTextVariable = _BdCutter.get_BackDoorTextVariable((object)":Notes.400").Variable as InternalTextVariable;
		foreach (string convertNote in _Data.ConvertNotes)
		{
			internalTextVariable.Value = internalTextVariable.Value + convertNote + "\n";
		}
	}

	private void AddSetToSetPressure()
	{
		if (_Data.OutletStreams.Count > 0 && !string.IsNullOrEmpty(_Data.OutletStreams[0]))
		{
			ProcessStream processStream = GetProcessStream(_Data.OutletStreams[0]);
			if (_Data.InletStreams.Count > 0 && !string.IsNullOrEmpty(_Data.InletStreams[0]))
			{
				ProcessStream processStream2 = GetProcessStream(_Data.InletStreams[0]);
				string name = NameHelper.CreateName(bIsStream: false, "_Set_Ref");
				SetOp obj = _Flowsheet.get_Operations((object)"SetOpObject").Add(name, "SetOpObject") as SetOp;
				obj.TargetVariable = processStream.Pressure;
				(obj as BackDoor).SendAttachObject(":FlowSheetObject.501", processStream2);
			}
		}
	}
}
internal class HYSYSEquilibriumReactorConverter : HYSYSObjectConverterBase, IDisposable
{
	private EquilibriumReactor _EquilibriumReactor;

	private HYSYSEquilibriumReactor _Data;

	public void Dispose()
	{
	}

	public HYSYSEquilibriumReactorConverter(SimulationCase simulationCase, HYSYSEquilibriumReactor data)
		: base(simulationCase)
	{
		_Data = data;
		try
		{
			_EquilibriumReactor = _Flowsheet.get_Operations((object)"EquilibriumReactorOp").Add(data.UID, "EquilibriumReactorOp") as EquilibriumReactor;
			CreateEquilibriumReactor();
		}
		catch (Exception)
		{
		}
	}

	private void CreateEquilibriumReactor()
	{
		BackDoor backDoor = null;
		ProcessStream processStream = null;
		ProcessStream processStream2 = null;
		ProcessStream[] array = null;
		ProcessStream processStream3 = null;
		BackDoor backDoor2 = null;
		InternalRealVariable internalRealVariable = null;
		BackDoor backDoor3 = null;
		BackDoor backDoor4 = null;
		try
		{
			backDoor = _EquilibriumReactor as BackDoor;
			try
			{
				if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(_Data.FluidPackage))
				{
					string methodName = HYSYSFluidPackages.GetMethodName(_Data.FluidPackage);
					if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(methodName))
					{
						backDoor4 = _SimCase.BasisManager.FluidPackages[methodName] as BackDoor;
						backDoor.SendAttachObject(":CompList.300", backDoor4);
					}
				}
			}
			catch (Exception)
			{
				DebugPrint.OutputDebugString(DebugLevel.BuildHYSYSCase, WarningLevel.Error, string.Format(P2Globalization.GetString("EquilRConverter_SetFPFailed"), _EquilibriumReactor.name));
			}
			array = new ProcessStream[_Data.InletStreams.Count];
			try
			{
				for (int i = 0; i < _Data.InletStreams.Count; i++)
				{
					array[i] = GetProcessStream(_Data.InletStreams[i].Trim());
					backDoor3 = array[i] as BackDoor;
					backDoor.SendAttachObject(":MaterialStream.502." + i, backDoor3);
				}
			}
			catch (Exception)
			{
				DebugPrint.OutputDebugString(DebugLevel.BuildHYSYSCase, WarningLevel.Error, string.Format(P2Globalization.GetString("EquilRConverter_SetFeedStreamsFailed"), _EquilibriumReactor.name));
			}
			try
			{
				processStream = GetProcessStream(_Data.V.Trim());
				backDoor3 = processStream as BackDoor;
				backDoor.SendAttachObject(":MaterialStream.501", backDoor3);
			}
			catch (Exception)
			{
				DebugPrint.OutputDebugString(DebugLevel.BuildHYSYSCase, WarningLevel.Error, string.Format(P2Globalization.GetString("EquilRConverter_SetVFailed"), _EquilibriumReactor.name));
			}
			try
			{
				processStream2 = GetProcessStream(_Data.L.Trim());
				backDoor3 = processStream2 as BackDoor;
				backDoor.SendAttachObject(":MaterialStream.500", backDoor3);
			}
			catch (Exception)
			{
				DebugPrint.OutputDebugString(DebugLevel.BuildHYSYSCase, WarningLevel.Error, string.Format(P2Globalization.GetString("EquilRConverter_SetLFailed"), _EquilibriumReactor.name));
			}
			try
			{
				string name = NameHelper.CreateEnergyStreamName();
				processStream3 = _Flowsheet.EnergyStreams.Add(name, "") as ProcessStream;
				backDoor2 = processStream3 as BackDoor;
				backDoor.SendAttachObject(":EnergyStream.503", backDoor2);
			}
			catch (Exception)
			{
				DebugPrint.OutputDebugString(DebugLevel.BuildHYSYSCase, WarningLevel.Error, string.Format(P2Globalization.GetString("EquilRConverter_SetEnergyStreamFailed"), _EquilibriumReactor.name));
			}
			try
			{
				if (_Data.AttachedDTFEED != null)
				{
					if (array[0].Temperature.Value == -32767.0)
					{
						DebugPrint.OutputDebugString(DebugLevel.BuildHYSYSCase, WarningLevel.Error, string.Format(P2Globalization.GetString("DTFEEDNotSet"), _EquilibriumReactor.name));
					}
					else
					{
						DoubleValue doubleValue = new DoubleValue();
						doubleValue.Value = array[0].Temperature.Value + HYSYSUOMMap.GetConvertedValue(UnitClassEnum.DropTemperature, _Data.AttachedDTFEED, "C").Value;
						doubleValue.Unit = "C";
						processStream.Temperature.SetValue(doubleValue.Value, doubleValue.Unit);
						processStream2.Temperature.SetValue(doubleValue.Value, doubleValue.Unit);
					}
				}
			}
			catch (Exception)
			{
				DebugPrint.OutputDebugString(DebugLevel.BuildHYSYSCase, WarningLevel.Error, string.Format(P2Globalization.GetString("EquilRConverter_SetDTFEEDFailed"), _EquilibriumReactor.name));
			}
			try
			{
				if (_Data.AttachedTemperature != null)
				{
					processStream.Temperature.SetValue(_Data.AttachedTemperature.Value, _Data.AttachedTemperature.Unit);
					processStream2.Temperature.SetValue(_Data.AttachedTemperature.Value, _Data.AttachedTemperature.Unit);
				}
			}
			catch (Exception)
			{
				DebugPrint.OutputDebugString(DebugLevel.BuildHYSYSCase, WarningLevel.Error, string.Format(P2Globalization.GetString("EquilRConverter_SetTemperatureFailed"), _EquilibriumReactor.name));
			}
			try
			{
				if (_Data.AttachedPressure != null)
				{
					processStream.Pressure.SetValue(_Data.AttachedPressure.Value, _Data.AttachedPressure.Unit);
					processStream2.Pressure.SetValue(_Data.AttachedPressure.Value, _Data.AttachedPressure.Unit);
					internalRealVariable = backDoor.get_BackDoorVariable((object)":PressureDrop.500").Variable as InternalRealVariable;
					internalRealVariable.SetValue(-32767.0, "");
				}
			}
			catch (Exception)
			{
				DebugPrint.OutputDebugString(DebugLevel.BuildHYSYSCase, WarningLevel.Error, string.Format(P2Globalization.GetString("EquilRConverter_SetPressureFailed"), _EquilibriumReactor.name));
			}
			try
			{
				if (_Data.DP != null)
				{
					internalRealVariable = backDoor.get_BackDoorVariable((object)":PressureDrop.500").Variable as InternalRealVariable;
					internalRealVariable.SetValue(_Data.DP.Value, _Data.DP.Unit);
				}
			}
			catch (Exception)
			{
				DebugPrint.OutputDebugString(DebugLevel.BuildHYSYSCase, WarningLevel.Error, string.Format(P2Globalization.GetString("EquilRConverter_SetDPFailed"), _EquilibriumReactor.name));
			}
			try
			{
				if (_Data.Duty != null)
				{
					internalRealVariable = backDoor2.get_BackDoorVariable((object)":HeatFlow.501.0").Variable as InternalRealVariable;
					internalRealVariable.SetValue(_Data.Duty.Value, _Data.Duty.Unit);
				}
			}
			catch (Exception)
			{
				DebugPrint.OutputDebugString(DebugLevel.BuildHYSYSCase, WarningLevel.Error, string.Format(P2Globalization.GetString("EquilRConverter_SetDutyFailed"), _EquilibriumReactor.name));
			}
			try
			{
				if (_Data.ReactionSet != null)
				{
					backDoor.SendAttachObject2(":RxnSet.400", "SimCase.200/FluidPkgMgr.300/RxnPackageManager.300/RxnSet.300(" + _Data.ReactionSet.ID + ")");
				}
			}
			catch (Exception)
			{
				DebugPrint.OutputDebugString(DebugLevel.BuildHYSYSCase, WarningLevel.Error, string.Format(P2Globalization.GetString("EquilRConverter_SetRXSetFailed"), _EquilibriumReactor.name));
			}
			try
			{
				InternalTextVariable internalTextVariable = backDoor.get_BackDoorTextVariable((object)":Notes.400").Variable as InternalTextVariable;
				foreach (string convertNote in _Data.ConvertNotes)
				{
					internalTextVariable.Value = internalTextVariable.Value + convertNote + "\n";
				}
			}
			catch (Exception)
			{
				DebugPrint.OutputDebugString(DebugLevel.BuildHYSYSCase, WarningLevel.Error, string.Format(P2Globalization.GetString("EquilRConverter_SetNotesFailed"), _EquilibriumReactor.name));
			}
		}
		catch (COMException)
		{
		}
		finally
		{
			CleanResource.ReleaseComObjectHelper(backDoor, bFinalRelease: false);
			CleanResource.ReleaseComObjectHelper(processStream, bFinalRelease: false);
			CleanResource.ReleaseComObjectHelper(processStream2, bFinalRelease: false);
			for (int j = 0; j < array.Length; j++)
			{
				CleanResource.ReleaseComObjectHelper(array[j], bFinalRelease: false);
			}
			CleanResource.ReleaseComObjectHelper(processStream3, bFinalRelease: false);
			CleanResource.ReleaseComObjectHelper(backDoor2, bFinalRelease: false);
			CleanResource.ReleaseComObjectHelper(backDoor3, bFinalRelease: false);
			CleanResource.ReleaseComObjectHelper(internalRealVariable, bFinalRelease: false);
			CleanResource.ReleaseComObjectHelper(backDoor4, bFinalRelease: false);
		}
	}
}
internal class HYSYSExpanderConverter : HYSYSObjectConverterBase, IDisposable
{
	private ExpandOp _Expander;

	private HYSYSExpander _Data;

	public void Dispose()
	{
	}

	public HYSYSExpanderConverter(SimulationCase simulationCase, HYSYSExpander data)
		: base(simulationCase)
	{
		_Data = data;
		try
		{
			_Expander = _Flowsheet.get_Operations((object)"ExpandOp").Add(data.UID, "ExpandOp") as ExpandOp;
			CreateExpander();
		}
		catch (Exception)
		{
		}
	}

	private void CreateExpander()
	{
		BackDoor backDoor = null;
		ProcessStream processStream = null;
		ProcessStream processStream2 = null;
		ProcessStream processStream3 = null;
		BackDoor backDoor2 = null;
		InternalRealVariable internalRealVariable = null;
		InternalRealVariable internalRealVariable2 = null;
		BackDoor backDoor3 = null;
		BackDoor backDoor4 = null;
		try
		{
			backDoor = _Expander as BackDoor;
			if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(_Data.FluidPackage))
			{
				try
				{
					string methodName = HYSYSFluidPackages.GetMethodName(_Data.FluidPackage);
					if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(methodName))
					{
						backDoor4 = _SimCase.BasisManager.FluidPackages[methodName] as BackDoor;
						backDoor.SendAttachObject(":CompList.300", backDoor4);
					}
				}
				catch (Exception)
				{
					DebugPrint.OutputDebugString(DebugLevel.BuildHYSYSCase, WarningLevel.Error, string.Format(P2Globalization.GetString("ExpanderConverter_SetFluidPackageFailed"), _Expander.name));
				}
			}
			processStream = GetProcessStream(_Data.OutletStreams[0].Trim());
			processStream2 = GetProcessStream(_Data.InletStreams[0].Trim());
			try
			{
				string name = NameHelper.CreateEnergyStreamName();
				processStream3 = _Flowsheet.EnergyStreams.Add(name, "") as ProcessStream;
				backDoor2 = processStream3 as BackDoor;
				backDoor.SendAttachObject(":EnergyStream.502", backDoor2);
			}
			catch (Exception)
			{
				DebugPrint.OutputDebugString(DebugLevel.BuildHYSYSCase, WarningLevel.Error, string.Format(P2Globalization.GetString("ExpanderConverter_SetEnergyStreamFailed"), _Expander.name));
			}
			if (_Data.AdiabaticEfficiency != 0.0)
			{
				try
				{
					internalRealVariable = backDoor.get_BackDoorVariable((object)":Index.500").Variable as InternalRealVariable;
					internalRealVariable.Value = _Data.AdiabaticEfficiency;
				}
				catch (Exception)
				{
					DebugPrint.OutputDebugString(DebugLevel.BuildHYSYSCase, WarningLevel.Error, string.Format(P2Globalization.GetString("ExpanderConverter_SetADIAEffFailed"), _Expander.name));
				}
			}
			if (_Data.OutletStreams != null)
			{
				try
				{
					backDoor3 = processStream as BackDoor;
					backDoor.SendAttachObject(":MaterialStream.501.0", backDoor3);
				}
				catch (Exception)
				{
					DebugPrint.OutputDebugString(DebugLevel.BuildHYSYSCase, WarningLevel.Error, string.Format(P2Globalization.GetString("ExpanderConverter_SetProductStreamFailed"), _Expander.name));
				}
			}
			if (_Data.InletStreams != null)
			{
				try
				{
					backDoor3 = processStream2 as BackDoor;
					backDoor.SendAttachObject(":MaterialStream.500.0", backDoor3);
				}
				catch (Exception)
				{
					DebugPrint.OutputDebugString(DebugLevel.BuildHYSYSCase, WarningLevel.Error, string.Format(P2Globalization.GetString("ExpanderConverter_SetFeedStreamFailed"), _Expander.name));
				}
			}
			if (_Data.AttachedPressure != null)
			{
				try
				{
					processStream.Pressure.SetValue(_Data.AttachedPressure.Value, _Data.AttachedPressure.Unit);
				}
				catch (Exception)
				{
					DebugPrint.OutputDebugString(DebugLevel.BuildHYSYSCase, WarningLevel.Error, string.Format(P2Globalization.GetString("ExpanderConverter_SetOutletPressureFailed"), _Expander.name));
				}
			}
			if (_Data.AttachedDP != null)
			{
				try
				{
					internalRealVariable2 = backDoor.get_BackDoorVariable((object)":PressureDrop.500").Variable as InternalRealVariable;
					internalRealVariable2.SetValue(_Data.AttachedDP.Value, _Data.AttachedDP.Unit);
				}
				catch (Exception)
				{
					DebugPrint.OutputDebugString(DebugLevel.BuildHYSYSCase, WarningLevel.Error, string.Format(P2Globalization.GetString("ExpanderConverter_SetDPFailed"), _Expander.name));
				}
			}
			if (_Data.AttachedPRatio != 0.0)
			{
				try
				{
					internalRealVariable2 = backDoor.get_BackDoorVariable((object)":Ratio.501").Variable as InternalRealVariable;
					internalRealVariable2.Value = _Data.AttachedPRatio;
				}
				catch (Exception)
				{
					DebugPrint.OutputDebugString(DebugLevel.BuildHYSYSCase, WarningLevel.Error, string.Format(P2Globalization.GetString("ExpanderConverter_SetPRatioFailed"), _Expander.name));
				}
			}
			if (_Data.Duty != null)
			{
				try
				{
					(backDoor2.get_BackDoorVariable((object)":Power.501").Variable as InternalRealVariable).SetValue(_Data.Duty.Value, _Data.Duty.Unit);
				}
				catch (Exception)
				{
					DebugPrint.OutputDebugString(DebugLevel.BuildHYSYSCase, WarningLevel.Error, string.Format(P2Globalization.GetString("ExpanderConverter_SetDutyFailed"), _Expander.name));
				}
			}
			try
			{
				InternalTextVariable internalTextVariable = backDoor.get_BackDoorTextVariable((object)":Notes.400").Variable as InternalTextVariable;
				foreach (string convertNote in _Data.ConvertNotes)
				{
					internalTextVariable.Value = internalTextVariable.Value + convertNote + "\n";
				}
			}
			catch (Exception)
			{
				DebugPrint.OutputDebugString(DebugLevel.BuildHYSYSCase, WarningLevel.Error, string.Format(P2Globalization.GetString("ExpanderConverter_SetConversionNotesFailed"), _Expander.name));
			}
		}
		catch (COMException)
		{
		}
		finally
		{
			CleanResource.ReleaseComObjectHelper(backDoor, bFinalRelease: false);
			CleanResource.ReleaseComObjectHelper(processStream, bFinalRelease: false);
			CleanResource.ReleaseComObjectHelper(processStream2, bFinalRelease: false);
			CleanResource.ReleaseComObjectHelper(processStream3, bFinalRelease: false);
			CleanResource.ReleaseComObjectHelper(backDoor2, bFinalRelease: false);
			CleanResource.ReleaseComObjectHelper(backDoor3, bFinalRelease: false);
			CleanResource.ReleaseComObjectHelper(internalRealVariable, bFinalRelease: false);
			CleanResource.ReleaseComObjectHelper(internalRealVariable2, bFinalRelease: false);
			CleanResource.ReleaseComObjectHelper(backDoor4, bFinalRelease: false);
		}
	}
}
public class HYSYSFiredHeaterConverter : HYSYSObjectConverterBase
{
	private FiredHeater _firedHeater;

	private HYSYSFiredHeater _data;

	public HYSYSFiredHeaterConverter(SimulationCase simulationCase, HYSYSFiredHeater data)
		: base(simulationCase)
	{
		try
		{
			_data = data;
			_firedHeater = _Flowsheet.get_Operations((object)"DynamicFiredHeaterOpObject").Add(data.UID, "DynamicFiredHeaterOpObject") as FiredHeater;
			CreateFiredHeater();
		}
		catch (Exception)
		{
		}
	}

	private void CreateFiredHeater()
	{
		BackDoor backDoor = null;
		BackDoor backDoor2 = null;
		ProcessStream processStream = null;
		ProcessStream processStream2 = null;
		ProcessStream processStream3 = null;
		ProcessStream processStream4 = null;
		ProcessStream processStream5 = null;
		BackDoor backDoor3 = null;
		bool flag = false;
		try
		{
			backDoor = _firedHeater as BackDoor;
			if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(_data.FluidPackage))
			{
				string methodName = HYSYSFluidPackages.GetMethodName(_data.FluidPackage);
				if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(methodName))
				{
					backDoor3 = (_SimCase.BasisManager.FluidPackages[methodName] as FluidPackage) as BackDoor;
					backDoor.SendAttachObject(":CompList.300", backDoor3);
				}
			}
			processStream3 = GetProcessStream(_data.COMBSIDEINLETSTREAMS[0].Trim());
			backDoor2 = processStream3 as BackDoor;
			backDoor.SendAttachObject(":MaterialStream.523.0", backDoor2);
			if (_data.TUBESIDETOUT != null)
			{
				flag = true;
				processStream3.MolarFlowValue = -32767.0;
				processStream3.MassFlowValue = -32767.0;
			}
			processStream4 = GetProcessStream(_data.COMBSIDEOUTLETSTREAMS[0].Trim());
			backDoor2 = processStream4 as BackDoor;
			backDoor.SendAttachObject(":MaterialStream.510", backDoor2);
			processStream = GetProcessStream(_data.TUBESIDEINLETSTREAMS[0].Trim());
			backDoor2 = processStream as BackDoor;
			backDoor.SendAttachObject(":MaterialStream.500.0", backDoor2);
			processStream2 = GetProcessStream(_data.TUBESIDEOUTLETSTREAMS[0].Trim());
			backDoor2 = processStream2 as BackDoor;
			backDoor.SendAttachObject(":MaterialStream.501.0", backDoor2);
			if (flag)
			{
				processStream2.Temperature.SetValue(_data.TUBESIDETOUT.Value, _data.TUBESIDETOUT.Unit);
			}
			processStream5 = GetProcessStream(_data.AIRSIDESTREAMS[0].Trim());
			backDoor2 = processStream5 as BackDoor;
			backDoor.SendAttachObject(":MaterialStream.507", backDoor2);
			processStream5.Temperature.SetValue(processStream3.Temperature.Value, "");
			processStream5.Pressure.SetValue(processStream3.Pressure.Value, "");
			_firedHeater.CombustionEfficiencyValue = 60.0;
			_firedHeater.ExcessAirPercentValue = 70.0;
			InternalTextVariable internalTextVariable = backDoor.get_BackDoorTextVariable((object)":Notes.400").Variable as InternalTextVariable;
			string text = P2Globalization.GetString("FiredHeater_Parameter_Info_1") + "\n" + P2Globalization.GetString("FiredHeater_Parameter_Info_2");
			internalTextVariable.Value = internalTextVariable.Value + text + "\n";
			foreach (string convertNote in _data.ConvertNotes)
			{
				internalTextVariable.Value = internalTextVariable.Value + convertNote + "\n";
			}
		}
		catch (Exception)
		{
			DebugPrint.OutputDebugString(DebugLevel.BuildHYSYSCase, WarningLevel.Error, string.Format(P2Globalization.GetString("UOConverter_Failed"), "HYSYS FiredHeater unit operation block", _data.UID));
		}
		finally
		{
			CleanResource.ReleaseComObjectHelper(backDoor, bFinalRelease: false);
			CleanResource.ReleaseComObjectHelper(backDoor2, bFinalRelease: false);
			CleanResource.ReleaseComObjectHelper(processStream, bFinalRelease: false);
			CleanResource.ReleaseComObjectHelper(processStream2, bFinalRelease: false);
			CleanResource.ReleaseComObjectHelper(processStream3, bFinalRelease: false);
			CleanResource.ReleaseComObjectHelper(processStream4, bFinalRelease: false);
			CleanResource.ReleaseComObjectHelper(processStream5, bFinalRelease: false);
		}
	}
}
internal class HYSYSFlowsheetParameterConverter : HYSYSObjectConverterBase
{
	private static SpreadsheetOp _GlobalDefine;

	private DEFINE _Def;

	private Specification _Spec;

	private HYSYSTree _HyTree;

	private string _MainUID;

	private HYSYSUnitOperationBase _Data;

	private static string _ColumnName = "A";

	private static int _RowNumber = 0;

	private string _MainType = string.Empty;

	private bool _AddSpecialVar;

	private static Dictionary<string, string> impCellCol = new Dictionary<string, string>();

	private static Dictionary<string, RealVariable> impVarColl = new Dictionary<string, RealVariable>();

	private bool _ImpVarExist;

	private bool _Import;

	private string _ImpKey = string.Empty;

	public HYSYSFlowsheetParameterConverter(SimulationCase simulationCase)
		: base(simulationCase)
	{
	}

	public HYSYSFlowsheetParameterConverter(SimulationCase simulationCase, HYSYSTree HYSYSTree, string UID, DEFINE def, int mode)
		: base(simulationCase)
	{
		_HyTree = HYSYSTree;
		_Def = def;
		_MainUID = UID;
		InitializeData(mode);
		if (_Data != null)
		{
			switch (mode)
			{
			case 0:
				_MainType = "MIXE";
				SetMixerParam();
				break;
			case 1:
				_MainType = "COLU";
				SetColumnParam();
				break;
			case 2:
				_MainType = "FLAS";
				SetFlashParam();
				break;
			case 3:
				_MainType = "PUMP";
				SetPumpParam();
				break;
			case 4:
				_MainType = "VALV";
				SetValveParam();
				break;
			case 5:
				_MainType = "SPLI";
				SetSplitterParam();
				break;
			case 6:
				_MainType = "COMP";
				SetCompressorParam();
				break;
			case 7:
				_MainType = "EXPA";
				SetExpanderParam();
				break;
			case 8:
				_MainType = "PIPE";
				SetPipeParam();
				break;
			case 9:
				_MainType = "HEAT";
				SetHXParam();
				break;
			case 10:
				_MainType = "COOL";
				SetHXParam();
				break;
			case 11:
				_MainType = "EXCH";
				SetHXParam();
				break;
			case 12:
				_MainType = "LNG";
				SetLNGHXParam();
				break;
			case 13:
				_MainType = "EQUR";
				SetEquilReactorParam();
				break;
			case 14:
				_MainType = "CONR";
				SetConvReactorParam();
				break;
			case 15:
				_MainType = "GIBB";
				SetGibbsReactorParam();
				break;
			case 16:
				_MainType = "PLUG";
				SetPFRReactorParam();
				break;
			case 17:
				_MainType = "CSTR";
				SetCSTRReactorParam();
				break;
			case 18:
				_MainType = "STCA";
				SetCSTRReactorParam();
				break;
			}
		}
	}

	public HYSYSFlowsheetParameterConverter(SimulationCase simulationCase, HYSYSTree HYSYSTree, string UID, Specification spec, int mode)
		: base(simulationCase)
	{
		_HyTree = HYSYSTree;
		_Spec = spec;
		_MainUID = UID;
	}

	public static void ResetSpreadsheet()
	{
		_GlobalDefine = null;
		_ColumnName = "A";
		_RowNumber = 0;
	}

	public static void EnableSpreadsheet()
	{
		if (_GlobalDefine != null)
		{
			RealVariable obj = (_GlobalDefine as BackDoor).get_BackDoorVariable((object)":Ignored.300").Variable as RealVariable;
			obj.Value = 1.0;
			obj.Value = 0.0;
		}
	}

	public static void ResetStaticMembers()
	{
		_GlobalDefine = null;
		_ColumnName = "A";
		_RowNumber = 0;
		impCellCol.Clear();
		impVarColl.Clear();
	}

	private void InitializeData(int mode)
	{
		switch (mode)
		{
		case 0:
		{
			foreach (HYSYSMixer hYSYSMixer in _HyTree._PFDObject.HYSYSMixers)
			{
				if (hYSYSMixer.UID == _MainUID)
				{
					_Data = hYSYSMixer;
					break;
				}
			}
			break;
		}
		case 1:
		{
			foreach (HYSYSColumn hYSYSColumn in _HyTree._PFDObject.HYSYSColumns)
			{
				if (hYSYSColumn.UID == _MainUID)
				{
					_Data = hYSYSColumn;
					break;
				}
			}
			break;
		}
		case 2:
		{
			foreach (HYSYSSeparator hysysSeparator in _HyTree._PFDObject.HysysSeparators)
			{
				if (hysysSeparator.UID == _MainUID)
				{
					_Data = hysysSeparator;
					break;
				}
			}
			break;
		}
		case 3:
		{
			foreach (HYSYSPump hYSYSPump in _HyTree._PFDObject.HYSYSPumps)
			{
				if (hYSYSPump.UID == _MainUID)
				{
					_Data = hYSYSPump;
					break;
				}
			}
			break;
		}
		case 4:
		{
			foreach (HYSYSValve hYSYSValf in _HyTree._PFDObject.HYSYSValves)
			{
				if (hYSYSValf.UID == _MainUID)
				{
					_Data = hYSYSValf;
					break;
				}
			}
			break;
		}
		case 5:
		{
			foreach (HYSYSSplitter hYSYSSplitter in _HyTree._PFDObject.HYSYSSplitters)
			{
				if (hYSYSSplitter.UID == _MainUID)
				{
					_Data = hYSYSSplitter;
					break;
				}
			}
			break;
		}
		case 6:
		{
			foreach (HYSYSCompressor hYSYSCompressor in _HyTree._PFDObject.HYSYSCompressors)
			{
				if (hYSYSCompressor.UID == _MainUID)
				{
					_Data = hYSYSCompressor;
					break;
				}
			}
			break;
		}
		case 7:
		{
			foreach (HYSYSExpander hYSYSExpander in _HyTree._PFDObject.HYSYSExpanders)
			{
				if (hYSYSExpander.UID == _MainUID)
				{
					_Data = hYSYSExpander;
					break;
				}
			}
			break;
		}
		case 8:
		{
			foreach (HYSYSPipe hYSYSPipe in _HyTree._PFDObject.HYSYSPipes)
			{
				if (hYSYSPipe.UID == _MainUID)
				{
					_Data = hYSYSPipe;
					break;
				}
			}
			break;
		}
		case 9:
		{
			foreach (HYSYSHeatExchanger hYSYSHeatExchange in _HyTree._PFDObject.HYSYSHeatExchanges)
			{
				if (hYSYSHeatExchange.UID == _MainUID)
				{
					_Data = hYSYSHeatExchange;
					break;
				}
			}
			break;
		}
		case 10:
		{
			foreach (HYSYSHeatExchanger hYSYSHeatExchange2 in _HyTree._PFDObject.HYSYSHeatExchanges)
			{
				if (hYSYSHeatExchange2.UID == _MainUID)
				{
					_Data = hYSYSHeatExchange2;
					break;
				}
			}
			break;
		}
		case 11:
		{
			foreach (HYSYSHeatExchanger hYSYSHeatExchange3 in _HyTree._PFDObject.HYSYSHeatExchanges)
			{
				if (hYSYSHeatExchange3.UID == _MainUID)
				{
					_Data = hYSYSHeatExchange3;
					break;
				}
			}
			break;
		}
		case 12:
		{
			foreach (HYSYSLNGHeatExchanger hYSYSLNGHX in _HyTree._PFDObject.HYSYSLNGHXs)
			{
				if (hYSYSLNGHX.UID == _MainUID)
				{
					_Data = hYSYSLNGHX;
					break;
				}
			}
			break;
		}
		case 13:
		{
			foreach (HYSYSEquilibriumReactor hYSYSEquilibriumReactor in _HyTree._PFDObject.HYSYSEquilibriumReactors)
			{
				if (hYSYSEquilibriumReactor.UID == _MainUID)
				{
					_Data = hYSYSEquilibriumReactor;
					break;
				}
			}
			break;
		}
		case 14:
		{
			foreach (HYSYSConversionReactor hYSYSConversionReactor in _HyTree._PFDObject.HYSYSConversionReactors)
			{
				if (hYSYSConversionReactor.UID == _MainUID)
				{
					_Data = hYSYSConversionReactor;
					break;
				}
			}
			break;
		}
		case 15:
		{
			foreach (HYSYSGibbsReactor hYSYSGibbsReactor in _HyTree._PFDObject.HYSYSGibbsReactors)
			{
				if (hYSYSGibbsReactor.UID == _MainUID)
				{
					_Data = hYSYSGibbsReactor;
					break;
				}
			}
			break;
		}
		case 16:
		{
			foreach (HYSYSPlugFlowReactor hYSYSPFReactor in _HyTree._PFDObject.HYSYSPFReactors)
			{
				if (hYSYSPFReactor.UID == _MainUID)
				{
					_Data = hYSYSPFReactor;
					break;
				}
			}
			break;
		}
		case 17:
		{
			foreach (HYSYSCSTReactor hYSYSCSTReactor in _HyTree._PFDObject.HYSYSCSTReactors)
			{
				if (hYSYSCSTReactor.UID == _MainUID)
				{
					_Data = hYSYSCSTReactor;
					break;
				}
			}
			break;
		}
		case 18:
		{
			foreach (HYSYSComponentSplitter hYSYSComponentSplitter in _HyTree._PFDObject.HYSYSComponentSplitters)
			{
				if (hYSYSComponentSplitter.UID == _MainUID)
				{
					_Data = hYSYSComponentSplitter;
					break;
				}
			}
			break;
		}
		}
	}

	private void InitializeSpreadsheet()
	{
		if (_GlobalDefine == null)
		{
			_GlobalDefine = _Flowsheet.get_Operations((object)"SpreadSheetOpObject").Add("GlobalDefines", "SpreadSheetOpObject") as SpreadsheetOp;
			_GlobalDefine.NumberOfRows = 20;
			_GlobalDefine.NumberOfColumns = 5;
		}
		else if (!((string[])_Flowsheet.get_Operations((object)"SpreadSheetOpObject").Names).Contains("GlobalDefines"))
		{
			_GlobalDefine = null;
			_GlobalDefine = _Flowsheet.get_Operations((object)"SpreadSheetOpObject").Add("GlobalDefines", "SpreadSheetOpObject") as SpreadsheetOp;
			_GlobalDefine.NumberOfRows = 20;
			_GlobalDefine.NumberOfColumns = 5;
		}
	}

	private string getOperator(string operName)
	{
		if (operName.StartsWith("MIN") || operName.StartsWith("DIF") || operName.StartsWith("SUB"))
		{
			return "-";
		}
		if (operName.StartsWith("PLUS") || operName.StartsWith("SUM") || operName.StartsWith("ADD"))
		{
			return "+";
		}
		if (operName.StartsWith("TIME") || operName.StartsWith("MULT"))
		{
			return "*";
		}
		if (operName.StartsWith("DIVI") || operName.StartsWith("RATI") || operName.StartsWith("OVER"))
		{
			return "/";
		}
		return string.Empty;
	}

	private Valve FindValveNextToMixer(string mixerUID)
	{
		HYSYSMixer hYSYSMixer = null;
		foreach (HYSYSMixer hYSYSMixer2 in _HyTree._PFDObject.HYSYSMixers)
		{
			if (hYSYSMixer2.UID == mixerUID)
			{
				hYSYSMixer = hYSYSMixer2;
				break;
			}
		}
		if (hYSYSMixer == null || !hYSYSMixer.OutletValveAdded)
		{
			return null;
		}
		Valve result = null;
		string index = ((string[])(_Flowsheet.get_Operations((object)"MixerOpObject")[mixerUID] as MixerOp).AttachedProducts.Names)[0];
		string[] array = (string[])(_Flowsheet.MaterialStreams[index] as ProcessStream).AttachedOpers.Names;
		foreach (string text in array)
		{
			if (!(text == mixerUID))
			{
				result = _Flowsheet.get_Operations((object)"ValveOpObject")[text] as Valve;
				break;
			}
		}
		return result;
	}

	private Valve FindValveBeforeSplitter(string splUID)
	{
		HYSYSSplitter hYSYSSplitter = null;
		foreach (HYSYSSplitter hYSYSSplitter2 in _HyTree._PFDObject.HYSYSSplitters)
		{
			if (hYSYSSplitter2.UID == splUID)
			{
				hYSYSSplitter = hYSYSSplitter2;
				break;
			}
		}
		if (hYSYSSplitter == null || !hYSYSSplitter.InletValveAdded)
		{
			return null;
		}
		Valve result = null;
		string index = ((string[])(_Flowsheet.get_Operations((object)"TeeOpObject")[splUID] as TeeOp).AttachedFeeds.Names)[0];
		string[] array = (string[])(_Flowsheet.MaterialStreams[index] as ProcessStream).AttachedOpers.Names;
		foreach (string text in array)
		{
			if (!(text == splUID))
			{
				result = _Flowsheet.get_Operations((object)"ValveOpObject")[text] as Valve;
				break;
			}
		}
		return result;
	}

	private RealVariable GetFlashDPVar(string UID)
	{
		return GetFlashVarGeneral(UID, 0);
	}

	private RealVariable GetFlashPressureVar(string UID)
	{
		return GetFlashVarGeneral(UID, 1);
	}

	private RealVariable GetFlashDutyVar(string UID)
	{
		return GetFlashVarGeneral(UID, 2);
	}

	private RealVariable GetFlashTempVar(string UID)
	{
		return GetFlashVarGeneral(UID, 3);
	}

	private RealVariable GetFlashVarGeneral(string UID, int varType)
	{
		RealVariable realVariable = null;
		foreach (HYSYSSeparator hysysSeparator in _HyTree._PFDObject.HysysSeparators)
		{
			if (!(hysysSeparator.UID == UID))
			{
				continue;
			}
			Separator separator = null;
			if (hysysSeparator.SeparatorType == HYSYSSeparatorType.Separator)
			{
				separator = _Flowsheet.get_Operations((object)"FlashTankObject")[UID] as Separator;
			}
			else if (hysysSeparator.SeparatorType == HYSYSSeparatorType.ThreePhaseSep)
			{
				separator = _Flowsheet.get_Operations((object)"Sep3OpObject")[UID] as Separator;
			}
			if (separator == null)
			{
				return null;
			}
			switch (varType)
			{
			case 0:
				if (_Import && impVarColl.Keys.Contains(UID + "_DP"))
				{
					realVariable = impVarColl[UID + "_DP"];
					_ImpVarExist = true;
				}
				else
				{
					realVariable = separator.VesselPressureDrop;
					if (_Import)
					{
						impVarColl.Add(UID + "_DP", realVariable);
					}
				}
				if (separator.VesselPressureDrop.Value == 0.0 && !_Import)
				{
					separator.VesselPressureDrop.Erase();
				}
				_ImpKey = UID + "_DP";
				break;
			case 1:
				if (_Import && impVarColl.Keys.Contains(UID + "_PRES"))
				{
					realVariable = impVarColl[UID + "_PRES"];
					_ImpVarExist = true;
				}
				else
				{
					realVariable = ((ProcessStream)separator.AttachedProducts[0]).Pressure;
					if (_Import)
					{
						impVarColl.Add(UID + "_PRES", realVariable);
					}
				}
				if (separator.VesselPressureDrop.Value == 0.0 && !_Import)
				{
					separator.VesselPressureDrop.Erase();
				}
				_ImpKey = UID + "_PRES";
				break;
			case 2:
				if (_Import && impVarColl.Keys.Contains(UID + "_Q"))
				{
					realVariable = impVarColl[UID + "_Q"];
					_ImpVarExist = true;
				}
				else
				{
					realVariable = separator.HeatFlow;
					if (_Import)
					{
						impVarColl.Add(UID + "_Q", realVariable);
					}
				}
				_ImpKey = UID + "_Q";
				break;
			case 3:
				if (_Import && impVarColl.Keys.Contains(UID + "_TEMP"))
				{
					realVariable = impVarColl[UID + "_TEMP"];
					_ImpVarExist = true;
				}
				else
				{
					realVariable = ((ProcessStream)separator.AttachedProducts[0]).Temperature;
					if (_Import)
					{
						impVarColl.Add(UID + "_TEMP", realVariable);
					}
				}
				_ImpKey = UID + "_TEMP";
				break;
			}
			return realVariable;
		}
		foreach (HYSYSHeatExchanger hYSYSHeatExchange in _HyTree._PFDObject.HYSYSHeatExchanges)
		{
			if (hYSYSHeatExchange.ExchangerType != HysysHeatExchangerType.Heater || !(hYSYSHeatExchange.UID == UID))
			{
				continue;
			}
			HeaterOp heaterOp = _Flowsheet.get_Operations((object)"HeaterOpObject")[UID] as HeaterOp;
			switch (varType)
			{
			case 0:
				if (_Import && impVarColl.Keys.Contains(UID + "_DP"))
				{
					realVariable = impVarColl[UID + "_DP"];
					_ImpVarExist = true;
				}
				else
				{
					realVariable = heaterOp.PressureDrop;
					if (_Import)
					{
						impVarColl.Add(UID + "_DP", realVariable);
					}
				}
				_ImpKey = UID + "_DP";
				break;
			case 1:
				if (_Import && impVarColl.Keys.Contains(UID + "_PRES"))
				{
					realVariable = impVarColl[UID + "_PRES"];
					_ImpVarExist = true;
				}
				else
				{
					realVariable = heaterOp.ProductPressure;
					if (_Import)
					{
						impVarColl.Add(UID + "_PRES", realVariable);
					}
					else if (heaterOp.PressureDrop.Value == 0.0)
					{
						heaterOp.PressureDrop.Erase();
					}
				}
				_ImpKey = UID + "_PRES";
				break;
			case 2:
				if (_Import && impVarColl.Keys.Contains(UID + "_Q"))
				{
					realVariable = impVarColl[UID + "_Q"];
					_ImpVarExist = true;
				}
				else
				{
					realVariable = heaterOp.EnergyStream.HeatFlow;
					if (_Import)
					{
						impVarColl.Add(UID + "_Q", realVariable);
					}
				}
				_ImpKey = UID + "_Q";
				break;
			case 3:
				if (_Import && impVarColl.Keys.Contains(UID + "_TEMP"))
				{
					realVariable = impVarColl[UID + "_TEMP"];
					_ImpVarExist = true;
				}
				else
				{
					realVariable = heaterOp.ProductTemperature;
					if (_Import)
					{
						impVarColl.Add(UID + "_TEMP", realVariable);
					}
				}
				_ImpKey = UID + "_TEMP";
				break;
			}
			return realVariable;
		}
		foreach (HYSYSValve hYSYSValf in _HyTree._PFDObject.HYSYSValves)
		{
			if (!(hYSYSValf.UID == UID))
			{
				continue;
			}
			Valve valve = _Flowsheet.get_Operations((object)"ValveOpObject")[UID] as Valve;
			switch (varType)
			{
			case 0:
				if (_Import && impVarColl.Keys.Contains(UID + "_DP"))
				{
					realVariable = impVarColl[UID + "_DP"];
					_ImpVarExist = true;
				}
				else
				{
					realVariable = valve.PressureDrop;
					if (_Import)
					{
						impVarColl.Add(UID + "_DP", realVariable);
					}
				}
				_ImpKey = UID + "_DP";
				break;
			case 1:
				if (_Import && impVarColl.Keys.Contains(UID + "_PRES"))
				{
					realVariable = impVarColl[UID + "_PRES"];
					_ImpVarExist = true;
				}
				else
				{
					realVariable = valve.ProductPressure;
					if (_Import)
					{
						impVarColl.Add(UID + "_PRES", realVariable);
					}
					else if (valve.PressureDrop.Value == 0.0)
					{
						valve.PressureDrop.Erase();
					}
				}
				_ImpKey = UID + "_PRES";
				break;
			case 3:
				if (_Import && impVarColl.Keys.Contains(UID + "_TEMP"))
				{
					realVariable = impVarColl[UID + "_TEMP"];
					_ImpVarExist = true;
				}
				else
				{
					realVariable = valve.ProductTemperature;
					if (_Import)
					{
						impVarColl.Add(UID + "_TEMP", realVariable);
					}
				}
				_ImpKey = UID + "_TEMP";
				break;
			}
			return realVariable;
		}
		return realVariable;
	}

	private RealVariable GetMixerVarGeneral(string UID, string varType)
	{
		RealVariable realVariable = null;
		Valve valve = FindValveNextToMixer(UID);
		if (valve != null && valve.AttachedProducts[0] is ProcessStream processStream)
		{
			switch (varType)
			{
			case "DP":
				if (_Import && impVarColl.Keys.Contains(processStream.name + "_DP"))
				{
					realVariable = impVarColl[processStream.name + "_DP"];
					_ImpVarExist = true;
				}
				else
				{
					realVariable = valve.PressureDrop;
					if (_Import)
					{
						impVarColl.Add(processStream.name + "_DP", realVariable);
					}
				}
				_ImpKey = processStream.name + "_DP";
				break;
			case "PRES":
				if (_Import && impVarColl.Keys.Contains(processStream.name + "_PRES"))
				{
					realVariable = impVarColl[processStream.name + "_PRES"];
					_ImpVarExist = true;
				}
				else
				{
					realVariable = processStream.Pressure;
					if (_Import)
					{
						impVarColl.Add(processStream.name + "_PRES", realVariable);
					}
					else if (valve.PressureDrop.Value == 0.0)
					{
						valve.PressureDrop.Erase();
					}
				}
				_ImpKey = UID + "_PRES";
				break;
			case "TEMP":
				if (_Import && impVarColl.Keys.Contains(processStream.name + "_TEMP"))
				{
					realVariable = impVarColl[processStream.name + "_TEMP"];
					_ImpVarExist = true;
				}
				else
				{
					realVariable = processStream.Temperature;
					if (_Import)
					{
						impVarColl.Add(processStream.name + "_TEMP", realVariable);
					}
				}
				_ImpKey = processStream.name + "_TEMP";
				break;
			}
		}
		return realVariable;
	}

	private RealVariable GetStreamVarGeneral(string UID, string varType, string flowbasis)
	{
		RealVariable realVariable = null;
		ProcessStream processStream = GetProcessStream(UID);
		if (processStream != null)
		{
			switch (varType)
			{
			case "PRES":
				if (_Import && impVarColl.Keys.Contains(UID + "_PRES"))
				{
					realVariable = impVarColl[UID + "_PRES"];
					_ImpVarExist = true;
				}
				else
				{
					realVariable = processStream.Pressure;
					if (_Import)
					{
						impVarColl.Add(UID + "_PRES", realVariable);
					}
				}
				_ImpKey = UID + "_PRES";
				break;
			case "RATE":
			{
				string text = UID + "_RATE_" + flowbasis;
				if (_Import && impVarColl.Keys.Contains(text))
				{
					realVariable = impVarColl[text];
					_ImpVarExist = true;
				}
				else
				{
					switch (flowbasis)
					{
					case "M":
						realVariable = processStream.MolarFlow;
						break;
					case "WT":
						realVariable = processStream.MassFlow;
						break;
					case "LV":
						realVariable = processStream.StdLiqVolFlow;
						break;
					case "GV":
						realVariable = processStream.StdGasFlow;
						break;
					}
					if (_Import)
					{
						impVarColl.Add(text, realVariable);
					}
				}
				_ImpKey = text;
				break;
			}
			case "TEMP":
				if (_Import && impVarColl.Keys.Contains(UID + "_TEMP"))
				{
					realVariable = impVarColl[UID + "_TEMP"];
					_ImpVarExist = true;
				}
				else
				{
					realVariable = processStream.Temperature;
					if (_Import)
					{
						impVarColl.Add(UID + "_TEMP", realVariable);
					}
				}
				_ImpKey = UID + "_TEMP";
				break;
			}
		}
		return realVariable;
	}

	private RealVariable GetPumpVarGeneral(string UID, string varType)
	{
		RealVariable realVariable = null;
		if (!(_Flowsheet.get_Operations((object)"PumpOpObject")[UID] is PumpOp pumpOp))
		{
			return null;
		}
		BackDoor backDoor = pumpOp as BackDoor;
		switch (varType)
		{
		case "TEMP":
			if (_Import && impVarColl.Keys.Contains(UID + "_TEMP"))
			{
				realVariable = impVarColl[UID + "_TEMP"];
				_ImpVarExist = true;
			}
			else
			{
				realVariable = pumpOp.ProductTemperature;
				if (_Import)
				{
					impVarColl.Add(UID + "_TEMP", realVariable);
				}
			}
			_ImpKey = UID + "_TEMP";
			break;
		case "PRES":
			if (_Import && impVarColl.Keys.Contains(UID + "_PRES"))
			{
				realVariable = impVarColl[UID + "_PRES"];
				_ImpVarExist = true;
			}
			else
			{
				realVariable = pumpOp.ProductPressure;
				if (_Import)
				{
					impVarColl.Add(UID + "_PRES", realVariable);
				}
				else
				{
					realVariable = backDoor.get_BackDoorVariable((object)":PressureDrop.500").Variable as RealVariable;
					if (realVariable.Value == 0.0)
					{
						realVariable.Erase();
					}
				}
			}
			_ImpKey = UID + "_PRES";
			break;
		case "DP":
			if (_Import && impVarColl.Keys.Contains(UID + "_DP"))
			{
				realVariable = impVarColl[UID + "_DP"];
				_ImpVarExist = true;
			}
			else
			{
				realVariable = backDoor.get_BackDoorVariable((object)":PressureDrop.500").Variable as RealVariable;
				if (_Import)
				{
					impVarColl.Add(UID + "_DP", realVariable);
				}
			}
			_ImpKey = UID + "_DP";
			break;
		case "WORK":
			if (_Import && impVarColl.Keys.Contains(UID + "_WORK"))
			{
				realVariable = impVarColl[UID + "_WORK"];
				_ImpVarExist = true;
			}
			else
			{
				realVariable = pumpOp.Work;
				if (_Import)
				{
					impVarColl.Add(UID + "_WORK", realVariable);
				}
			}
			_ImpKey = UID + "_WORK";
			break;
		case "HEAD":
			if (_Import && impVarColl.Keys.Contains(UID + "_HEAD"))
			{
				realVariable = impVarColl[UID + "_HEAD"];
				_ImpVarExist = true;
			}
			else
			{
				realVariable = pumpOp.Head;
				if (_Import)
				{
					impVarColl.Add(UID + "_HEAD", realVariable);
				}
			}
			_ImpKey = UID + "_HEAD";
			break;
		case "PRAT":
			if (_Import && impVarColl.Keys.Contains(UID + "_PRAT"))
			{
				realVariable = impVarColl[UID + "_PRAT"];
				_ImpVarExist = true;
			}
			else
			{
				realVariable = backDoor.get_BackDoorVariable((object)":Ratio.501").Variable as RealVariable;
				if (_Import)
				{
					impVarColl.Add(UID + "_PRAT", realVariable);
				}
			}
			_ImpKey = UID + "_PRAT";
			break;
		}
		return realVariable;
	}

	private RealVariable GetValveVarGeneral(string UID, string varType)
	{
		RealVariable realVariable = null;
		if (!(_Flowsheet.get_Operations((object)"ValveOpObject")[UID] is Valve valve))
		{
			return null;
		}
		switch (varType)
		{
		case "TEMP":
			if (_Import && impVarColl.Keys.Contains(UID + "_TEMP"))
			{
				realVariable = impVarColl[UID + "_TEMP"];
				_ImpVarExist = true;
				_ImpKey = UID + "_TEMP";
				break;
			}
			realVariable = valve.ProductTemperature;
			if (_Import)
			{
				impVarColl.Add(UID + "_TEMP", realVariable);
			}
			break;
		case "PRES":
			if (_Import && impVarColl.Keys.Contains(UID + "_PRES"))
			{
				realVariable = impVarColl[UID + "_PRES"];
				_ImpVarExist = true;
				_ImpKey = UID + "_PRES";
			}
			else
			{
				realVariable = valve.ProductPressure;
				if (_Import)
				{
					impVarColl.Add(UID + "_PRES", realVariable);
				}
			}
			if (valve.PressureDrop.Value == 0.0 && !_Import)
			{
				valve.PressureDrop.Erase();
			}
			_ImpKey = UID + "_PRES";
			break;
		case "DP":
			if (_Import && impVarColl.Keys.Contains(UID + "_DP"))
			{
				realVariable = impVarColl[UID + "_DP"];
				_ImpVarExist = true;
			}
			else
			{
				realVariable = valve.PressureDrop;
				if (_Import)
				{
					impVarColl.Add(UID + "_DP", realVariable);
				}
			}
			if (valve.PressureDrop.Value == 0.0 && !_Import)
			{
				valve.PressureDrop.Erase();
			}
			_ImpKey = UID + "_DP";
			break;
		}
		return realVariable;
	}

	private RealVariable GetColumnVarGeneral(string UID, string varType, string flowbasis, bool bImport)
	{
		RealVariable realVariable = null;
		if (!(_Flowsheet.get_Operations((object)"ColumnOp")[UID] is ColumnOp columnOp))
		{
			return null;
		}
		BackDoor backDoor = columnOp as BackDoor;
		HYSYSColumn columnData = GetColumnData(UID);
		if (columnData == null)
		{
			return null;
		}
		switch (varType.Substring(0, 4))
		{
		case "REFL":
			if (impVarColl.Keys.Contains(UID + "_REFL"))
			{
				realVariable = impVarColl[UID + "_REFL"];
				_ImpVarExist = true;
			}
			else
			{
				string moniker3 = "FlowSht.600/ColumnStage.500.0(" + UID + "_Condenser)/SepStage.300:MolarFlow.302";
				realVariable = backDoor.get_BackDoorVariable((object)moniker3).Variable as RealVariable;
				impVarColl.Add(UID + "_REFL", realVariable);
			}
			_ImpKey = UID + "_REFL";
			break;
		case "RRAT":
			if (impVarColl.Keys.Contains(UID + "_RRAT"))
			{
				realVariable = impVarColl[UID + "_RRAT"];
				_ImpVarExist = true;
			}
			else
			{
				realVariable = backDoor.get_BackDoorVariable((object)"FlowSht.600:ExtraData.500.34").Variable as RealVariable;
				impVarColl.Add(UID + "_RRAT", realVariable);
			}
			_ImpKey = UID + "_RRAT";
			break;
		case "TEMP":
			if (impVarColl.Keys.Contains(UID + "_TEMP"))
			{
				realVariable = impVarColl[UID + "_TEMP"];
				_ImpVarExist = true;
			}
			else
			{
				int num2 = _Def.TrayNumber;
				if (GetColumnData(UID).bCondenserExist && num2 > 0)
				{
					num2--;
				}
				string moniker2 = "FlowSht.600/ColumnStage.500." + num2 + "/SepStage.300/Fluid.300:Temperature.0";
				realVariable = backDoor.get_BackDoorVariable((object)moniker2).Variable as RealVariable;
				impVarColl.Add(UID + "_TEMP", realVariable);
			}
			_ImpKey = UID + "_TEMP";
			break;
		case "DUTY":
		{
			string text = ParseUtilty.GetParentheses(varType);
			if (text.Contains(","))
			{
				text = text.Remove(text.IndexOf(","));
			}
			int dutyNO = int.Parse(text);
			_ImpKey = UID + "_DUTY_" + dutyNO;
			realVariable = HandleColumnDuty(columnData, dutyNO, _ImpKey, backDoor);
			break;
		}
		case "DNAM":
		{
			int dutyIDFromName = GetDutyIDFromName(columnData, varType);
			if (dutyIDFromName != -1)
			{
				_ImpKey = UID + "_DUTY_" + dutyIDFromName;
				realVariable = HandleColumnDuty(columnData, dutyIDFromName, _ImpKey, backDoor);
			}
			break;
		}
		case "PCON":
			if (_Import && impVarColl.Keys.Contains(UID + "_PCON"))
			{
				realVariable = impVarColl[UID + "_PCON"];
				_ImpVarExist = true;
			}
			else
			{
				realVariable = backDoor.get_BackDoorVariable((object)"FlowSht.600/ColumnStage.500.1:Pressure.300").Variable as RealVariable;
				if (_Import)
				{
					impVarColl.Add(UID + "_PCON", realVariable);
				}
			}
			_ImpKey = UID + "_PCON";
			break;
		case "PTOP":
			if (_Import && impVarColl.Keys.Contains(UID + "_PTOP"))
			{
				realVariable = impVarColl[UID + "_PTOP"];
				_ImpVarExist = true;
			}
			else
			{
				realVariable = ((!columnData.bCondenserExist) ? (backDoor.get_BackDoorVariable((object)"FlowSht.600/ColumnStage.500.0:Pressure.300").Variable as RealVariable) : (backDoor.get_BackDoorVariable((object)"FlowSht.600/ColumnStage.500.1:Pressure.300").Variable as RealVariable));
				if (_Import)
				{
					impVarColl.Add(UID + "_PTOP", realVariable);
				}
			}
			_ImpKey = UID + "_PTOP";
			break;
		case "DPCO":
		{
			if (_Import)
			{
				_AddSpecialVar = true;
				break;
			}
			int num3 = ((HYSYSColumn)_Data).stageNumber;
			if (((HYSYSColumn)_Data).bCondenserExist)
			{
				num3--;
			}
			if (((HYSYSColumn)_Data).bReboilerExist)
			{
				num3--;
			}
			string moniker4 = "FlowSht.600/ColumnStage.500." + num3 + ":Pressure.300";
			realVariable = backDoor.get_BackDoorVariable((object)moniker4).Variable as RealVariable;
			break;
		}
		case "PROD":
			_AddSpecialVar = true;
			break;
		case "PRES":
			if (_Import && impVarColl.Keys.Contains(UID + "_PRES"))
			{
				realVariable = impVarColl[UID + "_PRES"];
				_ImpVarExist = true;
			}
			else
			{
				int num = _Def.TrayNumber;
				if (GetColumnData(UID).bCondenserExist && num > 0)
				{
					num--;
				}
				string moniker = "FlowSht.600/ColumnStage.500." + num + ":Pressure.300";
				realVariable = backDoor.get_BackDoorVariable((object)moniker).Variable as RealVariable;
				impVarColl.Add(UID + "_PRES", realVariable);
			}
			_ImpKey = UID + "_PRES";
			break;
		}
		return realVariable;
	}

	private HYSYSColumn GetColumnData(string UID)
	{
		HYSYSColumn result = null;
		foreach (HYSYSColumn hYSYSColumn in _HyTree._PFDObject.HYSYSColumns)
		{
			if (hYSYSColumn.UID == UID)
			{
				result = hYSYSColumn;
				break;
			}
		}
		return result;
	}

	private int GetDutyIDFromName(HYSYSColumn colData, string dutyName)
	{
		if (colData.DutySpec.Count == 0)
		{
			return -1;
		}
		int result = -1;
		foreach (int key in colData.DutySpec.Keys)
		{
			if (colData.DutySpec[key].Values.First().Name == ParseUtilty.GetStringValue(dutyName))
			{
				result = key;
				break;
			}
		}
		return result;
	}

	private int IsDutyForCondenserORReboiler(HYSYSColumn colData, int dutyID)
	{
		int result = -1;
		if (!colData.DutySpec.Keys.Contains(dutyID))
		{
			return -1;
		}
		int num = colData.DutySpec[dutyID].Keys.First();
		if (num == 1 && colData.bCondenserExist)
		{
			result = 0;
		}
		else if (num != colData.stageNumber)
		{
			result = ((!colData.QStreamForDuty.Keys.Contains(colData.UID + "_" + dutyID)) ? 1 : 3);
		}
		else if (colData.bReboilerExist)
		{
			result = 2;
		}
		else if (colData.QStreamForDuty.Keys.Contains(colData.UID + "_" + dutyID))
		{
			result = 3;
		}
		return result;
	}

	private RealVariable HandleColumnDuty(HYSYSColumn columnData, int dutyNO, string key, BackDoor colBD)
	{
		RealVariable realVariable = null;
		if (_Import)
		{
			if (impVarColl.Keys.Contains(key))
			{
				realVariable = impVarColl[key];
				_ImpVarExist = true;
			}
			else
			{
				if (IsDutyForCondenserORReboiler(columnData, dutyNO) == 0)
				{
					realVariable = columnData.conderser.HeatFlow;
				}
				else if (IsDutyForCondenserORReboiler(columnData, dutyNO) == 2)
				{
					realVariable = columnData.reboiler.HeatFlow;
				}
				else if (IsDutyForCondenserORReboiler(columnData, dutyNO) == 3)
				{
					realVariable = columnData.QStreamForDuty[columnData.UID + "_" + dutyNO].HeatFlow;
				}
				else
				{
					if (IsDutyForCondenserORReboiler(columnData, dutyNO) != 1)
					{
						return null;
					}
					if (columnData.paColl.Keys.Contains(dutyNO.ToString()))
					{
						realVariable = columnData.paColl[dutyNO.ToString()].get_BackDoorVariable((object)":HeatFlow.510").Variable as RealVariable;
					}
				}
				impVarColl.Add(key, realVariable);
			}
		}
		else if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(_Def.sColumnSpecName))
		{
			realVariable = colBD.get_BackDoorVariable((object)_Def.sColumnSpecName).Variable as RealVariable;
		}
		return realVariable;
	}

	private RealVariable GetSplitterVarGeneral(string UID, string varType)
	{
		RealVariable realVariable = null;
		Valve valve = FindValveBeforeSplitter(UID);
		if (valve != null && valve.AttachedProducts[0] is ProcessStream processStream)
		{
			switch (varType)
			{
			case "TEMP":
				if (_Import && impVarColl.Keys.Contains(processStream.name + "_TEMP"))
				{
					realVariable = impVarColl[processStream.name + "_TEMP"];
					_ImpVarExist = true;
				}
				else
				{
					realVariable = processStream.Temperature;
					if (_Import)
					{
						impVarColl.Add(processStream.name + "_TEMP", realVariable);
					}
				}
				_ImpKey = processStream.name + "_TEMP";
				break;
			case "PRES":
				if (_Import && impVarColl.Keys.Contains(processStream.name + "_PRES"))
				{
					realVariable = impVarColl[processStream.name + "_PRES"];
					_ImpVarExist = true;
				}
				else
				{
					realVariable = processStream.Pressure;
					if (_Import)
					{
						impVarColl.Add(processStream.name + "_PRES", realVariable);
					}
					else if (valve.PressureDrop.Value == 0.0)
					{
						valve.PressureDrop.Erase();
					}
				}
				_ImpKey = processStream.name + "_PRES";
				break;
			case "DP":
				if (_Import && impVarColl.Keys.Contains(processStream.name + "_DP"))
				{
					realVariable = impVarColl[processStream.name + "_DP"];
					_ImpVarExist = true;
				}
				else
				{
					realVariable = valve.PressureDrop;
					if (_Import)
					{
						impVarColl.Add(processStream.name + "_DP", realVariable);
					}
				}
				_ImpKey = processStream.name + "_DP";
				break;
			}
		}
		return realVariable;
	}

	private RealVariable GetPipeVarGeneral(string UID, string varType)

	private RealVariable GetHXVarGeneral(string UID, string varType)
	{
		RealVariable result = null;
		foreach (HYSYSHeatExchanger hYSYSHeatExchange in _HyTree._PFDObject.HYSYSHeatExchanges)
		{
			if (hYSYSHeatExchange.UID == UID)
			{
				if (hYSYSHeatExchange.ExchangerType == HysysHeatExchangerType.Heater)
				{
					result = GetHeaterVar(UID, varType);
				}
				else if (hYSYSHeatExchange.ExchangerType == HysysHeatExchangerType.Cooler)
				{
					result = GetCoolerVar(UID, varType);
				}
				else if (hYSYSHeatExchange.ExchangerType == HysysHeatExchangerType.HeatExchanger)
				{
					result = GetTwoSideHeatExchangerVar(UID, varType);
				}
				break;
			}
		}
		return result;
	}

	private RealVariable GetHXRIGVarGeneral(string UID, string varType)
	{
		return GetTwoSideHeatExchangerVar(UID, varType);
	}

	private RealVariable GetHeaterVar(string UID, string varType)
	{
		RealVariable realVariable = null;
		HeaterOp heaterOp = _Flowsheet.get_Operations((object)"HeaterOpObject")[UID] as HeaterOp;
		BackDoor backDoor = heaterOp as BackDoor;
		if (heaterOp == null)
		{
			return null;
		}
		if (varType.ToUpper().StartsWith("TEMP"))
		{
			varType = "CTEM";
		}
		else if (varType.ToUpper().StartsWith("DP"))
		{
			varType = "CDP";
		}
		switch (varType)
		{
		case "DUTY":
			if (_Import && impVarColl.Keys.Contains(UID + "_DUTY"))
			{
				realVariable = impVarColl[UID + "_DUTY"];
				_ImpVarExist = true;
			}
			else
			{
				realVariable = heaterOp.Duty;
				if (_Import)
				{
					impVarColl.Add(UID + "_DUTY", realVariable);
				}
			}
			_ImpKey = UID + "_DUTY";
			break;
		case "CTEM":
			if (_Import && impVarColl.Keys.Contains(UID + "_CTEM"))
			{
				realVariable = impVarColl[UID + "_CTEM"];
				_ImpVarExist = true;
			}
			else
			{
				realVariable = heaterOp.ProductTemperature;
				if (_Import)
				{
					impVarColl.Add(UID + "_CTEM", realVariable);
				}
			}
			_ImpKey = UID + "_CTEM";
			break;
		case "CLFR":
			if (_Import && impVarColl.Keys.Contains(UID + "_CLFR"))
			{
				realVariable = impVarColl[UID + "_CLFR"];
				_ImpVarExist = true;
			}
			else
			{
				realVariable = heaterOp.ProductStream.VapourFraction;
				if (_Import)
				{
					impVarColl.Add(UID + "_CLFR", realVariable);
				}
			}
			_ImpKey = UID + "_CLFR";
			break;
		case "CDTB":
			if (_Import && impVarColl.Keys.Contains(UID + "_CDTB"))
			{
				realVariable = impVarColl[UID + "_CDTB"];
				_ImpVarExist = true;
			}
			else
			{
				realVariable = GetHXRefStreamVariable(((HYSYSHeatExchanger)_Data).ColdSideOutletStreams[0].Trim(), bBubble: true);
				if (_Import)
				{
					impVarColl.Add(UID + "_CDTB", realVariable);
				}
			}
			_ImpKey = UID + "_CDTB";
			break;
		case "CDTA":
			if (_Import && impVarColl.Keys.Contains(UID + "_CDTA"))
			{
				realVariable = impVarColl[UID + "_CDTA"];
				_ImpVarExist = true;
			}
			else
			{
				realVariable = GetHXRefStreamVariable(((HYSYSHeatExchanger)_Data).ColdSideOutletStreams[0].Trim(), bBubble: false);
				if (_Import)
				{
					impVarColl.Add(UID + "_CDTA", realVariable);
				}
			}
			_ImpKey = UID + "_CDTA";
			break;
		case "CDP":
			if (_Import && impVarColl.Keys.Contains(UID + "_CDP"))
			{
				realVariable = impVarColl[UID + "_CDP"];
				_ImpVarExist = true;
			}
			else
			{
				realVariable = backDoor.get_BackDoorVariable((object)":PressureDrop.511").Variable as RealVariable;
				if (_Import)
				{
					impVarColl.Add(UID + "_CDP", realVariable);
				}
			}
			if (heaterOp.PressureDrop.Value == 0.0 && !_Import)
			{
				heaterOp.PressureDrop.Erase();
			}
			_ImpKey = UID + "_CDP";
			break;
		case "PRES":
			if (_Import && impVarColl.Keys.Contains(UID + "_PRES"))
			{
				realVariable = impVarColl[UID + "_PRES"];
				_ImpVarExist = true;
			}
			else
			{
				realVariable = heaterOp.ProductPressure;
				if (_Import)
				{
					impVarColl.Add(UID + "_PRES", realVariable);
				}
			}
			if (heaterOp.PressureDrop.Value == 0.0 && !_Import)
			{
				heaterOp.PressureDrop.Erase();
			}
			_ImpKey = UID + "_PRES";
			break;
		}
		return realVariable;
	}

	private RealVariable GetCoolerVar(string UID, string varType)
	{
		RealVariable realVariable = null;
		CoolerOp coolerOp = _Flowsheet.get_Operations((object)"CoolerOpObject")[UID] as CoolerOp;
		BackDoor backDoor = coolerOp as BackDoor;
		if (coolerOp == null)
		{
			return null;
		}
		if (varType.ToUpper().StartsWith("TEMP"))
		{
			varType = "CTEM";
		}
		else if (varType.ToUpper().StartsWith("DP"))
		{
			varType = "CDP";
		}
		switch (varType)
		{
		case "DUTY":
			if (_Import && impVarColl.Keys.Contains(UID + "_DUTY"))
			{
				realVariable = impVarColl[UID + "_DUTY"];
				_ImpVarExist = true;
			}
			else
			{
				realVariable = coolerOp.Duty;
				if (_Import)
				{
					impVarColl.Add(UID + "_DUTY", realVariable);
				}
			}
			_ImpKey = UID + "_DUTY";
			break;
		case "HTEM":
			if (_Import && impVarColl.Keys.Contains(UID + "_HTEM"))
			{
				realVariable = impVarColl[UID + "_HTEM"];
				_ImpVarExist = true;
			}
			else
			{
				realVariable = coolerOp.ProductTemperature;
				if (_Import)
				{
					impVarColl.Add(UID + "_HTEM", realVariable);
				}
			}
			_ImpKey = UID + "_HTEM";
			break;
		case "HLFR":
			if (_Import && impVarColl.Keys.Contains(UID + "_HLFR"))
			{
				realVariable = impVarColl[UID + "_HLFR"];
				_ImpVarExist = true;
			}
			else
			{
				realVariable = coolerOp.ProductStream.VapourFraction;
				if (_Import)
				{
					impVarColl.Add(UID + "_HLFR", realVariable);
				}
			}
			_ImpKey = UID + "_HLFR";
			break;
		case "HDTB":
			if (_Import && impVarColl.Keys.Contains(UID + "_HDTB"))
			{
				realVariable = impVarColl[UID + "_HDTB"];
				_ImpVarExist = true;
			}
			else
			{
				realVariable = GetHXRefStreamVariable(((HYSYSHeatExchanger)_Data).HotSideOutletStreams[0].Trim(), bBubble: true);
				if (_Import)
				{
					impVarColl.Add(UID + "_HDTB", realVariable);
				}
			}
			_ImpKey = UID + "_HDTB";
			break;
		case "HDTA":
			if (_Import && impVarColl.Keys.Contains(UID + "_HDTA"))
			{
				realVariable = impVarColl[UID + "_HDTA"];
				_ImpVarExist = true;
			}
			else
			{
				realVariable = GetHXRefStreamVariable(((HYSYSHeatExchanger)_Data).HotSideOutletStreams[0].Trim(), bBubble: false);
				if (_Import)
				{
					impVarColl.Add(UID + "_HDTA", realVariable);
				}
			}
			_ImpKey = UID + "_HDTA";
			break;
		case "HDP":
			if (_Import && impVarColl.Keys.Contains(UID + "_HDP"))
			{
				realVariable = impVarColl[UID + "_HDP"];
				_ImpVarExist = true;
			}
			else
			{
				realVariable = backDoor.get_BackDoorVariable((object)":PressureDrop.511").Variable as RealVariable;
				if (_Import)
				{
					impVarColl.Add(UID + "_HDP", realVariable);
				}
			}
			if (coolerOp.PressureDrop.Value == 0.0 && !_Import)
			{
				coolerOp.PressureDrop.Erase();
			}
			_ImpKey = UID + "_HDP";
			break;
		case "PRES":
			if (_Import && impVarColl.Keys.Contains(UID + "_PRES"))
			{
				realVariable = impVarColl[UID + "_PRES"];
				_ImpVarExist = true;
			}
			else
			{
				realVariable = coolerOp.ProductPressure;
				if (_Import)
				{
					impVarColl.Add(UID + "_PRES", realVariable);
				}
			}
			if (coolerOp.PressureDrop.Value == 0.0 && !_Import)
			{
				coolerOp.PressureDrop.Erase();
			}
			_ImpKey = UID + "_PRES";
			break;
		}
		return realVariable;
	}

	private ProcessStream GetHeatExchangerProduct(string UID, bool bHot)
	{
		ProcessStream result = null;
		foreach (HYSYSHeatExchanger hYSYSHeatExchange in _HyTree._PFDObject.HYSYSHeatExchanges)
		{
			if (!(hYSYSHeatExchange.UID != UID))
			{
				result = ((!bHot) ? GetProcessStream(hYSYSHeatExchange.ColdSideOutletStreams[0].Trim()) : GetProcessStream(hYSYSHeatExchange.HotSideOutletStreams[0].Trim()));
				break;
			}
		}
		return result;
	}

	private RealVariable GetHXRefStreamVariable(string sid, bool bBubble)
	{
		BackDoor backDoor = GetProcessStream(sid) as BackDoor;
		string streamName = NameHelper.CreateName(bIsStream: true);
		ProcessStream processStream = GetProcessStream(streamName);
		BackDoor obj = processStream as BackDoor;
		obj.SendAttachObject(":MaterialStream.500.500", backDoor);
		(obj.get_BackDoorVariable((object)":Boolean.540").Variable as InternalRealVariable).Value = 0.0;
		(obj.get_BackDoorVariable((object)":Boolean.541").Variable as InternalRealVariable).Value = 0.0;
		(obj.get_BackDoorVariable((object)":Boolean.542").Variable as InternalRealVariable).Value = 1.0;
		if (bBubble)
		{
			processStream.VapourFractionValue = 0.0;
		}
		else
		{
			processStream.VapourFractionValue = 1.0;
		}
		return processStream.Temperature;
	}

	private RealVariable GetTwoSideHeatExchangerVar(string UID, string varType)
	{

		{

	private RealVariable GetEquilReactorVarGeneral(string UID, string varType)
	{
		RealVariable realVariable = null;
		if (!(_Flowsheet.get_Operations((object)"EquilibriumReactorOp")[UID] is EquilibriumReactor equilibriumReactor))
		{
			return null;
		}
		ProcessStream processStream = null;
		switch (varType)
		{
		case "PRES":
			processStream = GetProcessStream((string)equilibriumReactor.AttachedProducts[0]);
			if (_Import && impVarColl.Keys.Contains(processStream.name + "_PRES"))
			{
				realVariable = impVarColl[processStream.name + "_PRES"];
				_ImpVarExist = true;
			}
			else if (processStream != null)
			{
				realVariable = processStream.Pressure;
				if (_Import)
				{
					impVarColl.Add(processStream.name + "_PRES", realVariable);
				}
				else if (equilibriumReactor.PressureDrop.Value == 0.0)
				{
					equilibriumReactor.PressureDrop.Erase();
				}
			}
			_ImpKey = processStream.name + "_PRES";
			break;
		case "DP":
			if (_Import && impVarColl.Keys.Contains(UID + "_DP"))
			{
				realVariable = impVarColl[UID + "_DP"];
				_ImpVarExist = true;
			}
			else
			{
				realVariable = equilibriumReactor.PressureDrop;
				if (_Import)
				{
					impVarColl.Add(UID + "_DP", realVariable);
				}
			}
			_ImpKey = UID + "_DP";
			break;
		case "TEMP":
			processStream = GetProcessStream((string)equilibriumReactor.AttachedProducts[0]);
			if (_Import && impVarColl.Keys.Contains(UID + "_TEMP"))
			{
				realVariable = impVarColl[UID + "_TEMP"];
				_ImpVarExist = true;
			}
			else if (processStream != null)
			{
				realVariable = processStream.Temperature;
				if (_Import)
				{
					impVarColl.Add(UID + "_TEMP", realVariable);
				}
			}
			_ImpKey = UID + "_TEMP";
			break;
		case "DUTY":
			if (_Import && impVarColl.Keys.Contains(UID + "_DUTY"))
			{
				realVariable = impVarColl[UID + "_DUTY"];
				_ImpVarExist = true;
				_ImpKey = UID + "_DUTY";
				break;
			}
			realVariable = equilibriumReactor.HeatFlow;
			if (_Import)
			{
				impVarColl.Add(UID + "_DUTY", realVariable);
			}
			break;
		}
		return realVariable;
	}
