	private RealVariable GetGibbsReactorVarGeneral(string UID, string varType)
	{
		RealVariable realVariable = null;
		if (!(_Flowsheet.get_Operations((object)"GibbsReactorOp")[UID] is GibbsReactor gibbsReactor))
		{
			return null;
		}
		ProcessStream processStream = null;
		switch (varType)
		{
		case "TEMP":
			processStream = GetProcessStream((string)gibbsReactor.AttachedProducts[0]);
			if (_Import && impVarColl.Keys.Contains(processStream.name + "_TEMP"))
			{
				realVariable = impVarColl[processStream.name + "_TEMP"];
				_ImpVarExist = true;
				_ImpKey = processStream.name + "_TEMP";
			}
			else if (processStream != null)
			{
				realVariable = processStream.Temperature;
				if (_Import)
				{
					impVarColl.Add(processStream.name + "_TEMP", realVariable);
				}
			}
			break;
		case "PRES":
			processStream = GetProcessStream((string)gibbsReactor.AttachedProducts[0]);
			if (_Import && impVarColl.Keys.Contains(processStream.name + "_PRES"))
			{
				realVariable = impVarColl[processStream.name + "_PRES"];
				_ImpVarExist = true;
				_ImpKey = processStream.name + "_PRES";
			}
			else if (processStream != null)
			{
				realVariable = processStream.Pressure;
				if (_Import)
				{
					impVarColl.Add(processStream.name + "_PRES", realVariable);
				}
				else if (gibbsReactor.PressureDrop.Value == 0.0)
				{
					gibbsReactor.PressureDrop.Erase();
				}
			}
			break;
		case "DUTY":
			if (_Import && impVarColl.Keys.Contains(UID + "_DUTY"))
			{
				realVariable = impVarColl[UID + "_DUTY"];
				_ImpVarExist = true;
				_ImpKey = UID + "_DUTY";
				break;
			}
			realVariable = gibbsReactor.HeatFlow;
			if (_Import)
			{
				impVarColl.Add(UID + "_DUTY", realVariable);
			}
			break;
		case "DP":
			if (_Import && impVarColl.Keys.Contains(UID + "_DP"))
			{
				realVariable = impVarColl[UID + "_DP"];
				_ImpVarExist = true;
				_ImpKey = UID + "_DP";
				break;
			}
			realVariable = gibbsReactor.PressureDrop;
			if (_Import)
			{
				impVarColl.Add(UID + "_DP", realVariable);
			}
			break;
		}
		return realVariable;
	}

	private RealVariable GetPFRVarGeneral(string UID, string varType)
	{
		RealVariable realVariable = null;
		if (!(_Flowsheet.get_Operations((object)"PFReactorOp")[UID] is PFReactor pFReactor))
		{
			return null;
		}
		switch (varType)
		{
		case "DIAM":
			if (_Import && impVarColl.Keys.Contains(UID + "_DIAM"))
			{
				realVariable = impVarColl[UID + "_DIAM"];
				_ImpVarExist = true;
				_ImpKey = UID + "_DIAM";
				break;
			}
			realVariable = pFReactor.TubeDiameter;
			if (_Import)
			{
				impVarColl.Add(UID + "_DIAM", realVariable);
			}
			break;
		case "DP":
			if (_Import && impVarColl.Keys.Contains(UID + "_DP"))
			{
				realVariable = impVarColl[UID + "_DP"];
				_ImpVarExist = true;
				_ImpKey = UID + "_DP";
				break;
			}
			realVariable = pFReactor.PressureDrop;
			if (_Import)
			{
				impVarColl.Add(UID + "_DP", realVariable);
			}
			break;
		case "DUTY":
			if (_Import && impVarColl.Keys.Contains(UID + "_DUTY"))
			{
				realVariable = impVarColl[UID + "_DUTY"];
				_ImpVarExist = true;
				_ImpKey = UID + "_DUTY";
				break;
			}
			realVariable = pFReactor.HeatFlow;
			if (_Import)
			{
				impVarColl.Add(UID + "_DUTY", realVariable);
			}
			break;
		case "LENG":
			if (_Import && impVarColl.Keys.Contains(UID + "_LENG"))
			{
				realVariable = impVarColl[UID + "_LENG"];
				_ImpVarExist = true;
				_ImpKey = UID + "_LENG";
				break;
			}
			realVariable = pFReactor.TubeLength;
			if (_Import)
			{
				impVarColl.Add(UID + "_LENG", realVariable);
			}
			break;
		case "PRES":
			if (_Import && impVarColl.Keys.Contains(UID + "_PRES"))
			{
				realVariable = impVarColl[UID + "_PRES"];
				_ImpVarExist = true;
				_ImpKey = UID + "_PRES";
				break;
			}
			realVariable = pFReactor.Product.Pressure;
			if (_Import)
			{
				impVarColl.Add(UID + "_PRES", realVariable);
			}
			else if (pFReactor.PressureDrop.Value == 0.0)
			{
				pFReactor.PressureDrop.Erase();
			}
			break;
		case "TEMP":
			if (_Import && impVarColl.Keys.Contains(UID + "_TEMP"))
			{
				realVariable = impVarColl[UID + "_TEMP"];
				_ImpVarExist = true;
				_ImpKey = UID + "_TEMP";
				break;
			}
			realVariable = pFReactor.Product.Temperature;
			if (_Import)
			{
				impVarColl.Add(UID + "_TEMP", realVariable);
			}
			break;
		case "U":
			if (_Import && impVarColl.Keys.Contains(UID + "_U"))
			{
				realVariable = impVarColl[UID + "_U"];
				_ImpVarExist = true;
				_ImpKey = UID + "_U";
				break;
			}
			realVariable = pFReactor.UserHeatTransCoef;
			if (_Import)
			{
				impVarColl.Add(UID + "_U", realVariable);
			}
			break;
		}
		return realVariable;
	}

	private RealVariable GetCSTRVarGeneral(string UID, string varType)
	{
		RealVariable realVariable = null;
		if (!(_Flowsheet.get_Operations((object)"KineticReactorOp")[UID] is KineticReactor kineticReactor))
		{
			return null;
		}
		ProcessStream processStream = null;
		switch (varType)
		{
		case "TEMP":
			processStream = GetProcessStream((string)kineticReactor.AttachedProducts[0]);
			if (_Import && impVarColl.Keys.Contains(processStream.name + "_TEMP"))
			{
				realVariable = impVarColl[processStream.name + "_TEMP"];
				_ImpVarExist = true;
				_ImpKey = processStream.name + "_TEMP";
			}
			else if (processStream != null)
			{
				realVariable = processStream.Temperature;
				if (_Import)
				{
					impVarColl.Add(processStream.name + "_TEMP", realVariable);
				}
			}
			break;
		case "PRES":
			processStream = GetProcessStream((string)kineticReactor.AttachedProducts[0]);
			if (_Import && impVarColl.Keys.Contains(processStream.name + "_PRES"))
			{
				realVariable = impVarColl[processStream.name + "_PRES"];
				_ImpVarExist = true;
				_ImpKey = processStream.name + "_PRES";
			}
			else if (processStream != null)
			{
				realVariable = processStream.Pressure;
				if (_Import)
				{
					impVarColl.Add(processStream.name + "_PRES", realVariable);
				}
				else if (kineticReactor.PressureDrop.Value == 0.0)
				{
					kineticReactor.PressureDrop.Erase();
				}
			}
			break;
		case "DP":
			if (_Import && impVarColl.Keys.Contains(UID + "_DP"))
			{
				realVariable = impVarColl[UID + "_DP"];
				_ImpVarExist = true;
				_ImpKey = UID + "_DP";
				break;
			}
			realVariable = kineticReactor.PressureDrop;
			if (_Import)
			{
				impVarColl.Add(UID + "_DP", realVariable);
			}
			break;
		case "DUTY":
			if (_Import && impVarColl.Keys.Contains(UID + "_DUTY"))
			{
				realVariable = impVarColl[UID + "_DUTY"];
				_ImpVarExist = true;
				_ImpKey = UID + "_DUTY";
				break;
			}
			realVariable = kineticReactor.HeatFlow;
			if (_Import)
			{
				impVarColl.Add(UID + "_DUTY", realVariable);
			}
			break;
		case "VOLU":
			if (_Import && impVarColl.Keys.Contains(UID + "_VOLU"))
			{
				realVariable = impVarColl[UID + "_VOLU"];
				_ImpVarExist = true;
				_ImpKey = UID + "_VOLU";
				break;
			}
			realVariable = kineticReactor.Volume;
			if (_Import)
			{
				impVarColl.Add(UID + "_VOLU", realVariable);
			}
			break;
		}
		return realVariable;
	}

	private RealVariable GetComponentsplitterVarGeneral(string UID, string varType)
	{
		RealVariable result = null;
		if (!(_Flowsheet.get_Operations((object)"FractOpObject")[UID] is FractOp fractOp))
		{
			return null;
		}
		ProcessStream processStream = null;
		string text = varType;
		string text2 = string.Empty;
		if (varType.Contains("("))
		{
			text = varType.Substring(0, varType.IndexOf("("));
			text2 = ParseUtilty.GetParentheses(varType);
		}
		if (text.Length > 4)
		{
			text = text.Substring(0, 4);
		}
		if (text2.Contains(","))
		{
			text2 = text2.Substring(0, text2.IndexOf(","));
		}
		if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(text2) && !text.ToUpper().Trim().StartsWith("DUTY"))
		{
			return null;
		}
		if (text2 == "3" || text2.ToUpper().Trim().StartsWith("PROD"))
		{
			return null;
		}
		if (text2 == "2" || text2.ToUpper().Trim().StartsWith("BTMS"))
		{
			text2 = ((HYSYSComponentSplitter)_Data).BTMStreams[0];
		}
		else if (text2 == "1" || text2.ToUpper().Trim().StartsWith("OVHD"))
		{
			text2 = ((HYSYSComponentSplitter)_Data).OVHDStreams[0];
		}
		switch (text)
		{
		case "TEMP":
			processStream = GetProcessStream(text2);
			if (processStream != null)
			{
				result = processStream.Temperature;
			}
			break;
		case "PRES":
			processStream = GetProcessStream(text2);
			if (processStream != null)
			{
				result = processStream.Pressure;
			}
			break;
		case "DUTY":
			result = ((ProcessStream)fractOp.EnergyInlets[0]).HeatFlow;
			break;
		}
		return result;
	}

	private BackDoor GetFlashBD(string UID)
	{
		BackDoor result = null;
		foreach (HYSYSSeparator hysysSeparator in _HyTree._PFDObject.HysysSeparators)
		{
			if (hysysSeparator.UID == UID)
			{
				Separator separator = null;
				if (hysysSeparator.SeparatorType == HYSYSSeparatorType.Separator)
				{
					separator = _Flowsheet.get_Operations((object)"FlashTankObject")[UID] as Separator;
				}
				else if (hysysSeparator.SeparatorType == HYSYSSeparatorType.ThreePhaseSep)
				{
					separator = _Flowsheet.get_Operations((object)"Sep3OpObject")[UID] as Separator;
				}
				result = separator as BackDoor;
				return result;
			}
		}
		foreach (HYSYSHeatExchanger hYSYSHeatExchange in _HyTree._PFDObject.HYSYSHeatExchanges)
		{
			if (hYSYSHeatExchange.UID == UID)
			{
				result = (_Flowsheet.get_Operations((object)"HeaterOpObject")[UID] as HeaterOp) as BackDoor;
				return result;
			}
		}
		foreach (HYSYSValve hYSYSValf in _HyTree._PFDObject.HYSYSValves)
		{
			if (hYSYSValf.UID == UID)
			{
				return (_Flowsheet.get_Operations((object)"ValveOpObject")[UID] as Valve) as BackDoor;
			}
		}
		return result;
	}

	private BackDoor GetHXBD(string UID)
	{
		BackDoor result = null;
		foreach (HYSYSHeatExchanger hYSYSHeatExchange in _HyTree._PFDObject.HYSYSHeatExchanges)
		{
			if (hYSYSHeatExchange.UID == UID)
			{
				if (hYSYSHeatExchange.ExchangerType == HysysHeatExchangerType.Cooler)
				{
					result = (_Flowsheet.get_Operations((object)"CoolerOpObject")[UID] as CoolerOp) as BackDoor;
				}
				else if (hYSYSHeatExchange.ExchangerType == HysysHeatExchangerType.Heater)
				{
					result = (_Flowsheet.get_Operations((object)"HeaterOpObject")[UID] as HeaterOp) as BackDoor;
				}
				else if (hYSYSHeatExchange.ExchangerType == HysysHeatExchangerType.HeatExchanger)
				{
					result = (_Flowsheet.get_Operations((object)"HeatExOpObject")[UID] as HeatExchanger) as BackDoor;
				}
				break;
			}
		}
		return result;
	}

	private RealVariable GetImportVariable(string UID, string sourceType, string varName, string flowbasis, bool bFirst)
	{
		_Import = true;
		RealVariable result = null;
		if ((!sourceType.ToUpper().Trim().StartsWith("COLU") || !varName.ToUpper().Trim().StartsWith("DUTY")) && (!sourceType.ToUpper().Trim().StartsWith("STCA") || varName.ToUpper().Trim().StartsWith("DUTY")) && !varName.Contains("=") && !varName.Contains(","))
		{
			if (varName.Contains("("))
			{
				varName = varName.Substring(0, varName.IndexOf('('));
			}
			if (varName.Length > 4)
			{
				varName = varName.Substring(0, 4);
			}
		}
		if (sourceType.ToUpper().Trim().StartsWith("FLAS"))
		{
			if (varName.ToUpper().Trim().StartsWith("PRES"))
			{
				result = GetFlashPressureVar(UID);
			}
			else if (varName.ToUpper().Trim().StartsWith("TEMP"))
			{
				result = GetFlashTempVar(UID);
			}
			else if (varName.ToUpper().Trim().StartsWith("DP"))
			{
				result = GetFlashDPVar(UID);
			}
			else if (varName.ToUpper().Trim().StartsWith("DUTY"))
			{
				result = GetFlashDutyVar(UID);
			}
		}
		else if (sourceType.ToUpper().Trim().StartsWith("MIXE"))
		{
			result = GetMixerVarGeneral(UID, varName.ToUpper().Trim());
		}
		else if (sourceType.ToUpper().Trim().StartsWith("STRE") || sourceType.ToUpper().Trim().StartsWith("STRM"))
		{
			if (varName.StartsWith("RATE"))
			{
				if (bFirst)
				{
					if (_Def.FirstRefCStartComp != 0)
					{
						_AddSpecialVar = true;
						return null;
					}
					result = GetStreamVarGeneral(UID, varName.ToUpper().Trim(), flowbasis);
				}
				else
				{
					if (_Def.SecondRefCStartComp != 0)
					{
						_AddSpecialVar = true;
						return null;
					}
					result = GetStreamVarGeneral(UID, varName.ToUpper().Trim(), flowbasis);
				}
			}
			else
			{
				result = GetStreamVarGeneral(UID, varName.ToUpper().Trim(), flowbasis);
			}
		}
		else if (sourceType.ToUpper().Trim().StartsWith("PUMP"))
		{
			result = GetPumpVarGeneral(UID, varName.ToUpper().Trim());
		}
		else if (sourceType.ToUpper().Trim().StartsWith("COLU"))
		{
			result = GetColumnVarGeneral(UID, varName.ToUpper().Trim(), flowbasis, bImport: true);
		}
		else if (sourceType.ToUpper().Trim().StartsWith("PIPE"))
		{
			result = GetPipeVarGeneral(UID, varName.ToUpper().Trim());
		}
		else if (sourceType.ToUpper().Trim().StartsWith("LNG"))
		{
			result = GetLNGVarGeneral(UID, varName.ToUpper().Trim());
		}
		else if (sourceType.ToUpper().Trim().StartsWith("HXRI"))
		{
			result = GetHXRIGVarGeneral(UID, varName.ToUpper().Trim());
		}
		else if (sourceType.ToUpper().Trim().StartsWith("HX"))
		{
			result = GetHXVarGeneral(UID, varName.ToUpper().Trim());
		}
		else if (sourceType.ToUpper().Trim().StartsWith("VALV"))
		{
			result = GetValveVarGeneral(UID, varName.ToUpper().Trim());
		}
		else if (sourceType.ToUpper().Trim().StartsWith("SPLI"))
		{
			result = GetSplitterVarGeneral(UID, varName.ToUpper().Trim());
		}
		else if (sourceType.ToUpper().Trim().StartsWith("COMP"))
		{
			result = GetCompressorVarGeneral(UID, varName.ToUpper().Trim());
		}
		else if (sourceType.ToUpper().Trim().StartsWith("EXPA"))
		{
			result = GetExpanderVarGeneral(UID, varName.ToUpper().Trim());
		}
		else if (sourceType.ToUpper().Trim().StartsWith("EQUR"))
		{
			result = GetEquilReactorVarGeneral(UID, varName.ToUpper().Trim());
		}
		else if (sourceType.ToUpper().Trim().StartsWith("CONR"))
		{
			result = GetConvReactorVarGeneral(UID, varName.ToUpper().Trim());
		}
		else if (sourceType.ToUpper().Trim().StartsWith("GIBB"))
		{
			result = GetGibbsReactorVarGeneral(UID, varName.ToUpper().Trim());
		}
		else if (sourceType.ToUpper().Trim().StartsWith("PLUG"))
		{
			result = GetPFRVarGeneral(UID, varName.ToUpper().Trim());
		}
		else if (sourceType.ToUpper().Trim().StartsWith("CSTR"))
		{
			result = GetCSTRVarGeneral(UID, varName.ToUpper().Trim());
		}
		else if (sourceType.ToUpper().Trim().StartsWith("STCA"))
		{
			result = GetComponentsplitterVarGeneral(UID, varName.ToUpper().Trim());
		}
		_Import = false;
		return result;
	}

	private RealVariable GetExportvariable(string UID, string sourceType, string varName, string flowbasis)
	{
		_Import = false;
		RealVariable result = null;
		if ((!sourceType.ToUpper().Trim().StartsWith("COLU") || !varName.ToUpper().Trim().StartsWith("DUTY")) && (!sourceType.ToUpper().Trim().StartsWith("STCA") || varName.ToUpper().Trim().StartsWith("DUTY")) && !varName.Contains("=") && !varName.Contains(","))
		{
			if (varName.Contains("("))
			{
				varName = varName.Substring(0, varName.IndexOf('('));
			}
			if (varName.Length > 4)
			{
				varName = varName.Substring(0, 4);
			}
		}
		if (sourceType.ToUpper().Trim().StartsWith("FLAS"))
		{
			if (varName.ToUpper().Trim().StartsWith("TEMP"))
			{
				result = GetFlashTempVar(UID);
			}
			else if (varName.ToUpper().Trim().StartsWith("PRES"))
			{
				result = GetFlashPressureVar(UID);
			}
			else if (varName.ToUpper().Trim().StartsWith("DP"))
			{
				result = GetFlashPressureVar(UID);
			}
			else if (varName.ToUpper().Trim().StartsWith("DUTY"))
			{
				result = GetFlashDutyVar(UID);
			}
		}
		else if (sourceType.ToUpper().Trim().StartsWith("MIXE"))
		{
			result = GetMixerVarGeneral(UID, varName.ToUpper().Trim());
		}
		else if (sourceType.ToUpper().Trim().StartsWith("STRE"))
		{
			result = GetStreamVarGeneral(UID, varName.ToUpper().Trim(), flowbasis);
		}
		else if (sourceType.ToUpper().Trim().StartsWith("PUMP"))
		{
			result = GetPumpVarGeneral(UID, varName.ToUpper().Trim());
		}
		else if (sourceType.ToUpper().Trim().StartsWith("COLU"))
		{
			result = GetColumnVarGeneral(UID, varName.ToUpper().Trim(), flowbasis, bImport: false);
		}
		else if (sourceType.ToUpper().Trim().StartsWith("PIPE"))
		{
			result = GetPipeVarGeneral(UID, varName.ToUpper().Trim());
		}
		else if (sourceType.ToUpper().Trim().StartsWith("LNG"))
		{
			result = GetLNGVarGeneral(UID, varName.ToUpper().Trim());
		}
		else if (sourceType.ToUpper().Trim().StartsWith("EXCH"))
		{
			result = GetTwoSideHeatExchangerVar(UID, varName.ToUpper().Trim());
		}
		else if (sourceType.ToUpper().Trim().StartsWith("HEAT"))
		{
			result = GetHeaterVar(UID, varName.ToUpper().Trim());
		}
		else if (sourceType.ToUpper().Trim().StartsWith("COOL"))
		{
			result = GetCoolerVar(UID, varName.ToUpper().Trim());
		}
		else if (sourceType.ToUpper().Trim().StartsWith("VALV"))
		{
			result = GetValveVarGeneral(UID, varName.ToUpper().Trim());
		}
		else if (sourceType.ToUpper().Trim().StartsWith("SPLI"))
		{
			result = GetSplitterVarGeneral(UID, varName.ToUpper().Trim());
		}
		else if (sourceType.ToUpper().Trim().StartsWith("COMP"))
		{
			result = GetCompressorVarGeneral(UID, varName.ToUpper().Trim());
		}
		else if (sourceType.ToUpper().Trim().StartsWith("EXPA"))
		{
			result = GetExpanderVarGeneral(UID, varName.ToUpper().Trim());
		}
		else if (sourceType.ToUpper().Trim().StartsWith("EQUR"))
		{
			result = GetEquilReactorVarGeneral(UID, varName.ToUpper().Trim());
		}
		else if (sourceType.ToUpper().Trim().StartsWith("CONR"))
		{
			result = GetConvReactorVarGeneral(UID, varName.ToUpper().Trim());
		}
		else if (sourceType.ToUpper().Trim().StartsWith("GIBB"))
		{
			result = GetGibbsReactorVarGeneral(UID, varName.ToUpper().Trim());
		}
		else if (sourceType.ToUpper().Trim().StartsWith("PLUG"))
		{
			result = GetPFRVarGeneral(UID, varName.ToUpper().Trim());
		}
		else if (sourceType.ToUpper().Trim().StartsWith("CSTR"))
		{
			result = GetCSTRVarGeneral(UID, varName.ToUpper().Trim());
		}
		else if (sourceType.ToUpper().Trim().StartsWith("STCA"))
		{
			result = GetComponentsplitterVarGeneral(UID, varName.ToUpper().Trim());
		}
		return result;
	}

	private BackDoor GetSourceBD(string UID, string sourceType)
	{
		BackDoor result = null;
		if (sourceType.ToUpper().Trim().StartsWith("FLAS"))
		{
			result = GetFlashBD(UID);
		}
		else if (sourceType.ToUpper().Trim().StartsWith("MIXE"))
		{
			Valve valve = FindValveNextToMixer(UID);
			if (valve != null)
			{
				result = (valve.AttachedProducts[0] as ProcessStream) as BackDoor;
			}
		}
		else if (sourceType.ToUpper().Trim().StartsWith("STRE"))
		{
			result = GetProcessStream(UID) as BackDoor;
		}
		else if (sourceType.ToUpper().Trim().StartsWith("PUMP"))
		{
			result = (_Flowsheet.get_Operations((object)"PumpOpObject")[UID] as PumpOp) as BackDoor;
		}
		else if (sourceType.ToUpper().Trim().StartsWith("COLU"))
		{
			result = (_Flowsheet.get_Operations((object)"ColumnOp")[UID] as ColumnOp) as BackDoor;
		}
		else if (sourceType.ToUpper().Trim().StartsWith("PIPE"))
		{
			result = (_Flowsheet.get_Operations((object)"PipeSegObject")[UID] as PipeSegment) as BackDoor;
		}
		else if (sourceType.ToUpper().Trim().StartsWith("LNG"))
		{
			result = (_Flowsheet.get_Operations((object)"LngOpObject")[UID] as LngOp) as BackDoor;
		}
		else if (sourceType.ToUpper().Trim().StartsWith("HXRI"))
		{
			result = (_Flowsheet.get_Operations((object)"HeatExOpObject")[UID] as HeatExchanger) as BackDoor;
		}
		else if (sourceType.ToUpper().Trim().StartsWith("HX"))
		{
			result = GetHXBD(UID);
		}
		else if (sourceType.ToUpper().Trim().StartsWith("VALV"))
		{
			result = (_Flowsheet.get_Operations((object)"ValveOpObject")[UID] as Valve) as BackDoor;
		}
		else if (sourceType.ToUpper().Trim().StartsWith("SPLI"))
		{
			result = (_Flowsheet.get_Operations((object)"TeeOpObject")[UID] as TeeOp) as BackDoor;
		}
		else if (sourceType.ToUpper().Trim().StartsWith("COMP"))
		{
			result = (_Flowsheet.get_Operations((object)"CompressorObject")[UID] as CompressOp) as BackDoor;
		}
		else if (sourceType.ToUpper().Trim().StartsWith("EXPA"))
		{
			result = (_Flowsheet.get_Operations((object)"ExpandOp")[UID] as ExpandOp) as BackDoor;
		}
		else if (sourceType.ToUpper().Trim().StartsWith("EQUR"))
		{
			result = (_Flowsheet.get_Operations((object)"EquilibriumReactorOp")[UID] as EquilibriumReactor) as BackDoor;
		}
		else if (sourceType.ToUpper().Trim().StartsWith("CONR"))
		{
			result = (_Flowsheet.get_Operations((object)"ConversionReactorOp")[UID] as ConversionReactor) as BackDoor;
		}
		else if (sourceType.ToUpper().Trim().StartsWith("GIBB"))
		{
			result = (_Flowsheet.get_Operations((object)"GibbsReactorOp")[UID] as GibbsReactor) as BackDoor;
		}
		else if (sourceType.ToUpper().Trim().StartsWith("PLUG"))
		{
			result = (_Flowsheet.get_Operations((object)"PFReactorOp")[UID] as PFReactor) as BackDoor;
		}
		else if (sourceType.ToUpper().Trim().StartsWith("CSTR"))
		{
			result = (_Flowsheet.get_Operations((object)"KineticReactorOp")[UID] as KineticReactor) as BackDoor;
		}
		else if (sourceType.ToUpper().Trim().StartsWith("STCA"))
		{
			result = (_Flowsheet.get_Operations((object)"FractOpObject")[UID] as FractOp) as BackDoor;
		}
		return result;
	}

	private void AddValveToCorrectMixerOutPressure(double mulitiVal, string defProp, string operName)
	{
		BackDoor backDoor = (_Flowsheet.get_Operations((object)"MixerOpObject")[_MainUID] as MixerOp) as BackDoor;
		BackDoor backDoor2 = null;
		string streamName = NameHelper.CreateName(bIsStream: true);
		backDoor2 = GetProcessStream(streamName) as BackDoor;
		BackDoor obj = (_Flowsheet.get_Operations((object)"ValveOpObject").Add("V_" + _MainUID, "ValveOpObject") as Valve) as BackDoor;
		backDoor.SendAttachObject(":Stream.500", backDoor2);
		obj.SendAttachObject(":MaterialStream.500", backDoor2);
		backDoor2 = GetProcessStream(_Data.OutletStreams[0].Trim()) as BackDoor;
		obj.SendAttachObject(":MaterialStream.501", backDoor2);
		((HYSYSMixer)_Data).OutletValveAdded = true;
		AddPropertyDefineInSpreadsheet(mulitiVal, defProp, operName, "");
	}

	private void AddValveToCorrectSplitterOutPressure(double mulitiVal, string defProp, string operName)
	{
		BackDoor backDoor = (_Flowsheet.get_Operations((object)"TeeOpObject")[_MainUID] as TeeOp) as BackDoor;
		BackDoor backDoor2 = null;
		string streamName = NameHelper.CreateName(bIsStream: true);
		backDoor2 = GetProcessStream(streamName) as BackDoor;
		BackDoor obj = (_Flowsheet.get_Operations((object)"ValveOpObject").Add("V_" + _MainUID, "ValveOpObject") as Valve) as BackDoor;
		backDoor.SendAttachObject(":MaterialStream.500", backDoor2);
		obj.SendAttachObject(":MaterialStream.501", backDoor2);
		backDoor2 = GetProcessStream(_Data.InletStreams[0].Trim()) as BackDoor;
		obj.SendAttachObject(":MaterialStream.500", backDoor2);
		((HYSYSSplitter)_Data).InletValveAdded = true;
		AddPropertyDefineInSpreadsheet(mulitiVal, defProp, operName, "");
	}

	private bool bSameHYSYSExchanger()
	{
		bool result = false;
		if (_Def.FirstRefType.ToUpper().Trim().StartsWith("FLAS"))
		{
			foreach (HYSYSHeatExchanger hYSYSHeatExchange in _HyTree._PFDObject.HYSYSHeatExchanges)
			{
				if (hYSYSHeatExchange.UID == _Def.FirstRefID)
				{
					result = true;
					break;
				}
			}
		}
		else if (_Def.FirstRefType.ToUpper().Trim().StartsWith("HX"))
		{
			foreach (HYSYSHeatExchanger hYSYSHeatExchange2 in _HyTree._PFDObject.HYSYSHeatExchanges)
			{
				if (hYSYSHeatExchange2.UID == _Def.FirstRefID && hYSYSHeatExchange2.ExchangerType != HysysHeatExchangerType.HeatExchanger)
				{
					result = true;
					break;
				}
			}
		}
		return result;
	}

	private bool bUseSet(string defProp)
	{
		if (_MainType.StartsWith("COL") && defProp.StartsWith("PTOP"))
		{
			return false;
		}
		if (_MainType.StartsWith("PIPE") && ((HYSYSPipe)_Data).SEGMENTSCOUNT > 1)
		{
			return false;
		}
		if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(_Def.SecondRefID) && !string.IsNullOrEmpty(_Def.SecondRefProperty))
		{
			return false;
		}
		bool result = false;
		bool flag = false;
		if (_Def.FirstRefType.ToUpper().Trim() == _MainType)
		{
			if (_MainType == "FLAS")
			{
				bool flag2 = false;
				bool flag3 = false;
				foreach (HYSYSSeparator hysysSeparator in _HyTree._PFDObject.HysysSeparators)
				{
					if (hysysSeparator.UID == _MainUID)
					{
						flag2 = true;
					}
					else if (hysysSeparator.UID == _Def.FirstRefID)
					{
						flag3 = true;
					}
				}
				if (flag2 && flag3)
				{
					flag = true;
				}
			}
			else
			{
				flag = true;
			}
		}
		else
		{
			switch (_MainType)
			{
			case "MIXE":
				if (_Def.FirstRefType.ToUpper().Trim().StartsWith("STRE"))
				{
					flag = true;
				}
				break;
			case "COOL":
				result = bSameHYSYSExchanger();
				break;
			case "HEAT":
				result = bSameHYSYSExchanger();
				break;
			case "VALV":
				if (!_Def.FirstRefType.ToUpper().Trim().StartsWith("FLAS"))
				{
					break;
				}
				foreach (HYSYSValve hYSYSValf in _HyTree._PFDObject.HYSYSValves)
				{
					if (hYSYSValf.UID == _Def.FirstRefID)
					{
						flag = true;
						break;
					}
				}
				break;
			}
		}
		if (flag && string.IsNullOrEmpty(_Def.SecondRefID))
		{
			result = true;
		}
		return result;
	}

	private void AddPropertyDefineInSpreadsheet(double mulitiVal, string defProp, string operName, string flowbasis)
	{
		if (bUseSet(defProp))
		{
			HandlePropRefToSameObjWithSet(mulitiVal, defProp, flowbasis);
			return;
		}
		Dictionary<string, RealVariable> dictionary = new Dictionary<string, RealVariable>();
		Dictionary<string, RealVariable> dictionary2 = new Dictionary<string, RealVariable>();
		dictionary = GetImportCells(_Def.FirstRefID, _Def.FirstRefType, _Def.FirstRefProperty, flowbasis, bFirst: true);
		if (_AddSpecialVar)
		{
			_AddSpecialVar = false;
		}
		if (dictionary == null || dictionary.Count == 0)
		{
			return;
		}
		if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(_Def.SecondRefID))
		{
			dictionary2 = GetImportCells(_Def.SecondRefID, _Def.SecondRefType, _Def.SecondRefProperty, flowbasis, bFirst: false);
			if (_AddSpecialVar)
			{
				_AddSpecialVar = false;
			}
			if (dictionary2 == null || dictionary2.Count == 0)
			{
				return;
			}
		}
		RealVariable expVar = null;
		string exportCell = GetExportCell(ref expVar, flowbasis);
		if (_AddSpecialVar)
		{
			_AddSpecialVar = false;
		}
		if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(exportCell))
		{
			return;
		}
		SpreadsheetCell spreadsheetCell = _GlobalDefine.get_Cell((object)exportCell, (object)"");
		if (expVar != null)
		{
			spreadsheetCell.ExportedVariable = expVar;
		}
		bool flag = true;
		if (_Def.MainProperty.ToUpper().Trim().StartsWith("HOCI"))
		{
			string empty = string.Empty;
			if (_ImpVarExist)
			{
				empty = impCellCol[_ImpKey];
			}
			else
			{
				empty = GetSpreadsheetCellName();
				impCellCol.Add(_ImpKey, empty);
			}
			SpreadsheetCell spreadsheetCell2 = _GlobalDefine.get_Cell((object)empty, (object)"");
			HeatExchanger heatExchanger = _Flowsheet.get_Operations((object)"HeatExOpObject")[_MainUID] as HeatExchanger;
			spreadsheetCell2.ImportedVariable = heatExchanger.TubeSideFeed.Temperature;
			spreadsheetCell.CellText = "=" + spreadsheetCell2;
			flag = false;
		}
		else if (_Def.MainProperty.ToUpper().Trim().StartsWith("HOCO"))
		{
			string empty2 = string.Empty;
			if (_ImpVarExist)
			{
				empty2 = impCellCol[_ImpKey];
			}
			else
			{
				empty2 = GetSpreadsheetCellName();
				impCellCol.Add(_ImpKey, empty2);
			}
			SpreadsheetCell spreadsheetCell3 = _GlobalDefine.get_Cell((object)empty2, (object)"");
			HeatExchanger heatExchanger2 = _Flowsheet.get_Operations((object)"HeatExOpObject")[_MainUID] as HeatExchanger;
			spreadsheetCell3.ImportedVariable = heatExchanger2.TubeSideProduct.Temperature;
			spreadsheetCell.CellText = "=" + spreadsheetCell3;
			flag = false;
		}
		else if (_Def.MainProperty.ToUpper().Trim().StartsWith("HICO"))
		{
			string empty3 = string.Empty;
			if (_ImpVarExist)
			{
				empty3 = impCellCol[_ImpKey];
			}
			else
			{
				empty3 = GetSpreadsheetCellName();
				impCellCol.Add(_ImpKey, empty3);
			}
			SpreadsheetCell spreadsheetCell4 = _GlobalDefine.get_Cell((object)empty3, (object)"");
			HeatExchanger heatExchanger3 = _Flowsheet.get_Operations((object)"HeatExOpObject")[_MainUID] as HeatExchanger;
			spreadsheetCell4.ImportedVariable = heatExchanger3.ShellSideFeed.Temperature;
			spreadsheetCell.CellText = "=" + spreadsheetCell4;
			flag = false;
		}
		else
		{
			spreadsheetCell.CellText = "=";
		}
		bool flag2 = false;
		if (dictionary.Count > 1)
		{
			flag2 = true;
		}
		if (flag2)
		{
			spreadsheetCell.CellText += "(";
		}
		foreach (string key in dictionary.Keys)
		{
			SpreadsheetCell spreadsheetCell5 = _GlobalDefine.get_Cell((object)key, (object)"");
			if (dictionary[key] != null)
			{
				spreadsheetCell5.ImportedVariable = dictionary[key];
			}
			if (key.StartsWith("M_"))
			{
				spreadsheetCell.CellText = spreadsheetCell.CellText + "-" + key.Remove(0, 2);
			}
			else if (!flag)
			{
				spreadsheetCell.CellText = spreadsheetCell.CellText + "+" + key;
			}
			else
			{
				spreadsheetCell.CellText += key;
			}
			flag = false;
		}
		if (flag2)
		{
			spreadsheetCell.CellText += ")";
		}
		if (dictionary2.Count > 0)
		{
			spreadsheetCell.CellText += operName;
			if (dictionary2.Count <= 1)
			{
				flag2 = false;
			}
			if (flag2)
			{
				spreadsheetCell.CellText += "(";
			}
			flag = true;
			foreach (string key2 in dictionary2.Keys)
			{
				SpreadsheetCell spreadsheetCell6 = _GlobalDefine.get_Cell((object)key2, (object)"");
				if (dictionary2[key2] != null)
				{
					spreadsheetCell6.ImportedVariable = dictionary2[key2];
				}
				if (key2.StartsWith("M_"))
				{
					spreadsheetCell.CellText = spreadsheetCell.CellText + "-" + key2.Remove(0, 2);
				}
				else if (!flag)
				{
					spreadsheetCell.CellText = spreadsheetCell.CellText + "+" + key2;
				}
				else
				{
					spreadsheetCell.CellText += key2;
				}
				flag = false;
			}
			if (flag2)
			{
				spreadsheetCell.CellText += ")";
			}
		}
		else if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(operName))
		{
			double value = Convert.ToDouble(_Def.SecondRefProperty);
			string text = string.Empty;
			if (_Def.FirstRefProperty.Contains('('))
			{
				text = ParseUtilty.GetParentheses(_Def.FirstRefProperty);
			}
			if (text.Contains(","))
			{
				text = ParseUtilty.CutByComma(text)[1];
			}
			if (operName == "+" || operName == "-")
			{
				DoubleValue specValue = GetSpecValue(_Def.FirstRefProperty, value, text, flowbasis);
				spreadsheetCell.CellText = spreadsheetCell.CellText + operName + specValue.Value;
			}
			else
			{
				spreadsheetCell.CellText = spreadsheetCell.CellText + operName + value;
			}
		}
		if (_MainType.StartsWith("COL") && defProp.StartsWith("PTOP"))
		{
			HandleColumnPTOPDefineWithSpreadsheet(spreadsheetCell.CellText, flowbasis);
		}
		if (_MainType.StartsWith("PIPE") && ((HYSYSPipe)_Data).SEGMENTSCOUNT > 1)
		{
			HandlePipeSpecialDefineWithSpreadsheet(defProp, spreadsheetCell.CellText);
		}
		_Def.bConverted = true;
	}

	private string GetExportCell(ref RealVariable expVar, string flowbasis)
	{
		string empty = string.Empty;
		expVar = GetExportvariable(_MainUID, _MainType, _Def.MainProperty, flowbasis);
		if (expVar == null)
		{
			if (!_AddSpecialVar)
			{
				return string.Empty;
			}
			if (_Def.MainProperty.ToUpper().Trim().StartsWith("HOCI"))
			{
				empty = GetSpreadsheetCellName();
				string uID = ((HYSYSHeatExchanger)_Data).HotSideOutletStreams[0];
				expVar = GetStreamVarGeneral(uID, "TEMP", "");
			}
			else if (_Def.MainProperty.ToUpper().Trim().StartsWith("HOCO"))
			{
				empty = GetSpreadsheetCellName();
				string uID2 = ((HYSYSHeatExchanger)_Data).HotSideOutletStreams[0];
				expVar = GetStreamVarGeneral(uID2, "TEMP", "");
			}
			else if (_Def.MainProperty.ToUpper().Trim().StartsWith("HICO"))
			{
				empty = GetSpreadsheetCellName();
				string uID3 = ((HYSYSHeatExchanger)_Data).ColdSideOutletStreams[0];
				expVar = GetStreamVarGeneral(uID3, "TEMP", "");
			}
			else
			{
				if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(_Def.sColumnSpecName))
				{
					return string.Empty;
				}
				empty = GetSpreadsheetCellName();
				BackDoor backDoor = (_Flowsheet.get_Operations((object)"ColumnOp")[_MainUID] as ColumnOp) as BackDoor;
				expVar = backDoor.get_BackDoorVariable((object)_Def.sColumnSpecName).Variable as RealVariable;
			}
		}
		else
		{
			empty = GetSpreadsheetCellName();
		}
		return empty;
	}

	private Dictionary<string, RealVariable> GetImportCells(string UID, string sourceType, string varName, string flowbasis, bool bFirst)
	{
		_Import = true;
		Dictionary<string, RealVariable> dictionary = new Dictionary<string, RealVariable>();
		RealVariable importVariable = GetImportVariable(UID, sourceType, varName, flowbasis, bFirst);
		if (importVariable == null)
		{
			if (!_AddSpecialVar)
			{
				return null;
			}
			InitializeSpreadsheet();
			_ = _GlobalDefine;
			if (sourceType.StartsWith("STR"))
			{
				BackDoor backDoor = GetProcessStream(UID) as BackDoor;
				if (varName.StartsWith("RATE"))
				{
					if (bFirst)
					{
						for (int i = _Def.FirstRefCStartComp; i <= _Def.FirstRefCEndComp; i++)
						{
							_ = string.Empty;
							P2Objects.P2Component.GetHYSYSCompName(i);
							int num = i;
							RealVariable value = backDoor.get_BackDoorVariable((object)(":CompMoleFlow.6666." + num)).Variable as RealVariable;
							string spreadsheetCellName = GetSpreadsheetCellName();
							dictionary.Add(spreadsheetCellName, value);
						}
					}
					else
					{
						for (int j = _Def.SecondRefCStartComp; j <= _Def.SecondRefCEndComp; j++)
						{
							_ = string.Empty;
							P2Objects.P2Component.GetHYSYSCompName(j);
							int num2 = j;
							RealVariable value2 = backDoor.get_BackDoorVariable((object)(":CompMoleFlow.6666." + num2)).Variable as RealVariable;
							string spreadsheetCellName2 = GetSpreadsheetCellName();
							dictionary.Add(spreadsheetCellName2, value2);
						}
					}
				}
			}
			else if (sourceType.StartsWith("HX"))
			{
				if (varName.StartsWith("HOCI"))
				{
					ProcessStream processStream = null;
					RealVariable realVariable = null;
					if (!(_Flowsheet.get_Operations((object)"HeatExOpObject")[UID] is HeatExchanger { ShellSideProduct: var shellSideProduct } heatExchanger))
					{
						return null;
					}
					if (shellSideProduct == null)
					{
						return null;
					}
					string empty = string.Empty;
					if (impCellCol.Keys.Contains(shellSideProduct.name + "_TEMP"))
					{
						empty = impCellCol[shellSideProduct.name + "_TEMP"];
						realVariable = impVarColl[shellSideProduct.name + "_TEMP"];
					}
					else
					{
						empty = GetSpreadsheetCellName();
						realVariable = shellSideProduct.Temperature;
						impCellCol.Add(shellSideProduct.name + "_TEMP", empty);
						impVarColl.Add(shellSideProduct.name + "_TEMP", realVariable);
					}
					if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(empty))
					{
						return null;
					}
					dictionary.Add(empty, realVariable);
					processStream = heatExchanger.TubeSideFeed;
					if (processStream == null)
					{
						return null;
					}
					empty = string.Empty;
					if (impCellCol.Keys.Contains(processStream.name + "_TEMP"))
					{
						empty = impCellCol[processStream.name + "_TEMP"];
						realVariable = impVarColl[processStream.name + "_TEMP"];
					}
					else
					{
						empty = GetSpreadsheetCellName();
						realVariable = processStream.Temperature;
						impCellCol.Add(processStream.name + "_TEMP", empty);
						impVarColl.Add(processStream.name + "_TEMP", realVariable);
					}
					if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(empty))
					{
						return null;
					}
					dictionary.Add("Minus_" + empty, realVariable);
				}
				else if (varName.StartsWith("HOCO"))
				{
					ProcessStream processStream2 = null;
					RealVariable realVariable2 = null;
					if (!(_Flowsheet.get_Operations((object)"HeatExOpObject")[UID] is HeatExchanger { ShellSideProduct: var shellSideProduct2 } heatExchanger2))
					{
						return null;
					}
					if (shellSideProduct2 == null)
					{
						return null;
					}
					string empty2 = string.Empty;
					if (impCellCol.Keys.Contains(shellSideProduct2.name + "_TEMP"))
					{
						empty2 = impCellCol[shellSideProduct2.name + "_TEMP"];
						realVariable2 = impVarColl[shellSideProduct2.name + "_TEMP"];
					}
					else
					{
						empty2 = GetSpreadsheetCellName();
						realVariable2 = shellSideProduct2.Temperature;
						impCellCol.Add(shellSideProduct2.name + "_TEMP", empty2);
						impVarColl.Add(shellSideProduct2.name + "_TEMP", realVariable2);
					}
					if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(empty2))
					{
						return null;
					}
					dictionary.Add(empty2, realVariable2);
					processStream2 = heatExchanger2.TubeSideProduct;
					if (processStream2 == null)
					{
						return null;
					}
					empty2 = string.Empty;
					if (impCellCol.Keys.Contains(processStream2.name + "_TEMP"))
					{
						empty2 = impCellCol[processStream2.name + "_TEMP"];
						realVariable2 = impVarColl[processStream2.name + "_TEMP"];
					}
					else
					{
						empty2 = GetSpreadsheetCellName();
						realVariable2 = processStream2.Temperature;
						impCellCol.Add(processStream2.name + "_TEMP", empty2);
						impVarColl.Add(processStream2.name + "_TEMP", realVariable2);
					}
					if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(empty2))
					{
						return null;
					}
					dictionary.Add("Minus_" + empty2, realVariable2);
				}
				else if (varName.StartsWith("HICO"))
				{
					ProcessStream processStream3 = null;
					RealVariable realVariable3 = null;
					if (!(_Flowsheet.get_Operations((object)"HeatExOpObject")[UID] is HeatExchanger { ShellSideFeed: var shellSideFeed } heatExchanger3))
					{
						return null;
					}
					if (shellSideFeed == null)
					{
						return null;
					}
					string empty3 = string.Empty;
					if (impCellCol.Keys.Contains(shellSideFeed.name + "_TEMP"))
					{
						empty3 = impCellCol[shellSideFeed.name + "_TEMP"];
						realVariable3 = impVarColl[shellSideFeed.name + "_TEMP"];
					}
					else
					{
						empty3 = GetSpreadsheetCellName();
						realVariable3 = shellSideFeed.Temperature;
						impCellCol.Add(shellSideFeed.name + "_TEMP", empty3);
						impVarColl.Add(shellSideFeed.name + "_TEMP", realVariable3);
					}
					if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(empty3))
					{
						return null;
					}
					dictionary.Add(empty3, realVariable3);
					processStream3 = heatExchanger3.TubeSideProduct;
					if (processStream3 == null)
					{
						return null;
					}
					empty3 = string.Empty;
					if (impCellCol.Keys.Contains(processStream3.name + "_TEMP"))
					{
						empty3 = impCellCol[processStream3.name + "_TEMP"];
						realVariable3 = impVarColl[processStream3.name + "_TEMP"];
					}
					else
					{
						empty3 = GetSpreadsheetCellName();
						realVariable3 = processStream3.Temperature;
						impCellCol.Add(processStream3.name + "_TEMP", empty3);
						impVarColl.Add(processStream3.name + "_TEMP", realVariable3);
					}
					if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(empty3))
					{
						return null;
					}
					dictionary.Add("M_" + empty3, realVariable3);
				}
			}
			else if (sourceType.StartsWith("DPCO"))
			{
				RealVariable realVariable4 = null;
				string importCN = string.Empty;
				realVariable4 = GetImpColumnPTopVar(ref importCN, UID);
				if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(importCN))
				{
					return null;
				}
				dictionary.Add("M_" + importCN, realVariable4);
				importCN = string.Empty;
				realVariable4 = GetImpColumnPBottomVar(ref importCN, UID);
				if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(importCN))
				{
					return null;
				}
				dictionary.Add(importCN, realVariable4);
			}
		}
		else
		{
			InitializeSpreadsheet();
			string spreadsheetCellName3 = GetSpreadsheetCellName();
			dictionary.Add(spreadsheetCellName3, importVariable);
		}
		_Import = false;
		_ImpVarExist = false;
		return dictionary;
	}

	private void HandleColumnPTOPDefineWithSpreadsheet(string sText, string flowbasis)
	{
		string spreadsheetCellName = GetSpreadsheetCellName();
		SpreadsheetCell spreadsheetCell = _GlobalDefine.get_Cell((object)spreadsheetCellName, (object)"");
		if (_Flowsheet.get_Operations((object)"ColumnOp")[_MainUID] is ColumnOp columnOp)
		{
			BackDoor backDoor = columnOp as BackDoor;
			int num = ((HYSYSColumn)_Data).stageNumber;
			if (((HYSYSColumn)_Data).bCondenserExist)
			{
				num--;
			}
			if (((HYSYSColumn)_Data).bReboilerExist)
			{
				num--;
			}
			string moniker = "FlowSht.600/ColumnStage.500." + num + ":Pressure.300";
			spreadsheetCell.ExportedVariable = backDoor.get_BackDoorVariable((object)moniker).Variable as RealVariable;
			double value = ((HYSYSColumn)_Data).ColumnPressureDrop.Value;
			string unit = ((HYSYSColumn)_Data).ColumnPressureDrop.Unit;
			DoubleValue specValue = GetSpecValue("DP", value, unit, flowbasis);
			spreadsheetCell.CellText = sText + "+" + specValue.Value;
		}
	}

	private void HandlePipeSpecialDefineWithSpreadsheet(string defProp, string sText)
	{
		int sEGMENTSCOUNT = ((HYSYSPipe)_Data).SEGMENTSCOUNT;
		BackDoor backDoor = (_Flowsheet.get_Operations((object)"PipeSegObject")[_MainUID] as PipeSegment) as BackDoor;
		string empty = string.Empty;
		if (defProp.StartsWith("DIAM"))
		{
			for (int i = 1; i < sEGMENTSCOUNT; i++)
			{
				string spreadsheetCellName = GetSpreadsheetCellName();
				SpreadsheetCell spreadsheetCell = _GlobalDefine.get_Cell((object)spreadsheetCellName, (object)"");
				empty = ":SmallLength.550." + i;
				RealVariable exportedVariable = backDoor.get_BackDoorVariable((object)empty).Variable as RealVariable;
				spreadsheetCell.ExportedVariable = exportedVariable;
				spreadsheetCell.CellText = sText;
			}
		}
		else if (defProp.StartsWith("LENG"))
		{
			for (int j = 1; j < sEGMENTSCOUNT; j++)
			{
				string spreadsheetCellName2 = GetSpreadsheetCellName();
				SpreadsheetCell spreadsheetCell2 = _GlobalDefine.get_Cell((object)spreadsheetCellName2, (object)"");
				empty = ":Length.551." + j;
				RealVariable exportedVariable2 = backDoor.get_BackDoorVariable((object)empty).Variable as RealVariable;
				spreadsheetCell2.ExportedVariable = exportedVariable2;
				spreadsheetCell2.CellText = sText;
			}
		}
		else if (defProp.StartsWith("AROU"))
		{
			for (int k = 1; k < sEGMENTSCOUNT; k++)
			{
				string spreadsheetCellName3 = GetSpreadsheetCellName();
				SpreadsheetCell spreadsheetCell3 = _GlobalDefine.get_Cell((object)spreadsheetCellName3, (object)"");
				empty = ":Length.552." + k;
				RealVariable exportedVariable3 = backDoor.get_BackDoorVariable((object)empty).Variable as RealVariable;
				spreadsheetCell3.ExportedVariable = exportedVariable3;
				spreadsheetCell3.CellText = sText;
			}
		}
	}

	private RealVariable GetImpColumnPTopVar(ref string importCN, string UID)
	{
		RealVariable realVariable = null;
		HYSYSColumn columnData = GetColumnData(UID);
		if (columnData == null)
		{
			return null;
		}
		if (!(_Flowsheet.get_Operations((object)"ColumnOp")[UID] is ColumnOp columnOp))
		{
			return null;
		}
		BackDoor backDoor = columnOp as BackDoor;
		if (impCellCol.Keys.Contains(UID + "_PTOP"))
		{
			importCN = impCellCol[UID + "_PTOP"];
			realVariable = impVarColl[UID + "_PTOP"];
		}
		else
		{
			importCN = GetSpreadsheetCellName();
			realVariable = ((!columnData.bCondenserExist) ? (backDoor.get_BackDoorVariable((object)"FlowSht.600/ColumnStage.500.0:Pressure.300").Variable as RealVariable) : (backDoor.get_BackDoorVariable((object)"FlowSht.600/ColumnStage.500.1:Pressure.300").Variable as RealVariable));
			impCellCol.Add(UID + "_PTOP", importCN);
			impVarColl.Add(UID + "_PTOP", realVariable);
		}
		return realVariable;
	}

	private RealVariable GetImpColumnPBottomVar(ref string importCN, string UID)
	{
		RealVariable realVariable = null;
		if (GetColumnData(UID) == null)
		{
			return null;
		}
		if (!(_Flowsheet.get_Operations((object)"ColumnOp")[UID] is ColumnOp columnOp))
		{
			return null;
		}
		BackDoor backDoor = columnOp as BackDoor;
		if (impCellCol.Keys.Contains(UID + "_DPCO"))
		{
			importCN = impCellCol[UID + "_DPCO"];
			realVariable = impVarColl[UID + "_DPCO"];
		}
		else
		{
			importCN = GetSpreadsheetCellName();
			int num = ((HYSYSColumn)_Data).stageNumber;
			if (((HYSYSColumn)_Data).bCondenserExist)
			{
				num--;
			}
			if (((HYSYSColumn)_Data).bReboilerExist)
			{
				num--;
			}
			string moniker = "FlowSht.600/ColumnStage.500." + num + ":Pressure.300";
			realVariable = backDoor.get_BackDoorVariable((object)moniker).Variable as RealVariable;
			impCellCol.Add(UID + "_DPCO", importCN);
			impVarColl.Add(UID + "_DPCO", realVariable);
		}
		return realVariable;
	}

	private DoubleValue GetSpecValue(string sProp, double value, string sUnit, string flowbasis)
	{
		if (sProp.Contains("("))
		{
			sProp = sProp.Substring(0, sProp.IndexOf('('));
		}
		if (sProp.Length > 4)
		{
			sProp = sProp.Substring(0, 4);
		}
		if (sProp == "PTOP")
		{
			sProp = "PRES";
		}
		DoubleValue result = null;
		string empty = string.Empty;
		switch (sProp)
		{
		case "PRES":
			result = UnitConverter.GetHysysValue(UnitClassEnum.DropPressure, value, sUnit);
			empty = GetHysysDisplayUnit("DP", flowbasis);
			result = HYSYSUOMMap.GetConvertedValue(UnitClassEnum.DropPressure, result, empty);
			break;
		case "DP":
			result = UnitConverter.GetHysysValue(UnitClassEnum.DropPressure, value, sUnit);
			empty = GetHysysDisplayUnit(sProp, flowbasis);
			result = HYSYSUOMMap.GetConvertedValue(UnitClassEnum.DropPressure, result, empty);
			break;
		case "TEMP":
			result = UnitConverter.GetHysysValue(UnitClassEnum.DropTemperature, value, sUnit);
			empty = GetHysysDisplayUnit(sProp, flowbasis);
			result = HYSYSUOMMap.GetConvertedValue(UnitClassEnum.DropTemperature, result, empty);
			break;
		case "DUTY":
			result = UnitConverter.GetHysysValue(UnitClassEnum.Duty, value, sUnit);
			empty = GetHysysDisplayUnit(sProp, flowbasis);
			result = HYSYSUOMMap.GetConvertedValue(UnitClassEnum.Duty, result, empty);
			break;
		}
		return result;
	}

	private void HandlePropRefToSameObjWithSet(double mulitiVal, string defProp, string flowbasis)
	{
		SetOp setOp = _Flowsheet.get_Operations((object)"SetOpObject").Add("Set_" + _Data.UID + "_DP", "SetOpObject") as SetOp;
		setOp.TargetVariable = GetExportvariable(_MainUID, _MainType, defProp, flowbasis);
		BackDoor obj = setOp as BackDoor;
		BackDoor sourceBD = GetSourceBD(_Def.FirstRefID, _Def.FirstRefType);
		obj.SendAttachObject(":FlowSheetObject.501", sourceBD);
		setOp.MultiplierValue = mulitiVal;
		if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(_Def.OperatorName))
		{
			double valueIn = Convert.ToDouble(_Def.SecondRefProperty);
			string unitKey = string.Empty;
			if (_Def.FirstRefProperty.Contains('('))
			{
				unitKey = ParseUtilty.GetParentheses(_Def.FirstRefProperty);
			}
			DoubleValue hysysValue = UnitConverter.GetHysysValue(UnitClassEnum.DropPressure, valueIn, unitKey);
			setOp.Offset.SetValue(hysysValue.Value * -1.0, hysysValue.Unit);
		}
		_Def.bConverted = true;
	}

	private string GetSpreadsheetCellName()
	{
		_ = string.Empty;
		if (_RowNumber < 20)
		{
			_RowNumber++;
		}
		else
		{
			_RowNumber = 1;
			switch (_ColumnName)
			{
			case "A":
				_ColumnName = "B";
				break;
			case "B":
				_ColumnName = "C";
				break;
			case "C":
				_ColumnName = "D";
				break;
			case "D":
				_ColumnName = "E";
				break;
			}
		}
		return _ColumnName + _RowNumber;
	}

	private void SetEquipParamGeneral()
	{
		string text = _Def.MainProperty.ToUpper().Trim();
		string flowbasis = "M";
		if (text.Contains('('))
		{
			text = text.Substring(0, text.IndexOf('('));
		}
		string text2 = string.Empty;
		if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(_Def.OperatorName))
		{
			text2 = getOperator(_Def.OperatorName.ToUpper().Trim());
		}
		if (_Def.bLinkToCalculator)
		{
			return;
		}
		if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(text2))
		{
			AddPropertyDefineInSpreadsheet(1.0, text, string.Empty, flowbasis);
			return;
		}
		switch (text2)
		{
		case "+":
			AddPropertyDefineInSpreadsheet(1.0, text, text2, flowbasis);
			break;
		case "-":
			AddPropertyDefineInSpreadsheet(1.0, text, text2, flowbasis);
			break;
		case "*":
			if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(_Def.SecondRefID) && !string.IsNullOrEmpty(_Def.SecondRefProperty))
			{
				double mulitiVal = Convert.ToDouble(_Def.SecondRefProperty);
				AddPropertyDefineInSpreadsheet(mulitiVal, text, text2, flowbasis);
			}
			break;
		case "/":
			if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(_Def.SecondRefID) && !string.IsNullOrEmpty(_Def.SecondRefProperty))
			{
				double num = Convert.ToDouble(_Def.SecondRefProperty);
				num = 1.0 / num;
				AddPropertyDefineInSpreadsheet(num, text, text2, flowbasis);
			}
			break;
		}
	}

	public void SetMixerParam()
	{
		string text = _Def.MainProperty.ToUpper().Trim();
		if (text.Contains('('))
		{
			text = text.Substring(0, text.IndexOf('('));
		}
		string text2 = string.Empty;
		if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(_Def.OperatorName))
		{
			text2 = getOperator(_Def.OperatorName.ToUpper().Trim());
		}
		if (_Def.bLinkToCalculator)
		{
			return;
		}
		if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(text2))
		{
			AddValveToCorrectMixerOutPressure(1.0, text, string.Empty);
			return;
		}
		switch (text2)
		{
		case "+":
			AddValveToCorrectMixerOutPressure(1.0, text, text2);
			((HYSYSMixer)_Data).OutletValveAdded = true;
			break;
		case "-":
			AddValveToCorrectMixerOutPressure(1.0, text, text2);
			((HYSYSMixer)_Data).OutletValveAdded = true;
			break;
		case "*":
			if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(_Def.SecondRefID) && !string.IsNullOrEmpty(_Def.SecondRefProperty))
			{
				double mulitiVal = Convert.ToDouble(_Def.SecondRefProperty);
				AddValveToCorrectMixerOutPressure(mulitiVal, text, text2);
				((HYSYSMixer)_Data).OutletValveAdded = true;
			}
			break;
		case "/":
			if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(_Def.SecondRefID) && !string.IsNullOrEmpty(_Def.SecondRefProperty))
			{
				double num = Convert.ToDouble(_Def.SecondRefProperty);
				num = 1.0 / num;
				AddValveToCorrectMixerOutPressure(num, text, text2);
				((HYSYSMixer)_Data).OutletValveAdded = true;
			}
			break;
		}
	}

	public void SetFlashParam()
	{
		SetEquipParamGeneral();
	}

	{

	}

	}

	public void SetSplitterParam()
	{
		string text = _Def.MainProperty.ToUpper().Trim();
		if (text.Contains('('))
		{
			text = text.Substring(0, text.IndexOf('('));
		}
		string text2 = string.Empty;
		if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(_Def.OperatorName))
		{
			text2 = getOperator(_Def.OperatorName.ToUpper().Trim());
		}
		if (_Def.bLinkToCalculator)
		{
			return;
		}
		if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(text2))
		{
			AddValveToCorrectSplitterOutPressure(1.0, text, string.Empty);
			return;
		}
		switch (text2)
		{
		case "+":
			AddValveToCorrectSplitterOutPressure(1.0, text, text2);
			((HYSYSSplitter)_Data).InletValveAdded = true;
			break;
		case "-":
			AddValveToCorrectSplitterOutPressure(1.0, text, text2);
			((HYSYSSplitter)_Data).InletValveAdded = true;
			break;
		case "*":
			if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(_Def.SecondRefID) && !string.IsNullOrEmpty(_Def.SecondRefProperty))
			{
				double mulitiVal = Convert.ToDouble(_Def.SecondRefProperty);
				AddValveToCorrectSplitterOutPressure(mulitiVal, text, text2);
				((HYSYSSplitter)_Data).InletValveAdded = true;
			}
			break;
		case "/":
			if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(_Def.SecondRefID) && !string.IsNullOrEmpty(_Def.SecondRefProperty))
			{
				double num = Convert.ToDouble(_Def.SecondRefProperty);
				num = 1.0 / num;
				AddValveToCorrectSplitterOutPressure(num, text, text2);
				((HYSYSSplitter)_Data).InletValveAdded = true;
			}
			break;
		}
	}
