internal class HYSYSGibbsReactorConverter : HYSYSObjectConverterBase, IDisposable
{
	private GibbsReactor _GibbsReactor;

	private HYSYSGibbsReactor _Data;

	public void Dispose()
	{
	}

	public HYSYSGibbsReactorConverter(SimulationCase simulationCase, HYSYSGibbsReactor data)
		: base(simulationCase)
	{
		_Data = data;
		try
		{
			_GibbsReactor = _Flowsheet.get_Operations((object)"GibbsReactorOp").Add(data.UID, "GibbsReactorOp") as GibbsReactor;
			CreateGibbsReactor();
		}
		catch (Exception)
		{
		}
	}

	private void CreateGibbsReactor()
	{
		BackDoor backDoor = null;
		ProcessStream processStream = null;
		ProcessStream processStream2 = null;
		ProcessStream processStream3 = null;
		ProcessStream[] array = null;
		ProcessStream processStream4 = null;
		BackDoor backDoor2 = null;
		InternalRealVariable internalRealVariable = null;
		BackDoor backDoor3 = null;
		BackDoor backDoor4 = null;
		try
		{
			backDoor = _GibbsReactor as BackDoor;
			if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(_Data.FluidPackage))
			{
				try
				{
					string methodName = HYSYSFluidPackages.GetMethodName(_Data.FluidPackage);
					if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(methodName))
					{
						backDoor4 = _SimCase.BasisManager.FluidPackages[methodName] as BackDoor;
						backDoor.SendAttachObject(":CompList.300", backDoor4);
					}
				}
				catch (Exception)
				{
					DebugPrint.OutputDebugString(DebugLevel.BuildHYSYSCase, WarningLevel.Error, string.Format(P2Globalization.GetString("GibbsRConverter_SetFPFailed"), _GibbsReactor.name));
				}
			}
			array = new ProcessStream[_Data.InletStreams.Count];
			try
			{
				(backDoor.get_BackDoorVariable((object)":Selection.501.99").Variable as InternalRealVariable).Value = 1.0;
			}
			catch (Exception)
			{
				DebugPrint.OutputDebugString(DebugLevel.BuildHYSYSCase, WarningLevel.Error, string.Format(P2Globalization.GetString("GibbsRConverter_ConvertTo3PhaseFailed"), _GibbsReactor.name));
			}
			try
			{
				for (int i = 0; i < _Data.InletStreams.Count; i++)
				{
					array[i] = GetProcessStream(_Data.InletStreams[i].Trim());
					backDoor3 = array[i] as BackDoor;
					backDoor.SendAttachObject(":MaterialStream.502." + i, backDoor3);
				}
			}
			catch (Exception)
			{
				DebugPrint.OutputDebugString(DebugLevel.BuildHYSYSCase, WarningLevel.Error, string.Format(P2Globalization.GetString("GibbsRConverter_SetFeedStreamsFailed"), _GibbsReactor.name));
			}
			try
			{
				processStream = GetProcessStream(_Data.V.Trim());
				backDoor3 = processStream as BackDoor;
				backDoor.SendAttachObject(":MaterialStream.501", backDoor3);
			}
			catch (Exception)
			{
				DebugPrint.OutputDebugString(DebugLevel.BuildHYSYSCase, WarningLevel.Error, string.Format(P2Globalization.GetString("GibbsRConverter_SetVFailed"), _GibbsReactor.name));
			}
			try
			{
				processStream2 = GetProcessStream(_Data.L.Trim());
				backDoor3 = processStream2 as BackDoor;
				backDoor.SendAttachObject(":MaterialStream.500", backDoor3);
			}
			catch (Exception)
			{
				DebugPrint.OutputDebugString(DebugLevel.BuildHYSYSCase, WarningLevel.Error, string.Format(P2Globalization.GetString("GibbsRConverter_SetLFailed"), _GibbsReactor.name));
			}
			try
			{
				processStream3 = GetProcessStream(_Data.W.Trim());
				backDoor3 = processStream3 as BackDoor;
				((_Flowsheet as BackDoor).get_BackDoorVariable((object)"SolverObject.400:Attribute.300").Variable as InternalRealVariable).Value = 1.0;
				backDoor.SendAttachObject(":MaterialStream.610", backDoor3);
			}
			catch (Exception)
			{
				DebugPrint.OutputDebugString(DebugLevel.BuildHYSYSCase, WarningLevel.Error, string.Format(P2Globalization.GetString("GibbsRConverter_SetWFailed"), _GibbsReactor.name));
			}
			try
			{
				string name = NameHelper.CreateEnergyStreamName();
				processStream4 = _Flowsheet.EnergyStreams.Add(name, "") as ProcessStream;
				backDoor2 = processStream4 as BackDoor;
				backDoor.SendAttachObject(":EnergyStream.503", backDoor2);
			}
			catch (Exception)
			{
				DebugPrint.OutputDebugString(DebugLevel.BuildHYSYSCase, WarningLevel.Error, string.Format(P2Globalization.GetString("GibbsRConverter_SetEnergyStreamFailed"), _GibbsReactor.name));
			}
			try
			{
				if (_Data.AttachedTemperature != null)
				{
					processStream.Temperature.SetValue(_Data.AttachedTemperature.Value, _Data.AttachedTemperature.Unit);
				}
				else if (_Data.AttachedTemperature == null && !_Data.IsAdiabatic && array[0].Temperature != null)
				{
					processStream.Temperature.SetValue(array[0].TemperatureValue, "C");
				}
			}
			catch (Exception)
			{
				DebugPrint.OutputDebugString(DebugLevel.BuildHYSYSCase, WarningLevel.Error, string.Format(P2Globalization.GetString("GibbsRConverter_SetTemperatureFailed"), _GibbsReactor.name));
			}
			if (_Data.AttachedPressure != null)
			{
				try
				{
					internalRealVariable = backDoor.get_BackDoorVariable((object)":PressureDrop.500").Variable as InternalRealVariable;
					internalRealVariable.SetValue(-32767.0, "");
					processStream.Pressure.SetValue(_Data.AttachedPressure.Value, _Data.AttachedPressure.Unit);
				}
				catch (Exception)
				{
					DebugPrint.OutputDebugString(DebugLevel.BuildHYSYSCase, WarningLevel.Error, string.Format(P2Globalization.GetString("GibbsRConverter_SetPressureFailed"), _GibbsReactor.name));
				}
			}
			if (_Data.DP != null)
			{
				try
				{
					internalRealVariable = backDoor.get_BackDoorVariable((object)":PressureDrop.500").Variable as InternalRealVariable;
					internalRealVariable.SetValue(_Data.DP.Value, _Data.DP.Unit);
				}
				catch (Exception)
				{
					DebugPrint.OutputDebugString(DebugLevel.BuildHYSYSCase, WarningLevel.Error, string.Format(P2Globalization.GetString("GibbsRConverter_SetDPFailed"), _GibbsReactor.name));
				}
			}
			if (_Data.Duty != null)
			{
				try
				{
					internalRealVariable = backDoor2.get_BackDoorVariable((object)":HeatFlow.501.0").Variable as InternalRealVariable;
					internalRealVariable.SetValue(_Data.Duty.Value, _Data.Duty.Unit);
				}
				catch (Exception)
				{
					DebugPrint.OutputDebugString(DebugLevel.BuildHYSYSCase, WarningLevel.Error, string.Format(P2Globalization.GetString("GibbsRConverter_SetDutyFailed"), _GibbsReactor.name));
				}
			}
			if (_Data.MaxIteration != 0)
			{
				try
				{
					internalRealVariable = backDoor.get_BackDoorVariable((object)":Count.700.0").Variable as InternalRealVariable;
					internalRealVariable.Value = _Data.MaxIteration;
				}
				catch (Exception)
				{
					DebugPrint.OutputDebugString(DebugLevel.BuildHYSYSCase, WarningLevel.Error, string.Format(P2Globalization.GetString("GibbsRConverter_SetMaxIterationFailed"), _GibbsReactor.name));
				}
			}
			if (_Data.Tolerance != 0.0)
			{
				try
				{
					internalRealVariable = backDoor.get_BackDoorVariable((object)":Error.700.0").Variable as InternalRealVariable;
					internalRealVariable.Value = _Data.Tolerance;
				}
				catch (Exception)
				{
					DebugPrint.OutputDebugString(DebugLevel.BuildHYSYSCase, WarningLevel.Error, string.Format(P2Globalization.GetString("GibbsRConverter_SetToleranceFailed"), _GibbsReactor.name));
				}
			}
			if (_Data.InertComponents != null)
			{
				try
				{
					for (int j = 0; j < _Data.InertComponents.Length; j++)
					{
						internalRealVariable = backDoor.get_BackDoorVariable((object)(":Boolean.609." + (_Data.InertComponents[j] - 1))).Variable as InternalRealVariable;
						internalRealVariable.Value = 1.0;
					}
				}
				catch (Exception)
				{
					DebugPrint.OutputDebugString(DebugLevel.BuildHYSYSCase, WarningLevel.Error, string.Format(P2Globalization.GetString("GibbsRConverter_SetInertComponentFailed"), _GibbsReactor.name));
				}
			}
			if (_Data.CompFixedSpec.Count != 0)
			{
				try
				{
					foreach (int key in _Data.CompFixedSpec.Keys)
					{
						internalRealVariable = backDoor.get_BackDoorVariable((object)(":MolarFlow.608." + (key - 1))).Variable as InternalRealVariable;
						internalRealVariable.SetValue(_Data.CompFixedSpec[key].Value, _Data.CompFixedSpec[key].Unit);
					}
				}
				catch (Exception)
				{
					DebugPrint.OutputDebugString(DebugLevel.BuildHYSYSCase, WarningLevel.Error, string.Format(P2Globalization.GetString("GibbsRConverter_SetFixedSpecFailed"), _GibbsReactor.name));
				}
			}
			if (_Data.CompFracSpec.Count != 0)
			{
				try
				{
					foreach (int key2 in _Data.CompFracSpec.Keys)
					{
						internalRealVariable = backDoor.get_BackDoorVariable((object)(":ExtraData.608." + (key2 - 1))).Variable as InternalRealVariable;
						internalRealVariable.Value = _Data.CompFracSpec[key2];
					}
				}
				catch (Exception)
				{
					DebugPrint.OutputDebugString(DebugLevel.BuildHYSYSCase, WarningLevel.Error, string.Format(P2Globalization.GetString("GibbsRConverter_SetFracSpecFailed"), _GibbsReactor.name));
				}
			}
			if (_Data.ReactionSet != null)
			{
				try
				{
					internalRealVariable = backDoor.get_BackDoorVariable((object)":Selection.601").Variable as InternalRealVariable;
					internalRealVariable.Value = 2.0;
					backDoor.SendAttachObject2(":RxnSet.400", "SimCase.200/FluidPkgMgr.300/RxnPackageManager.300/RxnSet.300(" + _Data.ReactionSet.ID + ")");
				}
				catch (Exception)
				{
					DebugPrint.OutputDebugString(DebugLevel.BuildHYSYSCase, WarningLevel.Error, string.Format(P2Globalization.GetString("GibbsRConverter_SetRXSetFailed"), _GibbsReactor.name));
				}
			}
			try
			{
				InternalTextVariable internalTextVariable = backDoor.get_BackDoorTextVariable((object)":Notes.400").Variable as InternalTextVariable;
				foreach (string convertNote in _Data.ConvertNotes)
				{
					internalTextVariable.Value = internalTextVariable.Value + convertNote + "\n";
				}
			}
			catch (Exception)
			{
				DebugPrint.OutputDebugString(DebugLevel.BuildHYSYSCase, WarningLevel.Error, string.Format(P2Globalization.GetString("GibbsRConverter_SetNotesFailed"), _GibbsReactor.name));
			}
		}
		catch (COMException)
		{
		}
		finally
		{
			CleanResource.ReleaseComObjectHelper(backDoor, bFinalRelease: false);
			CleanResource.ReleaseComObjectHelper(processStream, bFinalRelease: false);
			CleanResource.ReleaseComObjectHelper(processStream2, bFinalRelease: false);
			CleanResource.ReleaseComObjectHelper(processStream3, bFinalRelease: false);
			for (int k = 0; k < array.Length; k++)
			{
				CleanResource.ReleaseComObjectHelper(array[k], bFinalRelease: false);
			}
			CleanResource.ReleaseComObjectHelper(processStream4, bFinalRelease: false);
			CleanResource.ReleaseComObjectHelper(backDoor2, bFinalRelease: false);
			CleanResource.ReleaseComObjectHelper(backDoor3, bFinalRelease: false);
			CleanResource.ReleaseComObjectHelper(internalRealVariable, bFinalRelease: false);
			CleanResource.ReleaseComObjectHelper(backDoor4, bFinalRelease: false);
		}
	}
}
public class HYSYSHeatExchangerConverter : HYSYSObjectConverterBase, IDisposable
{
	private HYSYSHeatExchanger _Data;

	public HYSYSHeatExchangerConverter(SimulationCase simulationCase, HYSYSHeatExchanger data, bool bEDR)
		: base(simulationCase)
	{
		_Data = data;
		if (_Data.ExchangerType == HysysHeatExchangerType.Cooler)
		{
			CreateCooler();
		}
		else if (_Data.ExchangerType == HysysHeatExchangerType.Heater)
		{
			CreateHeater();
		}
		else if (_Data.ExchangerType == HysysHeatExchangerType.HeatExchanger)
		{
			if (_Data.MapForHXRIG)
			{
				InternalRealVariable obj = (_Flowsheet as BackDoor).get_BackDoorVariable((object)"SolverObject.400:Attribute.300").Variable as InternalRealVariable;
				obj.Value = 1.0;
				CreateHeatExchangerForHXRIG(bEDR);
				obj.Value = 0.0;
			}
			else
			{
				CreateHeatExchangerForHX();
			}
		}
	}

	private void CreateCooler()
	{
		CoolerOp coolerOp = null;
		ProcessStream processStream = null;
		BackDoor backDoor = null;
		BackDoor backDoor2 = null;
		ProcessStream processStream2 = null;
		BackDoor backDoor3 = null;
		try
		{
			coolerOp = _Flowsheet.get_Operations((object)"CoolerOpObject").Add(_Data.UID, "CoolerOpObject") as CoolerOp;
			backDoor = coolerOp as BackDoor;
			if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(_Data.HotSideMethod))
			{
				string methodName = HYSYSFluidPackages.GetMethodName(_Data.HotSideMethod);
				if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(methodName))
				{
					BackDoor backDoor4 = _SimCase.BasisManager.FluidPackages[methodName] as BackDoor;
					backDoor.SendAttachObject("ExchSide.500.0:CompList.300", backDoor4);
					CleanResource.ReleaseComObjectHelper(backDoor4, bFinalRelease: false);
				}
			}
			AddHCurveSetting(backDoor, HysysHeatExchangerType.Cooler);
			if (_Data.HotSideInletStreams != null && _Data.HotSideInletStreams.Count > 0)
			{
				processStream = GetProcessStream(_Data.HotSideInletStreams[0].Trim());
				backDoor2 = processStream as BackDoor;
				backDoor.SendAttachObject(":MaterialStream.400", backDoor2);
			}
			if (_Data.HotSideOutletStreams != null && _Data.HotSideOutletStreams.Count > 0)
			{
				processStream = GetProcessStream(_Data.HotSideOutletStreams[0].Trim());
				backDoor2 = processStream as BackDoor;
				backDoor.SendAttachObject(":MaterialStream.401", backDoor2);
				if (_Data.HotSideOutletTemperature != null)
				{
					processStream.Temperature.SetValue(_Data.HotSideOutletTemperature.Value, _Data.HotSideOutletTemperature.Unit);
				}
				else if (_Data.HLFRAC != null)
				{
					processStream.VapourFractionValue = 1.0 - _Data.HLFRAC.Value;
				}
			}
			string name = NameHelper.CreateEnergyStreamName();
			processStream2 = _Flowsheet.EnergyStreams.Add(name, "") as ProcessStream;
			backDoor3 = processStream2 as BackDoor;
			InternalRealVariable internalRealVariable = backDoor3.get_BackDoorVariable((object)":Enum.905").Variable as InternalRealVariable;
			if (_Data.UTILITYTYPE == UtilityType.Steam)
			{
				internalRealVariable.Value = 0.0;
			}
			else if (_Data.UTILITYTYPE == UtilityType.Water)
			{
				internalRealVariable.Value = 11.0;
			}
			else if (_Data.UTILITYTYPE == UtilityType.Air)
			{
				internalRealVariable.Value = 12.0;
			}
			else if (_Data.UTILITYTYPE == UtilityType.HeatingMedium)
			{
				internalRealVariable.Value = 3.0;
			}
			else if (_Data.UTILITYTYPE == UtilityType.Refrigerant)
			{
				internalRealVariable.Value = 7.0;
			}
			CleanResource.ReleaseComObjectHelper(internalRealVariable, bFinalRelease: false);
			backDoor.SendAttachObject(":EnergyStream.300", backDoor3);
			if (_Data.Duty != null)
			{
				InternalRealVariable obj = backDoor.get_BackDoorVariable((object)":HeatFlow.510").Variable as InternalRealVariable;
				obj.SetValue(_Data.Duty.Value, _Data.Duty.Unit);
				CleanResource.ReleaseComObjectHelper(obj, bFinalRelease: false);
			}
			else if (_Data.HDTAD != null)
			{
				SetDeltaTOperation("Oper_Set_" + _Data.UID, bHeater: false, _Data.HotSideInletStreams[0].Trim(), _Data.HotSideOutletStreams[0].Trim(), 1.0, _Data.HDTAD);
			}
			else if (_Data.HDTBB != null)
			{
				SetDeltaTOperation("Oper_Set_" + _Data.UID, bHeater: false, _Data.HotSideInletStreams[0].Trim(), _Data.HotSideOutletStreams[0].Trim(), 0.0, new DoubleValue(_Data.HDTBB.Unit, _Data.HDTBB.Value * -1.0));
			}
			bool bHSDPSetByDef = false;
			bool bCSDPSetByDef = false;
			AddLocalDefineForHX(backDoor, ref bCSDPSetByDef, ref bHSDPSetByDef);
			if (!bHSDPSetByDef)
			{
				AddHXHotOrColdSideDP(backDoor, ":PressureDrop.511", _Data.HotSideDeltaPressure);
			}
			AddUnconvertNotes(backDoor);
		}
		catch (COMException)
		{
			DebugPrint.OutputDebugString(DebugLevel.BuildHYSYSCase, WarningLevel.Error, string.Format(P2Globalization.GetString("CreateHysysCoolerForHXFailed"), _Data.UID));
		}
		catch (Exception)
		{
			DebugPrint.OutputDebugString(DebugLevel.BuildHYSYSCase, WarningLevel.Error, string.Format(P2Globalization.GetString("CreateHysysCoolerForHXFailed"), _Data.UID));
		}
		finally
		{
			CleanResource.ReleaseComObjectHelper(coolerOp, bFinalRelease: false);
			CleanResource.ReleaseComObjectHelper(processStream, bFinalRelease: false);
			CleanResource.ReleaseComObjectHelper(backDoor, bFinalRelease: false);
			CleanResource.ReleaseComObjectHelper(backDoor2, bFinalRelease: false);
			CleanResource.ReleaseComObjectHelper(processStream2, bFinalRelease: false);
			CleanResource.ReleaseComObjectHelper(backDoor3, bFinalRelease: false);
		}
	}

	private void CreateHeater()
	{
		HeaterOp heaterOp = null;
		ProcessStream processStream = null;
		BackDoor backDoor = null;
		BackDoor backDoor2 = null;
		ProcessStream processStream2 = null;
		BackDoor backDoor3 = null;
		bool flag = false;
		try
		{
			heaterOp = _Flowsheet.get_Operations((object)"HeaterOpObject").Add(_Data.UID, "HeaterOpObject") as HeaterOp;
			backDoor = heaterOp as BackDoor;
			if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(_Data.ColdSideMethod))
			{
				string methodName = HYSYSFluidPackages.GetMethodName(_Data.ColdSideMethod);
				if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(methodName))
				{
					BackDoor backDoor4 = _SimCase.BasisManager.FluidPackages[methodName] as BackDoor;
					backDoor.SendAttachObject("ExchSide.500.0:CompList.300", backDoor4);
					CleanResource.ReleaseComObjectHelper(backDoor4, bFinalRelease: false);
				}
			}
			AddHCurveSetting(backDoor, HysysHeatExchangerType.Heater);
			if (_Data.ColdSideInletStreams != null)
			{
				processStream = GetProcessStream(_Data.ColdSideInletStreams[0].Trim());
				backDoor2 = processStream as BackDoor;
				backDoor.SendAttachObject(":MaterialStream.400", backDoor2);
			}
			if (_Data.ColdSideOutletStreams != null)
			{
				processStream = GetProcessStream(_Data.ColdSideOutletStreams[0].Trim());
				if (processStream.Pressure.Value > 0.0)
				{
					flag = true;
				}
				backDoor2 = processStream as BackDoor;
				backDoor.SendAttachObject(":MaterialStream.401", backDoor2);
				int num = 0;
				if (_Data.ColdSideOutletTemperature != null)
				{
					processStream.Temperature.SetValue(_Data.ColdSideOutletTemperature.Value, _Data.ColdSideOutletTemperature.Unit);
					num++;
				}
				if (_Data.CLFRAC != null)
				{
					processStream.VapourFractionValue = 1.0 - _Data.CLFRAC.Value;
					num++;
				}
				if (_Data.ColdSideOutletPressure != null)
				{
					processStream.Pressure.SetValue(_Data.ColdSideOutletPressure.Value, _Data.ColdSideOutletPressure.Unit);
					flag = true;
				}
				if (num == 2)
				{
					flag = true;
				}
			}
			string name = NameHelper.CreateEnergyStreamName();
			processStream2 = _Flowsheet.EnergyStreams.Add(name, "") as ProcessStream;
			backDoor3 = processStream2 as BackDoor;
			InternalRealVariable internalRealVariable = backDoor3.get_BackDoorVariable((object)":Enum.905").Variable as InternalRealVariable;
			if (_Data.UTILITYTYPE == UtilityType.Steam)
			{
				internalRealVariable.Value = 0.0;
			}
			else if (_Data.UTILITYTYPE == UtilityType.Water)
			{
				internalRealVariable.Value = 11.0;
			}
			else if (_Data.UTILITYTYPE == UtilityType.Air)
			{
				internalRealVariable.Value = 12.0;
			}
			else if (_Data.UTILITYTYPE == UtilityType.HeatingMedium)
			{
				internalRealVariable.Value = 3.0;
			}
			else if (_Data.UTILITYTYPE == UtilityType.Refrigerant)
			{
				internalRealVariable.Value = 7.0;
			}
			CleanResource.ReleaseComObjectHelper(internalRealVariable, bFinalRelease: false);
			backDoor.SendAttachObject(":EnergyStream.300", backDoor3);
			if (_Data.Duty != null)
			{
				InternalRealVariable obj = backDoor.get_BackDoorVariable((object)":HeatFlow.510").Variable as InternalRealVariable;
				obj.SetValue(_Data.Duty.Value, _Data.Duty.Unit);
				CleanResource.ReleaseComObjectHelper(obj, bFinalRelease: false);
			}
			else if (_Data.CDTAD != null)
			{
				SetDeltaTOperation("Oper_Set_" + _Data.UID, bHeater: true, _Data.ColdSideInletStreams[0].Trim(), _Data.ColdSideOutletStreams[0].Trim(), 1.0, _Data.CDTAD);
			}
			else if (_Data.CDTBB != null)
			{
				SetDeltaTOperation("Oper_Set_" + _Data.UID, bHeater: true, _Data.ColdSideInletStreams[0].Trim(), _Data.ColdSideOutletStreams[0].Trim(), 0.0, new DoubleValue(_Data.CDTBB.Unit, _Data.CDTBB.Value * -1.0));
			}
			bool bHSDPSetByDef = false;
			bool bCSDPSetByDef = false;
			AddLocalDefineForHX(backDoor, ref bCSDPSetByDef, ref bHSDPSetByDef);
			if (!bCSDPSetByDef && !flag)
			{
				AddHXHotOrColdSideDP(backDoor, ":PressureDrop.511", _Data.ColdSideDeltaPressure);
			}
			AddUnconvertNotes(backDoor);
		}
		catch (COMException)
		{
			DebugPrint.OutputDebugString(DebugLevel.BuildHYSYSCase, WarningLevel.Error, string.Format(P2Globalization.GetString("CreateHysysHeaterForHXFailed"), _Data.UID));
		}
		catch (Exception)
		{
			DebugPrint.OutputDebugString(DebugLevel.BuildHYSYSCase, WarningLevel.Error, string.Format(P2Globalization.GetString("CreateHysysHeaterForHXFailed"), _Data.UID));
		}
		finally
		{
			CleanResource.ReleaseComObjectHelper(heaterOp, bFinalRelease: false);
			CleanResource.ReleaseComObjectHelper(processStream, bFinalRelease: false);
			CleanResource.ReleaseComObjectHelper(backDoor, bFinalRelease: false);
			CleanResource.ReleaseComObjectHelper(backDoor2, bFinalRelease: false);
			CleanResource.ReleaseComObjectHelper(processStream2, bFinalRelease: false);
			CleanResource.ReleaseComObjectHelper(backDoor3, bFinalRelease: false);
		}
	}

	private void CreateHeatExchangerForHX()
	{
		HeatExchanger heatExchanger = null;
		ProcessStream processStream = null;
		BackDoor backDoor = null;
		BackDoor backDoor2 = null;
		try
		{
			heatExchanger = _Flowsheet.get_Operations((object)"HeatExOpObject").Add(_Data.UID, "HeatExOpObject") as HeatExchanger;
			heatExchanger.HeatExchangerModel = HeatExchangerModelEnum_enum.hex_Weighted;
			backDoor = heatExchanger as BackDoor;
			AddHCurveSetting(backDoor, HysysHeatExchangerType.HeatExchanger);
			(backDoor.get_BackDoorVariable((object)"ExchPerf.500/HCurve.300.0:Count.300").Variable as InternalRealVariable).Value = 6.0;
			(backDoor.get_BackDoorVariable((object)"ExchPerf.500/HCurve.300.1:Count.300").Variable as InternalRealVariable).Value = 6.0;
			if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(_Data.ColdSideMethod))
			{
				string methodName = HYSYSFluidPackages.GetMethodName(_Data.ColdSideMethod);
				if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(methodName))
				{
					BackDoor backDoor3 = _SimCase.BasisManager.FluidPackages[methodName] as BackDoor;
					backDoor.SendAttachObject("ExchSide.501:CompList.300", backDoor3);
					CleanResource.ReleaseComObjectHelper(backDoor3, bFinalRelease: false);
				}
			}
			if (_Data.ColdSideInletStreams != null)
			{
				processStream = GetProcessStream(_Data.ColdSideInletStreams[0].Trim());
				backDoor2 = processStream as BackDoor;
				backDoor.SendAttachObject(":MaterialStream.400.0", backDoor2);
			}
			if (_Data.ColdSideOutletStreams != null)
			{
				processStream = GetProcessStream(_Data.ColdSideOutletStreams[0].Trim());
				backDoor2 = processStream as BackDoor;
				backDoor.SendAttachObject(":MaterialStream.401.0", backDoor2);
				if (_Data.ColdSideOutletTemperature != null)
				{
					processStream.Temperature.SetValue(_Data.ColdSideOutletTemperature.Value, _Data.ColdSideOutletTemperature.Unit);
				}
				else if (_Data.CLFRAC != null)
				{
					processStream.VapourFractionValue = 1.0 - _Data.CLFRAC.Value;
				}
			}
			if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(_Data.HotSideMethod))
			{
				string methodName2 = HYSYSFluidPackages.GetMethodName(_Data.HotSideMethod);
				if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(methodName2))
				{
					BackDoor backDoor4 = _SimCase.BasisManager.FluidPackages[methodName2] as BackDoor;
					backDoor.SendAttachObject("ExchSide.502:CompList.300", backDoor4);
					CleanResource.ReleaseComObjectHelper(backDoor4, bFinalRelease: false);
				}
			}
			if (_Data.HotSideInletStreams != null)
			{
				processStream = GetProcessStream(_Data.HotSideInletStreams[0].Trim());
				backDoor2 = processStream as BackDoor;
				backDoor.SendAttachObject(":MaterialStream.400.1", backDoor2);
			}
			if (_Data.HotSideOutletStreams != null)
			{
				processStream = GetProcessStream(_Data.HotSideOutletStreams[0].Trim());
				backDoor2 = processStream as BackDoor;
				backDoor.SendAttachObject(":MaterialStream.401.1", backDoor2);
				if (_Data.HotSideOutletTemperature != null)
				{
					processStream.Temperature.SetValue(_Data.HotSideOutletTemperature.Value, _Data.HotSideOutletTemperature.Unit);
				}
				else if (_Data.HLFRAC != null)
				{
					processStream.VapourFractionValue = 1.0 - _Data.HLFRAC.Value;
				}
			}
			bool bHSDPSetByDef = false;
			bool bCSDPSetByDef = false;
			AddLocalDefineForHX(backDoor, ref bCSDPSetByDef, ref bHSDPSetByDef);
			if (!bCSDPSetByDef)
			{
				AddHXHotOrColdSideDP(backDoor, ":PressureDrop.541", _Data.ColdSideDeltaPressure);
			}
			if (!bHSDPSetByDef)
			{
				AddHXHotOrColdSideDP(backDoor, ":PressureDrop.540", _Data.HotSideDeltaPressure);
			}
			if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(_Data.FlowDirection) && _Data.FlowDirection == "COCURRENT")
			{
				InternalRealVariable obj = backDoor.get_BackDoorVariable((object)":Selection.517").Variable as InternalRealVariable;
				obj.Value = 0.0;
				CleanResource.ReleaseComObjectHelper(obj, bFinalRelease: false);
			}
			if (_Data.ZONES != null)
			{
				InternalRealVariable obj2 = backDoor.get_BackDoorVariable((object)"ExchPerf.500/HCurve.300.0:Count.300").Variable as InternalRealVariable;
				obj2.Value = Convert.ToDouble(_Data.ZONES.Value);
				CleanResource.ReleaseComObjectHelper(obj2, bFinalRelease: false);
				InternalRealVariable obj3 = backDoor.get_BackDoorVariable((object)"ExchPerf.500/HCurve.300.1:Count.300").Variable as InternalRealVariable;
				obj3.Value = Convert.ToDouble(_Data.ZONES.Value);
				CleanResource.ReleaseComObjectHelper(obj3, bFinalRelease: false);
			}
			if (_Data.TubePasses > 0 && _Data.ShellPasses > 0)
			{
				int tubePasses = _Data.TubePasses;
				int shellPasses = _Data.ShellPasses;
				InternalRealVariable internalRealVariable = null;
				InternalRealVariable internalRealVariable2 = backDoor.get_BackDoorVariable((object)":Index.521").Variable as InternalRealVariable;
				switch (shellPasses)
				{
				case 1:
					internalRealVariable2.SetValue(tubePasses, "");
					break;
				case 2:
					tubePasses /= shellPasses;
					internalRealVariable2.SetValue(tubePasses, "");
					internalRealVariable = backDoor.get_BackDoorVariable((object)":Selection.514").Variable as InternalRealVariable;
					internalRealVariable.Value = 70.0;
					break;
				default:
					_Data.ConvertNotes.Add(string.Format(P2Globalization.GetString("HXTubeShellPassNotConvert"), _Data.UID));
					break;
				}
				CleanResource.ReleaseComObjectHelper(internalRealVariable, bFinalRelease: false);
				CleanResource.ReleaseComObjectHelper(internalRealVariable2, bFinalRelease: false);
			}
			if (_Data.FTFactor != null)
			{
				DebugPrint.OutputDebugString(DebugLevel.Convert, WarningLevel.Notes, string.Format(P2Globalization.GetString("HXFTNotConvert"), _Data.UID));
			}
			if (_Data.Tolerance != null)
			{
				InternalRealVariable obj4 = backDoor.get_BackDoorVariable((object)":Ratio.300").Variable as InternalRealVariable;
				obj4.Value = _Data.Tolerance.Value;
				CleanResource.ReleaseComObjectHelper(obj4, bFinalRelease: false);
			}
			if (_Data.OverallUA != null)
			{
				InternalRealVariable obj5 = backDoor.get_BackDoorVariable((object)":UA.500").Variable as InternalRealVariable;
				obj5.SetValue(_Data.OverallUA.Value, _Data.OverallUA.Unit);
				CleanResource.ReleaseComObjectHelper(obj5, bFinalRelease: false);
			}
			else
			{
				heatExchanger.Specifications.Remove(1);
			}
			if (_Data.HOCI != null)
			{
				HeatExchangerSpecification obj6 = heatExchanger.Specifications.Add("HOCI_Spec", "exchspec") as HeatExchangerSpecification;
				obj6.IsActive = true;
				BackDoor obj7 = obj6 as BackDoor;
				(obj7.get_BackDoorTextVariable((object)":ExtraData.398").Variable as InternalTextVariable).Value = _Data.HotSideOutletStreams[0].Trim();
				(obj7.get_BackDoorTextVariable((object)":ExtraData.399").Variable as InternalTextVariable).Value = _Data.ColdSideInletStreams[0].Trim();
				(obj7.get_BackDoorVariable((object)":ExtraData.200").Variable as InternalRealVariable).SetValue(_Data.HOCI.Value, _Data.HOCI.Unit);
			}
			if (_Data.HICO != null)
			{
				HeatExchangerSpecification obj8 = heatExchanger.Specifications.Add("HICO_Spec", "exchspec") as HeatExchangerSpecification;
				obj8.IsActive = true;
				BackDoor obj9 = obj8 as BackDoor;
				(obj9.get_BackDoorTextVariable((object)":ExtraData.398").Variable as InternalTextVariable).Value = _Data.HotSideInletStreams[0].Trim();
				(obj9.get_BackDoorTextVariable((object)":ExtraData.399").Variable as InternalTextVariable).Value = _Data.ColdSideOutletStreams[0].Trim();
				(obj9.get_BackDoorVariable((object)":ExtraData.200").Variable as InternalRealVariable).SetValue(_Data.HICO.Value, _Data.HICO.Unit);
			}
			if (_Data.HOCO != null)
			{
				HeatExchangerSpecification obj10 = heatExchanger.Specifications.Add("HOCO_Spec", "exchspec") as HeatExchangerSpecification;
				obj10.IsActive = true;
				BackDoor obj11 = obj10 as BackDoor;
				(obj11.get_BackDoorTextVariable((object)":ExtraData.398").Variable as InternalTextVariable).Value = _Data.HotSideOutletStreams[0].Trim();
				(obj11.get_BackDoorTextVariable((object)":ExtraData.399").Variable as InternalTextVariable).Value = _Data.ColdSideOutletStreams[0].Trim();
				(obj11.get_BackDoorVariable((object)":ExtraData.200").Variable as InternalRealVariable).SetValue(_Data.HOCO.Value, _Data.HOCO.Unit);
			}
			if (_Data.HDTBB != null)
			{
				HeatExchangerSpecification obj12 = heatExchanger.Specifications.Add("HDTBB_Spec", "exchspec") as HeatExchangerSpecification;
				obj12.IsActive = true;
				BackDoor obj13 = obj12 as BackDoor;
				(obj13.get_BackDoorVariable((object)":PageNumber.0").Variable as InternalRealVariable).SetValue(1.0, "");
				(obj13.get_BackDoorVariable((object)":Selection.300").Variable as InternalRealVariable).SetValue(11.0, "");
				(obj13.get_BackDoorTextVariable((object)":ExtraData.398").Variable as InternalTextVariable).Value = _Data.HotSideOutletStreams[0].Trim();
				(obj13.get_BackDoorVariable((object)":ExtraData.200").Variable as InternalRealVariable).SetValue(_Data.HDTBB.Value, _Data.HDTBB.Unit);
			}
			if (_Data.CDTBB != null)
			{
				HeatExchangerSpecification obj14 = heatExchanger.Specifications.Add("CDTBB_Spec", "exchspec") as HeatExchangerSpecification;
				obj14.IsActive = true;
				BackDoor obj15 = obj14 as BackDoor;
				(obj15.get_BackDoorVariable((object)":PageNumber.0").Variable as InternalRealVariable).SetValue(1.0, "");
				(obj15.get_BackDoorVariable((object)":Selection.300").Variable as InternalRealVariable).SetValue(11.0, "");
				(obj15.get_BackDoorTextVariable((object)":ExtraData.398").Variable as InternalTextVariable).Value = _Data.ColdSideOutletStreams[0].Trim();
				(obj15.get_BackDoorVariable((object)":ExtraData.200").Variable as InternalRealVariable).SetValue(_Data.CDTBB.Value, _Data.CDTBB.Unit);
			}
			if (_Data.HDTAD != null)
			{
				HeatExchangerSpecification obj16 = heatExchanger.Specifications.Add("HDTAD_Spec", "exchspec") as HeatExchangerSpecification;
				obj16.IsActive = true;
				BackDoor obj17 = obj16 as BackDoor;
				(obj17.get_BackDoorVariable((object)":PageNumber.0").Variable as InternalRealVariable).SetValue(1.0, "");
				(obj17.get_BackDoorVariable((object)":Selection.300").Variable as InternalRealVariable).SetValue(12.0, "");
				(obj17.get_BackDoorTextVariable((object)":ExtraData.398").Variable as InternalTextVariable).Value = _Data.HotSideOutletStreams[0].Trim();
				(obj17.get_BackDoorVariable((object)":ExtraData.200").Variable as InternalRealVariable).SetValue(_Data.HDTAD.Value, _Data.HDTAD.Unit);
			}
			if (_Data.CDTAD != null)
			{
				HeatExchangerSpecification obj18 = heatExchanger.Specifications.Add("CDTAD_Spec", "exchspec") as HeatExchangerSpecification;
				obj18.IsActive = true;
				BackDoor obj19 = obj18 as BackDoor;
				(obj19.get_BackDoorVariable((object)":PageNumber.0").Variable as InternalRealVariable).SetValue(1.0, "");
				(obj19.get_BackDoorVariable((object)":Selection.300").Variable as InternalRealVariable).SetValue(12.0, "");
				(obj19.get_BackDoorTextVariable((object)":ExtraData.398").Variable as InternalTextVariable).Value = _Data.ColdSideOutletStreams[0].Trim();
				(obj19.get_BackDoorVariable((object)":ExtraData.200").Variable as InternalRealVariable).SetValue(_Data.CDTAD.Value, _Data.CDTAD.Unit);
			}
			if (_Data.Duty != null)
			{
				HeatExchangerSpecification obj20 = heatExchanger.Specifications.Add("Duty_Spec", "exchspec") as HeatExchangerSpecification;
				obj20.IsActive = true;
				BackDoor obj21 = obj20 as BackDoor;
				(obj21.get_BackDoorVariable((object)":PageNumber.0").Variable as InternalRealVariable).SetValue(1.0, "");
				(obj21.get_BackDoorVariable((object)":Selection.300").Variable as InternalRealVariable).SetValue(5.0, "");
				(obj21.get_BackDoorVariable((object)":Selection.400").Variable as InternalRealVariable).SetValue(13.0, "");
				(obj21.get_BackDoorVariable((object)":ExtraData.200").Variable as InternalRealVariable).SetValue(_Data.Duty.Value, _Data.Duty.Unit);
			}
			if (_Data.MITA != null)
			{
				HeatExchangerSpecification obj22 = heatExchanger.Specifications.Add("Mita_Spec", "exchspec") as HeatExchangerSpecification;
				obj22.IsActive = true;
				BackDoor obj23 = obj22 as BackDoor;
				(obj23.get_BackDoorVariable((object)":Selection.300").Variable as InternalRealVariable).SetValue(6.0, "");
				(obj23.get_BackDoorVariable((object)":Selection.400").Variable as InternalRealVariable).SetValue(11.0, "");
				(obj23.get_BackDoorVariable((object)":ExtraData.200").Variable as InternalRealVariable).SetValue(_Data.MITA.Value, _Data.MITA.Unit);
			}
			AddUnconvertNotes(backDoor);
		}
		catch (Exception)
		{
			DebugPrint.OutputDebugString(DebugLevel.BuildHYSYSCase, WarningLevel.Error, string.Format(P2Globalization.GetString("CreateHysysHeatExchangerForHXFailed"), _Data.UID));
		}
		finally
		{
			CleanResource.ReleaseComObjectHelper(processStream, bFinalRelease: false);
			CleanResource.ReleaseComObjectHelper(backDoor2, bFinalRelease: false);
			CleanResource.ReleaseComObjectHelper(backDoor, bFinalRelease: false);
			CleanResource.ReleaseComObjectHelper(heatExchanger, bFinalRelease: false);
		}
	}

	private void CreateHeatExchangerForHXRIG(bool bConvertToEDR)
	{
		HeatExchanger heatExchanger = null;
		ProcessStream processStream = null;
		BackDoor backDoor = null;
		BackDoor backDoor2 = null;
		try
		{
			heatExchanger = _Flowsheet.get_Operations((object)"HeatExOpObject").Add(_Data.UID, "HeatExOpObject") as HeatExchanger;
			heatExchanger.HeatExchangerModel = HeatExchangerModelEnum_enum.hex_SimpleRating;
			backDoor = heatExchanger as BackDoor;
			AddHCurveSetting(backDoor, HysysHeatExchangerType.HeatExchanger);
			if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(_Data.HotSideMethod))
			{
				string methodName = HYSYSFluidPackages.GetMethodName(_Data.HotSideMethod);
				if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(methodName))
				{
					BackDoor backDoor3 = _SimCase.BasisManager.FluidPackages[methodName] as BackDoor;
					backDoor.SendAttachObject("ExchSide.502:CompList.300", backDoor3);
					CleanResource.ReleaseComObjectHelper(backDoor3, bFinalRelease: false);
				}
			}
			if (_Data.HotSideInletStreams != null)
			{
				processStream = GetProcessStream(_Data.HotSideInletStreams[0].Trim());
				backDoor2 = processStream as BackDoor;
				backDoor.SendAttachObject(":MaterialStream.400.1", backDoor2);
			}
			if (_Data.HotSideOutletStreams != null)
			{
				processStream = GetProcessStream(_Data.HotSideOutletStreams[0].Trim());
				backDoor2 = processStream as BackDoor;
				backDoor.SendAttachObject(":MaterialStream.401.1", backDoor2);
				if (_Data.HotSideOutletTemperature != null)
				{
					((HeatExchangerSpecification)heatExchanger.Specifications[1]).IsActive = false;
					processStream.Temperature.SetValue(_Data.HotSideOutletTemperature.Value, _Data.HotSideOutletTemperature.Unit);
				}
			}
			if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(_Data.ColdSideMethod))
			{
				string methodName2 = HYSYSFluidPackages.GetMethodName(_Data.ColdSideMethod);
				if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(methodName2))
				{
					BackDoor backDoor4 = _SimCase.BasisManager.FluidPackages[methodName2] as BackDoor;
					backDoor.SendAttachObject("ExchSide.501:CompList.300", backDoor4);
					CleanResource.ReleaseComObjectHelper(backDoor4, bFinalRelease: false);
				}
			}
			if (_Data.ColdSideInletStreams != null)
			{
				processStream = GetProcessStream(_Data.ColdSideInletStreams[0].Trim());
				backDoor2 = processStream as BackDoor;
				backDoor.SendAttachObject(":MaterialStream.400.0", backDoor2);
			}
			if (_Data.ColdSideOutletStreams != null)
			{
				processStream = GetProcessStream(_Data.ColdSideOutletStreams[0].Trim());
				backDoor2 = processStream as BackDoor;
				backDoor.SendAttachObject(":MaterialStream.401.0", backDoor2);
				if (_Data.ColdSideOutletTemperature != null)
				{
					((HeatExchangerSpecification)heatExchanger.Specifications[1]).IsActive = false;
					processStream.Temperature.SetValue(_Data.ColdSideOutletTemperature.Value, _Data.ColdSideOutletTemperature.Unit);
				}
			}
			if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(_Data.TEMAType))
			{
				InternalRealVariable internalRealVariable = backDoor.get_BackDoorVariable((object)":Selection.513").Variable as InternalRealVariable;
				InternalRealVariable internalRealVariable2 = backDoor.get_BackDoorVariable((object)":Selection.514").Variable as InternalRealVariable;
				InternalRealVariable internalRealVariable3 = backDoor.get_BackDoorVariable((object)":Selection.515").Variable as InternalRealVariable;
				string tEMAType = _Data.TEMAType;
				string text = tEMAType.Substring(0, 1);
				string text2 = tEMAType.Substring(1, 1);
				string text3 = tEMAType.Substring(2, 1);
				bool flag = false;
				switch (text)
				{
				case "A":
					internalRealVariable.Value = 65.0;
					break;
				case "B":
					internalRealVariable.Value = 66.0;
					break;
				case "C":
					internalRealVariable.Value = 67.0;
					break;
				case "N":
					internalRealVariable.Value = 78.0;
					break;
				case "D":
					internalRealVariable.Value = 68.0;
					break;
				default:
					flag = true;
					break;
				}
				if (text2 == "E")
				{
					internalRealVariable2.Value = 69.0;
				}
				else if (text2 == "F")
				{
					internalRealVariable2.Value = 70.0;
				}
				else
				{
					flag = true;
				}
				switch (text3)
				{
				case "L":
					internalRealVariable3.Value = 76.0;
					break;
				case "M":
					internalRealVariable3.Value = 77.0;
					break;
				case "N":
					internalRealVariable3.Value = 78.0;
					break;
				case "P":
					internalRealVariable3.Value = 80.0;
					break;
				case "S":
					internalRealVariable3.Value = 83.0;
					break;
				case "T":
					internalRealVariable3.Value = 84.0;
					break;
				case "U":
					internalRealVariable3.Value = 85.0;
					break;
				case "W":
					internalRealVariable3.Value = 87.0;
					break;
				default:
					flag = true;
					break;
				}
				if (flag)
				{
					_Data.ConvertNotes.Add(string.Format(P2Globalization.GetString("InvalidTEMATypeNotConvert"), _Data.UID, _Data.TEMAType));
				}
				CleanResource.ReleaseComObjectHelper(internalRealVariable, bFinalRelease: false);
				CleanResource.ReleaseComObjectHelper(internalRealVariable2, bFinalRelease: false);
				CleanResource.ReleaseComObjectHelper(internalRealVariable3, bFinalRelease: false);
			}
			if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(_Data.ExchangerOrientation))
			{
				InternalRealVariable internalRealVariable4 = backDoor.get_BackDoorVariable((object)":Selection.516").Variable as InternalRealVariable;
				if (_Data.ExchangerOrientation == "HORIZONTAL")
				{
					internalRealVariable4.Value = 0.0;
				}
				else if (_Data.ExchangerOrientation == "VERTICAL")
				{
					internalRealVariable4.Value = 1.0;
				}
				CleanResource.ReleaseComObjectHelper(internalRealVariable4, bFinalRelease: false);
			}
			if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(_Data.FlowDirection))
			{
				InternalRealVariable internalRealVariable5 = backDoor.get_BackDoorVariable((object)":Selection.517").Variable as InternalRealVariable;
				if (_Data.FlowDirection == "COCURRENT")
				{
					internalRealVariable5.Value = 0.0;
				}
				else if (_Data.FlowDirection == "COUNTERCURRENT")
				{
					internalRealVariable5.Value = 1.0;
				}
				CleanResource.ReleaseComObjectHelper(internalRealVariable5, bFinalRelease: false);
			}
			if (_Data.ShellInnerDiameter != null)
			{
				InternalRealVariable obj = backDoor.get_BackDoorVariable((object)":SmallLength.520").Variable as InternalRealVariable;
				obj.SetValue(_Data.ShellInnerDiameter.Value, _Data.ShellInnerDiameter.Unit);
				CleanResource.ReleaseComObjectHelper(obj, bFinalRelease: false);
			}
			if (_Data.ShellInSeries > 0)
			{
				InternalRealVariable obj2 = backDoor.get_BackDoorVariable((object)":Index.500").Variable as InternalRealVariable;
				obj2.SetValue(_Data.ShellInSeries, "");
				CleanResource.ReleaseComObjectHelper(obj2, bFinalRelease: false);
			}
			if (_Data.ShellInParallel > 0)
			{
				InternalRealVariable obj3 = backDoor.get_BackDoorVariable((object)":Index.501").Variable as InternalRealVariable;
				obj3.SetValue(_Data.ShellInParallel, "");
				CleanResource.ReleaseComObjectHelper(obj3, bFinalRelease: false);
			}
			if (_Data.ShellFouling != null)
			{
				InternalRealVariable obj4 = backDoor.get_BackDoorVariable((object)":Fouling.500.0").Variable as InternalRealVariable;
				obj4.SetValue(_Data.ShellFouling.Value, _Data.ShellFouling.Unit);
				CleanResource.ReleaseComObjectHelper(obj4, bFinalRelease: false);
			}
			if (_Data.ShellsideFilmCoeff != null)
			{
				InternalRealVariable obj5 = backDoor.get_BackDoorVariable((object)":HeatTranCoeff.510").Variable as InternalRealVariable;
				obj5.SetValue(_Data.ShellsideFilmCoeff.Value, _Data.ShellsideFilmCoeff.Unit);
				CleanResource.ReleaseComObjectHelper(obj5, bFinalRelease: false);
			}
			InternalRealVariable internalRealVariable6 = backDoor.get_BackDoorVariable((object)":PressureDrop.540").Variable as InternalRealVariable;
			if (_Data.SSDPSHELL != null)
			{
				internalRealVariable6.SetValue(_Data.SSDPSHELL.Value * (double)_Data.ShellInSeries * (double)_Data.ShellInParallel, _Data.SSDPSHELL.Unit);
			}
			if (_Data.SSDPUNIT != null)
			{
				internalRealVariable6.SetValue(_Data.SSDPUNIT.Value, _Data.SSDPUNIT.Unit);
			}
			CleanResource.ReleaseComObjectHelper(internalRealVariable6, bFinalRelease: false);
			if (_Data.TubeLength != null)
			{
				InternalRealVariable obj6 = backDoor.get_BackDoorVariable((object)":Length.522").Variable as InternalRealVariable;
				obj6.SetValue(_Data.TubeLength.Value, _Data.TubeLength.Unit);
				CleanResource.ReleaseComObjectHelper(obj6, bFinalRelease: false);
			}
			if (_Data.CalculateTube != TubeDiameterCalEnum.NONE)
			{
				if (_Data.CalculateTube == TubeDiameterCalEnum.ID)
				{
					DoubleValue convertedValue = HYSYSUOMMap.GetConvertedValue(UnitClassEnum.FineLength, _Data.TubeOD, _Data.TubeThickness.Unit);
					_Data.TubeID = new DoubleValue(_Data.TubeThickness.Unit, convertedValue.Value - _Data.TubeThickness.Value * 2.0);
				}
				else if (_Data.CalculateTube == TubeDiameterCalEnum.OD)
				{
					DoubleValue convertedValue2 = HYSYSUOMMap.GetConvertedValue(UnitClassEnum.FineLength, _Data.TubeID, _Data.TubeThickness.Unit);
					_Data.TubeOD = new DoubleValue(_Data.TubeThickness.Unit, convertedValue2.Value + _Data.TubeThickness.Value * 2.0);
				}
			}
			else
			{
				DoubleValue convertedValue3 = HYSYSUOMMap.GetConvertedValue(UnitClassEnum.FineLength, _Data.TubeOD, _Data.TubeID.Unit);
				_Data.TubeThickness = new DoubleValue(_Data.TubeID.Unit, (convertedValue3.Value - _Data.TubeID.Value) / 2.0);
			}
			if (_Data.TubeOD != null)
			{
				InternalRealVariable obj7 = backDoor.get_BackDoorVariable((object)":SmallLength.523").Variable as InternalRealVariable;
				obj7.SetValue(_Data.TubeOD.Value, _Data.TubeOD.Unit);
				CleanResource.ReleaseComObjectHelper(obj7, bFinalRelease: false);
			}
			if (_Data.TubeID != null)
			{
				InternalRealVariable obj8 = backDoor.get_BackDoorVariable((object)":SmallLength.524").Variable as InternalRealVariable;
				obj8.SetValue(_Data.TubeID.Value, _Data.TubeID.Unit);
				CleanResource.ReleaseComObjectHelper(obj8, bFinalRelease: false);
			}
			if (_Data.TubePasses > 0)
			{

	private void AddUnconvertNotes(BackDoor heatExchangerBD)
	{
		InternalTextVariable internalTextVariable = heatExchangerBD.get_BackDoorTextVariable((object)":Notes.400").Variable as InternalTextVariable;
		foreach (string convertNote in _Data.ConvertNotes)
		{
			internalTextVariable.Value = internalTextVariable.Value + convertNote + "\n";
		}
		CleanResource.ReleaseComObjectHelper(internalTextVariable, bFinalRelease: false);
	}

	{
	}

	private void SetHCurvePlotData(BackDoor heatExchangerBD, HysysHeatExchangerType exType)
	{
		string moniker = ":Selection.302";
		string moniker2 = ":Selection.303";
		if (exType != HysysHeatExchangerType.HeatExchanger)
		{
			InternalRealVariable obj = heatExchangerBD.get_BackDoorVariable((object)":Count.502").Variable as InternalRealVariable;
			obj.Value = 11.0;
			CleanResource.ReleaseComObjectHelper(obj, bFinalRelease: false);
			moniker = ":Selection.501";
			moniker2 = ":Selection.502";
		}
		InternalRealVariable obj2 = heatExchangerBD.get_BackDoorVariable((object)moniker).Variable as InternalRealVariable;
		obj2.Value = 3.0;
		CleanResource.ReleaseComObjectHelper(obj2, bFinalRelease: false);
		InternalRealVariable obj3 = heatExchangerBD.get_BackDoorVariable((object)moniker2).Variable as InternalRealVariable;
		obj3.Value = 0.0;
		CleanResource.ReleaseComObjectHelper(obj3, bFinalRelease: false);
	}

	private void AddLocalDefineForHX(BackDoor heatExBD, ref bool bCSDPSetByDef, ref bool bHSDPSetByDef)
	{
		foreach (DEFINE item in _Data.GlobalDefine)
		{
			string unitKey = string.Empty;
			_ = string.Empty;
			DoubleValue doubleValue = null;
			if (item.FirstRefProperty.Contains('('))
			{
				unitKey = ParseUtilty.GetParentheses(item.FirstRefProperty);
			}
			string text = item.MainProperty.ToUpper().Trim();
			string text2 = string.Empty;
			if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(item.OperatorName))
			{
				text2 = item.OperatorName.ToUpper().Trim();
			}
			if (text.StartsWith("CDP"))
			{
				if (text2.StartsWith("MIN") || text2.StartsWith("DIF") || text2.StartsWith("SUB"))
				{
					if (_Data.ColdSideInletStreams.Count > 1 || _Data.ColdSideOutletStreams.Count > 1)
					{
						_Data.ConvertNotes.Add(string.Format(P2Globalization.GetString("HXLDNotConverted"), item.P2DefineExpression));
						continue;
					}
					if (_Data.ColdSideInletStreams.Contains(item.FirstRefID) && string.IsNullOrEmpty(item.SecondRefID) && !string.IsNullOrEmpty(item.SecondRefProperty))
					{
						double valueIn = Convert.ToDouble(item.SecondRefProperty);
						doubleValue = UnitConverter.GetHysysValue(UnitClassEnum.DropPressure, valueIn, unitKey);
						AddSetForDP(_Data.ColdSideOutletStreams[0].Trim(), _Data.ColdSideInletStreams[0].Trim(), doubleValue);
						item.bConverted = true;
						bCSDPSetByDef = true;
					}
				}
			}
			else if (text.StartsWith("HDP"))
			{
				if (_Data.HotSideInletStreams.Count > 1 || _Data.HotSideOutletStreams.Count > 1)
				{
					_Data.ConvertNotes.Add(string.Format(P2Globalization.GetString("HXLDNotConverted"), item.P2DefineExpression));
					continue;
				}
				if ((text2.StartsWith("MIN") || text2.StartsWith("DIF") || text2.StartsWith("SUB")) && _Data.HotSideInletStreams.Contains(item.FirstRefID) && string.IsNullOrEmpty(item.SecondRefID) && !string.IsNullOrEmpty(item.SecondRefProperty))
				{
					double valueIn2 = Convert.ToDouble(item.SecondRefProperty);
					doubleValue = UnitConverter.GetHysysValue(UnitClassEnum.DropPressure, valueIn2, unitKey);
					AddSetForDP(_Data.HotSideOutletStreams[0].Trim(), _Data.HotSideInletStreams[0].Trim(), doubleValue);
					item.bConverted = true;
					bHSDPSetByDef = true;
				}
			}
			if (!item.bConverted)
			{
				_Data.ConvertNotes.Add(string.Format(P2Globalization.GetString("HXDefineNotConverted"), _Data.UID, item.P2DefineExpression));
			}
		}
	}

	private void AddSetForDP(string targetStm, string sourceStm, DoubleValue specVal)
	{
		ProcessStream processStream = null;
		SetOp obj = _Flowsheet.get_Operations((object)"SetOpObject").Add("Set_" + _Data.UID + "_CDP", "SetOpObject") as SetOp;
		processStream = GetProcessStream(targetStm);
		obj.TargetVariable = processStream.Pressure;
		processStream = GetProcessStream(sourceStm);
		BackDoor backDoor = processStream as BackDoor;
		(obj as BackDoor).SendAttachObject(":FlowSheetObject.501", backDoor);
		obj.MultiplierValue = 1.0;
		obj.Offset.SetValue(specVal.Value * -1.0, specVal.Unit);
	}

	private void AddHXHotOrColdSideDP(BackDoor heatExBD, string sMoniker, DoubleValue dpVal)
	{
		InternalRealVariable internalRealVariable = heatExBD.get_BackDoorVariable((object)sMoniker).Variable as InternalRealVariable;
		if (dpVal != null)
		{
			internalRealVariable.SetValue(dpVal.Value, dpVal.Unit);
		}
		else if (internalRealVariable.CanModify)
		{
			internalRealVariable.SetValue(0.0, "");
		}
		CleanResource.ReleaseComObjectHelper(internalRealVariable, bFinalRelease: false);
	}

	private void SetDeltaTOperation(string setName, bool bHeater, string inletStm, string outletStm, double OutStmFrac, DoubleValue dTVal)
	{
		BackDoor backDoor = _Flowsheet as BackDoor;
		InternalRealVariable obj = backDoor.get_BackDoorVariable((object)"/ConfigMgr.100:ExtraData.1.389(StreamConnect)").Variable as InternalRealVariable;
		obj.Value = 1.0;
		CleanResource.ReleaseComObjectHelper(backDoor, bFinalRelease: false);
		CleanResource.ReleaseComObjectHelper(obj, bFinalRelease: false);
		ProcessStream processStream = GetProcessStream(inletStm);
		ProcessStream processStream2 = GetProcessStream("Dupl_" + outletStm);
		ProcessStream processStream3 = GetProcessStream(outletStm);
		ProcessStream energyStream = GetEnergyStream("Q_Ref_" + _Data.UID);
		string name = "Ref_" + _Data.UID;
		DoubleValue doubleValue = null;
		BackDoor backDoor2;
		if (bHeater)
		{
			doubleValue = _Data.ColdSideDeltaPressure;
			backDoor2 = (_Flowsheet.get_Operations((object)"HeaterOpObject").Add(name, "HeaterOpObject") as HeaterOp) as BackDoor;
		}
		else
		{
			doubleValue = _Data.HotSideDeltaPressure;
			backDoor2 = (_Flowsheet.get_Operations((object)"CoolerOpObject").Add(name, "CoolerOpObject") as CoolerOp) as BackDoor;
		}
		backDoor2.SendAttachObject(":MaterialStream.400", processStream as BackDoor);
		backDoor2.SendAttachObject(":MaterialStream.401", processStream2 as BackDoor);
		backDoor2.SendAttachObject(":EnergyStream.300", energyStream as BackDoor);
		if (doubleValue == null)
		{
			doubleValue = new DoubleValue("", 0.0);
		}
		AddHXHotOrColdSideDP(backDoor2, ":PressureDrop.511", doubleValue);
		processStream2.VapourFractionValue = OutStmFrac;
		SetOp obj2 = _Flowsheet.get_Operations((object)"SetOpObject").Add(setName, "SetOpObject") as SetOp;
		obj2.TargetVariable = processStream3.Temperature;
		(obj2 as BackDoor).SendAttachObject(":FlowSheetObject.501", processStream2 as BackDoor);
		obj2.MultiplierValue = 1.0;
		obj2.Offset.SetValue(dTVal.Value, dTVal.Unit);
	}

	public void Dispose()
	{
	}
}
public static class HYSYSHelperCommonFunctions
{
	private static BackDoor fsBD;

	private static InternalRealVariable solver;

	public static void HoldOnSolver(Flowsheet Flowsht)
	{
		fsBD = Flowsht as BackDoor;
		solver = fsBD.get_BackDoorVariable((object)"SolverObject.400:Attribute.300").Variable as InternalRealVariable;
		solver.Value = 1.0;
	}

	public static void ActivateSolver()
	{
		solver.Value = 0.0;
		CleanResource.ReleaseComObjectHelper(fsBD, bFinalRelease: false);
		CleanResource.ReleaseComObjectHelper(solver, bFinalRelease: false);
	}
}
public class HYSYSLNGHeatExchangerConverter : HYSYSObjectConverterBase
{
	private HYSYSLNGHeatExchanger _data;

	private LngOp _lnghx;

	public HYSYSLNGHeatExchangerConverter(SimulationCase simulationCase, HYSYSLNGHeatExchanger data)
		: base(simulationCase)
	{
		try
		{
			_data = data;
			_lnghx = _Flowsheet.get_Operations((object)"LngOpObject").Add(_data.UID, "LngOpObject") as LngOp;
			CreateLNGHX();
		}
		catch (Exception)
		{
		}
	}

	private void CreateLNGHX()
	{
		BackDoor backDoor = null;
		ProcessStream processStream = null;
		ProcessStream processStream2 = null;
		BackDoor backDoor2 = null;
		int num = 0;
		SortedDictionary<int, string> sortedDictionary = new SortedDictionary<int, string>();
		try
		{
			backDoor = _lnghx as BackDoor;
			InternalRealVariable internalRealVariable = backDoor.get_BackDoorVariable((object)"ExchPerf.500:Selection.300").Variable as InternalRealVariable;
			if (_data.IsHeatLeak)
			{
				internalRealVariable.Value = 2.0;
			}
			else
			{
				internalRealVariable.Value = 0.0;
			}
			CleanResource.ReleaseComObjectHelper(internalRealVariable, bFinalRelease: false);
			for (int i = 0; i < _data.HotSidesInletStreams.Count; i++)
			{
				bool flag = false;
				bool flag2 = false;
				string format = "{0}-{1}";
				string text = string.Empty;
				if (_data.HotSideMethods.ContainsKey(i))
				{
					text = _data.HotSideMethods[i];
				}
				if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(text))
				{
					string methodName = HYSYSFluidPackages.GetMethodName(text);
					if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(methodName))
					{
						BackDoor backDoor3 = _SimCase.BasisManager.FluidPackages[methodName] as BackDoor;
						backDoor.SendAttachObject("ExchSide.500." + i + ":CompList.300", backDoor3);
						CleanResource.ReleaseComObjectHelper(backDoor3, bFinalRelease: false);
					}
				}
				List<string> list = _data.HotSidesInletStreams[i];
				processStream = GetProcessStream(list[0]);
				backDoor2 = processStream as BackDoor;
				if (i > 1)
				{
					backDoor.SendBackDoorMessage("AddSide");
				}
				if (i == 1)
				{
					InternalRealVariable obj = backDoor.get_BackDoorVariable((object)":Selection.501.1").Variable as InternalRealVariable;
					obj.Value = 0.0;
					CleanResource.ReleaseComObjectHelper(obj, bFinalRelease: false);
				}
				backDoor.SendAttachObject(":MaterialStream.400." + i, backDoor2);
				processStream2 = GetProcessStream(_data.HotSidesOutletStreams[i][0]);
				backDoor2 = processStream2 as BackDoor;
				backDoor.SendAttachObject(":MaterialStream.401." + i, backDoor2);
				format = string.Format(format, processStream.name.Trim(), processStream2.name.Trim());
				if (_data.HotSideTemperatures.ContainsKey(i) && _data.HotSideTemperatures[i] != null)
				{
					processStream2.Temperature.SetValue(_data.HotSideTemperatures[i].Value, _data.HotSideTemperatures[i].Unit);
					flag = true;
				}
				if (processStream.Pressure.Value != -32767.0)
				{
					CleanOutletStreamProperty(backDoor2);
				}
				InternalRealVariable obj2 = backDoor.get_BackDoorVariable((object)("ExchSide.500." + i + ":PressureDrop.300")).Variable as InternalRealVariable;
				obj2.SetValue(_data.HotSideDeltaPressures[i].Value, _data.HotSideDeltaPressures[i].Unit);
				CleanResource.ReleaseComObjectHelper(obj2, bFinalRelease: false);
				InternalRealVariable obj3 = backDoor.get_BackDoorVariable((object)("ExchPerf.500/HCurve.300." + i + ":Count.300")).Variable as InternalRealVariable;
				obj3.Value = Convert.ToDouble(_data.ZONES.Value);
				CleanResource.ReleaseComObjectHelper(obj3, bFinalRelease: false);
				if (_data.HotSideDuties.ContainsKey(i))
				{
					AddDutySpecification(format, _data.HotSideDuties[i], "Hot" + i, bHot: true);
					flag2 = true;
				}
				if (!flag2 && !flag)
				{
					if (!sortedDictionary.ContainsKey(num))
					{
						sortedDictionary.Add(num, processStream2.name);
					}
					num++;
				}
			}
			int count = _data.HotSidesInletStreams.Count;
			for (int j = 0; j < _data.ColdSidesInletStreams.Count; j++)
			{
				bool flag3 = false;
				bool flag4 = false;
				string format2 = "{0}-{1}";
				List<string> list2 = _data.ColdSidesInletStreams[j];
				processStream = GetProcessStream(list2[0]);
				backDoor2 = processStream as BackDoor;
				if (count > 1 || j > 0)
				{
					backDoor.SendBackDoorMessage("AddSide");
					InternalRealVariable obj4 = backDoor.get_BackDoorVariable((object)(":Selection.501." + (count + j))).Variable as InternalRealVariable;
					obj4.Value = 1.0;
					CleanResource.ReleaseComObjectHelper(obj4, bFinalRelease: false);
				}
				backDoor.SendAttachObject(":MaterialStream.400." + (count + j), backDoor2);
				processStream2 = GetProcessStream(_data.ColdSidesOutletStreams[j][0]);
				format2 = string.Format(format2, processStream.name.Trim(), processStream2.name.Trim());
				backDoor2 = processStream2 as BackDoor;
				backDoor.SendAttachObject(":MaterialStream.401." + (count + j), backDoor2);
				if (_data.ColdSideTemperatures.ContainsKey(j) && _data.ColdSideTemperatures[j] != null)
				{
					if (processStream2.Temperature.CanModify)
					{
						processStream2.Temperature.SetValue(_data.ColdSideTemperatures[j].Value, _data.ColdSideTemperatures[j].Unit);
					}
					flag3 = true;
				}
				if (processStream.Pressure.Value != -32767.0)
				{
					CleanOutletStreamProperty(backDoor2);
				}
				InternalRealVariable obj5 = backDoor.get_BackDoorVariable((object)("ExchSide.500." + (count + j) + ":PressureDrop.300")).Variable as InternalRealVariable;
				obj5.SetValue(_data.ColdSideDeltaPressures[j].Value, _data.ColdSideDeltaPressures[j].Unit);
				CleanResource.ReleaseComObjectHelper(obj5, bFinalRelease: false);
				string text2 = string.Empty;
				if (_data.ColdSideMethods.ContainsKey(j))
				{
					text2 = _data.ColdSideMethods[j];
				}
				if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(text2))
				{
					string methodName2 = HYSYSFluidPackages.GetMethodName(text2);
					if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(methodName2))
					{
						BackDoor backDoor4 = _SimCase.BasisManager.FluidPackages[methodName2] as BackDoor;
						backDoor.SendAttachObject("ExchSide.500." + (count + j) + ":CompList.300", backDoor4);
						CleanResource.ReleaseComObjectHelper(backDoor4, bFinalRelease: false);
					}
				}
				InternalRealVariable obj6 = backDoor.get_BackDoorVariable((object)("ExchPerf.500/HCurve.300." + (count + j) + ":Count.300")).Variable as InternalRealVariable;
				obj6.Value = Convert.ToDouble(_data.ZONES.Value);
				CleanResource.ReleaseComObjectHelper(obj6, bFinalRelease: false);
				if (_data.ColdSideDuties.ContainsKey(j))
				{
					AddDutySpecification(format2, _data.ColdSideDuties[j], "Cold" + j, bHot: false);
					flag4 = true;
				}
				if (!flag4 && !flag3)
				{
					if (!sortedDictionary.ContainsKey(num))
					{
						sortedDictionary.Add(num, processStream2.name);
					}
					num++;
				}
			}
			if (num >= 2)
			{
				for (int k = 0; k < num - 1; k++)
				{
					AddDTSpecification(sortedDictionary[k], sortedDictionary[k + 1], k.ToString());
				}
			}
			InternalTextVariable internalTextVariable = backDoor.get_BackDoorTextVariable((object)":Notes.400").Variable as InternalTextVariable;
			foreach (string convertNote in _data.ConvertNotes)
			{
				internalTextVariable.Value = internalTextVariable.Value + convertNote + "\n";
			}
		}
		catch (Exception)
		{
			DebugPrint.OutputDebugString(DebugLevel.BuildHYSYSCase, WarningLevel.Error, string.Format(P2Globalization.GetString("UOConverter_Failed"), CUnitOperation.LNGHEATEXCHANGER, _data.UID));
		}
		finally
		{
			CleanResource.ReleaseComObjectHelper(backDoor, bFinalRelease: false);
			CleanResource.ReleaseComObjectHelper(backDoor2, bFinalRelease: false);
			CleanResource.ReleaseComObjectHelper(processStream, bFinalRelease: false);
			CleanResource.ReleaseComObjectHelper(processStream2, bFinalRelease: false);
		}
	}

	private bool AddDutySpecification(string target, DoubleValue value, string index, bool bHot)
	{
		HeatExchangerSpecification heatExchangerSpecification = null;
		BackDoor backDoor = null;
		InternalTextVariable internalTextVariable = null;
		InternalRealVariable internalRealVariable = null;
		try
		{
			heatExchangerSpecification = _lnghx.Specifications.Add("Duty_Spec_" + index, "exchspec") as HeatExchangerSpecification;
			heatExchangerSpecification.IsActive = true;
			backDoor = heatExchangerSpecification as BackDoor;
			(backDoor.get_BackDoorVariable((object)":Selection.300").Variable as InternalRealVariable).SetValue(5.0, "");
			internalTextVariable = backDoor.get_BackDoorTextVariable((object)":ExtraData.398").Variable as InternalTextVariable;
			internalTextVariable.Value = target;
			internalRealVariable = backDoor.get_BackDoorVariable((object)":ExtraData.200").Variable as InternalRealVariable;
			if (bHot && value.Value > 0.0)
			{
				value.Value = 0.0 - value.Value;
			}
			internalRealVariable.SetValue(value.Value, value.Unit);
			return true;
		}
		catch (Exception)
		{
			DebugPrint.OutputDebugString(DebugLevel.BuildHYSYSCase, WarningLevel.Error, string.Format(P2Globalization.GetString("AddLNGHXSpecFailed"), "Duty", _data.UID));
			return false;
		}
		finally
		{
			CleanResource.ReleaseComObjectHelper(heatExchangerSpecification, bFinalRelease: false);
			CleanResource.ReleaseComObjectHelper(backDoor, bFinalRelease: false);
			CleanResource.ReleaseComObjectHelper(internalTextVariable, bFinalRelease: false);
			CleanResource.ReleaseComObjectHelper(internalRealVariable, bFinalRelease: false);
		}
	}

	private bool AddDTSpecification(string stream1, string stream2, string index)
	{
		HeatExchangerSpecification heatExchangerSpecification = null;
		BackDoor backDoor = null;
		InternalRealVariable internalRealVariable = null;
		try
		{
			heatExchangerSpecification = _lnghx.Specifications.Add("DT_Spec_" + index, "exchspec") as HeatExchangerSpecification;
			heatExchangerSpecification.IsActive = true;
			backDoor = heatExchangerSpecification as BackDoor;
			(backDoor.get_BackDoorTextVariable((object)":ExtraData.398").Variable as InternalTextVariable).Value = stream1.Trim();
			(backDoor.get_BackDoorTextVariable((object)":ExtraData.399").Variable as InternalTextVariable).Value = stream2.Trim();
			internalRealVariable = backDoor.get_BackDoorVariable((object)":ExtraData.200").Variable as InternalRealVariable;
			internalRealVariable.SetValue(0.0, "");
			return true;
		}
		catch (Exception)
		{
			DebugPrint.OutputDebugString(DebugLevel.BuildHYSYSCase, WarningLevel.Error, string.Format(P2Globalization.GetString("AddLNGHXSpecFailed"), "Delta Temp", _data.UID));
			return false;
		}
		finally
		{
			CleanResource.ReleaseComObjectHelper(heatExchangerSpecification, bFinalRelease: false);
			CleanResource.ReleaseComObjectHelper(backDoor, bFinalRelease: false);
			CleanResource.ReleaseComObjectHelper(internalRealVariable, bFinalRelease: false);
		}
	}

	private void CleanOutletStreamProperty(BackDoor outletBD)
	{
		InternalRealVariable internalRealVariable = null;
		try
		{
			internalRealVariable = outletBD.get_BackDoorVariable((object)":Pressure.502.0").Variable as InternalRealVariable;
			if (internalRealVariable.CanModify)
			{
				internalRealVariable.Value = -32767.0;
			}
		}
		catch (Exception)
		{
			DebugPrint.OutputDebugString(DebugLevel.BuildHYSYSCase, WarningLevel.Error, string.Format(P2Globalization.GetString("CleanLNGHXOutletPropsFailed"), _data.UID));
		}
		finally
		{
			CleanResource.ReleaseComObjectHelper(internalRealVariable, bFinalRelease: false);
		}
	}
}
public class HYSYSMixerConverter : HYSYSObjectConverterBase
{
	private MixerOp _Mixer;

	private HYSYSMixer _Data;

	public HYSYSMixerConverter(SimulationCase simulationCase, HYSYSMixer data)
		: base(simulationCase)
	{
		try
		{
			if (string.Compare(data.UID, "To be deleted for air cooler", ignoreCase: true) != 0)
			{
				_Data = data;
				_Mixer = _Flowsheet.get_Operations((object)"MixerOpObject").Add(data.UID, "MixerOpObject") as MixerOp;
				CreateMixer();
			}
		}
		catch (Exception)
		{
			DebugPrint.OutputDebugString(DebugLevel.BuildHYSYSCase, WarningLevel.Error, string.Format(P2Globalization.GetString("CreateMixerFailed"), _Data.UID));
		}
	}

	private void CreateMixer()
	{
		BackDoor backDoor = _Mixer as BackDoor;
		if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(_Data.FluidPackage) && !string.IsNullOrEmpty(HYSYSFluidPackages.GetMethodName(_Data.FluidPackage)))
		{
			BackDoor backDoor2 = _SimCase.BasisManager.FluidPackages[_Data.FluidPackage] as BackDoor;
			backDoor.SendAttachObject(":CompList.300", backDoor2);
			CleanResource.ReleaseComObjectHelper(backDoor2, bFinalRelease: false);
		}
		if (_Data.InletStreams != null)
		{
			int num = 0;
			foreach (string inletStream in _Data.InletStreams)
			{
				BackDoor backDoor3 = GetProcessStream(inletStream) as BackDoor;
				backDoor.SendAttachObject(":Stream.501." + num, backDoor3);
				num++;
			}
		}
		if (_Data.OutletStreams != null)
		{
			BackDoor backDoor4 = GetProcessStream(_Data.OutletStreams[0].Trim()) as BackDoor;
			backDoor.SendAttachObject(":Stream.500", backDoor4);
		}
		AddLocalDefine();
		InternalTextVariable internalTextVariable = backDoor.get_BackDoorTextVariable((object)":Notes.400").Variable as InternalTextVariable;
		foreach (string convertNote in _Data.ConvertNotes)
		{
			internalTextVariable.Value = internalTextVariable.Value + convertNote + "\n";
		}
		CleanResource.ReleaseComObjectHelper(internalTextVariable, bFinalRelease: false);
	}

	private void AddLocalDefine()
	{
		foreach (DEFINE item in _Data.GlobalDefine)
		{
			string unitKey = string.Empty;
			_ = string.Empty;
			DoubleValue doubleValue = null;
			if (item.FirstRefProperty.Contains('('))
			{
				unitKey = ParseUtilty.GetParentheses(item.FirstRefProperty);
			}
			string text = item.MainProperty.ToUpper().Trim();
			string text2 = string.Empty;
			if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(item.OperatorName))
			{
				text2 = item.OperatorName.ToUpper().Trim();
			}
			if (text.StartsWith("DP"))
			{
				if ((text2.StartsWith("MIN") || text2.StartsWith("DIF") || text2.StartsWith("SUB")) && _Data.InletStreams.Contains(item.FirstRefID) && string.IsNullOrEmpty(item.SecondRefID) && !string.IsNullOrEmpty(item.SecondRefProperty))
				{
					double valueIn = Convert.ToDouble(item.SecondRefProperty);
					doubleValue = UnitConverter.GetHysysValue(UnitClassEnum.DropPressure, valueIn, unitKey);
					AddValveToCorrectDefineOutPressure(item.FirstRefID, doubleValue);
					item.bConverted = true;
					_Data.OutletValveAdded = true;
				}
			}
			else if (text.StartsWith("PRE") && _Data.InletStreams.Contains(item.FirstRefID) && string.IsNullOrEmpty(text2))
			{
				AddValveToCorrectDefineOutPressure(item.FirstRefID, new DoubleValue("", 0.0));
				item.bConverted = true;
				_Data.OutletValveAdded = true;
			}
		}
	}

	private void AddValveToCorrectDefineOutPressure(string sourceStm, DoubleValue specVal)
	{
		BackDoor backDoor = _Mixer as BackDoor;
		BackDoor backDoor2 = null;
		string streamName = NameHelper.CreateName(bIsStream: true);
		backDoor2 = GetProcessStream(streamName) as BackDoor;
		BackDoor obj = (_Flowsheet.get_Operations((object)"ValveOpObject").Add("V_" + _Data.UID, "ValveOpObject") as Valve) as BackDoor;
		backDoor.SendAttachObject(":Stream.500", backDoor2);
		obj.SendAttachObject(":MaterialStream.500", backDoor2);
		backDoor2 = GetProcessStream(_Data.OutletStreams[0].Trim()) as BackDoor;
		obj.SendAttachObject(":MaterialStream.501", backDoor2);
		AddSetForDP(_Data.OutletStreams[0].Trim(), sourceStm, specVal);
	}

	private void AddSetForDP(string targetStm, string sourceStm, DoubleValue specVal)
	{
		ProcessStream processStream = null;
		SetOp obj = _Flowsheet.get_Operations((object)"SetOpObject").Add("Set_" + _Data.UID + "_DP", "SetOpObject") as SetOp;
		processStream = GetProcessStream(targetStm);
		obj.TargetVariable = processStream.Pressure;
		processStream = GetProcessStream(sourceStm);
		BackDoor backDoor = processStream as BackDoor;
		(obj as BackDoor).SendAttachObject(":FlowSheetObject.501", backDoor);
		obj.MultiplierValue = 1.0;
		obj.Offset.SetValue(specVal.Value * -1.0, specVal.Unit);
	}
}
internal class HYSYSCompressorConverter : HYSYSObjectConverterBase, IDisposable
{
	private CompressOp _Compressor;

	private HYSYSCompressor _Data;

	public HYSYSCompressorConverter(SimulationCase simulationCase, HYSYSCompressor data)
		: base(simulationCase)
	{
		_Data = data;
		try
		{
			_Compressor = _Flowsheet.get_Operations((object)"CompressorObject").Add(data.UID, "CompressorObject") as CompressOp;
			CreateCompressor();
		}
		catch (Exception)
		{
		}
	}

	private void CreateCompressor()
	{
		BackDoor backDoor = null;
		ProcessStream processStream = null;
		ProcessStream processStream2 = null;
		ProcessStream processStream3 = null;
		BackDoor backDoor2 = null;
		InternalRealVariable internalRealVariable = null;
		InternalRealVariable internalRealVariable2 = null;
		BackDoor backDoor3 = null;
		BackDoor backDoor4 = null;
		InternalRealVariable internalRealVariable3 = null;
		try
		{
			backDoor = _Compressor as BackDoor;
			processStream = GetProcessStream(_Data.OutletStreams[0].Trim());
			processStream2 = GetProcessStream(_Data.InletStreams[0].Trim());
			try
			{
				string name = NameHelper.CreateEnergyStreamName();
				processStream3 = _Flowsheet.EnergyStreams.Add(name, "") as ProcessStream;
				backDoor2 = processStream3 as BackDoor;
				backDoor.SendAttachObject(":EnergyStream.502", backDoor2);
			}
			catch (Exception)
			{
				DebugPrint.OutputDebugString(DebugLevel.BuildHYSYSCase, WarningLevel.Error, string.Format(P2Globalization.GetString("CompressorConverter_SetEnergyStreamFailed"), _Compressor.name));
			}
			if (_Data.AdiabaticEfficiency != 0.0)
			{
				try
				{
					internalRealVariable = backDoor.get_BackDoorVariable((object)":Index.500").Variable as InternalRealVariable;
					internalRealVariable.Value = _Data.AdiabaticEfficiency;
				}
				catch (Exception)
				{
					DebugPrint.OutputDebugString(DebugLevel.BuildHYSYSCase, WarningLevel.Error, string.Format(P2Globalization.GetString("CompressorConverter_SetADIAEffFailed"), _Compressor.name));
				}
			}
			if (_Data.PolytropicEfficiency != 0.0)
			{
				try
				{
					internalRealVariable = backDoor.get_BackDoorVariable((object)":Index.501").Variable as InternalRealVariable;
					internalRealVariable.Value = _Data.PolytropicEfficiency;
				}
				catch (Exception)
				{
					DebugPrint.OutputDebugString(DebugLevel.BuildHYSYSCase, WarningLevel.Error, string.Format(P2Globalization.GetString("CompressorConverter_SetPolyEffFailed"), _Compressor.name));
				}
			}
			if (_Data.RPM != 0.0)
			{
				try
				{
					internalRealVariable = backDoor.get_BackDoorVariable((object)":Frequency.500").Variable as InternalRealVariable;
					internalRealVariable.SetValue(_Data.RPM, "RPM");
				}
				catch (Exception)
				{
					DebugPrint.OutputDebugString(DebugLevel.BuildHYSYSCase, WarningLevel.Error, string.Format(P2Globalization.GetString("CompressorConverter_SetRPMFailed"), _Compressor.name));
				}
			}
			try
			{
				if (_Data.CurveHead != null)
				{
					_Compressor.Curves.Add("Curve-1", "");
					for (int i = 0; i < _Data.CurveRate.Values.Length; i++)
					{
						internalRealVariable2 = backDoor.get_BackDoorVariable((object)("CompCurve.500(Curve-1):ExtraData.103." + i)).Variable as InternalRealVariable;
						internalRealVariable2.SetValue(_Data.CurveRate.Values[i], _Data.CurveRate.Unit);
						internalRealVariable2 = backDoor.get_BackDoorVariable((object)("CompCurve.500(Curve-1):ExtraData.104." + i)).Variable as InternalRealVariable;
						internalRealVariable2.SetValue(_Data.CurveHead.Values[i], _Data.CurveHead.Unit);
					}
				}
				if (_Data.CurveEfficiency != null)
				{
					internalRealVariable2 = backDoor.get_BackDoorVariable((object)":Selection.500").Variable as InternalRealVariable;
					if (_Data.CurveEfficiencyType == "ADIA")
					{
						internalRealVariable2.Value = 502.0;
					}
					else if (_Data.CurveEfficiencyType == "POLY")
					{
						internalRealVariable2.Value = 501.0;
					}
					if (_Compressor.Curves == null)
					{
						_Compressor.Curves.Add("Curve-1", "");
					}
					for (int j = 0; j < _Data.CurveRate.Values.Length; j++)
					{
						internalRealVariable2 = backDoor.get_BackDoorVariable((object)("CompCurve.500(Curve-1):ExtraData.100." + j)).Variable as InternalRealVariable;
						internalRealVariable2.Value = _Data.CurveEfficiency.Values[j];
					}
				}
				if (_Data.CurveEfficiency != null && _Data.CurveHead != null)
				{
					internalRealVariable2 = backDoor.get_BackDoorVariable((object)":Index.502").Variable as InternalRealVariable;
					internalRealVariable2.Value = 1.0;
					internalRealVariable2 = backDoor.get_BackDoorVariable((object)"CompCurve.500(Curve-1):Selection.100").Variable as InternalRealVariable;
					internalRealVariable2.Value = 1.0;
				}
			}
			catch (Exception)
			{
				DebugPrint.OutputDebugString(DebugLevel.BuildHYSYSCase, WarningLevel.Error, string.Format(P2Globalization.GetString("CompressorConverter_SetPerformanceCurveFailed"), _Compressor.name));
			}
			if (_Data.OutletStreams != null)
			{
				try
				{
					backDoor3 = processStream as BackDoor;
					backDoor.SendAttachObject(":MaterialStream.501.0", backDoor3);
				}
				catch (Exception)
				{
					DebugPrint.OutputDebugString(DebugLevel.BuildHYSYSCase, WarningLevel.Error, string.Format(P2Globalization.GetString("CompressorConverter_SetProductStreamsFailed"), _Compressor.name));
				}
			}
			if (_Data.InletStreams != null)
			{
				try
				{
					backDoor3 = processStream2 as BackDoor;
					backDoor.SendAttachObject(":MaterialStream.500.0", backDoor3);
				}
				catch (Exception)
				{
					DebugPrint.OutputDebugString(DebugLevel.BuildHYSYSCase, WarningLevel.Error, string.Format(P2Globalization.GetString("CompressorConverter_SetFeedStreamsFailed"), _Compressor.name));
				}
			}
			if (_Data.AttachedPressure != null)
			{
				try
				{
					processStream.Pressure.SetValue(_Data.AttachedPressure.Value, _Data.AttachedPressure.Unit);
				}
				catch (Exception)
				{
					DebugPrint.OutputDebugString(DebugLevel.BuildHYSYSCase, WarningLevel.Error, string.Format(P2Globalization.GetString("CompressorConverter_SetOutletPressureFailed"), _Compressor.name));
				}
			}
			if (_Data.AttachedDP != null)
			{
				try
				{
					internalRealVariable3 = backDoor.get_BackDoorVariable((object)":PressureDrop.500").Variable as InternalRealVariable;
					internalRealVariable3.SetValue(_Data.AttachedDP.Value, _Data.AttachedDP.Unit);
				}
				catch (Exception)
				{
					DebugPrint.OutputDebugString(DebugLevel.BuildHYSYSCase, WarningLevel.Error, string.Format(P2Globalization.GetString("CompressorConverter_SetDPFailed"), _Compressor.name));
				}
			}
			if (_Data.AttachedPRatio != 0.0)
			{
				try
				{
					internalRealVariable3 = backDoor.get_BackDoorVariable((object)":Ratio.501").Variable as InternalRealVariable;
					internalRealVariable3.Value = _Data.AttachedPRatio;
				}
				catch (Exception)
				{
					DebugPrint.OutputDebugString(DebugLevel.BuildHYSYSCase, WarningLevel.Error, string.Format(P2Globalization.GetString("CompressorConverter_SetPRatioFailed"), _Compressor.name));
				}
			}
			if (_Data.Duty != null)
			{
				try
				{
					(backDoor2.get_BackDoorVariable((object)":Power.501").Variable as InternalRealVariable).SetValue(_Data.Duty.Value, _Data.Duty.Unit);
				}
				catch (Exception)
				{
					DebugPrint.OutputDebugString(DebugLevel.BuildHYSYSCase, WarningLevel.Error, string.Format(P2Globalization.GetString("CompressorConverter_SetDutyFailed"), _Compressor.name));
				}
			}
			if (_Data.AttachedTemperature != null)
			{
				try
				{
					internalRealVariable = backDoor.get_BackDoorVariable((object)":Index.500").Variable as InternalRealVariable;
					internalRealVariable.Value = -32767.0;
					processStream.Temperature.SetValue(_Data.AttachedTemperature.Value, _Data.AttachedTemperature.Unit);
				}
				catch (Exception)
				{
					DebugPrint.OutputDebugString(DebugLevel.BuildHYSYSCase, WarningLevel.Error, string.Format(P2Globalization.GetString("CompressorConverter_SetOutletTempFailed"), _Compressor.name));
				}
			}
			if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(_Data.FluidPackage))
			{
				try
				{
					string methodName = HYSYSFluidPackages.GetMethodName(_Data.FluidPackage);
					if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(methodName))
					{
						backDoor4 = _SimCase.BasisManager.FluidPackages[methodName] as BackDoor;
						backDoor.SendAttachObject(":CompList.300", backDoor4);
					}
				}
				catch (Exception)
				{
					DebugPrint.OutputDebugString(DebugLevel.BuildHYSYSCase, WarningLevel.Error, string.Format(P2Globalization.GetString("CompressorConverter_SetFPFailed"), _Compressor.name));
				}
			}
			try
			{
				InternalTextVariable internalTextVariable = backDoor.get_BackDoorTextVariable((object)":Notes.400").Variable as InternalTextVariable;
				foreach (string convertNote in _Data.ConvertNotes)
				{
					internalTextVariable.Value = internalTextVariable.Value + convertNote + "\n";
				}
			}
			catch (Exception)
			{
				DebugPrint.OutputDebugString(DebugLevel.BuildHYSYSCase, WarningLevel.Error, string.Format(P2Globalization.GetString("CompressorConverter_SetConversionNotesFailed"), _Compressor.name));
			}
		}
		catch (COMException)
		{
		}
		finally
		{
			CleanResource.ReleaseComObjectHelper(backDoor, bFinalRelease: false);
			CleanResource.ReleaseComObjectHelper(processStream, bFinalRelease: false);
			CleanResource.ReleaseComObjectHelper(processStream2, bFinalRelease: false);
			CleanResource.ReleaseComObjectHelper(processStream3, bFinalRelease: false);
			CleanResource.ReleaseComObjectHelper(backDoor2, bFinalRelease: false);
			CleanResource.ReleaseComObjectHelper(backDoor3, bFinalRelease: false);
			CleanResource.ReleaseComObjectHelper(internalRealVariable, bFinalRelease: false);
			CleanResource.ReleaseComObjectHelper(internalRealVariable3, bFinalRelease: false);
			CleanResource.ReleaseComObjectHelper(backDoor4, bFinalRelease: false);
			CleanResource.ReleaseComObjectHelper(internalRealVariable2, bFinalRelease: false);
		}
	}

	public void Dispose()
	{
	}
}
public class HYSYSObjectConverterBase
{
	protected SimulationCase _SimCase;

	protected Flowsheet _Flowsheet;

	protected BasisManager _BasisManager;

	protected string[] _FlowStreamNames
	{
		get
		{
			if (_Flowsheet != null)
			{
				return (string[])_Flowsheet.Streams.Names;
			}
			return null;
		}
	}

	public HYSYSObjectConverterBase(SimulationCase simulationCase)
	{
		_SimCase = simulationCase;
