		_BasisManager = simulationCase.BasisManager;
		_Flowsheet = simulationCase.Flowsheet;
	}

	protected ProcessStream GetProcessStream(Flowsheet flowSheet, string streamName)
	{
		string[] source = (string[])flowSheet.MaterialStreams.Names;
		if (source.Contains(streamName))
		{
			return flowSheet.MaterialStreams[streamName] as ProcessStream;
		}
		if (source.Contains(streamName + "+") && source.Contains(streamName + "-"))
		{
			ProcessStream processStream = flowSheet.MaterialStreams[streamName + "+"] as ProcessStream;
			ProcessStream processStream2 = flowSheet.MaterialStreams[streamName + "-"] as ProcessStream;
			try
			{
				BackDoor backDoor = processStream2.FluidPackage as BackDoor;
				(processStream as BackDoor).SendAttachObject(":CompList.300", backDoor);
			}
			catch (Exception)
			{
			}
			source = (string[])flowSheet.MaterialStreams.Names;
			if (source.Contains(streamName))
			{
				return flowSheet.MaterialStreams[streamName] as ProcessStream;
			}
			return flowSheet.MaterialStreams.Add(streamName, "") as ProcessStream;
		}
		return flowSheet.MaterialStreams.Add(streamName, "") as ProcessStream;
	}

	protected ProcessStream GetProcessStream(string streamName)
	{
		return GetProcessStream(_Flowsheet, streamName);
	}

	protected ProcessStream GetEnergyStream(Flowsheet flowSheet, string streamName)
	{
		if (((string[])flowSheet.EnergyStreams.Names).Contains(streamName))
		{
			return flowSheet.EnergyStreams[streamName] as ProcessStream;
		}
		return flowSheet.EnergyStreams.Add(streamName, "") as ProcessStream;
	}

	protected ProcessStream GetEnergyStream(string streamName)
	{
		return GetEnergyStream(_Flowsheet, streamName);
	}

	protected int GetComponentIndex(string componentName)
	{
		return GetComponentIndex(_Flowsheet, componentName);
	}

	protected int GetComponentIndex(Flowsheet flowsheet, string componentName)
	{
		Components components = flowsheet.FluidPackage.Components;
		bool flag = false;
		foreach (string item in (IEnumerable)components.Names)
		{
			if (string.Compare(item, componentName, ignoreCase: true) == 0)
			{
				flag = true;
				break;
			}
		}
		if (flag)
		{
			return components.get_index((object)componentName);
		}
		return -1;
	}

	protected void RemoveStream(string streamName)
	{
		Streams materialStreams = _Flowsheet.MaterialStreams;
		bool flag = false;
		foreach (string item in (IEnumerable)materialStreams.Names)
		{
			if (string.Compare(item, streamName, ignoreCase: true) == 0)
			{
				flag = true;
				break;
			}
		}
		if (flag)
		{
			_Flowsheet.MaterialStreams.Remove(streamName);
		}
	}

	protected int GetHysysCompIndex(string hysysCompName)
	{
		int result = -1;
		string[] source = (string[])_SimCase.Flowsheet.FluidPackage.ComponentList.Components.Names;
		if (source.Contains(hysysCompName))
		{
			result = _SimCase.Flowsheet.FluidPackage.ComponentList.Components.get_index((object)hysysCompName);
		}
		else if (source.Contains(hysysCompName + "*"))
		{
			result = _SimCase.Flowsheet.FluidPackage.ComponentList.Components.get_index((object)(hysysCompName + "*"));
		}
		else
		{
			string plusCompNameFromHYSYS = P2Objects.P2Component.GetPlusCompNameFromHYSYS(hysysCompName);
			if (source.Contains(plusCompNameFromHYSYS))
			{
				result = _SimCase.Flowsheet.FluidPackage.ComponentList.Components.get_index((object)plusCompNameFromHYSYS);
			}
		}
		return result;
	}
}
public class HYSYSPipeConverter : HYSYSObjectConverterBase
{
	private PipeSegment _pipe;

	private HYSYSPipe _data;

	private double[] ndColumn1 = new double[5] { 1.0, 1.5, 2.0, 3.0, 4.0 };

	private double[] ndColumn2 = new double[5] { 6.0, 8.0, 10.0, 12.0, 14.0 };

	private double[] ndColumn3 = new double[4] { 16.0, 18.0, 20.0, 24.0 };

	private int[] schedules = new int[3] { 40, 80, 160 };

	public HYSYSPipeConverter(SimulationCase simulationCase, HYSYSPipe data)
		: base(simulationCase)
	{
		try
		{
			_data = data;
			_pipe = _Flowsheet.get_Operations((object)"PipeSegObject").Add(data.UID, "PipeSegObject") as PipeSegment;
			CreatePipe();
		}
		catch (Exception)
		{
		}
	}

	private void CreatePipe()
	{
		ProcessStream processStream = null;
		ProcessStream processStream2 = null;
		ProcessStream processStream3 = null;
		BackDoor backDoor = null;
		BackDoor backDoor2 = null;
		BackDoor backDoor3 = null;
		try
		{
			backDoor2 = _pipe as BackDoor;
			if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(_data.FluidPackage))
			{
				string methodName = HYSYSFluidPackages.GetMethodName(_data.FluidPackage);
				if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(methodName))
				{
					backDoor3 = _SimCase.BasisManager.FluidPackages[methodName] as BackDoor;
					backDoor2.SendAttachObject(":CompList.300", backDoor3);
				}
			}
			processStream = GetProcessStream(_data.InletStreams[0]);
			backDoor = processStream as BackDoor;
			backDoor2.SendAttachObject(":MaterialStream.500", backDoor);
			processStream2 = GetProcessStream(_data.OutletStreams[0]);
			backDoor = processStream2 as BackDoor;
			backDoor2.SendAttachObject(":MaterialStream.501", backDoor);
			string name = NameHelper.CreateEnergyStreamName();
			processStream3 = _Flowsheet.EnergyStreams.Add(name, "") as ProcessStream;
			backDoor = processStream3 as BackDoor;
			backDoor2.SendAttachObject(":EnergyStream.502", backDoor);
			string pipeCorr = _pipe.GetPipeCorr();
			if (_data.BBDPCORR)
			{
				pipeCorr = "Beggs and Brill (1979)";
				_pipe.SetPipeCorr(pipeCorr);
				if (!_data.ACCEL)
				{
					InternalRealVariable obj = backDoor2.get_BackDoorVariable((object)":Boolean.601").Variable as InternalRealVariable;
					obj.Value = 0.0;
					CleanResource.ReleaseComObjectHelper(obj, bFinalRelease: false);
				}
			}
			else
			{
				pipeCorr = "HTFS, Homogeneous Flow";
				_pipe.SetPipeCorr(pipeCorr);
			}
			if (_data.UseDP)
			{
				InternalRealVariable obj2 = backDoor2.get_BackDoorVariable((object)":PressureDrop.500").Variable as InternalRealVariable;
				obj2.SetValue(_data.DP.Value, _data.DP.Unit);
				CleanResource.ReleaseComObjectHelper(obj2, bFinalRelease: false);
			}
			if (_data.HTC != null)
			{
				InternalRealVariable internalRealVariable = backDoor2.get_BackDoorVariable((object)":Selection.509").Variable as InternalRealVariable;
				internalRealVariable.Value = 1.0;
				InternalRealVariable internalRealVariable2 = backDoor2.get_BackDoorVariable((object)":HeatTranCoeff.500").Variable as InternalRealVariable;
				internalRealVariable2.SetValue(_data.HTC.Value, _data.HTC.Unit);
				_data.ConvertNotes.Add(P2Globalization.GetString("PipeUValueWarning"));
				InternalRealVariable obj3 = backDoor2.get_BackDoorVariable((object)":Temperature.500").Variable as InternalRealVariable;
				obj3.SetValue(_data.TAMB.Value, _data.TAMB.Unit);
				CleanResource.ReleaseComObjectHelper(internalRealVariable, bFinalRelease: false);
				CleanResource.ReleaseComObjectHelper(internalRealVariable2, bFinalRelease: false);
				CleanResource.ReleaseComObjectHelper(obj3, bFinalRelease: false);
			}
			else if (_data.ISOTHERMAL)
			{
				SetOp setOp = _Flowsheet.get_Operations((object)"SetOpObject").Add("Set_" + _data.UID + "_Temperature", "SetOpObject") as SetOp;
				setOp.TargetVariable = processStream2.Temperature;
				BackDoor obj4 = setOp as BackDoor;
				BackDoor backDoor4 = processStream as BackDoor;
				obj4.SendAttachObject(":FlowSheetObject.501", backDoor4);
				setOp.MultiplierValue = 1.0;
				CleanResource.ReleaseComObjectHelper(setOp, bFinalRelease: false);
				CleanResource.ReleaseComObjectHelper(obj4, bFinalRelease: false);
				CleanResource.ReleaseComObjectHelper(backDoor4, bFinalRelease: false);
			}
			else
			{
				processStream3.HeatFlow.SetValue(0.0 - _data.DUTY.Value, _data.DUTY.Unit);
			}
			AddAndSetSegment(backDoor2);
			InternalTextVariable internalTextVariable = backDoor2.get_BackDoorTextVariable((object)":Notes.400").Variable as InternalTextVariable;
			foreach (string convertNote in _data.ConvertNotes)
			{
				internalTextVariable.Value = internalTextVariable.Value + convertNote + "\n";
			}
		}
		catch (Exception)
		{
			DebugPrint.OutputDebugString(DebugLevel.BuildHYSYSCase, WarningLevel.Error, string.Format(P2Globalization.GetString("UOConverter_Failed"), "HYSYS Pipe unit operation block", _data.UID));
		}
		finally
		{
			CleanResource.ReleaseComObjectHelper(backDoor2, bFinalRelease: false);
			CleanResource.ReleaseComObjectHelper(processStream, bFinalRelease: false);
			CleanResource.ReleaseComObjectHelper(processStream2, bFinalRelease: false);
			CleanResource.ReleaseComObjectHelper(processStream3, bFinalRelease: false);
			CleanResource.ReleaseComObjectHelper(backDoor, bFinalRelease: false);
			CleanResource.ReleaseComObjectHelper(backDoor3, bFinalRelease: false);
		}
	}

	private void AddAndSetSegment(BackDoor pipeBD)
	{
		bool flag = false;
		bool flag2 = false;
		if (!_data.UseDP)
		{
			flag = true;
		}
		if (_data.LENGTH.Value.Equals(0.0))
		{
			flag = false;
			flag2 = true;
		}
		pipeBD.SendBackDoorMessage("AddSegment");
		(pipeBD.get_BackDoorVariable((object)":Selection.510").Variable as InternalRealVariable).Value = 0.0;
		if (flag)
		{
			(pipeBD.get_BackDoorVariable((object)":Length.551.0").Variable as InternalRealVariable).SetValue(_data.LENGTH.Value, _data.LENGTH.Unit);
		}
		if (flag2)
		{
			pipeBD.SendAttachObject2(":FittingType.500.0", "/FittingType.100.68");
		}
		if (_data.ELEVATION != null)
		{
			(pipeBD.get_BackDoorVariable((object)":Length.550.0").Variable as InternalRealVariable).SetValue(_data.ELEVATION.Value, _data.ELEVATION.Unit);
		}
		if (_data.DIAMETER != null)
		{
			(pipeBD.get_BackDoorVariable((object)":SmallLength.550.0").Variable as InternalRealVariable).SetValue(_data.DIAMETER.Value, _data.DIAMETER.Unit);
			DoubleValue convertedValue = HYSYSUOMMap.GetConvertedValue(UnitClassEnum.FineLength, _data.DIAMETER, "in");
			double val = DetermineODFromID(convertedValue.Value);
			if (!val.Equals(0.0))
			{
				(pipeBD.get_BackDoorVariable((object)":SmallLength.551.0").Variable as InternalRealVariable).SetValue(val, "in");
			}
		}
		pipeBD.SendBackDoorMessage("ViewSegment 1 0");
		if (_data.ROUGHNESS != null)
		{
			(pipeBD.get_BackDoorVariable((object)":Length.552").Variable as InternalRealVariable).SetValue(_data.ROUGHNESS.Value, _data.ROUGHNESS.Unit);
		}
		if (_data.NPS == null || _data.NPS.Count <= 0)
		{
			return;
		}
		IDictionaryEnumerator dictionaryEnumerator = _data.NPS.GetEnumerator();
		if (!dictionaryEnumerator.MoveNext())
		{
			return;
		}
		double value = ((DoubleValue)dictionaryEnumerator.Key).Value;
		int value2 = Convert.ToInt32(dictionaryEnumerator.Value);
		if (!schedules.Contains(value2))
		{
			_data.ConvertNotes.Add(string.Format(P2Globalization.GetString("KeyWordWasNotConverted"), "Schedule"));
			return;
		}
		(pipeBD.get_BackDoorVariable((object)":Selection.513").Variable as InternalRealVariable).Value = Convert.ToDouble(Array.IndexOf(schedules, value2) + 1 + ".000000000000e+000");
		string text = "SelectNomDiam {0} {1}";
		int num = -1;
		if (ndColumn1.Contains(value))
		{
			num = 0;
			text = string.Format(text, num.ToString(), Array.IndexOf(ndColumn1, value).ToString());
		}
		else if (ndColumn2.Contains(value))
		{
			num = 1;
			text = string.Format(text, num.ToString(), Array.IndexOf(ndColumn2, value).ToString());
		}
		else if (ndColumn3.Contains(value))
		{
			num = 2;
			text = string.Format(text, num.ToString(), Array.IndexOf(ndColumn3, value).ToString());
		}
		else
		{
			_data.ConvertNotes.Add(string.Format(P2Globalization.GetString("KeyWordWasNotConverted"), "Nominal Diameter"));
		}
		if (num != -1)
		{
			pipeBD.SendBackDoorMessage(text);
		}
	}

	private double DetermineODFromID(double id)
	{
		if (id > 0.0 && id <= 1.049)
		{
			return 1.315;
		}
		if (id > 1.049 && id <= 1.61)
		{
			return 1.9;
		}
		if (id > 1.61 && id <= 2.067)
		{
			return 2.375;
		}
		if (id > 2.067 && id <= 3.068)
		{
			return 3.5;
		}
		if (id > 3.068 && id <= 4.026)
		{
			return 4.5;
		}
		if (id > 4.026 && id <= 6.065)
		{
			return 6.625;
		}
		if (id > 6.065 && id <= 7.981)
		{
			return 8.625;
		}
		if (id > 7.981 && id <= 10.02)
		{
			return 10.75;
		}
		if (id > 10.02 && id <= 11.94)
		{
			return 12.75;
		}
		if (id > 11.94 && id <= 13.12)
		{
			return 14.0;
		}
		if (id > 13.12 && id <= 15.0)
		{
			return 16.0;
		}
		if (id > 15.0 && id <= 16.88)
		{
			return 18.0;
		}
		if (id > 16.88 && id <= 18.81)
		{
			return 20.0;
		}
		if (id > 18.81 && id <= 22.62)
		{
			return 24.0;
		}
		return 0.0;
	}
}
public class HYSYSPlugFlowReactorConverter : HYSYSObjectConverterBase, IDisposable
{
	private PFReactor _PFReactor;

	private HYSYSPlugFlowReactor _Data;

	private BackDoor _BdPFReactor;

	private InternalRealVariable _InterReal;

	public HYSYSPlugFlowReactorConverter(SimulationCase simulationCase, HYSYSPlugFlowReactor data)
		: base(simulationCase)
	{
		_Data = data;
		try
		{
			_PFReactor = _Flowsheet.get_Operations((object)"PFReactorOp").Add(data.UID, "PFReactorOp") as PFReactor;
			_BdPFReactor = _PFReactor as BackDoor;
			(_BdPFReactor.get_BackDoorVariable((object)":Name.0").Variable as InternalTextVariable).Value = data.UID;
			CreatePFReactor();
		}
		catch (Exception)
		{
			DebugPrint.OutputDebugString(DebugLevel.BuildHYSYSCase, WarningLevel.Error, string.Format(P2Globalization.GetString("UOConverter_Failed"), "Plug Flow Reactor", _Data.UID));
		}
	}

	private void CreatePFReactor()
	{
		if (_Data == null)
		{
			return;
		}
		if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(_Data.FluidPackage))
		{
			string methodName = HYSYSFluidPackages.GetMethodName(_Data.FluidPackage);
			if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(methodName) && ((string[])_SimCase.BasisManager.FluidPackages.Names).Contains(methodName))
			{
				BackDoor backDoor = (_SimCase.BasisManager.FluidPackages[methodName] as FluidPackage) as BackDoor;
				_BdPFReactor.SendAttachObject(":CompList.300", backDoor);
			}
		}
		SetStreams();
		SetSize();
		if (_Data.ReactionSet != null)
		{
			_BdPFReactor.SendAttachObject2(":RxnSet.400", "SimCase.200/FluidPkgMgr.300/RxnPackageManager.300/RxnSet.300(" + _Data.ReactionSet.ID + ")");
		}
		_InterReal = _BdPFReactor.get_BackDoorVariable((object)":Boolean.552").Variable as InternalRealVariable;
		_InterReal.Value = 1.0;
		_InterReal = _BdPFReactor.get_BackDoorVariable((object)":Selection.556").Variable as InternalRealVariable;
		_InterReal.Value = 0.0;
		if (_Data.DeltaPresure != null)
		{
			_InterReal = _BdPFReactor.get_BackDoorVariable((object)":PressureDrop.500").Variable as InternalRealVariable;
			_InterReal.SetValue(_Data.DeltaPresure.Value, _Data.DeltaPresure.Unit);
		}
		if (_Data.DeltaPresure == null && _Data.IsEngun)
		{
			_InterReal = _BdPFReactor.get_BackDoorVariable((object)":Selection.556").Variable as InternalRealVariable;
			_InterReal.Value = 1.0;
		}
		if (_Data.PackedBedVoidFraction != null && _Data.PackedBedVoidFraction.Value > 0.0 && _Data.PackedBedVoidFraction.Value <= 1.0)
		{
			_InterReal = _BdPFReactor.get_BackDoorVariable((object)":Fraction.500").Variable as InternalRealVariable;
			_InterReal.SetValue(_Data.PackedBedVoidFraction.Value, string.Empty);
		}
		if (_Data.ParticleDiameter != null)
		{
			_InterReal = _BdPFReactor.get_BackDoorVariable((object)":Length.502").Variable as InternalRealVariable;
			_InterReal.SetValue(_Data.ParticleDiameter.Value, _Data.ParticleDiameter.Unit);
		}
		if (_Data.ParticleSphericity != null)
		{
			_InterReal = _BdPFReactor.get_BackDoorVariable((object)":Fraction.501").Variable as InternalRealVariable;
			_InterReal.SetValue(_Data.ParticleSphericity.Value, _Data.ParticleSphericity.Unit);
		}
		InternalTextVariable internalTextVariable = _BdPFReactor.get_BackDoorTextVariable((object)":Notes.400").Variable as InternalTextVariable;
		foreach (string convertNote in _Data.ConvertNotes)
		{
			internalTextVariable.Value = internalTextVariable.Value + convertNote + "\n";
		}
	}

	private void SetStreams()
	{
		BackDoor backDoor = null;
		ProcessStream processStream = null;
		try
		{
			for (int i = 0; i < _Data.InletStreams.Count; i++)
			{
				processStream = GetProcessStream(_Data.InletStreams[i].Trim());
				backDoor = processStream as BackDoor;
				_BdPFReactor.SendAttachObject(":MaterialStream.501." + i, backDoor);
			}
			processStream = GetProcessStream(_Data.Product.Trim());
			backDoor = processStream as BackDoor;
			_BdPFReactor.SendAttachObject(":MaterialStream.500", backDoor);
			if (_Data.ProductPressure != null)
			{
				_InterReal = _BdPFReactor.get_BackDoorVariable((object)":Selection.556").Variable as InternalRealVariable;
				_InterReal.Value = 0.0;
				processStream.Pressure.SetValue(_Data.ProductPressure.Value, _Data.ProductPressure.Unit);
			}
			if (_Data.OutletStreamTemperature != null)
			{
				processStream.Temperature.SetValue(_Data.OutletStreamTemperature.Value, _Data.OutletStreamTemperature.Unit);
			}
			processStream = GetEnergyStream(_Data.EnergyName.Trim());
			backDoor = processStream as BackDoor;
			_BdPFReactor.SendAttachObject(":EnergyStream.500", backDoor);
			if (_Data.Duty != null)
			{
				_InterReal = backDoor.get_BackDoorVariable((object)":HeatFlow.501.0").Variable as InternalRealVariable;
				_InterReal.SetValue(_Data.Duty.Value, _Data.Duty.Unit);
			}
		}
		catch (COMException)
		{
			DebugPrint.OutputDebugString(DebugLevel.BuildHYSYSCase, WarningLevel.Error, string.Format(P2Globalization.GetString("SetPFRPropFailed"), "Streams", _Data.UID));
		}
	}

	private void SetSize()
	{
		try
		{
			if (_Data.TubleLength != null)
			{
				_InterReal = _BdPFReactor.get_BackDoorVariable((object)":Length.500").Variable as InternalRealVariable;
				_InterReal.SetValue(_Data.TubleLength.Value, _Data.TubleLength.Unit);
			}
			if (_Data.TubleDiameter != null)
			{
				_InterReal = _BdPFReactor.get_BackDoorVariable((object)":Length.501").Variable as InternalRealVariable;
				_InterReal.SetValue(_Data.TubleDiameter.Value, _Data.TubleDiameter.Unit);
			}
			if (_Data.NumberOfTubes != 0)
			{
				_InterReal = _BdPFReactor.get_BackDoorVariable((object)":Count.500").Variable as InternalRealVariable;
				_InterReal.SetValue(_Data.NumberOfTubes, string.Empty);
			}
		}
		catch (COMException)
		{
			DebugPrint.OutputDebugString(DebugLevel.BuildHYSYSCase, WarningLevel.Error, string.Format(P2Globalization.GetString("SetPFRPropFailed"), "Size", _Data.UID));
		}
	}

	public void Dispose()
	{
	}
}
public class HYSYSPumpConverter : HYSYSObjectConverterBase
{
	private PumpOp _pump;

	private HYSYSPump _data;

	public HYSYSPumpConverter(SimulationCase simulationCase, HYSYSPump data)
		: base(simulationCase)
	{
		try
		{
			_data = data;
			_pump = _Flowsheet.get_Operations((object)"PumpOpObject").Add(data.UID, "PumpOpObject") as PumpOp;
			CreatePump();
		}
		catch (Exception)
		{
		}
	}

	private void CreatePump()
	{
		BackDoor backDoor = null;
		ProcessStream processStream = null;
		ProcessStream processStream2 = null;
		ProcessStream processStream3 = null;
		BackDoor backDoor2 = null;
		BackDoor backDoor3 = null;
		BackDoor backDoor4 = null;
		InternalRealVariable internalRealVariable = null;
		InternalRealVariable internalRealVariable2 = null;
		try
		{
			backDoor = _pump as BackDoor;
			processStream2 = GetProcessStream(_data.OutletStreams[0].Trim());
			processStream = GetProcessStream(_data.InletStreams[0].Trim());
			if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(_data.FluidPackage))
			{
				string methodName = HYSYSFluidPackages.GetMethodName(_data.FluidPackage);
				if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(methodName))
				{
					backDoor4 = _SimCase.BasisManager.FluidPackages[methodName] as BackDoor;
					backDoor.SendAttachObject(":CompList.300", backDoor4);
				}
			}
			if (_data.InletStreams != null)
			{
				backDoor2 = processStream as BackDoor;
				backDoor.SendAttachObject(":MaterialStream.500", backDoor2);
			}
			if (_data.OutletStreams != null)
			{
				backDoor2 = processStream2 as BackDoor;
				backDoor.SendAttachObject(":MaterialStream.501", backDoor2);
			}
			string name = NameHelper.CreateEnergyStreamName();
			processStream3 = _Flowsheet.EnergyStreams.Add(name, "") as ProcessStream;
			backDoor3 = processStream3 as BackDoor;
			backDoor.SendAttachObject(":EnergyStream.502", backDoor3);
			if (_data.ATTACHED_PRESSUREDROP != null)
			{
				processStream2.Pressure.Value = -32767.0;
				processStream2.Temperature.Value = -32767.0;
				internalRealVariable = backDoor.get_BackDoorVariable((object)":PressureDrop.500").Variable as InternalRealVariable;
				internalRealVariable.SetValue(_data.ATTACHED_PRESSUREDROP.Value, _data.ATTACHED_PRESSUREDROP.Unit);
			}
			if (_data.AdiabaticEfficiency != 0.0)
			{
				internalRealVariable2 = backDoor.get_BackDoorVariable((object)":Percent.500").Variable as InternalRealVariable;
				internalRealVariable2.Value = _data.AdiabaticEfficiency;
			}
			if (_data.ATTACHED_PRESSURE != null)
			{
				internalRealVariable = backDoor.get_BackDoorVariable((object)":PressureDrop.500").Variable as InternalRealVariable;
				internalRealVariable.Value = -32767.0;
				processStream2.Temperature.Value = -32767.0;
				processStream2.Pressure.SetValue(_data.ATTACHED_PRESSURE.Value, _data.ATTACHED_PRESSURE.Unit);
			}
			if (_data.PRATIO != 0.0)
			{
				internalRealVariable = backDoor.get_BackDoorVariable((object)":PressureDrop.500").Variable as InternalRealVariable;
				internalRealVariable.Value = -32767.0;
				processStream2.Temperature.Value = -32767.0;
				processStream2.Pressure.Value = -32767.0;
				(backDoor.get_BackDoorVariable((object)":Ratio.501").Variable as InternalRealVariable).Value = _data.PRATIO;
			}
			InternalTextVariable internalTextVariable = backDoor.get_BackDoorTextVariable((object)":Notes.400").Variable as InternalTextVariable;
			foreach (string convertNote in _data.ConvertNotes)
			{
				internalTextVariable.Value = internalTextVariable.Value + convertNote + "\n";
			}
		}
		catch (Exception)
		{
			DebugPrint.OutputDebugString(DebugLevel.BuildHYSYSCase, WarningLevel.Error, string.Format(P2Globalization.GetString("UOConverter_Failed"), "HYSYS Pump unit operation block", _data.UID));
		}
		finally
		{
			CleanResource.ReleaseComObjectHelper(backDoor, bFinalRelease: false);
			CleanResource.ReleaseComObjectHelper(processStream2, bFinalRelease: false);
			CleanResource.ReleaseComObjectHelper(processStream, bFinalRelease: false);
			CleanResource.ReleaseComObjectHelper(processStream3, bFinalRelease: false);
			CleanResource.ReleaseComObjectHelper(backDoor3, bFinalRelease: false);
			CleanResource.ReleaseComObjectHelper(backDoor2, bFinalRelease: false);
			CleanResource.ReleaseComObjectHelper(internalRealVariable2, bFinalRelease: false);
			CleanResource.ReleaseComObjectHelper(backDoor4, bFinalRelease: false);
			CleanResource.ReleaseComObjectHelper(internalRealVariable, bFinalRelease: false);
			CleanResource.ReleaseComObjectHelper(internalRealVariable2, bFinalRelease: false);
		}
	}
}
internal class HYSYSReactionSetConverter
{
	private BasisManager _BasisManager;

	private IList<HYSYSReactionSet> _ReactionSets;

	private ReactionPackageManager _RXPackageManager;

	private string _DefaultFPName;

	public HYSYSReactionSetConverter(BasisManager manager, IList<HYSYSReactionSet> reactionsets, string DefaultFPName)
	{
		_ReactionSets = reactionsets;
		_BasisManager = manager;
		_DefaultFPName = DefaultFPName;
		try
		{
			CreateReactionSets();
		}
		catch (Exception)
		{
		}
	}

	private void CreateReactionSets()
	{
		_RXPackageManager = _BasisManager.ReactionPackageManager;
		try
		{
			for (int i = 0; i < _ReactionSets.Count; i++)
			{
				CreateReactionSet(_ReactionSets[i]);
			}
		}
		catch (Exception)
		{
		}
	}

	private void CreateReactionSet(HYSYSReactionSet rxset)
	{
		BackDoor backDoor = null;
		BackDoor backDoor2 = null;
		ConversionReaction conversionReaction = null;
		EquilibriumReaction equilibriumReaction = null;
		KineticReaction kineticReaction = null;
		ReactionPackageManager reactionPackageManager = null;
		BackDoor backDoor3 = null;
		FluidPackage fluidPackage = null;
		bool flag = false;
		backDoor = _BasisManager as BackDoor;
		reactionPackageManager = _BasisManager.ReactionPackageManager;
		reactionPackageManager.ReactionSets.Add(rxset.ID, "RxnSetObject");
		backDoor2 = reactionPackageManager as BackDoor;
		for (int i = 0; i < rxset.HYSYSReactions.Count; i++)
		{
			rxset.HYSYSReactions[i].ID = rxset.HYSYSReactions[i].ID.Replace("_", "-");
			if (rxset.HYSYSReactions[i].IsAspenPlusProperty)
			{
				(backDoor2.get_BackDoorVariable((object)":Enum.786").Variable as InternalRealVariable).Value = 1.0;
			}
			else
			{
				(backDoor2.get_BackDoorVariable((object)":Enum.786").Variable as InternalRealVariable).Value = 0.0;
			}
			string iD = rxset.HYSYSReactions[i].ID;
			try
			{
				if (((string[])reactionPackageManager.Reactions.Names).Contains(iD))
				{
					reactionPackageManager.Reactions.get_index((object)iD);
					flag = true;
				}
				else
				{
					flag = false;
				}
			}
			catch (Exception)
			{
				flag = false;
			}
			if (rxset.HYSYSReactions[i].GetType() == typeof(HYSYSConversionReaction))
			{
				if (!flag)
				{
					conversionReaction = reactionPackageManager.Reactions.Add(iD, "ConversionRxn") as ConversionReaction;
				}
				else if (flag)
				{
					conversionReaction = reactionPackageManager.Reactions[iD] as ConversionReaction;
				}
				backDoor.SendAttachObject("RxnPackageManager.300/RxnSet.300(" + rxset.ID + "):Rxn.400." + i, conversionReaction);
			}
			else if (rxset.HYSYSReactions[i].GetType() == typeof(HYSYSEquilibriumReaction))
			{
				if (!flag)
				{
					equilibriumReaction = reactionPackageManager.Reactions.Add(iD, "equilibriumrxn") as EquilibriumReaction;
				}
				else if (flag)
				{
					equilibriumReaction = reactionPackageManager.Reactions[iD] as EquilibriumReaction;
				}
				backDoor.SendAttachObject("RxnPackageManager.300/RxnSet.300(" + rxset.ID + "):Rxn.400." + i, equilibriumReaction);
			}
			else if (rxset.HYSYSReactions[i].GetType() == typeof(HYSYSKineticReaction))
			{
				if (!flag)
				{
					kineticReaction = reactionPackageManager.Reactions.Add(iD, "kineticrxn") as KineticReaction;
				}
				else if (flag)
				{
					kineticReaction = reactionPackageManager.Reactions[iD] as KineticReaction;
				}
				backDoor.SendAttachObject("RxnPackageManager.300/RxnSet.300(" + rxset.ID + "):Rxn.400." + i, kineticReaction);
			}
			try
			{
				int num = 0;
				IDictionary<string, double> dictionary = new Dictionary<string, double>();
				if (iD.ToUpper().StartsWith("SHIFT-"))
				{
					string[] array;
					if (rxset.HYSYSReactions[i].IsAspenPlusProperty)
					{
						array = new string[4]
						{
							P2Objects.P2Component.GetPlusCompNameFromHYSYS("CO"),
							P2Objects.P2Component.GetPlusCompNameFromHYSYS("H2O"),
							P2Objects.P2Component.GetPlusCompNameFromHYSYS("CO2"),
							P2Objects.P2Component.GetPlusCompNameFromHYSYS("Hydrogen")
						};
						dictionary.Add(P2Objects.P2Component.GetPlusCompNameFromHYSYS("CO"), -1.0);
						dictionary.Add(P2Objects.P2Component.GetPlusCompNameFromHYSYS("H2O"), -1.0);
					}
					else
					{
						array = new string[4] { "CO", "H2O", "CO2", "Hydrogen" };
						dictionary.Add("CO", -1.0);
						dictionary.Add("H2O", -1.0);
					}
					double[] array2 = new double[4] { -1.0, -1.0, 1.0, 1.0 };
					for (int j = 0; j < 4; j++)
					{
						backDoor.SendAttachObject2("RxnPackageManager.300/Rxn.300(" + iD + "):AbstractComp.400." + j, "ComponentList.200(Master Component List)/AbstractComp.300(" + array[j] + ") ");
						(backDoor.get_BackDoorVariable((object)("RxnPackageManager.300/Rxn.300(" + iD + "):Index.500." + j)).Variable as InternalRealVariable).SetValue(array2[j], "");
					}
				}
				else if (iD.ToUpper().StartsWith("METHANATION"))
				{
					string[] array3;
					if (rxset.HYSYSReactions[i].IsAspenPlusProperty)
					{
						array3 = new string[4]
						{
							P2Objects.P2Component.GetPlusCompNameFromHYSYS("CO"),
							P2Objects.P2Component.GetPlusCompNameFromHYSYS("Hydrogen"),
							P2Objects.P2Component.GetPlusCompNameFromHYSYS("Methane"),
							P2Objects.P2Component.GetPlusCompNameFromHYSYS("H2O")
						};
						dictionary.Add(P2Objects.P2Component.GetPlusCompNameFromHYSYS("CO"), -1.0);
						dictionary.Add(P2Objects.P2Component.GetPlusCompNameFromHYSYS("Hydrogen"), -1.0);
					}
					else
					{
						array3 = new string[4] { "CO", "Hydrogen", "Methane", "H2O" };
						dictionary.Add("CO", -1.0);
						dictionary.Add("Hydrogen", -3.0);
					}
					double[] array4 = new double[4] { -1.0, -3.0, 1.0, 1.0 };
					for (int k = 0; k < 4; k++)
					{
						backDoor.SendAttachObject2("RxnPackageManager.300/Rxn.300(" + iD + "):AbstractComp.400." + k, "ComponentList.200(Master Component List)/AbstractComp.300(" + array3[k] + ") ");
						(backDoor.get_BackDoorVariable((object)("RxnPackageManager.300/Rxn.300(" + iD + "):Index.500." + k)).Variable as InternalRealVariable).SetValue(array4[k], "");
					}
				}
				else
				{
					foreach (KeyValuePair<string, double> item in rxset.HYSYSReactions[i].Stoichiometry)
					{
						string text = item.Key;
						if (rxset.HYSYSReactions[i].IsAspenPlusProperty)
						{
							text = P2Objects.P2Component.GetPlusCompNameFromHYSYS(text);
						}
						double value = item.Value;
						backDoor.SendAttachObject2("RxnPackageManager.300/Rxn.300(" + iD + "):AbstractComp.400." + num, "ComponentList.200(Master Component List)/AbstractComp.300(" + text + ") ");
						(backDoor.get_BackDoorVariable((object)("RxnPackageManager.300/Rxn.300(" + iD + "):Index.500." + num)).Variable as InternalRealVariable).SetValue(value, "");
						num++;
						if (value < 0.0)
						{
							dictionary.Add(text, value);
						}
					}
				}
				if (conversionReaction != null)
				{
					backDoor3 = conversionReaction as BackDoor;
				}
				else if (equilibriumReaction != null)
				{
					backDoor3 = equilibriumReaction as BackDoor;
				}
				else if (kineticReaction != null)
				{
					backDoor3 = kineticReaction as BackDoor;
				}
				backDoor3.SendBackDoorMessage("BalanceStoich");
				if (rxset.HYSYSReactions[i].GetType() == typeof(HYSYSConversionReaction))
				{
					if (!(rxset.HYSYSReactions[i] is HYSYSConversionReaction hYSYSConversionReaction))
					{
						continue;
					}
					if (hYSYSConversionReaction.ReactionBasis != null)
					{
						if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(hYSYSConversionReaction.ReactionBasis.BaseComponent))
						{
							string text2 = hYSYSConversionReaction.ReactionBasis.BaseComponent;
							if (rxset.HYSYSReactions[i].IsAspenPlusProperty)
							{
								text2 = P2Objects.P2Component.GetPlusCompNameFromHYSYS(text2);
							}
							backDoor.SendAttachObject2("RxnPackageManager.300/Rxn.300(" + iD + "):Comp.500", "ComponentList.200(Master Component List)/AbstractComp.300(" + text2 + ")");
						}
						else
						{
							string text3 = dictionary.Keys.ToArray()[0];
							backDoor.SendAttachObject2("RxnPackageManager.300/Rxn.300(" + iD + "):Comp.500", "ComponentList.200(Master Component List)/AbstractComp.300(" + text3 + ")");
						}
						if (hYSYSConversionReaction.ReactionBasis.ReactionPhase == HYSYSConversionReactionPhase.LiquidPhase)
						{
							(backDoor2.get_BackDoorVariable((object)("Rxn.300(" + iD + ") :Selection.501")).Variable as InternalRealVariable).SetValue(1.0, "");
						}
						else if (hYSYSConversionReaction.ReactionBasis.ReactionPhase == HYSYSConversionReactionPhase.VapourPhase)
						{
							(backDoor2.get_BackDoorVariable((object)("Rxn.300(" + iD + ") :Selection.501")).Variable as InternalRealVariable).SetValue(0.0, "");
						}
						else
						{
							(backDoor2.get_BackDoorVariable((object)("Rxn.300(" + iD + ") :Selection.501")).Variable as InternalRealVariable).SetValue(5.0, "");
						}
					}
					if (hYSYSConversionReaction.C0 != 0.0)
					{
						(backDoor2.get_BackDoorVariable((object)("Rxn.300(" + iD + ") :ExtraData.600.0")).Variable as InternalRealVariable).SetValue(hYSYSConversionReaction.C0 * 100.0, "");
					}
					if (hYSYSConversionReaction.C1 != 0.0)
					{
						(backDoor2.get_BackDoorVariable((object)("Rxn.300(" + iD + ") :ExtraData.600.1")).Variable as InternalRealVariable).SetValue(hYSYSConversionReaction.C1 * 100.0, "");
					}
					if (hYSYSConversionReaction.C2 != 0.0)
					{
						(backDoor2.get_BackDoorVariable((object)("Rxn.300(" + iD + ") :ExtraData.600.2")).Variable as InternalRealVariable).SetValue(hYSYSConversionReaction.C2 * 100.0, "");
					}
					continue;
				}
				if (rxset.HYSYSReactions[i].GetType() == typeof(HYSYSEquilibriumReaction))
				{
					if (!(rxset.HYSYSReactions[i] is HYSYSEquilibriumReaction hYSYSEquilibriumReaction))
					{
						continue;
					}
					string.IsNullOrEmpty(hYSYSEquilibriumReaction.BaseComponent);
					if (hYSYSEquilibriumReaction.Phase == EquilibriumReactionPhase.LiquidPhase)
					{
						(backDoor2.get_BackDoorVariable((object)("Rxn.300(" + iD + ") :Selection.501")).Variable as InternalRealVariable).SetValue(1.0, "");
					}
					else if (hYSYSEquilibriumReaction.Phase == EquilibriumReactionPhase.VapourPhase)
					{
						(backDoor2.get_BackDoorVariable((object)("Rxn.300(" + iD + ") :Selection.501")).Variable as InternalRealVariable).SetValue(0.0, "");
					}
					if (hYSYSEquilibriumReaction.KeqParams != null)
					{
						if (hYSYSEquilibriumReaction.KeqParams.A != 0.0)
						{
							(backDoor2.get_BackDoorVariable((object)("Rxn.300(" + iD + "):Ratio.600")).Variable as InternalRealVariable).Value = hYSYSEquilibriumReaction.KeqParams.A;
						}
						if (hYSYSEquilibriumReaction.KeqParams.B != 0.0)
						{
							(backDoor2.get_BackDoorVariable((object)("Rxn.300(" + iD + "):Ratio.601")).Variable as InternalRealVariable).Value = hYSYSEquilibriumReaction.KeqParams.B;
						}
						if (hYSYSEquilibriumReaction.KeqParams.C != 0.0)
						{
							(backDoor2.get_BackDoorVariable((object)("Rxn.300(" + iD + "):Ratio.602")).Variable as InternalRealVariable).Value = hYSYSEquilibriumReaction.KeqParams.C;
						}
						if (hYSYSEquilibriumReaction.KeqParams.D != 0.0)
						{
							(backDoor2.get_BackDoorVariable((object)("Rxn.300(" + iD + "):Ratio.603")).Variable as InternalRealVariable).Value = hYSYSEquilibriumReaction.KeqParams.D;
						}
						if (hYSYSEquilibriumReaction.KeqParams.E != 0.0)
						{
							(backDoor2.get_BackDoorVariable((object)("Rxn.300(" + iD + "):Ratio.604")).Variable as InternalRealVariable).Value = hYSYSEquilibriumReaction.KeqParams.E;
						}
						if (hYSYSEquilibriumReaction.KeqParams.F != 0.0)
						{
							(backDoor2.get_BackDoorVariable((object)("Rxn.300(" + iD + "):Ratio.605")).Variable as InternalRealVariable).Value = hYSYSEquilibriumReaction.KeqParams.F;
						}
						if (hYSYSEquilibriumReaction.KeqParams.G != 0.0)
						{
							(backDoor2.get_BackDoorVariable((object)("Rxn.300(" + iD + "):Ratio.606")).Variable as InternalRealVariable).Value = hYSYSEquilibriumReaction.KeqParams.G;
						}
						if (hYSYSEquilibriumReaction.KeqParams.H != 0.0)
						{
							(backDoor2.get_BackDoorVariable((object)("Rxn.300(" + iD + "):Ratio.607")).Variable as InternalRealVariable).Value = hYSYSEquilibriumReaction.KeqParams.H;
						}
					}
					if (hYSYSEquilibriumReaction.DT != null)
					{
						(backDoor2.get_BackDoorVariable((object)("Rxn.300(" + iD + "):DeltaTemp.600")).Variable as InternalRealVariable).SetValue(hYSYSEquilibriumReaction.DT.Value, hYSYSEquilibriumReaction.DT.Unit);
					}
					if (hYSYSEquilibriumReaction.C0 != 0.0)
					{
						(backDoor2.get_BackDoorVariable((object)("Rxn.300(" + iD + "):Multiplier.600.0")).Variable as InternalRealVariable).Value = hYSYSEquilibriumReaction.C0 * 100.0;
					}
					if (hYSYSEquilibriumReaction.C1 != 0.0)
					{
						(backDoor2.get_BackDoorVariable((object)("Rxn.300(" + iD + "):Multiplier.600.1")).Variable as InternalRealVariable).Value = hYSYSEquilibriumReaction.C1 * 100.0;
					}
					if (hYSYSEquilibriumReaction.C2 != 0.0)
					{
						(backDoor2.get_BackDoorVariable((object)("Rxn.300(" + iD + "):Multiplier.600.2")).Variable as InternalRealVariable).Value = hYSYSEquilibriumReaction.C2 * 100.0;
					}
					continue;
				}
				if (!(rxset.HYSYSReactions[i].GetType() == typeof(HYSYSKineticReaction)) || !(rxset.HYSYSReactions[i] is HYSYSKineticReaction hYSYSKineticReaction))
				{
					continue;
				}
				if (hYSYSKineticReaction.FrequencyFactor != 0.0)
				{
					(backDoor2.get_BackDoorVariable((object)("Rxn.300(" + iD + ") :Ratio.600")).Variable as InternalRealVariable).SetValue(hYSYSKineticReaction.FrequencyFactor, "");
				}
				if (hYSYSKineticReaction.ActivationEnergy != null)
				{
					(backDoor2.get_BackDoorVariable((object)("Rxn.300(" + iD + "):Enthalpy.600")).Variable as InternalRealVariable).SetValue(hYSYSKineticReaction.ActivationEnergy.Value, hYSYSKineticReaction.ActivationEnergy.Unit);
				}
				(backDoor2.get_BackDoorVariable((object)("Rxn.300(" + iD + "):Multiplier.600")).Variable as InternalRealVariable).SetValue(hYSYSKineticReaction.ExtReacRateConst, string.Empty);
				if (hYSYSKineticReaction.Basis == RXBasis.ACTIVITY)
				{
					(backDoor2.get_BackDoorVariable((object)("Rxn.300(" + iD + "):Selection.600")).Variable as InternalRealVariable).SetValue(1.0, string.Empty);
				}
				else if (hYSYSKineticReaction.Basis == RXBasis.CONCENTRATION)
				{
					(backDoor2.get_BackDoorVariable((object)("Rxn.300(" + iD + "):Selection.600")).Variable as InternalRealVariable).SetValue(3.0, string.Empty);
				}
				else if (hYSYSKineticReaction.Basis == RXBasis.FUGACITY)
				{
					(backDoor2.get_BackDoorVariable((object)("Rxn.300(" + iD + "):Selection.600")).Variable as InternalRealVariable).SetValue(9.0, string.Empty);
				}
				else if (hYSYSKineticReaction.Basis == RXBasis.MASSFRACTION)
				{
					(backDoor2.get_BackDoorVariable((object)("Rxn.300(" + iD + "):Selection.600")).Variable as InternalRealVariable).SetValue(6.0, string.Empty);
				}
				else if (hYSYSKineticReaction.Basis == RXBasis.MOLEFRACTION)
				{
					(backDoor2.get_BackDoorVariable((object)("Rxn.300(" + iD + "):Selection.600")).Variable as InternalRealVariable).SetValue(5.0, string.Empty);
				}
				else if (hYSYSKineticReaction.Basis == RXBasis.PARTIALPRESSURE)
				{
					(backDoor2.get_BackDoorVariable((object)("Rxn.300(" + iD + "):Selection.600")).Variable as InternalRealVariable).SetValue(2.0, string.Empty);
				}
				if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(hYSYSKineticReaction.BaseComponent))
				{
					string text4 = hYSYSKineticReaction.BaseComponent;
					if (rxset.HYSYSReactions[i].IsAspenPlusProperty)
					{
						text4 = P2Objects.P2Component.GetPlusCompNameFromHYSYS(text4);
					}
					backDoor.SendAttachObject2("RxnPackageManager.300/Rxn.300(" + iD + "):Comp.500", "ComponentList.200(Master Component List)/AbstractComp.300(" + text4 + ")");
				}
				if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(hYSYSKineticReaction.RxnPhase))
				{
					if (hYSYSKineticReaction.RxnPhase.StartsWith("L"))
					{
						(backDoor2.get_BackDoorVariable((object)("Rxn.300(" + iD + ") :Selection.501")).Variable as InternalRealVariable).SetValue(1.0, "");
					}
					else if (hYSYSKineticReaction.RxnPhase.StartsWith("V"))
					{
						(backDoor2.get_BackDoorVariable((object)("Rxn.300(" + iD + ") :Selection.501")).Variable as InternalRealVariable).SetValue(0.0, "");
					}
					else
					{
						(backDoor2.get_BackDoorVariable((object)("Rxn.300(" + iD + ") :Selection.501")).Variable as InternalRealVariable).SetValue(5.0, "");
					}
				}
				if (hYSYSKineticReaction.FwdOrder == null)
				{
					continue;
				}
				foreach (KeyValuePair<string, double> item2 in hYSYSKineticReaction.FwdOrder)
				{
					int num2 = -1;
					foreach (KeyValuePair<string, double> item3 in rxset.HYSYSReactions[i].Stoichiometry)
					{
						num2++;
						if (item2.Key == item3.Key)
						{
							break;
						}
					}
					(backDoor2.get_BackDoorVariable((object)("Rxn.300(" + iD + "):ExtraData.600." + num2)).Variable as InternalRealVariable).Value = item2.Value;
				}
			}
			catch (Exception)
			{
				DebugPrint.OutputDebugString(DebugLevel.BuildHYSYSCase, WarningLevel.Error, string.Format(P2Globalization.GetString("ReactionsetConverter_SetReactionFailed"), rxset.ID, iD));
			}
		}
		backDoor2.SendAttachObject2(":RxnSet.301", "RxnSet.300(" + rxset.ID + ")");
		if (_BasisManager.FluidPackages.Names != null)
		{
			fluidPackage = _BasisManager.FluidPackages[0] as FluidPackage;
			if (_DefaultFPName != null && ((string[])_BasisManager.FluidPackages.Names).Contains(_DefaultFPName))
			{
				fluidPackage = _BasisManager.FluidPackages[_DefaultFPName] as FluidPackage;
			}
			backDoor2.SendBackDoorMessage(":attachTrackedSetToFPkg \"" + fluidPackage.name + "\"");
		}
	}
}
internal class HYSYSRecycleConverter : HYSYSObjectConverterBase, IDisposable
{
	private Recycle _Recycle;

	private HYSYSRecycle _Data;

	public void Dispose()
	{
	}

	public HYSYSRecycleConverter(SimulationCase simulationCase, HYSYSRecycle data)
		: base(simulationCase)
	{
		_Data = data;
		try
		{
			_Recycle = _Flowsheet.get_Operations((object)"RecycleObject").Add(data.UID, "RecycleObject") as Recycle;
			CreateRecycle();
		}
		catch (Exception)
		{
		}
	}

	private void CreateRecycle()
	{
		BackDoor backDoor = null;
		ProcessStream processStream = null;
		ProcessStream processStream2 = null;
		try
		{
			backDoor = _Recycle as BackDoor;
			processStream = GetProcessStream(_Data.OutletStreams[0].Trim());
			processStream2 = GetProcessStream(_Data.InletStreams[0].Trim());
			if (processStream2 != null)
			{
				try
				{
					backDoor.SendAttachObject(":Stream.501", processStream2);
				}
				catch (Exception)
				{
					DebugPrint.OutputDebugString(DebugLevel.BuildHYSYSCase, WarningLevel.Error, _Recycle.name + ": Set Inlet Stream Error");
				}
			}
			if (processStream != null)
			{
				try
				{
					backDoor.SendAttachObject(":Stream.500", processStream);
					return;
				}
				catch (Exception)
				{
					DebugPrint.OutputDebugString(DebugLevel.BuildHYSYSCase, WarningLevel.Error, _Recycle.name + ": Set Outlet Stream Error");
					return;
				}
			}
		}
		catch (COMException)
		{
		}
		finally
		{
			CleanResource.ReleaseComObjectHelper(backDoor, bFinalRelease: false);
		}
	}
}
public class SeparatorConverter : HYSYSObjectConverterBase
{
	private Separator _Separator;

	private HYSYSSeparator _Data;

	private BackDoor _SepBD;

	private InternalRealVariable _InternalVal;

	public SeparatorConverter(SimulationCase simulationCase, HYSYSSeparator data)
		: base(simulationCase)
	{
		_Data = data;
		if (_Data.SeparatorType == HYSYSSeparatorType.Separator)
		{
			try
			{
				_Separator = _Flowsheet.get_Operations((object)"FlashTankObject").Add(data.UID, "FlashTankObject") as Separator;
				_SepBD = _Separator as BackDoor;
				CreateSeparator();
				return;
			}
			catch (Exception)
			{
				return;
			}
		}
		if (_Data.SeparatorType == HYSYSSeparatorType.ThreePhaseSep)
		{
			try
			{
				_Separator = _Flowsheet.get_Operations((object)"Sep3OpObject").Add(data.UID, "Sep3OpObject") as Separator;
				_SepBD = _Separator as BackDoor;
				CreateThreePhaseSep();
			}
			catch (Exception)
			{
			}
		}
	}

	private void CreateSeparator()
	{
		if (_Separator == null || _Data == null)
		{
			return;
		}
		string methodName = HYSYSFluidPackages.GetMethodName(_Data.FluidPackage);
		if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(methodName) && ((string[])_SimCase.BasisManager.FluidPackages.Names).Contains(methodName))
		{
			BackDoor backDoor = _SimCase.BasisManager.FluidPackages[methodName] as BackDoor;
			_SepBD.SendAttachObject(":CompList.300", backDoor);
		}
		SetSeparatorStreams();
		SetIsoThermal();
		SetAdiabatic();
		SetSeparatorDew();
		SetBubble();
		SetSeparatorTPSpec();
		SetEntrainment();
		SetSpec();
		InternalTextVariable internalTextVariable = _SepBD.get_BackDoorTextVariable((object)":Notes.400").Variable as InternalTextVariable;
		foreach (string convertNote in _Data.ConvertNotes)
		{
			internalTextVariable.Value = internalTextVariable.Value + convertNote + "\n";
		}
		if (_Data.GlobalDefine == null || _Data.GlobalDefine.Count <= 0)
		{
			return;
		}
		foreach (DEFINE item in _Data.GlobalDefine)
		{
			if (item.MainProperty.StartsWith(CUnitOperation.PRESSURE) && string.IsNullOrEmpty(item.SecondRefID))
			{
				_InternalVal = _SepBD.get_BackDoorVariable((object)":PressureDrop.500").Variable as InternalRealVariable;
				_InternalVal.Value = -32767.0;
				break;
			}
		}
	}

	private void SetSeparatorStreams()
	{
		if (_Data.InletStreams != null)
		{
			int num = 0;
			foreach (string inletStream in _Data.InletStreams)
			{
				BackDoor backDoor = GetProcessStream(inletStream) as BackDoor;
				_SepBD.SendAttachObject(":MaterialStream.502." + num, backDoor);
				num++;
			}
		}
		if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(_Data.VapourOutletStream))
		{
			BackDoor backDoor2 = GetProcessStream(_Data.VapourOutletStream) as BackDoor;
			_SepBD.SendAttachObject(":MaterialStream.501", backDoor2);
		}
		if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(_Data.LiquidOutletStream))
		{
			BackDoor backDoor3 = GetProcessStream(_Data.LiquidOutletStream) as BackDoor;
			_SepBD.SendAttachObject(":MaterialStream.500", backDoor3);
		}
		if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(_Data.EnergyStream))
		{
			BackDoor backDoor4 = GetEnergyStream(_Data.EnergyStream) as BackDoor;
			_SepBD.SendAttachObject(":EnergyStream.503", backDoor4);
		}
	}

	private void SetSeparatorDew()
	{
		if (_Data.IsDew && !string.IsNullOrEmpty(_Data.VapourOutletStream))
		{
			ProcessStream processStream = GetProcessStream(_Data.VapourOutletStream);
			ProcessStream processStream2 = GetProcessStream(_Data.LiquidOutletStream);
			GetEnergyStream(_Data.EnergyStream);
			if (_Data.OutLetStreamTemp != null)
			{
				_InternalVal = _SepBD.get_BackDoorVariable((object)":PressureDrop.500").Variable as InternalRealVariable;
				_InternalVal.Value = -32767.0;
				processStream.Temperature.SetValue(_Data.OutLetStreamTemp.Value, _Data.OutLetStreamTemp.Unit);
			}
			else if (_Data.OutletStreamPressure != null)
			{
				_InternalVal = _SepBD.get_BackDoorVariable((object)":PressureDrop.500").Variable as InternalRealVariable;
				_InternalVal.Value = -32767.0;
				processStream.Pressure.SetValue(_Data.OutletStreamPressure.Value, _Data.OutletStreamPressure.Unit);
			}
			else if (_Data.InletDeltaPressure != null)
			{
				_InternalVal = _SepBD.get_BackDoorVariable((object)":PressureDrop.500").Variable as InternalRealVariable;
				_InternalVal.SetValue(_Data.InletDeltaPressure.Value, _Data.InletDeltaPressure.Unit);
			}
			processStream2.MolarFlow.Value = 0.0;
		}
	}

	private void SetSeparatorTPSpec()
	{
		if (_Data.HasSpec && !string.IsNullOrEmpty(_Data.VapourOutletStream))
		{
			ProcessStream processStream = GetProcessStream(_Data.VapourOutletStream);
			if (_Data.OutLetStreamTemp != null)
			{
				_InternalVal = _SepBD.get_BackDoorVariable((object)":PressureDrop.500").Variable as InternalRealVariable;
				_InternalVal.Value = -32767.0;
				processStream.Temperature.SetValue(_Data.OutLetStreamTemp.Value, _Data.OutLetStreamTemp.Unit);
			}
			else if (_Data.OutletStreamPressure != null)
			{
				_InternalVal = _SepBD.get_BackDoorVariable((object)":PressureDrop.500").Variable as InternalRealVariable;
				_InternalVal.Value = -32767.0;
				processStream.Pressure.SetValue(_Data.OutletStreamPressure.Value, _Data.OutletStreamPressure.Unit);
			}
			else if (_Data.InletDeltaPressure != null)
			{
				_InternalVal = _SepBD.get_BackDoorVariable((object)":PressureDrop.500").Variable as InternalRealVariable;
				_InternalVal.SetValue(_Data.InletDeltaPressure.Value, _Data.InletDeltaPressure.Unit);
			}
		}
	}

	private void CreateThreePhaseSep()
	{
		if (_Separator == null || _Data == null)
		{
			return;
		}
		string methodName = HYSYSFluidPackages.GetMethodName(_Data.FluidPackage);
		if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(methodName))
		{
			BackDoor backDoor = _SimCase.BasisManager.FluidPackages[methodName] as BackDoor;
			_SepBD.SendAttachObject(":CompList.300", backDoor);
		}
		SetThreeSepStreams();
		SetIsoThermal();
		SetAdiabatic();
		SetThreeSepDew();
		SetBubble();
		SetThreeSepTPSpec();
		SetEntrainment();
		SetSpec();
		InternalTextVariable internalTextVariable = _SepBD.get_BackDoorTextVariable((object)":Notes.400").Variable as InternalTextVariable;
		foreach (string convertNote in _Data.ConvertNotes)
		{
			internalTextVariable.Value = internalTextVariable.Value + convertNote + "\n";
		}
	}

	private void SetThreeSepStreams()
	{
		if (_Data.InletStreams != null)
		{
			int num = 0;
			foreach (string inletStream in _Data.InletStreams)
			{
				BackDoor backDoor = GetProcessStream(inletStream) as BackDoor;
				_SepBD.SendAttachObject(":MaterialStream.502." + num, backDoor);
				num++;
			}
		}
		if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(_Data.VapourOutletStream))
		{
			BackDoor backDoor2 = GetProcessStream(_Data.VapourOutletStream) as BackDoor;
			_SepBD.SendAttachObject(":MaterialStream.501", backDoor2);
		}
		if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(_Data.LiquidOutletStream))
		{
			BackDoor backDoor3 = GetProcessStream(_Data.LiquidOutletStream) as BackDoor;
			_SepBD.SendAttachObject(":MaterialStream.500", backDoor3);
		}
		if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(_Data.HeavyLiquidtStream))
		{
			BackDoor backDoor4 = GetProcessStream(_Data.HeavyLiquidtStream) as BackDoor;
			_SepBD.SendAttachObject(":MaterialStream.610", backDoor4);
		}
		if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(_Data.EnergyStream))
		{
			BackDoor backDoor5 = GetEnergyStream(_Data.EnergyStream) as BackDoor;
			_SepBD.SendAttachObject(":EnergyStream.503", backDoor5);
		}
	}

	private void SetThreeSepDew()
	{
		if (_Data.IsDew && !string.IsNullOrEmpty(_Data.VapourOutletStream))
		{
			ProcessStream processStream = GetProcessStream(_Data.VapourOutletStream);
			ProcessStream processStream2 = GetProcessStream(_Data.LiquidOutletStream);
			ProcessStream processStream3 = GetProcessStream(_Data.HeavyLiquidtStream);
			GetEnergyStream(_Data.EnergyStream);
			if (_Data.OutLetStreamTemp != null)
			{
				_InternalVal = _SepBD.get_BackDoorVariable((object)":PressureDrop.500").Variable as InternalRealVariable;
				_InternalVal.Value = -32767.0;
				processStream.Temperature.SetValue(_Data.OutLetStreamTemp.Value, _Data.OutLetStreamTemp.Unit);
			}
			else if (_Data.OutletStreamPressure != null)
			{
				_InternalVal = _SepBD.get_BackDoorVariable((object)":PressureDrop.500").Variable as InternalRealVariable;
				_InternalVal.Value = -32767.0;
				processStream.Pressure.SetValue(_Data.OutletStreamPressure.Value, _Data.OutletStreamPressure.Unit);
			}
			else if (_Data.InletDeltaPressure != null)
			{
				_InternalVal = _SepBD.get_BackDoorVariable((object)":PressureDrop.500").Variable as InternalRealVariable;
				_InternalVal.SetValue(_Data.InletDeltaPressure.Value, _Data.InletDeltaPressure.Unit);
			}
			processStream2.MolarFlow.Value = 0.0;
			processStream3.MolarFlow.Value = 0.0;
		}
	}

	private void SetThreeSepTPSpec()
	{
		if (_Data.HasSpec && !string.IsNullOrEmpty(_Data.VapourOutletStream))
		{
			ProcessStream processStream = GetProcessStream(_Data.VapourOutletStream);
			if (_Data.OutLetStreamTemp != null)
			{
				_InternalVal = _SepBD.get_BackDoorVariable((object)":PressureDrop.500").Variable as InternalRealVariable;
				_InternalVal.Value = -32767.0;
				processStream.Temperature.SetValue(_Data.OutLetStreamTemp.Value, _Data.OutLetStreamTemp.Unit);
			}
			else if (_Data.OutletStreamPressure != null)
			{
				_InternalVal = _SepBD.get_BackDoorVariable((object)":PressureDrop.500").Variable as InternalRealVariable;
				_InternalVal.Value = -32767.0;
				processStream.Pressure.SetValue(_Data.OutletStreamPressure.Value, _Data.OutletStreamPressure.Unit);
			}
			else if (_Data.InletDeltaPressure != null)
			{
				_InternalVal = _SepBD.get_BackDoorVariable((object)":PressureDrop.500").Variable as InternalRealVariable;
				_InternalVal.SetValue(_Data.InletDeltaPressure.Value, _Data.InletDeltaPressure.Unit);
			}
			string methodName = HYSYSFluidPackages.GetMethodName(_Data.FluidPackage);
			if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(methodName) && ((string[])_SimCase.BasisManager.FluidPackages.Names).Contains(methodName))
			{
				BackDoor backDoor = _SimCase.BasisManager.FluidPackages[methodName] as BackDoor;
				_SepBD.SendAttachObject(":CompList.300", backDoor);
			}
		}
	}

	private void SetSpec()
	{
		if (_Data.SPEC == null || string.IsNullOrEmpty(_Data.SPEC.PrimaryID) || _Data.SPEC.SpecType != SpecificationType.STREAM)
		{
			return;
		}
		Specification sPEC = _Data.SPEC;
		string flowbasis = "M";
		string unit = string.Empty;
		double refValue = -1.0;
		ProcessStream processStream = GetProcessStream(sPEC.PrimaryID);
		HYSYSStream hYSYSStream = new HYSYSStream();
		if (sPEC.Operator == Specification.OperatorType.divide && sPEC.PrimaryName.StartsWith(CUnitOperation.STREAM) && sPEC.ReferenceName.StartsWith(CUnitOperation.STREAM) && sPEC.PrimaryPropertyName.StartsWith(CUnitOperation.RATE) && sPEC.ReferencePropertyName.StartsWith(CUnitOperation.RATE))
		{
			try
			{
				ProcessStream processStream2 = GetProcessStream(sPEC.PrimaryID);
				ProcessStream processStream3 = GetProcessStream(sPEC.ReferenceID);
				if (processStream2 != null && processStream3 != null)
				{
					SetOp setOp = null;
					setOp = _Flowsheet.get_Operations((object)"SetOpObject").Add("Set_" + _Data.UID + "_Rate", "SetOpObject") as SetOp;
					string flowbasis2 = string.Empty;
					string unit2 = string.Empty;
					double refValue2 = 0.0;
					ParseUtilty.GetFlowbasisAndUnit(sPEC.PrimaryPropertyName, ref flowbasis2, ref unit2, ref refValue2);
					switch (UnitConverter.GetHysysValue(UnitClassEnum.Rate, new TypeUnitValue(unit2, flowbasis2, 1.0)).Type)
					{
					case "W":
					case "WT":
						setOp.TargetVariable = processStream2.MassFlow;
						break;
					case "M":
						setOp.TargetVariable = processStream2.MolarFlow;
						break;
					case "V":
					case "L":
					case "LV":
						setOp.TargetVariable = processStream2.StdLiqVolFlow;
						break;
					}
					setOp.SourceObject = processStream3;
					if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(sPEC.OperatorName) && (sPEC.OperatorName.StartsWith("DIVI") || sPEC.OperatorName.StartsWith("RATI")))
					{
						setOp.Multiplier.Value = sPEC.Value.Value;
					}
					else if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(sPEC.OperatorName) && (sPEC.OperatorName.StartsWith("MULT") || sPEC.OperatorName.StartsWith("TIME")))
					{
						setOp.Multiplier.Value = 1.0 / sPEC.Value.Value;
					}
				}
				return;
			}
			catch
			{
			}
		}
		else if (sPEC.Operator != Specification.OperatorType.none)
		{
			_Data.ConvertNotes.Add("Global specification \"" + sPEC.P2SpecExpression + "\"  was not converted");
			return;
		}
		ParseUtilty.GetFlowbasisAndUnit(sPEC.PrimaryPropertyName, ref flowbasis, ref unit, ref refValue);
		if (sPEC.PrimaryPropertyName.ToUpper().Trim().StartsWith(CStream.RATE) && sPEC.PrimaryPropertyName.ToUpper().IndexOf("COMP") == -1)
		{
			hYSYSStream.Rate = UnitConverter.GetHysysValue(UnitClassEnum.Rate, sPEC.Value.Value, unit, flowbasis);
			SetStreamTPR(hYSYSStream, processStream);
		}
		else if (sPEC.PrimaryPropertyName.ToUpper().Trim().StartsWith(CStream.RATE) && sPEC.PrimaryPropertyName.ToUpper().IndexOf("COMP") > -1)
		{
			_Data.ConvertNotes.Add(string.Format(P2Globalization.GetString("LSNotConvert"), sPEC.P2SpecExpression, _Data.UID));
		}
		else if (sPEC.PrimaryPropertyName.ToUpper().Trim().StartsWith(CStream.FRACTION) && sPEC.PrimaryPropertyName.ToUpper().IndexOf("COMP") > -1)
		{
			_Data.ConvertNotes.Add(string.Format(P2Globalization.GetString("LSNotConvert"), sPEC.P2SpecExpression, _Data.UID));
		}
		else if (sPEC.PrimaryPropertyName.ToUpper().Trim().StartsWith(CStream.PERCENT) && sPEC.PrimaryPropertyName.ToUpper().IndexOf("COMP") > -1)
		{
			_Data.ConvertNotes.Add(string.Format(P2Globalization.GetString("LSNotConvert"), sPEC.P2SpecExpression, _Data.UID));
		}
		else if (sPEC.PrimaryPropertyName.ToUpper().Trim().StartsWith(CStream.PPM) && sPEC.PrimaryPropertyName.ToUpper().IndexOf("COMP") > -1)
		{
			_Data.ConvertNotes.Add(string.Format(P2Globalization.GetString("LSNotConvert"), sPEC.P2SpecExpression, _Data.UID));
		}
		else if (sPEC.PrimaryPropertyName.ToUpper().Trim().StartsWith(CStream.GRAINS))
		{
			_Data.ConvertNotes.Add(string.Format(P2Globalization.GetString("LSNotConvert"), sPEC.P2SpecExpression, _Data.UID));
		}
		else if (sPEC.PrimaryPropertyName.ToUpper().Trim().StartsWith(CStream.TEMP))
		{
			hYSYSStream.Temp = UnitConverter.GetHysysValue(UnitClassEnum.Temperature, sPEC.Value);
			SetStreamTPR(hYSYSStream, processStream);
		}
		else if (sPEC.PrimaryPropertyName.ToUpper().Trim().StartsWith(CStream.PRESSURE))
		{
			hYSYSStream.Pressure = UnitConverter.GetHysysValue(UnitClassEnum.Pressure, sPEC.Value);
			SetStreamTPR(hYSYSStream, processStream);
		}
		else if (sPEC.PrimaryPropertyName.ToUpper().Trim().StartsWith(CStream.MOISTURE))
		{
			_Data.ConvertNotes.Add(string.Format(P2Globalization.GetString("LSNotConvert"), sPEC.P2SpecExpression, _Data.UID));
		}
		else if (sPEC.PrimaryPropertyName.ToUpper().Trim().StartsWith(CStream.LFACT))
		{
			_Data.ConvertNotes.Add(string.Format(P2Globalization.GetString("LSNotConvert"), sPEC.P2SpecExpression, _Data.UID));
		}
		else if (sPEC.PrimaryPropertyName.ToUpper().Trim().StartsWith(CStream.VFRAC))
		{
			_Data.ConvertNotes.Add(string.Format(P2Globalization.GetString("LSNotConvert"), sPEC.P2SpecExpression, _Data.UID));
		}
		else if (sPEC.PrimaryPropertyName.ToUpper().Trim().StartsWith(CStream.WFRAC))
		{
			_Data.ConvertNotes.Add(string.Format(P2Globalization.GetString("LSNotConvert"), sPEC.P2SpecExpression, _Data.UID));
		}
		else if (sPEC.PrimaryPropertyName.ToUpper().Trim().StartsWith(CStream.MW))
		{
			_Data.ConvertNotes.Add(string.Format(P2Globalization.GetString("LSNotConvert"), sPEC.P2SpecExpression, _Data.UID));
		}
		else if (sPEC.PrimaryPropertyName.ToUpper().Trim().StartsWith(CStream.SPGR))

			_Data.ConvertNotes.Add(string.Format(P2Globalization.GetString("LSNotConvert"), sPEC.P2SpecExpression, _Data.UID));

		else if (sPEC.PrimaryPropertyName.ToUpper().Trim().StartsWith(CStream.DENSITY))
