			_Data.ConvertNotes.Add(string.Format(P2Globalization.GetString("LSNotConvert"), sPEC.P2SpecExpression, _Data.UID));
		}
		else if (sPEC.PrimaryPropertyName.ToUpper().Trim().StartsWith(CStream.MOLVOL))
		{
			_Data.ConvertNotes.Add(string.Format(P2Globalization.GetString("LSNotConvert"), sPEC.P2SpecExpression, _Data.UID));
		}
		else if (sPEC.PrimaryPropertyName.ToUpper().Trim().StartsWith(CStream.HOTVOL))
		{
			_Data.ConvertNotes.Add(string.Format(P2Globalization.GetString("LSNotConvert"), sPEC.P2SpecExpression, _Data.UID));
		}
		else if (sPEC.PrimaryPropertyName.ToUpper().Trim().StartsWith(CStream.ENTHALPY))
		{
			_Data.ConvertNotes.Add(string.Format(P2Globalization.GetString("LSNotConvert"), sPEC.P2SpecExpression, _Data.UID));
		}
		else if (sPEC.PrimaryPropertyName.ToUpper().Trim().StartsWith(CStream.TBP))
		{
			_Data.ConvertNotes.Add(string.Format(P2Globalization.GetString("LSNotConvert"), sPEC.P2SpecExpression, _Data.UID));
		}
		else if (sPEC.PrimaryPropertyName.ToUpper().Trim().StartsWith(CStream.D86))
		{
			_Data.ConvertNotes.Add(string.Format(P2Globalization.GetString("LSNotConvert"), sPEC.P2SpecExpression, _Data.UID));
		}
		else if (sPEC.PrimaryPropertyName.ToUpper().Trim().StartsWith(CStream.D1160))
		{
			_Data.ConvertNotes.Add(string.Format(P2Globalization.GetString("LSNotConvert"), sPEC.P2SpecExpression, _Data.UID));
		}
		else if (sPEC.PrimaryPropertyName.ToUpper().Trim().StartsWith(CStream.RVP))
		{
			_Data.ConvertNotes.Add(string.Format(P2Globalization.GetString("LSNotConvert"), sPEC.P2SpecExpression, _Data.UID));
		}
		else if (sPEC.PrimaryPropertyName.ToUpper().Trim().StartsWith(CStream.TVP))
		{
			_Data.ConvertNotes.Add(string.Format(P2Globalization.GetString("LSNotConvert"), sPEC.P2SpecExpression, _Data.UID));
		}
		else if (sPEC.PrimaryPropertyName.ToUpper().Trim().StartsWith(CStream.VISCOSITY))
		{
			_Data.ConvertNotes.Add(string.Format(P2Globalization.GetString("LSNotConvert"), sPEC.P2SpecExpression, _Data.UID));
		}
		else if (sPEC.PrimaryPropertyName.ToUpper().Trim().StartsWith(CStream.CONDUCTIVITY))
		{
			_Data.ConvertNotes.Add(string.Format(P2Globalization.GetString("LSNotConvert"), sPEC.P2SpecExpression, _Data.UID));
		}
		else if (sPEC.PrimaryPropertyName.ToUpper().Trim().StartsWith(CStream.SURFACE))
		{
			_Data.ConvertNotes.Add(string.Format(P2Globalization.GetString("LSNotConvert"), sPEC.P2SpecExpression, _Data.UID));
		}
		else if (sPEC.PrimaryPropertyName.ToUpper().Trim().StartsWith(CStream.SPROPERTY))
		{
			_Data.ConvertNotes.Add(string.Format(P2Globalization.GetString("LSNotConvert"), sPEC.P2SpecExpression, _Data.UID));
		}
	}

	private void SetStreamTPR(HYSYSStream stream, ProcessStream hysysStream)
	{
		if (hysysStream == null)
		{
			return;
		}
		BackDoor backDoor = null;
		if (stream.Source != null)
		{
			return;
		}
		if (stream.Temp != null)
		{
			hysysStream.Temperature.SetValue(stream.Temp.Value, stream.Temp.Unit);
		}
		if (stream.Pressure != null)
		{
			hysysStream.Pressure.SetValue(stream.Pressure.Value, stream.Pressure.Unit);
		}
		if (stream.VaporFrac != null && (stream.Temp == null || stream.Pressure == null))
		{
			hysysStream.VapourFraction.SetValue(stream.VaporFrac.Value, "");
		}
		if (stream.NeedSetRateZero)
		{
			backDoor = hysysStream as BackDoor;
			InternalRealVariable obj = backDoor.get_BackDoorVariable((object)":MassFlow.502.0").Variable as InternalRealVariable;
			obj.Value = 0.0;
			CleanResource.ReleaseComObjectHelper(obj, bFinalRelease: false);
		}
		if (stream.Rate == null || stream.Rate.Value == 0.0)
		{
			return;
		}
		backDoor = hysysStream as BackDoor;
		string type = stream.Rate.Type;
		if (type == null)
		{
			return;
		}
		int length = type.Length;
		if (length != 1)
		{
			if (length != 2)
			{
				return;
			}
			char c = type[0];
			if (c != 'G')
			{
				if (c != 'L')
				{
					if (c != 'W' || !(type == "WT"))
					{
						return;
					}
					goto IL_018f;
				}
				if (!(type == "LV"))
				{
					return;
				}
				goto IL_01c7;
			}
			if (!(type == "GV"))
			{
				return;
			}
		}
		else
		{
			char c = type[0];
			if ((uint)c > 77u)
			{
				if (c != 'V')
				{
					if (c != 'W')
					{
						return;
					}
					goto IL_018f;
				}
				goto IL_01c7;
			}
			switch (c)
			{
			default:
				return;
			case 'M':
			{
				InternalRealVariable obj2 = backDoor.get_BackDoorVariable((object)":MolarFlow.502.0").Variable as InternalRealVariable;
				obj2.SetValue(stream.Rate.Value, stream.Rate.Unit);
				CleanResource.ReleaseComObjectHelper(obj2, bFinalRelease: false);
				return;
			}
			case 'G':
				break;
			}
		}
		if (stream.IsAssayStream)
		{
			InternalRealVariable obj3 = backDoor.get_BackDoorVariable((object)":LiqVolFlow.502.0").Variable as InternalRealVariable;
			obj3.SetValue(stream.Rate.Value, stream.Rate.Unit);
			CleanResource.ReleaseComObjectHelper(obj3, bFinalRelease: false);
			return;
		}
		string unit = HYSYSUOMMap.GetDefault(UnitClassEnum.Rate, "GV").Unit;
		DoubleValue convertedValue = HYSYSUOMMap.GetConvertedValue(UnitClassEnum.Rate, new DoubleValue(stream.Rate.Unit, stream.Rate.Value), unit, "GV");
		convertedValue = new DoubleValue("kgmole/s", convertedValue.Value / 22.414);
		if (convertedValue != null)
		{
			InternalRealVariable obj4 = backDoor.get_BackDoorVariable((object)":MolarFlow.502.0").Variable as InternalRealVariable;
			obj4.SetValue(convertedValue.Value, convertedValue.Unit);
			CleanResource.ReleaseComObjectHelper(obj4, bFinalRelease: false);
		}
		return;
		IL_018f:
		InternalRealVariable obj5 = backDoor.get_BackDoorVariable((object)":MassFlow.502.0").Variable as InternalRealVariable;
		obj5.SetValue(stream.Rate.Value, stream.Rate.Unit);
		CleanResource.ReleaseComObjectHelper(obj5, bFinalRelease: false);
		return;
		IL_01c7:
		InternalRealVariable obj6 = backDoor.get_BackDoorVariable((object)":LiqVolFlow.502.0").Variable as InternalRealVariable;
		obj6.SetValue(stream.Rate.Value, stream.Rate.Unit);
		CleanResource.ReleaseComObjectHelper(obj6, bFinalRelease: false);
	}

	private void SetCompostion(HYSYSStream stream, ProcessStream hysysStream)
	{
		try
		{
			int num = ((string[])_SimCase.Flowsheet.FluidPackage.ComponentList.Components.Names).Length;
			if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(stream.ConvertNotes))
			{
				((hysysStream as BackDoor).get_BackDoorTextVariable((object)":Attribute.500").Variable as InternalTextVariable).Value = stream.ConvertNotes;
			}
			double[] array = new double[num];
			if (stream.Composition != null && stream.Composition.Data != null)
			{
				foreach (KeyValuePair<string, double> datum in stream.Composition.Data)
				{
					int hysysCompIndex = GetHysysCompIndex(datum.Key);
					if (hysysCompIndex < num && hysysCompIndex >= 0)
					{
						array[hysysCompIndex] = datum.Value;
					}
				}
			}
			if (stream.Composition.Basis == CompostionBasisTypes.MassFlow)
			{
				hysysStream.ComponentMassFlow.SetValues(array, stream.Composition.CompositionUOM);
			}
			else if (stream.Composition.Basis == CompostionBasisTypes.MoleFlow)
			{
				hysysStream.ComponentMolarFlow.SetValues(array, stream.Composition.CompositionUOM);
			}
			else if (stream.Composition.Basis == CompostionBasisTypes.LVFlow)
			{
				hysysStream.ComponentVolumeFlow.SetValues(array, stream.Composition.CompositionUOM);
			}
			else if (stream.Composition.Basis == CompostionBasisTypes.MoleFrac)
			{
				hysysStream.ComponentMolarFraction.SetValues(array, "");
			}
			else if (stream.Composition.Basis == CompostionBasisTypes.MassFrac)
			{
				hysysStream.ComponentMassFraction.SetValues(array, "");
			}
			else if (stream.Composition.Basis == CompostionBasisTypes.LVFrac)
			{
				hysysStream.ComponentVolumeFraction.SetValues(array, "");
			}
		}
		catch (Exception)
		{
		}
	}

	private void SetIsoThermal()
	{
		if (_Data.IsIsothermal && !string.IsNullOrEmpty(_Data.VapourOutletStream))
		{
			ProcessStream processStream = GetProcessStream(_Data.VapourOutletStream);
			if (_Data.OutLetStreamTemp != null)
			{
				processStream.Temperature.SetValue(_Data.OutLetStreamTemp.Value, _Data.OutLetStreamTemp.Unit);
			}
			if (_Data.OutletStreamPressure != null)
			{
				_InternalVal = _SepBD.get_BackDoorVariable((object)":PressureDrop.500").Variable as InternalRealVariable;
				_InternalVal.Value = -32767.0;
				processStream.Pressure.SetValue(_Data.OutletStreamPressure.Value, _Data.OutletStreamPressure.Unit);
			}
			else if (_Data.InletDeltaPressure != null)
			{
				_InternalVal = _SepBD.get_BackDoorVariable((object)":PressureDrop.500").Variable as InternalRealVariable;
				_InternalVal.SetValue(_Data.InletDeltaPressure.Value, _Data.InletDeltaPressure.Unit);
			}
		}
	}

	private void SetAdiabatic()
	{
		if (_Data.IsAdiabatic && !string.IsNullOrEmpty(_Data.VapourOutletStream))
		{
			ProcessStream processStream = GetProcessStream(_Data.VapourOutletStream);
			ProcessStream energyStream = GetEnergyStream(_Data.EnergyStream);
			if (_Data.OutLetStreamTemp != null)
			{
				processStream.Temperature.SetValue(_Data.OutLetStreamTemp.Value, _Data.OutLetStreamTemp.Unit);
				_InternalVal = _SepBD.get_BackDoorVariable((object)":PressureDrop.500").Variable as InternalRealVariable;
				_InternalVal.Value = -32767.0;
			}
			else if (_Data.OutletStreamPressure != null)
			{
				_InternalVal = _SepBD.get_BackDoorVariable((object)":PressureDrop.500").Variable as InternalRealVariable;
				_InternalVal.Value = -32767.0;
				processStream.Pressure.SetValue(_Data.OutletStreamPressure.Value, _Data.OutletStreamPressure.Unit);
			}
			else if (_Data.InletDeltaPressure != null)
			{
				_InternalVal = _SepBD.get_BackDoorVariable((object)":PressureDrop.500").Variable as InternalRealVariable;
				_InternalVal.SetValue(_Data.InletDeltaPressure.Value, _Data.InletDeltaPressure.Unit);
			}
			if (_Data.Duty != null)
			{
				_InternalVal = (energyStream as BackDoor).get_BackDoorVariable((object)":HeatFlow.501.0").Variable as InternalRealVariable;
				_InternalVal.SetValue(_Data.Duty.Value, _Data.Duty.Unit);
			}
		}
	}

	private void SetBubble()
	{
		if (_Data.IsBubble && !string.IsNullOrEmpty(_Data.VapourOutletStream))
		{
			ProcessStream processStream = GetProcessStream(_Data.VapourOutletStream);
			if (_Data.OutLetStreamTemp != null)
			{
				_InternalVal = _SepBD.get_BackDoorVariable((object)":PressureDrop.500").Variable as InternalRealVariable;
				_InternalVal.Value = -32767.0;
				processStream.Temperature.SetValue(_Data.OutLetStreamTemp.Value, _Data.OutLetStreamTemp.Unit);
			}
			else if (_Data.OutletStreamPressure != null)
			{
				_InternalVal = _SepBD.get_BackDoorVariable((object)":PressureDrop.500").Variable as InternalRealVariable;
				_InternalVal.Value = -32767.0;
				processStream.Pressure.SetValue(_Data.OutletStreamPressure.Value, _Data.OutletStreamPressure.Unit);
			}
			else if (_Data.InletDeltaPressure != null)
			{
				_InternalVal = _SepBD.get_BackDoorVariable((object)":PressureDrop.500").Variable as InternalRealVariable;
				_InternalVal.SetValue(_Data.InletDeltaPressure.Value, _Data.InletDeltaPressure.Unit);
			}
			processStream.MolarFlow.Value = 0.0;
		}
	}

	private void SetEntrainment()
	{
		if (_Data.Entrainments == null)
		{
			return;
		}
		foreach (HYSYSSeparator.HYSYSEntrainment entrainment in _Data.Entrainments)
		{
			if (entrainment.RATE != null)
			{
				_InternalVal = _SepBD.get_BackDoorVariable((object)":Selection.504").Variable as InternalRealVariable;
				_InternalVal.Value = 2.0;
				_InternalVal = _SepBD.get_BackDoorVariable((object)"CarryOverCalculation.500 :Selection.602").Variable as InternalRealVariable;
				_InternalVal.Value = 1.0;
				if (entrainment.RATE.Type.StartsWith("M"))
				{
					_InternalVal = _SepBD.get_BackDoorVariable((object)"CarryOverCalculation.500:Enum.600").Variable as InternalRealVariable;
					_InternalVal.Value = 3.0;
				}
				else if (entrainment.RATE.Type.StartsWith("W"))
				{
					_InternalVal = _SepBD.get_BackDoorVariable((object)"CarryOverCalculation.500:Enum.600").Variable as InternalRealVariable;
					_InternalVal.Value = 4.0;
				}
				else if (entrainment.RATE.Type.StartsWith("L") || entrainment.RATE.Type.StartsWith("G"))
				{
					_InternalVal = _SepBD.get_BackDoorVariable((object)"CarryOverCalculation.500:Enum.600").Variable as InternalRealVariable;
					_InternalVal.Value = 1.0;
				}
				if (entrainment.FROM == "L" && entrainment.TO == "V")
				{
					_InternalVal = _SepBD.get_BackDoorVariable((object)"CarryOverCalculation.500 :ExtraData.600.0").Variable as InternalRealVariable;
					_InternalVal.SetValue(entrainment.RATE.Value, entrainment.RATE.Unit);
				}
				else if (entrainment.FROM == "W" && entrainment.TO == "V")
				{
					_InternalVal = _SepBD.get_BackDoorVariable((object)"CarryOverCalculation.500 :ExtraData.600.1").Variable as InternalRealVariable;
					_InternalVal.SetValue(entrainment.RATE.Value, entrainment.RATE.Unit);
				}
				else if (entrainment.FROM == "V" && entrainment.TO == "L")
				{
					_InternalVal = _SepBD.get_BackDoorVariable((object)"CarryOverCalculation.500 :ExtraData.600.2").Variable as InternalRealVariable;
					_InternalVal.SetValue(entrainment.RATE.Value, entrainment.RATE.Unit);
				}
				else if (entrainment.FROM == "W" && entrainment.TO == "L")
				{
					_InternalVal = _SepBD.get_BackDoorVariable((object)"CarryOverCalculation.500 :ExtraData.600.3").Variable as InternalRealVariable;
					_InternalVal.SetValue(entrainment.RATE.Value, entrainment.RATE.Unit);
				}
				else if (entrainment.FROM == "V" && entrainment.TO == "W")
				{
					_InternalVal = _SepBD.get_BackDoorVariable((object)"CarryOverCalculation.500 :ExtraData.600.4").Variable as InternalRealVariable;
					_InternalVal.SetValue(entrainment.RATE.Value, entrainment.RATE.Unit);
				}
				else if (entrainment.FROM == "L" && entrainment.TO == "W")
				{
					_InternalVal = _SepBD.get_BackDoorVariable((object)"CarryOverCalculation.500 :ExtraData.600.5").Variable as InternalRealVariable;
					_InternalVal.SetValue(entrainment.RATE.Value, entrainment.RATE.Unit);
				}
			}
			else
			{
				_InternalVal = _SepBD.get_BackDoorVariable((object)":Selection.504").Variable as InternalRealVariable;
				_InternalVal.Value = 1.0;
				_InternalVal = _SepBD.get_BackDoorVariable((object)":Enum.600").Variable as InternalRealVariable;
				_InternalVal.Value = 3.0;
				_InternalVal = _SepBD.get_BackDoorVariable((object)"CarryOverCalculation.500 :Selection.602").Variable as InternalRealVariable;
				_InternalVal.Value = 0.0;
				if (entrainment.FROM == "L" && entrainment.TO == "V")
				{
					_InternalVal = _SepBD.get_BackDoorVariable((object)"CarryOverCalculation.500 :Fraction.600.0").Variable as InternalRealVariable;
					_InternalVal.Value = entrainment.FRACTION;
				}
				else if (entrainment.FROM == "W" && entrainment.TO == "V")
				{
					_InternalVal = _SepBD.get_BackDoorVariable((object)"CarryOverCalculation.500 :Fraction.600.1").Variable as InternalRealVariable;
					_InternalVal.Value = entrainment.FRACTION;
				}
				else if (entrainment.FROM == "V" && entrainment.TO == "L")
				{
					_InternalVal = _SepBD.get_BackDoorVariable((object)"CarryOverCalculation.500 :Fraction.600.2").Variable as InternalRealVariable;
					_InternalVal.Value = entrainment.FRACTION;
				}
				else if (entrainment.FROM == "W" && entrainment.TO == "L")
				{
					_InternalVal = _SepBD.get_BackDoorVariable((object)"CarryOverCalculation.500 :Fraction.600.3").Variable as InternalRealVariable;
					_InternalVal.Value = entrainment.FRACTION;
				}
				else if (entrainment.FROM == "V" && entrainment.TO == "W")
				{
					_InternalVal = _SepBD.get_BackDoorVariable((object)"CarryOverCalculation.500 :Fraction.600.4").Variable as InternalRealVariable;
					_InternalVal.Value = entrainment.FRACTION;
				}
				else if (entrainment.FROM == "L" && entrainment.TO == "W")
				{
					_InternalVal = _SepBD.get_BackDoorVariable((object)"CarryOverCalculation.500 :Fraction.600.5").Variable as InternalRealVariable;
					_InternalVal.Value = entrainment.FRACTION;
				}
			}
		}
	}
}
public class HYSYSHelper
{
	private Aspentech.HYSYS.Application _AppHYSYS;

	private SimulationCase _SimCase;

	private readonly string _tempMvFolder = Path.GetTempPath() + "~ConverterApTemp";

	private Flowsheet _FlowSheet;

	private bool _ImportSuccess;

	private readonly List<FluidPackage> _FluidPackages = new List<FluidPackage>();

	public HYSYSTree _HYSYSTree { get; set; }

	public void SetHYSYSCase()
	{
		try
		{
			InitHYSYS();
			SetThermoProperty(_HYSYSTree._BasicManager.Packages);
			new HYSYSComponentConverter(_SimCase, _FluidPackages, _HYSYSTree._BasicManager.Components, _HYSYSTree._BasicManager.Packages, _ImportSuccess);
			SetThermoEnthalpyAndDensity(_HYSYSTree._BasicManager.Packages);
			new HYSYSReactionSetConverter(_SimCase.BasisManager, _HYSYSTree._BasicManager.HYSYSRXSets, _HYSYSTree._BasicManager.Packages.DefaultMehodeName);
		}
		catch (Exception)
		{
		}
		if (_SimCase.BasisManager.IsChangingBasis)
		{
			try
			{
				while (!_SimCase.BasisManager.CanEndBasisChange)
				{
					if (System.Windows.MessageBox.Show("You can not leave basic manager, please correct the issues manually and select OK to continue, cancel to stop model conversion.", P2Globalization.GetString("P2Convert"), MessageBoxButton.OKCancel, MessageBoxImage.Hand, MessageBoxResult.OK, System.Windows.MessageBoxOptions.DefaultDesktopOnly) == MessageBoxResult.Cancel)
					{
						return;
					}
				}
				_SimCase.BasisManager.EndBasisChange();
			}
			catch (Exception)
			{
				System.Windows.MessageBox.Show(P2Globalization.GetString("BasicManagerInitalizeError"), P2Globalization.GetString("P2Convert"), MessageBoxButton.OK, MessageBoxImage.Hand, MessageBoxResult.OK, System.Windows.MessageBoxOptions.DefaultDesktopOnly);
				return;
			}
		}
		try
		{
			Dictionary<string, BackDoor> columnColl = new Dictionary<string, BackDoor>();
			AddStreams();
			bool flag = false;
			foreach (HYSYSHeatExchanger hYSYSHeatExchange in _HYSYSTree._PFDObject.HYSYSHeatExchanges)
			{
				if (hYSYSHeatExchange.MapForHXRIG)
				{
					flag = true;
					break;
				}
			}
			if (flag && Options.IsHYSYSHeaterExchanger)
			{
				System.Windows.MessageBox.Show(P2Globalization.GetString("HCurveNotWorkMessage"), P2Globalization.GetString("P2Convert"), MessageBoxButton.OK, MessageBoxImage.Exclamation, MessageBoxResult.OK, System.Windows.MessageBoxOptions.DefaultDesktopOnly);
				DebugPrint.OutputDebugString(DebugLevel.BuildHYSYSCase, WarningLevel.Notes, P2Globalization.GetString("HCurveNotWorkMessage"));
			}
			foreach (HYSYSColumn hYSYSColumn in _HYSYSTree._PFDObject.HYSYSColumns)
			{
				new HYSYSDistillationColumnConverter(_SimCase, hYSYSColumn, ref columnColl);
			}
			foreach (HYSYSMixer hYSYSMixer in _HYSYSTree._PFDObject.HYSYSMixers)
			{
				new HYSYSMixerConverter(_SimCase, hYSYSMixer);
			}
			foreach (HYSYSFiredHeater hYSYSFiredHeater in _HYSYSTree._PFDObject.HYSYSFiredHeaters)
			{
				new HYSYSFiredHeaterConverter(_SimCase, hYSYSFiredHeater);
			}
			foreach (HYSYSLNGHeatExchanger hYSYSLNGHX in _HYSYSTree._PFDObject.HYSYSLNGHXs)
			{
				new HYSYSLNGHeatExchangerConverter(_SimCase, hYSYSLNGHX);
			}
			foreach (HYSYSHeatExchanger hYSYSHeatExchange2 in _HYSYSTree._PFDObject.HYSYSHeatExchanges)
			{
				new HYSYSHeatExchangerConverter(_SimCase, hYSYSHeatExchange2, !Options.IsHYSYSHeaterExchanger).Dispose();
			}
			foreach (HYSYSCompressor hYSYSCompressor in _HYSYSTree._PFDObject.HYSYSCompressors)
			{
				new HYSYSCompressorConverter(_SimCase, hYSYSCompressor).Dispose();
			}
			foreach (HYSYSExpander hYSYSExpander in _HYSYSTree._PFDObject.HYSYSExpanders)
			{
				new HYSYSExpanderConverter(_SimCase, hYSYSExpander).Dispose();
			}
			foreach (HYSYSValve hYSYSValf in _HYSYSTree._PFDObject.HYSYSValves)
			{
				new HYSYSValveConverter(_SimCase, hYSYSValf);
			}
			foreach (HYSYSPump hYSYSPump in _HYSYSTree._PFDObject.HYSYSPumps)
			{
				new HYSYSPumpConverter(_SimCase, hYSYSPump);
			}
			foreach (HYSYSConversionReactor hYSYSConversionReactor in _HYSYSTree._PFDObject.HYSYSConversionReactors)
			{
				new HYSYSConversionReactorConverter(_SimCase, hYSYSConversionReactor);
			}
			foreach (HYSYSEquilibriumReactor hYSYSEquilibriumReactor in _HYSYSTree._PFDObject.HYSYSEquilibriumReactors)
			{
				new HYSYSEquilibriumReactorConverter(_SimCase, hYSYSEquilibriumReactor);
			}
			foreach (HYSYSGibbsReactor hYSYSGibbsReactor in _HYSYSTree._PFDObject.HYSYSGibbsReactors)
			{
				new HYSYSGibbsReactorConverter(_SimCase, hYSYSGibbsReactor);
			}
			foreach (HYSYSPipe hYSYSPipe in _HYSYSTree._PFDObject.HYSYSPipes)
			{
				new HYSYSPipeConverter(_SimCase, hYSYSPipe);
			}
			foreach (HYSYSPlugFlowReactor hYSYSPFReactor in _HYSYSTree._PFDObject.HYSYSPFReactors)
			{
				new HYSYSPlugFlowReactorConverter(_SimCase, hYSYSPFReactor);
			}
			foreach (HYSYSAirCooler hYSYSAirCooler in _HYSYSTree._PFDObject.HYSYSAirCoolers)
			{
				new HYSYSAirCoolerConverter(_SimCase, hYSYSAirCooler, !Options.IsHYSYSAirCooler);
			}
			foreach (HYSYSCSTReactor hYSYSCSTReactor in _HYSYSTree._PFDObject.HYSYSCSTReactors)
			{
				new HYSYSCSTReactorConverter(_SimCase, hYSYSCSTReactor);
			}
			foreach (HYSYSSeparator hysysSeparator in _HYSYSTree._PFDObject.HysysSeparators)
			{
				new SeparatorConverter(_SimCase, hysysSeparator);
			}
			foreach (HYSYSRecycle hYSYSRecycle in _HYSYSTree._PFDObject.HYSYSRecycles)
			{
				new HYSYSRecycleConverter(_SimCase, hYSYSRecycle);
			}
			foreach (HYSYSSplitter hYSYSSplitter in _HYSYSTree._PFDObject.HYSYSSplitters)
			{
				new HYSYSSplitterConverter(_SimCase, hYSYSSplitter);
			}
			foreach (HYSYSComponentSplitter hYSYSComponentSplitter in _HYSYSTree._PFDObject.HYSYSComponentSplitters)
			{
				new HYSYSComponentSplitterConverter(_SimCase, hYSYSComponentSplitter);
			}
			foreach (HYSYSCutter hYSYSCutter in _HYSYSTree._PFDObject.HYSYSCutters)
			{
				new HYSYSCutterConverter(_SimCase, hYSYSCutter);
			}
			if (Options.IsBalanceStreamRef)
			{
				SetRefStreamUsingBalanceAndSet();
			}
			else
			{
				SetRefStreamUsingMutipleStream();
			}
			SetCutterTransferBasis();
			AddGlobalDefine();
			new HYSYSUtilityConverter(_SimCase, _HYSYSTree);
			BackDoor obj = _FlowSheet as BackDoor;
			obj.SendBackDoorMessage("PFDMgrObject.400:AutoPositionAll");
			SaveHYSYSCaseBeforeSolving();
			(obj.get_BackDoorVariable((object)"SolverObject.400:Attribute.300").Variable as InternalRealVariable).Value = 0.0;
			foreach (string key in columnColl.Keys)
			{
				columnColl[key].SendBackDoorMessage("Run");
			}
		}
		catch (Exception)
		{
		}
		finally
		{
			string strMessage = string.Format(P2Globalization.GetString("ConvertingCompleted"));
			DebugPrint.OutputDebugString(DebugLevel.BuildHYSYSCase, WarningLevel.Notes, strMessage);
		}
	}

	public HYSYSHelper()
	{
		if (!Directory.Exists(_tempMvFolder))
		{
			Directory.CreateDirectory(_tempMvFolder);
		}
		_HYSYSTree = new HYSYSTree();
	}

	private void SaveHYSYSCaseBeforeSolving()
	{
		try
		{
			string text = P2BaseObject.P2InputFileName.Trim();
			text = text.Remove(text.Length - 4);
			text += ".hsc";
			if (File.Exists(text))
			{
				if (System.Windows.MessageBox.Show(string.Format(P2Globalization.GetString("FileExists"), text), P2Globalization.GetString("P2Convert"), MessageBoxButton.YesNo, MessageBoxImage.Exclamation, MessageBoxResult.Yes, System.Windows.MessageBoxOptions.DefaultDesktopOnly) == MessageBoxResult.Yes)
				{
					foreach (SimulationCase simulationCase in _AppHYSYS.SimulationCases)
					{
						if (text.Equals(simulationCase.FullName))
						{
							simulationCase.Close(true, text);
							break;
						}
					}
					_SimCase.SaveAs(text);
					DebugPrint.OutputDebugString(DebugLevel.BuildHYSYSCase, WarningLevel.Notes, string.Format(P2Globalization.GetString("ReplaceCaseNote"), text));
				}
				else
				{
					BackDoor obj = _AppHYSYS as BackDoor;
					string fullName = _SimCase.FullName;
					obj.SendBackDoorMessage("SaveDocumentAs");
					if (_SimCase.FullName == fullName)
					{
						DebugPrint.OutputDebugString(DebugLevel.BuildHYSYSCase, WarningLevel.Notes, P2Globalization.GetString("CaseNotSavedNote"));
					}
					else
					{
						DebugPrint.OutputDebugString(DebugLevel.BuildHYSYSCase, WarningLevel.Notes, string.Format(P2Globalization.GetString("SavedCaseNote"), _SimCase.FullName));
					}
				}
			}
			else
			{
				_SimCase.SaveAs(text);
				DebugPrint.OutputDebugString(DebugLevel.BuildHYSYSCase, WarningLevel.Notes, string.Format(P2Globalization.GetString("SavedCaseNote"), text));
			}
		}
		catch (Exception)
		{
			string strMessage = string.Format(P2Globalization.GetString("SaveCaseError"));
			DebugPrint.OutputDebugString(DebugLevel.BuildHYSYSCase, WarningLevel.Error, strMessage);
		}
	}

	private void InitHYSYS()
	{
		if (_AppHYSYS == null)
		{
			Type typeFromProgID = Type.GetTypeFromProgID($"HYSYS.Application.{HYSYSVersionNumber.versionNumber}");
			_AppHYSYS = Activator.CreateInstance(typeFromProgID) as Aspentech.HYSYS.Application;
		}
		_AppHYSYS.Visible = true;
		if (_SimCase == null)
		{
			_SimCase = new SimulationCaseClass();
		}
		_SimCase.Activate();
		_FlowSheet = _SimCase.Flowsheet;
		((_FlowSheet as BackDoor).get_BackDoorVariable((object)"/ConfigMgr.100:ExtraData.1.389(StreamConnect)").Variable as InternalRealVariable).Value = 1.0;
	}

	public void SetP2Tree(P2BaseObject objTree)
	{
		if (objTree == null)
		{
			return;
		}
		try
		{
			foreach (P2BaseObject child in objTree.Children)
			{
				if (child != null)
				{
					convert(child);
				}
			}
			AddPseudoStreamToLogicTree();
			CheckProperty();
			ResetSideStripperAndSideRectifier();
			SeparateColumnMainFeedsProducts();
			SeparateColumnSpecsAndDefines();
			SetLogicReactionsActivityProperty();
			SetRefStreamSpliterForRate();
			CopyRefStreamInfoForHaveSouceStream();
			SetNoSourceRefStreamSource();
			string[] cutStreams = GetCutStreams();
			if (cutStreams != null && cutStreams.Length != 0)
			{
				HandleRecycles(cutStreams);
			}
		}
		catch (Exception)
		{
		}
	}

	private void SetRefStreamSpliterForRate()
	{
		bool flag = false;
		foreach (HYSYSStream stream in _HYSYSTree._PFDObject.Streams)
		{
			if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(stream.RefStream))
			{
				flag = true;
				break;
			}
		}
		if (!flag || _HYSYSTree._PFDObject.Streams == null)
		{
			return;
		}
		for (int i = 0; i < _HYSYSTree._PFDObject.Streams.Count; i++)
		{
			HYSYSStream hYSYSStream = _HYSYSTree._PFDObject.Streams[i];
			if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(hYSYSStream.RefStream))
			{
				continue;
			}
			foreach (HYSYSStream stream2 in _HYSYSTree._PFDObject.Streams)
			{
				if (stream2.Stream == hYSYSStream.RefStream)
				{
					break;
				}
			}
			if (hYSYSStream.Source == null && hYSYSStream.Rate != null)
			{
				HYSYSSplitter hYSYSSplitter = new HYSYSSplitter();
				hYSYSSplitter.UID = NameHelper.CreateName(bIsStream: false, "Ref_Spliter");
				if (hYSYSStream.Temp == null && hYSYSStream.Pressure == null && !Options.IsBalanceStreamRef)
				{
					hYSYSSplitter.InletStreams.Add(hYSYSStream.RefStream);
					hYSYSSplitter.OutletStreams.Add(hYSYSStream.Stream);
					string item = NameHelper.CreateName(bIsStream: true, "_Ref");
					hYSYSSplitter.OutletStreams.Add(item);
					hYSYSStream.IsRateHaveBeenSet = true;
				}
				else
				{
					string text = NameHelper.CreateName(bIsStream: true, "_Ref");
					HYSYSStream hYSYSStream2 = new HYSYSStream();
					hYSYSStream2.Stream = text;
					hYSYSStream2.Rate = null;
					hYSYSStream2.Temp = hYSYSStream.Temp;
					hYSYSStream2.Pressure = hYSYSStream.Pressure;
					hYSYSStream2.Destination = hYSYSSplitter;
					hYSYSStream2.Source = hYSYSStream.Source;
					hYSYSStream2.RefStream = hYSYSStream.RefStream;
					hYSYSStream.Temp = null;
					hYSYSStream.Pressure = null;
					hYSYSStream.Source = hYSYSSplitter;
					hYSYSStream.RefStream = string.Empty;
					hYSYSStream.IsNeedSetRateForRefStream = true;
					hYSYSSplitter.InletStreams.Add(text);
					hYSYSSplitter.OutletStreams.Add(hYSYSStream.Stream);
					string item2 = NameHelper.CreateName(bIsStream: true, "_Ref");
					hYSYSSplitter.OutletStreams.Add(item2);
					hYSYSSplitter.ConvertNotes.Add(string.Format(P2Globalization.GetString("Helper_HandleRefStream"), hYSYSStream.Stream, hYSYSStream.RefStream));
					_HYSYSTree._PFDObject.Streams.Add(hYSYSStream2);
				}
				_HYSYSTree._PFDObject.HYSYSSplitters.Add(hYSYSSplitter);
			}
		}
	}

	private void CheckProperty()
	{
		if (_HYSYSTree._BasicManager.Packages == null || _HYSYSTree._BasicManager.Packages.AllPropertyNames == null || _HYSYSTree._BasicManager.Packages.AllPropertyNames.Count == 0 || (_HYSYSTree._BasicManager.Packages.EOSFluidPackages != null && _HYSYSTree._BasicManager.Packages.EOSFluidPackages.Count == 0 && _HYSYSTree._BasicManager.Packages.ActivityFluidPackages != null && _HYSYSTree._BasicManager.Packages.ActivityFluidPackages.Count == 0))
		{
			if (_HYSYSTree._BasicManager.Packages != null && _HYSYSTree._BasicManager.Packages.AllPropertyNames != null)
			{
				foreach (string allPropertyName in _HYSYSTree._BasicManager.Packages.AllPropertyNames)
				{
					string strMessage = string.Format(P2Globalization.GetString("P2ThermoNotConverted"), allPropertyName);
					DebugPrint.OutputDebugString(DebugLevel.BuildHYSYSObject, WarningLevel.Warning, strMessage);
				}
			}
			_HYSYSTree._BasicManager.Packages = new HYSYSFluidPackages();
			_HYSYSTree._BasicManager.Packages.EOSFluidPackages = new List<HYSYSFluidPackage>();
			HYSYSFluidPackage item = new HYSYSFluidPackage("SRK", isIndividual: false, isDefault: true, "SRK", null, null, "SRK");
			_HYSYSTree._BasicManager.Packages.EOSFluidPackages.Add(item);
			_HYSYSTree._BasicManager.Packages.DefaultMehodeName = "SRK";
			_HYSYSTree._BasicManager.Packages.AllPropertyNames.Add("SRK");
			_HYSYSTree._BasicManager.Packages.IsAtuoCreatedDefault = true;
		}
		if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(_HYSYSTree._BasicManager.Packages.DefaultMehodeName))
		{
			return;
		}
		if (_HYSYSTree._BasicManager.Packages.EOSFluidPackages != null && _HYSYSTree._BasicManager.Packages.EOSFluidPackages.Count == 1 && _HYSYSTree._BasicManager.Packages.ActivityFluidPackages != null && _HYSYSTree._BasicManager.Packages.ActivityFluidPackages.Count == 0)
		{
			if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(_HYSYSTree._BasicManager.Packages.EOSFluidPackages[0].FluidPackageName))
			{
				_HYSYSTree._BasicManager.Packages.DefaultMehodeName = _HYSYSTree._BasicManager.Packages.EOSFluidPackages[0].ThomoKey;
				_HYSYSTree._BasicManager.Packages.EOSFluidPackages[0].FluidPackageName = _HYSYSTree._BasicManager.Packages.EOSFluidPackages[0].ThomoKey;
			}
			else
			{
				_HYSYSTree._BasicManager.Packages.DefaultMehodeName = _HYSYSTree._BasicManager.Packages.EOSFluidPackages[0].FluidPackageName;
			}
		}
		else if (_HYSYSTree._BasicManager.Packages.EOSFluidPackages != null && _HYSYSTree._BasicManager.Packages.EOSFluidPackages.Count == 0 && _HYSYSTree._BasicManager.Packages.ActivityFluidPackages != null && _HYSYSTree._BasicManager.Packages.ActivityFluidPackages.Count == 1)
		{
			if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(_HYSYSTree._BasicManager.Packages.ActivityFluidPackages[0].FluidPackageName))
			{
				_HYSYSTree._BasicManager.Packages.DefaultMehodeName = _HYSYSTree._BasicManager.Packages.ActivityFluidPackages[0].ThomoKey;
				_HYSYSTree._BasicManager.Packages.ActivityFluidPackages[0].FluidPackageName = _HYSYSTree._BasicManager.Packages.ActivityFluidPackages[0].ThomoKey;
			}
			else
			{
				_HYSYSTree._BasicManager.Packages.DefaultMehodeName = _HYSYSTree._BasicManager.Packages.ActivityFluidPackages[0].FluidPackageName;
			}
		}
	}

	private void convert(P2BaseObject p2Obj)
	{
		HYSYSBaseObject[] array = p2Obj.Convert2HYSYSObj();
		p2Obj.SetConvertStatus();
		if (array != null)
		{
			HYSYSBaseObject[] array2 = array;
			foreach (HYSYSBaseObject hYSYSBaseObject in array2)
			{
				Type type = hYSYSBaseObject.GetType();
				if (type == typeof(HYSYSTitle))
				{
					_HYSYSTree._BasicManager.Title = hYSYSBaseObject as HYSYSTitle;
				}
				else if (type == typeof(HYSYSObjects.HYSYSComponent))
				{
					_HYSYSTree._BasicManager.Components = hYSYSBaseObject as HYSYSObjects.HYSYSComponent;
				}
				else if (type == typeof(HYSYSFluidPackages))
				{
					_HYSYSTree._BasicManager.Packages = hYSYSBaseObject as HYSYSFluidPackages;
					string key = $"FluidPackages";
					HYSYSBaseObject.g_Map.Add(key, _HYSYSTree._BasicManager.Packages);
				}
				else if (type == typeof(HYSYSReactionSet))
				{
					HYSYSReactionSet hYSYSReactionSet = hYSYSBaseObject as HYSYSReactionSet;
					_HYSYSTree._BasicManager.HYSYSRXSets.Add(hYSYSReactionSet);
					string key2 = $"REACTIONSET\\{hYSYSReactionSet.ID}";
					HYSYSBaseObject.g_Map.Add(key2, hYSYSReactionSet);
				}
				else if (type == typeof(HYSYSCompressor))
				{
					HYSYSCompressor hYSYSCompressor = hYSYSBaseObject as HYSYSCompressor;
					_HYSYSTree._PFDObject.HYSYSCompressors.Add(hYSYSCompressor);
					string key3 = $"UNITOPERATION\\{hYSYSCompressor.UID}";
					HYSYSBaseObject.g_Map.Add(key3, hYSYSCompressor);
				}
				else if (type == typeof(HYSYSExpander))
				{
					HYSYSExpander hYSYSExpander = hYSYSBaseObject as HYSYSExpander;
					_HYSYSTree._PFDObject.HYSYSExpanders.Add(hYSYSExpander);
					string key4 = $"UNITOPERATION\\{hYSYSExpander.UID}";
					HYSYSBaseObject.g_Map.Add(key4, hYSYSExpander);
				}
				else if (type == typeof(HYSYSSeparator))
				{
					HYSYSSeparator hYSYSSeparator = hYSYSBaseObject as HYSYSSeparator;
					_HYSYSTree._PFDObject.HysysSeparators.Add(hYSYSSeparator);
					string key5 = $"UNITOPERATION\\{hYSYSSeparator.UID}";
					HYSYSBaseObject.g_Map.Add(key5, hYSYSSeparator);
				}
				else if (type == typeof(HYSYSFiredHeater))
				{
					HYSYSFiredHeater hYSYSFiredHeater = hYSYSBaseObject as HYSYSFiredHeater;
					_HYSYSTree._PFDObject.HYSYSFiredHeaters.Add(hYSYSFiredHeater);
					string key6 = $"UNITOPERATION\\{hYSYSFiredHeater.UID}";
					HYSYSBaseObject.g_Map.Add(key6, hYSYSFiredHeater);
				}
				else if (type == typeof(HYSYSLNGHeatExchanger))
				{
					HYSYSLNGHeatExchanger hYSYSLNGHeatExchanger = hYSYSBaseObject as HYSYSLNGHeatExchanger;
					_HYSYSTree._PFDObject.HYSYSLNGHXs.Add(hYSYSLNGHeatExchanger);
					string key7 = $"UNITOPERATION\\{hYSYSLNGHeatExchanger.UID}";
					HYSYSBaseObject.g_Map.Add(key7, hYSYSLNGHeatExchanger);
				}
				else if (type == typeof(HYSYSHeatExchanger))
				{
					HYSYSHeatExchanger hYSYSHeatExchanger = hYSYSBaseObject as HYSYSHeatExchanger;
					_HYSYSTree._PFDObject.HYSYSHeatExchanges.Add(hYSYSHeatExchanger);
					string key8 = $"UNITOPERATION\\{hYSYSHeatExchanger.UID}";
					HYSYSBaseObject.g_Map.Add(key8, hYSYSHeatExchanger);
				}
				else if (type == typeof(HYSYSMixer))
				{
					HYSYSMixer hYSYSMixer = hYSYSBaseObject as HYSYSMixer;
					_HYSYSTree._PFDObject.HYSYSMixers.Add(hYSYSMixer);
					string key9 = $"UNITOPERATION\\{hYSYSMixer.UID}";
					HYSYSBaseObject.g_Map.Add(key9, hYSYSMixer);
				}
				else if (type == typeof(HYSYSStream))
				{
					HYSYSStream hYSYSStream = hYSYSBaseObject as HYSYSStream;
					_HYSYSTree._PFDObject.Streams.Add(hYSYSStream);
					if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(hYSYSStream.Stream))
					{
						string key10 = $"STREAM\\{hYSYSStream.Stream}";
						HYSYSBaseObject.g_Map.Add(key10, hYSYSStream);
					}
				}
				else if (type == typeof(HYSYSSplitter))
				{
					HYSYSSplitter hYSYSSplitter = hYSYSBaseObject as HYSYSSplitter;
					_HYSYSTree._PFDObject.HYSYSSplitters.Add(hYSYSSplitter);
					string key11 = $"UNITOPERATION\\{hYSYSSplitter.UID}";
					HYSYSBaseObject.g_Map.Add(key11, hYSYSSplitter);
				}
				else if (type == typeof(HYSYSValve))
				{
					HYSYSValve hYSYSValve = hYSYSBaseObject as HYSYSValve;
					_HYSYSTree._PFDObject.HYSYSValves.Add(hYSYSValve);
					string key12 = $"UNITOPERATION\\{hYSYSValve.UID}";
					HYSYSBaseObject.g_Map.Add(key12, hYSYSValve);
				}
				else if (type == typeof(HYSYSPump))
				{
					HYSYSPump hYSYSPump = hYSYSBaseObject as HYSYSPump;
					_HYSYSTree._PFDObject.HYSYSPumps.Add(hYSYSPump);
					string key13 = $"UNITOPERATION\\{hYSYSPump.UID}";
					HYSYSBaseObject.g_Map.Add(key13, hYSYSPump);
				}
				else if (type == typeof(HYSYSConversionReactor))
				{
					HYSYSConversionReactor hYSYSConversionReactor = hYSYSBaseObject as HYSYSConversionReactor;
					_HYSYSTree._PFDObject.HYSYSConversionReactors.Add(hYSYSConversionReactor);
					string key14 = $"UNITOPERATION\\{hYSYSConversionReactor.UID}";
					HYSYSBaseObject.g_Map.Add(key14, hYSYSConversionReactor);
				}
				else if (type == typeof(HYSYSEquilibriumReactor))
				{
					HYSYSEquilibriumReactor hYSYSEquilibriumReactor = hYSYSBaseObject as HYSYSEquilibriumReactor;
					_HYSYSTree._PFDObject.HYSYSEquilibriumReactors.Add(hYSYSEquilibriumReactor);
					string key15 = $"UNITOPERATION\\{hYSYSEquilibriumReactor.UID}";
					HYSYSBaseObject.g_Map.Add(key15, hYSYSEquilibriumReactor);
				}
				else if (type == typeof(HYSYSGibbsReactor))
				{
					HYSYSGibbsReactor hYSYSGibbsReactor = hYSYSBaseObject as HYSYSGibbsReactor;
					_HYSYSTree._PFDObject.HYSYSGibbsReactors.Add(hYSYSGibbsReactor);
					string key16 = $"UNITOPERATION\\{hYSYSGibbsReactor.UID}";
					HYSYSBaseObject.g_Map.Add(key16, hYSYSGibbsReactor);
				}
				else if (type == typeof(HYSYSPlugFlowReactor))
				{
					HYSYSPlugFlowReactor hYSYSPlugFlowReactor = hYSYSBaseObject as HYSYSPlugFlowReactor;
					_HYSYSTree._PFDObject.HYSYSPFReactors.Add(hYSYSPlugFlowReactor);
					string key17 = $"UNITOPERATION\\{hYSYSPlugFlowReactor.UID}";
					HYSYSBaseObject.g_Map.Add(key17, hYSYSPlugFlowReactor);
				}
				else if (type == typeof(HYSYSCSTReactor))
				{
					HYSYSCSTReactor hYSYSCSTReactor = hYSYSBaseObject as HYSYSCSTReactor;
					_HYSYSTree._PFDObject.HYSYSCSTReactors.Add(hYSYSCSTReactor);
					string key18 = $"UNITOPERATION\\{hYSYSCSTReactor.UID}";
					HYSYSBaseObject.g_Map.Add(key18, hYSYSCSTReactor);
				}
				else if (type == typeof(HYSYSColumn))
				{
					HYSYSColumn hYSYSColumn = hYSYSBaseObject as HYSYSColumn;
					string key19 = $"UNITOPERATION\\{hYSYSColumn.UID}";
					HYSYSBaseObject.g_Map.Add(key19, hYSYSColumn);
					if (hYSYSColumn.IDENTIFICATION.StartsWith(CUnitOperation.COLUMN))
					{
						_HYSYSTree._PFDObject.HYSYSColumns.Add(hYSYSColumn);
					}
					else if (hYSYSColumn.IDENTIFICATION.StartsWith(CUnitOperation.SIDESTRIPPER))
					{
						_HYSYSTree._PFDObject.HYSYSSideStrippers.Add(hYSYSColumn);
					}
					else if (hYSYSColumn.IDENTIFICATION.StartsWith(CUnitOperation.SIDERECTIFIER))
					{
						_HYSYSTree._PFDObject.HYSYSSideRectifiers.Add(hYSYSColumn);
					}
				}
				else if (type == typeof(HYSYSPipe))
				{
					HYSYSPipe hYSYSPipe = hYSYSBaseObject as HYSYSPipe;
					_HYSYSTree._PFDObject.HYSYSPipes.Add(hYSYSPipe);
					string key20 = $"UNITOPERATION\\{hYSYSPipe.UID}";
					HYSYSBaseObject.g_Map.Add(key20, hYSYSPipe);
				}
				else if (type == typeof(HYSYSAirCooler))
				{
					HYSYSAirCooler hYSYSAirCooler = hYSYSBaseObject as HYSYSAirCooler;
					_HYSYSTree._PFDObject.HYSYSAirCoolers.Add(hYSYSAirCooler);
					string key21 = $"UNITOPERATION\\{hYSYSAirCooler.UID}";
					HYSYSBaseObject.g_Map.Add(key21, hYSYSAirCooler);
				}
				else if (type == typeof(HYSYSRecycle))
				{
					HYSYSRecycle hYSYSRecycle = hYSYSBaseObject as HYSYSRecycle;
					_HYSYSTree._PFDObject.HYSYSRecycles.Add(hYSYSRecycle);
					string key22 = $"UNITOPERATION\\{hYSYSRecycle.UID}";
					HYSYSBaseObject.g_Map.Add(key22, hYSYSRecycle);
				}
				else if (type == typeof(HYSYSPropertyTableUtility))
				{
					HYSYSPropertyTableUtility hYSYSPropertyTableUtility = hYSYSBaseObject as HYSYSPropertyTableUtility;
					_HYSYSTree._PFDObject.HYSYSPropertyTableUtilitys.Add(hYSYSPropertyTableUtility);
					string key23 = $"UNITOPERATION\\{hYSYSPropertyTableUtility.UID}";
					HYSYSBaseObject.g_Map.Add(key23, hYSYSPropertyTableUtility);
				}
				else if (type == typeof(HYSYSHydrateUtility))
				{
					HYSYSHydrateUtility hYSYSHydrateUtility = hYSYSBaseObject as HYSYSHydrateUtility;
					_HYSYSTree._PFDObject.HYSYSHydrateUtilitys.Add(hYSYSHydrateUtility);
					string key24 = $"UNITOPERATION\\{hYSYSHydrateUtility.UID}";
					HYSYSBaseObject.g_Map.Add(key24, hYSYSHydrateUtility);
				}
				else if (type == typeof(HYSYSPhaseEnvelope))
				{
					HYSYSPhaseEnvelope hYSYSPhaseEnvelope = hYSYSBaseObject as HYSYSPhaseEnvelope;
					_HYSYSTree._PFDObject.HYSYSPhaseEnvelopes.Add(hYSYSPhaseEnvelope);
					string key25 = $"UNITOPERATION\\{hYSYSPhaseEnvelope.UID}";
					HYSYSBaseObject.g_Map.Add(key25, hYSYSPhaseEnvelope);
				}
				else if (type == typeof(HYSYSDerpessureUtility))
				{
					HYSYSDerpessureUtility hYSYSDerpessureUtility = hYSYSBaseObject as HYSYSDerpessureUtility;
					_HYSYSTree._PFDObject.HYSYSDerpessureUtilitys.Add(hYSYSDerpessureUtility);
					string key26 = $"UNITOPERATION\\{hYSYSDerpessureUtility.UID}";
					HYSYSBaseObject.g_Map.Add(key26, hYSYSDerpessureUtility);
				}
				else if (type == typeof(HYSYSComponentSplitter))
				{
					HYSYSComponentSplitter hYSYSComponentSplitter = hYSYSBaseObject as HYSYSComponentSplitter;
					_HYSYSTree._PFDObject.HYSYSComponentSplitters.Add(hYSYSComponentSplitter);
					string key27 = $"UNITOPERATION\\{hYSYSComponentSplitter.UID}";
					HYSYSBaseObject.g_Map.Add(key27, hYSYSComponentSplitter);
				}
				else if (type == typeof(HYSYSCutter))
				{
					HYSYSCutter hYSYSCutter = hYSYSBaseObject as HYSYSCutter;
					_HYSYSTree._PFDObject.HYSYSCutters.Add(hYSYSCutter);
					string key28 = $"UNITOPERATION\\{hYSYSCutter.UID}";
					HYSYSBaseObject.g_Map.Add(key28, hYSYSCutter);
				}
			}
		}
		foreach (P2BaseObject child in p2Obj.Children)
		{
			if (child == null)
			{
				continue;
			}
			if (child.GetType() == typeof(P2StreamCalculator))
			{
				P2StreamCalculator p2StreamCalculator = child as P2StreamCalculator;
				if (p2StreamCalculator.bConvert)
				{
					convert(child);
				}
				else
				{
					DebugPrint.OutputDebugString(DebugLevel.Convert, WarningLevel.Warning, string.Format(P2Globalization.GetString("STCALNotConverted_2"), p2StreamCalculator.UID));
				}
			}
			else
			{
				convert(child);
			}
		}
	}

	private void ResetSideStripperAndSideRectifier()
	{
		foreach (HYSYSColumn hYSYSColumn in _HYSYSTree._PFDObject.HYSYSColumns)
		{
			hYSYSColumn.SIDESTRIPPERS = FindSideStripper(hYSYSColumn.OutletStreams);
			hYSYSColumn.SIDERECTIFIERS = FindSideRectifier(hYSYSColumn.OutletStreams);
		}
	}

	private void SetLogicReactionsActivityProperty()
	{
		try
		{
			if (_HYSYSTree._BasicManager.Packages == null || (!_HYSYSTree._BasicManager.Packages.HasAspenPlusActivityProperty && !_HYSYSTree._BasicManager.Packages.IsEOSUsingAspenPlus))
			{
				return;
			}
			foreach (HYSYSPlugFlowReactor hYSYSPFReactor in _HYSYSTree._PFDObject.HYSYSPFReactors)
			{
				SetReactionThemoIsAspenPlus(hYSYSPFReactor.FluidPackage, hYSYSPFReactor.ReactionSet.HYSYSReactions);
			}
			foreach (HYSYSEquilibriumReactor hYSYSEquilibriumReactor in _HYSYSTree._PFDObject.HYSYSEquilibriumReactors)
			{
				SetReactionThemoIsAspenPlus(hYSYSEquilibriumReactor.FluidPackage, hYSYSEquilibriumReactor.ReactionSet.HYSYSReactions);
			}
			foreach (HYSYSConversionReactor hYSYSConversionReactor in _HYSYSTree._PFDObject.HYSYSConversionReactors)
			{
				SetReactionThemoIsAspenPlus(hYSYSConversionReactor.FluidPackage, hYSYSConversionReactor.ReactionSet.HYSYSReactions);
			}
			foreach (HYSYSGibbsReactor hYSYSGibbsReactor in _HYSYSTree._PFDObject.HYSYSGibbsReactors)
			{
				SetReactionThemoIsAspenPlus(hYSYSGibbsReactor.FluidPackage, hYSYSGibbsReactor.ReactionSet.HYSYSReactions);
			}
			foreach (HYSYSCSTReactor hYSYSCSTReactor in _HYSYSTree._PFDObject.HYSYSCSTReactors)
			{
				SetReactionThemoIsAspenPlus(hYSYSCSTReactor.FluidPackage, hYSYSCSTReactor.ReactionSet.HYSYSReactions);
			}
		}
		catch (Exception)
		{
		}
	}

	private void SetReactionThemoIsAspenPlus(string reactorfluidPackage, List<HYSYSReaction> oHYSYSReactions)
	{
		string defaultMehodeName = _HYSYSTree._BasicManager.Packages.DefaultMehodeName;
		List<string> list = new List<string>();
		foreach (HYSYSFluidPackage eOSFluidPackage in _HYSYSTree._BasicManager.Packages.EOSFluidPackages)
		{
			list.Add(eOSFluidPackage.FluidPackageName);
		}
		foreach (HYSYSReaction oHYSYSReaction in oHYSYSReactions)
		{
			foreach (KeyValuePair<string, string> activityProperty in P2ToHYSYSThomoMap.ActivityProperties)
			{
				string key = activityProperty.Key;
				if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(reactorfluidPackage) && reactorfluidPackage.Contains(key))
				{
					oHYSYSReaction.IsAspenPlusProperty = true;
					break;
				}
				if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(reactorfluidPackage) && !string.IsNullOrEmpty(defaultMehodeName) && !list.Contains(key))
				{
					oHYSYSReaction.IsAspenPlusProperty = true;
					break;
				}
			}
		}
	}

	private List<HYSYSColumn> FindSideStripper(List<string> productNames)
	{
		List<HYSYSColumn> list = new List<HYSYSColumn>();
		foreach (HYSYSColumn hYSYSSideStripper in _HYSYSTree._PFDObject.HYSYSSideStrippers)
		{
			foreach (string inletStream in hYSYSSideStripper.InletStreams)
			{
				if (productNames.Contains(inletStream))
				{
					list.Add(hYSYSSideStripper);
				}
			}
		}
		if (list.Count > 0)
		{
			foreach (HYSYSColumn item in list)
			{
				_HYSYSTree._PFDObject.HYSYSSideStrippers.Remove(item);
			}
		}
		return list;
	}

	private List<HYSYSColumn> FindSideRectifier(List<string> productNames)
	{
		List<HYSYSColumn> list = new List<HYSYSColumn>();
		foreach (HYSYSColumn hYSYSSideRectifier in _HYSYSTree._PFDObject.HYSYSSideRectifiers)
		{
			foreach (string inletStream in hYSYSSideRectifier.InletStreams)
			{
				if (productNames.Contains(inletStream))
				{
					list.Add(hYSYSSideRectifier);
				}
			}
		}
		if (list.Count > 0)
		{
			foreach (HYSYSColumn item in list)
			{
				_HYSYSTree._PFDObject.HYSYSSideRectifiers.Remove(item);
			}
		}
		return list;
	}

	private void SeparateColumnSpecsAndDefines()
	{
		foreach (HYSYSColumn hYSYSColumn in _HYSYSTree._PFDObject.HYSYSColumns)
		{
			hYSYSColumn.SeparateGlobalAndLocalSpecs();
			hYSYSColumn.SeparateGlobalAndLocalDefines();
		}
	}

	private void SeparateColumnMainFeedsProducts()
	{
		foreach (HYSYSColumn hYSYSColumn in _HYSYSTree._PFDObject.HYSYSColumns)
		{
			hYSYSColumn.PickupColumnMainFeedsAndProducts(_HYSYSTree._PFDObject.Streams);
			List<string> list = new List<string>();
			foreach (string inletStream in hYSYSColumn.InletStreams)
			{
				if (!hYSYSColumn.HYSYSMainFlowsheetFeeds.Contains(inletStream))
				{
					list.Add(inletStream);
					RemoveColumnInternalStream(inletStream);
				}
			}
			foreach (string outletStream in hYSYSColumn.OutletStreams)
			{
				if (!hYSYSColumn.HYSYSMainFlowsheetProducts.Contains(outletStream))
				{
					list.Add(outletStream);
					RemoveColumnInternalStream(outletStream);
				}
			}
			hYSYSColumn.RemoveInternalStreams(list);
		}
	}

	private void RemoveColumnInternalStream(string sName)
	{
		string key = $"STREAM\\{sName}";
		HYSYSStream hYSYSStream = (HYSYSStream)HYSYSBaseObject.g_Map[key];
		if (hYSYSStream != null)
		{
			_HYSYSTree._PFDObject.Streams.Remove(hYSYSStream);
		}
	}

	private void CopyRefStreamInfoForHaveSouceStream()
	{
		foreach (HYSYSStream stream in _HYSYSTree._PFDObject.Streams)
		{
			if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(stream.RefStream) || stream.Source == null)
			{
				continue;
			}
			foreach (HYSYSStream stream2 in _HYSYSTree._PFDObject.Streams)
			{
				if (stream2.Stream == stream.RefStream)
				{
					if (stream.Temp == null && stream2.Temp != null)
					{
						stream.Temp = stream2.Temp;
					}
					if (stream.Pressure == null && stream2.Pressure != null)
					{
						stream.Pressure = stream2.Pressure;
					}
					if (stream.Rate == null && stream2.Rate != null)
					{
						stream.Rate = stream2.Rate;
					}
					stream.Composition = stream2.Composition;
					stream.VaporFrac = stream2.VaporFrac;
					break;
				}
			}
		}
	}

	private void SetNoSourceRefStreamSource()
	{
		foreach (HYSYSStream stream in _HYSYSTree._PFDObject.Streams)
		{
			if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(stream.RefStream) || stream.Source != null)
			{
				continue;
			}
			foreach (HYSYSStream stream2 in _HYSYSTree._PFDObject.Streams)
			{
				if (stream2.Stream == stream.RefStream)
				{
					stream.Source = stream2.Source;
					break;
				}
			}
		}
	}

	private void AddPseudoStreamToLogicTree()
	{
		foreach (HYSYSColumn hYSYSColumn in _HYSYSTree._PFDObject.HYSYSColumns)
		{
			if (hYSYSColumn.ExportNetLiquidFlow.Count > 0)
			{
				AddPseudoStreamGeneral(hYSYSColumn.ExportNetLiquidFlow.Keys.ToList(), hYSYSColumn);
			}
			if (hYSYSColumn.ExportNetVaporFlow.Count > 0)
			{
				AddPseudoStreamGeneral(hYSYSColumn.ExportNetVaporFlow.Keys.ToList(), hYSYSColumn);
			}
			if (hYSYSColumn.ExportPumpAroundLiquidFlow.Count > 0)
			{
				AddPseudoStreamGeneral(hYSYSColumn.ExportPumpAroundLiquidFlow.Keys.ToList(), hYSYSColumn);
			}
			if (hYSYSColumn.ExportPumpAroundVaporFlow.Count > 0)
			{
				AddPseudoStreamGeneral(hYSYSColumn.ExportPumpAroundVaporFlow.Keys.ToList(), hYSYSColumn);
			}
			if (hYSYSColumn.ExportTotalLiquidFlow.Count > 0)
			{
				AddPseudoStreamGeneral(hYSYSColumn.ExportTotalLiquidFlow.Keys.ToList(), hYSYSColumn);
			}
			if (hYSYSColumn.ExportTotalVaporFlow.Count > 0)
			{
				AddPseudoStreamGeneral(hYSYSColumn.ExportTotalVaporFlow.Keys.ToList(), hYSYSColumn);
			}
		}
	}

	private void AddPseudoStreamGeneral(List<string> stmColl, HYSYSColumn columnData)
	{
		foreach (string item in stmColl)
		{
			bool flag = false;
			foreach (HYSYSStream stream in _HYSYSTree._PFDObject.Streams)
			{
				if (stream.Stream.ToUpper().Trim() == item.ToUpper().Trim())
				{
					flag = true;
					stream.bSourceIsTFlow = true;
					break;
				}
			}
			if (!flag)
			{
				HYSYSStream hYSYSStream = new HYSYSStream();
				hYSYSStream.Stream = item;
				hYSYSStream.Source = columnData;
				hYSYSStream.IsPseudoStreamWithNoTarget = true;
				_HYSYSTree._PFDObject.Streams.Add(hYSYSStream);
			}
		}
	}

	private void HandleRecycles(string[] Tears)
	{
		for (int i = 0; i < Tears.Length; i++)
		{
			int value = 0;
			bool flag = false;
			bool flag2 = false;
			bool flag3 = false;
			bool flag4 = false;
			bool flag5 = false;
			HYSYSColumn hYSYSColumn = null;
			HYSYSHeatExchanger hYSYSHeatExchanger = null;
			HYSYSAirCooler hYSYSAirCooler = null;
			HYSYSFiredHeater hYSYSFiredHeater = null;
			HYSYSLNGHeatExchanger hYSYSLNGHeatExchanger = null;
			HYSYSStream streamByName = HYSYSStream.GetStreamByName(Tears[i]);
			HYSYSUnitOperationBase destination = streamByName.Destination;
			if (destination.GetType() == typeof(HYSYSColumn))
			{
				flag = true;
				hYSYSColumn = destination as HYSYSColumn;
				hYSYSColumn.ColumnFeeds.TryGetValue(streamByName.Stream, out value);
			}
			else if (destination.GetType() == typeof(HYSYSHeatExchanger))
			{
				flag2 = true;
				hYSYSHeatExchanger = destination as HYSYSHeatExchanger;
			}
			else if (destination.GetType() == typeof(HYSYSAirCooler))
			{
				flag3 = true;
				hYSYSAirCooler = destination as HYSYSAirCooler;
			}
			else if (destination.GetType() == typeof(HYSYSFiredHeater))
			{
				flag4 = true;
				hYSYSFiredHeater = destination as HYSYSFiredHeater;
			}
			else if (destination.GetType() == typeof(HYSYSLNGHeatExchanger))
			{
				flag5 = true;
				hYSYSLNGHeatExchanger = destination as HYSYSLNGHeatExchanger;
			}
			else
			{
				for (int j = 0; j < destination.InletStreams.Count; j++)
				{
					if (destination.InletStreams[j] == streamByName.Stream)
					{
						value = j;
					}
				}
			}
			HYSYSRecycle hYSYSRecycle = new HYSYSRecycle();
			hYSYSRecycle.UID = "R_" + streamByName.Stream;
			List<HYSYSBaseObject> list = new List<HYSYSBaseObject>();
			list.Add(hYSYSRecycle);
			hYSYSRecycle.InletStreams.Add(streamByName.Stream);
			HYSYSStream hYSYSStream = HYSYSStream.Clone(streamByName);
			hYSYSStream.Stream = "S_" + streamByName.Stream + "_R";
			hYSYSStream.FluidPackage = streamByName.Destination.FluidPackage;
			streamByName.FluidPackage = streamByName.Source.FluidPackage;
			hYSYSRecycle.OutletStreams.Add(hYSYSStream.Stream);
			if (flag)
			{
				hYSYSColumn.ColumnFeeds.Remove(streamByName.Stream);
				hYSYSColumn.ColumnFeeds.Add(hYSYSStream.Stream, value);
				hYSYSColumn.HYSYSMainFlowsheetFeeds.Remove(streamByName.Stream);
				hYSYSColumn.HYSYSMainFlowsheetFeeds.Add(hYSYSStream.Stream);
			}
			else if (flag2)
			{
				if (hYSYSHeatExchanger.ColdSideInletStreams.Contains(streamByName.Stream))
				{
					hYSYSHeatExchanger.ColdSideInletStreams.Remove(streamByName.Stream);
					hYSYSHeatExchanger.ColdSideInletStreams.Add(hYSYSStream.Stream);
				}
				else if (hYSYSHeatExchanger.HotSideInletStreams.Contains(streamByName.Stream))
				{
					hYSYSHeatExchanger.HotSideInletStreams.Remove(streamByName.Stream);
					hYSYSHeatExchanger.HotSideInletStreams.Add(hYSYSStream.Stream);
				}
			}
			else if (flag3)
			{
				if (hYSYSAirCooler.TubeSideInletStreams.Contains(streamByName.Stream))
				{
					hYSYSAirCooler.TubeSideInletStreams.Remove(streamByName.Stream);
					hYSYSAirCooler.TubeSideInletStreams.Add(hYSYSStream.Stream);
				}
			}
			else if (flag4)
			{
				if (hYSYSFiredHeater.COMBSIDEINLETSTREAMS.Contains(streamByName.Stream))
				{
					hYSYSFiredHeater.COMBSIDEINLETSTREAMS.Remove(streamByName.Stream);
					hYSYSFiredHeater.COMBSIDEINLETSTREAMS.Add(hYSYSStream.Stream);
				}
				else if (hYSYSFiredHeater.TUBESIDEINLETSTREAMS.Contains(streamByName.Stream))
				{
					hYSYSFiredHeater.TUBESIDEINLETSTREAMS.Remove(streamByName.Stream);
					hYSYSFiredHeater.TUBESIDEINLETSTREAMS.Add(hYSYSStream.Stream);
				}
				else if (hYSYSFiredHeater.AIRSIDESTREAMS.Contains(streamByName.Stream))
				{
					hYSYSFiredHeater.AIRSIDESTREAMS.Remove(streamByName.Stream);
					hYSYSFiredHeater.AIRSIDESTREAMS.Add(hYSYSStream.Stream);
				}
			}
			else if (flag5)
			{
				for (int k = 0; k < hYSYSLNGHeatExchanger.HotSidesInletStreams.Count; k++)
				{
					if (hYSYSLNGHeatExchanger.HotSidesInletStreams[k].Contains(streamByName.Stream))
					{
						hYSYSLNGHeatExchanger.HotSidesInletStreams[k].Remove(streamByName.Stream);
						hYSYSLNGHeatExchanger.HotSidesInletStreams[k].Add(hYSYSStream.Stream);
						break;
					}
				}
				for (int l = 0; l < hYSYSLNGHeatExchanger.ColdSidesInletStreams.Count; l++)
				{
					if (hYSYSLNGHeatExchanger.ColdSidesInletStreams[l].Contains(streamByName.Stream))
					{
						hYSYSLNGHeatExchanger.ColdSidesInletStreams[l].Remove(streamByName.Stream);
						hYSYSLNGHeatExchanger.ColdSidesInletStreams[l].Add(hYSYSStream.Stream);
						break;
					}
				}
			}
			else
			{
				destination.InletStreams[value] = hYSYSStream.Stream;
			}
			hYSYSRecycle.SetCreateStreams(list);
			_HYSYSTree._PFDObject.HYSYSRecycles.Add(hYSYSRecycle);
			string key = $"UNITOPERATION\\{hYSYSRecycle.UID}";
			HYSYSBaseObject.g_Map.Add(key, hYSYSRecycle);
			if (hYSYSStream.Temp == null)
			{
				hYSYSStream.Temp = new DoubleValue("K", 298.0);
			}
			if (hYSYSStream.Pressure == null)
			{
				hYSYSStream.Pressure = new DoubleValue("bar", 1.0);
			}
			if (hYSYSStream.Composition.Data.Count == 0)
			{
				hYSYSStream.Composition = new HYSYSStream.StreamComposition();
				hYSYSStream.Composition.Basis = CompostionBasisTypes.MoleFrac;
				if (P2Objects.P2Component.GetAllComponents() != null)
				{
					for (int m = 0; m < P2Objects.P2Component.GetAllComponents().Count; m++)
					{
						string hYSYSCompName = P2Objects.P2Component.GetHYSYSCompName(m + 1);
						if (hYSYSCompName != null && !hYSYSStream.Composition.Data.ContainsKey(hYSYSCompName))
						{
							hYSYSStream.Composition.Data.Add(hYSYSCompName, 1.0);
						}
					}
				}
				if (hYSYSStream.Rate == null)
				{
					hYSYSStream.Rate = new TypeUnitValue("gmole/s", "M", 30.0);
				}
			}
			_HYSYSTree._PFDObject.Streams.Add(hYSYSStream);
		}
	}

	private string[] GetCutStreams()
	{
		bool flag = false;
		List<string> list = new List<string>();
		GetStreamsInfo();
		StartDoTear();
		try
		{
			StreamReader streamReader = new FileInfo(_tempMvFolder + "\\CutResults.dat").OpenText();
			string text;
			while ((text = streamReader.ReadLine()) != null)
			{
				if (flag && !string.IsNullOrEmpty(text))
				{
					list.Add(text.Trim());
				}
				if (text.Contains("TEAR STREAM"))
				{
					flag = true;
				}
				if (flag && text == "")
				{
					flag = false;
				}
			}
			streamReader.Close();
			return list.ToArray();
		}
		catch (Exception)
		{
			return null;
		}
		finally
		{
			if (File.Exists(_tempMvFolder + "\\Streams.dat"))
			{
				File.Delete(_tempMvFolder + "\\Streams.dat");
			}
			if (File.Exists(_tempMvFolder + "\\CutResults.dat"))
			{
				File.Delete(_tempMvFolder + "\\CutResults.dat");
			}
		}
	}

	private void GetStreamsInfo()
	{
		try
		{
			int num = 0;
			int num2 = 0;
			if (P2Objects.P2Component.GetAllComponents() != null)
			{
				num2 = P2Objects.P2Component.GetAllComponents().Count;
			}
			if (File.Exists(_tempMvFolder + "\\Streams.dat"))
			{
				File.Delete(_tempMvFolder + "\\Streams.dat");
			}
			FileStream fileStream = new FileStream(_tempMvFolder + "\\Streams.dat", FileMode.Create);
			StreamWriter streamWriter = new StreamWriter(fileStream);
			foreach (HYSYSStream stream in _HYSYSTree._PFDObject.Streams)
			{
				int num3 = num2 + 2;
				num = ((stream.Composition.Data.Count == 0) ? (4 * num3) : (2 * num3));
				string text = ((stream.Source == null) ? null : stream.Source.UID);
				string text2 = ((stream.Destination == null) ? null : stream.Destination.UID);
				if (text == null)
				{
					text = "----";
				}
				if (text2 == null)
				{
					text2 = "----";
				}
				string value = stream.Stream + "   " + text + "   " + text2 + "     " + num;
				streamWriter.WriteLine(value);
			}
			streamWriter.Flush();
			streamWriter.Close();
			fileStream.Close();
		}
		catch (Exception)
		{
		}
	}

	private void StartDoTear()
	{
		string applicationFolder = BaseObject.ApplicationFolder;
		if (File.Exists(_tempMvFolder + "\\CutResults.dat"))
		{
			File.Delete(_tempMvFolder + "\\CutResults.dat");
		}
		string text = applicationFolder + "\\dotear.exe";
		string text2 = _tempMvFolder + "\\Streams.dat";
		string text3 = _tempMvFolder + "\\CutResults.dat";
		if (!File.Exists(text) || !File.Exists(text2))
		{
			return;
		}
		Process process = new Process();
		process.StartInfo.FileName = text;
		process.StartInfo.Arguments = "\"" + text2 + "\" \"" + text3 + "\"";
		try
		{
			process.Start();
			bool flag = false;
			for (int i = 0; i < 60; i++)
			{
				Thread.Sleep(50);
				if (process.HasExited)
				{
					flag = true;
					break;
				}
			}
			if (!flag)
			{
				throw new Exception("DoTear Timed Out");
			}
			process.Close();
		}
		catch (Exception)
		{
			throw;
		}
	}

	public void Save(string FilePath)
	{
		if (_SimCase != null)
		{
			_SimCase.SaveAs(FilePath);
		}
	}

	public void SaveXML(string FilePath)
	{
		if (_SimCase != null)
		{
			_SimCase.SaveAs(FilePath);
		}
	}

	public void UninitHYSYS()
	{
		if (_AppHYSYS != null)
		{
			foreach (FluidPackage fluidPackage in _FluidPackages)
			{
				Marshal.FinalReleaseComObject(fluidPackage);
			}
			_FluidPackages.Clear();
			Marshal.FinalReleaseComObject(_FlowSheet);
			_FlowSheet = null;
			Marshal.FinalReleaseComObject(_SimCase);
			_SimCase = null;
			Marshal.FinalReleaseComObject(_AppHYSYS);
			_AppHYSYS = null;
		}
		_HYSYSTree = new HYSYSTree();
	}

	public void SetThermoProperty(HYSYSFluidPackages packages)
	{
		if (_SimCase == null || packages == null)
		{
			return;
		}
		bool flag = false;
		if (_HYSYSTree._BasicManager.Components != null && _HYSYSTree._BasicManager.Components.Assays != null && _HYSYSTree._BasicManager.Components.Assays.Count > 0)
		{
			flag = true;
		}
		HYSYSActivityPropertyConverter hYSYSActivityPropertyConverter = null;
		if (!flag && (packages.IsEOSUsingAspenPlus || packages.HasAspenPlusActivityProperty))
		{
			hYSYSActivityPropertyConverter = new HYSYSActivityPropertyConverter(_SimCase.BasisManager, _HYSYSTree._BasicManager.Packages);
			_ImportSuccess = hYSYSActivityPropertyConverter.ImportSuccesse;
			if (hYSYSActivityPropertyConverter != null && !hYSYSActivityPropertyConverter.ImportSuccesse && _HYSYSTree._BasicManager.HYSYSRXSets != null)
			{
				for (int i = 0; i < _HYSYSTree._BasicManager.HYSYSRXSets.Count; i++)
				{
					for (int j = 0; j < _HYSYSTree._BasicManager.HYSYSRXSets[i].HYSYSReactions.Count; j++)
					{
						if (_HYSYSTree._BasicManager.HYSYSRXSets[i].HYSYSReactions[j].IsAspenPlusProperty)
						{
							_HYSYSTree._BasicManager.HYSYSRXSets[i].HYSYSReactions[j].IsAspenPlusProperty = false;
						}
					}
				}
			}
		}
		if ((hYSYSActivityPropertyConverter != null && !hYSYSActivityPropertyConverter.ImportSuccesse) || flag || !packages.HasAspenPlusActivityProperty)
		{
			foreach (HYSYSFluidPackage activityFluidPackage in packages.ActivityFluidPackages)
			{
				CreatFluidPackage(activityFluidPackage);
			}
		}
		if (packages.IsEOSUsingAspenPlus && (hYSYSActivityPropertyConverter == null || hYSYSActivityPropertyConverter.ImportSuccesse))
		{
			return;
		}
		foreach (HYSYSFluidPackage eOSFluidPackage in packages.EOSFluidPackages)
		{
			if (packages.EOSFluidPackages.Count == 1 && eOSFluidPackage.IsDefault && packages.IsAtuoCreatedDefault && _SimCase.BasisManager.FluidPackages.Count > 0)
			{
				break;
			}
			CreatFluidPackage(eOSFluidPackage);
		}
	}
