	private void SetThermoEnthalpyAndDensity(HYSYSFluidPackages packages)
	{
		if (_SimCase == null || _FluidPackages == null)
		{
			return;
		}
		foreach (FluidPackage fluidPackage in _FluidPackages)
		{
			HYSYSFluidPackage hYSYSFluidPackage = null;
			foreach (HYSYSFluidPackage eOSFluidPackage in packages.EOSFluidPackages)
			{
				if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(eOSFluidPackage.FluidPackageName))
				{
					if (fluidPackage.PropertyPackageName == eOSFluidPackage.ThomoKey)
					{
						hYSYSFluidPackage = eOSFluidPackage;
						break;
					}
				}
				else if (fluidPackage.name == eOSFluidPackage.FluidPackageName)
				{
					hYSYSFluidPackage = eOSFluidPackage;
					break;
				}
			}
			if (hYSYSFluidPackage == null)
			{
				continue;
			}
			BackDoor backDoor = fluidPackage.PropertyPackage as BackDoor;
			if (hYSYSFluidPackage.Enthalpy != null && hYSYSFluidPackage.Enthalpy.Value == "LK")
			{
				if (hYSYSFluidPackage.FluidPackageName == "GLYCOLPKG")
				{
					if (hYSYSFluidPackage.Enthalpy.Qualifier == "L")
					{
						(backDoor.get_BackDoorVariable((object)":Selection.597").Variable as InternalRealVariable).SetValue(1.0, string.Empty);
					}
					if (hYSYSFluidPackage.Enthalpy.Qualifier == "V")
					{
						(backDoor.get_BackDoorVariable((object)":Selection.599").Variable as InternalRealVariable).SetValue(2.0, string.Empty);
					}
				}
				else if (hYSYSFluidPackage.FluidPackageName == "KDSRK")
				{
					(backDoor.get_BackDoorVariable((object)":Selection.300").Variable as InternalRealVariable).SetValue(1.0, string.Empty);
				}
				else
				{
					(backDoor.get_BackDoorVariable((object)":Selection.390").Variable as InternalRealVariable).SetValue(1.0, string.Empty);
				}
			}
			if (hYSYSFluidPackage.Density != null && hYSYSFluidPackage.Density.Value == "RACKETT")
			{
				(backDoor.get_BackDoorVariable((object)":Selection.591").Variable as InternalRealVariable).SetValue(0.0, string.Empty);
				(backDoor.get_BackDoorVariable((object)":Selection.594").Variable as InternalRealVariable).SetValue(1.0, string.Empty);
				backDoor.SendBackDoorMessage("EstimateVolTransForPR");
			}
		}
	}

	private int GetHysysCompIndex(string hysysCompName)
	{
		int result = -1;
		string[] source = (string[])_SimCase.Flowsheet.FluidPackage.ComponentList.Components.Names;
		if (source.Contains(hysysCompName))
		{
			result = _SimCase.Flowsheet.FluidPackage.ComponentList.Components.get_index((object)hysysCompName);
		}
		else if (source.Contains(hysysCompName + "*"))
		{
			result = _SimCase.Flowsheet.FluidPackage.ComponentList.Components.get_index((object)(hysysCompName + "*"));
		}
		else
		{
			string plusCompNameFromHYSYS = P2Objects.P2Component.GetPlusCompNameFromHYSYS(hysysCompName);
			if (source.Contains(plusCompNameFromHYSYS))
			{
				result = _SimCase.Flowsheet.FluidPackage.ComponentList.Components.get_index((object)plusCompNameFromHYSYS);
			}
		}
		return result;
	}

	private void AddStreams()
	{
		int num;
		try
		{
			num = ((string[])_SimCase.Flowsheet.FluidPackage.ComponentList.Components.Names).Length;
		}
		catch (Exception)
		{
			return;
		}
		foreach (HYSYSStream stream in _HYSYSTree._PFDObject.Streams)
		{
			if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(stream.Stream) || stream.IsPseudoStreamWithNoTarget)
			{
				continue;
			}
			if (!(_FlowSheet.MaterialStreams.Names as string[]).ToList().Contains(stream.Stream))
			{
				_FlowSheet.MaterialStreams.Add(stream.Stream, "");
			}
			ProcessStream processStream = null;
			try
			{
				processStream = _FlowSheet.MaterialStreams[stream.Stream] as ProcessStream;
				string[] source = (string[])_SimCase.BasisManager.FluidPackages.Names;
				BackDoor backDoor = null;
				if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(stream.FluidPackage) && stream.FluidPackage.ToUpper().Trim() != "DEFAULT")
				{
					backDoor = (_SimCase.BasisManager.FluidPackages[stream.FluidPackage] as FluidPackage) as BackDoor;
				}
				if (source.Contains(stream.FluidPackage) && processStream.FluidPackage.name != stream.FluidPackage)
				{
					(processStream as BackDoor).SendAttachObject(":CompList.300", backDoor);
				}
				else if (stream.Destination != null)
				{
					HYSYSUnitOperationBase destination = stream.Destination;
					if (destination != null && !string.IsNullOrEmpty(destination.FluidPackage) && source.Contains(destination.FluidPackage))
					{
						backDoor = (_SimCase.BasisManager.FluidPackages[destination.FluidPackage] as FluidPackage) as BackDoor;
						if (processStream.FluidPackage.name != destination.FluidPackage)
						{
							(processStream as BackDoor).SendAttachObject(":CompList.300", backDoor);
						}
					}
				}
			}
			catch (Exception)
			{
				continue;
			}
			try
			{
				if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(stream.ConvertNotes))
				{
					((processStream as BackDoor).get_BackDoorTextVariable((object)":Attribute.500").Variable as InternalTextVariable).Value = stream.ConvertNotes;
				}
				double[] array = new double[num];
				double num2 = 0.0;
				foreach (KeyValuePair<string, double> datum in stream.Composition.Data)
				{
					num2 += datum.Value;
				}
				if (stream.Composition != null && stream.Composition.Data != null)
				{
					bool flag = false;
					foreach (KeyValuePair<string, double> datum2 in stream.Composition.Data)
					{
						int hysysCompIndex = GetHysysCompIndex(datum2.Key);
						if (hysysCompIndex >= num || hysysCompIndex < 0)
						{
							continue;
						}
						if (stream.Composition.Basis == CompostionBasisTypes.MassFlow || stream.Composition.Basis == CompostionBasisTypes.MoleFlow || stream.Composition.Basis == CompostionBasisTypes.LVFlow)
						{
							array[hysysCompIndex] = datum2.Value;
						}
						else if (stream.Composition.Basis == CompostionBasisTypes.GVFlow)
						{
							if (!stream.IsAssayStream)
							{
								string unit = HYSYSUOMMap.GetDefault(UnitClassEnum.Rate, "GV").Unit;
								DoubleValue convertedValue = HYSYSUOMMap.GetConvertedValue(UnitClassEnum.Rate, new DoubleValue(stream.Composition.CompositionUOM, datum2.Value), unit, "GV");
								array[hysysCompIndex] = convertedValue.Value * 0.0422115;
								flag = true;
							}
						}
						else if (stream.Composition.Basis == CompostionBasisTypes.GVFrac)
						{
							flag = true;
							array[hysysCompIndex] = datum2.Value / num2;
						}
						else
						{
							array[hysysCompIndex] = datum2.Value / num2;
						}
					}
					if (flag)
					{
						stream.Composition.CompositionUOM = "kgmole/s";
						if (stream.Composition.Basis == CompostionBasisTypes.GVFlow)
						{
							stream.Composition.Basis = CompostionBasisTypes.MoleFlow;
						}
						else if (stream.Composition.Basis == CompostionBasisTypes.GVFrac)
						{
							stream.Composition.Basis = CompostionBasisTypes.MoleFrac;
						}
					}
				}
				if (stream.bSourceIsTFlow)
				{
					continue;
				}
				if (stream.Source == null && !stream.IsAssayStream && string.IsNullOrEmpty(stream.RefStream))
				{
					if (stream.Composition.Basis == CompostionBasisTypes.MassFlow)
					{
						processStream.ComponentMassFlow.SetValues(array, stream.Composition.CompositionUOM);
					}
					else if (stream.Composition.Basis == CompostionBasisTypes.MoleFlow)
					{
						processStream.ComponentMolarFlow.SetValues(array, stream.Composition.CompositionUOM);
					}
					else if (stream.Composition.Basis == CompostionBasisTypes.LVFlow)
					{
						processStream.ComponentVolumeFlow.SetValues(array, stream.Composition.CompositionUOM);
					}
					else if (stream.Composition.Basis == CompostionBasisTypes.MoleFrac)
					{
						processStream.ComponentMolarFraction.SetValues(array, "");
					}
					else if (stream.Composition.Basis == CompostionBasisTypes.MassFrac)
					{
						processStream.ComponentMassFraction.SetValues(array, "");
					}
					else if (stream.Composition.Basis == CompostionBasisTypes.LVFrac)
					{
						processStream.ComponentVolumeFraction.SetValues(array, "");
					}
				}
				goto IL_0542;
			}
			catch (Exception)
			{
				goto IL_0542;
			}
			IL_0542:
			try
			{
				HYSYSStream hYSYSStream = null;
				foreach (HYSYSStream stream2 in _HYSYSTree._PFDObject.Streams)
				{
					if (stream2.Stream == stream.RefStream)
					{
						hYSYSStream = stream2;
						break;
					}
				}
				if ((stream.Source != null && hYSYSStream != null && hYSYSStream.Source != null && hYSYSStream.Source.UID == stream.Source.UID) || (stream.Source == null && hYSYSStream != null && hYSYSStream.Source == null) || stream.IsNeedSetRateForRefStream)
				{
					SetStreamTPR(stream, processStream, isForSetRef: true);
				}
				else
				{
					SetStreamTPR(stream, processStream, isForSetRef: false);
				}
			}
			catch (Exception)
			{
			}
		}
	}

	private void SetStreamTPR(HYSYSStream stream, ProcessStream hysysStream, bool isForSetRef)
	{
		if (hysysStream == null)
		{
			return;
		}
		BackDoor backDoor = null;
		if (!(stream.Source == null || isForSetRef))
		{
			return;
		}
		if (stream.Temp != null)
		{
			hysysStream.Temperature.SetValue(stream.Temp.Value, stream.Temp.Unit);
		}
		if (stream.Pressure != null)
		{
			hysysStream.Pressure.SetValue(stream.Pressure.Value, stream.Pressure.Unit);
		}
		if (stream.VaporFrac != null && (stream.Temp == null || stream.Pressure == null))
		{
			hysysStream.VapourFraction.SetValue(stream.VaporFrac.Value, "");
		}
		if (stream.NeedSetRateZero)
		{
			backDoor = hysysStream as BackDoor;
			InternalRealVariable obj = backDoor.get_BackDoorVariable((object)":MassFlow.502.0").Variable as InternalRealVariable;
			obj.Value = 0.0;
			CleanResource.ReleaseComObjectHelper(obj, bFinalRelease: false);
		}
		if (stream.Rate == null || stream.Rate.Value == 0.0)
		{
			return;
		}
		backDoor = hysysStream as BackDoor;
		string type = stream.Rate.Type;
		if (type != null)
		{
			int length = type.Length;
			if (length != 1)
			{
				if (length == 2)
				{
					char c = type[0];
					if (c != 'G')
					{
						if (c != 'L')
						{
							if (c == 'W' && type == "WT")
							{
								goto IL_01ac;
							}
						}
						else if (type == "LV")
						{
							goto IL_01e4;
						}
					}
					else if (type == "GV")
					{
						goto IL_0254;
					}
				}
			}
			else
			{
				char c = type[0];
				if ((uint)c <= 77u)
				{
					if (c == 'G')
					{
						goto IL_0254;
					}
					if (c == 'M')
					{
						InternalRealVariable obj2 = backDoor.get_BackDoorVariable((object)":MolarFlow.502.0").Variable as InternalRealVariable;
						obj2.SetValue(stream.Rate.Value, stream.Rate.Unit);
						CleanResource.ReleaseComObjectHelper(obj2, bFinalRelease: false);
						return;
					}
				}
				else
				{
					if (c == 'V')
					{
						goto IL_01e4;
					}
					if (c == 'W')
					{
						goto IL_01ac;
					}
				}
			}
		}
		InternalRealVariable obj3 = backDoor.get_BackDoorVariable((object)":MolarFlow.502.0").Variable as InternalRealVariable;
		obj3.SetValue(stream.Rate.Value, stream.Rate.Unit);
		CleanResource.ReleaseComObjectHelper(obj3, bFinalRelease: false);
		return;
		IL_01e4:
		InternalRealVariable obj4 = backDoor.get_BackDoorVariable((object)":LiqVolFlow.502.0").Variable as InternalRealVariable;
		obj4.SetValue(stream.Rate.Value, stream.Rate.Unit);
		CleanResource.ReleaseComObjectHelper(obj4, bFinalRelease: false);
		return;
		IL_01ac:
		InternalRealVariable obj5 = backDoor.get_BackDoorVariable((object)":MassFlow.502.0").Variable as InternalRealVariable;
		obj5.SetValue(stream.Rate.Value, stream.Rate.Unit);
		CleanResource.ReleaseComObjectHelper(obj5, bFinalRelease: false);
		return;
		IL_0254:
		if (stream.IsAssayStream)
		{
			InternalRealVariable obj6 = backDoor.get_BackDoorVariable((object)":LiqVolFlow.502.0").Variable as InternalRealVariable;
			obj6.SetValue(stream.Rate.Value, stream.Rate.Unit);
			CleanResource.ReleaseComObjectHelper(obj6, bFinalRelease: false);
			return;
		}
		string unit = HYSYSUOMMap.GetDefault(UnitClassEnum.Rate, "GV").Unit;
		DoubleValue convertedValue = HYSYSUOMMap.GetConvertedValue(UnitClassEnum.Rate, new DoubleValue(stream.Rate.Unit, stream.Rate.Value), unit, "GV");
		convertedValue = new DoubleValue("kgmole/s", convertedValue.Value * 0.0422115);
		if (convertedValue != null)
		{
			InternalRealVariable obj7 = backDoor.get_BackDoorVariable((object)":MolarFlow.502.0").Variable as InternalRealVariable;
			obj7.SetValue(convertedValue.Value, convertedValue.Unit);
			CleanResource.ReleaseComObjectHelper(obj7, bFinalRelease: false);
		}
	}

	private ProcessStream GetProcessStream(Flowsheet flowSheet, string streamName)
	{
		ProcessStream result = null;
		string[] source = (string[])flowSheet.MaterialStreams.Names;
		if (source.Contains(streamName))
		{
			result = flowSheet.MaterialStreams[streamName] as ProcessStream;
		}
		else if (source.Contains(streamName + "-"))
		{
			result = flowSheet.MaterialStreams[streamName + "-"] as ProcessStream;
		}
		else if (source.Contains(streamName + "+"))
		{
			result = flowSheet.MaterialStreams[streamName + "+"] as ProcessStream;
		}
		return result;
	}

	private void SetCutterTransferBasis()
	{
		Operations operations = _FlowSheet.get_Operations((object)"StreamCutterOp");
		if (operations == null && operations.Count == 0)
		{
			return;
		}
		List<string> list = new List<string>();
		foreach (HYSYSCutter hYSYSCutter in _HYSYSTree._PFDObject.HYSYSCutters)
		{
			list.Add(hYSYSCutter.UID);
		}
		foreach (StreamCutterOp item in operations)
		{
			if (!list.Contains(item.name) && item != null)
			{
				((item as BackDoor).get_BackDoorVariable((object)"Transition.500(FluidPkgTransitionObject) :Enum.300").Variable as InternalRealVariable).Value = 0.0;
			}
		}
	}

	private void SetRefStreamUsingBalanceAndSet()
	{
		try
		{
			bool flag = false;
			foreach (HYSYSStream stream in _HYSYSTree._PFDObject.Streams)
			{
				if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(stream.RefStream))
				{
					flag = true;
					break;
				}
			}
			if (!flag)
			{
				return;
			}
			foreach (HYSYSStream stream2 in _HYSYSTree._PFDObject.Streams)
			{
				if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(stream2.RefStream))
				{
					continue;
				}
				HYSYSStream hYSYSStream = null;
				foreach (HYSYSStream stream3 in _HYSYSTree._PFDObject.Streams)
				{
					if (stream3.Stream == stream2.RefStream)
					{
						hYSYSStream = stream3;
						break;
					}
				}
				if (hYSYSStream == null)
				{
					continue;
				}
				if ((stream2.Source != null && hYSYSStream != null && hYSYSStream.Source != null && hYSYSStream.Source.UID != stream2.Source.UID) || (stream2.Source != null && hYSYSStream != null && hYSYSStream.Source == null))
				{
					DebugPrint.OutputDebugString(DebugLevel.BuildHYSYSCase, WarningLevel.Notes, string.Format(P2Globalization.GetString("Helper_RefStreamNotConverted"), stream2.Stream, stream2.RefStream, stream2.Source.UID));
					continue;
				}
				string name = NameHelper.CreateName(bIsStream: false, "_Balance_Ref");
				BackDoor backDoor = (_FlowSheet.get_Operations((object)"BalanceOpObject").Add(name, "BalanceOpObject") as BalanceOp) as BackDoor;
				BackDoor backDoor2 = GetProcessStream(_FlowSheet, hYSYSStream.Stream) as BackDoor;
				backDoor.SendAttachObject(":Stream.500.0", backDoor2);
				ProcessStream processStream = GetProcessStream(_FlowSheet, stream2.Stream);
				BackDoor backDoor3 = processStream as BackDoor;
				backDoor.SendAttachObject(":Stream.501.0", backDoor3);
				FluidPackage fluidPackage = GetProcessStream(_FlowSheet, hYSYSStream.Stream).FluidPackage;
				BackDoor backDoor4 = fluidPackage as BackDoor;
				if (processStream.FluidPackage.name != fluidPackage.name)
				{
					backDoor3.SendAttachObject(":CompList.300", backDoor4);
				}
				if (stream2.Temp == null && stream2.Pressure == null)
				{
					(backDoor.get_BackDoorVariable((object)":Selection.500").Variable as InternalRealVariable).Value = 1.0;
					string name2 = NameHelper.CreateName(bIsStream: false, "_Set_Ref");
					SetOp obj = _FlowSheet.get_Operations((object)"SetOpObject").Add(name2, "SetOpObject") as SetOp;
					obj.TargetVariable = processStream.Pressure;
					(obj as BackDoor).SendAttachObject(":FlowSheetObject.501", backDoor2);
					string name3 = NameHelper.CreateName(bIsStream: false, "_Set_Ref");
					SetOp obj2 = _FlowSheet.get_Operations((object)"SetOpObject").Add(name3, "SetOpObject") as SetOp;
					obj2.TargetVariable = processStream.Temperature;
					(obj2 as BackDoor).SendAttachObject(":FlowSheetObject.501", backDoor2);
				}
				else if (stream2.Temp == null || stream2.Pressure == null)
				{
					(backDoor.get_BackDoorVariable((object)":Selection.500").Variable as InternalRealVariable).Value = 1.0;
					if (stream2.Temp == null)
					{
						string name4 = NameHelper.CreateName(bIsStream: false, "_Set_Ref");
						SetOp obj3 = _FlowSheet.get_Operations((object)"SetOpObject").Add(name4, "SetOpObject") as SetOp;
						obj3.TargetVariable = processStream.Temperature;
						(obj3 as BackDoor).SendAttachObject(":FlowSheetObject.501", backDoor2);
					}
					else if (stream2.Pressure == null)
					{
						string name5 = NameHelper.CreateName(bIsStream: false, "_Set_Ref");
						SetOp obj4 = _FlowSheet.get_Operations((object)"SetOpObject").Add(name5, "SetOpObject") as SetOp;
						obj4.TargetVariable = processStream.Pressure;
						(obj4 as BackDoor).SendAttachObject(":FlowSheetObject.501", backDoor2);
					}
				}
				else if (stream2.Temp != null && stream2.Pressure != null)
				{
					(backDoor.get_BackDoorVariable((object)":Selection.500").Variable as InternalRealVariable).Value = 1.0;
				}
			}
		}
		catch
		{
		}
	}

	private void SetRefStreamUsingMutipleStream()
	{
		bool flag = false;
		foreach (HYSYSStream stream in _HYSYSTree._PFDObject.Streams)
		{
			if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(stream.RefStream))
			{
				flag = true;
				break;
			}
		}
		if (!flag)
		{
			return;
		}
		foreach (HYSYSStream stream2 in _HYSYSTree._PFDObject.Streams)
		{
			if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(stream2.RefStream))
			{
				continue;
			}
			HYSYSStream hYSYSStream = null;
			foreach (HYSYSStream stream3 in _HYSYSTree._PFDObject.Streams)
			{
				if (stream3.Stream == stream2.RefStream)
				{
					hYSYSStream = stream3;
					break;
				}
			}
			if (hYSYSStream == null)
			{
				continue;
			}
			if ((stream2.Source != null && hYSYSStream != null && hYSYSStream.Source != null && hYSYSStream.Source.UID != stream2.Source.UID) || (stream2.Source != null && hYSYSStream != null && hYSYSStream.Source == null))
			{
				DebugPrint.OutputDebugString(DebugLevel.BuildHYSYSCase, WarningLevel.Notes, string.Format(P2Globalization.GetString("Helper_RefStreamNotConverted"), stream2.Stream, stream2.RefStream, stream2.Source.UID));
				continue;
			}
			if (stream2.Temp == null && !stream2.IsRateHaveBeenSet)
			{
				HYSYSValve hYSYSValve = new HYSYSValve();
				hYSYSValve.UID = NameHelper.CreateName(bIsStream: false, "Ref_Valve");
				hYSYSValve.InletStreams.Add(stream2.RefStream);
				hYSYSValve.OutletStreams.Add(stream2.Stream);
				if (stream2.Pressure != null)
				{
					hYSYSValve.ATTACHED_PRESSURE = stream2.Pressure;
				}
				else
				{
					hYSYSValve.ATTACHED_PRESSUREDROP = new DoubleValue("", 0.0);
				}
				hYSYSValve.ConvertNotes.Add(string.Format(P2Globalization.GetString("Helper_HandleRefStream"), stream2.Stream, stream2.RefStream));
				new HYSYSValveConverter(_SimCase, hYSYSValve);
			}
			else if (stream2.Temp != null)
			{
				HYSYSHeatExchanger hYSYSHeatExchanger = new HYSYSHeatExchanger(stream2.Pressure != null);
				hYSYSHeatExchanger.ExchangerType = HysysHeatExchangerType.Heater;
				hYSYSHeatExchanger.UID = NameHelper.CreateName(bIsStream: false, "Ref_Exchanger");
				hYSYSHeatExchanger.ColdSideOutletTemperature = stream2.Temp;
				hYSYSHeatExchanger.InletStreams.Add(stream2.RefStream);
				hYSYSHeatExchanger.OutletStreams.Add(stream2.Stream);
				hYSYSHeatExchanger.LoadStreams();
				hYSYSHeatExchanger.ConvertNotes.Add(string.Format(P2Globalization.GetString("Helper_HandleRefStream"), stream2.Stream, stream2.RefStream));
				new HYSYSHeatExchangerConverter(_SimCase, hYSYSHeatExchanger, !Options.IsHYSYSHeaterExchanger);
			}
			ProcessStream processStream = GetProcessStream(_FlowSheet, stream2.Stream);
			FluidPackage fluidPackage = GetProcessStream(_FlowSheet, hYSYSStream.Stream).FluidPackage;
			BackDoor backDoor = fluidPackage as BackDoor;
			BackDoor backDoor2 = processStream as BackDoor;
			if (processStream.FluidPackage.name != fluidPackage.name)
			{
				backDoor2.SendAttachObject(":CompList.300", backDoor);
			}
		}
	}

	private void AddGlobalDefine()
	{
		HYSYSFlowsheetParameterConverter.ResetSpreadsheet();
		AddMixerGlobalDefine();
		AddHeatExchangerGlobalDefine();
		AddSeparatorGlobalDefine();
		AddPipeSegGlobalDefine();
		AddEquilReactorGlobalDefine();
		AddConvReactorGlobalDefine();
		AddGibbsReactorGlobalDefine();
		AddCSTRGlobalDefine();
		AddPFReactorGlobalDefine();
		AddCompressorGlobalDefine();
		AddExpanderGlobalDefine();
		AddPumpGlobalDefine();
		AddValveGlobalDefine();
		AddSplitterGlobalDefine();
		AddLNGGlobalDefine();
		AddColumnGlobalDefine();
		AddComponentsplitterGlobalDefine();
		HYSYSFlowsheetParameterConverter.EnableSpreadsheet();
		HYSYSFlowsheetParameterConverter.ResetStaticMembers();
	}

	private void AddComponentsplitterGlobalDefine()
	{
		foreach (HYSYSComponentSplitter hYSYSComponentSplitter in _HYSYSTree._PFDObject.HYSYSComponentSplitters)
		{
			if (hYSYSComponentSplitter.GlobalDefine.Count == 0)
			{
				continue;
			}
			foreach (DEFINE item in hYSYSComponentSplitter.GlobalDefine)
			{
				if (item.bConverted)
				{
					continue;
				}
				try
				{
					new HYSYSFlowsheetParameterConverter(_SimCase, _HYSYSTree, hYSYSComponentSplitter.UID, item, 18);
				}
				catch (Exception)
				{
				}
				finally
				{
					if (!item.bConverted)
					{
						DebugPrint.OutputDebugString(DebugLevel.BuildHYSYSCase, WarningLevel.Warning, string.Format(P2Globalization.GetString("ComponentSplitterDefineNotConverted"), hYSYSComponentSplitter.UID, item.P2DefineExpression));
					}
					else
					{
						item.bConverted = false;
					}
				}
			}
		}
	}

	private void AddColumnGlobalDefine()
	{
		foreach (HYSYSColumn hYSYSColumn in _HYSYSTree._PFDObject.HYSYSColumns)
		{
			if (hYSYSColumn.GlobalDefine.Count == 0)
			{
				continue;
			}
			foreach (DEFINE item in hYSYSColumn.GlobalDefine)
			{
				if (item.bConverted && string.IsNullOrEmpty(item.sColumnSpecName))
				{
					continue;
				}
				try
				{
					new HYSYSFlowsheetParameterConverter(_SimCase, _HYSYSTree, hYSYSColumn.UID, item, 1);
				}
				catch (Exception)
				{
				}
				finally
				{
					if (!item.bConverted)
					{
						if (item.MainProperty.ToUpper().Trim().StartsWith("PTOP"))
						{
							DebugPrint.OutputDebugString(DebugLevel.BuildHYSYSCase, WarningLevel.Warning, string.Format(P2Globalization.GetString("PressureDataInComplete"), item.P2DefineExpression, hYSYSColumn.UID));
						}
						else
						{
							DebugPrint.OutputDebugString(DebugLevel.BuildHYSYSCase, WarningLevel.Warning, string.Format(P2Globalization.GetString("ColumnDefineNotConvert"), hYSYSColumn.UID, item.P2DefineExpression));
						}
					}
					else
					{
						item.bConverted = false;
					}
				}
			}
		}
	}

	private void AddMixerGlobalDefine()
	{
		foreach (HYSYSMixer hYSYSMixer in _HYSYSTree._PFDObject.HYSYSMixers)

				finally

	private void AddHeatExchangerGlobalDefine()
	{
		foreach (HYSYSHeatExchanger hYSYSHeatExchange in _HYSYSTree._PFDObject.HYSYSHeatExchanges)
		{
			if (hYSYSHeatExchange.GlobalDefine.Count == 0)
			{
				continue;
			}
			foreach (DEFINE item in hYSYSHeatExchange.GlobalDefine)
			{
				if (item.bConverted)
				{
					continue;
				}
				try
				{
					if (item.MainProperty.StartsWith(CUnitOperation.DUTY) && string.IsNullOrEmpty(item.SecondRefID))
					{
						SetDutyDefine(item);
					}
					if (!item.bConverted)
					{
						if (hYSYSHeatExchange.ExchangerType == HysysHeatExchangerType.Cooler)
						{
							new HYSYSFlowsheetParameterConverter(_SimCase, _HYSYSTree, hYSYSHeatExchange.UID, item, 10);
						}
						else if (hYSYSHeatExchange.ExchangerType == HysysHeatExchangerType.Heater)
						{
							new HYSYSFlowsheetParameterConverter(_SimCase, _HYSYSTree, hYSYSHeatExchange.UID, item, 9);
						}
						else if (hYSYSHeatExchange.ExchangerType == HysysHeatExchangerType.HeatExchanger)
						{
							new HYSYSFlowsheetParameterConverter(_SimCase, _HYSYSTree, hYSYSHeatExchange.UID, item, 11);
						}
					}
				}
				catch (Exception)
				{
				}
				finally
				{
					if (!item.bConverted)
					{
						DebugPrint.OutputDebugString(DebugLevel.BuildHYSYSCase, WarningLevel.Warning, string.Format(P2Globalization.GetString("HXDefineNotConverted"), hYSYSHeatExchange.UID, item.P2DefineExpression));
					}
					else
					{
						item.bConverted = false;
					}
				}
			}
		}
	}

	private void AddSeparatorGlobalDefine()
	{
		foreach (HYSYSSeparator hysysSeparator in _HYSYSTree._PFDObject.HysysSeparators)
		{
			if (hysysSeparator.GlobalDefine.Count == 0)
			{
				continue;
			}
			foreach (DEFINE item in hysysSeparator.GlobalDefine)
			{
				if (item.bConverted)
				{
					continue;
				}
				try
				{
					if (item.MainProperty.StartsWith(CUnitOperation.DUTY) && string.IsNullOrEmpty(item.SecondRefID))
					{
						SetDutyDefine(item);
					}
					else
					{
						SetSeparatorTempOrPressureDefine(item, hysysSeparator);
					}
					if (!item.bConverted)
					{
						new HYSYSFlowsheetParameterConverter(_SimCase, _HYSYSTree, hysysSeparator.UID, item, 2);
					}
				}
				catch (Exception)
				{
				}
				finally
				{
					if (!item.bConverted)
					{
						Separator separator = null;
						separator = ((hysysSeparator.SeparatorType != HYSYSSeparatorType.Separator) ? (_FlowSheet.get_Operations((object)"Sep3OpObject")[hysysSeparator.UID] as Separator) : (_FlowSheet.get_Operations((object)"FlashTankObject")[hysysSeparator.UID] as Separator));
						InternalTextVariable internalTextVariable = (separator as BackDoor).get_BackDoorTextVariable((object)":Notes.400").Variable as InternalTextVariable;
						internalTextVariable.Value = "\n" + internalTextVariable.Value + string.Format(P2Globalization.GetString("FlashDefineNotConverted"), hysysSeparator.UID, item.P2DefineExpression) + "\n";
						DebugPrint.OutputDebugString(DebugLevel.BuildHYSYSCase, WarningLevel.Warning, string.Format(P2Globalization.GetString("FlashDefineNotConverted"), hysysSeparator.UID, item.P2DefineExpression));
					}
					else
					{
						item.bConverted = false;
					}
				}
			}
		}
	}

		}
	}

	private void AddConvReactorGlobalDefine()
	{
		foreach (HYSYSConversionReactor hYSYSConversionReactor in _HYSYSTree._PFDObject.HYSYSConversionReactors)
		{
			if (hYSYSConversionReactor.GlobalDefine.Count == 0)
			{
				continue;
			}
			foreach (DEFINE item in hYSYSConversionReactor.GlobalDefine)
			{
				if (item.bConverted)
				{
					continue;
				}
				try
				{
					if (item.MainProperty.StartsWith(CUnitOperation.DUTY) && string.IsNullOrEmpty(item.SecondRefID))
					{
						SetDutyDefine(item);
					}
					if (!item.bConverted)
					{
						new HYSYSFlowsheetParameterConverter(_SimCase, _HYSYSTree, hYSYSConversionReactor.UID, item, 14);
					}
				}
				catch (Exception)
				{
				}
				finally
				{
					if (!item.bConverted)
					{
						DebugPrint.OutputDebugString(DebugLevel.BuildHYSYSCase, WarningLevel.Warning, string.Format(P2Globalization.GetString("ConvRDefineNotConverted"), hYSYSConversionReactor.UID, item.P2DefineExpression));
					}
					else
					{
						item.bConverted = false;
					}
				}
			}
		}
	}

	private void AddExpanderGlobalDefine()
	{
		foreach (HYSYSExpander hYSYSExpander in _HYSYSTree._PFDObject.HYSYSExpanders)
		{
			if (hYSYSExpander.GlobalDefine.Count == 0)
			{
				continue;
			}
			foreach (DEFINE item in hYSYSExpander.GlobalDefine)
			{
				if (item.bConverted)
				{
					continue;
				}
				try
				{
					if (item.MainProperty.StartsWith(CUnitOperation.WORK) && string.IsNullOrEmpty(item.SecondRefID))
					{
						SetDutyDefine(item);
					}
					if (item.FirstRefType.ToUpper() == "EXPANDER")
					{
						ExpandOp expandOp = _FlowSheet.get_Operations((object)"ExpandOp")[hYSYSExpander.UID] as ExpandOp;
						ExpandOp expandOp2 = _FlowSheet.get_Operations((object)"ExpandOp")[item.FirstRefID] as ExpandOp;
						BackDoor backDoor = null;
						BackDoor backDoor2 = null;
						if (item.FirstRefProperty.ToUpper() == "EFF")
						{
							if (expandOp.ExpAdiabaticEff != null)
							{
								expandOp.ExpAdiabaticEff.Value = -32767.0;
							}
							SetOp obj = _FlowSheet.get_Operations((object)"SetOpObject").Add("Set_" + hYSYSExpander.UID + "_EFF", "SetOpObject") as SetOp;
							obj.TargetVariable = expandOp.ExpAdiabaticEff;
							backDoor = obj as BackDoor;
							backDoor2 = expandOp2 as BackDoor;
							backDoor.SendAttachObject(":FlowSheetObject.501", backDoor2);
							item.bConverted = true;
							CleanResource.ReleaseComObjectHelper(obj, bFinalRelease: false);
						}
						CleanResource.ReleaseComObjectHelper(backDoor, bFinalRelease: false);
						CleanResource.ReleaseComObjectHelper(backDoor2, bFinalRelease: false);
						CleanResource.ReleaseComObjectHelper(expandOp, bFinalRelease: false);
						CleanResource.ReleaseComObjectHelper(expandOp2, bFinalRelease: false);
					}
					if (!item.bConverted)
					{
						new HYSYSFlowsheetParameterConverter(_SimCase, _HYSYSTree, hYSYSExpander.UID, item, 7);
					}
				}
				catch (Exception)
				{
				}
				finally
				{
					if (!item.bConverted)
					{
						DebugPrint.OutputDebugString(DebugLevel.BuildHYSYSCase, WarningLevel.Warning, string.Format(P2Globalization.GetString("ExpanderDefineNotConverted"), hYSYSExpander.UID, item.P2DefineExpression));
					}
					else
					{
						item.bConverted = false;
					}
				}
			}
		}
	}

	private void SetDutyDefine(DEFINE def)
	{
		ProcessStream energyStreamFromUID = GetEnergyStreamFromUID(def.MainObjID);
		if (energyStreamFromUID.HeatFlow.Value == 0.0)
		{
			energyStreamFromUID.HeatFlow.Erase();
		}
		ProcessStream energyStreamFromUID2 = GetEnergyStreamFromUID(def.FirstRefID);
		if (energyStreamFromUID != null && energyStreamFromUID2 != null && (def.OperatorName == null || def.OperatorName.ToUpper().Contains("MULT") || def.OperatorName.ToUpper().Contains("TIME")))
		{
			SetOp obj = _FlowSheet.get_Operations((object)"SetOpObject").Add("Set_" + def.MainObjID + "_Duty", "SetOpObject") as SetOp;
			obj.TargetVariable = energyStreamFromUID.HeatFlow;
			BackDoor obj2 = obj as BackDoor;
			BackDoor backDoor = energyStreamFromUID2 as BackDoor;
			obj2.SendAttachObject(":FlowSheetObject.501", backDoor);
			double num = -100.0;
			num = (string.IsNullOrEmpty(def.OperatorName) ? 1.0 : (string.IsNullOrEmpty(def.Value) ? double.Parse(def.SecondRefProperty) : double.Parse(def.Value)));
			obj.MultiplierValue = num;
			def.bConverted = true;
		}
	}

	private void SetSeparatorTempOrPressureDefine(DEFINE def, HYSYSSeparator separator)
	{
		try
		{
			bool flag = false;
			bool flag2 = false;
			if (def.MainProperty.StartsWith(CUnitOperation.TEMPERATURE) && string.IsNullOrEmpty(def.SecondRefID))
			{
				flag = true;
			}
			else if (def.MainProperty.StartsWith(CUnitOperation.PRESSURE) && string.IsNullOrEmpty(def.SecondRefID))
			{
				flag2 = true;
			}
			if (!flag && !flag2)
			{
				return;
			}
			ProcessStream processStream = GetProcessStream(_FlowSheet, separator.VapourOutletStream);
			ProcessStream processStream2 = GetProcessStream(_FlowSheet, def.FirstRefID);
			if (processStream != null && processStream2 != null && (def.FirstRefType == "STRM" || def.FirstRefType.StartsWith("STRE")))
			{
				SetOp setOp = null;
				if (flag)
				{
					setOp = _FlowSheet.get_Operations((object)"SetOpObject").Add("Set_" + def.FirstRefID + "_Temp", "SetOpObject") as SetOp;
					setOp.TargetVariable = processStream.Temperature;
				}
				if (flag2)
				{
					setOp = _FlowSheet.get_Operations((object)"SetOpObject").Add("Set_" + def.FirstRefID + "_Pres", "SetOpObject") as SetOp;
					setOp.TargetVariable = processStream.Pressure;
				}
				setOp.SourceObject = processStream2;
				if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(def.OperatorName) && (def.OperatorName.StartsWith("MINU") || def.OperatorName.StartsWith("DIFF") || def.OperatorName.StartsWith("SUBT")))
				{
					double.TryParse(def.SecondRefProperty, out var result);
					setOp.OffsetValue = -1.0 * result;
				}
				else if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(def.OperatorName) && (def.OperatorName.StartsWith("PLUS") || def.OperatorName.StartsWith("SUM") || def.OperatorName.StartsWith("ADD")))
				{
					double.TryParse(def.SecondRefProperty, out var result2);
					setOp.OffsetValue = result2;
				}
				double multiplierValue = -100.0;
				if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(def.OperatorName))
				{
					multiplierValue = 1.0;
				}
				else if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(def.OperatorName) && (def.OperatorName.StartsWith("MULT") || def.OperatorName.StartsWith("TIME")))
				{
					double.TryParse(def.SecondRefProperty, out var result3);
					multiplierValue = result3;
				}
				else if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(def.OperatorName) && (def.OperatorName.StartsWith("DIVI") || def.OperatorName.StartsWith("RATI")))
				{
					double.TryParse(def.SecondRefProperty, out var result4);
					multiplierValue = 1.0 / result4;
				}
				setOp.MultiplierValue = multiplierValue;
				def.bConverted = true;
			}
		}
		catch
		{
		}
	}

	private ProcessStream GetEnergyStreamFromUID(string strUID)
	{
		foreach (HYSYSColumn hYSYSColumn in _HYSYSTree._PFDObject.HYSYSColumns)
		{
			if (hYSYSColumn.UID == strUID)
			{
				return null;
			}
		}
		ProcessStream processStream = null;
		string[] array = (string[])_FlowSheet.EnergyStreams.Names;
		foreach (string index in array)
		{
			processStream = _FlowSheet.EnergyStreams[index] as ProcessStream;
			if (((string[])processStream.AttachedOpers.Names).Contains(strUID))
			{
				return processStream;
			}
		}
		return null;
	}
}
internal class HYSYSSplitterConverter : HYSYSObjectConverterBase
{
	private enum PropertyBasis
	{
		WT,
		LV,
		GV,
		M
	}

	private TeeOp _Tee;

	private HYSYSSplitter _Data;

	private double totalMolorFlow;

	private double totalMassFlow;

	private double totalStdLV;

	public HYSYSSplitterConverter(SimulationCase simulationCase, HYSYSSplitter data)
		: base(simulationCase)
	{
		_Data = data;
		try
		{
			_Tee = _Flowsheet.get_Operations((object)"TeeOpObject").Add(data.UID, "TeeOpObject") as TeeOp;
			CreateTee();
		}
		catch (Exception)
		{
		}
	}

	private void CreateTee()
	{
		BackDoor backDoor = null;
		ProcessStream processStream = null;
		ProcessStream processStream2 = null;
		BackDoor backDoor2 = null;
		BackDoor backDoor3 = null;
		new List<Specification>();
		try
		{
			backDoor = _Tee as BackDoor;
			if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(_Data.FluidPackage))
			{
				string methodName = HYSYSFluidPackages.GetMethodName(_Data.FluidPackage);
				if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(methodName))
				{
					backDoor3 = _SimCase.BasisManager.FluidPackages[methodName] as BackDoor;
					backDoor.SendAttachObject(":CompList.300", backDoor3);
				}
			}
			if (_Data.InletStreams != null)
			{
				processStream = GetProcessStream(_Data.InletStreams[0]);
				backDoor2 = processStream as BackDoor;
				backDoor.SendAttachObject(":MaterialStream.500", backDoor2);
				GetFeedInfo(backDoor2);
			}
			if (_Data.OutletStreams != null)
			{
				for (int i = 0; i < _Data.OutletStreams.Count; i++)
				{
					processStream2 = GetProcessStream(_Data.OutletStreams[i]);
					backDoor2 = processStream2 as BackDoor;
					backDoor.SendAttachObject(":MaterialStream.501." + i, backDoor2);
				}
			}
			if (_Data.OutletStreams.Count.Equals(_Data.LocalSpec.Count))
			{
				double num = 0.0;
				foreach (Specification item in _Data.LocalSpec)
				{
					if (item.Operator == Specification.OperatorType.none)
					{
						num += item.Value.Value;
					}
				}
				for (int j = 0; j < _Data.LocalSpec.Count; j++)
				{
					Specification specification = _Data.LocalSpec[j];
					if (specification.Operator == Specification.OperatorType.none)
					{
						specification.Operator = Specification.OperatorType.divide;
						specification.ReferenceName = "REFFEED";
						specification.Value.Value = specification.Value.Value / num;
					}
				}
			}
			foreach (Specification item2 in _Data.LocalSpec)
			{
				if (item2.SpecType == SpecificationType.STREAM && item2.PrimaryPropertyName.ToUpper().Trim().StartsWith(CStream.RATE))
				{
					AddSpecificationForCurrentProduct(backDoor, item2);
				}
			}
			InternalTextVariable internalTextVariable = backDoor.get_BackDoorTextVariable((object)":Notes.400").Variable as InternalTextVariable;
			foreach (string convertNote in _Data.ConvertNotes)
			{
				internalTextVariable.Value = internalTextVariable.Value + convertNote + "\n";
			}
		}
		catch (Exception)
		{
			DebugPrint.OutputDebugString(DebugLevel.BuildHYSYSCase, WarningLevel.Error, string.Format(P2Globalization.GetString("UOConverter_Failed"), "HYSYS Splitter unit operation block", _Data.UID));
		}
		finally
		{
			CleanResource.ReleaseComObjectHelper(backDoor, bFinalRelease: false);
			CleanResource.ReleaseComObjectHelper(processStream2, bFinalRelease: false);
			CleanResource.ReleaseComObjectHelper(processStream, bFinalRelease: false);
			CleanResource.ReleaseComObjectHelper(backDoor2, bFinalRelease: false);
			CleanResource.ReleaseComObjectHelper(backDoor3, bFinalRelease: false);
		}
	}

	private void AddSpecificationForCurrentProduct(BackDoor teeBD, Specification currentSpec)
	{
		InternalRealVariable internalRealVariable = null;
		ProcessStream processStream = null;
		try
		{
			int index = _Data.OutletStreams.IndexOf(currentSpec.PrimaryID);
			if (index.Equals(-1))
			{
				return;
			}
			processStream = GetProcessStream(_Data.OutletStreams[index]);
			internalRealVariable = teeBD.get_BackDoorVariable((object)(":Ratio.500." + index)).Variable as InternalRealVariable;
			switch (currentSpec.Operator)
			{
			case Specification.OperatorType.plus:
				_Data.ConvertNotes.Add(string.Format(P2Globalization.GetString("LSNotConvert"), currentSpec.P2SpecExpression, _Data.UID));
				break;
			case Specification.OperatorType.minus:
				_Data.ConvertNotes.Add(string.Format(P2Globalization.GetString("LSNotConvert"), currentSpec.P2SpecExpression, _Data.UID));
				break;
			case Specification.OperatorType.times:
				_Data.ConvertNotes.Add(string.Format(P2Globalization.GetString("LSNotConvert"), currentSpec.P2SpecExpression, _Data.UID));
				break;
			case Specification.OperatorType.divide:
			{
				if (currentSpec.ReferenceName.ToUpper().Contains("REFFEED") || currentSpec.ReferenceID.Equals(_Data.InletStreams[0]))
				{
					if (internalRealVariable.CanModify)
					{
						internalRealVariable.Value = currentSpec.Value.Value;
					}
					break;
				}
				int index2 = _Data.OutletStreams.IndexOf(currentSpec.ReferenceID);
				if (index2.Equals(-1) || !(teeBD.get_BackDoorVariable((object)(":Ratio.500." + index2)).Variable is InternalRealVariable internalRealVariable2))
				{
					break;
				}
				if (internalRealVariable2.Value != -32767.0)
				{
					if (internalRealVariable.CanModify)
					{
						internalRealVariable.Value = internalRealVariable2.Value * currentSpec.Value.Value;
					}
				}
				else
				{
					ProcessStream processStream2 = GetProcessStream(_Data.OutletStreams[index2]);
					RealVariable molarFlow = processStream2.MolarFlow;
					if (molarFlow.Value != -32767.0)
					{
						_ = molarFlow.Value;
						_ = currentSpec.Value.Value;
						CleanResource.ReleaseComObjectHelper(processStream.MolarFlow, bFinalRelease: false);
					}
					CleanResource.ReleaseComObjectHelper(processStream2, bFinalRelease: false);
					CleanResource.ReleaseComObjectHelper(molarFlow, bFinalRelease: false);
				}
				CleanResource.ReleaseComObjectHelper(internalRealVariable2, bFinalRelease: false);
				break;
			}
			case Specification.OperatorType.none:
			{
				PropertyBasis rateBasis = PropertyBasis.M;
				string text = RetrieveRateUnit(currentSpec.PrimaryPropertyName, out rateBasis);
				if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(text))
				{
					currentSpec.Value.Unit = text;
				}
				bool flag = false;
				double num = 0.0;
				if (currentSpec.ReferenceStartComponent != 0)
				{
					flag = true;
					num = GetComponentCoefficient(currentSpec.ReferenceStartComponent - 1, processStream);
				}
				TypeUnitValue typeUnitValue = null;
				DoubleValue doubleValue = null;
				switch (rateBasis)
				{
				case PropertyBasis.WT:
					typeUnitValue = UnitConverter.GetHysysValue(UnitClassEnum.Rate, currentSpec.Value.Value, currentSpec.Value.Unit, "WT");
					doubleValue = new DoubleValue(typeUnitValue.Unit, typeUnitValue.Value);
					doubleValue = HYSYSUOMMap.GetConvertedValue(UnitClassEnum.Rate, doubleValue, "kg/h", "WT");
					if (flag && totalMassFlow != 0.0)
					{
						if (internalRealVariable.CanModify)
						{
							if (num != 0.0)
							{
								internalRealVariable.Value = doubleValue.Value / num / totalMassFlow;
							}
							else
							{
								internalRealVariable.Value = doubleValue.Value / totalMassFlow;
							}
						}
					}
					else if (flag && totalMassFlow == 0.0)
					{
						SetMassFlow(typeUnitValue, processStream, currentSpec.ReferenceStartComponent - 1);
					}
					else
					{
						SetMassFlow(typeUnitValue, processStream, -1);
					}
					break;
				case PropertyBasis.LV:
					typeUnitValue = UnitConverter.GetHysysValue(UnitClassEnum.Rate, currentSpec.Value.Value, currentSpec.Value.Unit, "LV");
					doubleValue = new DoubleValue(typeUnitValue.Unit, typeUnitValue.Value);
					doubleValue = HYSYSUOMMap.GetConvertedValue(UnitClassEnum.Rate, doubleValue, "m3/h", "LV");
					if (flag && totalStdLV != 0.0)
					{
						if (internalRealVariable.CanModify)
						{
							if (num != 0.0)
							{
								internalRealVariable.Value = doubleValue.Value / num / totalStdLV;
							}
							else
							{
								internalRealVariable.Value = doubleValue.Value / totalStdLV;
							}
						}
					}
					else if (flag && totalStdLV == 0.0)
					{
						SetLiquidVolumn(typeUnitValue, processStream, currentSpec.ReferenceStartComponent - 1);
					}
					else
					{
						SetLiquidVolumn(typeUnitValue, processStream, -1);
					}
					break;
				case PropertyBasis.GV:
					typeUnitValue = UnitConverter.GetHysysValue(UnitClassEnum.Rate, currentSpec.Value.Value, currentSpec.Value.Unit, "GV");
					doubleValue = new DoubleValue(typeUnitValue.Unit, typeUnitValue.Value);
					doubleValue = HYSYSUOMMap.GetConvertedValue(UnitClassEnum.Rate, doubleValue, "m3/h", "GV");
					doubleValue = new DoubleValue("kgmole/h", doubleValue.Value * 0.0422115);
					if (flag && totalMolorFlow != 0.0)
					{
						if (internalRealVariable.CanModify)
						{
							if (num != 0.0)
							{
								internalRealVariable.Value = doubleValue.Value / num / totalMolorFlow;
							}
							else
							{
								internalRealVariable.Value = doubleValue.Value / totalMolorFlow;
							}
						}
					}
					else if (flag && totalMolorFlow == 0.0)
					{
						SetMolorFlow(doubleValue, processStream, currentSpec.ReferenceStartComponent - 1);
					}
					else
					{
						SetMolorFlow(doubleValue, processStream, -1);
					}
					break;
				case PropertyBasis.M:
					typeUnitValue = UnitConverter.GetHysysValue(UnitClassEnum.Rate, currentSpec.Value.Value, currentSpec.Value.Unit, "M");
					doubleValue = new DoubleValue(typeUnitValue.Unit, typeUnitValue.Value);
					doubleValue = HYSYSUOMMap.GetConvertedValue(UnitClassEnum.Rate, doubleValue, "kgmole/h", "M");
					if (flag && totalMolorFlow != 0.0)
					{
						if (internalRealVariable.CanModify)
						{
							if (num != 0.0)
							{
								internalRealVariable.Value = doubleValue.Value / num / totalMolorFlow;
							}
							else
							{
								internalRealVariable.Value = doubleValue.Value / totalMolorFlow;
							}
						}
					}
					else if (flag && totalMolorFlow == 0.0)
					{
						SetMolorFlow(doubleValue, processStream, currentSpec.ReferenceStartComponent - 1);
					}
					else
					{
						SetMolorFlow(doubleValue, processStream, -1);
					}
					break;
				}
				break;
			}
			}
		}
		catch (Exception)
		{
			DebugPrint.OutputDebugString(DebugLevel.BuildHYSYSCase, WarningLevel.Error, string.Format(P2Globalization.GetString("SetUOSplitsFailed"), "Splitter", _Data.UID));
		}
		finally
		{
			CleanResource.ReleaseComObjectHelper(internalRealVariable, bFinalRelease: false);
			CleanResource.ReleaseComObjectHelper(processStream, bFinalRelease: false);
		}
	}

	private string RetrieveRateUnit(string rateExpress, out PropertyBasis rateBasis)
	{
		string text = string.Empty;
		rateBasis = PropertyBasis.M;
		if (rateExpress.Contains("("))
		{
			int num = rateExpress.IndexOf('(');
			int num2 = rateExpress.IndexOf(')');
			string text2 = rateExpress.Substring(num + 1, num2 - num - 1);
			if (text2.Contains(","))
			{
				string[] array = ParseUtilty.CutByComma(text2);
				text = array.GetValue(1).ToString();
				string strA = array.GetValue(0).ToString();
				if (string.Compare(strA, "w", ignoreCase: true) == 0 || string.Compare(strA, "wt", ignoreCase: true) == 0)
				{
					rateBasis = PropertyBasis.WT;
				}
				else if (string.Compare(strA, "lv", ignoreCase: true) == 0 || string.Compare(text, "bbl", ignoreCase: true) == 0)
				{
					rateBasis = PropertyBasis.LV;
				}
				else if (string.Compare(strA, "gv", ignoreCase: true) == 0)
				{
					rateBasis = PropertyBasis.GV;
				}
				else
				{
					rateBasis = PropertyBasis.M;
				}
			}
			else if (string.Compare(text2, "w", ignoreCase: true) == 0 || string.Compare(text2, "wt", ignoreCase: true) == 0)
			{
				rateBasis = PropertyBasis.WT;
			}
			else if (string.Compare(text2, "lv", ignoreCase: true) == 0)
			{
				rateBasis = PropertyBasis.LV;
			}
			else if (text2.StartsWith("bbl/", StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase))
			{
				rateBasis = PropertyBasis.LV;
				text = text2;
			}
			else if (string.Compare(text2, "gv", ignoreCase: true) == 0)
			{
				rateBasis = PropertyBasis.GV;
			}
			else
			{
				rateBasis = PropertyBasis.M;
				text = text2;
			}
		}
		return text;
	}

	private void GetFeedInfo(BackDoor feedBD)
	{
		InternalRealVariable internalRealVariable = null;
		try
		{
			internalRealVariable = feedBD.get_BackDoorVariable((object)":MassFlow.502.0").Variable as InternalRealVariable;
			if (internalRealVariable != null && internalRealVariable.Value != -32767.0)
			{
				totalMassFlow = internalRealVariable.Value * 3600.0;
			}
			internalRealVariable = feedBD.get_BackDoorVariable((object)":MolarFlow.502.0").Variable as InternalRealVariable;
			if (internalRealVariable != null && internalRealVariable.Value != -32767.0)
			{
				totalMolorFlow = internalRealVariable.Value * 3600.0;
			}
			internalRealVariable = feedBD.get_BackDoorVariable((object)":StdLiqVolFlow.505.0").Variable as InternalRealVariable;
			if (internalRealVariable != null && internalRealVariable.Value != -32767.0)
			{
				totalStdLV = internalRealVariable.Value * 3600.0;
			}
		}
		catch (Exception)
		{
			DebugPrint.OutputDebugString(DebugLevel.BuildHYSYSCase, WarningLevel.Error, string.Format(P2Globalization.GetString("GetUOFeedInfoFailed"), "Splitter", _Data.UID));
		}
		finally
		{
			CleanResource.ReleaseComObjectHelper(internalRealVariable, bFinalRelease: false);
		}
	}

	private void SetMolorFlow(DoubleValue value, ProcessStream stream, int CompIndex)
	{
		InternalRealVariable internalRealVariable = null;
		BackDoor backDoor = null;
		try
		{
			backDoor = stream as BackDoor;
			if (CompIndex != -1)
			{
				backDoor.SendBackDoorMessage("View SettingsView");
				InternalRealVariable obj = backDoor.get_BackDoorVariable((object)":Enum.501").Variable as InternalRealVariable;
				obj.Value = 12.0;
				backDoor.SendBackDoorMessage("CloseView");
				CleanResource.ReleaseComObjectHelper(obj, bFinalRelease: false);
				if (backDoor.get_BackDoorVariable((object)(":CompMoleFlow.530.0." + CompIndex)).Variable is InternalRealVariable { CanModify: not false } internalRealVariable2)
				{
					internalRealVariable2.SetValue(value.Value, value.Unit);
				}
			}
			else
			{
				internalRealVariable = backDoor.get_BackDoorVariable((object)":MolarFlow.502.0").Variable as InternalRealVariable;
				if (internalRealVariable.CanModify)
				{
					internalRealVariable.SetValue(value.Value, value.Unit);
				}
			}
		}
		catch (Exception ex)
		{
			throw ex;
		}
		finally
		{
			CleanResource.ReleaseComObjectHelper(internalRealVariable, bFinalRelease: false);
			CleanResource.ReleaseComObjectHelper(backDoor, bFinalRelease: false);
		}
	}

	private void SetMassFlow(TypeUnitValue value, ProcessStream stream, int CompIndex)
	{
		InternalRealVariable internalRealVariable = null;
		BackDoor backDoor = null;
		try
		{
			backDoor = stream as BackDoor;
			if (CompIndex != -1)
			{
				backDoor.SendBackDoorMessage("View SettingsView");
				InternalRealVariable obj = backDoor.get_BackDoorVariable((object)":Enum.501").Variable as InternalRealVariable;
				obj.Value = 13.0;
				backDoor.SendBackDoorMessage("CloseView");
				CleanResource.ReleaseComObjectHelper(obj, bFinalRelease: false);
				if (backDoor.get_BackDoorVariable((object)(":CompMassFlow.530.0." + CompIndex)).Variable is InternalRealVariable { CanModify: not false } internalRealVariable2)
				{
					internalRealVariable2.SetValue(value.Value, value.Unit);
				}
			}
			else
			{
				internalRealVariable = backDoor.get_BackDoorVariable((object)":MassFlow.502.0").Variable as InternalRealVariable;
				if (internalRealVariable.CanModify)
				{
					internalRealVariable.SetValue(value.Value, value.Unit);
				}
			}
		}
		catch (Exception ex)
		{
			throw ex;
		}
		finally
		{
			CleanResource.ReleaseComObjectHelper(internalRealVariable, bFinalRelease: false);
			CleanResource.ReleaseComObjectHelper(backDoor, bFinalRelease: false);
		}
	}

	private void SetLiquidVolumn(TypeUnitValue value, ProcessStream stream, int CompIndex)
	{
		InternalRealVariable internalRealVariable = null;
		BackDoor backDoor = null;
		try
		{
			backDoor = stream as BackDoor;
			if (CompIndex != -1)
			{
				backDoor.SendBackDoorMessage("View SettingsView");
				InternalRealVariable obj = backDoor.get_BackDoorVariable((object)":Enum.501").Variable as InternalRealVariable;
				obj.Value = 11.0;
				backDoor.SendBackDoorMessage("CloseView");
				CleanResource.ReleaseComObjectHelper(obj, bFinalRelease: false);
				if (backDoor.get_BackDoorVariable((object)(":CompVolFlow.530.0." + CompIndex)).Variable is InternalRealVariable { CanModify: not false } internalRealVariable2)
				{
					internalRealVariable2.SetValue(value.Value, value.Unit);
				}
			}
			else
			{
				internalRealVariable = backDoor.get_BackDoorVariable((object)":LiqVolFlow.502.0").Variable as InternalRealVariable;
				if (internalRealVariable.CanModify)
				{
					internalRealVariable.SetValue(value.Value, value.Unit);
				}
			}
		}
		catch (Exception ex)
		{
			throw ex;
		}
		finally
		{
			CleanResource.ReleaseComObjectHelper(internalRealVariable, bFinalRelease: false);
			CleanResource.ReleaseComObjectHelper(backDoor, bFinalRelease: false);
		}
	}

	private double GetComponentCoefficient(int compIndex, ProcessStream stream)
	{
		InternalRealVariable internalRealVariable = null;
		BackDoor backDoor = null;
		double result = 0.0;
		try
		{
			backDoor = stream as BackDoor;
			internalRealVariable = backDoor.get_BackDoorVariable((object)(":MoleFraction.530.0." + compIndex)).Variable as InternalRealVariable;
			if (internalRealVariable != null && internalRealVariable.Value != -32767.0)
			{
				result = internalRealVariable.Value;
			}
		}
		catch (Exception ex)
		{
			throw ex;
		}
		finally
		{
			CleanResource.ReleaseComObjectHelper(internalRealVariable, bFinalRelease: false);
			CleanResource.ReleaseComObjectHelper(backDoor, bFinalRelease: false);
		}
		return result;
	}
}

		HYSYSSideStrippers = new List<HYSYSColumn>();
		HYSYSSideRectifiers = new List<HYSYSColumn>();
		HYSYSPipes = new List<HYSYSPipe>();
		HYSYSPFReactors = new List<HYSYSPlugFlowReactor>();

public class HYSYSTree
{
	public BasicManager _BasicManager { get; set; }
