	{
		_BasicManager = new BasicManager();
		_PFDObject = new PFDObject();
	}
}
public class HYSYSUtilityConverter : HYSYSObjectConverterBase
{
	private HYSYSTree _HYSYSTree;

	private Flowsheet _FlowSheet;

	public HYSYSUtilityConverter(SimulationCase simulationCase, HYSYSTree hyHYSYSTree)
		: base(simulationCase)
	{
		_HYSYSTree = hyHYSYSTree;
		_FlowSheet = simulationCase.Flowsheet;
		AddUtilities();
	}

	private void AddUtilities()
	{
		try
		{
			PreHandlePropertyTableData();
			AddPhaseEnvelop();
			AddHydrate();
			AddPropertyTable();
			AddDepressuring();
		}
		catch (Exception)
		{
		}
	}

	private void AddPhaseEnvelop()
	{
		foreach (HYSYSPhaseEnvelope hYSYSPhaseEnvelope in _HYSYSTree._PFDObject.HYSYSPhaseEnvelopes)
		{
			try
			{
				EnvelopeUtility envelopeUtility = _SimCase.UtilityObjects.Add(hYSYSPhaseEnvelope.UID, "EnvelopeUtilityObject") as EnvelopeUtility;
				BackDoor backDoor = envelopeUtility as BackDoor;
				if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(hYSYSPhaseEnvelope.StreamName))
				{
					ProcessStream processStream = GetProcessStream(_FlowSheet, hYSYSPhaseEnvelope.StreamName);
					if (processStream != null)
					{
						envelopeUtility.AttachedStream = processStream;
					}
				}
				(backDoor.get_BackDoorVariable((object)":Boolean.541").Variable as InternalRealVariable).Value = 1.0;
				if (hYSYSPhaseEnvelope.Points > 100)
				{
					(backDoor.get_BackDoorVariable((object)":Index.551").Variable as InternalRealVariable).Value = hYSYSPhaseEnvelope.Points;
				}
				(backDoor.get_BackDoorVariable((object)":Boolean.500").Variable as InternalRealVariable).Value = 1.0;
				InternalTextVariable internalTextVariable = backDoor.get_BackDoorTextVariable((object)":Notes.400").Variable as InternalTextVariable;
				foreach (string convertNote in hYSYSPhaseEnvelope.ConvertNotes)
				{
					internalTextVariable.Value = internalTextVariable.Value + convertNote + "\n";
				}
			}
			catch (Exception)
			{
			}
		}
	}

	private void AddHydrate()
	{
		foreach (HYSYSHydrateUtility hYSYSHydrateUtility in _HYSYSTree._PFDObject.HYSYSHydrateUtilitys)
		{
			try
			{
				HydrateUtility hydrateUtility = _SimCase.UtilityObjects.Add(hYSYSHydrateUtility.UID, "HydrateUtilityObject") as HydrateUtility;
				BackDoor backDoor = hydrateUtility as BackDoor;
				if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(hYSYSHydrateUtility.StreamName))
				{
					ProcessStream processStream = GetProcessStream(_FlowSheet, hYSYSHydrateUtility.StreamName);
					if (processStream != null)
					{
						hydrateUtility.AttachedStream = processStream;
					}
				}
				if (hYSYSHydrateUtility.InitPressure != null)
				{
					(backDoor.get_BackDoorVariable((object)":Index.503").Variable as InternalRealVariable).Value = 3.0;
					(backDoor.get_BackDoorVariable((object)":Pressure.535").Variable as InternalRealVariable).SetValue(hYSYSHydrateUtility.InitPressure.Value, hYSYSHydrateUtility.InitPressure.Unit);
				}
				InternalTextVariable internalTextVariable = backDoor.get_BackDoorTextVariable((object)":Notes.400").Variable as InternalTextVariable;
				foreach (string convertNote in hYSYSHydrateUtility.ConvertNotes)
				{
					internalTextVariable.Value = internalTextVariable.Value + convertNote + "\n";
				}
			}
			catch (Exception)
			{
			}
		}
	}

	private string GetHYSYSYCaseCompName(string hysysCompName)
	{
		string result = string.Empty;
		string[] source = (string[])_SimCase.Flowsheet.FluidPackage.ComponentList.Components.Names;
		if (source.Contains(hysysCompName))
		{
			result = hysysCompName;
		}
		else if (source.Contains(hysysCompName + "*"))
		{
			result = hysysCompName + "*";
		}
		else
		{
			string plusCompNameFromHYSYS = P2Objects.P2Component.GetPlusCompNameFromHYSYS(hysysCompName);
			if (source.Contains(plusCompNameFromHYSYS))
			{
				result = plusCompNameFromHYSYS;
			}
		}
		return result;
	}

	private void AddPropertyTable()
	{
		foreach (HYSYSPropertyTableUtility hYSYSPropertyTableUtility in _HYSYSTree._PFDObject.HYSYSPropertyTableUtilitys)
		{
			try
			{
				if (hYSYSPropertyTableUtility.Kvalue)
				{
					PropertyTable propertyTable = _SimCase.UtilityObjects.Add(hYSYSPropertyTableUtility.UID, "PropertyTableObject") as PropertyTable;
					BackDoor backDoor = propertyTable as BackDoor;
					if (_FlowSheet.MaterialStreams[0] is ProcessStream attachedStream)
					{
						propertyTable.AttachedStream = attachedStream;
					}
					if (hYSYSPropertyTableUtility.PRESSURE != null)
					{
						(backDoor.get_BackDoorVariable((object)":Enum.500").Variable as InternalRealVariable).Value = 2.0;
						(backDoor.get_BackDoorVariable((object)":Selection.502").Variable as InternalRealVariable).Value = 0.0;
						(backDoor.get_BackDoorVariable((object)":ExtraData.506.0").Variable as InternalRealVariable).SetValue(hYSYSPropertyTableUtility.PRESSURE.Value, hYSYSPropertyTableUtility.PRESSURE.Unit);
						(backDoor.get_BackDoorVariable((object)":Enum.501").Variable as InternalRealVariable).Value = 1.0;
						(backDoor.get_BackDoorVariable((object)":ExtraData.501").Variable as InternalRealVariable).SetValue(0.0, "C");
						(backDoor.get_BackDoorVariable((object)":ExtraData.503").Variable as InternalRealVariable).SetValue(200.0, "C");
					}
					else if (hYSYSPropertyTableUtility.TEMP != null)
					{
						(backDoor.get_BackDoorVariable((object)":Enum.500").Variable as InternalRealVariable).Value = 1.0;
						(backDoor.get_BackDoorVariable((object)":Selection.502").Variable as InternalRealVariable).Value = 0.0;
						(backDoor.get_BackDoorVariable((object)":ExtraData.506.0").Variable as InternalRealVariable).SetValue(hYSYSPropertyTableUtility.TEMP.Value, hYSYSPropertyTableUtility.TEMP.Unit);
						(backDoor.get_BackDoorVariable((object)":Enum.501").Variable as InternalRealVariable).Value = 2.0;
						(backDoor.get_BackDoorVariable((object)":ExtraData.501").Variable as InternalRealVariable).SetValue(100.0, "kPa");
						(backDoor.get_BackDoorVariable((object)":ExtraData.503").Variable as InternalRealVariable).SetValue(1000.0, "kPa");
					}
					int hysysCompIndex = GetHysysCompIndex(hYSYSPropertyTableUtility.COMPi);
					string hYSYSYCaseCompName = GetHYSYSYCaseCompName(hYSYSPropertyTableUtility.COMPi);
					if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(hYSYSYCaseCompName))
					{
						string message = string.Format("AddNewDepProp KValue.501.{0}({1}) Comp K Value - Mixed Liquid ({1})", hysysCompIndex, hYSYSYCaseCompName);
						backDoor.SendBackDoorMessage(message);
					}
					hysysCompIndex = GetHysysCompIndex(hYSYSPropertyTableUtility.COMPj);
					hYSYSYCaseCompName = GetHYSYSYCaseCompName(hYSYSPropertyTableUtility.COMPj);
					if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(hYSYSYCaseCompName))
					{
						string message2 = string.Format("AddNewDepProp KValue.501.{0}({1}) Comp K Value - Mixed Liquid ({1})", hysysCompIndex, hYSYSYCaseCompName);
						backDoor.SendBackDoorMessage(message2);
					}
					backDoor.SendBackDoorMessage("ExecutePropertyTable");
					InternalTextVariable internalTextVariable = backDoor.get_BackDoorTextVariable((object)":Notes.400").Variable as InternalTextVariable;
					foreach (string convertNote in hYSYSPropertyTableUtility.ConvertNotes)
					{
						internalTextVariable.Value = internalTextVariable.Value + convertNote + "\n";
					}
				}
				if (!hYSYSPropertyTableUtility.IsHCurve)
				{
					continue;
				}
				PropertyTable propertyTable2 = _SimCase.UtilityObjects.Add(hYSYSPropertyTableUtility.UID, "PropertyTableObject") as PropertyTable;
				BackDoor backDoor2 = propertyTable2 as BackDoor;
				ProcessStream processStream = GetProcessStream(hYSYSPropertyTableUtility.StreamID);
				if (processStream != null)
				{
					propertyTable2.AttachedStream = processStream;
				}
				if (!hYSYSPropertyTableUtility.IsDewBubP && !hYSYSPropertyTableUtility.IsDewBubT)
				{
					InternalRealVariable internalRealVariable = backDoor2.get_BackDoorVariable((object)":Enum.500").Variable as InternalRealVariable;
					internalRealVariable.Value = 2.0;
					internalRealVariable = backDoor2.get_BackDoorVariable((object)":Selection.502").Variable as InternalRealVariable;
					if (hYSYSPropertyTableUtility.PRESSUREs != null && hYSYSPropertyTableUtility.PRESSUREs.Values.Length >= 2)
					{
						internalRealVariable.Value = 1.0;
						internalRealVariable = backDoor2.get_BackDoorVariable((object)":ExtraData.500").Variable as InternalRealVariable;
						internalRealVariable.SetValue(hYSYSPropertyTableUtility.PRESSUREs.Values[0], hYSYSPropertyTableUtility.PRESSUREs.Unit);
						internalRealVariable = backDoor2.get_BackDoorVariable((object)":ExtraData.502").Variable as InternalRealVariable;
						internalRealVariable.SetValue(hYSYSPropertyTableUtility.PRESSUREs.Values[1], hYSYSPropertyTableUtility.PRESSUREs.Unit);
					}
					else if (hYSYSPropertyTableUtility.DP != null && hYSYSPropertyTableUtility.DP.Value > 0.0)
					{
						internalRealVariable.Value = 1.0;
					}
					else
					{
						internalRealVariable.Value = 0.0;
						if (hYSYSPropertyTableUtility.PRESSURE != null)
						{
							internalRealVariable = backDoor2.get_BackDoorVariable((object)":ExtraData.506.0").Variable as InternalRealVariable;
							internalRealVariable.SetValue(hYSYSPropertyTableUtility.PRESSURE.Value, hYSYSPropertyTableUtility.PRESSURE.Unit);
						}
					}
					internalRealVariable = backDoor2.get_BackDoorVariable((object)":Enum.501").Variable as InternalRealVariable;
					internalRealVariable.Value = 1.0;
					if (hYSYSPropertyTableUtility.TEMPERATUREs != null && hYSYSPropertyTableUtility.TEMPERATUREs.Values.Length >= 2)
					{
						internalRealVariable = backDoor2.get_BackDoorVariable((object)":ExtraData.501").Variable as InternalRealVariable;
						internalRealVariable.SetValue(hYSYSPropertyTableUtility.TEMPERATUREs.Values[0], hYSYSPropertyTableUtility.TEMPERATUREs.Unit);
						internalRealVariable = backDoor2.get_BackDoorVariable((object)":ExtraData.503").Variable as InternalRealVariable;
						internalRealVariable.SetValue(hYSYSPropertyTableUtility.TEMPERATUREs.Values[1], hYSYSPropertyTableUtility.TEMPERATUREs.Unit);
					}
				}
				else
				{
					InternalRealVariable internalRealVariable = backDoor2.get_BackDoorVariable((object)":Enum.500").Variable as InternalRealVariable;
					internalRealVariable.Value = 0.0;
					internalRealVariable = backDoor2.get_BackDoorVariable((object)":Selection.502").Variable as InternalRealVariable;
					internalRealVariable.Value = 0.0;
					if (hYSYSPropertyTableUtility.VaporFraction != null)
					{
						internalRealVariable = backDoor2.get_BackDoorVariable((object)":ExtraData.506.0").Variable as InternalRealVariable;
						internalRealVariable.Value = hYSYSPropertyTableUtility.VaporFraction.Value;
					}
					if (hYSYSPropertyTableUtility.IsDewBubP)
					{
						internalRealVariable = backDoor2.get_BackDoorVariable((object)":Enum.501").Variable as InternalRealVariable;
						internalRealVariable.Value = 1.0;
						if (hYSYSPropertyTableUtility.TEMPERATUREs != null && hYSYSPropertyTableUtility.TEMPERATUREs.Values.Length >= 2)
						{
							internalRealVariable = backDoor2.get_BackDoorVariable((object)":ExtraData.501").Variable as InternalRealVariable;
							internalRealVariable.SetValue(hYSYSPropertyTableUtility.TEMPERATUREs.Values[0], hYSYSPropertyTableUtility.TEMPERATUREs.Unit);
							internalRealVariable = backDoor2.get_BackDoorVariable((object)":ExtraData.503").Variable as InternalRealVariable;
							internalRealVariable.SetValue(hYSYSPropertyTableUtility.TEMPERATUREs.Values[1], hYSYSPropertyTableUtility.TEMPERATUREs.Unit);
						}
					}
					else if (hYSYSPropertyTableUtility.IsDewBubT)
					{
						internalRealVariable = backDoor2.get_BackDoorVariable((object)":Enum.501").Variable as InternalRealVariable;
						internalRealVariable.Value = 2.0;
						if (hYSYSPropertyTableUtility.PRESSUREs != null && hYSYSPropertyTableUtility.PRESSUREs.Values.Length >= 2)
						{
							internalRealVariable = backDoor2.get_BackDoorVariable((object)":ExtraData.501").Variable as InternalRealVariable;
							internalRealVariable.SetValue(hYSYSPropertyTableUtility.PRESSUREs.Values[0], hYSYSPropertyTableUtility.PRESSUREs.Unit);
							internalRealVariable = backDoor2.get_BackDoorVariable((object)":ExtraData.503").Variable as InternalRealVariable;
							internalRealVariable.SetValue(hYSYSPropertyTableUtility.PRESSUREs.Values[1], hYSYSPropertyTableUtility.PRESSUREs.Unit);
						}
					}
				}
				string message3 = $"AddNewDepProp HeatFlow Heat Flow";
				backDoor2.SendBackDoorMessage(message3);
				if (hYSYSPropertyTableUtility.Points > 0.0)
				{
					InternalRealVariable internalRealVariable = backDoor2.get_BackDoorVariable((object)":ExtraData.504.0").Variable as InternalRealVariable;
					internalRealVariable.Value = hYSYSPropertyTableUtility.Points;
					internalRealVariable = backDoor2.get_BackDoorVariable((object)":ExtraData.505.0").Variable as InternalRealVariable;
					internalRealVariable.Value = hYSYSPropertyTableUtility.Points;
				}
				backDoor2.SendBackDoorMessage("ExecutePropertyTable");
				InternalTextVariable internalTextVariable2 = backDoor2.get_BackDoorTextVariable((object)":Notes.400").Variable as InternalTextVariable;
				foreach (string convertNote2 in hYSYSPropertyTableUtility.ConvertNotes)
				{
					internalTextVariable2.Value = internalTextVariable2.Value + convertNote2 + "\n";
				}
			}
			catch (Exception)
			{
			}
		}
	}

	private void PreHandlePropertyTableData()
	{
		foreach (HYSYSPropertyTableUtility hYSYSPropertyTableUtility in _HYSYSTree._PFDObject.HYSYSPropertyTableUtilitys)
		{
			try
			{
				if (!hYSYSPropertyTableUtility.IsHCurve)
				{
					continue;
				}
				HYSYSStream streamByName = HYSYSStream.GetStreamByName(hYSYSPropertyTableUtility.StreamID);
				if (streamByName == null)
				{
					continue;
				}
				if (hYSYSPropertyTableUtility.TEMPERATUREs == null || hYSYSPropertyTableUtility.TEMPERATUREs.Values.Length == 0)
				{
					if (hYSYSPropertyTableUtility.DT != null && hYSYSPropertyTableUtility.DT.Value != 0.0 && streamByName.Temp != null && streamByName.Temp.Value != 0.0 && hYSYSPropertyTableUtility.Points > 1.0)
					{
						DoubleValue dT = hYSYSPropertyTableUtility.DT;
						DoubleValue convertedValue = HYSYSUOMMap.GetConvertedValue(UnitClassEnum.Temperature, streamByName.Temp, dT.Unit);
						hYSYSPropertyTableUtility.TEMPERATUREs = new DoubleValues();
						hYSYSPropertyTableUtility.TEMPERATUREs.Unit = dT.Unit;
						hYSYSPropertyTableUtility.TEMPERATUREs.Values = new double[2]
						{
							convertedValue.Value,
							dT.Value * (hYSYSPropertyTableUtility.Points - 1.0) + convertedValue.Value
						};
					}
					else if (hYSYSPropertyTableUtility.DT != null && hYSYSPropertyTableUtility.DT.Value != 0.0 && hYSYSPropertyTableUtility.Points > 0.0)
					{
						string item = $"Stream {streamByName.Stream} Temperature is empty, Please enter Temperature Lower & Upper Bound  in Design->Connection->Independent Variables after the stream converged.";
						hYSYSPropertyTableUtility.ConvertNotes.Add(item);
					}
					else if (hYSYSPropertyTableUtility.DT != null && hYSYSPropertyTableUtility.DT.Value == 0.0 && streamByName.Temp == null)
					{
						string item2 = $"Stream {streamByName.Stream} Temperature is empty, Please enter Temperature  in Design->Connection->Independent Variables after the stream converged.";
						hYSYSPropertyTableUtility.ConvertNotes.Add(item2);
					}
					else if (hYSYSPropertyTableUtility.DT != null && hYSYSPropertyTableUtility.DT.Value == 0.0 && streamByName.Temp != null)
					{
						hYSYSPropertyTableUtility.TEMP = streamByName.Temp;
					}
				}
				if (hYSYSPropertyTableUtility.PRESSUREs == null || hYSYSPropertyTableUtility.PRESSUREs.Values.Length == 0)
				{
					if (hYSYSPropertyTableUtility.DP != null && hYSYSPropertyTableUtility.DP.Value != 0.0 && streamByName.Pressure != null && streamByName.Pressure.Value != 0.0 && hYSYSPropertyTableUtility.Points > 1.0)
					{
						DoubleValue convertedValueToDefaultKey = HYSYSUOMMap.GetConvertedValueToDefaultKey(UnitClassEnum.DropPressure, hYSYSPropertyTableUtility.DP);
						DoubleValue convertedValueToDefaultKey2 = HYSYSUOMMap.GetConvertedValueToDefaultKey(UnitClassEnum.Pressure, streamByName.Pressure);
						hYSYSPropertyTableUtility.PRESSUREs = new DoubleValues();
						hYSYSPropertyTableUtility.PRESSUREs.Unit = convertedValueToDefaultKey.Unit;
						hYSYSPropertyTableUtility.PRESSUREs.Values = new double[2]
						{
							convertedValueToDefaultKey2.Value,
							convertedValueToDefaultKey.Value * (hYSYSPropertyTableUtility.Points - 1.0) + convertedValueToDefaultKey2.Value
						};
					}
					else if (hYSYSPropertyTableUtility.DP != null && hYSYSPropertyTableUtility.DP.Value != 0.0 && hYSYSPropertyTableUtility.Points > 0.0)
					{
						string item3 = $"Stream {streamByName.Stream} Pressure is empty, Please enter Pressure Lower & Upper Bound in Design->Connection->Independent Variables  after the stream converged.";
						hYSYSPropertyTableUtility.ConvertNotes.Add(item3);
					}
					else if (hYSYSPropertyTableUtility.DP != null && hYSYSPropertyTableUtility.DP.Value == 0.0 && streamByName.Pressure == null)
					{
						string item4 = $"Stream {streamByName.Stream} Pressure is empty, Please enter Pressure in Design->Connection->Independent Variables  after the stream converged.";
						hYSYSPropertyTableUtility.ConvertNotes.Add(item4);
					}
					else if (hYSYSPropertyTableUtility.DP != null && hYSYSPropertyTableUtility.DP.Value == 0.0 && streamByName.Pressure != null)
					{
						hYSYSPropertyTableUtility.PRESSURE = streamByName.Pressure;
					}
				}
			}
			catch
			{
			}
		}
	}

	private void AddDepressuring()
	{
		foreach (HYSYSDerpessureUtility hYSYSDerpessureUtility in _HYSYSTree._PFDObject.HYSYSDerpessureUtilitys)
		{
			try
			{
				BackDoor backDoor = (_SimCase.UtilityObjects.Add(hYSYSDerpessureUtility.UID, "DynamicsDepressuringObject") as CDynDepressuring) as BackDoor;
				BackDoor backDoor2 = GetProcessStream(hYSYSDerpessureUtility.Feed) as BackDoor;
				backDoor.SendAttachObject(":MaterialStream.500.0", backDoor2);
				InternalRealVariable internalRealVariable = backDoor.get_BackDoorVariable((object)":Enum.506").Variable as InternalRealVariable;
				if (hYSYSDerpessureUtility.IsVertical)
				{
					internalRealVariable.Value = 1.0;
				}
				else
				{
					internalRealVariable.Value = 0.0;
				}
				internalRealVariable = backDoor.get_BackDoorVariable((object)":Volume.500").Variable as InternalRealVariable;
				internalRealVariable.Value = -32767.0;
				internalRealVariable = backDoor.get_BackDoorVariable((object)":Length.510").Variable as InternalRealVariable;
				internalRealVariable.SetValue(hYSYSDerpessureUtility.Height.Value, hYSYSDerpessureUtility.Height.Unit);
				internalRealVariable = backDoor.get_BackDoorVariable((object)":Length.511").Variable as InternalRealVariable;
				internalRealVariable.SetValue(hYSYSDerpessureUtility.Diameter.Value, hYSYSDerpessureUtility.Diameter.Unit);
				internalRealVariable = backDoor.get_BackDoorVariable((object)":Enum.501").Variable as InternalRealVariable;
				if (hYSYSDerpessureUtility.HeatFlux.Mode == HYSYSDerpessureUtility.HeatOperatingMode.Fire)
				{
					internalRealVariable.Value = 0.0;
					if (hYSYSDerpessureUtility.HeatFlux != null && hYSYSDerpessureUtility.HeatFlux.FireDataC.Count == 5)
					{
						for (int i = 0; i < 5; i++)
						{
							internalRealVariable = backDoor.get_BackDoorVariable((object)(":ExtraData.509." + i)).Variable as InternalRealVariable;
							internalRealVariable.Value = hYSYSDerpessureUtility.HeatFlux.FireDataC[i];
						}
					}
				}
				else if (hYSYSDerpessureUtility.HeatFlux.Mode == HYSYSDerpessureUtility.HeatOperatingMode.FireAPI521)
				{
					internalRealVariable.Value = 1.0;
					internalRealVariable = backDoor.get_BackDoorVariable((object)":ExtraData.509.2").Variable as InternalRealVariable;
					internalRealVariable.Value = 1.0;
					internalRealVariable = backDoor.get_BackDoorVariable((object)":Boolean.509").Variable as InternalRealVariable;
					internalRealVariable.Value = 1.0;
					internalRealVariable = backDoor.get_BackDoorVariable((object)":Selection.500").Variable as InternalRealVariable;
					internalRealVariable.Value = 0.0;
				}
				if (hYSYSDerpessureUtility.ValvePara.BackPressure != null)
				{
					internalRealVariable = backDoor.get_BackDoorVariable((object)":SmallArea.505").Variable as InternalRealVariable;
					internalRealVariable.SetValue(hYSYSDerpessureUtility.ValvePara.BackPressure.Value, hYSYSDerpessureUtility.ValvePara.BackPressure.Unit);
				}
				if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(hYSYSDerpessureUtility.MakeUpStringName))
				{
					double molarFlowValue = GetProcessStream(hYSYSDerpessureUtility.MakeUpStringName).MolarFlowValue;
					if (molarFlowValue != 0.0)
					{
						internalRealVariable = backDoor.get_BackDoorVariable((object)":MolarFlow.500.0").Variable as InternalRealVariable;
						internalRealVariable.Value = molarFlowValue;
					}
				}
				if (hYSYSDerpessureUtility.ValvePara.Equation == HYSYSDerpessureUtility.VapourFlowEquation.Supersonic)
				{
					internalRealVariable = backDoor.get_BackDoorVariable((object)":Enum.502").Variable as InternalRealVariable;
					internalRealVariable.Value = 1.0;
					internalRealVariable = backDoor.get_BackDoorVariable((object)":ExtraData.504.2").Variable as InternalRealVariable;
					internalRealVariable.Value = hYSYSDerpessureUtility.ValvePara.Cd;
					internalRealVariable = backDoor.get_BackDoorVariable((object)":SmallArea.505").Variable as InternalRealVariable;
					internalRealVariable.SetValue(hYSYSDerpessureUtility.ValvePara.Area.Value, hYSYSDerpessureUtility.ValvePara.Area.Unit);
				}
				else if (hYSYSDerpessureUtility.ValvePara.Equation == HYSYSDerpessureUtility.VapourFlowEquation.Subsonic)
				{
					internalRealVariable = backDoor.get_BackDoorVariable((object)":Enum.502").Variable as InternalRealVariable;
					internalRealVariable.Value = 2.0;
					internalRealVariable = backDoor.get_BackDoorVariable((object)":ExtraData.504.2").Variable as InternalRealVariable;
					internalRealVariable.Value = hYSYSDerpessureUtility.ValvePara.Cd;
					internalRealVariable = backDoor.get_BackDoorVariable((object)":SmallArea.505").Variable as InternalRealVariable;
					internalRealVariable.SetValue(hYSYSDerpessureUtility.ValvePara.Area.Value, hYSYSDerpessureUtility.ValvePara.Area.Unit);
				}
				else if (hYSYSDerpessureUtility.ValvePara.Equation == HYSYSDerpessureUtility.VapourFlowEquation.Masoneilan)
				{
					internalRealVariable = backDoor.get_BackDoorVariable((object)":Enum.502").Variable as InternalRealVariable;
					internalRealVariable.Value = 2.0;
					if (hYSYSDerpessureUtility.ValvePara.Cv != null)
					{
						internalRealVariable = backDoor.get_BackDoorVariable((object)":ExtraData.504.2").Variable as InternalRealVariable;
						internalRealVariable.Value = hYSYSDerpessureUtility.ValvePara.Cv.Value;
					}
					if (hYSYSDerpessureUtility.ValvePara.Cf != null)
					{
						internalRealVariable = backDoor.get_BackDoorVariable((object)":ExtraData.504.3").Variable as InternalRealVariable;
						internalRealVariable.Value = hYSYSDerpessureUtility.ValvePara.Cf.Value;
					}
				}
				if (hYSYSDerpessureUtility.OperationConditions.TimeStepSize != null && hYSYSDerpessureUtility.OperationConditions.TimeStepSize.Value != 0.0)
				{
					internalRealVariable = backDoor.get_BackDoorVariable((object)":Time.501").Variable as InternalRealVariable;
					internalRealVariable.SetValue(hYSYSDerpessureUtility.OperationConditions.TimeStepSize.Value, hYSYSDerpessureUtility.OperationConditions.TimeStepSize.Unit);
				}
				if (hYSYSDerpessureUtility.OperationConditions.DepressuringTime != null && hYSYSDerpessureUtility.OperationConditions.DepressuringTime.Value != 0.0)
				{
					internalRealVariable = backDoor.get_BackDoorVariable((object)":Time.500").Variable as InternalRealVariable;
					internalRealVariable.SetValue(hYSYSDerpessureUtility.OperationConditions.DepressuringTime.Value, hYSYSDerpessureUtility.OperationConditions.DepressuringTime.Unit);
				}
				InternalTextVariable internalTextVariable = backDoor.get_BackDoorTextVariable((object)":Notes.400").Variable as InternalTextVariable;
				foreach (string convertNote in hYSYSDerpessureUtility.ConvertNotes)
				{
					internalTextVariable.Value = internalTextVariable.Value + convertNote + "\n";
				}
			}
			catch
			{
			}
		}
	}
}
public class HYSYSValveConverter : HYSYSObjectConverterBase
{
	private HYSYSValve _data;

	private Valve _valve;

	private double feedPress;

	private double productPress;

	public HYSYSValveConverter(SimulationCase simulationCase, HYSYSValve data)
		: base(simulationCase)
	{
		try
		{
			_data = data;
			_valve = _Flowsheet.get_Operations((object)"ValveOpObject").Add(data.UID, "ValveOpObject") as Valve;
			CreateValve();
		}
		catch (Exception)
		{
		}
	}

	private void CreateValve()
	{
		BackDoor backDoor = null;
		ProcessStream processStream = null;
		ProcessStream processStream2 = null;
		BackDoor backDoor2 = null;
		BackDoor backDoor3 = null;
		InternalRealVariable internalRealVariable = null;
		bool flag = false;
		try
		{
			backDoor = _valve as BackDoor;
			if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(_data.FluidPackage))
			{
				string methodName = HYSYSFluidPackages.GetMethodName(_data.FluidPackage);
				if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(methodName))
				{
					backDoor3 = _SimCase.BasisManager.FluidPackages[methodName] as BackDoor;
					backDoor.SendAttachObject(":CompList.300", backDoor3);
				}
			}
			processStream2 = GetProcessStream(_data.OutletStreams[0].Trim());
			processStream = GetProcessStream(_data.InletStreams[0].Trim());
			if (_data.InletStreams != null)
			{
				backDoor2 = processStream as BackDoor;
				backDoor.SendAttachObject(":MaterialStream.500", backDoor2);
				feedPress = processStream.Pressure.Value;
			}
			if (_data.OutletStreams != null)
			{
				backDoor2 = processStream2 as BackDoor;
				backDoor.SendAttachObject(":MaterialStream.501", backDoor2);
				productPress = processStream2.Pressure.Value;
			}
			if (_data.ATTACHED_PRESSURE != null && processStream2.Pressure.CanModify)
			{
				processStream2.Pressure.SetValue(_data.ATTACHED_PRESSURE.Value, _data.ATTACHED_PRESSURE.Unit);
				internalRealVariable = backDoor.get_BackDoorVariable((object)":PressureDrop.500").Variable as InternalRealVariable;
				if (internalRealVariable.CanModify)
				{
					internalRealVariable.Value = -32767.0;
				}
				else if (feedPress == -32767.0 && internalRealVariable.Value != -32767.0)
				{
					DoubleValue convertedValue = HYSYSUOMMap.GetConvertedValue(UnitClassEnum.Pressure, _data.ATTACHED_PRESSURE, "kpa");
					processStream.Pressure.SetValue(convertedValue.Value + internalRealVariable.Value, "");
				}
				flag = true;
			}
			else if (_data.ATTACHED_PRESSUREDROP != null && !flag)
			{
				internalRealVariable = backDoor.get_BackDoorVariable((object)":PressureDrop.500").Variable as InternalRealVariable;
				if (internalRealVariable.CanModify)
				{
					internalRealVariable.SetValue(_data.ATTACHED_PRESSUREDROP.Value, _data.ATTACHED_PRESSUREDROP.Unit);
					if (processStream2.Pressure.CanModify)
					{
						processStream2.Pressure.SetValue(-32767.0, "");
					}
					else if (feedPress == -32767.0)
					{
						DoubleValue convertedValue2 = HYSYSUOMMap.GetConvertedValue(UnitClassEnum.DropPressure, _data.ATTACHED_PRESSUREDROP, "kpa");
						processStream.Pressure.SetValue(productPress + convertedValue2.Value, "");
					}
				}
			}
			else if (_data.ATTACHED_Temperature != null)
			{
				internalRealVariable = backDoor.get_BackDoorVariable((object)":PressureDrop.500").Variable as InternalRealVariable;
				if (internalRealVariable.CanModify)
				{
					internalRealVariable.Value = -32767.0;
				}
				processStream2.Temperature.SetValue(_data.ATTACHED_Temperature.Value, _data.ATTACHED_Temperature.Unit);
			}
			InternalTextVariable internalTextVariable = backDoor.get_BackDoorTextVariable((object)":Notes.400").Variable as InternalTextVariable;
			foreach (string convertNote in _data.ConvertNotes)
			{
				internalTextVariable.Value = internalTextVariable.Value + convertNote + "\n";
			}
		}
		catch (Exception)
		{
			DebugPrint.OutputDebugString(DebugLevel.BuildHYSYSCase, WarningLevel.Error, string.Format(P2Globalization.GetString("UOConverter_Failed"), "HYSYS Valve unit operation block", _data.UID));
		}
		finally
		{
			CleanResource.ReleaseComObjectHelper(backDoor, bFinalRelease: false);
			CleanResource.ReleaseComObjectHelper(backDoor2, bFinalRelease: false);
			CleanResource.ReleaseComObjectHelper(processStream, bFinalRelease: false);
			CleanResource.ReleaseComObjectHelper(processStream2, bFinalRelease: false);
			CleanResource.ReleaseComObjectHelper(backDoor3, bFinalRelease: false);
			CleanResource.ReleaseComObjectHelper(internalRealVariable, bFinalRelease: false);
		}
	}
}

Latest version is '11.2.0.10050' (yours is '9.1.0.7988')
